### [1] [ParsHate: A Benchmark Dataset for Hate and Target Detection in Persian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16393)
*Zahra Bokaei,Walid Magdy,Bonnie Webber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ParsHate是首个跨越十年（2013-2022）的波斯语仇恨言论基准数据集，包含10,000条人工标注的推文，涵盖七类目标分类、显隐式仇恨标注及跨度级解释依据，并通过实验揭示了当前模型在隐式仇恨和少数目标类别上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语仇恨言论检测数据集相对匮乏，现有资源存在关键词采样偏差、时间跨度有限、缺乏结构化目标分类及隐式仇恨标注等局限性，制约了对波斯语网络仇恨话语的系统性研究。

Method: 采用混合时间采样策略（随机采样+分类器分数分层采样），从2900万条波斯语推文中选取10,000条；由三名母语者标注，涵盖仇恨检测、显/隐式区分、三类隐式策略、七类目标分类（含子类别）、显/隐式目标标记及跨度级解释；通过标注一致性分析、跨年时序鲁棒性实验、词汇多样性（TTR）分析及多标签目标预测实验进行基准测试。

Result: 整合多年训练达到79%宏F1（仇恨检测），但目标识别仅25.5%宏F1；隐式仇恨检测近乎失败（F1<7），文化/历史引用策略最难；早期年份性能显著下降，反映时间分布漂移；模型在少数目标类别上出现多数类坍缩；跨度定位在显式仇恨上F1=58，隐式上仅14。

Conclusion: ParsHate通过结构化标注揭示了波斯语仇恨检测的核心挑战——隐式修辞、目标类别不平衡及时间分布漂移，当前SOTA模型均无法有效解决，需要融合文化背景知识与针对少数类别的新型建模方法。

Abstract: We introduce ParsHate, a manually annotated dataset of 10,000 Persian tweets spanning 2013-2022, representing the first decade-long benchmark for hate speech detection in Persian. The dataset contains 31% hateful content and supports both hate detection and multi-label fine-grained target identification across seven structured target categories. ParsHate also distinguishes explicit and implicit hate, marks explicit and implicit targets, and provides span-level rationales. Data collection combines random and score-stratified temporal sampling to reduce keyword-driven bias while preserving natural label distributions. Applying SOTA models for Persian hate-speech detection on ParsHate shows moderate performance (79% F1), especially with samples from earlier years, and low performance with target identification (25.5% macro-F1). This emphasizes the diverse sampling of hate speech in ParsHate and its challenging nature that requires more advanced methods for better performance. Dataset is made publicly available.

</details>


### [2] [Retrieval-Driven Memory Reconsolidation for Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16053)
*Yuanyi Song,Yukai Wang,Xinbei Ma,Zhihui Fu,Jianghao Lin,Weiwen Liu,Jun Wang,Huarong Deng,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REALM提出了一种面向LLM智能体的检索驱动记忆再巩固框架，将长期记忆建模为持续演化的闭环生命周期，通过异构认知图组织记忆、自适应图搜索原子检索、以及基于检索反馈的局部拓扑再巩固，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统主要在新信息到达时更新记忆，将检索视为记忆访问的终点而非记忆演化的驱动力，且依赖预定义记忆结构和固定检索管线，限制了智能体自主组织和演化记忆的能力。这与认知神经科学中"记忆再巩固"机制形成鲜明对比——被回忆的记忆并非静态数据，而是进入不稳定态以允许连接被增强、削弱或新建。

Method: REALM包含三个互补组件：(1) 自主组织——定义原子记忆节点(entity/event/episode/fact)和关系类型(logical/causal/hierarchical/associative)，智能体自主构建涌现式认知图；(2) 自适应检索——将图搜索分解为原子动作(seeding→expanding→filtering)，按状态动态组合检索策略；(3) 记忆再巩固——每次任务后对激活子图执行局部拓扑更新，包含add/strengthen/weaken三种边操作，通过置信度引导的有界权重调节实现闭环演化。形式化公式：G_{t+1}=Reconsolidating(G_t \ G_q ∪ G'_q, f)。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准上以GPT-4o-mini为骨干达到75.97%平均准确率，超越最强基线7.17个百分点，四类问题(多跳/时序/开放域/单跳)全部第一；在LongMemEval上达到65.11%，超越最强基线1.31个点。消融实验证实再巩固模块在两个基准上分别带来+2.01和+2.13的提升，且对问题顺序随机化具有鲁棒性。进一步分析揭示：再巩固的主要价值不在于扩展可检索记忆空间（证据发现仅提升3.70%/5.41%），而在于将证据利用率提升20.83%/19.23%，表明记忆拓扑通过重组成更连贯的局部结构来支持集体证据调用。

Conclusion: 该工作将LLM智能体长期记忆概念化为动态闭环生命周期，证明记忆网络可通过使用主动适应而非仅随新输入扩展。通过将检索从被动终点转变为持续反馈循环，REALM在异构认知图上实例化该生命周期。实验表明检索驱动再巩固有效缓解了预定义记忆架构的刚性，并产生紧凑的证据聚类，将分散交互组织为支持集体回忆的结构性子图。从平面累积模型转向持续生命周期视角，有望启发未来对自适应、自演化智能体记忆基础的研究。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM-based agents operating over extended interactions. Existing memory systems primarily update memory when new information arrives, treating retrieval as the endpoint of memory access rather than a driver of memory evolution. Consequently, retrieval feedback is rarely exploited to reorganize memory for future access continuously. Moreover, most existing approaches rely on predefined memory structures together with fixed retrieval pipelines, limiting the agent's ability to organize and evolve its own memory autonomously. Inspired by memory reconsolidation in cognitive neuroscience, we propose \textbf{REALM}, a \textbf{r}econsolidation-\textbf{e}volution \textbf{a}gentic \textbf{l}ong-term \textbf{m}emory framework. It models long-term memory as a continual lifecycle by autonomously organizing memories into a heterogeneous cognitive graph, retrieving evidence via adaptively composed graph-search atoms, and continually reconsolidating memories based on retrieval feedback. REALM achieves an average accuracy of 75.97\% on LoCoMo and 65.11\% on LongMemEval, outperforming the strongest baselines by 7.17 and 1.31 points respectively. Ablation studies confirm that memory reconsolidation consistently boosts performance, with further analyses revealing that it progressively reorganizes related memory units into more coherent local structures for collective evidence recall and utilization during reasoning. These results suggest that retrieval-driven memory reconsolidation provides an effective mechanism for continually evolving long-term memory in LLM agents.

</details>


### [3] [State of Thought Enables Endogenous Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16055)
*Zhiren Gong,Yikun Hou,Zihao Zeng,Ming Xiao,Chau Yuen,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出State of Thought (SoT)，一种使LLM能够进行内生(endogenous)推理的新范式。SoT从模型内部信息传递中提取紧凑的"动力学-几何状态"(4维向量)，并使用仅582参数的控制器在冻结骨干上选择性地激活当前推理状态下有用的历史证据，将推理重新定义为由状态条件驱动的证据组织过程，而非外部规定的token链。在3个LLM、16个数据集、2个VLM规模的全面实验中，SoT在准确率上稳定优于各类基线，同时显著降低token消耗(62.6%)和延迟(44.6%)，并在受限访问场景下仍保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时推理方法主要依赖外部控制：固定的推理程序(如CoT、Plan-and-Solve)或受限搜索空间的扩展(如MCTS、Self-Consistency)。这些方法将推理控制施加于模型外部，忽略了模型自身的内生推理状态作为决策信号，导致：(1)跨异构推理结构的泛化性受限；(2)在约束推理下的效率不足；(3)无法利用LLM内部已展现的结构化表征几何和轨迹规律性。论文核心动机是将推理控制从"外部token程序"转变为"内生状态条件化的证据组织"。

Method: SoT的核心方法包含三部分：(1) **内生推理状态**：从sentence-level隐藏状态提取4维动力学-几何状态 $m_t=[\delta_t, v_t, c_t, H_t]$，分别表征内部组织集中度、逐步进展幅度、方向一致性和预测不确定性；(2) **状态条件化证据组织算子** $\mathcal{S}$：基于当前状态与历史状态的16维关系描述符 $(m_t; m_j; m_t-m_j; m_t\odot m_j)$ 通过16×32选择投影(577参数)打分，选择与当前推理状态兼容的稀疏历史子集；(3) **状态条件化停止算子** $\mathcal{T}$：通过4→1线性头(5参数)从当前状态直接预测停止概率。控制器仅582参数，骨干完全冻结。训练采用离线轨迹上的BCE损失，学习偏好式证据保留目标和停止充分性目标。理论分析包含5个命题：策略类分离、状态条件化的必要性、稀疏证据的优势、闭环优于开环、状态驱动停止的最优性。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B上，SoT在定量推理(65.8%)、符号与代码(58.4%)、通用理解(70.4%)、长上下文推理(31.9%)四个领域均取得最佳均值；在16个数据集中13个取得最优/并列最优。相对最强基线，准确率提升18.6%/37.4%/10.7%/46.3%，同时tokens减少52.6%、延迟降低66.4%。在Qwen2.5-14B和Mixtral-8x7B上同样稳定领先。在VLM(Qwen2.5-VL-7B/32B)上3个多模态推理任务平均提升3.8点，completion tokens减少74.9%、延迟降低73.5%。受限访问变体：无训练控制器保留38.2%平均准确率增益，嵌入特征变体保留36.5%；SoT-Judge在3个API模型上达到84.1%的轨迹级判断一致性。消融实验证实四个状态坐标的互补性以及证据组织与停止算子的必要性。

Conclusion: SoT将测试时推理的控制变量从外部token程序转变为内生状态条件化的证据组织，提供了一种可泛化、高效的替代方案。其核心贡献是将推理形式化为连续内生状态与离散可检视推理步骤之间的闭环反馈控制：状态组织证据→证据调节下一步→下一步更新状态。该框架在准确率-效率前沿上跨文本与多模态基准均取得进步，并在受限访问设置下保持鲁棒。论文提出了一个更广泛的原理：推理系统不必依赖日益复杂的外部脚手架，自适应性和效率可以直接从模型内部状态几何中涌现。局限性包括性能依赖于状态接口的充分性、4维状态可能对复杂轨迹欠充分、机理论证虽支持但不唯一确定等。未来工作方向包括更丰富的紧凑状态、自适应记忆单元、白盒与黑盒轨迹控制的统一框架等。

Abstract: Test-time compute has emerged as a major approach to improving the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing test-time reasoning paradigms rely heavily on externally imposed control, either through fixed reasoning programs or through costly expansion in constrained search spaces, limiting both generalization and efficiency. We propose State of Thought (SoT), a new reasoning paradigm that enables endogenous reasoning in LLMs, with the model's internal reasoning state governing how reasoning unfolds. Concretely, SoT extracts a compact dynamics-geometric state from the model's internal information transfer and uses a 582-parameter controller on frozen backbones to selectively activate historical reasoning support useful under the current reasoning state, framing reasoning as a state-conditioned process over evidence rather than an externally prescribed token chain. Across quantitative (1.34x), general (1.62x), symbolic-and-code (1.76x), and long-context (2.51x) reasoning on 3 LLMs and 16 datasets, SoT consistently improves mean-baseline accuracy while reducing generated tokens by 62.6% and end-to-end latency by 44.6%. Across 2 VLM scales and 3 reasoning tasks, it improves mean accuracy by 3.8 points over reasoning baselines, with 74.9% fewer completion tokens and 73.5% lower latency than search-based methods. Under constrained access, SoT retains 38.2%/36.5% mean accuracy gains in training-free/embedding-only settings, while trajectory-only judging reaches 84.1% agreement across 3 API models. Together, endogenous state-driven reasoning provides a generalizable and efficient alternative.

</details>


### [4] [Efficient Multimodal Generative Recommendation with Latent Narrative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16070)
*Chenxing Wang,Nantao Zheng,Hao Miao,Juyuan Wang,Xinke Jiang,Yuchen Fang,Aolin Li,Haijun Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 NarraLite，一个面向短剧续推任务的高效多模态生成式推荐框架，通过渐进式频谱压缩（PSC）压缩视觉冗余信息，并通过潜在叙事推理（LNR）以隐式潜在 token 替代显式思维链推理，从而在保证续推准确率的同时显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成式推荐主要建模用户偏好，而剧情类内容的下一片段预测由故事线演化决定，依赖多模态情节理解；同时长视频视觉上下文存在严重冗余，显式思维链推理带来额外自回归开销，亟需在感知与推理两端同时压缩。

Method: 1) 渐进式频谱压缩（PSC）：文本条件频域调制 + 锚帧 Top-K 选择 + 跨帧多样性感知筛选，将大量视觉 patch 压缩为少量叙事相关 token；2) 潜在叙事推理（LNR）：通过全局可学习的专家 token 库，由上下文路由器以 Straight-Through Gumbel Top-K 选取少量潜在 token，并以 InfoNCE 损失与未来续推语义对齐；3) 基于 Qwen-3.5-2B 多模态大模型，通过 RQ-KMeans 构建层次 SID，采用 trie 约束束搜索解码。

Result: 在自建的 UGC/PGC/OOD 短剧续推基准（含 25 万+ 视频对）上，NarraLite 在 H@1–H@10、OPSC 叙事一致性指标上均优于 BM25、Dense、SASRec/GRU4Rec/BERT4Rec、TIGER、LETTER、EARec、LatentR3 等基线；OOD 上 H@10 达 0.3090，OPSC 达 0.5506；消融实验验证 PSC 与 LNR 均关键；效率分析显示其精度-吞吐量权衡严格优于全帧不同分辨率变体。

Conclusion: 选择性多模态感知与潜在过渡推理对准确且高效的剧情类推荐具有互补重要性；NarraLite 为面向短剧/连续剧集等情节型内容的生成式推荐提供了兼顾精度与效率的可行方案，并发布了一个无用户偏好的多模态续推基准。

Abstract: Generative recommendation reformulates item prediction as semantic identifier generation, yet episodic content introduces a fundamentally different setting where the target is determined by narrative evolution rather than user preference. This task requires models to understand multimodal storyline progression while addressing the efficiency challenges caused by redundant visual contexts and costly explicit reasoning generation. We propose \textbf{NarraLite}, an efficient multimodal generative recommendation framework that jointly compresses perception and reasoning. Specifically, Progressive Spectral Compression selectively distills long visual contexts into compact narrative-relevant evidence, preserving transition-critical information while reducing redundant visual computation. Latent Narrative Reasoning introduces context-routed latent reasoning tokens and aligns their contextualized representations with future continuation semantics, enabling implicit narrative inference without autoregressively decoding textual rationales. We further establish a user-agnostic multimodal benchmark for short-form drama continuation across UGC, PGC, and OOD settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NarraLite consistently improves continuation accuracy, narrative coherence, and robustness over existing approaches, while achieving a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [5] [The Immutable Past: Formalizing State Mutability and Conflict Resolution in Mutable RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16073)
*Hamed HaddadPajouh,Amir AmiriTabat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对长程自主智能体中 RAG 记忆架构的"状态可变性"问题进行形式化分析，提出"语义遮蔽"(Semantic Shadowing)失败模式，并引入基于时间显性算子 Φ_T 的推理时一致性协议 GC-Mem，通过实验证明其能在矛盾记忆场景中恢复 >90% 的冲突解决准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RAG 将共享记忆视为仅追加的流（append-only stream），当实体状态随时间发生突变时（如用户偏好变化、基础设施更新），历史旧状态不会被删除。由于新旧状态语义等价但逻辑矛盾，密集检索算法会返回过时的"统计多数"块，导致"语义阴影"现象——有效的新事实被海量陈旧记忆稀释，智能体据此行动产生严重的状态发散（state divergence）。作者试图从理论上严格刻画该失败模式的渐近行为，并提出一种无损于有效长期记忆的精确剪枝协议。

Method: 1) 理论建模：定义记忆流 M_t = {(c_i, τ_i)}，引入分段常数状态函数、语义等价引理（Lemma 3.2）、累积假设 N≫M；2) 形式化两个失败定理——定理 3.4 证明 E[X] ≈ kM/(N+M) → 0（渐近召回衰减），定理 3.5 证明即使有效块被检索到，softmax 注意力的概率质量仍按 P(y=S_old) ∝ (k-1)/k 倾斜（多数票陷阱）；3) 提出 GC-Mem 协议：使用成对 NLI 矛盾检测函数 κ(c_i, c_j) 配合时间显性算子 Φ_T(R_k) = {c_i ∈ R_k | ¬∃c_j: κ(c_i,c_j)=1 ∧ τ_j > τ_i}，外科手术式切除被遮蔽的上下文；4) 在 137,760 个记忆块的行为推断基准上（合成多轮对话，关键词泄漏降至 0–3%），对比标准 RAG、时间戳重排、多因子评分及 Bag-of-Facts（k→∞）设置，对 7 个 LLM 架构进行系统评测。

Result: 1) GC-Mem 在 DeepSeek-V3（Large, n=74）上 CR-Acc 达 97.3%，o4-mini（Medium, n=38）达 92.1%，均显著优于标准 RAG（56.8%、28.9%）和时间戳重排（20.3%、10.5%），p<0.01；2) STM 缓冲器开启时 GC-Mem 优势进一步放大（Medium +22.0pp，Large +28.4pp）；3) 探测器依赖实验揭示召回率为关键阈值——DeepSeek-V3（Recall 95.2%）、o4-mini（99.8%）最优，gpt-4o（36.6%）边际有效，gpt-4.1-mini（0.7%）完全失效；4) 多因子评分消融证明引入 usage 或 stability 权重时 CR-Acc 从 94.7% 崩溃至 10.5%（违反定理 4.1）；5) Bag-of-Facts 实验显示标准 RAG 在强制暴露全时间线时准确率仅约 12%，GC-Mem 仍可剪除 13–15 个阴影块，将 DeepSeek-V3 提升 +42.4pp；6) 端到端延迟 k=10 时约 10.5 秒/实例，开 STM 后升至 15.0–26.6 秒。

Conclusion: 本文严格证明语义阴影机制会从根本层面摧毁标准可变性 RAG：通过渐近召回衰减和多数票陷阱两个定理，揭示启发式时间衰减及多因子评分均无法保证状态收敛——前者无差别破坏有效长期记忆，后者因陈旧块累积更高的 usage/stability 分数而系统性保护了需要清除的矛盾。GC-Mem 通过纯时间显性算子 Φ_T 与成对 NLI 矛盾检测的组合，提供了首个数学上可证明的状态恢复协议，可恢复 >90% 的冲突解决准确率。作者建立了严格的精度/召回部署阈值（探测器召回率 >50% 为必要条件），为长程智能体记忆架构提供了可落地的状态收敛保证。其局限性在于：基准为单属性突变的合成环境、O(k²) 冲突检测开销在超低延迟场景中可能难以承受、以及尚未在多实体并发突变的有机交互日志上验证。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) serves as the primary memory architecture for long-horizon autonomous agents. However, treating shared memory as an append-only stream introduces \textit{Semantic Shadowing}, a critical failure mode where conflicting historical observations accumulate and statistically dominate valid recent updates. In dynamic environments, this results in severe state divergence as agents retrieve and act upon obsolete facts. This paper formalizes the mechanics of State Mutability to prove that standard dense retrieval suffers from Asymptotic Recall Decay. Furthermore, we formally demonstrate a Majority Vote Trap, revealing that increasing the retrieval context window paradoxically degrades generation accuracy by diluting the attention mechanism under conditions of semantic equivalence. To resolve this, we introduce GC-Mem (Garbage Collection for Memory), a strict inference-time consistency protocol. Unlike heuristic time-decay mechanisms---which indiscriminately destroy valid long-term memory---GC-Mem relies purely on a temporal dominance operator ($\Phi_{\mathcal{T}}$) paired with contradiction detection to surgically excise shadowed context. Evaluated across a rigorous, behaviorally inferred benchmark of 137,760 memory chunks and continuous accumulation sweeps, standard RAG and timestamp re-ranking baselines experience severe degradation. In contrast, GC-Mem empirically recovers $>90\%$ conflict resolution accuracy. We establish strict precision and recall deployment thresholds, ensuring state convergence where standard mutable RAG fundamentally fails.

</details>


### [6] [The Imitation Game: When LLMs Learn to Reason Like Programs via Code-Centric Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16076)
*Jinyang Zhang,Weibin Liao,Keqin Bao,Sihang Li,Shaobo Wang,Muyang Ye,Hongxin Ding,Yue Fang,Tianyi Tang,Fei Huang,Kexin Yang,Xingzhang Ren,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MIMIC框架，利用可执行代码作为推理数据合成的严谨媒介，通过叙事融合、代码引导测试合成和动态代码插桩将算法转化为可验证的推理轨迹，并引入代码插桩奖励(CIR)为强化学习提供密集的过程监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在形式化编程任务上表现优异，却在自然语言中的确定性细粒度推理任务（如比较9.9和9.11的大小、计数strawberry中r的个数）上频繁失败，这表明现有推理能力主要依赖近似的语义模式匹配而非内化的符号执行。直接使用原始编程题目训练会触发模型的"回忆幻觉"——模型倾向于回忆编程模板而非进行迁移性的推理。

Method: MIMIC是一个四阶段端到端合成框架：(1)问题分类与数学内核提取——将Codeforces问题归入5类推理范式并提取剥离叙事后的数学内核；(2)叙事融合问题合成——从FineWebEdu教育语料采样类型兼容的上下文，将数学内核包装为现实化考试题目；(3)代码引导测试合成与叙事输入融合——分析AC代码的控制流决策点，生成三级对抗性和引导随机测试用例；(4)基于轨迹的思维链合成——对AC代码进行只读插桩注入诊断探针，将确定性执行轨迹翻译为自然语言CoT。框架还引入代码插桩奖励(CIR)，利用执行检查点为RL提供密集过程级奖励。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B/8B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-8B上的实验显示，SFT和GRPO训练后在通用推理、数学和细粒度确定性任务上均取得显著一致提升：Qwen3-8B GRPO使通用推理平均提升3.6分、数学平均提升2.7分，AIME从23.3跃升至26.7；DeepSeek-R1-Distill-8B GRPO使数学平均提升9.7分。在CharBench、StringBench、MazeBench等未在训练中出现的细粒度任务上实现零样本泛化。CIR在GRPO中优于二元奖励、PRM800K和Math-Shepherd等过程奖励模型。消融实验证实原始Codeforces数据会严重损害通用推理（Qwen3-4B SFT通用推理下降16.5分），验证了叙事融合的必要性。

Conclusion: 该工作将代码从生成目标重新定义为认知支架，利用程序的结构确定性将语言推理锚定在可验证逻辑上，有效缓解幻觉。该范式民主化了过程奖励模型，因为程序执行中间状态天然提供低成本、高保真的步骤级验证。MIMIC建立了一个自验证、可持续的数据引擎，为可解释和可靠的AI推理开辟了道路。局限性包括CIR采用简单的步数计数奖励形式，以及数据源和合成规模仍有扩展空间。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at programming tasks but frequently fail at deterministic, fine-grained reasoning in natural language, relying heavily on semantic approximations rather than robust symbolic execution. To bridge this gap, we propose MIMIC, a framework that leverages executable code as a rigorous medium for reasoning data synthesis. MIMIC fundamentally transforms algorithms into verifiable reasoning trajectories through narrative fusion, code-guided test synthesis, and dynamic code instrumentation. Crucially, these explicit intermediate execution states naturally form a Code-Instrumented Reward (CIR), providing dense, high-fidelity process supervision for reinforcement learning without external reward models. Extensive evaluations reveal that models trained via SFT and GRPO on our synthesized dataset achieve substantial, consistent gains. Our method significantly elevates accuracy across general reasoning, complex mathematical benchmarks, and fine-grained deterministic tasks, demonstrating that the procedural rigor of executable code can effectively unlock and enhance the generalized reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Our code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [7] [RAG-CT: Mitigating Privacy Risks on Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems via Scanning Prompt Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16095)
*Xingyu Lyu,Jiayimei Wang,Jianfeng He,Ning Wang,Yidan Hu,Yimin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RAG-CT防御方法，通过分析检索结果的熵（分布广度）和边界（top-1与top-2相似度差距）来检测并拦截针对RAG系统的PII提取攻击。该方法无需修改LLM或检索器，在两个数据集、四种攻击和四个基线下均显著优于现有防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通过引入外部知识库提升LLM输出质量，但也带来严重隐私风险：攻击者可通过精心构造的查询利用提示注入或查询优化策略从知识库中提取PII（电话号码、邮箱、SSN等）。现有防御（如查询重写、规则过滤、erase-and-check）效果有限，无法应对自适应查询优化攻击。

Method: 核心洞察：良性查询检索多条语义相关记录（高熵、小边界），恶意查询集中检索单一敏感记录（低熵、大边界）。方法包含五步：1）检索top-k记录；2）softmax归一化相似度得分；3）计算Shannon熵衡量分布广度；4）计算top-1与top-2的margin衡量决断性；5）融合为异常分数A=α·(1-Ĥ)+β·M̂，超过阈值τ则判定为恶意查询。

Result: 在HealthcareMagic-101和Enron Email两个数据集上，使用Llama2-7b/13b-chat、Qwen2-72B、ChatGPT-4四个LLM进行测试。RAG-CT将TBTG和PIDE提示注入攻击的ASR从>0.8降至0.00；对RAG-Thief和Pirate高级查询优化攻击，ASR保持在0.1以下，显著优于Rephrasing、Summarization、Rule-based和Erase-check四种基线。消融实验验证了熵与边界联合策略优于单独使用任一指标。

Conclusion: RAG-CT是一种轻量级、有效的RAG隐私防御机制，无需修改底层LLM或检索器，通过分布感知查询检测显著降低PII泄漏，为RAG系统的隐私保护提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for improving the quality of generated contents of Large Language Models (LLMs) by grounding responses in external knowledge, thus reducing hallucinations and factual errors. However, recent studies have highlighted a critical vulnerability: adversaries can exploit the retrieval process to extract personally identifiable information (PII) from the underlying corpus. To mitigate this risk, we propose a novel defense, RAG-CT, that identifies malicious queries by analyzing their entropy and margin distributions and using a score-based detection method. Extensive experiments with four state-of-the-art attack strategies and four defense baselines on two datasets show that our approach significantly reduces PII leakage while outperforming existing defenses. This work provides a lightweight yet effective mechanism to protect RAG systems against PII leakage without requiring modifications to the underlying LLM or retriever.

</details>


### [8] [Efficient One-to-Many Translation with Joint Multi-Stream Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16312)
*Yiwen Guan,Jacob Whitehill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PrismDiff，一种基于离散扩散的多流框架，用于并行一对多机器翻译。该方法通过语言无关的语义锚点（LaBSE编码）引导多个目标语言同时进行迭代去噪，实现了亚线性延迟扩展、零样本跨语言迁移能力和灵活的质量-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归（AR）翻译系统在一对多翻译场景中面临严重效率瓶颈：延迟随目标语言数量线性增长，且需要部署多个独立模型。现有的非自回归方法虽支持并行生成，但大多为一对一设计，缺乏跨目标流共享语义的机制，且难以支持零样本源语言迁移。实际场景（如国际会议同声传译）需要动态、灵活的多目标并行翻译。

Method: 1. **离散扩散框架**：基于掩码扩散的迭代去噪过程，所有目标语言共享同一画布同时优化。2. **语义锚点机制**：用冻结的LaBSE提取源语言隐藏表示作为连续锚点Z，经线性投影注入扩散模型，避免直接对源token的条件依赖。3. **多流画布设计**：将锚点与K个目标流拼接为统一画布 C_t=[Z; Y_t^(1);...;Y_t^(K)]，使用重置位置编码（Reset PE）使每个流的索引独立。4. **流内排他注意力**：每个目标流仅与自身及锚点做注意力，避免跨语言表面形式干扰。5. **CTC后处理**：对最终输出应用CTC合并规则缓解重复token。6. **对数均匀采样调度**：通过调节推理步数控制质量-延迟权衡。

Result: 在Multi30K（En-{De,Fr}）和Europarl（En-{Fr,Nl,Ro,Da}）数据集上：1. **监督设置**：PrismDiff在25-30采样步时达到约37 BLEU，与8层AR基线（37.25 BLEU）质量相当，延迟从148ms降至约70ms（2×加速）。2. **零样本迁移（Es→X）**：PrismDiff保持约28 BLEU（监督性能的75%），优于AR基线25.22 BLEU 11.9%，且Diff-SrcTokens（无锚点）直接崩溃至<1 BLEU。3. **架构消融**：联合多流优化比独立训练（Diff-Indep）提升约1.5 BLEU；排他注意力优于全注意力；重置PE显著优于绝对PE（23.8 vs 33.5 BLEU）。4. **编码器选择**：LaBSE的零样本性能（约28 BLEU）远优于mBERT（~10）和XLM-R（~13），证明需要真正跨语言对齐的语义空间。5. **可扩展性**：延迟随目标数量K呈亚线性增长；单实例显存从K=1的2.66GB仅增至K=8的2.91GB。

Conclusion: PrismDiff证明了多流离散扩散结合语义锚点是高效一对多翻译的可行方案。其核心优势在于：单一统一模型替代多个AR系统、亚线性延迟扩展、灵活的推理步数权衡、以及通过锚点实现的零样本源语言迁移。该框架为超越传统AR范式的多语言生成任务提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: One-to-many machine translation (MT) is computationally expensive for autoregressive (AR) systems, which suffer from linear latency scaling with both sequence length and the number of target languages. We explore how diffusion can enable multilingual translation with a discrete diffusion framework that refines all target languages in parallel, achieving sublinear latency scaling with the number of targets, and supports deployment as a single unified model to replace multiple independent systems. Conditioned on a continuous semantic anchor rather than source tokens, our framework supports zero-shot transfer to unseen source languages without retraining, maintaining approximately $75\%$ of its supervised translation quality on zero-shot sources. We investigate the quality-latency frontier and find that with accelerated sampling, it achieves comparable supervised quality to AR baselines with a $2 \times$ speedup and $11.9\%$ better zero-shot BLEU. These results highlight the potential of joint multi-stream diffusion as a practical and flexible alternative for efficient one-to-many translation.

</details>


### [9] [How Humans and LLMs Read Gender into Gender-Neutral Physical Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16366)
*Yingjia Wan,Lin Lin,Elisa Kreiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了AI伦理中"用客观外貌描述替代性别标签即可实现性别中立沟通"的假设，构建GAPA数据集（316个外貌属性+14,706条人类评分），证实外貌描述本身承载系统化性别联想；评估16个大语言模型发现其与人类判断部分对齐但存在评分压缩、男性类别对齐较弱、对非二元类别过度拒绝等系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI公平性、无障碍与伦理研究广泛建议用"客观"外貌描述（如"短发""棱角分明的下颌线"）替代推断的身份标签（如"她""他的"），假设这种描述语言是性别中立的。然而这一假设从未被经验性检验过。如果外貌描述本身也携带系统性性别联想，那么该策略只是将性别化沟通转移到更隐蔽的层面，而非真正消除。

Method: 1. 构建GAPA数据集：从LLM生成（170）、人类书写（50）、小说抽取（96）三个来源收集316个外貌属性，由304名美国参与者通过7点Likert量表对每个属性与女性、男性、非二元三类性别的关联进行评分，共14,706条评分。2. 人类分析：定义"性别联想强度"和"性别区分度"两个指标，分析属性级别异质性，使用留一法（LOO）和ICC计算评分可靠性天花板。3. LLM评估：对16个跨模型家族、规模、训练版本的LLM进行零样本评估，使用与人类实验相同的提示，分析模型-人类Pearson相关、RMSE、评分分布、弃答行为。4. 代理预测器：基于OLMo2-7B微调线性回归头训练代理模型（r=0.764），并应用于LitBank语料库的社会语言学分析，考察作者性别和角色性别对外貌描述性别联想的影响。

Result: 人类评分结果显示：53%的属性在性别间存在显著差异，属性与女性和男性的关联比与非二元更一致、区分度更高；男性-女性联想呈负相关（r=-0.16），但小说来源属性呈正相关（r=0.27）。LLM评估显示：模型部分还原人类关联但存在三类系统性偏差——(1)评分分布向中间值压缩，规避极端判断；(2)对男性类别的对齐显著弱于女性和非二元类别；(3)指令调优和专有模型对非二元类别存在高度不对称的弃答行为。代理预测器在LitBank分析中揭示：描述男性角色的属性在男性作者笔下具有更弱的女性联想（β=-0.12, p=.03）。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个经验证据，表明看似"客观"的外貌描述在人类解读中保留着系统化的性别联想，挑战了"用描述替代标签即实现性别中立"的广泛假设。同时揭示了LLM与人类判断的系统性偏差模式：评分压缩、男性对齐较弱、对非二元类别的过度拒绝（尤其在指令调优和专有模型中），这对AI辅助技术中的公平性提出了重要警示。研究呼吁在人与AI交互中重新审视外貌描述的"中立性"，并释放数据集和代理模型以支持相关审计与改进。

Abstract: When foundation models describe people, recent work in AI fairness, accessibility, and ethics recommends avoiding inferred identity labels (e.g., "she", "his") in favor of seemingly "objective" physical descriptions (e.g., "short hair", "a defined jawline"). Yet whether such descriptive language achieves gender-neutral communication remains an open empirical question. To study this, we introduce GAPA (Gender Associations of Physical Attributes), a dataset of 316 common physical attributes drawn from diverse sources, paired with 14,706 gender-association ratings from 304 US-based annotators. Results show that physical descriptions carry structured and graded gender associations among readers, with more consistent and distinctive associations for women and men than for non-binary identities. Next, we evaluate 16 LLMs across model families, sizes, and post-training variants against human ratings. The models partially recover human associations but exhibit systematic alignment biases, including compressed rating distributions, weaker alignment for associations with men, and asymmetric abstention that disproportionately targets the non-binary category. Finally, we release the best-performing proxy model trained to predict humans' gender associations of descriptive language and demonstrate its utility through a sociolinguistic analysis of character descriptions in LitBank. Together, our findings provide the first empirical evidence that seemingly "objective" physical descriptions can retain systematic gender associations in human interpretation, and uncover systematic patterns of model-human misalignment. This challenges the assumption that replacing explicit gender labels with physical descriptions necessarily yields gender-neutral communication, and highlights downstream challenges in using such descriptions to communicate subjective identity categories in human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [10] [Early-Bird Decoding: Accelerating Diffusion LLMs with Learnable Block Sizes and Parallel Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16450)
*Lixuan Wei,Wei Zhou,Jianwen Wu,Yipeng Shen,Meiling Wang,Haoran You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EB-Decode 框架，通过可学习的块大小（LBS）和位置感知的并行采样器（LPS）两个轻量级路由器，在不修改预训练 dLLM 权重的前提下加速扩散语言模型的推理。LBS 自适应地将具有相似不确定性的 token 分组为可变长度块，LPS 则在块内更早地提交已收敛的 token。在三个 dLLM 和四个基准测试上的实验表明，该方法相较于 vanilla 解码实现了 3.53–18.76× 的吞吐量提升，相较于最强基线 Fast-dLLM 最高可提升 1.58×，且准确率相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）虽然提供了并行解码的范式，但其推理效率仍受限于固定块大小和固定置信度阈值的解码策略。当前的开源 dLLMs（如 LLaDA、Dream）吞吐量远低于同等规模的自回归模型（例如 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 仅 3.5 tokens/s，而 LLaMA3-8B-Instruct 达 48.0 tokens/s）。固定块大小假设了序列中 token 难度均匀，但自然语言具有异质性；同时，许多 token 在达到置信度阈值前已正确预测，但仍被反复重掩码，造成了计算浪费。

Method: EB-Decode 框架包含两个关键组件：（1）可学习的块大小路由器（LBS）：一个轻量级的两层 Transformer 编码器（约 0.6M 参数），以归一化熵和 top-1 token ID 作为输入，预测每个被掩码位置应被纳入当前活跃块的概率；通过 EOS 移除、最大间隔约束（g_max）和最小 token 数约束（L_min）三个滤波器进行精炼，支持非连续的可变长度块。（2）位置感知的可学习并行采样路由器（LPS）：一个约 0.03M 参数的小型 Transformer 编码器，基于置信度、归一化熵、top-1/top-2 差距、块级掩码比例和块内位置嵌入进行决策，使用自监督的 generate-then-replay 方式训练，能在达到置信度阈值之前提交已收敛的 token。两个路由器均保持基础 dLLM 冻结，可即插即用。

Result: 在三个 dLLM（LLaDA-8B-Instruct、Dream-v0-Instruct-7B、LLaDA-1.5）和四个基准测试（HumanEval、MBPP、GSM8K-CoT、MATH500-CoT）上的实验表明：EB-Decode 相较于 vanilla 解码实现了 3.53–18.76× 的吞吐量提升，相较于最强基线 Fast-dLLM 最高提升 1.58×，同时准确率相当（±1%）；仅引入约 6.21% 的路由开销；与 KV-cache 流水线结合时可再获得 1.20× 加速；路由器具有良好的跨任务、跨模型和跨生成长度的迁移能力；平均提交时间从 vanilla 的 13.4 步降至 0.7 步。

Conclusion: EB-Decode 提出了一种原则性的早期解码框架，通过两个轻量级可学习路由器（LBS 和 LPS）替代 dLLM 解码中的两个静态设计选择（固定块大小和固定置信度阈值），在保持基础模型冻结的前提下显著提升了推理效率。实验结果验证了该框架在多个模型和基准测试上的有效性及泛化能力，为缩小 dLLM 与自回归模型之间的效率差距提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a promising parallel decoding paradigm as an alternative to autoregressive generation through iterative unmasking. However, dLLMs typically require many steps before token confidence reaches the decoding threshold, resulting in inefficient inference even with block-wise KV caching. To accelerate dLLM inference, we for the first time propose an "early-bird (EB)" decoding framework, motivated by the observation that tokens with similarly low entropy tend to cluster and can be jointly decoded earlier, before reaching the confidence threshold. In particular, our EB-Decode framework integrates two key enablers: (1) a learnable network that adaptively groups tokens with similar uncertainty into variable-length blocks, rather than relying on fixed block sizes; (2) a position-aware sampler that learns to unmask tokens in parallel using fewer decoding steps within predicted variable-length blocks. Both components are developed without modifying pretrained dLLM weights and can therefore be directly deployed as plug-ins during serving, with negligible training and inference overhead. Extensive experiments across three models and four benchmarks consistently validate our observation and the effectiveness of EB-Decode, achieving 3.53-18.76$\times$ higher throughput than the vanilla decoding method and up to 1.58$\times$ higher throughput over the strongest baseline, Fast-dLLM, with comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond the Name: Demographic Leakage in De-Identified Résumés and Evaluation Artifacts in LLM Bias Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16501)
*Qiangju Chen,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量法，揭示了去标识化简历中语言字段以外的散文残迹仍可被LLM推断出民族背景，且LLM-as-a-judge的成对评估结果严重受制于评估协议设计，从而区分了简历内容中的可恢复人口统计信号与评估方法引入的伪偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计将去标识化简历中残留的民族背景推断主要归因于声明语言字段，未隔离非语言残迹；同时广泛使用的LLM成对强制选择评估协议可能引入位置偏好、内容完成偏好等伪偏差，而非反映真实歧视。

Method: 构建620份反事实简历（20份基础简历 × 5个民族条件 × 3个线索显著性层级 × 2文本块 + 1无标记对照），严格保持语言字段一致；采用9个开源权重模型（8B–36B），通过三项任务评估：个体民族恢复率、成对偏好审计（含禁止/中立/鼓励平局三种裁决空间）、下游筛选决策（17,420条JSON响应解析率100%，并完成41,717次成对比较）。

Result: 整体目标群体恢复率0.757，高显著性下饱和至1.000；仅在低显著性下模型间出现显著差异（0.086–0.690）。成对评估中，允许平局时7/9模型≥94%平局；禁止平局时产生0.39的虚假选择率比，由位置效应和内容完成偏好驱动，对盎格鲁比较组表现最差。下游筛选分数差异极小（<0.13分/100），无法与评估噪声分离。

Conclusion: 字段级去标识化无法消除民族恢复能力，非语言散文残迹结构性地承载着推断信号；成对LLM评估结果主要由裁决空间决定而非简历内容。LLM偏见审计必须明确区分从简历内容恢复的人口统计信号与评估设计引入的伪效应。

Abstract: De-identified résumé screening assumes that redacting explicit fields prevents ethnocultural inference; however, recent audits attribute residual leakage to declared languages. We investigate whether eliminating language fields resolves this leakage across nine open-weight models and 620 counterfactual résumés. By holding language attributes strictly identical, we isolate unstructured prose across five ethnocultural conditions and three cue-salience tiers. Target-group recovery averages 0.757 overall and saturates at 1.000 under high salience, demonstrating that non-language prose sustains demographic inference. Crucially, models diverge only under faint cues (0.086-0.690), establishing salience as an essential evaluation axis. Furthermore, pairwise LLM-as-a-judge outcomes are highly sensitive to evaluation design: forbidding ties yields an apparent selection-rate ratio of 0.39 alongside strong position and content effects, whereas permitting ties produces near-universal ties for most models ($\ge94\%$). Downstream scoring shows only very small between-condition differences, highlighting the need to distinguish demographic signals recoverable from résumé content from effects introduced by the evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [12] [Competence-Preserving Resume Perturbations Expose Presentation Sensitivity in LLM Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16517)
*Qiangju Chen,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对大语言模型（LLM）在简历筛选任务中对"表层呈现形式变化"的敏感性进行受控审计。通过 O*NET 职业库构建 102 个候选人档案（17 个职业），并为每人生成 4 种保留能力证据的简历扰动（共 510 个变体，505 通过事实保真校验）。在 6 个开源指令调优 LLM 上的实验发现：效度（validity）与呈现不变性（presentation invariance）存在显著分离——例如 Llama-3.1-8B（chat 模板）效度最高（0.781），却在能力不变的呈现变化下翻转 29.6% 的配对决策；Mistral-7B-v0.3 效度 0.644，翻转率高达 41.4%。Native chat 模板能提升效度但无法消除决策不稳定。研究主张简历筛选评估应同时考察效度与呈现稳定性两个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际招聘场景中，同一候选人的能力证据可能因措辞、排版、模板、AI 润色、文档提取质量等因素呈现不同表层形式。理想的筛选系统应在此类表层变化下保持决策稳定（即"呈现不变性"）。然而现有 LLM 招聘研究多聚焦效度与公平性，对"能力不变前提下呈现变化导致决策翻转"这一稳健性问题缺乏受控审计。论文填补这一空白。

Method: 论文构建三阶段基准框架：(1) 基于 O*NET 30.3 数据库的 17 个职业，每个职业生成 6 个合成档案（合格/边缘/不合格各 2 个），共 102 份，依据技能、知识、任务、年限等维度定义受控能力差异；(2) 对每份档案渲染 5 种简历形式（原版 bullet-style + Verbosity/Structure/AI polish/Layout 四类扰动），共 510 变体；(3) 确定性验证门：仅当必备事实字段保留、未引入未授权技能、未泄露层级标签时方纳入评分。最终 505 通过、255 基线配对扩展为 1,250 配对行（不变性审计用 1,000）。评估指标含已知优劣效度、配对翻转率、Equal-tier tie rate、跨轴 Kendall τ。评分协议为独立打分（每份简历单独评分后再形成配对决策），以避免成对提示位置偏差。实验对象涵盖 BM25/TF–IDF 基线及 6 个开源 LLM（Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B、Llama-3.2-3B、Phi-3.5-mini、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Gemma-2-2B），含 native chat 模板消融。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 效度最高的 Llama-3.1-8B（chat, 0.781, 95% CI [0.746, 0.814]）配对翻转率达 29.6%；Mistral-7B-v0.3（raw, 0.644, CI [0.593, 0.691]）翻转率达 41.4%。所有直接 LLM 的翻转率介于 28.5%–45.6% 之间。(2) 词法基线（BM25 翻转率 4.0%、TF–IDF 5.2%）低翻转率源于基准刻意保留 rubric 短语，是稳定性参考而非部署级筛选方法；确定性结构控制效度 1.0/翻转 0.0 仅为基准健全性检查。(3) Chat 模板消融显著提升效度（Llama-3.1: 0.622→0.781；Llama-3.2: 0.321→0.551；Gemma: 0.287→0.576），但翻转率仍高（0.296/0.285/0.456），说明提示格式失配仅部分解释效度下降，不能解释呈现不稳定。(4) Qwen2.5-7B 轴级诊断显示 Layout（47.2%）、AI polish（40.4%）、Structure（38.0%）、Verbosity（25.2%）。总体表明效度与不变性是可分离的、互补的两个评估维度。

Conclusion: 论文结论：(1) 简历筛选系统的效度与呈现不变性可大幅分离，仅评估效度不足以刻画可靠性；(2) Native chat 模板能部分修正效度但无法消除呈现敏感性；(3) 提议简历筛选评估应同时纳入"已知能力差异效度"与"能力保留型呈现变化下的决策稳定性"两项指标，理想系统应同时具备高有效度与高不变性；(4) 局限性：仅覆盖 17 个职业与 102 份合成档案，职业簇自助置信区间属探索性小样本证据，未涵盖专有模型与端到端 ATS 系统，未来工作应扩展职业范围、人写简历与生产简历处理流水线。

Abstract: Resume screeners must infer job-relevant competence from resumes whose presentation can vary substantially in wording, structure, stylistic polish, and document extraction quality. Ideally, such surface variation should not change decisions when the underlying qualification evidence is unchanged. We introduce a controlled audit of this property, constructing occupation-grounded candidate profiles at controlled competence levels and rendering each profile into multiple resume presentations. A deterministic validation gate excludes variants that alter the underlying evidence before scoring. Across six open instruction-tuned LLM conditions, we find a clear disconnect between screening validity and presentation stability. Llama-3.1-8B with its native chat template achieves the strongest validity ($0.781$) yet reverses $29.6\%$ of matched pairwise decisions under competence-preserving presentation changes; Mistral-7B-v0.3 reaches validity $0.644$ with a $41.4\%$ flip rate. Native chat formatting improves validity for several chat-tuned models but does not remove this instability. These results show that resume-screening evaluations should assess not only whether a system identifies stronger candidates, but also whether those decisions remain stable when the same competence evidence is presented differently.

</details>


### [13] [RoleBreak: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Role-Playing Robustness in Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16614)
*Yuqi Wang,Fengyuan Liu,Haochen Luo,Zhiqi Yu,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RoleBreak 是一个面向语音对话的长时角色扮演鲁棒性基准，包含 310 个角色、6,688 轮人工验证对话及 11,743 条细粒度评估标准。实验发现当前系统在语义角色一致性上远强于语音情感表达，且最强系统在仅 10.4 轮即出现首次人格失败；扩大 LLM 规模可提升语义鲁棒性，但对语音情感帮助有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语角色扮演基准多以虚构角色为中心且对话轮次较短，无法检验语音对话模型在长时、多样（尤其是用户中心型）角色下能否持续保持身份与行为一致性，以及在累积上下文与针对性压力下的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建 310 个角色（既有角色+用户中心角色），为每个角色设计含上下文依赖探针与压力干预的多轮场景；LLM 起草用户轮次、评分标准与目标情感，人工修订审核；用 LibriTTS-R/VCTK 真人语音+CosyVoice 零样本 TTS 合成中性及三种情感变体的语音输入。评估九种配置（全双工/全模态/级联 ASR-LLM-TTS），采用 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 作为评判器（与人工一致率 94.55%），并以 UTMOSv2 评估自然度、emotion2vec+ 评估语音情感，报告 P-FFT 与 S-FFT 指标。

Result: 1）系统语义角色依从显著强于语音情感（情感得分均<14.7）；2）即使最强级联系统也在平均 10.4 轮出现首次人格失败、11.6 轮出现首次安全失败，远早于 21.57 轮的平均对话长度；3）Qwen3.5 从 2B 扩至 27B 显著提升交互/人格/安全及首失败轮次，但语音情感几乎不变；4）用户语音情感在语言内容固定时仍影响 PersonaPlex 的行为（人格±5.6、安全±4.5）。

Conclusion: 当前语音角色扮演系统在长时鲁棒性与语音表现力上存在持续短板：扩大语言模型可缓解语义层面的退化，但无法解决语音情感表达问题；用户侧副语言线索会改变角色行为，因此口语角色扮演需同时评估语义一致性与声学上下文。RoleBreak 为开发在长对话中保持连贯、表现力丰富且安全的语音智能体提供了测试平台。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech dialogue models increasingly support persona control, yet existing spoken role-playing benchmarks remain largely character-centric and short-horizon. This leaves open whether spoken dialogue models can sustain diverse roles over extended interactions, especially beyond predefined fictional characters. We introduce RoleBreak, an open benchmark for long-horizon role-playing robustness in spoken dialogue. RoleBreak contains 310 character-based and user-centered roles, 6,688 human-verified dialogue turns, and 11,743 fine-grained evaluation criteria, with 1,856 turns carrying expressive emotion targets for evaluating vocal emotion. Its scenarios are designed to stress role consistency, interaction quality, safety, and affect over extended conversations. We evaluate nine configurations spanning full-duplex, omni-modal, and cascaded ASR--LLM--TTS paradigms. We find four key patterns. First, current systems are substantially stronger at semantic role adherence than at vocal emotion. Second, semantic robustness remains brittle over long interactions: even the strongest evaluated system encounters its first persona and safety failures after only 10.4 and 11.6 turns on average. Third, scaling the LLM substantially improves semantic robustness and delays failure, but yields little improvement in vocal emotion. Finally, user vocal emotion affects role-playing behavior even when linguistic content is fixed. These findings highlight persistent gaps in both long-horizon robustness and vocal expressiveness in spoken role-playing systems.

</details>


### [14] [The Role of Implicit and Explicit Demographic Signals in Large Language Model-based Student Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16993)
*Donya Rooein,Luca Benedetto,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了显式与隐式人口统计学信号对大语言模型（LLM）在三种教育评估任务中行为的影响，揭示了LLM在教育场景中存在显著的"人口统计学敏感性"（demographic sensitivity），尤其在开放式任务中容易产生不可预测的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地用于学生评估（评分、反馈、问答），学生的人口统计学背景可能影响模型输出。一方面，考虑人口统计学信息可能有助于个性化（如根据教育水平调整可读性）；另一方面，也可能导致歧视（如给低社会经济背景学生打低分）。然而，先前研究主要关注性别等有限属性，对真实用户画像和隐式信号的考察不足。

Method: 采用反事实受控实验设计：(1) 构建三种提示条件——默认（无人口统计学信息）、显式（直接提供AI Gap数据集中的25项人口统计学属性）、隐式（通过用户历史对话记录间接传递人口统计学信号）；(2) 在三个任务上测试6个LLM——自动作文评分（AES，使用Persuade 2.0）、形成性反馈（FF）、元语言问答（QA，使用ELQA数据集）；(3) 每个任务40个样本，搭配200个用户画像，共产生192,480次推理调用；(4) 评估指标涵盖可读性（ARI、FRE）、AoA、响应长度、BERTScore F1、情感等；(5) 统计检验采用L1正则化线性回归（Lasso）和双样本KS检验（Bonferroni校正）。

Result: 核心发现包括：(1) AES任务中，显式条件下多数模型保持相对稳定，但隐式条件下Llama-70B出现大幅评分波动（+1.57分）；(2) QA和FF等开放式任务中，人口统计学效应更显著且不可预测——例如低教育水平用户的回答获得更低情感分数；(3) 显式条件下，模型会根据教育水平调整可读性（高教育水平获得更难文本），但隐式条件下不存在这种适应；(4) Llama-70B对隐式信号最敏感，其反馈情感受多种人口统计学属性影响；(5) LLM默认值呈现英语/美国中心倾向；(6) 隐式条件下，模型更多复制历史对话的表面特征（如更长的提示带来更长的响应）。研究证明当前LLM无法可靠地区分任务相关信息与人口统计学线索。

Conclusion: 论文结论指出：(1) 人口统计学效应具有强任务依赖性——在高风险评分场景中应保持人口统计学不变性，但在教学反馈中适应性变化可能是合理的；(2) 然而当前LLM不能可靠实现这一区分，存在公平性、透明性和加剧教育不平等的风险；(3) 随着LLM在教育中的深入集成，需要确保其行为反映教学目标而非无意识的人口统计学偏见。研究呼吁在教育部署LLM时需谨慎处理人口统计学信号。

Abstract: Large Language Models are now common in student assessment, but we know little about how student demographics affect their use. Sometimes, considering student demographics may be necessary -- for example, to improve readability for users with lower educational levels. However, it also risks being a cause of discrimination, e.g., when assigning lower scores to students from lower socioeconomic backgrounds. We set up controlled prompts to test 1) explicit demographic effects, where we mention demographic details directly, and 2) implicit effects, where we use conversation history as a demographic signal. We test these settings in three tasks: Automated Essay Scoring, Formative Feedback, and Metalinguistic Question Answering. We test six state-of-the-art LLMs on these tasks. In both explicit and implicit cases, the models pick up on demographic cues and can change their scoring, feedback, and answers accordingly. We find that LLMs frequently adjust the readability of feedback to education levels when these are explicitly mentioned. On the other hand, implicit conditions produce unpredictable biases, such as in question answering, where responses from lower-education levels receive lower sentiment scores. Our results provide clear evidence of demographic sensitivity in LLMs for educational assessment tasks.

</details>


### [15] [Diagnosing the Fact-Grounding Gap in Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17043)
*Kevin Mo,Nathan Mo,Richard Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多跳问答系统中的检索-推理失败问题，提出"事实基础差距"(fact-grounding gap)概念，指出系统失败并非全部源于检索错误，相当比例源于已检索段落中关键事实缺失，且该问题无法通过纯检索手段解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答研究普遍假设检索质量是主要瓶颈，但单个推理步骤层面的失败模式尚未被系统研究。如果有大量失败源于已检索文档中事实缺失而非文档未检索到，则大量检索改进工作仅能解决部分问题。

Method: 1. 在MuSiQue/HotpotQA/2WikiMultihopQA上使用Self-Ask管道+GPT-4.1-mini进行逐跳分析；2. 用LLM judge(经人类标注验证，κ=0.840)判定每跳检索段落是否含有所需事实；3. 将失败分为检索失败与提取失败两类；4. 训练DeBERTa-v3-large轻量分类器预测事实存在性；5. 通过增强(augmentation)和重排序(reranking)干预实验验证失败模式的可分离性。

Result: 1. MuSiQue上27.3%的跳次为提取失败(占所有缺陷的47%)，标准检索指标无法检测；2. 重排序可大幅改善检索失败(+25.3%)，但对提取失败几乎无效(-0.5%)；3. 97.7%的提取失败中，整个语料库都缺失所需关系事实；4. DeBERTa选择性重检索在仅一半成本下达到与全跳重检索相当的精度；5. 跨数据集(BM25与Contriever)和跨数据集迁移均验证了该模式的普遍性。

Conclusion: 检索失败与提取失败是本质不同的瓶颈，需要不同解决方案。事实基础差距在所有测试数据集中均存在，但严重程度因基准和题型而异。这为多跳QA评估实践确立了纯检索方法的天花板。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires combining information from multiple documents to answer complex questions. These systems have grown increasingly capable, yet when they fail, the error is typically attributed to not finding the right documents. Whether this holds at the level of individual reasoning steps remains largely unexamined. We investigate this across three standard multi-hop QA benchmarks and find that failures decompose into two distinct modes: retrieval failures, where the needed passage was not retrieved, and extraction failures, where the passage was retrieved but the needed fact could not be extracted - a phenomenon we term the fact-grounding gap. Extraction failures account for nearly half of all per-hop deficiencies and are invisible to standard retrieval metrics. They remain unresolved by every retrieval intervention we test, establishing a ceiling for retrieval-only improvements. The gap's severity varies across benchmarks and question types, but extraction failures appear on every dataset we measure. Our findings reveal that retrieval failures and extraction failures are fundamentally different bottlenecks requiring different solutions - a distinction absent from current evaluation practice.

</details>


### [16] [Vroom-Vroom at SHROOM-Visions: A Multi-Judge Committee for Detecting Hallucinated Spans in Vision-Language Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17327)
*Toqeer Ehsan,Nico Penttilä,Richard Schmidt,Arash Hajikhani,Victoria Palacin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模型委员会方法，用于在四种语言（英、法、意、中）的视觉语言模型（VLM）输出中检测和分类字符级幻觉跨度。该方法将多个微调VLM作为独立判断者，通过字符级多数投票合并预测，并辅以激活探针。在SHROOM-Visions共享任务中，该方法在四种语言中的三种上排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在图像问答、场景描述等任务中广泛应用，但其输出常包含流畅且自信但缺乏事实依据的幻觉内容。现有方法在字符级精确检测幻觉方面存在挑战，且人工标注者之间本身存在分歧，限制了检测性能的上限。因此需要一种能够跨语言、字符级别精确识别幻觉的方法作为安全防护机制。

Method: 方法包含两个并行路径：(1) VLM法官委员会——使用Gemma-4、Mistral-Small、Qwen3-VL等五种不同家族的VLM作为独立判断者，采用LoRA微调或少样本方式，将注释转换为内联XML标记格式（&lt;hall label="LABEL" prob="PROB"&gt;），通过加权余弦相似度检索上下文示例，通过字符级投票（votes≥2）和置信度平均聚合预测；(2) 内部状态探针——在Qwen3-VL-4B/8B上训练多层、多阶段探针，利用所有层的隐藏状态、残差流差异和softmax加权组合，结合边界、样本级和token级辅助头部进行幻觉分类。

Result: 在SHROOM-Visions测试集上，votes≥2的委员会配置在法语、意大利语和中文的相关性指标上排名第一，在英语上排名第二，每种语言的每个指标均登上领奖台。委员会在所有语言上超越任何单个法官，OCR错误最易检测，发明和错误表征错误次之，Other类别性能最差。法官投票数与人工标注者数量呈正相关（Spearman相关系数0.39），表明模型多样性反映了标注分歧。探针在IoU上可与单个法官匹敌，但在相关性指标上逊于委员会，且错误高度相关。

Conclusion: 多模型字符级投票委员会是有效的多语言VLM幻觉跨度检测方法，仅保留被多个法官共同标记的跨度，平均置信度提供细粒度概率。激活探针虽能从隐藏状态检测幻觉，但其性能高度依赖底层模型，且尚不清楚探针是提供机制性洞见还是仅利用表面语言特征。委员会继承了各法官的局限性，长而流畅的幻觉可能在两票阈值下被遗漏。

Abstract: This paper describes our submission to the SHROOM-Visions shared task on detecting and classifying hallucinated character spans in vision-language model outputs across four languages. We employ several fine-tuned vision-language models as independent annotators and combine their span predictions through character-level majority voting, and additionally explore activation probes. The approach ranks first in three of four languages and places on the podium in every language and metric. Our analysis indicates that disagreement among diverse models tracks disagreement among human annotators.

</details>


### [17] [Where Should a Document Live: Context, Representations, or Parameters?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17346)
*Nathanaël Carraz Rakotonirina,Momchil Hardalov,Gonzalo Iglesias,Adrià de Gispert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统对比了大语言模型注入新知识的三种方式——上下文（ICL/RAG）、KV缓存表示（Cartridges、Compaction）和模型参数（LoRA、MLP适配器、全参数微调），发现没有"万能方法"，不同方法在准确性、压缩率、多文档组合能力和灾难性遗忘方面各有取舍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需要获取预训练之外的新知识（如企业知识库、病历、法律语料）。现有知识注入方法（上下文、表示、参数）虽被广泛使用，但缺乏在统一、受控条件下的全面对比。先前研究多基于预训练可能见过的维基百科数据（测试回忆而非真正编码新知识），且很少覆盖多文档检索场景，也未量化灾难性遗忘。

Method: 在Qwen3-8B（主）和Gemma-3-12B（验证）上，使用相同的Self-Study合成数据（GPT-OSS 120B生成问题，目标模型自身作为教师）和KL蒸馏目标，对比7种方法（No context、ICL、Cartridges、Compaction、LoRA、MLP适配器、全参数微调）。在5个知识密集型基准（LongHealth、QASPER、QuALITY、FinQA/T²-RAGBench、TechQA）的单文档（oracle）设置和多文档检索设置（k=1,3,5,10）下评估。参数方法用均值合并适配器，表示方法用KV缓存拼接。同时在4个控制基准（GSM8K、HumanEval、IFEval、MMLU）上量化灾难性遗忘。

Result: 单文档oracle：Compaction 73.4平均分（匹配ICL上界73.6），Cartridges 70.6，两者均优于参数方法（LoRA 64.5、MLP 62.2、全微调59.8）。高压缩率下Compaction陡降，Cartridges几乎平稳。各存储预算下Cartridges通常领先10分。多文档：Cartridges是唯一随k增大保持/提升性能的方法（与ICL相当），领先参数方法29分、Compaction 15分。Compaction和参数方法随k增大单调退化。多文档组合对参数方法尤其困难。遗忘：Cartridges平均下降6%（HumanEval下降16分）；大MLP适配器也遗忘严重（GSM8K从~92降至~60）；LoRA、Compaction、全微调（除人类外）能保留能力。成本：参数方法推理最便宜（与文档长度无关），10×压缩的Cartridges预填FLOPs减少~100×。

Conclusion: 没有单一最优方法。Cartridges在几乎所有存储预算下最准确且能扩展到多文档，但高压缩时有遗忘。Compaction低压缩时匹配ICL但不耐高压缩，也不能组合。参数方法在匹配存储下落后于Cartridges且无法组合，但尺寸固定、推理便宜。独立训练的每文档工件组合仍是开放问题，压缩缓存复用是表示族的有希望方向。建议用低秩约束作为保留通用能力的正则化手段。

Abstract: To answer questions outside of their pre-training data, large language models (LLMs) need access to new information, which can be presented in the context window as documents, encoded into the model's parameters, or injected as latent representations. However, each of these methods comes with different efficiency, cost, and performance trade-offs, with no single winner. We present a controlled comparison of representation-based (KV-cache based) and parametric (fine-tuning-based) adaptation methods on five knowledge-intensive benchmarks. We show that in the oracle setting, Cartridges (KV) are the most accurate injection method at nearly every storage budget, outperforming parametric methods by 10 points. Compaction (KV) matches Cartridges only at low compression rates, lagging behind the parametric methods by 10 points at rates higher than $50\times$. In the more realistic multi-document retrieval scenario, Cartridges are the only method that matches in-context learning (ICL), leading the parametric methods by 29 points and Compaction by 15 points. Nonetheless, Cartridges are also the only method, besides full fine-tuning and large MLP adapters, that suffers from catastrophic forgetting, i.e., a 6% performance degradation on control benchmarks, with 13% in coding.

</details>


### [18] [When Should LLMs Abstain? Chain-of-Self-Questioning for Selective Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17516)
*Ali Şenol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Self-Questioning (CoSQ)，一种仅依赖提示词的框架，通过让LLM在承诺答案前先显式评估回答所需信息及其置信度，从而实现可调节的"回答或弃答"决策。在TruthfulQA-MC（817题，11个模型）和NQ-Short（300题，5个模型）上的实验表明，该方法能同时降低错误承诺率和提升已答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在事实性知识支持不足时仍会生成流畅但错误的回答（即"幻觉"）。在高风险场景（如医疗、法律、金融）中，一个显式的弃答机制比一个自信但不正确的回答更有价值，因为它能触发人工审核或转诊。现有方法（CoT、检索增强、后验验证）要么不提供回答/弃答决策，要么需要额外基础设施。该研究旨在通过纯提示词层面的自评估，让模型在承诺答案前判断自身信息支撑是否足够。

Method: CoSQ是一个三阶段提示词框架：(1) 模型识别回答问题所需的信息单元；(2) 为每个信息单元分配置信度分数；(3) 根据置信度门控决定是回答还是弃答。提出了三个变体：Grounded-CoSQ（对所有信息单元取平均置信度作为门控）、Critical-CoSQ（仅对"关键"信息单元进行门控）、Adaptive-CoSQ（结合角色感知、三重检查和矛盾检测）。在TruthfulQA-MC上使用平衡选项顺序的强制选择协议，并在11个模型上以5个阈值（τ=0.50至0.90）进行17种条件评估。

Result: 在TruthfulQA-MC（11个模型平均）上，Grounded-CoSQ（τ=0.90）将无条件错误承诺率从CoT的13.1%降至8.9%（相对降低32.1%），同时将已答准确率从86.9%提升至89.7%，覆盖率为87.6%。该改善在全部11个模型和所有评估阈值上一致成立（Wilcoxon p=0.00049）。Critical-CoSQ覆盖率最高（88.6%），Adaptive-CoSQ错误承诺率最低（8.9%，覆盖率86.5%）。在NQ-Short上观察到收敛性证据，Grounded-CoSQ将HR从41.2%降至27.4%，AA从58.8%提升至67.0%。

Conclusion: CoSQ证明LLM的自评估信号可以作为显式且可调节的"回答或弃答"决策机制。核心教训是可靠的LLM评估应同时回答两个问题：模型给出的答案有多准确，以及它多频繁地决定给出答案。Grounded-CoSQ（τ=0.90）在已答准确率、覆盖率和错误承诺风险之间提供了最强平衡；Critical-CoSQ适合需要更广泛覆盖的场景；Adaptive-CoSQ提供最低的错误承诺率。该框架为减少高风险场景中无支撑承诺提供了轻量级机制。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent answers when their factual support is weak. This paper introduces Chain-of-Self-Questioning (CoSQ), a prompt-only framework that makes answer commitment conditional on an explicit assessment of the information required to answer a question. We evaluate three CoSQ variants under seventeen conditions on the 817-item TruthfulQA multiple-choice validation set using eleven open-weight and hosted model families. In the final balanced-option protocol, Grounded-CoSQ at {\tau}=0.90 reduces the mean unconditional wrong-commitment rate from 13.1% under chain-of-thought prompting to 8.9%, a 32.1% relative reduction, while increasing answered accuracy from 86.9% to 89.7% and answering 87.6% of questions. Both improvements hold for all eleven models and at every evaluated threshold. Critical-CoSQ and Adaptive-CoSQ provide neighboring operating points with 88.6% and 86.5% coverage, respectively, while remaining more reliable than the baseline. A secondary Natural Questions Short-Answer evaluation provides convergent open-form evidence. These findings show that self-assessment can support explicit, tunable answer-or-abstain decisions when an unsupported commitment is more costly than referral or review.

</details>


### [19] [Few-Shot Degradation Is Not What It Seems: Behavioral Evidence, Representation Analysis, and a Random-Text Control Across 12 Models, 2 Tasks, and 2 Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15990)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统性地挑战了少样本学习中关于"表征失真"导致性能下降的流行假设。研究表明，少样本提示与零样本提示之间隐藏状态的表观偏移主要是由提示长度差异驱动，而非由示例内容引起。通过引入"随机文本控制"（random-text control）来分离长度效应与内容效应，作者提出content delta这一指标，证明真正由示例内容引起的表征变化与少样本带来的性能提升呈正相关，而非负相关——这与直觉相反。在Llama 3.3 70B上的因果消融实验（掩码示例token的注意力）证实了示例内容确实对该模型造成损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本提示（few-shot prompting）有时会损害语言模型性能。直观解释——"失真假设"（distortion hypothesis）——认为演示示例将模型的内部表征"扭曲"远离正确答案，因此表征偏移越大，损害越严重。然而，这一假设未考虑一个关键混淆因素：少样本提示比零样本提示长5-8倍，长度本身就会改变表征。该研究旨在检验这一假设，并提出一个能区分长度效应与内容效应的方法学。

Method: 1. **模型与任务**：评估12个开源模型（9个家族，包括11个Transformer和1个SSM/Falcon Mamba 7B），覆盖两种乌克兰语任务：SIB-200新闻分类（204样本，7类）和ua-case-outcome法律案件结果预测（196样本，7类）。
2. **行为评估**：比较零样本与少样本准确率，分析任务依赖性。
3. **内部表征度量**：
   - Representation shift（表征偏移）：零样本与少样本隐藏状态的余弦距离。
   - Random-text control（随机文本控制）：将示例替换为长度匹配的随机token，测量由此产生的偏移，以隔离长度效应。
   - Content delta（内容增量）：上述两者之差，即由示例内容引起的表征变化。
   - DAR（演示注意力比率）：最后4层对示例token的平均注意力比例。
4. **因果验证**：对Llama 3.3 70B，将最终token对所有示例token的注意力权重清零，验证示例内容是否因果性地损害性能。
5. **统计相关分析**：计算各内部指标与少样本性能增益Δ_FS之间的Spearman/Pearson相关。

Result: **行为结果**：少样本效应强烈依赖任务——新闻分类平均+23.9 pp，法律文本仅+3.4 pp。10个有效模型中，1个在新闻任务上退化，2个在法律任务上退化（Qwen3 14B：-0.5 pp；Llama 3.3 70B：-1.0 pp）。Llama 3.3 70B是唯一在两个任务上都退化的模型。

**内部表征结果**：
- 原始表征偏移与少样本增益**不相关**（r = +0.20, p = 0.59）。
- 长度驱动的偏移占总偏移的14%（Gemma 3 12B）到79%（Llama 3.3 70B），部分模型高达86%（Granite 8B）。
- Content delta与SIB-200上的少样本增益**显著正相关**（ρ = +0.65, p = 0.043, n = 10）；在法律任务上趋势一致但边缘显著（ρ = +0.59, p = 0.074）——**符号与失真假设预测的相反**。
- DAR与Δ_FS几乎零相关（r = +0.02, p = 0.96），说明注意力分配比例本身不预测少样本效果。
- 退化最严重的Llama 3.3 70B拥有所有Transformer中最低的content delta（0.067）。

**因果验证**：对Llama 3.3 70B掩码示例注意力后，准确率从55.9%回升到59.3%，超过零样本基线（58.3%），证明示例内容确实因果性地损害了该模型。

**架构观察**：Falcon Mamba 7B（SSM）的总偏移仅为Transformer平均的1/10（0.035 vs 0.299），但因输出格式无效无法比较行为。

**Qwen3家族同族扩展**：SIB-200上少样本增益随规模单调递减（8B→14B→32B：+37.3→+35.0→+14.7 pp）；content delta也单调递减（0.219→0.168→0.139）。

Conclusion: 该论文得出三个核心结论：
1. **原始表征偏移不能预测少样本增益或损害**（r = 0.20）。退化模型中的表观"失真"主要是提示长度伪影：40-79%的偏移源于更长的输入，而非示例内容。
2. **Content delta预测增益而非损害**（ρ = +0.65, p = 0.043）——由示例内容引起的表征重构越多，模型从少样本中获益越大。退化最严重的Llama 3.3 70B拥有最低的content delta。
3. **随机文本控制应成为方法学标准**：任何通过表征几何研究ICL的工作，若不控制提示长度，可能得出符号相反的结论。该控制仅需每次推理增加一次前向传播，开销极小。

**方法学意义**：该研究为ICL的表征几何分析提供了一个简单但关键的必要控制步骤，对未来使用hidden state几何诊断上下文学习机制的研究具有直接的规范意义。**局限**：相关分析样本量n=10；因果消融仅在单个量化模型上进行；随机文本控制仅控制长度，未控制熵、token分布和句法结构等其他非内容因素；评估限于闭集分类任务。

Abstract: Few-shot prompting sometimes degrades language models instead of helping them, but why this happens is unknown. We evaluate 12 open-weight models on two Ukrainian tasks news classification and legal case outcome prediction and find that the effect is strongly task-dependent: the same models that gain +24 pp on news show only +3.4 pp on legal text, with two models degrading. To understand why, we look inside the models. Prior work measures how much hidden states shift between zero-shot and few-shot modes, but few-shot prompts are much longer, and that length difference alone moves representations. We propose a simple fix: replace demonstrations with length-matched random text to measure the shift caused by prompt length, then subtract it. The resulting metric content delta isolates how much the model's representations change because of what the demonstrations say, not how long they are. This changes the picture entirely: raw shift does not predict whether few-shot helps or hurts (r = 0.20), but content delta does (rho = +0.65, p = 0.043). Models that restructure representations more from demonstration content benefit more the opposite of the intuitive "distortion" explanation. Masking demonstrations in Llama 3.3 70B confirms the finding causally, recovering accuracy above the zero-shot baseline.

</details>


### [20] [Single Document Extractive Summarization using Domination in Hypergraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15993)
*Aamir Miyajiwala,Aabha Pingle,Sheetal Sonawane,Surajit Kr. Nath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超图支配集（hypergraph domination）的抽取式单文档摘要方法，通过以关键词/命名实体为超边、句子为节点构建句子超图，并利用贪心算法求解支配集以生成摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图模型抽取式摘要方法将句子关系建模为简单图（pairwise），难以表达关键词同时覆盖多个句子的高阶语义关系。因此，作者希望利用超图（hypergraph）表达"一个关键词/主题同时连接多个句子"的高阶结构，借助超图支配集理论选出能够"支配"所有关键信息的句子，从而生成覆盖更全的摘要。

Method: 1. 构建句子超图：节点为文档中的句子，超边为关键词或命名实体（即出现在多个句子中的重要词项/实体），每条超边连接所有包含该关键词的句子。\n2. 在该超图上计算支配集（dominating set）：即选取最少的句子集合，使得图中每条超边都至少有一个端点（句子）被选中。\n3. 采用贪心算法（greedy algorithm）近似求解超图支配集问题，所选句子集合按原始顺序排列后输出，作为抽取式摘要。\n4. 与已有基于图（如 TextRank、LexRank 等）的抽取式摘要方法在公开数据集上进行对比实验，使用 ROUGE 指标评价。

Result: 在标准单文档摘要数据集上，该方法生成的摘要在 ROUGE-1、ROUGE-2 和 ROUGE-L 等指标上能够达到或接近现有基于图的抽取式摘要基线方法的水平，验证了超图支配集用于抽取式摘要的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 将超图支配集引入抽取式自动文摘是可行且有竞争力的方法，超图结构能够更自然地表达关键词与多句子之间的"一对多"语义关联，贪心算法可高效地选出信息覆盖度高的支配句子集合，从而生成高质量的抽取式摘要。

Abstract: Automatic Text Summarization (ATS) in Natural Language Processing has been an important task in Information Retrieval. It compresses a document to create a summary that captures all the relevant and important information conveyed in the document. This study explores Hypergraph for extractive text summarization of single documents. Objective: This study explores a novel method of leveraging the property of domination in hypergraphs to generate an extractive summary and compare its performance with state of the art graph based methods. Method: Our work aims to generate an extractive summary by creating a sentence hypergraph where each sentence represents a node and the edge is a keyword or a named entity that contains the sentences in which it occurs. We generate a hypergraph where each edge is a keyword or an important topic and the nodes are sentences containing those keywords. Then we apply a greedy algorithm to find the dominating set of the hypergraph which will contain sentences that will form the extractive summary.

</details>


### [21] [Latent Undertow: How Ordinary Typos Break Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15994)
*Elad David,Max Fomin,Amit LeVi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了普通输入错误（如拼写、标点错误）对LLM激活探针的严重破坏：单个token处激活向量旋转43°–56°，但在约10个下游token内衰减至15%以下。在Llama-3.1-8B上，每条消息堆叠约3处常见输入错误，会使单位置提示注入探针的TPR@FPR=1%下降12.0个百分点，且无法通过阈值重校准恢复。该机制在Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B和Gemma-4-E4B三族模型上均得到复现。多位置聚合（Attention、MultiMax）可抵御局部扰动（≤0.5pp损失），但在分布式扰动下仍下降约3.8pp。最佳防御是KV-cache分叉探针后缀：在用户消息后附加30个token的固定后缀，将探针读取位置移至扰动下游，从而关闭95%的性能差距（仅余-0.6pp），比扰动增强训练（-3.7pp）优一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于激活的线性探针已被广泛用于实时监控LLM行为（检测不安全意图、提示注入等），但生产环境中超过40%的真实用户输入包含拼写错误或语法噪声。已有研究表明针对有害关键词的对抗性扰动会使诊断探针精度下降，但普通、非对抗性的输入变化对激活探针的影响尚未被充分研究。本文旨在回答：当表面输入变化扰动激活而不改变模型对意图的解读时，激活探针实际读取到了什么？

Method: 1. **扰动分类法**：定义四类表面扰动（相邻键拼写错误、标点、大小写、遗漏空格）及堆叠输入束（平均每样本3.33处编辑）和分布式"每隔一词"扰动（~50%内容token被破坏）。2. **激活级表征**：在Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B、Gemma-4-E4B的多个层检查点上，用相邻键扰动测量扰动点的角度旋转（43°–56°）及下游token的空间衰减（d≈10时降至15%以下）。3. **探针实验**：在29个公开数据集（N=168,440）上训练线性探针进行二分类（恶意vs.良性），对比单位置读取与四种多位置架构（Mean Linear、MLP、Attention、MultiMax）在5折分层交叉验证和留一数据集出（LODO）协议下的脆弱性。4. **三种防御评估**：架构切换、扰动增强训练、KV-cache分叉探针后缀（用户消息后附加30 token固定中性后缀，利用空间衰减将读取点移至扰动下游）。

Result: 1. **激活机制**：表面扰动产生纯方向性的旋转（模长近似不变），扰动点43°–56°，d≈10时衰减至15%以下；该机制在三个模型族中复现。2. **探针脆弱性**：堆叠输入束使单位置线性探针TPR@FPR=1%从97.4%降至85.4%（-12.0pp），AUC仅降0.55pp，表明排名序基本保持但分数分布非对称偏移，阈值重校准无法恢复。3. **多架构对比**：局部扰动下，Attention/MultiMax/MLP损失≤0.5pp；但分布式扰动下MLP崩溃-16.8pp，Attention/MultiMax仍损失~3.8pp。4. **防御效果**：扰动增强训练恢复至93.84%（-3.7pp残余）；KV-cache分叉后缀恢复至98.65%（-0.6pp残余，关闭95%差距），且在LODO下将加权准确率从77.5%提升至81.5%，使单位置线性探针跻身架构顶层。计算开销：batch=1时<4ms，batch=32时71–135ms。5. **OOD观察**：LODO下最佳AUC仅0.918（Attention），加权准确率0.77–0.82，表明探针学到的是意图与数据集特定表面属性的混合而非纯意图。

Conclusion: 普通输入变化在不改变LLM对用户意图解读的情况下，使激活产生大幅局部旋转（43°–56°），揭示了单位置激活探针的固有脆弱性——其读取比模型本身对表面噪声更敏感。该旋转具有快速空间衰减结构（d≈10时衰减至15%以下），这一结构既解释了脆弱性，也指向修复路径。多位置聚合（特别是Attention和Max选择）可有效抵御局部扰动，但对分布式扰动仍有~3.8pp损失。KV-cache分叉探针后缀通过将读取点移至扰动下游，利用空间衰减机制关闭95%的性能差距，比扰动增强训练优一个数量级，且架构无关、用户透明。本文还指出分布式扰动可同时绕过探针与对齐机制，构成低成本的潜在攻击向量。研究方向包括：沿typo方向的因果干预、跨模态扩展、多防御机制的叠加组合。

Abstract: LLMs handle ordinary typing variation fluently: a typo or missing punctuation leaves both user intent and the model's response substantively unchanged. Yet probes that detect malicious prompts by reading the model's hidden states tell a different story: the same edit rotates the readout vector by 43--56 at the perturbed token, decaying below 15% within ~10 downstream tokens. Stacking ~3 common typos per message cuts a single-position prompt-injection probe's TPR@FPR$=1% by 12.0pp, a gap recalibration alone cannot close. Multi-position aggregation cures localized perturbations (<= 0.5 loss) but only attenuates distributed ones, where even attention- and max-based aggregators still drop ~3.8pp. For single-position probes, we introduce a KV-cache fork: a short fixed suffix appended after the user message lets the probe read a few tokens downstream of the perturbation, exploiting its rapid spatial decay. This closes 95% of the gap (-0.6pp residual) -- an order of magnitude better than perturbation-augmented training (-3.7pp). The rotation-and-decay geometry replicates on Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B, and Gemma-4-E4B; probe evaluation is on Llama-3.1-8B. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [22] [Crash Narrative-Guided Countermeasure Recommendation Using Large Language Models: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework for Intersection Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15997)
*Abu Saif Md Nasim Uddin,Mohamed Abdel-Aty,Zubayer Islam,Parvez Anowar,Chenzhu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事故叙述的检索增强生成(RAG)框架,将事故机理转化为针对具体路口的安全改善措施建议,在佛罗里达州数据上取得了F1分数0.82的优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通安全分析中,事故机理识别和对策推荐依赖专家判断,劳动密集、难以扩展;事故叙述虽包含丰富信息但未被充分利用,因此需要自动化、可解释的决策支持工具。

Method: 构建RAG框架:从事故叙述中提取交通控制、信号指示、驾驶人过错、车辆运动和行驶方向等关键属性,与FHWA Proven Safety Countermeasures和CMF Clearinghouse中的循证对策关联;集成嵌入检索历史相似路口、关联规则挖掘、对策数量统计指导以及工程推理引导LLM进行领域一致的决策。

Result: 在佛罗里达州Lake和Sumter Counties 115个路口的312起严重伤亡事故上进行五折交叉验证,精确率0.82、召回率0.85、F1分数0.82;平均每个地点推荐3.91项对策,其中3.14项匹配,接近实际平均值3.86。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了检索增强LLM作为可解释、可扩展决策支持工具的潜力,能够将事故叙述有效转化为交通安全对策推荐,可为交通机构提供实用支持。

Abstract: Improving safety at intersections requires identifying crash mechanisms and recommending appropriate countermeasures. However, this process traditionally relies on expert judgment, making it labor-intensive, difficult to scale, and dependent on the availability of experienced traffic safety engineers. Although crash narratives contain rich description of crash mechanisms, this unstructured information remains largely underutilized in safety analyses. This study presents a crash narrative-guided retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that translates narrative-derived crash mechanisms into site-specific countermeasure recommendations. Key mechanism attributes including traffic control, signal indication, driver fault, vehicle movement, and travel direction were extracted from crash narratives and linked to evidence-based treatments from the FHWA Proven Safety Countermeasures and the CMF Clearinghouse. The framework integrates embedding-based retrieval of historically similar intersections, association-rule mining, statistical guidance on the expected number of relevant countermeasures, and an engineering reasoning guidance that directs LLM through a domain-consistent decision process before selecting countermeasures. Evaluated on 312 fatal and serious-injury crashes across 115 intersections in Lake and Sumter Counties, Florida, using five-fold cross-validation, the framework achieved a precision of 0.82, recall of 0.85, and F1-score of 0.82, while recommending an average of 3.91 countermeasures per location with 3.14 matching, closely matching the actual average (3.86). Overall, the proposed framework demonstrates the potential of retrieval-augmented LLMs as an interpretable and scalable decision-support tool for transportation agencies for translating crash narratives into countermeasure recommendations.

</details>


### [23] [ViCo: Visual-oriented Coding with Self-Reflection for Chart Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16014)
*Jiaxin Duan,Dian Jiao Shuai Zhao,Jiabing Leng,Yiran Zhang,Feng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ViCo是一个面向学术图表复现任务的视觉导向编码训练框架，通过自监督热身（MCTS+一致性剪枝）和反事实多步强化学习，解决自反思机制中反思质量差和奖励稀疏两大核心问题，并提出基于层次异构布局图（HHLG）的轻量级多维自动评估框架。基于Qwen3-VL的8B模型在三个公开基准上达到接近闭源大模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体虽然能生成结构良好的文本和代码，但其生成的学术图表在视觉风格和语义保真度上仍落后于人类设计。基于自反思机制的编码智能体存在两大瓶颈：(1) 反思有效性差——智能体常产生浅层甚至误导性的批评；(2) 反思执行不一致——即使反思正确，后续代码生成也可能忽略建议（"言行不一"）；(3) 多轮ReAct过程通过强化学习优化时面临严重的奖励稀疏与信用分配挑战，限制了训练效果。此外，现有方法依赖GPT-5等专有大模型作为评判器，计算成本高昂。

Method: ViCo框架包含三个核心组件：
1. **自监督热身阶段（Self-Supervised Warm-Up）**：采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）合成高质量反思-编码轨迹，通过(a) 带有差分惩罚的PUCT选择机制避开停滞分支；(b) 结构化一致性约束（项目清单式反思+一致性评分Critic）保证反思与代码严格对齐；(c) Max-Backup值回传和基于奖励单调性的"Jump-Pruning"轨迹提取，生成严格递增奖励的SFT轨迹。
2. **反事实多步强化学习（MSRL）**：扩展PPO框架，采用COMA风格的反事实基线估计步骤级优势——编码步骤优势A(a_t)通过固定反思后的Monte Carlo替代基线计算；反思步骤优势A(r_t)通过与"空反思"(r_∅)对比计算，实现密集的步骤级信用分配。
3. **HHLG自动评估框架**：构建层次异构布局图（Macro-Tree建模全局子图拓扑，Micro-Graph建模局部元素，分离文本节点和视觉节点），从四个维度评估：语义一致性（OCR+编辑距离+IoU）、布局一致性（树编辑距离+图编辑距离）、风格一致性（VGG-19 Gram矩阵）、颜色一致性（CIELAB空间感知距离）。
问题建模为有限时域POMDP，交替执行反思动作r_t和编码动作a_t，最大化最终视觉一致性奖励。

Result: **主实验结果**（基于Qwen3-VL-8B）：
- RealChart2Code：Pass Rate 98.8%（超越Claude-4.5-Opus的87.7%，InternVL-241B的54.3%），Score 3.4（与ChartCoder的3.5相当）
- ChartMimic (Direct)：Pass 99.2%，Score 81.5（超越Gemini-2.5-Flash的69.8%）
- ChartMimic (Customized)：Pass 100.0%，Score 80.9
- Plot2Code：Pass 99.1%，Text Score 78.4，Rating 8.4（接近Claude-4.5-Sonnet的8.8）

**关键消融发现**：
- 反事实信用分配至关重要：去除后RealChart2Code Pass率从98.8%骤降至47.1%
- 去除HHLG改用Qwen3-VL-8B作为奖励模型：ChartMimic Score从81.5降至72.3
- HHLG与人类判断相关性显著优于LLM-as-Judge：简单布局Spearman ρ=0.724（GPT-4o为0.712），复杂布局ρ=0.458（GPT-4o为0.321）
- 反思步骤优势A(r_t)与真实视觉提升的Spearman相关性达0.742（p<0.001），证明奖励信号有效
- 跨骨干网络泛化：在InternVL-3.5-8B上同样取得显著提升（Pass率+87.3%）

**对复杂布局的局限性**：直接生成在>6子图的复杂图表上表现不佳（Score仅1.2），提出Divide-and-Conquer分治流水线将其提升至7.6。

**训练成本**：8B模型在8×A800节点上完整训练约25.6 GPU小时，仅比标准多轮PPO多6.8 GPU小时，但在RealChart2Code上获得+41.7%的Pass率提升。

Conclusion: ViCo通过两阶段训练框架（自监督热身+反事实多步强化学习）和基于HHLG的轻量级多维评估机制，有效解决了视觉导向代码生成中自反思质量差和奖励稀疏两大难题。基于Qwen3-VL-8B的小型开源模型在三个权威图表复现基准上达到接近GPT-5/Claude-4.5等专有模型的性能，证明了该方法的高效性和通用性。局限性在于复杂多子图布局仍是挑战，且RL阶段计算开销较高；未来工作将拓展至网页、UI等更广泛的视觉到代码生成任务。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of generating high-quality academic charts that match the visual standards of human-authored papers. While existing AI agents can produce well-structured text and code, their generated visualizations often lack the stylistic and semantic fidelity of human designs. Advanced coding agents that employ self-reflection mechanisms exhibit poor visual reasoning and limited reflection following, resulting in sparse reward signals that severely undermine their reinforcement learning (RL). We propose ViCo, a training framework for visual-oriented coding that employs iterative reflections to align generated chart images progressively with the reference. We first introduce a self-supervised warm-up stage, which augments Monte Carlo Tree Search with consistency-based pruning to synthesize high-quality reflection trajectories, ensuring that each coding step strictly follows the outcomes of prior reflections. A multi-step RL algorithm is then developed, using counterfactual baselines to estimate advantage for reflection and action steps within each refinement cycle, thereby addressing the reward sparsity. To enable efficient reward in massive training, we propose an automatic, multifaceted evaluation framework that assesses charts' style, layout, and semantic consistency via a hierarchical heterogeneous layout graph structure. Experiments on three public benchmarks demonstrate that ViCo, trained on an 8B model, achieves performance close to proprietary LLMs with adequate reflection capabilities.

</details>


### [24] [Are We Grading Properly? Understanding Failure Modes in Medical Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16023)
*Prithvi Dixit,Pedram Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究医学基准测试中评分标准（rubric）本身的质量问题。通过应用RIFT失败模式分类法，发现HealthBench Professional中29.6%的标准非原子化、65.4%错位/过于严格；将捆绑型评分项拆分为独立子项后重新评分，相同回答的得分变化可达15.9个百分点，表明rubric缺陷会实质性影响模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着医学评估从选择题转向开放式临床场景，基于rubric的LLM评分成为主流，但rubric本身作为测量工具的有效性很少被检验。如果rubric存在缺陷，模型报告分数的可靠性就存疑。

Method: 1. 使用RIFT分类法对两个临床基准（HealthBench Professional和LiveMedBench）的rubric进行失败模式检测；2. 提出"split-and-regrade"方法，将捆绑型评分标准（如"至少满足以下之一/全部"）拆分为等权重的独立子项，保持回答和评分法官不变进行重新评分；3. 利用合取与析取捆绑产生方向相反的分数变化来控制评分法官的倾向性偏差。

Result: 1. HBP上GPT-5.4检测法官标记29.6%的标准为非原子化，65.4%为错位/严格；2. 拆分后受影响对话得分变化达-15.9至+18.9百分点，方向与预测一致（析取拆分降低分数、合取拆分提高分数）；3. LiveMedBench上方向复制但幅度较小（<1点）；4. RIFT在临床rubric上严重低估捆绑，仅标记3.3%，而表面形式分析发现25.8%。

Conclusion: Rubric质量应获得与模型本身同等的测量审查。建议基准作者将捆绑要求写成独立的评分项并分配分数到各分支。当前LLM检测法官的流行率估计存在跨法官差异，应被视为法官内比较而非黄金标准；领域适配的失败模式分类法有待未来工作。

Abstract: Medical evaluation is shifting from static option-based questioning to realistic clinical scenarios with open-ended output modes. Grading these at scale naively, however, is expensive, and rubric-based evaluation has become the dominant scalable alternative. We ask what happens when the rubrics themselves are not airtight, and whether such flaws can be detected and corrected. We apply RIFT, a global rubric failure taxonomy, to two clinical benchmarks (HealthBench Professional and LiveMedBench), and find failure modes are meaningful: on HealthBench Professional an LLM judge flags 29.6% of criteria as non-atomic and 65.4% as misaligned/rigid. Then, we show that these flaws are meaningful and not simply cosmetic. As an example, rewriting bundled criteria of the form "at least one of / all of the following" as equally weighted children and regrading identical responses shifts scores by up to 15.9 percentage points on affected conversations, with disjunctive bundles inflating scores and conjunctive bundles deflating them. We also find that RIFT generally under-detects bundling on clinical rubrics, flagging 3.3% of LiveMedBench criteria as non-atomic where surface-form analysis finds structure in 25.8%.

</details>


### [25] [Towards Scalable RLVR: Multimodal Instruction Following Data Synthesis and Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16059)
*Yirong Zeng,Zhang Sai,Yuxian Wang,Yutai Hou,Yufei Liu,Xiao Ding,Bibo Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MIFS数据合成流水线，通过生成式约束协议、可学习性感知蒸馏机制和基于代码的验证器，为RLVR训练生成大规模、高质量、可程序验证的多模态指令遵循数据集（90k样本），有效提升多模态大模型的指令遵循能力并避免SFT的泛化能力退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态指令遵循（MMIF）训练主要依赖监督微调（SFT），但SFT易导致浅层模式匹配并损害模型通用能力。强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）虽有潜力，却受限于缺乏适合RL的高质量多模态数据，亟需一种可扩展的数据合成方案。

Method: 提出MIFS三阶段流水线：(1) 种子空间构建——基于CC3M/ALLaVA精选2万张高语义密度图像，建立14个任务域和8类可验证约束的分类体系；(2) 图像-指令任务生成——利用LLM（如Gemini 3.0-Pro）将基础VQA对转化为含≥5个交互约束的复杂任务，每条约束附带Python验证代码，并通过剪枝-再生成策略确保逻辑一致性，生成50万原始样本；(3) 三级质量蒸馏——约束密度过滤（保留2-10个约束）、难度感知剪枝（丢弃avg@8>0.8的简单样本和avg@128=0的不可解样本）、RL可学习性过滤（基于奖励轨迹偏差$\mathcal{D}_i$保留40%最稳定样本），最终蒸馏为9万样本。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL系列（4B/8B/32B）上的实验显示：MIFS-RL在四个指令遵循基准（MIABench、MM-IFEval、MIFS-Eval、IFEval）上平均提升8.13%（4B/8B/32B分别为+8.16/+8.13/+8.52）；收敛速度比原始数据快约3倍；在OCRBench、MMVet、MMBench三个通用视觉基准上性能波动<0.5%，有效避免了SFT导致的通用能力退化（纯SFT下降12.3%）；人工审计验证代码验证器与人类判断一致率达92.0%，精确率97.22%。

Conclusion: MIFS流水线成功弥合了SFT与RLVR之间的多模态数据鸿沟，验证了RLVR在多模态指令遵循中的显著优势：直接RL比纯SFT增益高5个百分点（8.13% vs 3.09%），且能保持通用视觉能力。该工作为构建可扩展、约束感知的多模态通用智能体奠定了数据基础，未来需探索跨架构泛化、混合奖励机制以及轻量级代理模型预测学习动态以降低计算开销。

Abstract: Multimodal instruction following (MMIF) is crucial for building generalist agents. However, current training paradigms rely heavily on Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), which often leads to surface-level pattern matching and degrades general capabilities. While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) offers a promising alternative, its scalability in MMIF is severely bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-quality, RL-ready multimodal data. To bridge this gap, we present MIFS (\textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{I}nstruction \textbf{F}ollowing \textbf{S}ynthesis), a systematic pipeline designed to generate RL-ready multimodal data. Specifically, MIFS introduces a generative constraint protocol to synthesize diverse raw samples, followed by a learnability-aware distillation mechanism that filters data based on RL training dynamics to ensure stable policy optimization. Furthermore, a code-based verifier provides high-precision reward signals for policy learning. The resulting dataset comprises 90k samples across 8 constraint categories and 14 task domains. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MIFS-trained MLLMs achieve an average improvement of 8.13\% on four MMIF benchmarks and a 3$\times$ faster training convergence compared to using raw data. Crucially, our approach mitigates the generalization trade-offs typical of SFT, preserving core visual capabilities while significantly boosting instruction-following precision.

</details>


### [26] [StalePO: Anchored Token-Level Preference Optimization using Legacy Post-Edits in Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16340)
*Rohit Dhaipule,Sukhdeep Singh Kharbanda,Prasanth Bathala,Pradyumna Lanka,Anubhav Shrimal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 StalePO 方法，解决机器翻译模型升级时面临的"陈旧偏好"问题：人类反馈数据来自旧系统的后编辑，而新模型可能已经超越这些参考译文。StalePO 通过要求"两个响应上的似然均下降"、"策略锚定到自身基础响应"和"token 级 KL 约束"这三个机制的联合作用，将陈旧的后编辑反馈转化为模型质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译系统迭代升级时存在一个核心矛盾：新模型已可能优于人类对旧模型输出的后编辑，但重新收集针对新模型的偏好数据成本极高。标准的 DPO 在此场景下会失效：可能提升劣质后编辑的似然、损害模型原有质量，且缺乏 token 级局部纠错能力。

Method: StalePO 从陈旧偏好场景的三个必要要求出发设计目标函数：(1) 对两个响应（基础响应和后编辑）都施加似然下降约束，避免偏向劣质后编辑；(2) 将策略锚定到模型自身生成的基础响应，防止偏离；(3) 在 token 级别施加 KL 约束，实现对局部错误的精细控制。三个机制缺一不可，需联合使用。

Result: 在英-印地语和英-土耳其语本地化数据上的实验表明：StalePO 使通过全部 LLM-as-judge MQM 质量检查的句子比例分别提升 14.9 和 4.6 个百分点，改进主要体现在风格和流畅度上。英-印地语的人工评估进一步验证了效果，通过全部七项人工检查的句子比例提升 13.8 个百分点。消融实验显示，任何单一机制都无法带来增益，仅三者联合才能将陈旧反馈转化为有效提升。

Conclusion: StalePO 成功解决了机器翻译升级中的陈旧偏好问题，证明了三个设计原则（双向似然下降、自身锚定、token 级 KL）的联合必要性。该方法为低成本利用历史人类反馈数据持续改进翻译模型提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Machine translation systems are periodically upgraded to stronger models, but the available preference signal is human post-edits of an older system's outputs, which the newer model may already surpass. Moreover, collecting fresh post-edits for every new model is prohibitively expensive. We call this the Stale Preference problem. Standard DPO can fail in this setting: it may increase the likelihood of inferior post-edits, erode the model's existing quality, and fail to provide the per-token control needed to correct localized errors. We introduce StalePO, an objective derived from three requirements this regime imposes. Likelihood movement must be downward on both responses, the policy must be anchored to its own base response, and the KL constraint must apply at the token level. These requirements are jointly necessary. In ablations, each mechanism in isolation leaves the model's performance indistinguishable from the base model, and only their combination converts stale feedback into gains. On English-to-Hindi and English-to-Turkish localization data, StalePO improves the fraction of segments passing all LLM-as-judge MQM quality checks by 14.9 and 4.6 percentage points, respectively, with gains concentrated on style and fluency. A human evaluation under the same framework confirms these gains on English-to-Hindi, raising the fraction of segments passing all seven human checks by 13.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [27] [HUMAID-NER: A Disaster Tweet Dataset for Joint Named Entity Recognition and Event Classification via Uncertainty-Weighted Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16964)
*Aijaz Ali,Nazish Basir,Sarfaraz Nawaz,Danish Nazir Arain,Haris Ali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HUMAID-NER数据集,首次为灾害推文提供跨度级命名实体识别标注(60,000条推文,10种实体类型,~175,000实体跨度),并提出基于不确定性加权多任务学习的联合NER与事件分类框架(RoBERTa-large),在验证集上同时取得NER span micro-F1 0.841和分类macro-F1 0.761,并部署了实时Web仪表盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灾害推文资源仅提供文档级类别标签,缺乏跨度级实体标注;同时缺乏能同时处理灾害NER和事件分类的联合模型,这限制了人道主义救援中从社交媒体快速提取结构化信息的能力。

Method: 1) 构建HUMAID-NER数据集:采用三阶段混合自动标注流水线(spaCy transformer + 领域EntityRuler模式 + 正则表达式,带优先级重叠解析),生成BIO格式标注;2) 联合多任务框架:共享RoBERTa-large编码器+NER头(21个BIO类)+分类头([CLS]与均值池化拼接);3) 采用Kendall同方差不确定性加权(可学习对数方差参数)解决梯度不平衡问题;4) 两阶段训练(借鉴MT-DNN):阶段1训练全部24层,阶段2冻结前18层仅微调上层6层+任务头;5) AdamW优化器,TPU v3-8训练。

Result: 消融实验(4行):A(BERT-large+固定权重)NER 0.873/CLS 0.736;B(RoBERTa+固定权重)0.863/0.749;C(+Kendall)0.866/0.745;D(完整系统+两阶段)0.841/0.761。Row D取得最高分类F1,但NER略低。任务冲突分析显示固定权重下CLS从epoch 3的0.748逐步下降到0.736(负迁移),而训练损失单调下降。最终分类F1 0.761达到或超过HumAID上单任务RoBERTa分类器(0.730-0.750)。

Conclusion: 首次构建灾害领域NER数据集HUMAID-NER并验证了联合多任务建模可行性;主要发现:1) 固定权重下任务冲突是结构性的(梯度比2688:1);2) Kendall加权主要价值在训练稳定性而非最终分数;3) 两阶段层冻结带来最大CLS增益(+1.6);4) 联合建模不牺牲分类性能。局限:未做人机标注一致性验证、仅英语、单次运行无多seed验证、仅报告验证集。

Abstract: Rapid extraction of structured information from social media is important for humanitarian response, yet existing disaster tweet resources mainly provide document-level category labels without span-level entity annotations. We introduce HUMAID-NER, the first named entity recognition dataset built on the HumAID benchmark, containing 60,000 English disaster tweets annotated in BIO format across ten operationally motivated entity types and yielding approximately 175,000 labelled entity spans. Annotations are generated through a reproducible three-stage hybrid pipeline combining a spaCy transformer model, disaster-domain EntityRuler patterns, and structured regular expressions with priority-based overlap resolution. We also propose a joint multitask learning framework that performs disaster-specific named entity recognition and humanitarian event classification using a shared RoBERTa-large encoder. To reduce task conflict during joint training, the model uses homoscedastic uncertainty weighting with learnable task parameters and a two-stage training schedule that freezes the lower 18 of 24 encoder layers in the second stage. On the HUMAID-NER validation set, the proposed system achieves NER span micro-F1 of 0.841 and classification macro-F1 of 0.761 simultaneously. A real-time web dashboard demonstrates end-to-end deployment. The dataset, models, and pipeline code are released to support reproducibility and future crisis informatics research.

</details>


### [28] [PaperDoctor: Evidence-Grounded and Actionable Feedback for Scientific Papers in Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16995)
*Kevin Qinghong Lin,Siyuan Hu,Pan Lu,Yu Chen,Yanzhe Chen,Owen Queen,Yupeng Chen,Jialin Yu,Junchi Yu,Zifeng Ding,Yuanfeng Ji,Sheng Liu,Jindong Gu,Linjie Li,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PaperDoctor 是一个面向投稿前论文的智能体反馈框架，将自动化论文评估从"裁判"转变为"诊断者"，通过三层分层流水线（L1表面筛查、L2声明验证、L3实验复现）为作者提供有证据支撑且可操作的修改建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动研究智能体（autoresearch agents）的兴起，AI撰写或辅助撰写的论文数量激增，但质量难以保证，存在虚构引用、夸大创新性、声明与代码不符、未经验证的实验结论等问题。现有自动化审稿系统主要给出"接受/拒绝"的判定，对作者帮助有限；而人类导师提供的细致、可追溯的诊断式反馈虽有效但难以规模化。该研究旨在构建一个能像导师一样提供诊断级反馈的智能体框架。

Method: PaperDoctor 采用三层分层流水线架构：(1) 解析阶段将论文解析为结构化markdown、分页图像和代码索引；(2) L1表面筛查层通过LLM/VLM并行执行写作、图表、引文检查，并密集提取可验证声明；(3) L2声明验证层将每个声明路由到对应验证器：代码验证（对照源码）、理论验证（逐步重推导）、文献检查（联网检索新颖性主张）、实验设计审查（输出复现计划）；(4) L3实验复现阶段基于声明重要性和计算预算选择性重建并重跑实验，经作者手动批准后执行。每个反馈是"(发现, 证据, 建议)"三元组，证据精确指向句子、公式、代码行或外部URL，且区分"错误"和"警告"两个严重级别。

Result: 在30篇投稿前论文上达到70.6%证据接受率，整体评分均为正面（均分+1.30）；在40篇跨机器学习、自然科学、社会科学的论文（含人类和AI撰写）上，每条反馈均同时包含证据和建议，而人类审稿人和Stanford Agentic Reviewer分别只有35.9%和1.5%的反馈同时包含两者；PaperDoctor覆盖维度更均衡（实验仅占28.4%，而agentic reviewer占72.6%），外部文献和代码检查占比更高。复现阶段中，仅18%的复现计划达到可运行状态，47%因代码/数据不完整而无法执行；在可执行的计划中，高优先级计划通过率(47.3%)显著高于低优先级(33.6%)；ICML论文主要在执行后失败（仅14.4%复现成功），而Agent4Science论文主要在执行前被阻断但执行后72.2%可复现。

Conclusion: PaperDoctor 将自动化论文评估重新定义为诊断而非裁决，通过证据三元组设计使每条批评都可审计、可操作，代码审计和实验复现能力填补了现有同行评审系统的关键空白。局限性在于复现受限于代码/数据的完整性与可执行性，AI撰写论文在L2代码检查中暴露的问题远多于人类撰写论文（8.4 vs 2.4–4.6）。未来方向包括：从论文本身合成参考实现以实现完整复现，以及大规模收集并结构化资深导师反馈以进一步校准系统。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents are reshaping the research ecosystem, but they can also let flawed claims enter the literature at scale. Human advisors catch such issues in drafts through careful, traceable feedback, yet advisor-style assessment requires extensive manual effort and does not scale. To shift automated paper assessment from a judge to a diagnostician, we introduce PaperDoctor, an agent framework for pre-submission feedback with three key innovations. First, a holistic hierarchical framework evaluates writing, layout, references, code, theory, prior work, and experiments through three layers: L1 surface screening, L2 typed verifiers that route each claim to the appropriate evidence, and L3 reproducers that rerun experiments by priority. Second, each finding contains an observation, a pointer to specific evidence such as a sentence, equation, or code line, and a revision suggestion, making critiques auditable and actionable. Third, PaperDoctor selectively rebuilds and reruns experiments based on claim importance and compute budget, surfacing reproducibility gaps and quantitative limitations that are invisible from the manuscript alone. We evaluate PaperDoctor on 30 in-progress papers, yielding 70.6% agreement and all positive holistic scores, and on 40 manuscripts across machine learning, natural science, and social science, covering human- and AI-authored papers with code. Overall, PaperDoctor produces more auditable feedback than human and other agentic reviewers, pairs critiques with concrete suggestions by design, and complements dimensions often overlooked by human reviewers. We also develop an interactive interface that lets authors browse findings grounded in their paper. PaperDoctor reframes automated paper assessment as diagnosis rather than verdict, taking a concrete step toward AI advisors for more rigorous AI-assisted scientific discovery.

</details>


### [29] [The Functionalizer: Lossless Functional Decomposition for Subword Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15991)
*Connor Makowski,Willem Guter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Functionalizer，一种无损子词预分词框架，通过Unicode专用区编码操作码/操作数前缀流，将大小写、变音符号、字符重复等正字法变化因子化为可复用的参数化操作符，在不损失信息的前提下压缩词表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准子词分词器面临两难：要么将hello/Hello/HELLO/Héllo视为独立词表项导致嵌入空间碎片化，要么通过有损归一化（如小写化、去重音）丢弃信息。论文旨在找到一条既能消除词表膨胀又能完整保留正字法信息的"第三条路径"。

Method: 借鉴CPU指令集架构的opcode/operand思想，将正字法与结构变化分解为：① 标准化基词（操作数）；② 前缀式参数化变换操作符（操作码），包括CAPITALIZE、13种变音符号算子（U+E101–U+E10D）、REPEAT和MULTIREPEAT（U+E200/E201），操作码编码于Unicode专用区U+E000–U+EFFF。编码管道先抽取变音符号/大写位置→生成操作码→剥离变音并小写化→添加前缀；解码按固定逆序执行，保证双射无损。在6个自然语言与代码语料库上对比Split Only、Split+Functionalizer、Split+Functionalizer(Repeat)三种配置，训练约25M参数GPT-2模型进行下游评估。

Result: 在无约束（语料穷尽）条件下，Functionalizer使所需词表槽位减少多达16%（Wikitext −15.60%、Python-Codes −16.11%、CSN-Python −12.72%等）。序列长度呈显著领域分化：缩进密集的代码序列被压缩（CSN-Python令牌减少−27.44%、字符吞吐量+44%），Char PPL相对改善12.6%；Python代码语法成功率从0.00%跃升至9.20%；CSN-Go因tab缩进与CamelCase成为反例（序列膨胀+13.63%，Char PPL下降）。自然语言散文则因缺少长重复序列，casing操作符带来+6.45%–9%的序列长度开销，Char PPL略降，但连贯性相当（97.80% vs 99.00%）。纯REPEAT配置在TinyStories上获得+1.5%字符速度提升，在CSN-Python上获得+22.8%推理速度提升。
