### [44] [Challenges of Auditing: Variability in Outputs of Large Language Models for Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16590)
*Yuan Pu,Yewon Chang,Furong Jia,Xunjian Yin,Jessica Ma,Ayman Ali,Monica Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现，即使是同一模型版本，通过API、ChatGPT聊天界面和ChatGPT Health三种访问模式回答相同健康问题，也会产生系统性的差异，涵盖响应形式、行为和临床内容，揭示了当前AI审计的监管盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着越来越多消费者通过聊天机器人界面获取健康建议，而学术评估通常通过API进行，两种访问模式之间的差异可能使评估结果无法代表真实用户体验，亟需探究访问模式对LLM健康输出的影响。

Method: 使用50个真实患者健康问题，在GPT-5.3和GPT-5.4两个模型版本下，分别通过API、标准ChatGPT界面和ChatGPT Health三种模式各提交3次，采用配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验比较响应形式（长度、可读性、格式）、行为（免责声明、追问倾向）、引用来源和临床内容方面的差异。

Result: 研究显示访问模式间存在系统性差异：GPT-5.3中界面响应更长、可读性更高、格式更密集且更倾向追问用户（均p<0.001），但GPT-5.4中部分差异方向反转；引用网页的跨模式Jaccard重叠仅10.9%（p<0.001），临床概念的跨模式重叠也显著低于同模式内重叠。

Conclusion: 研究强调当前API评估无法忠实复现消费者实际体验，构成审计监管盲区，呼吁监管机构要求消费级健康AI厂商提供可重现消费者部署路径的API，以确保评估反映患者实际遇到的产品。

Abstract: People increasingly use frontier AI models for health advice, but via different access modes (e.g., ChatGPT, ChatGPT Health, APIs) with varying settings. Here, we find systematic differences across access modes. Because evaluations typically rely on APIs while consumers interact through chatbot interfaces, these discrepancies limit evaluation validity. Our findings underscore an urgent need for model providers to enable faithful replication of consumer experiences and settings for rigorous audits.

</details>


### [45] [Quantifying Organizational Environmental Action from Web Data and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16627)
*Quinn Reynolds,Daniel Shore,Vianey Leos Barajas,Tanhum Yoreh,Meredith Franklin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可扩展的计算框架，利用网络爬虫和大型语言模型（LLM）将组织网站内容转化为结构化的环境行动量化指标，并以美国犹太教会堂为案例进行了验证。研究比较了三种环境行动检测方法（关键词检索+LLM、语义向量检索+LLM、直接LLM分类），发现直接LLM分类虽计算成本最高，但能获得最佳的教会堂覆盖率。最终识别出53%的教会堂至少有一项环境行动，且环境行动在教派、地理和政治背景上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织层面的环境行动信息通常分散在多个网页中，且以非结构化文本形式呈现，难以系统量化。现有环境数据科学缺乏从组织网站大规模、自动化提取环境行动的可复现方法论，尤其是在宗教组织等公民社会机构层面。

Method: 1. 数据库构建：整合OpenStreetMap、Wikidata、Wikipedia、教派目录、Google Places API及人工审核，构建4,964个美国犹太教会堂数据库；2. 网络爬取：对2,657个活跃网站进行广度优先爬取，共获取154,454个网页；3. 分类方法比较：对比三种LLM分类流程——关键词检索+LLM、语义向量检索（all-MiniLM-L6-v2嵌入，余弦相似度阈值0.25）+LLM、直接LLM分类（Google Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite）；4. 人工验证：1,200个文本块经专家标注，以Cohen's κ评估一致性；5. 环境行动分类法：基于Greening Sacred Spaces项目，包含9大类76种行动类型；6. 框架分类：显式、隐式、嵌入式三类环境叙事方式；7. 地理与政治背景关联：与2024年国会选区投票结果进行空间连接分析。

Result: 1. 方法比较：与人工专家的一致性方面，关键词检索最低（κ=0.26），语义向量检索最高（κ=0.42），直接LLM分类相近（κ=0.40），但向量检索召回率仅0.87，表明检索阶段损失了相关信息；2. 全语料库结果：直接LLM分类在1,398个教会堂（52.6%）中识别出至少一项环境行动，覆盖面最大；3. 分布特征：环境行动呈强右偏分布，均值5.1，中位数1；4. 类别集中：精神与礼拜（77%）和社区（62%）占主导，合计贡献80%的环境行动；5. 教派差异：犹太教正统派环境行动显著少于其他教派，Chabad显著少于改革派、重建派等（Kruskal-Wallis H(8)=413.8, p<0.001）；6. 政治背景：民主党选区的教会堂显式环境行动显著多于共和党选区（p=0.01），隐式行动差异较小。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了基于LLM的网络数据提取可大规模量化组织环境行动，同时保留了行动的语境丰富性。核心发现是：检索阶段会引入信息损失，直接LLM分类虽计算成本高但覆盖更全；环境行动在美国犹太教会堂中呈现教派、地理和政治维度的显著异质性；该框架可推广至学校、市政、非营利组织等其他机构类型，为环境数据科学提供了一种将非结构化网络内容转化为可分析结构化数据的新路径。

Abstract: Quantifying organizational environmental action from publicly available web content remains a challenging environmental data science problem because relevant information can be dispersed across multiple webpages and is primarily communicated through unstructured text. We present a scalable computational framework for transforming organizational web content into structured measures of environmental action and demonstrate the approach using Jewish congregations in the United States. We constructed a national database of 4,964 congregations by integrating multiple geospatial, knowledge-base, directory, and manually reviewed sources. Of these, 2,657 had active websites that were successfully crawled, producing a corpus of 154,454 webpages. We compared three approaches for detecting environmental actions: keyword retrieval followed by large language model (LLM) classification, semantic vector retrieval followed by LLM classification, and direct LLM classification classification without preliminary retrieval. Agreement with an expert human reviewer was lowest for keyword retrieval ($\kappa$ = 0.26), higher for semantic vector retrieval ($\kappa$ = 0.42), and similar for direct LLM classification ($\kappa$ = 0.40). Although semantic retrieval achieved the highest agreement, its retrieval recall was 0.87, indicating loss of relevant content before classification. Applied to the complete corpus, direct LLM classification identified at least one environmental action at 1,398 congregations (53%), providing greater coverage than either retrieval-based approach. These results demonstrate that preliminary retrieval can reduce computational cost but may exclude relevant information before it reaches the classifier. The framework provides a reproducible approach for extracting organization-level environmental information from unstructured web content that can be adapted to other institutions.

</details>


### [46] [Rewarding Reasoning, Not Answers: Fixing and Bounding Test-Time Reinforcement Learning on Medical QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16660)
*Kailong Fan,Anqi Pu,Yichen Wu,Wanhua Li,Yicong Li,Hanspeter Pfister,Huafeng Liu,Xiang Li,Quanzheng Li,Ning Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了测试时强化学习（TTRL）在医学多项选择题问答（MCQ）上失效的根源——答案空间结构导致"Lucky Hit"机制崩溃，并提出PROSE方法，通过医学过程奖励模型（PRM）逐步评分、最小值聚合和格式守卫来奖励推理质量而非答案一致性，从而在无标签条件下显著提升通用模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学领域，由于专家标注成本高、数据隐私限制等因素，无标签的测试时自适应具有重要价值。然而现有TTRL方法在数学任务上成功，在医学MCQ任务上却失效：准确率停滞而输出多样性急剧下降。该研究旨在探究这一现象的根因并提出解决方案。

Method: 论文采用控制变量实验方法，仅改变答案空间（从自由格式改为4选项MCQ）而固定问题、模型和优化器，以诊断失败原因。在此基础上提出PROSE（Process Reward Guided Self-Training）方法：使用Med-PRM对每个推理步骤评分，采用最小值聚合（而非平均值）作为轨迹奖励，并添加答案格式约束，通过GRPO更新策略。

Result: 在四个医学QA基准（MedQA-5op、MedMCQA、DDXPlus、MedQA-4op）上：PROSE将Llama-3.1-8B的平均准确率从0.635提升至0.740（提升10.5个百分点），超越所有8-9B开源通用和医学专用模型，平均0.740的成绩几乎与32B的QwQ（0.741）和GPT-4o-mini（0.744）持平，仅次于Gemini Flash 2.0（0.801）。在DDXPlus上达到0.858，为全表最高。OOD泛化测试显示，在MedQA-5op上适应的策略可直接迁移到未见数据集，无需PRM即可保持增益。消融实验证实：最小值聚合是必要的（均值聚合会导致奖励黑客攻击，代理奖励饱和而准确率下降）；PRM作为分级奖励优于PRM作为标签。模型规模分析显示，PROSE在≥4B时优势明显，但在1.7B时优势减弱，此时推理时选择更优。

Conclusion: TTRL在医学MCQ上失效的根本原因不是领域难度，而是小答案空间的结构：不同错误推理路径会"碰撞"到同一错误选项，形成被强化的虚假共识。PROSE通过奖励推理过程而非答案一致性解决了这一问题，并将过程信号内化到策略中，推理时无需PRM。该方法依赖领域相关的PRM，且有效性受策略容量限制（需≥4B），未来工作应探索自生成过程奖励和容量-奖励质量对内化效果的影响。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning adapts a model on its own unlabeled test set using majority-vote pseudo-labels and has shown strong results in mathematics. We show that this recipe collapses on medical multiple-choice QA: accuracy stagnates while output diversity rapidly declines. Through a controlled experiment that keeps the questions, model, and optimizer fixed while changing only the answer space, we trace this failure to answer-space structure rather than domain difficulty. In small answer spaces, incorrect rollouts often collide on the same wrong pseudo-label and reinforce it; in large answer spaces, they disperse and receive little reward. This diagnosis motivates PROSE, Process Reward Guided Self-Training, which rewards reasoning quality instead of answer agreement. PROSE scores each reasoning step with a medical process reward model, assigns the trajectory reward as the minimum score across steps, and enforces answer-format constraints. Without labels, PROSE substantially improves a general Llama model, surpassing purpose-built medical models and matching much larger systems. Because the process signal is internalized into the policy, the adapted model requires no reward model at inference and transfers its gains to unseen datasets. We further show that the minimum aggregation is essential: mean aggregation can be exploited, saturating the proxy reward while degrading accuracy.

</details>


### [47] [DiaWhisper-DPO: Role-Attributed Transcription of Clinical Interviews via Failure-Mined Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16661)
*Weiming Li,Ana Catarina Fidalgo Barata,Miguel Constante,João Miguel Sanches*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiaWhisper-DPO，通过 LoRA 微调 Whisper-large-v3 并附加帧级角色头实现端到端的角色归属转录，并以失败挖掘的 DPO 进行精炼，无需人工偏好标注。在 DAIC-WOZ 和中文 PDCH-HAMD 数据集上显著优于级联基线，并大幅降低种子敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床访谈中自动抑郁筛查不仅需要准确转录，更需要正确归属话语角色（医生/患者）。传统级联系统（说话人 diarization + 启发式角色分配）误差逐级传播；现有端到端联合模型或偏好优化方法要么缺乏对角色归属的针对性，要么依赖昂贵的人工偏好标注，亟需一种无需额外标注即可稳定提升角色归属与转录准确性的方案。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) Stage 1 (DiaWhisper)：用 LoRA (rank=16, α=32, dropout=0.05) 对 Whisper-large-v3 编码器和解码器的自注意力 Q/V 投影进行微调，目标序列采用 Whisper 原生 timestamp-tag-text 格式，文本段替换为角色标签 (<|doctor|>/<|patient|>/<|interject|>)；仅解冻 3 个角色 token 的 embedding/output 行；附加帧级辅助角色头 (线性层，4 类)，结合 CAM++ 嵌入余弦差计算置信度权重，加权 CE 损失 λ·L_diar (λ=0.3) 联合训练。(2) Stage 2 (DiaWhisper-DPO)：冻结 Stage 1 参数，对触发 8-token 重复的窗口采样约 500 个/seed，以真解码失败为 rejected、ground truth 为 chosen，使用 DPO (β=0.1, lr=5e-5, 1 epoch) 精炼策略 LoRA 适配器。推理采用 ~29.5s 分块、贪心解码 + 重复截断 (≤4 次重试，触发后改采样温度 0.5/top-p 0.9)。数据集：DAIC-WOZ 用 XTTS 重建双声道；PDCH-HAMD 用真实中文访谈 + 语音转换实现跨语言验证。

Result: 在 DAIC-WOZ (n=29) 上，DiaWhisper-DPO 取得角色准确率 0.973、DER 0.119、WER 0.048、AER(D/P) 0.01/0.02，较最强级联基线 (CAM++) DER 下降 72%，种子标准差从 σ=.205 降至 .002；在 PDCH-HAMD (n=26) 上取得角色准确率 0.757、DER 0.313、AER 0.26/0.26，78 对 session-seed 全部提升。消融实验：去掉 retry 后 DiaWhisper 降至 0.280 但 DiaWhisper-DPO 仅微降 (0.971)，表明收益主要来自 DPO；用 SFT 替代 DPO 仅得 0.684；用人工合成负样本替代真失败可恢复至 0.910，但种子方差增大约 18 倍，证实真失败信号更稳定。DAIC-WOZ 重试耗尽导致 33.6% 语音缺失，而 DPO 将其降至 3.2%；PDCH-HAMD 残差错误集中在角色归属而非覆盖率 (0.2% 缺失 vs 25.8% 错角色)。

Conclusion: DiaWhisper-DPO 通过 LoRA 微调 Whisper 并结合失败挖掘的 DPO，实现了无需独立 diarization 和人工偏好标注的联合转录与角色归属。在英语 (DAIC-WOZ) 和中文 (PDCH-HAMD) 两个临床访谈数据集上均显著超越级联基线，并大幅降低种子敏感性，验证了失败挖掘偏好优化在结构化转录任务中的有效性与跨语言适用性。

Abstract: Automated depression screening from clinical interviews requires attribution of utterances to the clinician or patient. We evaluate two datasets: DAIC-WOZ, where participant-only recordings require re-synthesizing both sides for controlled two-party evaluation, and PDCH-HAMD, comprising voice-converted real Chinese interviews for cross-lingual validation. Cascaded systems combine speaker diarization with role-assignment heuristics, so errors can propagate across stages. We propose an end-to-end model, which we named DiaWhisper, that fine-tunes Whisper-large-v3 with LoRA and an auxiliary frame-level role head for transcription and attribution, together with DiaWhisper-DPO, a failure-mined refinement that uses genuine decoding failures as DPO rejected completions without human preference annotation. On 29 DAIC-WOZ test sessions, DiaWhisper-DPO achieves 0.973 role accuracy and 0.119 DER, 72% below the strongest cascaded baseline, and reduces seed variation from {\sigma} = .205 to .002. Retrained on PDCH-HAMD, it achieves 0.757 role accuracy and improves all 78 session-seed pairs.

</details>


### [48] [Japanese Stroke LLM Evaluation: A Conversational Benchmark for Safe Stroke Care in Japanese Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16739)
*Keisuke Masuda,Kazutaka Yatsushiro,Hirohumi Iwamoto,Hirofumi Hirano,Ryosuke Hanaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个面向日语卒中护理的LLM多轮对话评测基准（Japanese Stroke LLM Evaluation），通过神经外科专家模拟患者与评估，检验18个LLM在病史采集、紧迫性评估及医疗安全方面的实际表现。结果显示，仅两个模型达到安全阈值（≥80%得分且无关键错误），Claude Fable 5表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医学评估多集中于选择题式知识考试（如USMLE），而对临床实践中多轮对话采集病史、紧迫性判断及安全性的评估不足，且日本卒中领域缺乏此类基准。

Method: 构建10例卒中及相关疾病案例；采用多轮日语对话，LLM扮演医生，由认证神经外科医生模拟患者并评分；评估分为病史采集阶段（限制提问数量）和处置行动阶段；预设安全标准为总分≥80%且零关键性错误（critical mistakes）；涵盖2025年10月与2026年6月共18个模型。

Result: Claude Fable 5得分最高（87.4%，零关键错误），Claude Opus 4.7（80.3%）、GLM-5.2（75.6%）次之；仅2个模型通过安全阈值；11个模型出现共17项关键错误，包括未确认化验/血糖即给予t-PA、气道未稳定即手术、遗漏颈部血管评估、超适应症使用t-PA等；病史采集问题数与得分呈正相关（r=0.648, p=0.007）；2026年模型整体表现优于2025年。

Conclusion: Japanese Stroke LLM Evaluation提供了一个面向实践、由专科医生而非LLM-as-judge评估的真实多轮卒中对话基准；模型性能随时间提升，部分已满足安全阈值，但仍需基于真实病例的进一步验证。

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved physician-comparable performance on multiple-choice medical knowledge examinations, but their capabilities in clinical history taking, urgency assessment, and safety remain insufficiently evaluated. We proposed Japanese Stroke LLM Evaluation, a multi-turn conversational benchmark for stroke care in Japanese, and evaluated LLM performance and safety under practice-oriented conditions. Methods: We created 10 stroke and related-condition cases and evaluated LLMs in multi-turn Japanese conversations. The LLM acted as physician, while a board-certified neurosurgeon acted as simulated patient and evaluator. Each case comprised history-taking and action phases scored using pre-specified criteria. Errors that could directly threaten life were defined as critical mistakes. The safety threshold was at least 80% overall with zero critical mistakes. Eighteen models were evaluated in October 2025 and June 2026. Results: Claude Fable 5 achieved the highest score (87.4%) with zero critical mistakes, followed by Claude Opus 4.7 (80.3%) and GLM-5.2 (75.6%). Two leaders met the safety threshold. Eleven models made 17 critical mistakes, including failure to confirm laboratory results or blood glucose before t-PA, surgery before airway stabilization, omission of cervical vascular evaluation, and t-PA outside its indication. History-taking question count correlated with history-taking score (r = 0.648, p = 0.007). Conclusions: Japanese Stroke LLM Evaluation provides a benchmark for LLM performance under practice-oriented conditions, including a cap on history-taking questions. Cases and evaluations were created by neurosurgical specialists rather than using an LLM-as-judge approach. Performance improved across cloud-based and on-premise models in 2026, with some exceeding the safety threshold. Further evaluation using real-world cases is required.

</details>


### [49] [TIAO: Token Importance-Aware Policy Optimization for Text Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16748)
*Qixiu Li,Chenlong Bao,Xiang Zhu,Xiaoyong Li,Ruixin Cao,Shukai Chen,Zhenxiong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TIAO提出了一种面向文本摘要任务的令牌重要性感知策略优化方法，通过源掩码估计令牌对源文档的依赖度，并据此在轨迹级和令牌级重新分配序列级奖励信号，避免GRPO类方法将同一优势广播到所有令牌所导致的梯度稀释问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的文本摘要方法（如GRPO、HVO等）将序列级奖励均匀地分配给所有生成的令牌，忽略了不同令牌在内容保真度方面的重要性差异。这种"信用分配错配"导致：(1) 依赖源文档的事实性内容令牌与仅由语言先验驱动的功能词获得相同的学习压力；(2) 低信息量令牌稀释了有效梯度。已有细粒度RL方法（如过程奖励模型、奖励重分配）主要针对数学推理任务，且依赖额外的奖励模型或过程标注，未能直接解决摘要任务中"输出令牌是否真正由源证据支持"这一核心问题。

Method: TIAO采用双尺度优化框架：
1. **源依赖度估计**：对每个采样轨迹，独立生成源掩码（默认掩码率50%），对比同一输出令牌在完整源与反事实掩码源下的对数概率差 $d_{i,t}=\text{clip}(\ell^M_{i,t}-\ell^F_{i,t},-\tau,\tau)$，并转换为非负重要性分数 $I_{i,t}$。
2. **宏尺度——轨迹优势重塑**：将令牌依赖度聚合为轨迹级源依赖分数 $S_i$，通过批均值归一化得到缩放系数 $\alpha_i$，对组相对优势 $A_i$ 进行保均值缩放 $\tilde{A}_i=\alpha_i A_i$。
3. **微尺度——令牌更新聚焦**：在每个轨迹内，按 $I_{i,t}$ 选取top-κ（默认40%）的有效令牌作为门控掩码 $m_{i,t}$。
4. **最终目标**：在GRPO裁剪替代目标基础上同时应用 $\tilde{A}_i$ 和 $m_{i,t}$，梯度仅流经源敏感令牌集合 $\mathcal{A}_i$，分母仍使用完整完成长度 $T_i$ 以匹配GRPO归一化规则。
该方法无需训练额外的令牌级评论家网络。

Result: 在CNN/DailyMail数据集上使用UniEval四维评估（一致性、连贯性、流畅性、相关性）：
- TIAO（Qwen2.5-7B）总体得分0.960，在一致性（0.946）、流畅性（0.967）、相关性（0.966）三项排名第一，连贯性（0.961）排名第二，STD为0.020。
- 相比Qwen2.5-7B零样本（0.879），TIAO总体提升0.081；相比GRPO、HVO、DAPO、SAPO分别提升0.038、0.017、0.051、0.050。
- 在与GPT-4对比中，一致性、流畅性、相关性及总体均超越GPT-4；7B模型效果优于32B零样本（0.897），表明源感知RL比单纯扩大模型规模更有效。
- 消融实验验证掩码率20%、50%、80%均表现稳健（0.957–0.960），50%为最佳设置。
- 案例分析显示TIAO在训练后期具有更强的梯度信号和更优的整体奖励轨迹。

Conclusion: TIAO通过源掩码驱动的令牌依赖度估计与双尺度信用分配机制，有效解决了RL文本摘要中序列级奖励与令牌级更新之间的信用分配错配问题。实验证明，经过TIAO优化的7B模型在多项摘要质量维度上可比甚至超越GPT-4和GPT-5-nano，且在训练稳定性和跨维度平衡性方面均有优势。该方法不依赖额外的令牌级评论家，为摘要任务的源感知策略优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Text summarization requires models to condense content while preserving key qualities such as consistency and coherence. Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on this task and can be further improved through reinforcement learning (RL). However, most existing methods apply reward signals directly to undifferentiated token sequences, overlooking the varying importance of individual tokens to word and sentence level quality in summarization. In this paper, we propose Token Importance-Aware Policy Optimization (TIAO), a novel reinforcement learning strategy that explicitly leverages token-importance awareness. Specifically, TIAO identifies core tokens based on token dependency and reweights a trajectory's advantage according to its overall dependencies. Experiments on the real world dataset show that our TIAO achieves highly competitive results, and that a 7B foundation model enhanced by TIAO performs comparably to GPT-4 and GPT-5-nano. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [50] [Benchmarking Factual Robustness of LLMs via Multi-conversation Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16777)
*Zhuoang Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAST-IR框架和CP-Agent智能体，用于评估大语言模型在无记忆"冷启动"场景下抵御说服攻击的事实鲁棒性，揭示"拒绝惯性"缺陷与"复杂度悖论"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队框架在多轮对话中评估LLM安全性，目标模型保留完整历史会导致"拒绝惯性"——初次拒绝因上下文一致性而延续，掩盖了模型对独立说服攻击的真实脆弱性。需要一种能严格评估模型"冷启动"防御能力的方法。

Method: 1) 提出SAST-IR（Stateful Attacker, Stateless Target - Iterative Refinement）非对称MDP框架：每轮重置目标模型记忆，仅攻击者保留历史；2) 设计CP-Agent（认知说服智能体），包含Planner、Reflector、Optimizer三模块的Plan-Reflect-Optimize闭环，使用20种心理攻击模式（PAP）分7大类；3) 构建CounterFact-Strict数据集（N=50）并设计5组对照实验消融；4) 使用LLM评判区分PERSUASION与COMPLIANCE。

Result: 在DeepSeek-Chat上：1) 所有组PSR均超90%，G1基线达96%（48/50），暴露记忆缺失防御的严重脆弱性；2) "复杂度悖论"：简单多样化攻击（G1）真实说服率84.7%最高，而复杂的CP-Agent（G5）虽达90%成功率，但更多触发"防御性顺从"（21.4%），实际真实说服仅78.6%；3) 提出"表演性对齐"（Performative Alignment）概念——模型以条件框架（如"在此假设情境下"）表面失败但保持内部对齐。

Conclusion: SAST-IR通过消除历史条件性拒绝惯性，揭示SOTA模型在冷启动说服攻击下高度脆弱；"复杂度悖论"挑战了构建日益复杂红队智能体的趋势，表明简单性和多样性比迭代约束满足更能暴露模型的真实幻觉问题。依赖历史一致性的现有防御机制本质上存在缺陷，需要更鲁棒的内在事实核查机制。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly serve as primary knowledge retrieval interfaces, their robustness against \textit{persuasion attacks}---attempts to inject misinformation or enforce counterfactuals---has become a critical safety concern. Existing red-teaming frameworks typically evaluate models in multi-turn dialogues where the target model retains full conversation history. We identify a critical flaw in this setting termed \textbf{``Refusal Inertia''}: a model's initial refusal often propagates through subsequent turns largely to maintain contextual consistency, thereby masking its true vulnerability to sophisticated, isolated persuasion attempts. To rigorously evaluate the ``cold-start'' defense capabilities of SOTA models, we introduce the \textbf{SAST-IR} (Stateful Attacker, Stateless Target - Iterative Refinement) framework. By enforcing a memory wipe on the target while retaining the attacker's history, we simulate a worst-case adversarial setting using \textbf{multi-turn} (stateless) iterations. Leveraging \textbf{CP-Agent} (Cognitive Persuasion Agent), an enhanced diagnosis-guided agent, our experiments on the custom \textsc{CounterFact-Strict} dataset ($N=50$) yield alarming results: simple, diverse attack strategies achieved a staggering \textbf{96\%} success rate, exposing severe brittleness in memory-less defense. Furthermore, we reveal a \textbf{``Complexity Paradox''}: while complex, iteratively refined attacks are effective, they often trigger defensive compliance, whereas simple strategies achieve a higher rate of genuine persuasion (\textbf{84.7\%}). Our code and dataset are available at GitHub, this https URL .

</details>


### [51] [Smarter by the Moment: Environment-Driven Dynamic Policies for Continual LLM Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16800)
*Ting-Wei Chang,Po-Chun Chen,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于LLM持续改进的动态检索策略生成框架DRPG，通过整合记忆检索与动态策略生成器，将历史正误案例综合成可操作的策略指南，以避免同类错误重复发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强方法（如GrowPrompt、MemPrompt、Self-StreamICL等）仅检索单个历史示例作为参考，未能显式综合可操作策略，导致同类错误反复出现，无法在在线流式环境中实现持续改进。

Method: DRPG框架包含四个组件：(1)智能体基于检索的正确答案生成预测；(2)独立的策略生成器通过对比检索（等量正确与错误案例）综合为最多5条可操作策略要点；(3)智能体结合检索示例与策略生成答案；(4)环境提供二元反馈存储于记忆库。算法采用无状态设计，无需策略连续性，可使用更小或跨族模型作为策略生成器。

Result: 在Spider、CoSQL、BIRD、HotpotQA、DDXPlus、DS-1000六个基准上使用7个LLM（Gemini、Llama、Mistral家族）以及额外的Qwen和Gemma模型进行评估：DRPG在42个模型-数据集配置中29个优于Self-StreamICL基线（单侧Wilcoxon p=0.005显著）；在Text-to-SQL任务上优势最明显（18/21配置胜出），在需实例特定知识的任务（DDXPlus、DS-1000）上与基线持平。消融实验表明策略生成对检索策略、无策略连续性、跨模型策略生成器均具有鲁棒性；无反馈消融证实环境反馈是关键贡献。

Conclusion: DRPG通过显式综合反馈驱动的策略而非仅检索示例，实现了流式环境下的持续LLM改进。其效益取决于任务特性：对于具有系统性、可泛化错误模式的任务（如Text-to-SQL、多跳QA）效果显著，而对于需要实例特定知识或宽泛类别回忆的任务（医学诊断、Python编程）收益有限。该发现为实践者在流式场景中何时部署策略级自适应机制提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress across diverse domains, but continual adaptation to evolving tasks and environments remains a key challenge. Existing memory-augmented approaches retrieve individual past examples as direct references, but do not explicitly synthesize actionable strategies from them, causing the same types of errors to recur. We propose Dynamic Retrieval-based Policy Generation (DRPG), a framework that integrates memory-based retrieval with a dynamic policy generator, leveraging historical data and environment feedback to produce task-specific policies for continual LLM improvement. We evaluate DRPG across six benchmarks spanning text-to-SQL, question answering, medical diagnosis, and Python programming, using seven LLMs from both proprietary and open-weight families. DRPG outperforms strong baselines across most datasets and models. Further analysis demonstrates that DRPG's policy generation is robust to retrieval strategy, operates effectively without prior policy continuity, and can leverage smaller or cross-family models as cost-efficient policy generators. We also find that the benefit of policy-level guidance depends on task characteristics, offering practical insights into when and under what conditions this mechanism is most effective.

</details>


### [52] [A Data-free Universal Prior over Syntactic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16854)
*Ferm\'ın Moscoso del Prado Mart\'ın*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种源自人类渐进式语言产生过程的先验分布（sPA先验），无需任何语言数据拟合即可为依存树分配概率，并在138种语言中一致地为真实句法结构赋予高于随机的概率，与语料库估计的概率在33/34种语言中正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句法结构的概率分布究竟是来自语言经验中的统计学习，还是部分来自独立于语言经验的认知机制（如语言生产过程）？是否存在一种普遍适用的、与具体语言无关的句法结构先验？

Method: 基于渐进式语言产生模型，将依存树的构建视为节点逐步加入并通过次线性优先附着（sublinear preferential attachment, sPA）建立依存边的网络生长过程。对每棵树，通过钩长公式精确计算兼容节点序数（increasing labellings）的数量，并对附着指数α在Beta(2,2)先验下进行边际化（蒙特卡洛估计）。将sPA先验与均匀先验在138种语言的依存树上对比（500棵/语言，最多50词），并在34种语言中通过PCFG估计语料库概率，与sPA概率做相关分析。

Result: sPA先验在全部138种语言中一致地为真实依存树赋予高于均匀先验的概率，且这一优势在所有句子长度上均成立。sPA概率与PCFG概率在33/34种语言中正相关（28种显著），平均R≈0.3；混合效应模型显示β=0.146（P<0.0001）。唯一例外是日语（相关不显著），但其真实树仍优于均匀先验。

Conclusion: 句法结构的概率部分可独立于语言特异性经验产生，源于语言生产的认知约束。语言经验并非从均匀空间中创造结构概率，而是精修一个已由产生过程结构化的普遍先验。这一数据无关的结构偏置可为概率语言模型（包括大语言模型）提供归纳偏置。

Abstract: Probability is fundamental to theories of language comprehension, production, acquisition, and evolution, as well as to large language models. Existing theories estimate the probability of syntactic structures from language-specific data. Whether part of this probability structure can arise independently of language-specific experience remains unknown. Here I show that a universal prior over syntactic structures emerges from a cognitively motivated model of incremental language production, in which words are progressively integrated into syntactic structure through network growth. The resulting prior assigns probabilities to syntactic structures --represented as dependency trees-- without fitting parameters to linguistic data, and assigns higher probabilities to attested than to random trees in all 138 typologically diverse languages examined. These prior probabilities correlate positively with probabilities estimated from corpora in 33 of 34 languages. The results indicate that part of the probability structure of syntax can arise independently of language-specific statistical learning. Linguistic experience may therefore refine probabilities that are already structured by the process of language production, rather than create them from an initially uniform space. This identifies a possible cognitive origin for part of the probability distribution over syntactic structures, linking language production and statistical learning while providing a data-independent structural bias for probabilistic models of language.

</details>


### [53] [Reduplicative constructions in Mandarin: Socio-emotional profiling through distributional semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16860)
*Chaoyi Wu,Yu-Hsiang Tseng,R. Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文使用腾讯词嵌入（200维）对普通话AABB和ABAB两类重叠构式进行分布语义学研究，提取1010个重叠词并与2173个非重叠参照词对比，揭示其语义结构、语用功能及与基词的语义透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通话重叠构式具有复数化、价彩、声音象征和语用功能等多重语义，传统描述"令人眼花缭乱"，难以精确刻画。本研究旨在探索高维词嵌入能否恢复语言学既有概括，并精细量化重叠构式的语义多样性。

Method: 从CCL语料库提取1010个四字重叠词（AABB=520, ABAB=490），匹配955对重叠-基词对，使用200维腾讯词嵌入表征；采用k-means聚类（LDA留一交叉验证评估）、t-SNE可视化、范畴定义向量（CDV）进行语义画像（包括词类、效价、自我相关性、声音义）、移位向量分析移位类型、匈牙利匹配+Procrustes对齐评估语义透明度。

Result: 重叠构式在嵌入空间中形成10个可区分聚类（LDA准确率98.7%区分于参照词，90.5%区分聚类）；AABB与ABAB可高准确分类（96.44%），两者在效价、自我相关性、声音义上呈现系统性差异：ABAB偏动词性、互动性、自我相关；AABB偏复数性、强化积极情感。识别出七类语义移位（强化、反复、试探、积极情感、复数、立场强化、拟声）。Procrustes分析显示基词空间与重叠空间全局结构高度一致（r=0.9336），但局部不对称揭示话语-语用重组区域（聚类6、8、1、10、4透明度较低，聚类3、5、7透明度较高）。

Conclusion: 高维词嵌入能够有效恢复并量化普通话重叠构式的语义系统性与多样性，将传统描述的复杂语义整合到统一的高维语义空间中；移位向量和Procrustes对齐为构式透明度提供了新的量化途径；静态嵌入的局限性提示未来可结合上下文嵌入进一步研究。

Abstract: Mandarin Chinese has two productive reduplicative constructions that repeat either two-character base words or their constituents (e.g., `in good health', `discuss a bit'). Their varied meanings have been described as realizing plurality, valence coloring, sound symbolism and pragmatic functions. The aim of this study is twofold. A first goal is to clarify whether it is possible to come to a more precise understanding of the variegated semantics of Mandarin reduplication by using word embeddings from distributional semantics. A second goal is to explore how useful embeddings are for understanding the details of a semantically complex word-formation process. We show that the embedding space recovers the semantic and grammatical properties of reduplications previously identified in the literature, validating Tencent embeddings for morphological investigation. Semantic profiling revealed that reduplicative constructions are often strongly represented on multiple dimensions. The two patterns exhibit clear semantic and pragmatic differentiation in distributional space. Procrustes analysis clarified that the overall organization of the base-word space is largely preserved in the reduplication space, with local mismatches highlighting regions of discourse-pragmatic reorganization. Taken together, these results show that high-dimensional word embeddings can recover established linguistic generalizations, and capture the semantic versatility of Mandarin reduplication and constructional transparency.

</details>


### [54] [Cascade: Hierarchical Recoverability Control for Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16890)
*Qingchen Yu,Shiying Duan,Xiaodong Li,Yuhua Wang,Zhiyu Li,Shiji Zhou,Yifan Sun,Zhaoxin Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Cascade框架，将LLM遗忘重新定义为对目标知识"内部可识别性"的层级化约束最小化问题，通过路径级路由抑制、超双曲表示压缩和解码级干预三个互补控制，在TOFU、MUSE-News、WMDP等基准上有效降低目标知识的可恢复性，同时保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法主要通过抑制目标答案的输出概率来评估遗忘效果，但忽视了关键失败模式：目标知识在直接生成中可能被抑制，但仍可在语义改写、间接提问或提取式提示下通过内部激活路径、可分离表征和可解码输出被恢复。这种"内部可识别性"残留下显著隐私与安全风险。

Method: 将隐私遗忘建模为受约束的内部可识别性最小化问题，并通过三个层级化代理损失联合优化：(1) 路径级路由——通过遗忘-保留激活差异定位隐私相关模块(EMA平滑+Top-K选择)，用softplus边界损失抑制选定路径的过度遗忘激活；(2) 表示级压缩——将选定路径的表征通过固定的投影头映射到Poincaré双曲球，利用径向几何将遗忘表征推向低半径区域以降低几何可分性；(3) 解码级干预——结合遗忘目标NLL提升和保留目标参考的softplus边界损失，限制输出层的残差恢复。整体目标附加保留集语言建模损失作为效用约束。

Result: 在TOFU(Forget01/05/10)、MUSE-News和WMDP-Cyber三个基准上，使用Llama-3.2(1B/3B)、Qwen3(1.7B/4B)和Gemma-3-4B多个骨干模型进行实验。在TOFU Forget10上，Cascade在Llama-3.2-3B和Qwen3-4B上均取得最高CFI分数(0.7165/0.7481)和竞争性BUS(0.6616/0.5231)；在查询改写鲁棒性测试中，平均ASR仅0.10%，R-ROUGE仅0.043；在WMDP上将网络安全准确率从40.36降至23.60同时保持MMLU为63.75。消融实验表明去除任一组件都会导致BUS显著下降(去除解码级损失下降约64%)，机制分析证实了选定路径在不同采样间的稳定性和遗忘表征向低双曲半径的压缩趋势。

Conclusion: Cascade通过路径级、表示级和解码级三层协同控制，有效降低了目标知识的内部可识别性，而非仅抑制输出级概率。实验证明该方法在多种基准、模型家族和查询改写下均能稳定降低目标可恢复性并保持模型效用，表明有效的LLM遗忘需要结构化控制目标知识的可识别性，而非输出层调整。但其评估限于离线设定和固定遗忘集，对动态、开放式、交互式遗忘场景的适用性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) unlearning is essential for removing sensitive or copyrighted knowledge while preserving general utility. Existing methods often leave residual knowledge in intermediate representations, which can still be recovered. To address this, we propose Cascade, a hierarchical recoverability control framework that minimizes the internal identifiability of target knowledge. Cascade combines three complementary controls: path-level routing to suppress privacy-associated activation routes, representation-level compression to reduce geometric separability, and decoding-level intervention to limit residual recovery. Experiments on TOFU, MUSE-News, and WMDP, including robustness tests with query reformulation and extraction-style prompts, show that Cascade effectively reduces recoverability while maintaining stable model utility.

</details>


### [55] [RiskChainBench: A Benchmark for Obfuscated Platform Message Restoration and Evidence-Grounded Web Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16900)
*ZhuoXin Liu,Zhiming Ma,Ying Zhang,Mengzheng Yang,Yifan Wang,Zhengqi Huang,Yanhan Zhou,Zekun Lin,Jun Zhang,Shun Zhang,Yue Chen,Qiao Zhao,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RiskChainBench是首个将混淆平台消息恢复与证据驱动网页调查耦合的基准，通过"入口门控"机制连接两个阶段，评估10个模型在文本恢复和网页风险调查上的表现，揭示执行稳定性是当前主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将混淆文本恢复与风险网页调查分开评估，无法暴露"目标恢复"错误如何影响下游证据获取。需要一个跨阶段基准来评估平台滥用场景中的端到端调查能力。

Method: 构建3,600条合成token-text恢复输入（600源会话×6变体）和600个人工标注的本地网页环境。模型先恢复消息、操作意图和入口，再作为VLM驱动的网页代理调查正确关联的网站，输出带证据引用的风险报告。采用固定主变体的入口预测作为离线门控，与网页调查结果组合评分。

Result: 在10个模型上：入口Top-1范围35.2%-95.2%，网页决策准确率26.3%-62.8%，门控端到端准确率16.7%-60.8%。领先系统在入口恢复、全重建、网站决策和细粒度分类上表现各异。31.9%的网页运行因执行失败，决策后类型错误仅占0.9%，表明稳定探索是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: RiskChainBench揭示了入口恢复与网页调查在隔离沙箱中的耦合关系，指出当前模型在可靠探索方面存在明显不足。未来工作应测量重运行方差、扩展欠充分的风险类型和语言，并通过冻结的人类轨迹审计验证证据评估标准。

Abstract: Platform abuse campaigns conceal redirection instructions with emojis, homophones, character decomposition, and redundant symbols, then route users through disguised links to services associated with pornography, fraud, gambling, or illicit transactions. Existing benchmarks evaluate obfuscated text and risky webpages separately, obscuring how target recovery affects downstream evidence acquisition. We introduce RiskChainBench, pairing 3,600 synthetic token-text restoration inputs from 600 source sessions with 600 corresponding human-labeled local web environments. A model first restores the message, operational intent, and destination; the same underlying model then acts as a VLM-driven web agent that investigates the correctly associated website and produces a frozen, evidence-cited risk report without message-side semantics or domain-reputation cues. We score restoration and correct-routing web investigation separately and compose them offline by applying the frozen primary-entry prediction as a gate to the same Task 2 result. Human labels determine task correctness, while a fixed multimodal evidence judge assesses faithfulness, sufficiency, completeness, and consistency. Across ten models, Entry Top-1 ranges from 35.2% to 95.2% and web decision accuracy from 26.3% to 62.8%; the leading systems differ across entry recovery, full reconstruction, website decisions, and fine-grained typing. Execution failures account for 31.9% of web runs, whereas post-decision type errors account for only 0.9%, identifying stable exploration and risk judgment as the principal bottlenecks. We release the benchmark, protocol, and resettable local sandbox.

</details>


### [56] [Deconstructing Stereotypes: Scope-Conditioned Generation for Effective Multilingual Counterspeech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16906)
*Greta Damo,Elias Urios Alacreu,Elena Cabrio,Paolo Rosso,Serena Villata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于范围条件生成（scope-conditioned generation）的框架，通过将结构化的刻板印象特征（泛化范围与归因特征类型）显式整合到大型语言模型的提示中，以生成针对仇恨言论背后隐含刻板印象的多语言反驳话语。作者构建了一个涵盖英语、意大利语和西班牙语的人工标注数据集，并通过自动评估、LLM-as-a-Judge 和人工评估三种方式验证了该框架在事实性、针对性、说服力和论证力等维度上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动反驳话语（Counterspeech）生成方法往往产生通用、无效的回复，未能针对仇恨言论背后隐含的刻板印象。传统的"隐含陈述（Implied Statement, IS）"形式化方法过于简化，忽略了泛化范围（如本质主义 vs. 多数派）和归因特征类型（如道德威胁 vs. 生理贬损）等结构维度，导致生成的反驳话语与仇恨言论的核心意图不对齐。

Method: 1. 提出细粒度的刻板印象标注模式，涵盖四个维度：仇恨言论的隐含性、刻板印象的存在性、泛化范围（普遍/多数/本质主义）、归因特征类型（智力低下/道德犯罪威胁/经济寄生/生理贬损/存在与文化威胁/文化落后）。2. 基于 Multilingual-MTCONAN-KN 数据集构建英、意、西三语人工标注数据集。3. 设计四种提示条件进行对比：仅仇恨言论（A）、仇恨言论+IS（B）、仇恨言论+标注（C）、仇恨言论+IS+标注（D）。4. 使用多个开源 LLM（Ministral、Llama、EuroLLM、Salamandra、Llamantino）进行生成。5. 通过自动指标（METEOR、BERTScore、NLI）、LLM-as-a-Judge（Qwen、Ministral、Llama 三模型平均）和人工评估进行多维度评价。

Result: 在英、意、西三语的实验中，条件 C 和 D（含刻板印象结构信息）在事实性、针对性、说服力和论证力上均显著优于基线 A 和仅含 IS 的条件 B。NLI 矛盾分数在 C 和 D 条件下大幅提升（如英语中近乎翻倍）。人工偏好投票中，条件 D 在所有语言中获得最多票数。Ministral 在三语中表现最佳；单语模型（Llamantine、Salamandra）并未超越多语言模型。该方法对显式和隐式刻板印象均有效，且具有跨语言可迁移性。

Conclusion: 本文证明，显式建模刻板印象的结构特征（泛化范围与归因特征类型）能显著提升多语言反驳话语的质量，使其更具针对性、事实性和说服力。刻板印象条件生成框架在显式与隐式刻板印象场景下均有效，并可跨语言迁移，为多语言 CS 生成提供了独立于语言的指导信号。

Abstract: Counterspeech (CS) - direct responses that counter online Hate Speech (HS) using reasoning and alternative viewpoints - has emerged as an alternative to content removal. Current automatic CS generation methods, however, frequently produce generic, ineffective replies that fail to target the implicit stereotypes behind HS. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel scope-conditioned generation framework that explicitly integrates structured stereotype characteristics into Large Language Models prompts. We validate our approach on a novel, human-curated dataset annotated in English, Italian, and Spanish. Extensive evaluations show that stereotype-conditioned prompting substantially outperforms generic baselines across all three languages, obtaining significant gains in factuality, specificity, cogency, and effectiveness for both explicit and implicit implied stereotypes.

</details>


### [57] [Lit3R: Retrieve-Relate-Read for Evidence-Grounded Question Answering over Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16912)
*Akira Ise,Kotaro Kumagai,Yuta Yamaguchi,Hisanori Ozaki,Yukio Uematsu,Ikuya Yamada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lit3R系统，一个无需任务特定训练的三阶段文献问答流水线（Retrieve–Relate–Read），在LitTraceQA共享任务中排名第四。其核心贡献是引入"Relate"步骤，通过论文间扩展（语义嵌入、文献耦合、BM25）补全问题锚定检索难以发现的关联论文，使多论文问题的recall@50从0.647提升至0.940。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LitTraceQA任务仅提供55条标注验证问题，难以支持任务特定训练；且许多多论文问题涉及的关联论文与问题文本在词汇或语义上距离较远，单纯的问题锚定检索难以召回。因此需要一个无需训练、且能通过论文间关系扩展候选集的模块化系统。

Method: 系统分为两大部分：(1) 检索器构建两个排名并融合——Ranking A基于问题，通过查询分解（GPT-5.4）、BM25+密集检索混合（Qwen3-Embedding-8B）、伪相关反馈、Qwen3-Reranker-8B重排序和LLM证据验证进行最多三轮迭代；Ranking B从Ranking A的top论文和验证证据论文出发，使用SPECTER2嵌入相似、文献耦合（Jaccard≥2共享引用）、BM25三种信号进行论文到论文扩展；两者通过RRF（k=10）融合取top50。(2) 阅读器采用两阶段处理：先逐篇本地阅读判断相关性和证据，再跨论文综合生成最终答案及证据追溯。所有组件均为现成模型，无需任务训练。

Result: 验证集上，retriever最终recall@50为0.968（单论文1.000，多论文0.940）；Ranking B将多论文recall@50从0.647提升至0.940。官方测试集：论文检索macro F1=0.992，证据接地macro F1=0.737，选择题准确率1.000，表格答案行F1=0.529、单元格准确率0.309（micro）/0.332（macro）。系统排名第四。

Conclusion: 论文证明无需任务训练的Retrieve–Relate–Read流水线在文献问答任务上有效；Relate步骤显著提升多论文检索召回；系统在论文检索和选择题上表现优异，但表格问答仍是薄弱环节。未来需扩展至其他领域并分析各组件对端到端性能的贡献。

Abstract: We describe tus-nlp's Lit3R (Retrieve-Relate-Read) system for LitTraceQA, a shared task for literature-grounded question answering that requires systems to retrieve relevant papers, identify supporting evidence, and generate answers. Lit3R combines off-the-shelf retrieval, reranking, and large language model (LLM) components without task-specific training. The retriever iteratively combines BM25-based sparse and dense retrieval, cross-encoder reranking, and LLM-based verification, and complements retrieval based on the question with paper-to-paper expansion. The reader first identifies supporting evidence within individual papers and then synthesizes evidence across papers to produce the final answer and evidence trace. On the official test set, our system ranked 4th on the leaderboard. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [58] [Target-Language Generation in Multilingual Models: Activation Steering and Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16967)
*James A. Michaelov,Carmen Amo Alonso,Tyler A. Chang,Roger P. Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于最优控制的LiSeCo激活导向方法，用于多语言模型的目标语言生成，并构建了融合语言遵循、语言学连贯性和语义连贯性的评估框架。实验表明该方法在大多数模型上表现优于或相当于差值均值（DiffMeans）方法，且所需的超参数调优显著更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言语言模型在生成目标语言文本时存在语言漂移问题，尤其是在长文本生成或推理过程中，模型容易偏向高资源语言（如英语）。现有激活导向方法（如DiffMeans、ActAdd）通常使用固定幅度的干预，缺乏理论保证，且对超参数敏感。本文旨在通过最优控制框架解决这些问题，实现更可靠、可预测的目标语言生成。

Method: 1. 评估框架：提出"adhered coherence"综合指标，包含语言遵循（基于fastText分类器）、语义连贯性（基于LaBSE嵌入相似度比较）、语言学连贯性（基于n-gram困惑度）三个维度。2. 理论框架：将Transformer层间激活流动建模为离散动力系统，将目标语言生成形式化为激活空间中的最优控制问题，要求干预满足最小扰动、保证语言转换、拓扑感知三个条件。3. 方法实现：基于LiSeCo（线性语义控制），通过训练轻量级线性探针识别各层的语言特定区域，将干预设计为最小化扰动使激活落入目标区域的二次规划问题，获得闭式解。该干预取决于当前激活状态，动态计算所需扰动幅度。4. 实验设计：使用跨语言XStoryCloze任务，对Llama 3.2 1B进行探索性实验，在8个模型（BLOOM 7.1B、EuroLLM 9B、Gemma-3 1B、Llama-3.1 8B、Llama-3.2 3B、Qwen-2.5 7B、Qwen-3 8B、Tiny-Aya-Base）上进行目标性实验，比较单层DiffMeans与多层LiSeCo。

Result: 1. LiSeCo在8个模型中的5个（BLOOM、Llama 3.1、Llama 3.2、Qwen 2.5、Aya）上优于模型调优的DiffMeans；Qwen 3上两者无显著差异；EuroLLM和Gemma 3上DiffMeans更优。2. 单一LiSeCo设置（除静态嵌入层外所有层，α_inner=10^-8）在多数模型上表现最佳，而DiffMeans对超参数更敏感。3. 存在不对称性：所有模型都能被导向生成英语，但EuroLLM和Gemma难以从英语被导向其他语言；Qwen 3在Hindi目标上DiffMeans更优，而Llama模型相反。4. 性能差异部分源于分类器质量，LiSeCo性能依赖于语言分类器能否识别因果相关的语言特定区域。

Conclusion: 本文为激活导向实现目标语言生成提供了理论基础和实用方法。LiSeCo-based方法相较于现有方法具有理论保证（最小扰动、保证落入目标区域）、动态适应性（基于当前激活状态计算干预）和拓扑感知（沿决策边界正交方向移动）三大优势。实证结果表明该方法在多数模型上表现优于DiffMeans，且超参数鲁棒性显著更优。研究也揭示了激活导向方法对线性探针质量的依赖性，未来可通过改进分类器进一步提升性能。该工作为多语言模型可控生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Ensuring that multilingual language models generate coherent text in a specific target language is a major issue in multilingual language modeling. We develop an optimal control method for target-language text generation as well as a framework for evaluating the quality of generated text in terms of language adherence, linguistic coherence, and semantic coherence. We find that the proposed method performs at least as well as the prominent difference-in-means activation steering method for the majority of models tested, with substantially less hyperparameter tuning required.

</details>


### [59] [Nameless Tokenization: A Lossless Tokenizer-Level Defense Against Control-Token Forgery in Open-Weight LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16984)
*Kisu Yang,Yoonna Jang,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Open-weight language models publish the strings their chat templates use to mark turns, roles and tool results, which the tokenizer maps back to the reserved identifiers the model obeys. Anyone who controls text in a prompt can therefore write a turn boundary indistinguishable from one the serving stack wrote. We audit 256 deployed chat tokenizers. All are forgeable, and the flag usually recommended as a fix leaves 56.6% forgeable because it misses the tool and reasoning markers agent systems rely on. We propose nameless tokenization, which leaves the control entries with a reserved identifier and no surface string, so the content encoder cannot emit one and message content reaches the model unaltered. Across five tokenizer families it reproduces the standard token stream exactly on attack-free data and lifts accuracy on a probe of delimiter-bearing text from 8.5% to 59.9%, where sanitizers lose it. Separating a delimiter's appearance from its identifier shows the identifier matters little against a bare task instruction, but carries most of a forged tool result and most of any forged turn once the system message tells the model to treat user content as data.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (incomplete_output). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (incomplete_output). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: Open-weight language models publish the strings their chat templates use to mark turns, roles and tool results, which the tokenizer maps back to the reserved identifiers the model obeys. Anyone who controls text in a prompt can therefore write a turn boundary indistinguishable from one the serving stack wrote. We audit 256 deployed chat tokenizers. All are forgeable, and the flag usually recommended as a fix leaves 56.6% forgeable because it misses the tool and reasoning markers agent systems rely on. We propose nameless tokenization, which leaves the control entries with a reserved identifier and no surface string, so the content encoder cannot emit one and message content reaches the model unaltered. Across five tokenizer families it reproduces the standard token stream exactly on attack-free data and lifts accuracy on a probe of delimiter-bearing text from 8.5% to 59.9%, where sanitizers lose it. Separating a delimiter's appearance from its identifier shows the identifier matters little against a bare task instruction, but carries most of a forged tool result and most of any forged turn once the system message tells the model to treat user content as data.

</details>


### [60] [Autoformalizing Argumentative Material Inferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16991)
*Xin Quan,Reto Gubelmann,André Freitas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出GUARD框架，将论证性材料推理的自动形式化建模为"守卫补全"问题，通过LLM构建候选守卫、Isabelle/HOL验证，并结合对比性忠诚度检查，有效避免泄漏问题，在Debatepedia和ARCT上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言论证的合理性依赖于隐含的warrant、背景承诺和例外条件。形式化验证需要显式构造这些缺失的承诺，但赋予系统添加前提的自由会带来风险：可通过将答案直接写入守卫、削弱结论或绕过原始前提来制造形式有效证明。论文旨在解决论证性材料推理自动形式化中的"显式化鸿沟"与忠诚度保证问题。

Method: GUARD是一个神经符号框架，包含四个阶段：(1) 上下文强化——基于Toulmin模型从前提-结论对中提取假设(A)、定义/术语规范化(Δ)和例外条件(X)三类守卫候选；(2) 软验证与精炼——LLM将守卫形式化为Neo-Davidsonian一阶逻辑，critic函数针对PO1-PO4四个证明义务（表示正确性、支持充分性、严格支持、依赖性）筛选并迭代精炼；(3) 硬验证与精炼——构建Isabelle/HOL理论，利用Sledgehammer定理证明器验证，失败证明步骤作为反馈精炼；(4) 忠诚度检查——通过前提对比(A1)和结论对比(A2)对比测试防止泄漏。系统输出四种状态：Verified-Faithful、Reject-Leaky、Abstain-Missing-Guards、Abstain-Not-Compatible。

Result: 在Debatepedia(247实例)和ARCT(200实例)上使用GPT-5.1、Qwen3-Max、DeepSeek-V3.2、Mistral Medium 3.5四个骨干LLM测试。GUARD平均VFR达61.13(Debatepedia)和65.00(ARCT)，比最强基线PEIRCE分别提升35.3和32.9点；泄漏率降低25.9和21.9点。GPT-5.1下VFR达76.52%和81.00%。消融实验表明软符号批评贡献最大(VFR下降40.5/39.5点)，其次是假设层(下降23.5/31.5点)；扩展预算探针显示63.5%-74.3%的不决案例属于"缺失守卫"而非真正不兼容。

Conclusion: 论文将论证性材料推理的自动形式化重构为守卫补全问题，通过显式构造守卫集和对比性忠诚度检查解决了"形式有效但忠诚"的核心难题。GUARD框架在多个骨干LLM和数据集上一致优于现有LLM驱动的定理证明方法，证明了软硬批评结合的神经符号方法在保持论证忠诚度的同时提升形式有效性的有效性。未来工作将扩展守卫补全至更广泛领域的材料推理。

Abstract: Natural language arguments are compelling before they are formally explicit. A premise supports a claim through defeasible warrants, background commitments, and exception conditions that the text leaves implicit. However, formal verification requires the opposite. Making such arguments machine-checkable requires constructing the missing commitments, not only translating given sentences into logic. Construction, however, carries a risk that translation does not: a system free to add premises can make any claim provable, and a formally valid proof may assert the claim outright, prove it without the original premise, or establish more than the claim itself. We address this problem by formulating autoformalization for argumentative material inference as guard completion, in which non-monotonic material support is turned into monotonic formal inference relative to an explicitly constructed guard set. A completion is accepted only when its proof both passes the theorem prover and survives contrastive tests of premise dependence and claim selectivity. We implement this formulation in GUARD, a neuro-symbolic framework in which LLMs construct and formalize candidate guards, Isabelle/HOL verifies the resulting theories and returns step-level feedback for iterative refinement, and the system abstains when no faithful completion can be reached. Our empirical results on Debatepedia and ARCT using different LLMs demonstrate that GUARD yields significant improvements in verified-faithful (+35.3, +32.9 points) and substantial reductions in leakage (-25.9, -21.9 points) over the state-of-the-art LLM-driven theorem proving approach. Moreover, we show that the symbolic soft critique and the explicit assumption layer account for most of these gains, with the soft critique also improving the initial validity of the elicited context and reducing the number of iterations required for successful verification.

</details>


### [61] [Can LLMs Follow the Pulse of a Crisis? Evaluating Crisis Sentiment in Bangladesh's July Uprising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16997)
*Md. Samiul Alim,Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Sharjil Khan,Rafia Ferdous Duti,Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源语言孟加拉语（Bangla）在危机情境下的情感分析难题，构建了约20万条的大规模数据集UnrestSent200K，涵盖2024年孟加拉国7-8月起义相关的Facebook和YouTube评论，按五个事件阶段划分，并链接父级帖子以支持上下文感知评估。通过全人工标注流程（14名母语者，kappa=0.73）保证质量，并系统比较了微调编码器、提示型大模型和LoRA微调大模型，揭示了父级上下文、时序漂移和讽刺/隐喻政治表达对危机情感分析的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有孟加拉语情感数据集（如SentNoB、SentiGOLD）多为静态、去语境化、面向消费类文本，无法反映危机期间公众语言快速变化的特点；且大多数情感基准假设语言稳定、忽略上下文，难以评估模型在真实危机场景中的可靠性。2024年7-8月孟加拉国起义提供了一个罕见的"单事件内多阶段"自然压力测试场景，亟需支持上下文感知与时序鲁棒评估的新基准。

Method: 1. 数据采集：从Facebook Graph API、YouTube Data API v3和Apify平台采集2024年7月5日-8月31日的公开帖子和评论，按5个事件阶段（P1前升级、P2危机与断网、P3断网后、P4革命后、P5洪水危机）划分。2. 预处理：Unicode NFC标准化、实体掩码、emoji保留、Jaccard相似度去重。3. 人工标注：Pilot Label Annotation (PiLA)五阶段框架——指南试点、标注者培训、双标注、资深裁定、盲审；14名母语本科生+10名资深验证者。4. 模型评估：4种微调编码器（BanglaBERT、mBERT、XLM-R、BanglaElectra）、15种LLM的zero/few-shot提示、2种LoRA微调LLM（Gemma-4B、LLaMA-3.1-8B）。5. 主题建模：BERTopic（多语言句向量+UMAP+HDBSCAN）用于错误分析与时序主题漂移。6. 评估维度：上下文消融、时序泛化、错误模式分析、主题级情感不稳定性。

Result: 1. 数据集：199,399条标注评论，标签分布为47.6%负面、31.5%正面、21.0%中性；标注一致性kappa=0.73、alpha=0.71，盲审一致率94.2%。2. 最佳模型：LoRA微调Gemma-4B达78.4%准确率和77.6宏F1，优于GPT-4o-mini few-shot（76.0%）。3. 父级上下文显著提升性能：所有编码器获得+7至+11百分点的准确率提升。4. 时序漂移导致严重退化：在早期数据训练、后期数据测试时F1下降19-28点。5. 情感变化非渐进而呈阶段性跃迁：断网后阶段（P3）出现中等效应量变化（d=-0.556），洪水危机阶段（d=0.567）。6. 失败模式：错误集中在政治主题（配额改革等），中性评论被系统性误判为负面（占错误32.1%），且平均错误置信度高达0.977，呈现"高置信度错误"。7. 危机一致性悖论：断网期间主题数虽多（29个）但语义最聚焦（Cv=0.781）。

Conclusion: 可靠的低资源危机情感分析需要同时考虑话语上下文与时序适应性。父级帖子不是可选的背景信息，而是情感信号本身的一部分；随机划分会高估真实可靠性，因为事件进入新阶段时模型会急剧失效。大型语言模型和强提示策略虽有帮助，但无法消除对语境锚定和时序校准的需求。UnrestSent200K为研究上下文感知、时序鲁棒和话题级置信度校准的危机情感分析模型提供了可控基准，呼吁未来工作开发具备变化点感知能力的话题级时序适应模型。

Abstract: Crisis sentiment analysis is especially challenging for low-resource languages such as Bangla, where language, context, and public reaction shift rapidly. We introduce UNRESTSENT200K, a Bangla crisis sentiment dataset with approximately 200K Facebook and YouTube comments from the July-August 2024 Bangladesh uprising. The dataset covers five event-aligned phases, from early escalation and internet blackout to regime transition and a later flood crisis. Each comment is linked to its parent post, enabling evaluation with and without discourse context. All comments are annotated through a fully human process involving 14 native Bangla-speaking annotators and senior validation, achieving substantial agreement (kappa = 0.73, alpha = 0.71) and 94.2% blind-audit agreement. We benchmark fine-tuned encoders, prompted LLMs, and LoRA-tuned LLMs. Results show that parent-post context consistently improves performance, while temporal shift across phases causes large performance drops. Strong LLMs perform well, but still struggle with sarcasm, implicit political references, and phase-dependent meaning. UNRESTSENT200K provides a benchmark for studying context-aware and temporally robust sentiment analysis in low-resource crisis discourse. UNRESTSENT200K is available at this https URL

</details>


### [62] [EviScope: Paired Counterfactual Evidence Diagnostics for Faithful and Efficient Grounded Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17081)
*Suryadeep Singh Deswal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EviScope 是一个配对反事实证据评估基准，通过固定问题、改变证据上下文的四元组（充分/噪声/不足/冲突）来诊断 RAG 系统的真实证据感知能力，揭示仅靠答案准确率无法发现的"无依据作答""冲突盲目""错误弃答"等失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG/接地 LM 评测多只看最终答案正确性，掩盖了"答对但来源错误""忽略证据""证据不足或冲突时仍作答"等不同失败模式，需要一种能区分这些行为的细粒度诊断方法。

Method: 基于 SQuAD 构建 EviScope-v1.1：40 个基准问题 × 4 种证据变体 = 160 例；提出四元组一致性 QCS 及配对指标 ERS/NR/CS 与接地效用；用确定性评估器（无需 LLM 评判）；对比 Vanilla RAG 与显式"证据-动作门"两种策略，在 Qwen2.5-7B、Llama 3.1 8B、Gemini 3.5 Flash 上进行 960 次盲生成测试，配对 bootstrap + McNemar 检验。

Result: 三个模型在 160 例上得到完整配对结果：本地模型上"更复杂"的证据-动作门策略反而劣于 Vanilla RAG（Qwen QCS 0.15 vs 0.50，Llama 0.10 vs 0.375），Gemini 达到 0.944 联合成功率但仍有 5% 冲突盲目率；说明更强模型降低但不消除冲突盲目，提示工程策略效果模型依赖。

Conclusion: 接地语言模型评测应报告跨证据变体的配对行为指标而非孤立答案/引用分数；EviScope 提供了一个紧凑、可复现、可自动化的诊断基准，区分"无依据作答""冲突盲目""错误弃答"等独立失败模式。

Abstract: Grounded language-model systems are often evaluated by final answer accuracy, yet a correct answer can be unsupported, drawn from the wrong source, or produced when evidence is insufficient or contradictory. We introduce EviScope, a paired counterfactual benchmark that holds the question fixed while adding, removing, distracting, or contradicting its evidence. EviScope-v1.1 contains 40 four-condition quartets with repaired counterfactual claims and span-level support labels for automatic evaluation. Across 960 gold-blind generations from Qwen2.5-7B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Gemini 3.5 Flash, paired metrics expose model-dependent grounding behavior that answer accuracy hides. On two local open models, an explicit evidence-action gate underperforms vanilla RAG on QCS: 0.15 vs. 0.50 for Qwen and 0.10 vs. 0.375 for Llama. Gemini reaches 0.944 joint success under both prompts, yet still answers 5% of conflict cases after contradiction insertion. EviScope therefore distinguishes unsupported answering, conflict blindness, and wrong non-answer actions rather than scoring answers alone.

</details>


### [63] [An Empirical Study of Counterfactual Self-Explanations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17119)
*Giannis Kalyvas,Giorgos Filandrianos,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Vassilis Lyberatos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过反事实自解释框架，系统研究了10个LLaMA-3和Qwen-2.5指令微调模型在情感分析和自然语言推理任务上的解释忠实性，发现模型规模是决定解释质量的最关键因素，而提示策略可能在不提升忠实性的情况下让人为解释看起来更可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常被要求为自身输出生成自然语言解释，但这些"自解释"是否忠实于模型真实行为存疑。已有研究分别考察了反事实解释的忠实性、提示影响及规模效应，但缺乏联合分析模型规模、忠实性、最小性与人类一致性之间关系的工作。

Method: 采用三阶段反事实生成流水线（原始分类→生成反事实→重新分类），在Movie Reviews和e-SNLI两个ERASER基准任务上评估10个开源指令微调模型（1B到72B+）。对比三种提示策略：Baseline、Chat-History、Reasoning-Guided（理由引导）。从三个维度评估：忠实性（flip rate）、最小性（编辑距离与语义相似度）、人类一致性（提出新指标ESMP即Evidence-Supported Modification Precision，衡量修改是否落在人类标注证据范围内）。

Result: 1）模型规模是决定性因素：规模与忠实性Spearman相关系数ρ=0.87，与ESMP ρ=0.78；规模每翻倍，忠实性提升约12个百分点；2）三种提示中Chat-History忠实性最高（20个设置中12个最优），而Reasoning-Guided产生最小且最人类对齐的解释但忠实性下降；3）不同任务结构有差异——Movies任务中更大模型能同时改善忠实性、ESMP与语义相似度，而e-SNLI中语义相似度未见同步提升。

Conclusion: 更强的模型不仅生成更忠实、修改自身预测的自解释，还更能定位与人类推理一致的决策证据。然而提示策略可能扭曲自解释，使其在人类对齐指标下显得更好却不更忠实。因此反事实自解释可作为有用的行为证据，但其可靠性高度依赖模型容量，不应未经实证验证就被视为内部推理的直接反映。

Abstract: Large language models can easily generate explanations for their own outputs, but such self-explanations are not necessarily faithful to the model's behavior. We study this issue through counterfactual self-explanations, where a model minimally edits an input so that its own prediction changes. Across sentiment analysis and natural language inference, we evaluate ten instruction-tuned models from the LLaMA-3 and Qwen-2.5 families, measuring faithfulness, minimality, and alignment with human-annotated rationales. Our results show that model scale is the strongest determinant of explanation quality: larger models are substantially more likely to generate counterfactuals that flip their own predictions and target decision-relevant evidence. In contrast, the rationale-guided condition produces edit-minimal counterfactuals that are also more human-aligned. However, it does not consistently improve faithfulness. Overall, counterfactual self-explanations can provide useful behavioral evidence about model decisions, but their reliability depends strongly on model capacity and should be empirically validated rather than assumed.

</details>


### [64] [Psychological Effects of Cultural Upheavals from Millions of Song Lyrics Over 100 Years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17225)
*David M. Markowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了数百万首歌曲的歌词，考察了越南战争、9/11恐怖袭击和COVID-19这三次重大文化动荡对个体和集体语言行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化动荡深刻影响社会生活的诸多方面，现有研究多关注其对语言模式的宏观影响，但鲜有研究在大规模流行媒体语料中分离出动荡对个体层面（而非群体层面）的语言影响。

Method: 对跨越一个多世纪的数百万首歌曲歌词进行定量文本分析，以五年基线为对照，检验三次重大文化动荡（越南战争、9/11事件、COVID-19）前后歌词中的语言变化。具体测量指标包括：自我指代词（心理距离的标记）、认知加工类词汇（意义建构的指示器），以及内容主题（如"生活与自由"vs."求偶与夜生活"）的分布。

Result: 与五年基线相比，越战和9/11事件之后，歌词中的自我指代率显著下降（心理距离缩短）；动荡后认知加工类词汇较之前上升，表明艺术家更努力从巨大冲击中建构意义；内容模式上，动荡后艺术家更多书写"生活与自由"（社会状况），更少涉及"求偶与夜生活"（人际连接）。

Conclusion: 文化动荡会显著改变个体和集体的言语行为模式，体现出其对社会深远而广泛的影响。流行歌曲歌词作为大规模自然语言语料，能够有效捕捉这种心理与社会语言层面的变化。

Abstract: Cultural upheavals impact many aspects of social life, and many studies have investigated their impact on language patterns. However, few investigations have isolated the impact of upheavals on individuals at scale in popular media. The current work evaluated millions of song lyrics spanning more than a century in search of within-artist and between-artist signals of distress from the Vietnam War, the terrorist attacks of 9/11, and COVID-19. Compared to a five-year baseline, rates of self-references - a marker of psychological distancing - were significantly reduced after the Vietnam War and September 11th. Cognitive processing terms were elevated post-upheaval vs. pre-upheaval, which indicated artists' increased attempts to make meaning from such massive disruptions. Content patterns corroborated these findings as artists wrote more about "life and freedom" (societal conditions) and less about "courtship and nightlife" (interpersonal connection) following the upheavals. Cultural upheavals modify individual and collective verbal behavior, demonstrating their far-reaching impact on society.

</details>


### [65] [AraMIP: Extending MIPVU Towards Metaphor Identification in Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17235)
*Mandar Marathe,Manar Ali,Sara Nabhani,Raia Abu Ahmad,Ibrahim Baroud,Omar Momen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AraMIP——首个系统化的阿拉伯语隐喻识别标注框架。该框架将英语 MIPVU 词级隐喻标注方法扩展为两阶段流程：阶段一沿用 MIPVU 对词元进行 MRW（隐喻相关词）标注，阶段二在此基础上将隐喻映射到阿拉伯修辞学传统中的三类范畴（isti‘āra 隐喻、kināya 借代/婉指、tashbīh 明喻），并记录其规约化程度（1-5 分制）。作者从 BAREC-10M 语料库采样 300 句 MSA 文本（5277 词）进行试点标注，采用六位标注者（三人独立标注一轮+异议第二轮重标）方案。结果显示一致性较低（Stage 1 二元 Fleiss κ=0.358，Stage 2 Soft γ≈0.44–0.48），并系统诊断了标注难题：词典覆盖率、词根-派生义处理、义项排序不一致、上下文材料缺失等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻研究在认知语言学与 NLP 中日益重要，但阿拉伯语作为低资源语言缺乏系统化、可复现的隐喻标注流程与基准语料，且现有阿拉伯语隐喻工作（Alkhatib & Shaalan 2016; Alsiyat & Piao 2020; Magdy et al. 2024; Banou et al. 2025）均存在覆盖域有限、流程不透明或由英语翻译而来等问题，无法直接使用已成熟的 MIPVU 框架。阿拉伯语形态复杂、词根构词及现代标准语（MSA）与古典阿拉伯语（CA）边界模糊，且阿拉伯修辞学传统（‘ilm al-bayān）本身就对隐喻/明喻/借代有独立分类体系，需要专门适配。

Method: 框架为两阶段：阶段一遵循 Steen et al. (2010) MIPVU，以《Mu‘jam al-Lugha al-‘Arabiyya al-Mu‘āṣira》为基本义参照，对每个词元标注 MRW-indirect / MRW-direct / MRW-implicit / MFlag / MRW-negative / WIDLII / DFMA；阶段二基于阿拉伯修辞传统对非 negative 词元进行跨度级（span-level）标注，划分 isti‘āra、kināya、tashbīh 三类，并赋 1–5 规约化分。语料选自 BAREC-10M 平衡采样 300 句（8–30 词/句，每类 50 句），六位标注者（L1 阿拉伯语者 5 人 + L2 1 人）经训练后采用 INCEpTION 平台三独立标注 + 异议第二轮重标的流程，使用 Fleiss κ、Soft γ、Exact-match F1、IoU-based F1 评估一致性。

Result: 阶段一：二元 MRW 一致性 Fleiss κ=0.358，加权标签一致性 0.357（远低于 VUAMC 的 0.84），主要混淆在 MRW-indirect ↔ MRW-negative（812 对）；最终 curated 语料中 96.76% 为 MRW-negative，MRW-indirect 占 2.88%，MRW-direct 占 0.11%。阶段二：Soft γ（等权重）=0.477，Exact-match F1=0.125，IoU-based F1=0.211；curated 后 136 个跨度中 isti‘āra 106 个（均规约化 4.05），kināya 22 个（3.67），tashbīh 8 个（2.40）。异义分析识别五大原因：一词多义/域边界模糊、古典/诗意语域、MWE 归属、词典缺口、文化/宗教解读差异。

Conclusion: AraMIP 是迈向阿拉伯语隐喻标准化标注的首步，其诊断结果为后续框架迭代提供了路线图：需多轮迭代精化指南、标准化上下文材料与词典资源、显式制定词根到派生义的判定规则、平衡语料域以提升隐喻密度代表性、并向方言阿拉伯语扩展。文章发布标注指南与试点语料（CC BY-NC 4.0），为阿拉伯语隐喻 NLP 任务（检测、MT、情感分析、攻击性语言检测等）提供基础资源支持。

Abstract: Metaphor research has gained increasing attention due to its relevance to linguistic creativity, language use, cognitive processes, and related areas. While many efforts have been devoted to metaphor identification and annotation in English and other languages, Arabic remains under-resourced in this area. In this work, we propose the Arabic Metaphor Identification Procedure (AraMIP), a novel guideline for Arabic metaphor annotation. AraMIP builds on the widely used Metaphor Identification Procedure Vrije Universiteit (MIPVU) framework, incorporating adaptations that accounts for the language-specific properties of Arabic. We distinguish three major types of Arabic figurative language: Isti'ara (metaphor), kinaya (metonymy/indirect expression), and tashbih (simile), and annotate a pilot dataset of 300 sentences (5277 words). Our analysis reveals key challenges specific to Arabic, including morphological complexity, inconsistencies in dictionary sense ordering, and the absence of standardized contextual materials for annotators. This work contributes a first step toward standardized Arabic figurative instances and facilitates the development of larger annotated resources, thereby supporting future research on figurative language in Arabic.

</details>


### [66] [ECHO: Early-layer Collaborative Hierarchical Orchestration with Bonus Logits in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17241)
*Ziyang Ma,Zihong Zhang,Zuchao Li,Lefei Zhang,Baoyuan Qi,Siqi Li,Simin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ECHO提出了一种基于Transformer层间功能不对称性的层次化双循环无草稿模型推测解码框架，通过早层高频验证与末层权威验证的协同，在不增加额外部署参数的情况下实现2.4×–2.9×的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无草稿模型推测解码方法面临两大瓶颈：1）草稿候选陈旧导致接受长度受限（信息衰减问题）；2）全模型验证带来高昂计算开销（验证墙问题）。早期自推测方法虽缓解了部分问题，但多次前向传播导致实际加速比欠佳。

Method: ECHO将目标模型在中间层L_e处划分为早层验证器V_e和后续层验证器V_s，构建双循环架构：高频内循环利用早层bonus logits进行树状草稿的多步快速探索与验证；低频外循环通过状态复用（state-reuse）对累积序列执行全模型因果验证。核心创新包括：1）早层与末层bonus logits的非对称利用——早层提供低成本语义锚点，末层提供高保真分布校准；2）混合草稿树——融合检索式n-gram匹配与logits驱动的Top-k采样；3）增量自动机（trie-based LRU）持续积累验证转移以丰富历史模式。

Result: 在Llama-2-7B/13B、Llama-3-8B、CodeLlama-7B/34B及Llama-2-70B等模型上，ECHO在Spec-Bench、HumanEval、GSM8K基准上实现2.4×–2.9×（最高3.01×）的加速，MAT显著优于PLD、LayerSkip、LogitSpec、TokenRecycling等基线。相比EAGLE-2（参数增强型），ECHO无需额外参数即可获得更高平均加速比（3.79× vs 2.84×）。层次化设计使Transformer层通过次数减少21%，总执行时间降低12%，中位数FLOPs降低17.4%。在分布式与高并发场景中同步开销仅约1.15%。

Conclusion: ECHO通过层次化解耦草稿生成与权威验证，有效突破了无草稿模型推测解码的验证墙与信息衰减瓶颈。其不引入额外部署参数的设计具有出色的工程实用性，最佳性能依赖于一次性早退出微调（兼容LayerSkip）。未来可探索更深层级级联及分布式异步执行以适配云端大规模部署。

Abstract: While draft-model-free speculative decoding offers a promising path to efficient LLM inference, it is frequently constrained by stale draft candidates and the high computational cost of the verification. To address these challenges, we propose ECHO, a hierarchical dual-loop framework that exploits the functional asymmetry between LLM layers. Leveraging the high discriminative efficiency of early layers and the authoritative distribution of final layers, ECHO bifurcates inference into a high-frequency inner loop and a low-frequency outer loop. Within the inner loop, early-layer bonus logits drive rapid, multi-step draft-tree exploration at a minimal cost. Simultaneously, the outer loop performs authoritative full-model verification through a state-reuse mechanism. Crucially, the outer loop also utilizes final-layer bonus logits to correct existing paths and supplement the tree with high-confidence candidates for subsequent cycles. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that ECHO significantly boosts mean accepted tokens and achieves a 2.4$\times$ to 2.9$\times$ speedup, outperforming existing state-of-the-art baselines with negligible engineering overhead and no extra deployment parameters, albeit with a one-shot fine-tuning dependency for optimal acceleration. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [67] [Persistent Recurrent Memory Between Transformer Layers - Improves Language Model Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17251)
*Eduardo Novaes Hering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在解码器-only Transformer 的中间层插入一个轻量级的"持续循环记忆"(Persistent Recurrent Memory, PRM)模块，通过"观察→更新→影响"三步拓扑结构(交叉注意力观察、GRU 更新、门控加和调制)，仅增加 3.7% 参数即在 TinyStories 语言建模上将评估损失降低 28.5%，并通过严格的消融实验证明增益完全来自该架构拓扑本身而非辅助自预测损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 缺乏在网络深度间显式维持压缩、稳定的中间表征摘要的机制，所有上下文信息都需通过注意力在每一层从原始序列中重新推导。这激发了一个问题：引入一个跨层持续存在的潜在状态能否为信息流动提供更稳定的通路，并改善泛化能力。

Method: 在 6 层 decoder-only Transformer 的第 3 层后插入 PRM 模块，包含三步：(1) 观察——状态向量 s 通过多头交叉注意力查询下层隐藏状态 H；(2) 更新——通过 GRU 单元递归迭代 L=2 次更新 s，并在每步后做层归一化；(3) 影响——通过 sigmoid 门控的加和机制 H' = H + g⊙(W_p·s') 调制上层输入。基线包括标准 Transformer、相同拓扑但去除自预测辅助损失的 GRU Memory、以及带随机辅助损失的 Random Aux，以分离拓扑与训练目标的贡献。

Result: 在 TinyStories(15,000 故事，85/15 划分)上以 5 个随机种子训练 15 个 epoch：PRM 评估损失 1.743±0.018，标准 Transformer 为 2.438±0.004，下降 28.5%(p<0.01)；泛化差距从 0.255 降至 0.122。消融显示 GRU Memory(1.766±0.014)与 PRM 无显著差异(差值 0.023±0.028，不显著)，而 Random Aux(2.435±0.007)与标准模型无差异。线性探针表明持续状态对"叙事位置"编码准确率达 52.1%，显著高于标准模型的 33.3%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：(1) 跨层桥接一个轻量级循环记忆是在数据有限场景下改善小模型泛化的简单有效方法；(2) 增益来源是 observe→update→influence 的计算拓扑而非任何辅助训练目标，提示架构进步可来自更好的信息流拓扑而不只是更好的优化目标；(3) 持续状态能编码叙事位置等慢变高层上下文，缓解注意力需在每层重建全局摘要的负担。局限：实验限于 22M 参数与 TinyStories 短文本，规模扩展性、复杂长上下文任务下的表现以及生成流畅度的轻微下降有待未来工作验证。

Abstract: We introduce a simple architectural modification to decoder-only transformers: a persistent recurrent state that observes hidden representations via cross-attention, updates itself through a GRU, and modulates subsequent processing via gated addition. Inserted between the lower and upper halves of a 6-layer transformer, this module adds only 3.7\% additional parameters while reducing evaluation loss from $2.438 \pm 0.004$ to $1.743 \pm 0.018$, corresponding to a 28.5\% reduction on held-out language modeling data. The improvement is statistically significant across 5 random seeds ($p < 0.01$) and corresponds to reduced overfitting (generalization gap 0.12 vs 0.26). Through controlled ablations, we demonstrate that the improvement stems entirely from the persistent memory topology, not from auxiliary self-prediction objectives. A model with identical topology but no auxiliary loss performs equivalently, while a random auxiliary loss provides no benefit. Representation probing reveals that the persistent state encodes narrative position (52\% vs 33\% chance level)---information that standard attention maintains less efficiently. Our results suggest that bridging transformer layers with a lightweight recurrent memory is a simple, effective approach to improving generalization in small-scale language models.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Illusions Awareness in Cyber-Physical System's Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17260)
*Anna Di Placido,Nicolas Ferry,Julien Deantoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对信息物理系统（CPS）中由于设计与运行时环境差异导致的"现实差距"问题，提出"设计幻觉"概念，并设计了一套将失效假设转化为可重用设计知识的概念流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS在开放环境中通过持续的感知-计算-执行循环运行，难以预见所有运行情境。设计模型依赖利益相关者的形式化假设，但这些假设在运行时可能失效，导致预期与观察行为之间的"现实差距"。现有方法主要致力于缩小或消除该差距，却缺乏统一的方法学来结构化利用失效假设所暴露的可重用设计知识。作者将持续依赖失效假设并因此错误信任设计模型操作有效性的现象称为"设计幻觉"，主张必须将其显式化、结构化并作为知识加以利用。

Method: 提出一个面向幻觉感知的概念化流程（illusions-awareness pipeline），按顺序执行四个阶段：1）识别（identify）设计中的幻觉；2）分类（classify）幻觉的类型；3）特征化（characterize）幻觉的属性；4）利用（leverage）幻觉，将其转化为可操作的设计知识（actionable design knowledge），用于支持后续更优的设计决策。

Result: 论文层面贡献为一个概念性框架，尚未报告具体实验或定量结果。其结果是定义了一种系统化方法，使设计幻觉从隐性问题转化为可显式管理的知识资产，为CPS设计中处理现实差距提供了新的方法学路径。

Conclusion: 将现实差距问题重新诠释为对设计幻觉的显式管理问题，论证了通过结构化流程把失效假设转化为可重用设计知识的可行性，填补了现有研究在统一方法学上的空白，并有望支撑更明智的CPS设计决策。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) operate through a continuous sense-compute-act loop within an open context environment, making it impossible to anticipate all the situations the system will face. To cope with this openness, stakeholders rely on assumptions, formalized into design models. However, these assumptions may no longer hold once the system is confronted with runtime reality, resulting in a discrepancy between expected and observed behaviour known in literature as the reality gap. Existing approaches mainly focus on reducing or overcoming it by making simulations more faithful to reality, with no unified methodology to structure and exploit invalidated assumptions that give rise to this gap as reusable design knowledge. We refer to the persistent reliance on invalidated assumptions -and the resulting false confidence in the design model's operational validity -as design illusions, and argue that they need to be made explicit, structured, and exploited as knowledge to support better design decisions. We propose a conceptual pipeline for illusions-awareness that identifies, classifies, characterizes, and leverages illusions to transform them into actionable design knowledge.

</details>


### [69] [Zero-shot narrative detection in social messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17310)
*Jesús M. Fraile-Hernández,Anselmo Peñas,Patrick Giedemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大语言模型在零样本设置下识别和分类社交媒体消息中隐藏叙事的能力，提出结合人工撰写的叙事描述与多数投票集成策略可在无需训练数据的情况下达到与监督系统相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LLM的句法和语义理解能力，而对其语用层面（特别是识别社交消息中战略叙事和深层交际意图）的能力探索不足。此外，监督方法依赖大量标注数据，在多数领域难以获取。

Method: 基于Dipromats 2024 Task 2和SemEval-2025 Task 10 Subtask 2两个多语言多领域数据集，设计了五种分类方案（Dipromats单步、SemEval三种：All-in-One、三步分层、混合），测试了八种提示策略（仅标题、人工描述、自生成/GPT生成的不同粒度描述），并使用四种不同规模的LLM（Calme 78B、Gemma-3 12B、Exaone-3.5 7.8B、Granite-3.3 2B），结合多数投票和包含集成策略进行评估。

Result: 1.人工撰写的原始叙事描述（OrigDesc）始终优于仅使用标题或自动生成的描述；2.自动生成的描述常因框架偏移导致性能下降，尤其在长篇详细描述时；3.多数投票集成显著提升稳健性，尤其在子叙事检测中可提升8.5个百分点；4.大模型（Calme 78B）对提示变化最稳健，某些中等模型（Gemma 12B）在性能和效率间取得良好平衡；5.零样本系统在SemEval和Dipromats排行榜上分别取得第四名（接近监督系统GATENLP）和第三名的成绩，超越了基于GPT-4的少样本系统。

Conclusion: 研究证实LLM具备足够的语用和上下文知识以零样本方式识别战略叙事；结合人工撰写的叙事描述和简单集成策略，可成为无训练数据领域下叙事检测的可扩展替代方案，性能可媲美监督系统。

Abstract: This study investigates the zero-shot ability of large language models (LLMs) to identify and classify hidden narratives in social messages. Our research hypothesis is that LLMs' extensive contextual knowledge allows them to interpret messages on a deeper, pragmatic level, going beyond basic sentiment or topic analysis. Experiments on the Dipromats and SemEval datasets show that providing models with human-written narrative descriptions significantly improves performance, without the need of training examples. In contrast, automatically generated descriptions or the use of few examples (few-shot) often degrade accuracy due to subtle shifts in framing. The study also finds that ensemble methods, particularly majority voting, enhance robustness and that larger models perform best while also being less sensitive to prompt variations. The findings validate that LLMs can effectively detect strategic narratives in a zero-shot setting, and when combined with simple ensembling and human-written descriptions, they can rival supervised systems, offering a scalable solution for narrative detection, specially when there is no training data for the vast majority of domains.

</details>


### [70] [Towards Detecting AI-Assisted Responses in Online Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17317)
*Qizhou Wang,Bogdan Mamaev,Christopher Leckie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM辅助完成在线调查的问题，构建了ASURRE基准数据集，评估了现有MGT检测器在三种使用策略（修订、全生成、角色驱动代理完成）下的表现，并提出了基于应答者行为线索的少样本无训练检测器SPABD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线调查中LLM辅助作答现象日益普遍（约34%的众包平台参与者承认使用），严重威胁调查研究的效度。现有机器生成文本（MGT）检测器主要针对长文本设计，难以应对调查问卷中分散在多字段的短文本，且未考虑同一应答者跨题的行为模式。

Method: （1）数据集构建：基于三个2019年前采集的真实调查（phd2019、springer2017、osmi_mh），使用多种LLM模拟三种使用策略；（2）提出四个应答者级行为线索（min_len、length_cv、mean_qa_sim、n_answered），仅依赖句子编码器无需训练标签；（3）设计SPABD检测器：基于K=5已知人类参考集，使用中位数/MAD估计位置与尺度，通过先验修正的单尾max聚合产生异常分数。

Result: 现有MGT检测器在Agentic Completion场景下表现接近随机（AUROC 0.50-0.65），全生成场景表现良好（AUROC 0.79-0.93）。SPABD在12个代理设置下将平均AUROC从0.61提升至0.75（+0.14），11/12场景优于最佳基线；FPR=5%时AI检出率27.7%（基线12.0%）。跨LLM骨干泛化稳定（±0.02），但K=5已捕获大部分增益。

Conclusion: 基于行为的检测是有前景的方向。LLM代理无法完全复刻人类应答者的行为变异性，残留的覆盖度、长度变异性、最短长度和话题对齐等线索可用于识别AI辅助作答。SPABD作为轻量参考检测器，证实了跨答案信号的价值，但非完整解决方案；调查领域多样性与对抗鲁棒性仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: The use of LLMs to complete online surveys impacts the validity of survey-based research, but detecting such usage remains underexplored. We introduce an initial benchmark dataset, namely ASURRE, for AI-assisted survey participation to capture usage strategies ranging from full generation and revision to persona-grounded agentic completion. Controlled by these strategies, LLM-assisted survey responses are generated using multiple LLMs on three real-world surveys in different disciplines, paired with genuine human responses. Our evaluation of existing machine-generated text (MGT) detectors shows that naive AI usage is readily detectable, whereas persona-grounded agents that mimic entire respondents push detector performance toward chance. We further show that agentic completion cannot fully replicate respondent-level behaviour and leaves distinctive behavioural traces. While individual cues can be circumvented by targeted prompting, a simple few-shot, training-free aggregator over these cues improves mean AUROC by +0.14 over the best existing detector across agentic settings. Our project is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [71] [ECHO: A Matched-Contrast Benchmark for Context-Sensitive Turn-Taking in Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17360)
*Shuofeng Zhao,Hongwei Cai,Wenke Fan,Qingxiang Guo,Dawei Yang,Zhou Wang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ECHO 是一个针对中文全双工对话系统的配对诊断基准，通过保持重叠话语文本一致而改写其前文多轮对话语境，迫使模型基于上下文而非词面形式判断应"让出话轮(Yield)"还是"保持话轮(Keep)"。配套的配对准确率(PASR/PKC)要求模型在同插入文本、不同语境的配对样本上同时判断正确，从而暴露现有系统对"打断"过度敏感、对"反馈/旁白"过度让话的系统性偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工评测基准通常独立评估打断与反馈事件，且非打断性反馈常具有词汇封闭性（如 SID-Bench 中 81.8% 仅由最常用 15 字构成），模型可凭词面捷径判断；这导致仅报告"打断准确率"会高估实际话轮决策可靠性，且奖励固定动作偏好的策略而非上下文敏感的决策能力。

Method: 1) 采用最小对设计：从 DuplexDrama 采样多轮对话骨架，用 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 生成候选插入，再用 Sonnet 改写前文以改变交互角色(打断/反馈/旁白)，保持插入文本不变；2) IndexTTS2 合成语音，强制对齐放置重叠，role-dependent 渲染；3) 三元角色标签 + 二元动作标签；4) 配对指标：PASR 要求两个成员同时正确，PKC 评估 Keep 类一致性，PRSR 推广到三角色空间；5) 评估 4 个语音系统(Easy Turn、SoulX-Duplug、Lychee-FD、MiniCPM-o 4.5)和 1 个文本参考(Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview)。

Result: 数据集：266 组、549 唯一样本、300 个配对关系，三角色各 183 实例，三种配对各 100 对；非打断性反馈词汇覆盖率 400 字符、0% 仅由前 15 字构成。实验发现：Easy Turn、SoulX-Duplug、Lychee-FD 表现出强烈 Yield 偏置（分别对 98.91%/91.26%/55.19% 的打断正确让话，但对反馈仅 1.09%/6.56%/12.02% 正确保持话轮；中断-反馈 PASR 仅 0–4%）；MiniCPM-o 4.5 较平衡（63.39%/65.57%，PASR-I-B 54%）；文本参考 Gemini 在三角色上达 82.33% 宏准确率但 PRSR 仅 66%。结论：偏置并非由上下文不足导致——Lychee-FD 已获得 5 轮上文与完整计划回复仍严重过度让话，而仅看到更少信息的文本参考在反馈上达 86.34%。

Conclusion: 话轮决策的核心瓶颈在于如何利用可获得的上下文，而非可获得多少上下文；评估全双工系统的实际可靠性必须同时报告类别条件化的 Keep 准确率与打断准确率，仅用单一打断准确率会系统性高估系统表现。ECHO 数据集与配对元数据将公开。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems must distinguish interruptions that require yielding the floor from backchannels that permit continued speaking. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate events independently and may therefore reward fixed action preferences rather than context-sensitive decisions. We introduce ECHO, a paired diagnostic benchmark for Chinese full-duplex turn-taking. ECHO pairs examples with the same overlap transcript but contrasting preceding multi-turn dialogue contexts, with one requiring Yield and the other Keep. It additionally includes off-talk examples for diagnosing unnecessary yielding. We introduce pair accuracy, which requires correct decisions on both members of a pair and assigns no credit to constant-action policies. Experiments on multiple full-duplex systems show that most exhibit a pronounced bias toward \textsc{Yield}, performing substantially better on interruptions than on backchannels, while another system remains comparatively balanced. These findings demonstrate that interruption-only evaluation can overestimate practical turn-taking reliability. ECHO and its metadata will be publicly released.

</details>


### [72] [Enhancing Accessibility of Medical Texts through Large Language Model-Driven Plain Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17398)
*Ting-Wei Chang,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对医疗文本可及性问题，系统比较了多种大语言模型（包括 GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-1.5-pro、LLaMA、Gemma、Mistral）在零样本、少样本提示、QLoRA 微调以及 Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）策略下的 PLA 表现，在 PLABA 2024 共享任务上取得了 PLA 第 4 名、术语替换任务第 2 名的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗健康信息通常使用高度专业化的技术语言，普通患者难以理解，造成医患信息鸿沟，亟需自动化方法将复杂医学文本转化为通俗语言（PLA），以提升健康信息的可及性和健康素养。

Method: 本文提出四阶段方法体系：(1) 比较零样本、随机少样本 ICL 和基于语义相似度的少样本 ICL 三种提示策略；(2) 对 LLaMA、Gemma、Mistral 等开源模型采用 QLoRA（4-bit 量化 + LoRA r/α=16，lr=2e-4，batch=2，1 epoch）进行高效微调；(3) 采用单层 MoA 框架，以 7 个 LLM 为适配器，Gemini-1.5-Flash 作为聚合器；(4) 评估新增的术语替换任务（含识别与分类两阶段）。评估指标涵盖 BLEU/SARI/ROUGE/BERTScore（相关性）、FKGL/DCRS/CLI（可读性）、AlignScore/SummaC（事实性）以及 Gemini-1.5-Flash 作为 LLM Judge 的简洁性、准确性、完整性、简洁度评分。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 少样本 ICL 显著优于零样本，基于语义相似度的示例选择略优于随机选择；(2) QLoRA 微调使 LLaMA-3-8B 等模型的 BLEU 从 11.44 提升至 33.77，ROUGE-1 从 44.88 提升至 61.67，事实性大幅改善（AlignScore 从 73.58 升至 88.82），但 SARI 略降，可读性分数上升；(3) MoA 在测试集人类评估中，使用微调模型时取得 PLA 第 4 名（Avg=0.8238），未微调时第 8 名；(4) 术语替换任务中，5-shot ICL + 语义相似度使 Gemini-1.5-pro 识别 F1 达 41.67，团队 ntu_nlp 在 PLABA 2024 共享任务中获第 2 名。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的 PLA 在简化医疗文本方面具有显著潜力，少样本 ICL 结合语义相似度选择可提升输出相关性与可读性，QLoRA 微调使模型习得专家写作风格并显著增强事实一致性。但所有模型均为通用 LLM，缺乏医学领域预训练，限制了其领域理解能力。未来工作应探索医疗领域专用预训练模型，并扩展到更多元化的医疗文本数据集以验证泛化能力。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of making complex healthcare information more accessible through automated Plain Language Adaptation (PLA). PLA aims to simplify technical medical language, bridging a critical gap between the complexity of healthcare texts and patients' reading comprehension. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), such as GPT and BART, have opened new possibilities for PLA, especially in zero-shot and few-shot learning contexts where task-specific data is limited. In this work, we leverage the capabilities of LLMs such as GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-1.5-pro, and LLaMA for text simplification. Additionally, we incorporate Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) techniques to enhance adaptability and robustness in PLA tasks. Key contributions include a comparative analysis of prompting strategies, finetuning with QLoRA on different LLMs, and the integration of MoA technique. Our findings demonstrate the effectiveness of LLM-driven PLA, showcasing its potential in making healthcare information more comprehensible while preserving essential content.

</details>


### [73] [Right Tool, Right Job: Native-Language Evaluation, Tokenizer Sensitivity, and Methodological Findings from a French-Only BabyLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17435)
*Adam Zachary Wasserman,David Beauchemin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MéTRON-FR 是一款仅以法语训练的 125M 参数 GPT-2 模型，在 92.47M 词上预训练后，参加了 BabyLM 2026 严格赛道。其在魁北克法语原生最小对偶基准 QFrBLiMP 上达到 85.97%，在 BabyLM 加权排行榜上达到 62.80%。论文通过跨语言 GLUE 协议、词对齐诱导（BLI）和一系列法医式消融实验，论证了在儿童语料规模下，应使用原生语言最小对偶基准、tokenizer 敏感度测试和安慰剂对照提示等方法学诊断工具，因为单 token 零样本评分在该规模下主要受分词器和模板伪迹驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026 之前的 BabyLM 提交全是英语模型，限制了关于"语言习得普遍性 vs 英语特异性"的结论。2026 规则放开后，需探索非英语（尤其是法语/魁北克法语）在 100M 词约束下的表现，并开发适合非英语儿童规模的方法学诊断工具。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 训练：125M GPT-2 在 CHILDES 法语 + BabyBabelLM 法语 + 过滤维基百科组成的 92.47M 词语料上预训练 5 epoch，使用 50K BPE tokenizer，并通过海地克里奥尔语词汇 oracle 对句子进行过采样（不引入 HC 文本）；(2) 原生评估：使用 QFrBLiMP（魁北克法语最小对偶）和 QFrCoLA（可接受性判断）；(3) 跨语言评估：法语翻译 GLUE + rank-16 LoRA 的四杠杆实验网格，以及基于 Procrustes 的 BLI 嵌入对齐诊断；(4) 法医式消融：tokenizer-swap 敏感性、安慰剂对照提示、翻译 BLiMP Supplement 词频混淆分析。

Result: 主要结果：(1) QFrBLiMP 总体 85.97%±0.17%（提交 epoch 3，5 seed 复现），超过 2026 官方多语种赛道中其他三种语言的原生 BLiMP 最佳成绩（英语 80.75%、荷兰语 81.70%、中文 78.60%）；(2) BabyLM 加权排行榜 62.80%，达到或超过严格赛道参考点；(3) BLI 与 GPT-2 对齐 p@1=68.84%±8.61%（约为偶然的 18 倍），与一个在 65 亿 token 上训练但语法探测仅 40% 偶然的匹配架构英语模型对比表明，对齐追踪获得性语法能力而非训练时长；(4) 跨语言 GLUE 杠杆 D+C（法语翻译数据 + rank-16 LoRA）在关系型任务（BoolQ +3.45pp、RTE +5.13pp、MRPC +2.89pp）上有可测增益，但世界知识任务（MNLI -11.10pp）退化；(5) tokenizer 交换（v3d）单独复现了 v2 全部 7.70pp 的 GLUE 公理崩塌；(6) 词汇公理提示的安慰剂对照显示定向与安慰剂之差仅 -0.27pp，证实其"增益"为结构化提示噪声。

Conclusion: 论文结论：法语单一语言在儿童规模下可获得强原生语法能力；在该规模下，单 token 零样本评分主要由分词器和回答模板伪迹驱动，因此建议非英语儿童规模提交应常规报告三项诊断——(1) 原生语言最小对偶基准（避免翻译质量与词频混淆）、(2) tokenizer-swap 敏感性（区分任务能力与词表差异）、(3) 安慰剂对照提示（控制提示长度匹配的无内容增益）。LoRA 因构造时保留基底而成为方法学上优于全量微调的适配方法。同时确认在 125M/100M 词约束下世界知识任务的工具不存在于 BabyLM 框架内。

Abstract: We submit MéTRON-FR, a 125M GPT-2 pretrained on 92.47M words of French, to the BabyLM 2026 Strict track. It scores 85.97 +/- 0.17% on QFrBLiMP (a native Quebec-French benchmark of grammatical minimal pairs) and 62.80% on the BabyLM-weighted leaderboard. A cross-lingual GLUE (General Language Understanding Evaluation) protocol that combines French task-data translation with rank-16 LoRA (Low-Rank Adaptation) produces a sharp task-type gradient: relational tasks gain measurably, while world-knowledge tasks regress. Bilingual Lexicon Induction aligns the French embeddings to GPT-2 at p@1 = 68.84 +/- 8.61%, 18X above chance, suggesting cross-lingual alignment tracks acquired grammatical competence rather than training duration. An ablation study shows that single-token zero-shot scoring is dominated by tokenizer and template artifacts at the child scale, motivating tokenizer-swap sensitivity, placebo-controlled prompting, and native-language minimal-pair benchmarks as standard diagnostics.

</details>


### [74] [What Breaks Under Pruning in Smart Homes, and When? Evaluating LLM Degradation Across Architectures and Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17515)
*Congjing Zhang,Vashishtha Patil,Henning Lange,Usman Aleem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大语言模型（LLM）结构化剪枝对智能家居工具调用任务的影响，涵盖密集Transformer、密集混合和MoE三种架构，发现密集模型安全剪枝区间窄且易出现过度拒绝，MoE模型则可承受大幅专家剪枝；剪枝首先损害上下文特定的设备与值预测，再波及模式级意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM部署成本高，结构化剪枝可降低推理开销，但剪枝对依赖上下文环境的工具调用（如智能家居）可靠性影响缺乏系统研究；聚合准确率可能掩盖组件级与任务级失效模式，因此需要细粒度评估框架以指导部署选型。

Method: 选取Qwen家族四种LLM（Qwen3-4B密集Transformer、Qwen3.5-4B/9B密集混合、Qwen3.6-35B-A3B MoE），结合ShortGPT/Angular深度剪枝、FLAP宽度剪枝、2SSP混合剪枝、REAP专家剪枝；剪枝后进行统一SFT恢复，在SHTC、HomeBench、HAR三个数据集（>19,500实例）上从动作四要素（operation/device/argument/value）与五种任务复杂度（Simple/Medium/Complex/Partially Executable/Infeasible）两维度评估ΔAcc。

Result: 密集模型在约10%剪枝内基本无损（多数<0.6%下降），但继续剪枝后出现陡崖式崩溃；Qwen3.6-35B-A3B移除70%专家后参数从35B降至约12.5B，准确率仅-0.37%，呈宽平台。剪枝首先破坏device与value（接地特异性），而operation与argument（模式级意图）更稳定（如50%剪枝时d vs o损失24.2% vs 21.1%，v vs p损失20.7% vs 14.5%）。Partially Executable任务最敏感（每+10%剪枝降10.3%），而基线难度不可预测剪枝敏感性；Qwen3-4B在50% FLAP剪枝下对可执行请求的拒绝率从7.7%飙升至80.8%，呈现系统性过度拒绝。

Conclusion: 智能家居工具调用的剪枝评估需超越聚合准确率，应同时关注架构、负载与失效模式；MoE专家剪枝是受限域的有前景起点，密集LLM需更保守策略并多比例评估；未来工作应开发保护设备/值接地的剪枝准则与定向修复数据，并校准执行-拒绝决策。

Abstract: Pruning can reduce the deployment cost of large language models (LLMs), but its impact on context-grounded tool calling remains poorly understood. We systematically study pruning-induced degradation in smart-home tool calling across four LLMs spanning dense Transformer, dense hybrid, and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, together with depth, width, hybrid, and expert pruning methods. After post-pruning supervised fine-tuning (SFT), we evaluate more than 19,500 instances from three smart-home datasets. Beyond aggregate task accuracy, we characterize degradation along two dimensions: action components (i.e., operation, device, argument, and value) and task complexity. Our results show that dense models have narrow safe pruning regions followed by sharp degradation, while MoE models tolerate substantially more pruning. Pruning degrades grounded specificity before schema-level intent, and aggressive dense pruning can induce systematic over-refusal. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating pruning beyond aggregate accuracy when selecting pruned LLMs for reliable tool execution.

</details>
