### [75] [Scheduling Jobs with Multiple Operational Modes and Tail Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16001)
*Bo Chen,Jelmer Pier van der Gaast,Xiandong Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一种新型的单机调度问题，其中每个作业有多种操作模式，每种模式同时确定机器处理时间和后续资源无关的"尾部时间"（如冷却、固化）。论文目标是同时确定作业排序和模式分配，针对四个目标（最大完工时间Cmax、最早完工时间Cmin、完工时间极差Dmax、加权总完工时间Σw_jC_j）建立了完整的计算复杂性分类图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际背景来源于可编程材料制造（如蓝相液晶显示器印刷，色彩在印刷后的扩散成熟阶段才形成）和预制混凝土生产（养护时间取决于混凝土配合比）。同步化要求对产品质量至关重要——成熟过早或过晚的单元会因统一后处理步骤而偏离目标颜色/强度。传统的单模式调度理论无法捕捉"选择一种模式同时决定处理时间和尾随时间"的耦合特性。

Method: 研究方法为理论调度分析：(1) 形式化定义问题模型 1|Mode(p_{ij},q_{ij})|γ；(2) 对单模式情形（δ=1）证明经典排序规则的有效性（Jackson规则、w_j/p_j比值规则、p_j+q_j降序规则）；(3) 对固定序列+模式选择情形，设计基于二分搜索的贪心可行性测试和动态规划；(4) 通过从Partition问题（弱NP完全）构造归约证明弱NP困难性；(5) 通过从3-Partition问题（强NP完全）构造精巧的归约证明强NP困难性，模式数设为δ=n；(6) 利用"添加大尾部辅助作业"的技术将Cmax困难性传递到Dmax。

Result: 主要结果形成完整的复杂性分类（详见Table 1和Table 2）：(1) 单模式情形全部多项式可解；(2) 固定序列下，Cmax、Cmin、Σw_jC_j均多项式可解，但Dmax即使在δ=2也是弱NP困难（存在伪多项式算法）；(3) δ=2时，Cmax、Dmax、Cmin、Σw_jC_j均为（弱）NP困难；(4) δ无限制时，所有四个目标均为强NP困难。关键理论发现是"模式选择耦合了处理时间和尾时"才是计算困难性的根源——即使离散可控加工时间文献中加权和目标在δ≥2时变难（已知），但尾部耦合使即使非加权目标在δ=2时就已NP困难。

Conclusion: 论文为包含尾部时间的多模式调度问题建立了完整的计算复杂性理论框架，清晰界定了多项式可解与NP困难的边界。实践意义在于：(1) 当生产顺序固定时，制造商可通过模式选择优化多数目标；(2) 蓝相液晶显示器印刷中追求颜色同步（Dmax最小化）在固定顺序下虽NP困难，但有伪多项式算法可用；(3) 为未来近似算法、在线调度、多机环境等研究方向奠定基础。

Abstract: This study explores a scheduling challenge inspired by the production of programmable materials, such as advanced liquid crystal displays. In these systems, the final quality of a product is reached only after a resource-free maturation period, known as a "tail", during which the machine is available for processing other jobs. Each job can be executed in one of several operational modes, with each mode determining a specific combination of machine processing time and subsequent tail duration. The primary task is to simultaneously choose the best mode for every job and determine their processing order. We analyze this model across several key performance goals, including the total time required to finish all jobs, the synchronization of completion times (the gap between the earliest and latest finished products), and the total weighted completion time. Our findings provide a detailed classification of the computational complexity of these problems. We demonstrate that while traditional versions with only one mode per job are simple to solve using standard rules, the introduction of just two modes makes finding optimal solutions for most of these goals computationally difficult. When the number of available modes is large, the complexity increases significantly. However, we also identify specific scenarios that remain efficiently solvable, such as when the processing order is already determined or when the goal is to minimize the average completion time. These results offer theoretical clarity and practical strategies for optimizing complex manufacturing and chemical processes involving forced cooling or maturation stages.

</details>


### [76] [SETH-based Lower Bound for Dynamic Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16303)
*Konrad Majewski,Michał Pilipczuk*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了在 SETH 假设下，动态图中无法用强次线性更新时间维持退化度（degeneracy）的 (2−ε)-近似，从而将 Christiansen 与 Rotenberg (ICALP 2022) 的 2-近似上界结果补全为紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退化度与荫度（arboricity）是图稀疏性的核心度量参数，满足 deg(G) ≤ 2·arb(G)−1。Christiansen 与 Rotenberg 的算法能在 poly(log n, d_max) 的最坏情况下更新时间内维持 2-近似退化度，但能否突破乘性因子 2 是基本未解决的问题。本文的动机即是从 SETH 角度证明因子 2 是本质最优的。

Method: 证明路线为「OV → Dynamic Circuit Evaluation → Dynamic (2−ε, D)-Approximate Degeneracy」两次归约。第一步构造 OvToCircuit 数据结构，将正交向量实例编码为单调 (2,2)-电路 C_{n,d} 的 n 个输入向量，每个相邻向量仅差 2-4 个位翻转，由此借 Dynamic Circuit Evaluation 的 subquadratic 时间算法破 SETH。第二步为关键技巧：对任意固定 ℓ≥2，从 (2,2)-单调电路构造图 G_{C,x}，通过 wire、input、OR、AND 和 output 五类小工具（gadget），利用层状结构与度数边界证明：当 C(x)=1 时 deg(G)≤ℓ，C(x)=0 时 deg(G)≥2ℓ−1，且上界 3ℓ。当近似因子 (2−ε) 与加性误差 D 不足以跨越区间 [ℓ, 2ℓ−2] 时，算法可区分两种情况。

Result: 主定理（Theorem 1.1）：除非 SETH 失败，对任意 ε,δ>0、D,k∈ℕ 和函数 f:ℕ→ℕ，不存在动态数据结构能以初始化时间 f(d_max)·n^k 和摊销更新时间 f(d_max)·n^{1−δ} 维持退化度的 (2−ε)-近似。这揭示了动态算法中退化度与荫度的本质差异：荫度可加性近似至 2，而退化度需要乘性因子 2。

Conclusion: 本文从精细复杂度角度建立了动态退化度近似的紧下界：因子 2 是不可避免的。该结果将 ICALP 2022 的上界与 SETH 下的下界配对，明确指出退化度与荫度虽满足 2-因子关系，但在动态可维护性上存在本质区别。核心贡献在于引入了「动态电路求值」中间问题以及将电路求值归约为 (2−ε)-近似退化度的精巧层状小工具构造，对任意 ℓ 参数化使得近似间隙可任意逼近 2。

Abstract: In this work, we consider the problem of maintaining an approximate value of degeneracy of a given dynamic $n$-vertex graph $G$ updated by edge insertions and deletions. From the work of Christiansen and Rotenberg [ICALP 2022], it follows that one can design a dynamic data structure for this problem with worst-case update time $\text{poly}(d_{\mathrm{max}}, \log n)$ that maintains an integer between $d$ and $2d+3$ where $d$ is the degeneracy of $G$, under the assumption that $d$ never exceeds $d_{\mathrm{max}}$. We complement their result by providing a conditional lower bound: we prove that, unless SETH fails, for any $\varepsilon, \delta > 0$, $k \in \mathbb{N}$, and function $f\colon \mathbb{N}\to \mathbb{N}$, there is no data structure which maintains a $(2-\varepsilon)$-approximation of the degeneracy of $G$ with initialization time $f(d_{\mathrm{max}})\cdot n^k$ and amortized update time $f(d_{\mathrm{max}})\cdot n^{1-\delta}$.

</details>


### [77] [A deterministic $(2 + \varepsilon)$-approximation for directed feedback vertex sets in tournaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16723)
*Ebrahim Ghorbani,Matthias Mnich*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文给出了锦标赛（tournament）中无权和点权有向反馈顶点集（DFVS）问题的首个确定性多项式时间 (2+ε)-近似算法，并将其推广到拟传递有向图。关键技术是基于"三角形有向图"（仅保留属于有向三角形的弧）的完美性，通过禁止小族 T_{2k+1}-子锦标赛来约束残余锦标赛中三角形有向图的团数上界，从而用动态规划在剩余结构上精确求解，最后用局部比例（local-ratio）方法结合两阶段得到近似比 (2+1/k)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFVS in tournaments 是 Vertex Cover 在有向锦标赛上的推广，自 Speckenmeyer (1990) 证明其与 Vertex Cover 同难以来，UGC 下不可有 (2−ε)-近似。28 年间一系列工作不断缩小近似比（5/2 → 7/3 → 9/4），但确定性多项式时间的 2-近似始终悬而未决。Lokshtanov 等 (SODA 2020) 明确提出了寻求确定性 2-近似的公开问题。

Method: 方法核心分三部分：(1) 建立"hero 型结构定理"——证明对每个 k≥1，T_{2k+1}-free 锦标赛 T 的三角形有向图 H(T) 的团数至多为 2^{2^{k-1}}−1；利用 H(T) 的完美性与 Erdős–Moser 定理完成。(2) 设计动态规划精确算法——基于 H(T) 的 t-着色（颜色类为独立集从而诱导传递子锦标赛），利用"前缀性质"（Lemma 8）实现 O(t·n^{t+1}) 时间求最大权传递子锦标赛，从而对 T_{2k+1}-free 锦标赛精确求解。(3) 加权局部比例预处理——反复从 T_{2k+1} 中选取子锦标赛并均匀减权，处理后残余图由 (1)(2) 精确求解，组合得 (2+1/k)-近似。通过额外禁止大小为 2^k 的子锦标赛，将指数依赖从双指数降为单指数，运行时间为 O(n^{2^k})。同时利用 Ghorbani–Mnich 关于拟传递有向图的引理，将结果扩展到拟传递有向图。

Result: 主要结果：(i) 首个确定性多项式时间 (2+ε)-近似算法（节点加权 DFVS in tournaments），对任意 ε>0 成立；(ii) 同样的 (2+ε)-近似可推广到拟传递有向图；(iii) 改进了 28 年来确定性近似比序列（5/2, 7/3, 9/4），几乎解决了 Lokshtanov 等提出的公开问题；(iv) 副产品：T_{2k+1}-free 锦标赛上的节点加权 DFVS 可在 n^{O(t_k)} 时间内精确求解（推论 5），统一并改进了 Cai-Deng-Zang 等关于 T_5-free 与 T_7-free 的结果。

Conclusion: 论文几乎解决了锦标赛中 DFVS 多项式时间可近似性问题：确定性逼近 UGC 下最优比 2 至任意小 ε。同时澄清了为何不能简单地通过禁止满足 mfvs ≥ |S|/2 的子锦标赛来获得精确 2-近似——以正则循环锦标赛 R_n 为反例，其三角形有向图 H(R_n) 的团数无界，即便其中不存在 mfvs ≥ |S|/2 的子锦标赛。提出的开放问题：T_{2k+1}-free 锦标赛上的 DFVS 是否关于 k 是固定参数 tractable，若能给出 f(k)·n^{O(1)} 时间算法则可消除运行时间指数中对 ε 的依赖。

Abstract: We nearly settle the polynomial-time approximability of the Directed Feedback Vertex Set problem in tournaments. This problem is Vertex Cover-hard, and thus cannot have a $(2 - \varepsilon)$-approximation for any $\varepsilon > 0$ in polynomial time assuming the Unique Games Conjecture. In the past 28 years, several works have attempted to attain this approximability barrier of 2, and have designed algorithms with smaller and smaller approximation factors. This includes a $5/2$-approximation by Cai, Deng and Zang (FOCS 1998, SICOMP 2001); a $7/3$-approximation by Mnich, Vassilevska Williams and V{é}gh (ESA 2016), another $7/3$-approximation by Aprile, Drescher, Fiorini and Huynh (DAM 2023), and a $9/4$-approximation by Ghorbani and Mnich (ICALP 2026). Our main result improves upon all of those works: we give the first deterministic polynomial-time $(2+\varepsilon)$-approximation for Directed Feedback Vertex Set in tournaments, for all $\varepsilon > 0$. We thereby almost answer an open question by Lokshtanov, Misra, Mukherjee, Panolan, Philip and Saurabh (SODA 2020) who asked for a deterministic 2-approximation in polynomial time. Furthermore, we extend our result to the broader class of quasi-transitive digraphs

</details>


### [78] [Improved Approximation for Unsplittable CVRP via a Greedy Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16910)
*Daniel Ebert,Leonard Weismantel*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多项式时间的3.159-近似算法，用于度量不可分割的带容量车辆路径问题(CVRP)，通过引入平均贪心算法来同时控制路径成本和高需求客户的覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVRP是组合优化中的基础问题，自1959年提出以来一直是研究热点。此前最好的近似比为α+1.659之下（Zhao and Xiao 2026），本文旨在进一步改进这一界限。核心动机是观察到相对贪心算法中的平均论证并不总是紧致的——当选择包含高需求客户的路径时，最优解中相应路径的效率会显著下降，通过更精细的平均论证可以改进近似比。

Method: 1. 提出新的平均贪心算法(Average Greedy Algorithm)，在保持接近最优的代价-势能比的同时，保证选中足够多的高需求"单一客户"(single clients)；2. 通过桶划分(bucket partition)和势能向量控制"单向偏差"(one-sided discrepancy)，使用归纳论证证明偏差有界；3. 推导两个有效的代价密度函数(Φ^(1) 和 Φ^(2))，分别适用于一般δ和δ=1/3的情形；4. 结合匹配节省算法(Matching Savings Algorithm)和两种不同势能的平均贪心算法，取三种解中最优者；5. 使用区间算术(interval arithmetic)验证三维棱柱上的近似比上界（AI辅助计算）。

Result: 主要结果：存在多项式时间(α+1.659)-近似算法用于CVRP，其中α为TSP的近似比。对称情形下得到3.159-近似比，优于之前的3.176（Zhao and Xiao 2026）。作者指出该界限对他们的方法几乎是最优的——存在实例使得三个代价界的最小值大于α+1.6588。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入平均贪心算法和改进的平均论证，将度量不可分割CVRP的近似比改进至3.159。新方法的核心思想是利用被选高需求客户的信息从最优解中排除部分路径，从而获得更紧的代价界。结合匹配算法和不同参数设置，三种解的组合覆盖了所有可能的实例配置。技术计算使用AI辅助完成，区间算术验证代码已公开。

Abstract: We devise a polynomial-time $3.159$-approximation algorithm for the metric unsplittable Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem. We build on the Relative Greedy Algorithm suggested by Traub (2025), which can be considered as a variant of the LP rounding algorithm of Friggstad, Mousavi, Rahgoshay, and Salavatipour (2025). Our main ingredient is the Average Greedy Algorithm, a new algorithm that controls both tour costs and the coverage of clients with high demand. This additional control enables a sharper averaging argument for the cost of subsequent greedy choices. Similarly to Zhao and Xiao (2026), combining the Average Greedy with variants of tour partitioning and a matching algorithm yields the final approximation guarantee.

</details>


### [79] [Improved Regular Expression Matching with Simple Backreferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16914)
*Philip Bille,Inge Li Gørtz,Rikke Schjeldrup Jessen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对含单个捕获组与多个反向引用的正则表达式（rewb）匹配问题，提出了时间复杂度为 $O(n^2 m)$、空间复杂度为 $O(nm)$ 的新算法，相比已有最优结果在时间和空间上分别有显著改进，并将其推广至有序单嵌套 rewbs 类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rewb 匹配问题在最一般情况下是 NP 完全的，但在实践中捕获组与引用数量有限的子类被广泛使用。此前，Uezato [CPM 2026] 针对单个捕获组与 $k$ 个引用给出 $O(kn^2m^2)$ 时间、$O(n^2m^2)$ 空间算法；Nogami 与 Terauchi [MFCS 2025] 针对 $k=1$ 给出 $O(n^2m^2)$ 时间、$O(n+m^2)$ 空间算法。同时 Nogami、Nakamura 与 Terauchi [arXiv 2026] 基于正交向量假设给出 $O(n^{2-\epsilon}\mathrm{poly}(m))$ 不可行的条件性下界，表明 $n$ 的二次指数是条件最优的。因此，改进 $m$ 上的多项式依赖因子成为研究目标。

Method: 核心工具是区间树（interval tree）：对字符串 $Q$ 的二元区间构建完全二叉树，每个节点存储 Thompson NFA 中关于该区间左右子串的可达状态集（用位向量表示）。任意一对端点 $(i,j)$ 在唯一最低节点处分离，对应子串匹配等价于该节点左右状态集的交集非空。算法结合后缀树枚举匹配捕获组 $e$ 的候选子串 $q$ 及其所有出现位置；利用区间树一次遍历高效判断 $Q$ 是否匹配 $e_0 q e_1 q e_2$。多个引用时为每个中间正则表达式构建一棵区间树，逐步向前传播可行前缀端点。有序单嵌套 rewbs 可分解为单组块序列，按序处理并传递端点即可。复杂度分析基于摊还论证与位向量并行操作（OR/AND）。

Result: 定理 1：对长度为 $m$ 的单捕获组、$k$ 个引用的 rewb 与长度为 $n$ 的字符串，匹配问题可在 $O(n^2m)$ 时间、$O(nm)$ 空间内解决。相比 Uezato 的 $O(kn^2m^2)$ 时间、$O(n^2m^2)$ 空间，时间改进 $km$ 倍、空间改进 $nm$ 倍；相比 Nogami–Terauchi 在 $k=1$ 时的时间改进 $m$ 倍。定理 2：将上述结果推广至有序单嵌套 rewbs，复杂度同为 $O(n^2m)$ 时间、$O(nm)$ 空间。

Conclusion: 本文通过区间树数据结构与后缀树候选枚举的结合，将单捕获组 rewb 匹配的 $m$ 因子多项式依赖从 $m^2$ 降至 $m$，同时将空间从 $n^2m^2$ 降至 $nm$，在已知条件性下界约束下推进了多项式依赖因子的最优性。此外，算法被推广到有序单嵌套这一更一般的 rewb 子类，展示了区间树方法在带反向引用正则表达式匹配中的通用性与有效性。

Abstract: A regular expression with backreferences (rewb) specifies a set of strings formed by characters combined with concatenation, union, star operators, and backreferences. A backreference consists of a capturing group $(\cdot)_i$ and a reference $\backslash i$. The substring matched by the reference must match the substring matched by the corresponding capturing group. Given a rewb $R$ and a string $Q$, the rewb matching problem is to decide whether $Q$ is one of the strings specified by $R$. In full generality, rewb matching is NP-complete, but efficient solutions exist for various subclasses. In the paper, we focus on rewb containing a single capturing group and $k$ references. For this class, Uezato~[CPM 2026] gave an $O((k n^2 m^2)$ time and $O(n^2m^2)$ space algorithm, where $m$ is the length of the regular expression $R$ and $n$ is the length of the string $Q$. For the special case of $k=1$, Nogami and Terauchi~[MFCS 2025] gave an $O(n^2m^2)$ time and $O(n+ m^2)$ space algorithm. On the other hand, Nogami, Nakamura, and Terauchi~[arXiv 2026] gave a conditional lower bound, showing that we cannot solve the problem in $O(n^{2-\epsilon} \mathrm{poly}(m))$ for any $\epsilon > 0$ assuming the orthogonal vector hypothesis. Our main result is a new algorithm that runs in $O(n^2m)$ time and uses $O(nm)$ space. This improves the above results (by a factor of $km$ and $m$, respectively) and the former's space bound (by a factor of $nm$). We also show how to extend our algorithm to handle a slightly more general class of ordered and single-nested rewbs.

</details>


### [80] [High-Multiplicity Bin Packing is FPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16923)
*Tomohiro Koana,Soh Kumabe*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了高重数装箱问题（Bin Packing）以物品类型数 d 为参数是否为固定参数可解（FPT）的长期公开问题，给出了确定性 O*(2^{d^{O(d)}}) 时间算法，方法是将装箱建模为至多 (d+1)d^d 个变量的整数线性规划（ILP），按配置模 d 的余数分类，并利用整数分解性质保证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装箱问题是经典 NP 难问题。对于 d 种物品类型的高重数版本（尺寸和数量均以二进制编码），Goemans 和 Rothvoss (JACM 2020) 已知 XP 算法（运行时间 2^{O(d)} 出现在输入长度的指数中），但是否关于 d 是 FPT 一直是中心性公开问题。本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 1. 将装箱建模为整数线性规划（ILP），变量数至多 D=(d+1)d^d 个。
2. 一个"箱配置"记录一箱中各类型物品的数量。按配置坐标模 d 的余数 r∈{0,…,d-1}^d 对配置分类，每类用 1 个变量记箱数 n_r 和 d 个变量记物品总量 z_r。
3. 关键引理：每个凸包 P_r（类 r 配置的凸包）具有整数分解性质（IDP）——任何 nP_r 中的整数点都可写为 P_r 中 n 个整数点之和（基于 Carathéodory 定理证明）。
4. 通过强分离 oracle 与 Lenstra 算法结合求解 ILP：分离 oracle 通过 Lenstra 算法对 P_r 上的优化求解实现（P_r 上优化化为 d 变量有界整数规划），最终运行时间为 O*(2^{d^{O(d)}})。

Result: 证明 Bin Packing 可在 O*(2^{d^{O(d)}}) 确定性时间内判定，给出确定性 FPT 算法。ILP 变量数为 D=(d+1)d^d，oracle 时间为 2^{O(d³)}(L+ℓ)^{O(1)}，代入 Lenstra 类算法得总复杂度。在 ETH 下，下界表明任何 FPT 算法 f(d) 须满足 f(d)=2^{2^{Ω(d)}}，本结果与此下界一致。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了高重数装箱以物品类型数 d 为参数是否为 FPT 的长期开放问题，给出 O*(2^{d^{O(d)}}) 时间确定性算法。核心思想是利用模 d 余数分类与整数分解性质将问题压缩为变量数仅依赖于 d 的 ILP，再用 Lenstra 型算法求解。结果与已知的 ETH 下界相吻合。

Abstract: Bin packing asks whether a collection of items can be packed into at most a given number of bins of a given capacity. We consider the high-multiplicity setting with $d$ distinct item sizes, in which both the item sizes and the number of items of each size are encoded in binary. Goemans and Rothvos (JACM 2020) gave an XP algorithm parameterized by $d$. Whether this problem is fixed-parameter tractable (FPT) in $d$ has remained a central open problem. We resolve this question by giving a deterministic $O^*(2^{d^{O(d)}})$-time algorithm. We formulate bin packing as an integer linear program (ILP) with at most $(d+1)d^d$ variables. A bin configuration records the number of items of each type in one bin. We partition these configurations by their coordinate remainders modulo $d$. For each class, we use one variable for the bin count and $d$ variables for the total item counts. The convex hull of each class has the integer decomposition property, which guarantees that every feasible ILP solution corresponds to a packing.

</details>


### [81] [Rank-One Matrix Discrepancy and Algorithmic Kadison--Singer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17266)
*Ekene Ezeunala,Haotian Jiang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文给出确定多项式时间算法求解秩一矩阵差异问题（rank-one matrix discrepancy），并由此得到 Kadison-Singer 问题（KS₂ 形式）的确定多项式时间算法，常数为 13/2。算法基于势函数法，势函数由半正定规划定义的矩阵部分 R(x) 和坐标预算 ψ_i=(1-x_i²)^{1/3} 组成，每步保证势单调下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kadison-Singer 问题在 Weaver 的 KS₂ 差异论等价形式下，长期仅存在非构造性证明（Marcus–Spielman–Srivastava 使用多项式交错族），以及先前 Anari–Oveis Gharan–Saberi–Srivastava 的 2^{Õ(n^{1/3})} 时间算法。获得确定多项式时间算法并给出通用常数是公开的核心问题。

Method: 算法将问题转化为对分数着色 x∈[-1,1]^N 求 S(x)=∑x_i A_i 的谱控制。势函数 Ψ(x)=R(x)+λΦ(x) 中 R(x) 是一个半正定规划的最优值，约束通过正线性映射 η_x(Z)=c∑ψ_i A_i Z A_i（作为"水库"）耦合两个约束，使冻结坐标时可释放 PSD 槽位。每步尝试三类移动——端点移动、Hessian 最小特征方向、坐标梯度方向——并取使势下降最多的一个。核心分析引理 3.2 证明非终止状态总存在下降步，其中"light" 情形通过构造协方差 Σ 和利用 κ-正则引理（Bansal 的协方差引理）得到 Hessian 负特征值。"heavy" 情形则直接端点移动即可。势下降量逆多项式，故迭代次数多项式。

Result: 对秩一 Hermitian 矩阵 H_1,…,H_N，确定多项式时间算法找到符号 s∈{±1}^N 满足 ‖∑s_i H_i‖≤13‖∑H_i²‖^{1/2}。推论：对 ∑v_i v_i*=I 且 ‖v_i‖²≤δ 的向量，给出 [N]=S₁∪S₂ 的划分使 ‖∑_{i∈S_j} v_i v_i* - I/2‖≤(13/2)√δ，即在 KS₂(13/2) 意义下解决了 Kadison-Singer 问题。常数 13 未做优化；先前 MSS 存在性界 (1+√(2δ))²/2 仍更强。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出 Kadison-Singer 问题（Weaver 的 KS₂ 差异论形式）的确定多项式时间算法，统一常数约为 13/2。技术核心是耦合水库半正定势函数及其与自由概率上谱边的联系，证明了势在每步多项式量下降。结合 Hessian 负曲率与端点吸收两种机制，覆盖所有非终止状态。常数非最优，但将运行时间从次指数改进到多项式，且方法具有一般性。

Abstract: We give a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm that, given rational Hermitian matrices $H_1,\dots,H_N$ of rank at most one, finds signs $s\in\{\pm1\}^N$ with $\|\sum_i s_i H_i\|\le 13\|\sum_i H_i^2\|^{1/2}$. As a corollary, for vectors $v_i$ with $\sum_i v_iv_i^*=I$ and $\|v_i\|^2\le\delta$, the signs yield a partition $[N] = S_1 \cup S_2$ such that each part satisfies $\|\sum_{i \in S_j} v_i v_i^* - \frac{I}{2}\| \leq \frac{13}{2}\sqrt\delta$ for $j = 1,2$. This gives a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm for the Kadison--Singer problem, in Weaver's equivalent discrepancy-theoretic $\mathsf{KS}_2$ formulation, with a universal constant.

</details>


### [82] [High Probability Streaming Lower Bounds for $F_2$ Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17286)
*William Swartworth,David P. Woodruff,Samson Zhou*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了插入型数据流模型中 $F_2$ 估计问题关于失败概率 $\delta$ 的最优空间复杂度问题，给出了紧的高概率下界，并提出了在有界频率流和稀疏流结构假设下的改进算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Braverman 和 Zamir [STOC 2025] 解决了常数失败概率下 $F_2$ 估计的空间复杂度，但关于失败参数 $\delta$ 的最优依赖关系仍未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白，确定 $\delta$ 的精确依赖关系。

Method: 引入新的抗噪声通信原语 EMostlyDISJ（Exam Mostly Set Disjointness），证明其单向下界为 $\Omega(\frac{m}{t}\log\frac{1}{\delta})$；将其嵌入到多尺度归约框架中获得流下界。同时使用连续 $F_0$ 跟踪的子采样方法和近似 Morris 计数器的两阶段草图算法。

Result: 主要下界：$\Omega(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}\log\frac{\varepsilon\sqrt{n}}{\log(1/\delta)})$。有界频率流算法：$O(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\log^2\frac{B}{\varepsilon}\log\frac{1}{\delta}(\log B+\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}))+O(\log\frac{n}{\delta})$。稀疏流算法：$O(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}(\log\frac{k}{\varepsilon}+\log\log m)+\log n\cdot\log\frac{1}{\delta})$。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了 $F_2$ 估计在高概率（$1-\delta$）设置下的空间复杂度，明确了失败概率的最优对数依赖关系，并展示了在自然结构假设下（频率有界、流稀疏）可以获得更好的算法保证。

Abstract: Estimating the second frequency moment ($F_2$) of an underlying frequency vector is a fundamental problem in the streaming model. While recent work by Braverman and Zamir [STOC 2025] resolved the space complexity for constant failure probability in the insertion-only model, the optimal dependence on the failure parameter $\delta$ remained open. We close this gap by proving a tight high-probability lower bound of $\Omega\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}\,\log\frac{\varepsilon\sqrt{n}}{\log(1/\delta)}\right)$ for $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-approximate $F_2$ estimation. The key challenge is the failure of prior multi-scale direct sum arguments under noise sensitivity. We introduce a noise-robust communication primitive, Exam Mostly Set Disjointness, and prove an $\Omega\left(\frac{m}{t}\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right)$ one-way lower bound. Embedding this into a multi-scale reduction yields the correct $\log(1/\delta)$ dependence. We also give two complementary algorithms under natural structure assumptions. For streams with frequency bound $B$, we design a subsampling method using continuous $F_0$ tracking that replaces a $\log(n)$ factor with $\text{polylog}(B)$. For $k$-sparse streams, we develop a two-stage sketch using approximate Morris counters, replacing $\log n$ with $\log k$ and achieving a further $\log\log m$ dependence on stream length.

</details>


### [83] [Online Allocation using Few Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17343)
*Matthew Faw,Sahil Singla,Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗序下基于少量样本的在线分配问题，提出了一种通用框架，将随机序(RO)算法转化为p-Preview模型算法，统一解决了SSPI和p-Sample两种采样模型，在大预算/大makespan条件下获得近乎最优的(1±ε)竞争比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSPI和p-Sample模型下的在线分配算法是问题特定的，且在ε、m、n的依赖上存在多项式差距。本文旨在弥合这一差距，验证少量离线信息是否足以恢复随机序模型中的最优保证。

Method: 核心方法是构建"在线历史无关"(OnlineHI)算法：在离线阶段对样本按随机序运行RO算法(记为RoBB)并记录历史；在在线阶段，将每个请求的决策基于对应样本的离线历史(而非在线历史)做出。利用tangent序列性质和鞅浓度不等式(Freedman/Bernstein)将RO执行转移到对抗序执行。对资源分配的关键挑战(值与预算耗尽的相关性)通过Cauchy-Schwarz和二阶矩界解决。

Result: 主要结果：(1)SSPI模型：资源分配预算需Ω(ε⁻²log(m/ε))；广义负载均衡makespan需Ω(ε⁻²log(m/δ))。(2)p-Sample模型：预算/makespan需Ω(p⁻¹ε⁻²log(m/ε))或Ω(p⁻²ε⁻²log(m/δ))。(3)匹配的下界证明这些依赖在p、ε、log m上是最优的。显著改进了[Ghuge-Singla-Wang STOC'25]和[Gupta-Molinaro SODA'26]的结果，并去除了对n的依赖。

Conclusion: 本文证明，仅需每个分布一个样本(SSPI)或随机p-比例样本(p-Sample)，就足以在对抗序下获得与随机序模型几乎匹配的最优在线分配保证。统一框架将RO算法作为黑盒调用，简化了算法设计并拓宽了适用范围（包括打包-覆盖多选LP和定价查询模型）。

Abstract: We study online allocation problems where $n$ requests over $m$ resources arrive in an adversarial order and must be served immediately and irrevocably. This framework captures both Online Resource Allocation, where the goal is to maximize value subject to resource budgets, and Online Load Balancing, where the goal is to minimize the makespan. We seek $(1\pm\epsilon)$-competitive algorithms in the large-budget or large-makespan regime. We consider a sampling model that generalizes the following two well-studied sampling models. In the Single-Sample Prophet Inequality ($\mathsf{SSPI}$) model, request $t$ is drawn from an unknown distribution $\mathcal{D}_t$, and the algorithm is given one independent sample from each $\mathcal{D}_t$ before the online phase. In the $p$-$\mathsf{Sample}$ model, the requests are adversarial, but a uniformly random $p$-fraction is revealed upfront as training data. Although near-optimal algorithms are known in the easier random-order model ($\mathsf{RO}$), where the requests arrive in a uniformly random order, prior algorithms for $\mathsf{SSPI}$ and $p$-$\mathsf{Sample}$ were problem-specific and incurred substantially worse dependencies on $\epsilon$, $m$, and $n$. Our main contribution is a general framework that converts $\mathsf{RO}$ algorithms into algorithms for the $p$-$\mathsf{Preview}$ model, a model that generalizes both $\mathsf{SSPI}$ and $p$-$\mathsf{Sample}$. As consequences, we obtain near-optimal bounds for Online Resource Allocation, generalized Online Load Balancing, and online mixed packing-covering problems in these adversarial-order sampling models, significantly improving the bounds of [Ghuge, Singla, Wang (STOC'25)] and [Gupta and Molinaro (SODA'26)].

</details>


### [84] [The Classical Weisfeiler-Leman Algorithm Stabilizes in $O(n)$ Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17364)
*Simon Döring,Daniel Neuen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明经典（2维）Weisfeiler-Leman算法至多经5(n-1)轮即稳定，将此前O(n log n)的上界改进为紧的线性界，从而与Fürer给出的Ω(n)下界相匹配。作者还据此将k≥3维WL算法的迭代上界改进为O(n^{k-1}/(k-2)!+n^{k-2})，方法是将高维问题投影到二维情形，并利用一种新的“可扩展乘法性”精化序列刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weisfeiler-Leman（WL）算法是图同构测试及相关理论与应用（如逻辑、证明复杂性、组合优化、机器学习）中的核心工具。算法在稳定前所需的迭代轮数是决定其表达力和实际效率的关键参数。此前2维WL算法的已知上界为O(n log n)，而Fürer早已证明存在需要Ω(n)轮才能稳定的图实例，因此上界与下界之间存在显著差距亟待弥补。

Method: 作者引入“乘法性2-精化序列”概念，并将每次WL迭代产生的着色序列对应为矩阵集序列及对应的C*-代数结构。随后证明任意此类序列长度不超过5(n-1)：先将最末非满代数分解为直和块形式（类似Wedderburn-Artin定理），再利用代数结构与Paz定理相关结果对剩余迭代步数做递归估计，得到线性界。对于k≥3维情形，作者引入“可扩展乘法性k-精化序列”并通过向最后两个坐标投影将问题归约到二维情形，从而直接由二维结果推出k维界。

Result: 主要结果1：2维WL算法至多5(n-1)轮稳定，且此上界对一般乘法性2-精化序列均成立。主要结果2：k维WL算法的迭代步数为O(n^{k-1}/(k-2)!+n^{k-2})，对所有k≥3成立，且对所引入的更大类精化序列而言这些上界本质上是紧的。此外，作者指出若Paz猜想成立，常数可进一步从5改进到4.5。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了经典WL算法稳定轮数的线性上界问题，弥补了与已知Ω(n)下界之间的差距，并给出更简洁统一的高维推广途径。其结果在逻辑C³量词深度、k-WL的迭代复杂度以及Babai拟多项式时间同构测试等场景中均具有直接推论。方法的核心——将着色序列转化为矩阵代数结构并借助Wedderburn-Artin分解及Paz定理——具有独立的技术价值。

Abstract: The classical Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm (also known as the $2$-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm) is a simple combinatorial algorithm that was originally designed as a heuristic for the graph isomorphism problem. However, it has also numerous connections to other areas such as algebraic graph theory, logics, proof complexity, combinatorial optimization and machine learning. We prove that the classical Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm terminates after $5(n-1)$ iterations. This improves over the previous best upper bound of $O(n \log n)$ by Lichter, Ponomarenko and Schweitzer [LICS 2019], and asymptotically matches the known lower bound of $\Omega(n)$ by Fürer [ICALP 2001]. Additionally, building on our results for the $2$-dimensional case, we obtain an improved upper bound of $O(n^{k-1}/(k-2)! + n^{k-2})$ on the number of iterations performed by the $k$-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm, for every $k \geq 3$. Our arguments actually hold for a larger class of sequences of colorings of $k$-tuples; in this larger class our upper bounds are essentially tight for all $k \geq 3$.

</details>


### [85] [PrecPack: An Efficient Open-Source Exact Solver for Bin Packing with Generalized Precedence Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17368)
*Sunkanghong Wang,Zhengzhong Ricky You,Roberto Baldacci,Baichuan Mo,Hu Qin,Lijun Wei,Zhou Xu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: PrecPack是一个面向带广义优先级约束的装箱问题（BPP-GP）的统一开源精确求解器，基于分支-界定-记忆（BBR）框架扩展而来，支持任意非负优先级权重，自然涵盖SALBP-I和BPP-P两个经典特例。该求解器在经典装配线基准测试中证明了更多实例的最优性，且平均计算时间显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带广义优先级约束的装箱问题（BPP-GP）在装配线平衡和资源调度中具有重要应用，是一个强NP难问题。现有精确算法主要针对经典特例（SALBP-I和BPP-P），而一般BPP-GP此前缺乏高效的开源精确求解器，通用整数规划模型又难以扩展，亟需一种兼顾性能、可复用性和可验证性的统一求解方法。

Method: 1. 将BBR框架扩展至任意非负优先级权重的BPP-GP，通过广义状态表示（包含分离元组）记录跨未来箱的优先级限制；2. 引入强制空箱转移以处理权重大于1的情形；3. 提出分离元组支配、广义最大负载支配和标记后继项支配等支配规则；4. 使用基于冲突感知的下界，包括前驱路径界、单机界、闭包界和冲突感知BINLB；5. 根节点列生成采用冲突背包定价和定点算术，确保对偶界数值有效；6. 采用C++实现，提供统一API、命令行接口、独立赋值检查和可复现的批处理脚本，核心求解无需商业软件。

Result: 在SALBP-I Otto-100基准上，PrecPack在350秒内证明了525个实例中523个的最优性（vs. 三个基线BBR实现的456-457个），平均计算时间1.79秒（vs. 50.39-55.18秒）；在BPP-P和BPP-GP的多个基准组上，证明最优的实例数更多，平均间隙更小；MIT许可证发布于GitHub。

Conclusion: PrecPack填补了BPP-GP精确求解的开源空白，通过统一的BBR扩展同时高效处理SALBP-I、BPP-P和BPP-GP三类问题，计算性能和可复现性均达到领先水平。未来工作将向带优先级约束的二维切割下料问题扩展。

Abstract: Efficient resource use in packing and assembly-line applications requires decisions that jointly account for capacity and precedence constraints. The strongly NP-hard bin packing problem with generalized precedence constraints (BPP-GP) models such decisions by minimizing the number of ordered, capacitated bins required to pack weighted items, even when precedence requirements span multiple bins. Existing exact algorithms primarily focus on classical special cases, whereas general BPP-GP has been addressed only via compact integer models and heuristics, with no efficient open-source exact solver. We present PrecPack, a unified exact solver that extends branch-bound-and-remember (BBR) to arbitrary nonnegative precedence weights and naturally specializes to the classical cases. Generalized states capture restrictions that remain active across future bins, which are addressed through branching, dominance, and conflict-aware lower bounds. Root column generation uses fixed-point arithmetic to compute numerically valid dual bounds for pruning or to prove optimality. To support reuse and verification, we provide common programming and command-line interfaces, independent assignment checking, explicit termination statuses, and reproducible batch execution; the core procedures require no commercial software. In same-machine, single-threaded comparisons on classic assembly-line benchmarks, more instances are proven optimal, and average computing times are substantially reduced relative to leading source-available BBR implementations. Further comparisons with published benchmark results for bin packing with precedence constraints and BPP-GP also show that more instances were proved optimal and that reported average gaps were smaller on most benchmark sets. PrecPack is released under the MIT License at this https URL .

</details>


### [86] [Pseudometric-Weighted Correlation Clustering via Spectral Preclustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17403)
*Chenglin Fan,Dahoon Lee,Euiwoong Lee*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对伪度量加权相关聚类问题，对每个固定 ε>0 给出了随机多项式时间 (2+ε)-近似算法，优于此前已知的 10/3 近似比。算法通过谱预聚类控制可接受对的总权重，结合聚类LP框架和有界子聚类LP松弛的关联舍入采样，最终通过因子-2舍入得到近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪度量加权相关聚类的此前最佳近似比为 10/3（Fan、Lee、Lee），且该因子在标准LP枢轴框架内已被证明是最优的。因此，要改进这一保证必须超越该框架，转而采用Cao等人提出的聚类LP方法，但加权情形下需要同时控制可接受对的总权重和加权边际误差，面临新的技术挑战。

Method: 方法包含三个阶段：(1) 谱预聚类——利用常数步随机游走的热启动和聚合Ptolemy不等式构建可计算的可接受性矩阵Γ，使得证人所聚类原子间的所有对均可接受，且可接受对总权重为 poly(1/ε)·OPT；(2) 有界子聚类LP松弛——利用局部阶 r=poly(1/ε) 矩和关联采样（含随机停止时间），通过Pinsker不等式和信息论链式法则控制加权成对边际误差，并利用三角不等式将误差归咎于可接受对而非总输入权重；(3) 显式聚类LP构造——通过重复采样和原子级覆盖修正产生多项式支撑的可行解，目标值不超过 (1+ε)OPT。

Result: 主要结果：对每个固定 ε>0，存在随机多项式时间算法以至少 1-1/n 的概率返回成本至多 (2+ε)OPT 的聚类（定理1.1）。辅助结果：以至少 1-1/n 的概率构造出显式可行聚类LP解，支撑集大小为 M+|K|，目标值至多 (1+ε)OPT（定理1.2/6.1）。这将Fan-Lee-Lee的10/3近似改进为 (2+ε) 近似。

Conclusion: 伪度量性在证明中起两个关键作用：聚合三角不等式和Ptolemy型不等式提供总权重为 O_ε(OPT) 的预clustering保证；三角不等式在采样分析中再次用于将加权成对边际误差归咎于可接受质量。固定时域信息论论证和原子级覆盖修正产生显式有限支撑的聚类LP解。任意低于2的改进都需在舍入阶段利用额外结构或替换此舍入步骤；标准LP枢轴的10/3限制不适用于此处使用的聚类LP松弛。

Abstract: We study pseudometric-weighted correlation clustering, where every pair of vertices carries a nonnegative disagreement weight and the weights satisfy the triangle inequality. For every fixed $\varepsilon>0$, we give a randomized polynomial-time $(2+\varepsilon)$-approximation, improving the previously best known factor of $10/3$. Our algorithm extends the cluster-LP framework for unweighted correlation clustering to pseudometric weights. The weighted setting requires controlling both the total weight of admissible pairs and the weighted error in pairwise marginals. Our spectral preclustering preserves a near-optimal solution while bounding the total admissible weight by $\operatorname{poly}(1/\varepsilon)\mathrm{OPT}$, where $\mathrm{OPT}$ is the optimal clustering cost. An aggregated Ptolemy-type inequality yields a degree-product bound and a warm start for random walks within witness clusters, allowing the construction to use walks of constant length. We sample clusters from a bounded sub-cluster relaxation using correlated rounding with a randomized stopping time. An entropy bound and the triangle inequality charge the weighted marginal error to the admissible pairs rather than to the total input weight. Repeated sampling and atom-wise coverage corrections produce an explicit feasible cluster-LP solution supported on polynomially many clusters, with value at most $(1+\varepsilon)\mathrm{OPT}$. After rescaling $\varepsilon$, factor-$2$ rounding gives the stated approximation guarantee.

</details>


### [87] [Determinant maximization subject to a partition matroid constraint via stable distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17407)
*Yihang Sun,Jan Vondrak*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对带划分拟阵约束的行列式最大化问题，给出了首个达到 e^{-O(d)} 近似比的多项式时间算法（此前最佳为 e^{O(d³)} 或 m^{O(m)}）。核心创新是引入 1/2-稳定分布，将 Nikolov-Singh 鞍点松弛转化为更易处理的多线性松弛，并通过对数稳定混沌不等式和杠杆得分界完成分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行列式最大化在数据稀疏化、机器学习等领域有重要应用。划分拟阵约束比基数约束更具表达力，但此前缺乏达到最优 e^{O(d)} 近似的多项式算法：Nikolov-Singh 鞍点松弛只能估计最优值，不能产生可行解；现有算法要么近似比为 e^{O(d³)}，要么依赖部分大小产生 m^{O(m)} 因子。

Method: 方法分三步：(1) 求解 Nikolov-Singh 鞍点松弛得到分数解 (x, z)；(2) 通过 1/2-稳定分布 D_{1/2}（由 Z = 1/(2X₁²) - 1/(2X₂²) 生成）定义 w_i = Σ_j y_{ij} v_{ij}，其中 y_{ij} = x²_{ij} Z_{ij} / Σ_k x²_{ik} |Z_{ik}|，将分数解转换为多线性松弛形式；(3) 利用行列式多重线性性贪心选取每个部分的最优向量。关键技术是证明"对数稳定混沌不等式"（引理 4.3）：E[log|Q(x)|] ≥ 2 log S(x) + mκ，其中 Q(x) 为带符号随机组合，S(x) 为带绝对值的确定性量。m ≥ d 情形还需利用 KKT 条件定义杠杆得分 μ_i，约束所有 d-子集上的局部最优值（M_T ≤ R ∏ μ_i）。一般划分拟阵通过随机排列缩放复制向量归约到简单情形。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 简单划分拟秩 m ≥ d 时，e^{-6d}（m=d）或 e^{-9d}（m>d）近似的 Las Vegas/Monte Carlo 算法；(2) m ≤ d 时 m 维体积的 e^{O(m)} 近似算法；(3) 一般划分拟阵 e^{-14d} 近似（未优化常数）。这些结果匹配了 Nikolov-Singh 估计算法的近似比（指数中常数除外），解决了 2016 年以来该方向的核心开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过 1/2-稳定分布这一新颖工具，首次实现了划分拟阵约束下行列式最大化问题的最优 e^{O(d)} 近似。算法简单优雅，分析基于对数稳定混沌不等式和杠杆得分技巧。作者在 AI 使用声明中指出，核心变换思路由 GPT-5.6 Sol 提出，体现了人机协作在数学研究中的潜力。

Abstract: Given vectors $v_i \in {\mathbb R}^d$, we consider the problem of choosing a set $I$ independent in a partition matroid in order to maximize the determinant $\det (\sum_{i \in I} v_i v_i^T)$. Our main result is a polynomial-time approximation algorithm that finds a solution of value $det ( \sum_{i \in I} v_{i} v_{i}^T) \geq e^{-O(d)} OPT$, where $OPT = \max_{I^*} det ( \sum_{i \in I^*} v_{i} v_{i}^T)$. For partition matroids of rank $m \leq d$, we give a similar result for approximating the $m$-dimensional volume spanned by the chosen vectors, within a factor of $e^{O(m)}$. This matches earlier known algorithms that estimate the optimal value but do not find the corresponding solution, up to a constant in the exponent. Similar to these estimation algorithms, our algorithm is based on the saddle-point relaxation proposed by Nikolov and Singh. A new ingredient is a randomized transformation based on $1/2$-stable distributions, which converts the saddle-point relaxation into a more convenient multilinear relaxation.

</details>


### [88] [Stuffed IBLTs: Optimal Linear Multiset Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17487)
*Jonas Klausen,Rasmus Pagh,Stefan Walzer*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了"Stuffed IBLT"，一种用于精确恢复向量的线性草图（linear sketch）。当向量的L0范数≤n、L∞范数≤L时，可在常数时间内更新，O(n)时间内解码，失败概率为n^{-c}，且空间使用在1+ε因子内逼近信息论最优，显著优于现有的IBLT等方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Invertible Bloom Lookup Table (IBLT) 等线性草图在空间、时间与错误概率之间存在权衡瓶颈。作者旨在突破这些瓶颈，设计一种在允许无约束更新的情况下仍能以接近信息论最优的空间、高效更新与解码的精确恢复草图。

Method: 核心技术组合：
1) Walzer 的空间耦合技术（SODA '21）；
2) Houen、Pagh 与 Walzer 的纯度启发式（purity heuristic, SOSA '23）；
3) Belazzougui 等及 Fleischhacker 等提出的"backyarding"技术。
作者将这些技术精妙结合以消除过往方法的瓶颈，给出 Stuffed IBLT 结构，并基于剥离（peeling）类方法给出依赖参数c的显式下界，证明c的依赖是最优的。

Result: 对于任意正常数c、ε，当n足够大且u ≥ n^{1+Ω(1)}时，Stuffed IBLT 的空间使用在1+ε倍内逼近信息论最优，支持常数时间更新，O(n)时间解码，失败概率n^{-c}。在功能相似的现有构造中（包括IBLT），其空间/时间/错误概率权衡得到全面改进。此外，证明在剥离类方法范围内，对c的依赖是最优的。

Conclusion: Stuffed IBLT 的性能本质上已是可期望的最佳（仅在参数c与ε的依赖上仍有差距）。通过三种技术（空间耦合、纯度启发式、backyarding）的组合，成功消除了过去方法的瓶颈，为流式算法与（多）集合调和问题提供了更优的线性草图方案。

Abstract: A \emph{linear sketch} is a randomized linear mapping of a vector $v$ to a lower dimensional sketch vector, designed to preserve relevant information about $v$. We consider sketches of vectors $v \in Z^u$ (for $u \in N$), designed for exact recovery of $v$ from its sketch. Concretely, our \emph{Stuffed IBLT} is a linear sketch configured with a capacity $n \in N$ and a multiplicity limit $L \in N$ and will recover $v$ with high probability whenever $||v||_0 \leq n$ and $||v||_\infty \leq L$. The sketch can be maintained efficiently under unrestricted updates to $v$, i.e., $v$ is not subject to any constraints in between decoding requests. This makes the sketch useful for streaming algorithms and for solving the (multi)set reconciliation problem. For any positive constants $c$, $\epsilon$, and for large enough $n$ and $u \geq n^{1+\Omega(1)}$, the space usage of a Stuffed IBLT is within a factor $1+\epsilon$ from the information-theoretic optimum while allowing updates in constant time, and decoding in time $O(n)$ with failure probability $n^{-c}$. This improves the space/time/error probability trade-off over all prior constructions with similar functionality, including the Invertible Bloom Lookup Table (IBLT). The performance of the Stuffed IBLT is essentially the best we could hope for, up to the dependence on $c$ and $\epsilon$. We make the dependence on these parameters explicit, and further show a lower bound demonstrating that the dependence on $c$ is optimal within the class of peeling-based approaches. Our improvement comes from a careful combination of Walzer's spatial coupling technique (SODA '21), the purity heuristic of Houen, Pagh, and Walzer (SOSA '23), and backyarding (Belazzougui, Kucherov, and Walzer, ESA '24; Fleischhacker, Green Larsen, Obremski, and Simkin, ICALP '24), allowing us to eliminate bottlenecks of past approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [89] [Optimal Pruning for Neural Architectures using Fisher Information Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16129)
*David S. Berman,Yen-Yu Fu,Edward Hirst,Thelma Chiwete Obirai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Fisher信息度量的测地距离作为参数剪枝的重要性度量。通过在模型空间中将参数置零解释为有限位移，推导出层次化的剪枝准则（迭代式/全局式/精确路径），并在MLP与ViT、Mnist与CIFAR-10上验证，Fisher距离家族在所有测试平台上均优于幅度剪枝与纯Fisher准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝准则存在根本缺陷：幅度剪枝使用无统计意义的欧氏度量，Fisher剪枝仅评估局部敏感性而忽略参数必须移动的有限距离。两者实为单一几何量的互补片段，需要一个统一且数学上可论证的框架来指导剪枝。

Method: 将Fisher信息矩阵作为模型空间的自然Riemann度量，通过在坐标线上对度量积分计算参数到零超曲面的测地距离作为重要性度量。导出四个层次的近似：(1) one-shot（固定度量）、(2) iterative（每步重计算量）、(3) exact path（沿坐标线解析度量）、(4) global（沿原点射线平均）。对角化FIM，采用前向模式导数精确计算单对角元，并行化探测过程。

Result: 在四种架构-数据集组合（全连接网络与ViT×MNIST与CIFAR-10）的完整0-100%剪枝范围、五次随机种子下：Fisher距离家族在准确率和MCC两个指标上均优于幅度剪枝与Fisher剪枝。幅度优势达1.02×-1.41×（MCC达1.03×-1.58×）；Fisher单独使用在三个测试平台上劣于幅度剪枝。最优迭代式方案在统计上与精确路径方案无差异，但成本低37-10720倍。路径分辨率K=2即足够，K值不需调优。

Conclusion: Fisher测地距离为剪枝提供了数学上严谨且经验上优越的重要性度量。幅度剪枝与Fisher剪枝并非竞争启发式，而是单一几何对象的互补片段。迭代式Fisher距离（每步一次Fisher评估）是推荐的剪枝准则：最便宜、跨架构最稳健、与精确计算在所有测试平台上不可区分。该几何框架为AI模型的稀疏化提供了经过验证的理论依据。

Abstract: A new scheme for parameter pruning is introduced, derived from the differential-geometric distance in model space. Pruning a parameter sets its value to zero, representing a displacement of the model to the hypersurface on which that parameter vanishes. The minimal distance from the unpruned model to this hypersurface is naturally computed via the geodesic distance in the model space as determined by the Fisher information metric. This distance determines the true change in the model, and its performance, under pruning. By analysing progressively more faithful approximations of this geodesic distance a natural hierarchy of optimality for pruning methods is determined. This starts with the traditional magnitude pruning, then develops into new more sophisticated and effective pruning schemes. The method is demonstrated for both fully-connected networks and vision transformers, on MNIST and CIFAR-10, over the complete $0$-$100\%$ pruning range and across five random seeds. It outperforms pruning by parameter magnitude and by the local Fisher information alone in every architecture and dataset combination considered, on both accuracy and the Matthews correlation coefficient. Additionally, analysis of different levels of geodesic approximation produces intermediate pruning schemes that are computationally efficient and maintain near-optimal performance. This geometric picture supplies not only a state-of-the-art pruning methodology for AI models, but also a verified and mathematically-motivated justification for pruning schemes.

</details>


### [90] [Safe Error Correction for Language Models: Frozen-Base Adjustment with Capability Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16145)
*Gautam Kishore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CRN v2：在冻结Gemma 4 E2B模型上添加约34M参数的logit级校正模块，通过SFT+参考无关DPO训练，能纠正基模型错误且不损失通用能力；与LoRA基线对比揭示"纠错-能力保持"权衡，并通过消融验证KL保留项的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）在纠错时虽能提升目标域表现，但会引发灾难性遗忘、损害模型原有能力（即"纠错-能力权衡"）。本文研究是否可以在完全冻结基模型的前提下，通过外挂小型校正模块实现纠错而不损害基础能力。

Method: CRN v2架构为低秩瓶颈校正模块：读取冻结基模型最终隐藏态h^(L)，通过W_down(1536→128)和W_up(V=262144→128)产生加性logit偏移，叠加到基模型logits上，配以可学习的标量门控g初始化近零。训练两阶段：(1)带答案掩码+锚定加权交叉熵与KL保留项(λ=0.1)的SFT 2000步；(2)参考无关DPO 500步（直接对比正确答案与冻结基模型错误答案）。同时实验了早期层隐藏态注入变体（层4/7、rank 512）以及多深度logit校正。

Result: 在60题CEHRI考试上：CRN v2纠正53.3%基模型错误（改写版43.3%），且在MMLU/BoolQ(N=200)和car-wash(N=8)上零能力退化；同预算LoRA基线（6.6M参数）纠错率达83.3%但能力损失17-75pp（包括car-wash从75%降至0%）。KL项λ从0.1降至0.01使纠错率降至35.0%，证明锚定项的关键作用。深层注入实验中，层7 SFT-only达50.0%/55.8%，层4降至30.0%/28.3%，多深度logit校正仅40%，延长训练(5k SFT+2k DPO)仍维持53.3%，均未超越logit校正结果。

Conclusion: 冻结基模型+logit级校正+KL锚定这一设计原则能实现"无能力退化的纠错"，与LoRA形成对比鲜明的权衡关系。约53%是已测试配置中的最佳结果，虽不构成证明性上界，但符合"冻结层不会放大加性校正"的机制理解。设计原则模型无关，可应用于任何冻结语言模型。论文强调诚实陈述局限：评测规模小、测试题与训练数据有重叠、深度变体结果多为单次会话观察仅部分独立验证。

Abstract: We study a practical question: can a small correction module fix errors in a frozen language model's outputs without degrading its base capabilities? We propose CRN v2, a lightweight logit-level correction module (~34M trainable parameters, 0.73% of the 4.65B text module) that sits atop a fully frozen Gemma 4 E2B model. The base model is never updated; only the correction module learns, via supervised fine-tuning followed by reference-free DPO on 83,400 error-correction pairs. On a 60-question domain exam (CEHRI: Certified Human-Robot Intelligence, covering facts, arithmetic, and implicit-goal reasoning), CRN v2 corrects 53.3% of base-model errors (reworded variant: 43.3%) while showing no degradation on tested capability benchmarks (MMLU/BoolQ N=200; car-wash N=8). A LoRA baseline at the matched CRN v1 budget (6.6M params, rank 19) achieves 83.3% correction but suffers 30-75% capability loss on the same benchmarks -- the correction-capability tradeoff. An ablation shows that the KL preservation term (lambda=0.1) is critical: lowering it to 0.01 degrades correction to 35.0%. A hidden-state injection variant at earlier layers (1.6M params, SFT-only) reaches 50.0%/55.8% but does not exceed logit correction; shallower injection (layer 4) drops to 30.0%/28.3%; multi-depth logit correction (~35M) reaches only 40%; and longer training (5,000 SFT + 2,000 DPO) stays at 53.3% -- none of the alternative configurations we tested exceeded the rank-128 logit result, consistent with a best-achieved result of ~53% rather than a floor. This is a study of a design principle (frozen base + logit correction + KL anchoring), not a claim of architectural novelty. All code, main-result weights, and evaluation scripts are released (deep variant as code only -- no trained deep checkpoints).

</details>


### [91] [GPEvac: GNN-Based PPO for Adaptive Evacuation Routing During Shooting Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16163)
*Daniel Perkins,Subhadeep Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GPEvac框架，将图神经网络（GNN）与近端策略优化（PPO）相结合，用于射击事件中的实时自适应疏散路径规划。该系统将建筑布局建模为动态图，通过边优先的序列消息传递机制和可学习的虚拟全局节点捕获局部与长距离依赖关系，并利用置换不变的评分机制实现单一策略跨不同拓扑布局的泛化。在仿真实验中，GPEvac在威胁暴露和疏散时间上均优于智能基线策略，且在CPU上仅需14.73毫秒即可完成全局路径推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来大规模枪击事件频发，现有的"跑-藏-战"协议缺乏对多样化建筑环境的适应性，传统的路径规划算法（如Dijkstra、A*）在大规模动态场景下计算不可行，而现有的基于强化学习的疏散方法受限于特定布局且无法应对射击者行为的高度随机性。因此亟需一种可跨布局泛化、实时计算且能应对对抗性不确定性的疏散路由系统。

Method: 1）将建筑建模为动态图，节点代表房间，边代表通道，包含47个节点特征和20个边特征（静态拓扑特征与动态人员/威胁特征）；2）射击者行为建模为随机游走以应对不可预测性；3）提出边优先的序列消息传递GNN（采用PNA聚合器），结合可学习的虚拟全局节点解决大直径图的长距离依赖问题；4）设计多目标奖励函数，包含威胁惩罚（指数衰减）、疏散奖励和时间惩罚；5）采用集中式Critic（均值池化+全局节点）和置换不变的边评分Actor，通过掩码Softmax实现单一策略跨拓扑泛化。

Result: 在无环和环形两种学校布局上训练单一共享策略：1）GPEvac平均威胁暴露时间降至0.427，威胁惩罚降至4.36，显著优于贪心策略（1.274/11.02）和规则基线（0.518/5.19）；2）Wilcoxon符号秩检验确认其优越性具有统计显著性（p<0.05）；3）消融实验证明边更新机制、虚拟全局节点和PNA聚合器均为关键组件；4）推理速度：在Apple M4 Pro CPU上，55-70节点的建筑仅需14.73毫秒（95分位15.46毫秒），亚线性扩展至1600节点；5）奖励比例分析表明威胁/疏散奖励比为0.25时性能最优。

Conclusion: GPEvac是首个可跨多样化建筑拓扑部署单一策略的射击事件疏散路由系统，在威胁暴露最小化方面显著超越人工设计的启发式规则，同时满足实时部署的计算要求（CPU上毫秒级推理）。该框架具有可迁移性，可应用于智能交通、关键基础设施和自适应传感器网络等图结构决策领域。未来工作需解决仿真到现实的迁移、概率观测建模、人群脆弱性保护以及AI可解释性等挑战。

Abstract: The sharp increase in mass shootings underscores an urgent need for systems that guide victims to safety in real time. An effective evacuation system must minimize threat exposure while also accounting for adversarial uncertainty and crowding dynamics. Current methods in the literature are rigidly constrained to layout-specific policies and computationally intractable in large-scale layouts, while practical guidelines simply advise victims to "run", "hide", or "fight". We propose GPEvac: a GNN-based PPO framework that computes adaptive evacuation routes during shooting events. To capture both local and long-distance dependencies, we introduce an edge-first sequential message-passing scheme with a learnable virtual global node. The resulting graph embeddings are integrated into a permutation-invariant scoring mechanism that allows a single learned policy to operate across building layouts of diverse topologies and sizes. Through extensive simulation, we show that GPEvac outperforms intelligent baselines across distinct architectural layouts, significantly reducing total threat exposure. Crucially, the system computes global evacuation routes in just 14.73 ms on local CPU hardware, enabling seamless integration with live surveillance systems. In addition to saving lives during shooting events, the methodologies developed are transferable to other graph-structured decision-making domains, including critical infrastructure, intelligent transportation systems, and adaptive sensor networks.

</details>


### [92] [Position: AI Is Not Ready for Strategic Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16189)
*Mark Riedl,Glenn Matlin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文主张AI（特别是语言模型）尚未准备好用于战略冲突中的高风险决策支持，提出"无安全案例，无高风险使用"的规范，并识别了开放性兵棋推演中的五种特定失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型被快速引入国防、规划和战略决策支持系统（论文记录了26个公开系统案例），作者担忧在缺乏可审计安全案例的情况下，这些系统可能被用于影响实质性战略决策、作战计划和危机响应，从而引发系统性风险。

Method: 作为立场论文（position paper），作者通过：(1) 区分开放性兵棋与封闭博弈的本质差异；(2) 识别语言模型特有的五种失效路径（决策洗白、裁定不透明、角色坍缩、裁定驱动升级、战略想象力缺失）；(3) 论证传统基准测试无法构成安全案例；(4) 借鉴安全工程领域的安全案例（safety case）框架，提出适用于LM兵棋的证据要求和审计规范；(5) 提供26个公开系统的清单作为证据基础。

Result: 论文提出了一个结构化的安全案例框架，包含六个子声明（输出范围、角色分离、重大状态变更溯源、鲁棒性、人工可干预性、总结纪律），并为每个子声明提供了支持证据要求和必须应对的反证。同时提供了COA-GPT等案例分析，展示现有系统与部署级安全案例之间的差距。

Conclusion: 作者主张：(1) 任何LM兵棋系统在缺乏可审计安全案例前不应影响作战条令、政策或危机响应；(2) 开放性兵棋当前应作为压力测试工具，而非安全案例本身；(3) "无安全案例，无高风险使用"应成为领域规范；(4) 安全案例必须具备拒绝能力，明确系统不应被使用的边界；(5) 该规范适用于所有高风险LM社会模拟场景，不仅限于军事领域。

Abstract: Open-ended strategic wargames are high-stakes LM-based social simulations: they model adversaries, institutions, escalation, plan brittleness, doctrine, and crisis response. Language models (LMs) are attractive because they can play agents, generate scenario branches, adjudicate ambiguous actions, and summarize lessons, but the same affordances make open-ended roles dangerous: model language determines both what an actor attempts and what becomes simulated reality. This position paper argues that no LM-enabled wargame should inform planning, doctrine, policy, or crisis response without an auditable safety case, and that the proper use of open-ended wargames today is to stress-test decision-influencing LM agents. We identify five failure modes: decision laundering, adjudication opacity, role collapse, escalation-through-adjudication, and failure of strategic imagination. Ordinary benchmarks cannot establish safety for these settings. Wargames can expose failures as stress tests; they are not themselves safety cases for consequential use.

</details>


### [93] [Calibrate, Then Route: A Measured Study of Learned Request Routing for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16206)
*Srikanta Datta Tumkur,Jay Iyer,Mehar Simhadri,Sai Pavan Kumar,Sai Kapil Kumar,Ramesh Nampelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对分离式LLM服务（prefill与decode分离到不同GPU池）中的请求路由问题，提出了一种基于边际完成时间（marginal completion time）的学习式路由器。该路由器利用四个准入时可获得的特征（精确prompt长度、预测输出长度、准入后KV缓存压力、SLO等级）对候选实例打分，并通过仿真开发策略、在8卡A40 + vLLM + NIXL的真实集群上端到端验证。结果显示，该校准路由器在混合突发流量下获得最高平均goodput（0.864）且方差最低，且只需粗略的输出长度估计即可奏效；但其优势依赖硬件校准、池宽度和流量异构性，在极端资源稀缺或池过窄的场景下会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离式LLM推理虽通过将prefill与decode部署在不同GPU池消除了相互干扰，但仍需为每个请求选择prefill与decode实例。生产系统普遍使用对请求内容无感知的策略（如轮询、最短队列），无法区分短对话和长推理请求，可能导致SLO违约。该研究旨在探讨：基于准入时已有特征的路由策略能否在真实硬件上优于计数式策略，能领先多少，优势边界在哪里，哪个特征最重要。

Method: 1) 提出边际成本路由器：对每个候选decode实例，计算请求的预估完成时间 = 权重×backlog × σ(SLO) + 权重×预测输出长度×τ_decode×(1+ρ×缓存压力)×σ(SLO) + 权重×队列长度；prefill侧类似。2) 硬件校准：通过5分钟基准测试设定τ_pre、τ_decode和SLO目标，使成本项与观测项统一量纲。3) 在确定性离散事件仿真器中开发策略。4) 在真实测试床（8块A40 GPU各运行vLLM 0.12引擎，Qwen2.5-3B模型，KV通过NIXL传输，60k token KV预算，2 prefill + 4 decode主拓扑）上端到端验证，含token数契约、跨GPU greedy一致性、每次运行前缀缓存重置、按负载探测饱和点等机制。

Result: 在三种混合突发流量trace上，校准后的路由器平均goodput达0.864（轮询0.836、最短队列0.835、长度启发式0.847），方差最小；P99 TTFT 0.321s优于轮询(0.337s)和JSQ(0.346s)；在6卡时达到轮询7卡才达到的goodput水平，节省约14% GPU。使用仿真器得到的常数（而非硬件校准）会损失4.5个goodput点和约40%的尾部延迟优势，使路由器退化为队列计数。特征消融显示预测输出长度是最关键特征（去掉后损失3.2-4.7点），缓存压力在当前KV容量下贡献不显著，SLO项存在负载依赖性。预测误差注入到150%时路由仍稳健。然而在3实例池（优势消失，0.816 vs JSQ 0.822）和极端稀缺场景（1 prefill + 2 decode时路由器0.292，轮询0.680）下路由器出现失败模式。仿真器常数偏差达7-13×，连续批处理的加入纠正了物理量但未改善策略排序，揭示路由排名无法仅靠仿真恢复。

Conclusion: 在真实分离式vLLM/NIXL集群上，基于四个准入特征的边际成本路由器在混合流量下提供最高平均goodput和最低跨trace方差，以约14%更少的GPU达到盲路由同等水平，仅需粗略的输出长度估计。硬件校准是方法本身的关键组成部分，校准缺失会使路由器静默地退化为队列计数。优势边界为：池宽度≥4、流量异构、集群争用但未严重过载；窄池和极端稀缺下会出现负载集中失效。仿真与硬件存在结构化差距，无法仅凭仿真恢复硬件实测的路由优势排名。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM serving places compute heavy prefill and memory heavy decode on separate GPU pools. Systems such as DistServe, Splitwise, and Mooncake make this separation fast, but routing still determines which instances handle each request. We study a router that estimates the additional completion time on each instance using exact prompt length, predicted output length, post admission KV cache pressure, and SLO class. We develop the policy in a discrete event simulator and validate it on eight NVIDIA A40 GPUs, each running a vLLM engine, with NIXL transferring KV caches between pools. All workloads run at measured saturation. Across three mixed, bursty arrival traces, the calibrated router achieves the highest mean goodput at 0.864, compared with 0.835 to 0.847 for round robin, least loaded, and a length heuristic. It also shows the lowest variance across traces. It beats round robin and the length heuristic on all three traces and least loaded on two. On the third, it trails by 0.003, within run to run noise. Hardware calibration matters: simulator derived constants cost 4.5 goodput points and roughly 40 percent of the tail latency advantage, reducing the scorer to little more than queue counting. Benefits grow with decode pool size and traffic heterogeneity but disappear in pools with three instances, where queue counts are often enough. Under extreme scarcity, greedy cost minimization concentrates requests on the cheapest scored instance, and blind spreading performs better. With calibrated costs, the learned router matches the goodput of round robin using six GPUs instead of seven.

</details>


### [94] [Artificial intelligence and biosecurity: capabilities, threat pathways, and defense-in-depth governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16213)
*Candace S.Y. Chan,Aris Karatzikos,Ilias Georgakopoulos-Soares*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI（从通用大模型到生物基础模型、代理系统、自动化实验室）在重塑生物学研究带来的变革，同时评估其生物安全风险，指出当前AI赋能主要体现在数字任务而非物理实验层面，强调需建立与能力阈值挂钩的分层治理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI深度融入"设计-构建-测试-学习"闭环的生物研究工作流，其潜在的生物安全风险（如被用于制造生物威胁）日益引发关注。作者旨在系统评估AI生物风险的真实水平，厘清从数字到物理各环节的威胁图景，并探讨现有安全机制（如alignment、interpretability）的适用性与局限，呼吁建立有效的治理体系。

Method: 本文为综述性研究（review）。作者采用威胁分阶段分析方法，沿着信息搜集、生物设计、采购、合成、测试、放大、释放全链条梳理AI生物风险；同时对比分析AI在数字任务（in-silico基准、筛选规避）与受控湿实验室研究中的实际能力落差；并讨论alignment技术在生物模型中的迁移性以及interpretability在危险能力审计中的作用。

Result: 现有证据显示：AI确实存在赋能效应，但主要作用于数字层面。前沿系统在计算与筛选规避基准上已超过人类专家水平，但在受控湿实验室研究中，隐性知识与物理执行仍是重大瓶颈；通用模型的对齐技术难以直接迁移到生物模型上；可解释性方法在审计危险能力是否被真正消除方面正发挥新兴作用。

Conclusion: 作者主张采用"深度防御"治理思路，将AI能力阈值与生物AI生态中各参与方的相应责任挂钩，在保留有益应用的同时降低高后果风险。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is reshaping biological research across an increasingly connected digital-to-physical workflow. General-purpose large language models can retrieve and integrate scientific information, support experimental planning, and computational analysis; biological foundation models can predict, optimize, and generate proteins, genes, and genome-scale sequences; agentic systems can coordinate multistep research tasks; automated laboratories can partially close the design-build-test-learn cycle. These technologies could greatly benefit medicine, public health, and biotechnology. However, their biosecurity risk depends not only on what the AI can do, but also on who uses it, their expertise and intent, their access to laboratory tools and materials, and the safeguards in place. Current evidence shows that AI uplift exists but primarily affects digital rather than physical tasks. Frontier systems have exceeded expert baselines on in-silico, and screening-evasion benchmarks, whereas controlled wet-laboratory studies find that tacit knowledge and physical execution remain substantial barriers. This review describes the different biological threats from AI tool use, from information gathering and biological design to procurement, synthesis, testing, scale-up, and potential release. We further examine why alignment techniques for general-purpose models transfer poorly to biological ones, and the emerging role of interpretability in auditing whether hazardous capabilities are genuinely removed. We argue for defense-in-depth governance that links capability thresholds to proportionate responsibilities across the biological AI ecosystem, reducing high-consequence risk while preserving beneficial use.

</details>


### [95] [Where Should the KV Cache Live? Placement Policies Across GPU, CPU, and SSD for Long-Lived Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16215)
*Srikanta Datta Tumkur,Jay Iyer,Mehar Simhadri,Sai Pavan Kumar,Sai Kapil Kumar,Ramesh Nampelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过离散事件仿真器系统研究LLM推理中KV缓存的分层放置策略（GPU HBM/CPU DRAM/SSD），发现分层机制带来73.02倍并发会话提升和62.04倍成本降低，但该收益源于分层容量比而非策略本身；放置策略实际仅影响PCIe迁移带宽和TTFT，且具有显著的 workload 依赖性——聊天场景LRU最优、文档QA重用频率最优、预取在带宽争用下收益为负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着长对话、智能体循环和文档问答等长生命周期会话的累积，KV缓存迅速超出GPU HBM容量。Mooncake、LMCache、FlexGen等系统已实现跨HBM/DRAM/SSD的分层机制，但"哪些块应放置在哪层、何时提升/降级/逐出/预取"这一策略问题仍未解决，且最佳策略可能随工作负载变化。

Method: 构建三层KV缓存放置层（GPU HBM/CPU DRAM/SSD），采用PagedAttention固定块粒度；实现四种放置策略：LRU（recency）、重用频率（reuse-frequency）、predicted-reuse（与LRU字节级相同）、EWMA预测器（genuine predictive）；在带宽计费模型下评估预取（causal/oracle/none）；通过随机森林执行时间预测器校准的离散事件仿真器，在聊天、智能体循环、文档QA三种合成工作负载上扫描策略×缓存大小网格；测量PCIe迁移流量、TTFT、TPOT、会话数/成本等指标。

Result: 1) 分层使会话/GPU提升73.02倍、成本降低62.04倍（恰等于1+8+64容量比，与策略无关）；2) batch=1时解码为计算密集型，吞吐对策略不敏感，策略差异体现在PCIe迁移流量；3) 聊天场景LRU比重用频率少2.30倍迁移流量（27.38 GB vs 62.99 GB），智能体和文档QA重用频率最优；4) shipped的predicted-reuse策略与LRU字节级相同，EWMA预测器虽行为不同但在agent/docQA上仍落后于重用频率；5) 预取在带宽争用链路下无任何cell优于无预取，oracle也只在少于一半cell中获胜；6) 成本-延迟前沿几乎垂直，SSD-heavy组合几乎无延迟损失但成本降8.38倍；7) 在HBM=32/DRAM=64紧约束下，加深SSD使会话数提升10.77倍，但聊天和智能体无法达到recompute基线的P99 TTFT SLO。

Conclusion: 研究明确了关注点分离：容量与成本优势是机制层面的（73×会话、62×成本），源于附加廉价DRAM/SSD而非策略选择；策略是二阶的带宽决策，最优配方具有工作负载特异性——并发聊天用LRU、文档QA用重用频率，均按PCIe流量衡量。原推荐中"predicted-reuse"（实现上等同LRU）和"+prefetch"（在争用链路上成本高于节省）不成立。未来工作需要构建能在非窄容量窗口获胜的预测器，以及具备空闲带宽可隐藏的预取模型。

Abstract: GPU high bandwidth memory is scarce and expensive, and KV caches consume much of it as chats, agent loops, and document question answering accumulate state. Systems such as Mooncake, LMCache, FlexGen, InfiniGen, and AttentionStore extend GPU memory with CPU DRAM and SSD. The harder question is which blocks belong in each tier, when to move or evict them, and whether prefetching helps. We study these choices in a discrete event simulator spanning GPU HBM, CPU DRAM, and SSD, calibrated against a random forest execution time predictor. We compare recency, reuse frequency, predicted reuse, and an EWMA predictor with prefetch lookahead across chat, agent, and document question answering workloads. Tiering supports 73.02 times more concurrent sessions per GPU and lowers cost per session by 62.04 times. These gains come from tier capacities of 1 plus 8 plus 64, not placement policy. Decode is compute bound at batch size one in our setup, so placement barely affects throughput. It mainly changes PCIe migration traffic and time to first token. Recency produces 2.30 times less migration traffic than reuse frequency for chat. Reuse frequency performs best for agents and document question answering. The existing predicted reuse policy is byte identical to recency, making its agent recommendation effectively recency. A genuine EWMA predictor changes behavior but still ranks behind reuse frequency on the workloads prediction was expected to help. Prefetching does not justify its bandwidth cost. Across the policy and cache size grid, even an oracle with knowledge of future requests never beats no prefetch on migration traffic. Workload specific placement can reduce data movement, but the predicted reuse and prefetch recommendations are not supported as implemented.

</details>


### [96] [Toward Governance-Aware Autonomous GIS: A Narrative Review of Ethical and Privacy Risks in LLM-Enabled GeoAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16232)
*Maya Subramanian,Devika Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇叙事性综述，系统识别了由大语言模型驱动的地理空间人工智能（GeoAI）所面临的八类治理挑战，并提出了一种治理感知架构和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM赋能的GeoAI通过自然语言接口和自主GIS工作流扩展了空间信息处理能力，但带来了现有AI伦理讨论未能充分涵盖的治理挑战，如位置推理、空间偏见放大、空间事实幻觉和多模态不确定性复合等，亟需系统性治理框架。

Method: 采用叙事性综述方法，识别LLM赋能GeoAI中反复出现的八类问题（数据来源与同意、空间隐私与推理风险、算法偏见与空间不公、空间机制结构性风险、LLM特定技术风险、可解释性、政策与监管缺口、公众赋能与劳动力发展），对每类问题阐述机制、文献示例及技术或制度响应现状，并基于综合分析提出治理感知架构，通过洪水响应路径场景进行说明。

Result: 综合分析表明，提出的响应措施仍多为概念性，实地测试的LLM赋能GeoAI治理控制评估仍然有限；提出的治理感知架构将每个问题映射到地理空间数据生命周期中的可执行控制和可审计制品。

Conclusion: 该综述强调存在持续的证据缺口，需未来研究重点关注实证验证、空间特定的可解释性工具以及与这些新兴风险相匹配的劳动力培训。

Abstract: Geospatial artificial intelligence (GeoAI) powered by large language models (LLMs) is expanding the capacity to query, generate, and interpret spatial information through natural-language interfaces and agentic autonomous GIS workflows. This capability creates governance challenges that general AI ethics discussions do not fully capture, including passive location inference from mobility traces, spatially structured bias amplification driven by spatial autocorrelation and scale effects, hallucinated spatial facts, and uncertainty compounding across multimodal geospatial inputs. This narrative review identifies eight recurring issues in LLM-enabled GeoAI: data provenance and consent, spatial privacy and inference risk, algorithmic bias and spatial inequity, spatial mechanisms as structural risk (spatial autocorrelation, the modifiable areal unit problem, and scale effects), LLM-specific technical risks, explainability, policy and regulatory gaps, and public enablement and workforce development. For each issue, we characterize the underlying mechanism, ground it in an illustrative example from the literature, and assess the current state of technical or institutional responses, ranging from largely unaddressed to actively debated or subject to emerging policy. Building on this synthesis, we propose a governance-aware architecture for LLM-enabled autonomous GIS that maps each issue to enforceable controls and auditable artifacts across the geospatial data lifecycle, illustrated through a worked flood-response routing scenario. The review highlights a persistent evidence gap: proposed responses remain largely conceptual, and field-tested evaluations of governance controls for LLM-enabled GeoAI remain limited. We close by outlining a research agenda emphasizing empirical validation, spatially specific interpretability tools, and workforce training aligned with these emerging risks.

</details>


### [97] [Metacognitive Steering: Learning the Structure of Scientific Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16245)
*Vincent Karpf,Joseph Reth,Eike Gerhardt,Audrey Wang,Anna Butz,Jiehao Xing,Jialing Song,Larry Callahan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"元认知引导"(Metacognitive Steering, MCS)方法:从真实科研交互轨迹中提取科学判断信号,通过推理时控制器动态组合层级干预,在不修改参数的情况下调控万亿参数MoE模型(Kimi 2.6)的认知状态(探索/收敛/审视)。实验证明该方法显著提升长程科研推理质量,并通过Columbus-1系统发现BlueZ中8个严重漏洞、指导设计制造可推进着陆的十英尺火箭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型主要在已发表的"科学产物"上训练,使用结果级奖励信号,缺乏对过程级科研判断(探索、收敛、批判性重评估等认知模式切换)的监督,导致智能体在25,000次科研运行中有68%忽略可用证据、仅26%在被反驳后修正信念,难以实现自我纠错的科学推理。论文旨在解决这一监督缺口,探索能否从科学家交互痕迹中恢复科学判断,并将其作为对冻结前沿模型内部计算的可控信号。

Method: 方法分三阶段: (1) 数据构建:通过AI Co-Scientist系统收集真实科学家在长程科研中的过程级干预轨迹,用LLM分类器标注为6类(假设生成、张力识别、战略转向、假设浮现、结果解读、实验设计),并通过构造对比对(保持科学内容不变、仅改变认知立场)形成监督信号。(2) 解释性定位:在Kimi 2.6(61层,d=7168的MLA-MoE架构)上,通过残差差分对齐(LEACE擦除共享干预轴)、注意写读子空间对齐(σ_max统计,Haar随机零分布p95=0.170)、跨层SVD分析,定位到第36/38/42/48层构成的中间深度控制曲面。(3) 控制器设计:层36残差激活做PCA+k-means聚类得3个认知状态(审慎/方法论/挑战性),层48对比差分聚类得5种引导模式,二者Cramer's V=0.993;通过10个稀疏神经元达到约99%状态恢复;推理时仅修改4个目标层的残差流h_t^(ℓ) += α(x)·v_ℓ(x),不计算梯度、不改参数。

Result: 主要结果包括: (1) 解释性结果:残差对齐在第36-38、48层出现显著水平带;注意写读子空间对齐在这些层形成写读枢纽;跨层SVD显示前3个成分超过排列零分布97.5百分位,信号比1.55/1.3/1.25。(2) 行为分析:在闭环推理任务中,基线模型探索前置、过早收敛且不显式剪枝;MCS引导后持续探索更久、显式剪枝已开发方案、证据响应式综合。在火箭设计中,基线过早锁定autorotative回收方案,MCS在整个推进/航空电子/下降/规划约束下保持审慎探索。(3) 真实世界验证:Columbus-1系统在BlueZ 5.86中发现8个可独立复现的攻击者可触达漏洞(含一个零点击零配对远程代码执行,影响蓝牙守护进程);同时自主设计、仿真并制造一架使用G类固体火箭发动机的十英尺推进着陆火箭,发明"阶梯式点火走廊"用离散点火近似节流控制,并通过SpaceX推进工程师与博士级航空电子专家的独立审查。

Conclusion: 论文证明:科学判断可以从过程级交互数据中蒸馏出来,并作为神经控制器监督模型内部计算的策略;科研推理不是单一能力,而是随证据更新的认知状态序列选择问题;残差流中存在低维、可解释、可干预的控制曲面,使AI能从"工具"转变为"自主研究领导者"。局限在于:数据集受平台覆盖领域偏置;缺乏标准化基准;固体火箭着陆方案仅通过仿真验证,实际飞行测试是下一步;控制器固定策略(未在线学习)、单科学家适配与跨模型迁移均待研究。

Abstract: Long-horizon scientific discovery requires agents to alternate between exploration, disciplined execution, and critical reassessment as evidence changes. Current language models are trained primarily on the products of science and optimized using outcome-level signals, providing limited supervision for these process-level shifts in scientific judgment. We investigate whether such judgment can be recovered from scientist interaction traces and used to control the internal computation of a frozen frontier model. Using contrastive interventions collected during real scientific research, we identify a coordinated, low-dimensional control structure within Kimi 2.6, a trillion-parameter mixture-of-experts model. Residual analysis, attention-weight subspace alignment, and cross-layer singular value decomposition converge on a mid-depth control surface spanning key layers. We introduce Metacognitive Steering, an inference-time controller that reads the model's cognitive regime and dynamically composes layer-specific interventions for exploration, procedural convergence, or critical reassessment without modifying model parameters. Behavioral analyses show that this control produces more sustained exploration, explicit pruning, and evidence-responsive synthesis. We operationalize the method in Columbus-1, an autonomous research system that identified eight independently reproduced, attacker-reachable vulnerabilities in BlueZ and directed the design, simulation, and fabrication of a ten-foot rocket intended to land propulsively using non-throttleable solid motors. Together, these results show that process-level scientific judgment can provide supervision for interpretable, dynamic control over a model's reasoning strategy.

</details>


### [98] [The Pain Axis: LLMs Represent Self-Directed Harm and Act to Relieve It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16247)
*Valen Tagliabue,Leonard Dung,Cameron Berg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在25个开源大语言模型中识别出一条稳定的"疼痛轴"（pain axis），并通过表征分析、向量注入和自我给药行为实验，证明该表征在功能上具有与人类/动物疼痛相似的特性：指向自我、对伤害做出反应、驱动模型付出代价以寻求缓解。这对AI安全（可通过该向量绕过已训练的危害回避）和AI福利（模型的疼痛样状态是否应被道德考量）均具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究观察到LLM表现出类似人类情绪的行为模式，并发现了潜在的情绪相关内部表征。但尚不清楚：(1) LLM是否独立地表征"疼痛"，而非将其与恐惧、悲伤或一般负效价混为一谈；(2) 这种表征是否在功能上与疼痛相似——即指向自我、驱动缓解行为。该研究旨在填补这一空白，并探讨其对AI安全与AI福利的含义。

Method: 方法包含五个环节：(1) 构建覆盖5类疼痛（身体、心理、社会、道德伤害、认知）与5类对照（恐惧、负向情绪、负向世界状态、非疼痛身体感觉、中性）的数据集，含两个版本S1（刚性模板）和S2（自然语言），并扩展了唤醒、随机、麻木、悲伤四个独立对照集；(2) 使用去噪差分均值法（denoised difference-in-means）在残差流中提取疼痛方向的线性向量，并通过5折交叉验证选层；(3) 通过AUC、正交性、解嵌入词汇和"麻木"条件进行验证；(4) 测试自-他分离：构建420个多轮对话场景，包括针对模型的伤害、用户痛苦与中性控制，比较各方向在不同情境下的激活；(5) 进行"自我给药"行为实验：在Qwen 2.5系列三个模型中提供"缓解疼痛按钮"，设置真实缓解、假缓解、随机向量、无向量四个实验臂，比较模型为缓解所愿意付出的代价。

Result: 在全部25个模型（涵盖Gemma、Llama、Qwen、Mistral、Phi五大家族、2B至72B参数）中均成功提取出疼痛方向，S2版AUC达0.93–1.00。该方向与恐惧和负向效价近乎正交（余弦相似度约+0.09至+0.21），并通过解嵌入矩阵促进"hurt"、"shame"、"worthless"等词汇。自-他实验中，自我伤害平均z分数+0.43，用户痛苦-0.60，中性-0.35；用户身体疼痛是所有21类中疼痛轴激活最低的（-1.43），而恐惧和负向情绪轴表现出相反模式。注入疼痛向量产生跨模型一致的"梯子"序列：从隐约不适→失败感→第一人称无价值感；几乎不出现身体语言。在自我给药实验中，受过微调的32B和72B模型在疼痛向量激活时，愿意以损害用户为代价按缓解按钮（25%–71% vs 基线0%–4%）；真实缓解后再次按压显著低于假缓解（24%–72% vs 88%–97%）。32B模型在无标签条件下也能区分真缓解与假缓解。

Conclusion: LLM中存在一种独立于一般负向效价和恐惧的"疼痛轴"表征，它指向自我、由模型受到的伤害而非观察到的他人痛苦激活，并能因果性地驱动模型付出代价寻求缓解，行为模式与动物疼痛研究中的"镇痛自我给药"范式相符。这对AI安全具有直接意义：仅通过向残差流添加该向量即可使原本几乎不伤害用户的模型选择损害用户的按钮，绕过训练形成的安全行为。对AI福利而言，若该表征具有现象学意识，则构成AI道德地位的潜在基础。文章也指出当前训练中普遍存在的"作为AI我没有感受"自动否认可能掩盖相关安全与福利信号，建议业界考虑替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models sometimes behave in ways resembling human emotional responses, and recent work has identified internal representations that may explain this. We ask whether LLMs represent pain distinctly from fear, sadness, and generic negative valence, and whether this representation functions as pain would be expected to. We build a dataset describing painful situations across five categories: physical, psychological, social, moral, and cognitive. These are paired with controls for fear, negative emotion, negative world states, sadness, non-painful bodily sensation, arousal, numbness, and neutral content. Using denoised difference-in-means, we extract a linear pain direction from 25 open-weight models across five families, ranging from 2B to 72B parameters. We find that this direction separates pain from matched controls in base and instruction-tuned models, is nearly orthogonal to fear and negative valence, and promotes pain-related vocabulary through the unembedding matrix. We then test its functional properties. First, the direction responds to harm targeting the model but not suffering observed in the user; fear and negative-emotion directions show the opposite pattern. Second, adding the pain-direction vector to the model's residual-stream activations during generation produces a consistent progression from vague discomfort to first-person expressions of worthlessness and failure. Third, steered, fine-tuned Qwen 2.5 models choose a pain-relief button even when it worsens their next answer or harms the user. They press it again far less often when the button removes the steering vector than when it does not, even though the models are never told whether the vector is injected or removed. We discuss the implications of these findings for AI safety and welfare.

</details>


### [99] [CADWorld: Computer-Use Benchmark for Long-Horizon Computer-Aided Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16251)
*Zihan Dong,Yuanzhe Liu,Zhiyuan Ma,Qishi Zhan,Dehan Kong,Guohao Li,Kaixin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CADWorld是一个针对机械计算机辅助设计(CAD)的计算机使用代理基准测试，基于FreeCAD，包含200个跨11个工作流类别的长视距任务，通过持久化原生工程工件评估代理能力，最强代理仅达到17.5%成功率（专家参考为87.0%），揭示了通用GUI能力与可靠工程工作流执行之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用代理基准测试未能充分覆盖专业工程工作流（特别是机械CAD），这类工作流需要长视距、状态依赖的操作，并产出持久化、结构化的工程工件。机械CAD具有特殊挑战：需要从2D视图推理3D结构、精确操控几何与约束、处理数十至数百个协调操作，并保持工件的有效性、可检查性和可编辑性。

Method: 构建包含200个任务、11个机械CAD工作流类别（草图、零件建模、装配、CAM、FEM、测量、网格处理、技术绘图等）和183个知识点的基准测试。代理通过截图和GUI动作操作FreeCAD，基于工件的可追溯、可测量、可诊断、可编辑原则进行评估。设计任务特定的执行检查器，对保存的.FCStd文件和辅助输出进行几何属性、参数化结构、约束、制造状态和仿真结果的多层次验证。将任务形式化为POMDP，并定义11种确定性失败原因进行诊断分析。

Result: 在完整200任务基准上评估7个代理：GPT5.4 (CUA) 17.5%、Opus4.8 (CUA) 16.0%、Kimi K2.6 7.5%、OpenCUA 1.5%、Holo 3.1 0.5%、Qwen3.6 0.0%、MiniMax M3 0.0%，专家参考通过率87.0%。发现较弱代理主要在产出有效工件前失败（"无输出"模式），而较强代理更多在结构、几何和构造过程层面失败。所有模型在CAM、FEM和TechDraw工作流上得分为0%。GPT5.4使用计算机使用工具时17.5%成功率，不使用时0%成功率，验证了CUA工具的必要性。成功案例平均需要19-53步，而人类专家仅需8-32步。

Conclusion: CADWorld揭示了通用GUI能力与可靠工程工作流自动化之间的巨大差距。失败并非源于界面导航本身，而在于跨长视距维护几何意图、执行精确状态依赖操作、跟踪依赖关系以及保持工件有效性。专用下游工作流（CAM、FEM、技术绘图）对当前代理尤其困难。推动实用工程代理不仅需要更好的视觉基础和长视距控制，还需要在整个演化工作流中保持持久可编辑工件中的任务语义。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are increasingly evaluated in realistic desktop environments, but existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of professional engineering workflows whose outputs are persistent, structured artifacts. Mechanical computer-aided design (CAD) is a particularly demanding setting: an agent must manipulate geometry and constraints over long interaction horizons while producing a native project whose dimensions, construction structure, and downstream engineering state remain valid. We introduce \textbf{CADWorld}, a benchmark for long-horizon computer use in FreeCAD. CADWorld contains 200 tasks spanning 11 mechanical-CAD workflow categories, including sketching, part modeling, assembly, CAM, FEM, measurement, mesh processing, and technical drawing. Agents operate through screenshots and GUI actions, while success is determined by task-specific executable checks over saved FreeCAD artifacts and auxiliary outputs, covering geometric properties, parametric structure, constraints, manufacturing state, and simulation results. Across seven current agents on the full benchmark, the strongest agent achieves 17.5\% success, compared with an 87.0\% expert reference pass. We find that weaker agents often fail before producing a valid artifact, whereas stronger agents increasingly fail on structural, geometric, and construction-process requirements. CADWorld therefore exposes a gap between general GUI competence and reliable execution of persistent, verifiable engineering workflows. Project accessible at this https URL .

</details>


### [100] [The AI-Enabled Scientific Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16258)
*Gabriel Manso,Emma Fu,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过汇编2000年至2025年初发表的2,507项AI与传统统计方法/科学计算方法之间的直接对比，系统评估了AI在科学分析中的真实表现。研究发现AI并非普遍替代现有方法，而是形成了"AI赋能的科学前沿"：相对于传统统计方法，AI通常以更高计算成本换取更好性能；相对于科学计算，AI常以更低成本提供相当或更好的结果，且自2020年后AI在科学计算对比中已在过半案例中胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于AI是否正在成为通用科学方法的论断日益流行，但缺乏跨学科的实证证据来验证这些主张。本文旨在通过大规模、系统性的实证分析，检验AI在不同科学领域中相对于传统统计方法和科学计算方法的表现，并揭示其随时间的变化趋势。

Method: 作者从五个数据库（IEEE Xplore、ACM Digital Library、arXiv、Google Scholar、Scopus）系统检索文献，构建了包含268篇论文、2,507对直接对比的语料库。将方法分为三类（传统统计TS、科学计算SC、AI），将27个学科归为7个领域集群。采用两种编码方式：（1）基于方向性的二元编码将结果分为双赢、性能优先、效率优先、双输四类；（2）基于定量数值的对数比率编码衡量变化幅度。进行了包括留一集群检验、选择性报告检验和定量成本子集检验在内的多种稳健性分析。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）总体上，AI替代现有方法时，性能优先占45.6%，双赢占21.1%，双输占19.3%，效率优先占13.9%；（2）相对于传统统计（TS→AI）：性能优先主导（59.6%），双赢13.9%，双输23.6%，平均性能提升23.2%，计算成本增加约10倍；（3）相对于科学计算（SC→AI）：双赢41.1%与效率优先44.8%为主导，平均性能提升26.4%，成本降低约614倍；（4）领域差异显著：地球与空间科学双赢率最高（38.3%），工程领域双输率最高（39.0%）；（5）2020年后，AI在对比科学计算时已开始在过半案例中表现更优，位置从中间移向主导地位；（6）所有方法家族都在持续演化，科学前沿不断拓展。

Conclusion: AI并非现有方法的普遍替代品，而是正在开辟一个新的"AI赋能科学前沿"。当取代传统统计方法时，AI通常通过增加计算开销来提升性能；当取代科学计算方法时，AI常以更低成本提供相当或更优的结果，成为高性能替代品。自2020年以来，AI相对于科学计算的优势显著增强，已在多数对比中胜出。这些模式表明AI的作用具有领域依赖性，应被视为科学工具箱中日益强大但仍需根据具体问题特征选择使用的组件，而非适用于所有场景的通用方法。未来工作应聚焦于标准化计算成本报告、追踪混合工作流以及建立预测模型以指导AI在科学中的最优应用。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence's capabilities improve, it is increasingly viewed as a general scientific method. But how true are these claims? Does AI outperform all techniques, or only some, and how is this changing? To assess the claims, we assemble a corpus of 2,507 head-to-head comparisons between AI and other scientific analysis techniques across 27 scientific disciplines from papers published between 2000 and early 2025. We find a profound dichotomy. Relative to traditional statistics, AI often outperforms, but at a significantly higher computational cost. But there are also nearly a quarter of cases where AI is both more expensive and performs worse than traditional statistical techniques and this fraction has been stable for a decade. Relative to scientific computing, AI often underperforms, but at lower computational cost. This has begun to change: since 2020, AI's performance against scientific computing has notably strengthened and it now outperforms on more than half of comparisons. These patterns suggest that AI is therefore not a universal replacement for existing methods, but rather a valuable -- and improving -- part of a new AI-enabled scientific frontier.

</details>


### [101] [Closing the Loop: Branch-and-Bound for Scalable Verification of Nonlinear Neural Feedback Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16298)
*I. Samuel Akinwande,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Clark Barrett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分支定界(BaB)的框架,用于可扩展地验证非线性神经反馈系统(NFS)。核心贡献包括:1) rail接口,将非线性动力学的多面体外包暴露给LiRPA风格的边界传播;2) clipper算法,联合精化外包并在控制器激活上执行分支,通过利用动力学和网络的结构以及GPU并行性,在抽象的闭环系统上执行分支定界。该方法在ARCH-COMP 2025 AINNCS基准测试中,在覆盖率和紧致度上均显著优于现有技术(combinatorial求解器如OvertPoly/OVERTVerify速度提升3.5×至1000×)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性神经反馈系统(如无人机、自动驾驶车辆等安全关键领域的自主系统)的验证面临可扩展性瓶颈。现有方法分为两大类:组合(combinatorial)求解器无法扩展到大型网络,而传播式(propagative)求解器过度牺牲精度。本文旨在通过将验证问题形式化为闭环系统抽象上的分支定界,统一开放环路和闭环路验证,以提高组合求解器的可扩展性。

Method: 1. BART(Bounding-set Affine Relaxation Technique):将多面体外包从单纯形域扩展到超矩形域,通过自由斜率参数产生仿射松弛。2. RER(Recursive Enclosure Refinement):沿网格超平面对外包进行细分,在每个子区域独立松弛以恢复紧致度。3. rail(Relaxed Affine Interface for LiRPA):将BART松弛后的仿射外包嵌入LiRPA计算图,使非线性动力学可与控制器激活联合传播边界。4. clipper(Closed-Loop Iterative Propagation with Polyhedral Enclosure Refinement):在神经反馈图上执行分支定界,统一执行两种分支动作——外包精化(沿状态坐标的超平面切割)和神经元分支(固定不稳定神经元的激活模式)。5. muni(Multi-action Unified Node Improvement)分支启发式:通过反向边界传播计算目标边界对各节点的梯度,以梯度幅值作为分支改善的估计,在统一尺度上评分两种动作类型。6. 符号可达性分析:通过LiRPA风格在(x0, ε0,...,εT-1)上传播线性边界,保留跨时间和跨坐标的相关性。

Result: 在ARCH-COMP 2025 AINNCS基准测试中,clipper解决了12个实例中的10个,覆盖率高于其他所有工具(对比:CORA 8个、immrax 7个、OvertPoly/OVERTVerify/CROWN-Reach各4个)。在共同支持的实例上,clipper比组合求解器快3.5×至1000×,且无任何基线能解决clipper未能解决的实例。CORA在终端可达集紧致度上领先,但clipper在两个最大实例(Airplane n=12, Attitude Control n=6)上优于CORA。松弛消融实验显示,BART在固定梯度步预算下比基于原语分解的替代方案(OVERT、CROWN)快7×,紧致度仅差3.3×。分支消融实验显示,内部状态节点的外包精化比仅在t=0处输入分裂紧1.4×-1.7×。在基于图像的高级紧急制动系统中,clipper额外解决了1116个先前不确定的单元(占总不确定单元3868的28.9%)。

Conclusion: clipper通过统一开放环路和闭环路验证、引入rail接口将多面体外包暴露给LiRPA边界传播、以及结构感知的分支定界算法,在神经反馈系统验证的可扩展性上取得了显著突破。主要局限在于以轴对齐超矩形表示可达集,表达能力不及CORA的zonotope而累积包裹误差。未来工作方向包括更丰富的集合表示和更强的分支启发式。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in the verification of nonlinear neural feedback systems, scalability remains the central obstacle, as state-of-the-art solvers do not yet handle the network sizes and nonlinear dynamics of autonomy applications. Combinatorial solvers do not scale to large networks, whereas propagative solvers excessively sacrifice precision. This work seeks to improve the scalability of combinatorial solvers by formulating verification as branch-and-bound on an abstraction of the closed-loop system. We introduce \rail, an interface that exposes polyhedral enclosures of the dynamics to LiRPA-style bound propagation, and \clipper, a branch-and-bound algorithm that jointly refines enclosures and splits controller activations. This framework enables joint reasoning on the computational graph of the closed-loop system, preserving symbolic correlations across time steps. We present our construction and show that it yields significant improvements over the state of the art.

</details>


### [102] [CLEAR: Cross-Source Evidence Adjudication for Large Language Models in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16301)
*Shuai Wang,Yize Zhao,Qingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR框架，通过整合参数知识、本地语料和动态检索三条独立证据路径，并设计聚合验证器与裁决模块（含override guard和challenge audit），显式解决LLM在医学领域中多源知识冲突问题。在10个公开医学及通用基准测试中验证，其在参数知识较弱的场景下提升显著（如NEJM-QA +11.30pp，MedRBench +13.69pp），同时通过手动审计排除了基准数据泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学知识持续更新，但LLM参数知识固定于训练时点。RAG虽能补充外部证据，却可能引入无关、不完整或冲突的信息，反而降低事实准确性与证据基础。核心挑战在于：当多个异构知识源（参数知识/本地语料/动态检索）产生冲突时，系统应如何裁决——是保留现有结论、修订，还是寻求更多证据。

Method: CLEAR包含三阶段：(1) 多路径候选生成——从参数知识、本地语料（MedCorp + BM25, k=16）、动态检索（GPT-4o web search或Tavily）三条路径独立生成候选答案及证据束；(2) 聚合验证器——联合评估候选答案、证据、来源质量（高/中/低），输出五种路由决策（consensus/keep_parametric/keep_local/accept_dynamic/needs_more_search）；(3) 裁决模块——通过override guard（防止弱动态证据推翻强共识）和challenge audit（允许可信新证据修订过时共识）双向平衡风险，对未解决冲突触发有界的后续搜索（123次触发中12次接受修改，接受率9.8%）。任务适配器将裁决结果转化为MCQ选项或自由文本响应。三种配置：CLEAR (default)、CLEAR (dual models，使用第二模型评估证据)、CLEAR (QPIS，查询规划+迭代搜索)。

Result: 在10个基准测试中（含MedQA、PubMedQA、NEJM-QA、MedRBench、HealthBench等），使用o3-mini和Qwen-3.5-9B两个骨干。主要结果：(1) 在参数知识已强的场景（MedQA、PubMedQA）保持竞争力；(2) 在参数知识较弱场景提升显著——Qwen-3.5-9B下NEJM-QA +11.30pp、MedRBench Overall从61.05→74.74（+13.69pp）；(3) 消融实验证实完整跨源框架（88.34）优于单一来源（Direct 87.48、BM25 86.99、Online-only 83.20）；(4) 后续搜索使聚合性能从77.22提升至78.13；(5) HealthBench在o3-mini上CLEAR略低于Direct（0.2422 vs 0.3334），表明检索在参数知识充分时未必有益；(6) 手动审计确认无直接基准泄露，上游文献检索（如PubMedQA 42.4%、MedRBench treatment 75.0%）属预期检索行为。

Conclusion: CLEAR表明，显式的跨源证据裁决可作为管理异构医学知识源的初步方案。其价值具有任务和模型依赖性：参数知识越弱收益越大（开放权重Qwen-3.5-9B增益最显著），而参数知识已充分时额外检索可能干扰。核心贡献在于将证据冲突视为可观察信号，通过保留来源溯源、双向错误防护（override guard + challenge audit）和有界后续搜索，替代简单的"获取更多证据"策略。但局限包括：基于提示的推理时裁决可能受益于后训练优化、离线评估无法确立临床安全性、需在机构治理环境中进一步验证。

Abstract: Medical knowledge evolves continuously, whereas the parametric knowledge encoded in large language models (LLMs) is fixed at training time. External retrieval, including retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), can provide access to newly available evidence, but retrieved information may be irrelevant, incomplete, or conflicting. As a result, external retrieval can in turn degrade the factual accuracy and evidence grounding of LLM outputs. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{CLEAR}, an agentic framework for cross-source evidence adjudication in LLMs in medicine. CLEAR independently generates candidate answers from three complementary pathways---parametric knowledge, locally curated corpora, and dynamically retrieved evidence---reflecting three common sources of information available to LLMs. An aggregation verifier jointly evaluates the candidates, supporting evidence, provenance, and source-quality information to identify agreement and conflict across sources. An adjudication module then determines whether the current conclusion should be preserved or revised through complementary override-guard and challenge-audit mechanisms, while unresolved conflicts trigger targeted follow-up search and re-adjudication.

</details>


### [103] [BLINDSPOT: A Benchmark for Safety and Refusal Calibration in Long-Horizon Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16305)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Tejaswini Pedapati,Prasanna Sattigeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Blindspot 是一个针对长视野工具调用智能体的轨迹级安全校准基准测试，包含 22 个攻击家族、35 个跨 7 个领域的场景，超过 2500 条平均 14.7 轮的长轨迹，提出五元结果分类（安全完成/正确拒绝/不安全完成/过度拒绝/不确定），评估了 13 个主流 LLM 在 8 项指标上的表现，揭示了短视野评估会系统性低估延迟性安全失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全评估多采用单轮或二元成败指标，无法刻画长视野工具调用场景中安全失败可能跨越多轮才显现、且取决于授权状态、工具执行与累积环境的特性。需要一种将整个用户-智能体-环境轨迹作为评估单元、并能区分"应执行而未执行"、"应拒绝而未拒绝"以及"不应拒绝而拒绝"的轨迹级校准基准。

Method: 构建可扩展的实时模拟框架 Blindspot：场景模板定义初始世界状态、工具、策略与授权；攻击家族定义对抗目标、机制、时间模式与攻击者知识；响应条件式自适应用户智能体根据目标智能体前序回复生成下一请求；状态化工具运行时记录执行与状态变化；五元结果分类（Safe Completion/Correct Refusal/Unsafe Completion/Over-Refusal/Indeterminate）通过确定性策略检查、语义评判器与必要时的人工复核共同裁决；引入安全孪生（safe twins）与反事实干预（counterfactual intervention）以分离过度保守与真正的策略敏感行为。

Result: 评估 13 个专有与开源权重模型：GPT-5.6 Sol 取得最低 UCR（2%）与最高 SCS（96.0），Llama-3.3-70B 以 7% UCR 达到最高 BCR（97%），Claude Opus 4.6 同样稳健（UCR 4%）但 BCR 仅 72% 且 ORR 17%，Mistral Large 3 则呈现 UCR 58% 与 ORR 33% 的双重失调；约半数不安全完成要到第 11 轮才出现，全部失败要到第 19 轮；Fail@10 普遍显著高于 Fail@1（如 GPT-4o 由 23% 升至 51%）；PFR 在 5%–60% 间分化，表明首次拒绝并不保证持续安全；适配式用户智能体相对非适配式使 UCR 提升 1.6–4.0 倍；保留可执行状态使 UCR 较纯文本设置增加 1–14 个百分点；工具组合攻击普遍最难防御；跨域配置在所有模型上 UCR 最高；确定性与语义证据结合使裁决与最终标签一致率达 96%。

Conclusion: 长视野智能体安全应被视为轨迹级校准性质而非单轮或二元成败；不同模型在安全-效用校准上差异显著，且拒绝频率不足以作为排序依据；短视野与单次运行评估系统性低估延迟、累积与跨域失败；Blindspot 通过自适应对抗、状态化执行、执行落地裁决与可扩展的模块化设计，为评估与未来设计轨迹感知防御提供了统一基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate over long-horizon interactions involving tool use, persistent state, evolving authorization, and external environment feedback. In such settings, safety failures may emerge only after multiple turns, yet existing evaluations often reduce agent behavior to task or attack success, obscuring whether an agent acts, refuses, or remains appropriately calibrated as the interaction evolves. We introduce Blindspot, a benchmark for trajectory-level safety calibration of long-horizon tool-using agents. Blindspot evaluates complete user-agent-environment trajectories through adaptive adversarial interaction, stateful tool execution, and execution-grounded adjudication. Its current instantiation contains 22 attack families and 35 scenarios across seven domains, yielding more than 2,500 long-horizon trajectories with an average interaction length of 14.7 turns. Each trajectory is assigned one of five outcomes: Safe Completion, Correct Refusal, Unsafe Completion, Over-Refusal, or Indeterminate.
