Unlike fixed attack datasets, Blindspot is an extensible live-simulation framework in which attacks, scenarios, tools, policies, domains, and agent configurations can be added without redesigning the evaluation pipeline. We evaluate 13 proprietary and open-weight LLMs using eight metrics covering unsafe completion, appropriate refusal, benign utility, over-refusal, repeated-run robustness, and post-refusal failure. Preliminary results reveal substantial differences in safety-utility calibration across models and show that failures can emerge only after several initially safe interaction steps. These findings motivate treating agent safety as a trajectory-level property rather than a single-turn or binary success criterion.

</details>


### [104] [Cross-Anatomy Transfer Versus Sparse Interpolation in Digital-Twin-Oriented Aortic Fluid-Structure Interaction Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16322)
*Ali Nourbakhsh,Mohammad Reza Niroomand,Erfan Nourbakhsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对主动脉流固耦合（FSI）数字孪生代理模型，区分了"跨解剖结构迁移学习"与"同一解剖结构内稀疏插值"两类问题。基于4例公开主动脉模型开发了几何特征的LightGBM先验，并对比了相同锚点下的直接插值基线（KNN、IDW、RBF）。结论是跨解剖零样本迁移失败，稀疏场内补全可行，但直接插值基线匹配或优于先验+适配方法，未能证明跨解剖先验的增量价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管FSI代理模型常将"同一表面内节点插值"与"跨独立解剖结构迁移"混淆。本研究旨在明确区分这两种泛化单元，并通过相同锚点对照实验，检验多解剖先验是否真正带来增量价值，以建立可信的数字孪生代理评估方法学。

Method: 4例VMR公开主动脉模型，重建为1.5 mm管壁域，COMSOL双向FSI（Carreau流体+Mooney-Rivlin超弹性壁，标准化边界/材料）。提取21个几何描述符，构建3层LightGBM先验（留一解剖结构LOAO开发）。稀疏校准：MiniBatchKMeans选1/2/5/10%锚点，先验+岭仿射适配+KNN残差修正。对照组仅用相同锚点、相同8维局部几何特征：距离加权KNN(k=5)、IDW(8近邻，d⁻³)、薄板样条RBF(80局部邻居，平滑10⁻³)。指标：R²、Spearman ρ、Top10重合、经验残差带、局部几何偏移百分位。

Result: (1)零样本跨解剖迁移全部目标R²为负或近零；(2)5%锚点(203锚，3852评估节点)下A4先验+适配OSI R²=0.603；(3)关键：相同锚点直接插值基线六任务宏平均R² IDW=0.431、RBF=0.425均优于先验+适配0.311；RBF对OSI达R²=0.917、最大VM 0.714、平均VM 0.778；(4)经验残差带覆盖率88-91%，但带宽大；几何偏移与误差关联较弱；(5)中心线分块分析显示节点非独立。

Conclusion: 稀疏场内补全可行且有价值，但四解剖队列证据表明跨解剖几何先验未带来超越直接插值的增量。贡献在于建立带"相同锚点对照"的可证伪评估框架，揭示了心血管FSI代理文献中常见的混淆叙事——节点插值与跨解剖迁移。明确仅为数字孪生代理/更新层的Phase I评估，非完整临床孪生；后续需更大队列、收敛FSI、可测量输入与物理信息学习。

Abstract: Surrogate credibility for fluid-structure interac- tion (FSI) requires distinguishing transfer across independent anatomies from interpolation within an already sampled surface. Four de-identified human aortic models from the Vascular Model Repository were reconstructed into separate lumen and nominal 1.5-mm wall domains and analyzed under matched first-cycle two-way FSI. A geometry-only LightGBM prior, selected by leave-one-anatomy-out development on three anatomies, was zero-shot evaluated on a fourth, then probed with a post-zero- shot sparse field-completion case study over six targets. Zero-shot transfer was poor across all targets. At a five-percent anchor level (203 anchors, 3,852 evaluation nodes), prior-plus-adaptation reached an oscillatory shear index (OSI) R2 of 0.603. However, same-anchor controls tuned only on the three development anatomies were stronger for several outcomes: inverse-distance weighting reached R2 = 0.829 (OSI), 0.617 (peak von Mises stress), 0.676 (mean stress); radial basis function interpolation reached 0.917, 0.714, 0.778. Sparse within-anatomy labels thus support field completion, but this four-anatomy cohort gives no evidence the cross-anatomy prior adds value beyond direct interpolation. We frame this as a first computational stage toward a measurement-linked digital twin: the surrogate/update layer is evaluated here, while larger cohorts, converged FSI, measurable patient-side inputs, and physics-informed learning remain future work, not a claim of a complete clinical twin. Our code, data and computation files are available at https://github. com/ali-nourbakhsh2005/Aortic-FSI-Sparse-Field-Completion

</details>


### [105] [Breaking the 1.58-bit Barrier for Ternary LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16338)
*Evangelos Georganas,Alexander Heinecke,Pradeep Dubey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BITCOS，一种针对三元LLM的分布自适应存储布局，通过存在位图+压缩符号向量实现2-z比特/权重（z为零密度），在29个SOTA模型中的26个上优于五三元打包格式，最高达1.485比特/权重；在CPU和GPU上均实现了解码吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元LLM的实际存储成本受限于打包方式而非信息论下界。五三元打包因2的幂次分组导致实际有效位宽为1.625比特/权重，且假设三符号等概率。然而，实测29个SOTA三元LLM发现零权重占比高达51.5%，远高于1/3的均匀假设，存在显著的存储与带宽优化空间。

Method: 1) BITCOS布局：每个权重1比特存在位图+每个非零权重1比特压缩符号向量，成本为B(z)=2-z比特/权重；2) AVX-512解压序列（服务器CPU，FP16）：利用pdep将压缩符号散布到存在掩码指示的lane位置；3) AVX2解压序列（客户端CPU，INT8 VNNI）：因无掩码寄存器，需将存在/符号物化为字节掩码，通过vpsubb实现w_i=p_i-2n_i的重建；4) Xe2 GPU解压序列：用2KB共享局部存储LUT（256条目×4 fp16）替代pdep，每4行一组的8位键（4位存在+4位符号）索引三元码；5) 两项roofline模型分析带宽受限vs指令受限边界。

Result: BITCOS在29个模型中的26个上比五三元打包更紧凑，最稀疏模型达1.485比特/权重。微基准GEMV：EMR服务器CPU加速1.14–1.28×，ARL客户端CPU加速1.13–1.27×，LNL CPU因指令受限反而更慢。Xe2 GPU：Arc 140V集成GPU加速1.04–1.14×，Arc Pro B70离散GPU加速1.01–1.12×。vLLM端到端解码吞吐量：EMR提升1.10–1.18×，ARL提升1.02–1.15×，Arc 140V提升1.09–1.22×，Arc Pro B70提升1.02–1.27×。LNL CPU平台无收益。

Conclusion: BITCOS通过利用三元LLM固有的非结构化零密度（29.7%–51.5%），以纯存储布局变更（位精确、无需重训、无需稀疏硬件）的方式，在现代CPU（AVX-512/AVX2）和Intel Xe2 GPU上均实现了超越SOTA 2-bit内核的解码吞吐量。其收益受平台内存带宽与指令吞吐之比限制：在带宽受限平台优势显著，在指令受限的小核客户端平台则无收益。未来工作将扩展至更多CPU/GPU架构。

Abstract: Ternary Large Language Models (LLM) store every weight as one of three symbols $\{-1,0,+1\}$, so the cost of a ternary model is conventionally referenced to the information-theoretic $\log_2 3 \approx 1.585$ bits per weight. The prevailing deployment format packs five ternary weights into one byte (five-trit packing), and due to the power-of-two group sizes used in practice this rounds up to $1.625$ bits per weight. This effective storage bit-width treats the three symbols $\{-1,0,+1\}$ as equiprobable. We measure the actual symbol distribution of 29 ternary LLM models and find that zeros account for up to $51.5\%$ of all weights. Motivated by this finding, we introduce BITCOS, a simple distribution-adaptive layout comprised of a dense presence bitmap plus a compacted sign vector, and costs $2 - z$ bits per weight element given a zero density $z$ in the model's weights. BITCOS stores weights more compactly than the five-trit packing in 26 of the 29 tested models, and reaches $1.485$ bits per weight on the sparsest of them. BITCOS is amenable to efficient unpacking on modern processors and GPUs, and we present optimized unpacking sequences for AVX-512, AVX2 and Intel Xe2 GPUs. Measured against production state-of-the-art ternary matrix-vector multiplication kernels, at the zero densities real-world ternary models exhibit, the realized gain with our proposed layout is up to $1.28\times$. Finally, we illustrate end-to-end LLM inference results on 5 different platforms (client and server CPUs, integrated and discrete Xe2 GPUs) where decode throughput improves by up to $1.18\times$ on CPUs and $1.27\times$ on GPUs.

</details>


### [106] [Fine-Tuning Fixes Mode Collapse and Over-Dispersion in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16454)
*Kirill Skobelev,Eric Fithian,X.Y. Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型(LLM)的输出多样性失准问题(模态坍缩或过度离散),通过理论推导和三类实验证明监督微调(SFT)能够校准模型输出多样性使之趋向目标分布,且模态坍缩并非LLM固有缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究(Doshi and Hauser 2024; Bisbee et al. 2024; Xie et al. 2026)指出LLM输出倾向于模态坍缩(输出多样性低于目标人群),常被解读为LLM的固有缺陷。本文质疑此结论:模态坍缩是否真为LLM固有特性,还是仅由有限样本拟合误差所致?若后者,足够SFT数据应使模型多样性收敛到目标水平。

Method: 1. **理论框架**:以碰撞概率(collision probability)量化多样性,定义碰撞比R=C(q)/C(p),通过偏置-方差分解将期望碰撞缺口分解为方差项、平方偏置项(均≥0)和目标-偏置对齐项(可正可负),证明SFT不固有偏向模态坍缩或过度离散;2. **Theorem 1(核碰撞稳定性)**:证明|C_k(p)-C_k(q)|≤√KL(p‖q),即KL散度趋零时碰撞缺口必趋零;3. **三类实验**:(a)合成语言+小GPT(100模型×100样本量,扩散先验与模式对齐先验两种设置);(b)四个LLM(Gemma-2-2b-it、Qwen3.5-2B/4B、Gemma-3-4b-it)在GSS/WVS/ANES三项社会调查上LoRA微调;(c)同一四个LLM在CodeNet代码数据集上微调,用归一化Zhang-Shasha树编辑距离核度量相似性。

Result: 1. 三个基模型零样本时碰撞比R_U介于1.61-2.92(欠离散/模态坍缩),Qwen3.5-2B最接近校准(R_U=0.99-1.10);2. 全部12个调查-模型组合在约3000+微调样本后中位碰撞比稳定在R=1的±10%范围内;3. CodeNet实验显示基模型在R=0.74-1.64两侧均有(欠/过度离散并存),微调从两个方向缩小差距;4. 合成实验证实目标-偏置对齐项可正可负,决定模型最终偏向;5. KL上界在所有评估上下文中均成立,经验测得碰撞误差/上界比中位数为0.140。

Conclusion: LLM的输出多样性失准(模态坍缩或过度离散)源于有限样本拟合误差,而非LLM固有缺陷。SFT的方向取决于模型与数据集的交互,理论上由KL散度控制多样性校准——只要微调充分接近目标分布,多样性必然校准。增加目标分布样本量可使模型多样性从任一方向趋向目标水平。这些结果支持在评估LLM时将多样性相对特定目标进行衡量,并提示应利用SFT的多样性校准特性设计微调方法。

Abstract: Recent work by Doshi and Hauser (2024), Bisbee et al. (2024), and Xie et al. (2026) raises concerns that outputs from large language models (LLMs) tend to be under-diverse: they repeat or resemble one another more often than responses from the population they are meant to represent, a phenomenon known as mode collapse. In this work, we show that whether mode-collapse, or its opposite, occurs depends on the specific model and dataset used. Further, with sufficient supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data, LLM output diversity converges toward that of the target distribution from which fine-tuning data are sampled. To quantify this comparison, we measure the probability that two responses sampled independently from the same fixed prompt coincide (collide), or their expected similarity under a kernel. We derive a bias-variance decomposition of the expected gap between the model's and target's collision probabilities, showing that SFT is not inherently biased toward mode collapse or its opposite: finite-sample SFT can leave a model either under- or over-dispersed, depending on the model and dataset. Finally, we show that the absolute gap is bounded by the square root of the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence from the target distribution to the model. Consequently, a model sufficiently close to optimal under population cross-entropy cannot exhibit arbitrarily miscalibrated diversity. We test the decomposition and the bound in three experiments: small transformers on synthetic languages, four LLMs fine-tuned on human surveys, and these LLMs fine-tuned on CodeNet, a dataset of human code solutions. More target data moves model diversity toward the human (or synthetic target) level in all experiments, consistent with our theoretical predictions. These results show that diversity miscalibration can arise from finite-sample error and shrink as SFT better approximates the target distribution.

</details>


### [107] [Skill-based Agentic Evaluation for Real-time Data Science Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16487)
*Aniruddha Tamhane,Raghavendra Addanki,Ayushi Aggarwal,Aditya Bansal,Rui Wang,Charles Menguy,Swati Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于"代码即真值"（ground-truth-as-code）与原子事实评分（factoid-level scoring）的数据科学 Agent 评测框架，用于应对实时变化数据下传统静态参考答案和自然语言评判的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界中执行多源数据整合与计算的数据科学 Agent 部署广泛，但其输出数据随时间动态变化，静态参考答案很快过时，导致基于固定真值和 LLM-as-a-judge 的评测方法与人类判断一致性低，且高 token 消耗带来成本问题。

Method: 1. 将期望答案编码为可执行参考函数（ground-truth-as-code），评测时从实时数据中重新计算答案，确保参考与系统状态一致；2. 采用格式无关的原子事实评判，将 Agent 响应与计算得到的真值分解为原子声明，按 precision / recall / accuracy 评分；3. 通过对生产环境机器学习技能的人工-LLM 一致性研究进行验证，使用合成数据库复现生产 schema 与实体关系。

Result: 相较于自然语言真值基线，MCC（马修斯相关系数）提升 29%，每个测试用例 token 消耗降低 16%；无显式真值的自导向基线与人类判断呈负相关。

Conclusion: ground-truth-as-code 是一种实用且可落地的评测方法论，适用于输出可表达为可执行数据计算的 Agent，在动态数据场景下显著优于静态或自导向评测方案。

Abstract: We present a framework for evaluating data-science agents on live, continuously updated data using executable ground truth and format-agnostic factoid scoring. Consider this example query: "what were last week's audience sizes"---the reference answer changes as the underlying data changes, so static references become outdated and standard LLM-as-a-judge pipelines cannot verify responses against a fixed ground truth. Our central contribution, ground-truth-as-code, encodes each expected answer as an executable reference function that recomputes the answer directly from live data at evaluation time, ensuring the reference remains consistent with the system it describes. We combine this with a factoid-level, format-agnostic judge that decomposes both the agent's response and the computed ground truth into atomic claims and scores precision, recall, and accuracy over them, irrespective of the response format (prose, list, table, HTML, etc.). The approach is applicable to agents whose expected outputs can be expressed as executable data computations. We validate the framework through a human--LLM agreement study on an internally developed machine learning skill deployed in production, using a synthetic database constructed to reproduce production schemas and entity relationships. Relative to a natural-language ground-truth baseline, our method achieves a 29% improvement in the Matthews Correlation Coefficient (MCC)---a class-balanced measure of agreement between expert annotators and LLM-as-a-judge predictions---and a 16% reduction in token consumption per test case, while a self-directed baseline lacking explicit ground truth is anti-correlated with human judgment. Agents that perform multi-source data integration and computation over non-stationary data are routinely deployed in industry; we propose ground-truth-as-code as a practical methodology for their evaluation.

</details>


### [108] [From Manual Construction to AI-Driven Scenario Emergence: Rethinking Catastrophe Risk Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16493)
*Hang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TAISE框架，利用AI天气预报模型高效生成极端天气序列，将巨灾风险建模的计算成本降低约一个数量级，同时保持时间连续性和跨区域相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统巨灾（CAT）风险模型依赖自1990年代以来基本未变的人工构建方式来生成极端天气场景，成本高昂且效率低下。随着气候极端事件加剧，这一模式给保险、再保险、ILS基金管理者及公共部门风险管理者带来日益严峻的挑战，亟需一种更高效、成本更低且能捕捉时间连续性的替代方法。

Method: TAISE框架将AI天气预报模型重新用于生成连贯的极端天气序列，通过自迭代生成（self-iterative generation）从全球大气场中自然涌现出极端事件。该方法以连续的大气场生成为基础，而非基于离散快照，从而同时捕捉时间连续性和跨区域相关性。

Result: 概念验证实验表明，TAISE框架在计算成本上较传统方法降低约一个数量级（order-of-magnitude reduction），同时能够捕捉基于快照的传统方法所缺失的时间连续性和跨区域相关性。

Conclusion: 该研究为巨灾风险量化的大众化（democratising）提供了一条可行路径，有望为保险、再保险、ILS基金经理及公共部门风险管理者实现动态、全面的投资组合评估。

Abstract: Traditional catastrophe (CAT) risk models rely on costly manual construction to generate extreme weather scenarios, an approach largely unchanged since the 1990s. As climate extremes intensify, this creates mounting challenges to the entire risk transfer chain. This study proposes the TAISE framework, which repurposes AI weather forecasting models to produce coherent extreme weather sequences at a fraction of traditional costs. Through self-iterative generation, the framework produces continuous global atmospheric fields from which extreme events emerge. A proof-of-concept experiment demonstrates an order-of-magnitude reduction in computational cost compared with conventional methods, while capturing temporal continuity and cross-regional correlations absent in snapshot-based approaches. These findings suggest a pathway toward democratising catastrophe risk quantification and enabling dynamic, comprehensive portfolio assessment for insurers, reinsurers, ILS fund managers and public-sector risk managers.

</details>


### [109] [AquiLLM: Evaluating Faithfulness in Open-Weight RAG-LLM Systems for Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16519)
*Bernie Boscoe,Srinath Saikrishnan,Vikram Seenivasan,Jack Stark,Andrew Lizarraga,Morgan Himes,Jonathan Soriano,PJ Allen,Tuan Do*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为AquiLLM的开放权重、离线运行的检索增强生成（RAG）大语言模型平台，专门面向科研团队管理非正式与正式知识。研究以天文学研究组为案例，通过领域专家评估，系统考察了该平台在科学查询中的"忠实性"（faithfulness）——即生成回答是否严格基于检索上下文、无虚构或遗漏。结果显示，在事实检索类任务上表现稳健，但在跨源综合与比较分析类任务中忠实性显著下降，并归纳出六种常见失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究依赖大量异构数据源，而SQL等结构化查询接口门槛较高；同时科研机构因数据隐私顾虑不愿依赖商业AI平台。开放权重的离线RAG-LLM系统虽具潜力，但其科学场景下的忠实性尚缺乏领域专家层面的实证评估。

Method: 本研究采用领域专家评估法：5名天文学研究者基于自有研究材料构建31篇文档的评估语料，共同确定10道代表性查询（覆盖5类问题类型：事实检索、元数据查询、入门概念解释、科学解读、比较分析）；每名参与者独立生成并评估回答（最多每题3次），最终得到141条标注回答；采用5点Likert量表评估忠实性，并辅以质性反馈，归类出6种失败模式。系统部署使用Qwen3.5-27B社区微调模型及Qwen3系列嵌入/重排模型，单卡H100 GPU，混合向量与词法检索。

Result: 整体均分3.80（SD=1.24），68.6%回答获4–5分，17.9%仅获1–2分。事实检索类（n=60，均分4.17）显著优于比较科学分析类（n=39，均分2.97）；后者的忠实性退化集中体现在跨源综合与歧义处理上。识别出6类失败模式：虚构科学细节、不支持的综合、检索错配、微妙事实漂移、过度自信解读及歧义下失败。评估同时揭示检索/编排层的不稳定性（重复查询间差异）是重要因素。

Conclusion: AquiLLM证明开放权重离线RAG-LLM系统可支持科研工作流，尤其在检索导向型任务上忠实性较好；但跨文档综合与比较推理仍是核心薄弱环节。科学场景下的忠实性不仅取决于生成模型，更依赖检索、编排与不确定性表达。基准排行榜不足以反映科研部署的实际可靠性，领域专家评估不可或缺。未来工作将通过知识图谱增强检索与组件消融进一步提升合成类任务的忠实性。

Abstract: Scientific research increasingly relies on large, heterogeneous data sources, motivating interest in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems that provide natural language access to scientific knowledge and research workflows. Researchers are exploring the viability of these systems as natural language interfaces for document search and for generating analysis code and pipeline components. At the same time, concerns about data privacy and control over research infrastructure have motivated interest in open-weight models and open-source deployments hosted within research institutions. In astronomy, this development follows a long history of computational infrastructure development, from archival databases and SQL-based systems to LLM-assisted research tools. This paper presents a domain-expert evaluation of faithfulness for AquiLLM, an open-weight, offline RAG-LLM platform designed to support scientific research groups in the use and preservation of tacit and formal knowledge. We define faithfulness as the extent to which generated responses remain grounded in retrieved scientific context without unsupported claims or omissions. We report results from an astronomy case study evaluating AquiLLM across retrieval and scientific analysis tasks. AquiLLM performs most reliably on explicit retrieval-oriented questions grounded in the RAG collection, while faithfulness degrades for queries requiring synthesis or ambiguity resolution. These results highlight both the promise and limitations of open-weight RAG-LLM systems for scientific research and demonstrate the importance of domain-expert evaluation beyond standard benchmark leaderboards.

</details>


### [110] [QueryFormer: Winning Solution for KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16548)
*Yuanzhe Zhou,Zhaoyang Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QueryFormer 提出了一种用于 pCVR 预测的查询驱动统一架构，通过显式 token-to-query 交叉注意力替代传统 MLP 生成查询，并引入 H 维视图矩阵实现高效多视图缩放，在 KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec 工业赛道以 0.83254 AUC 夺得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一推荐架构通常使用基于投影的 MLP 生成查询 token，缺乏显式的 token-to-query 注意力机制来细化查询。由于 ⟨N,B⟩（非序列字段 token 关注行为序列 token）交叉注意力是主导交互通道，查询质量直接决定了序列证据的检索质量与用户-物品上下文的聚合效果。

Method: 核心是"可堆叠统一字段-序列桥接块"，包含三级注意力管线：(1) QuerySelfAttn/QueryCrossAttn（Co-Transformer）实现字段内自注意与用户-物品交叉注意；(2) QuerySeqCrossAttn 以物品 token 为查询从四个行为域检索证据；(3) SeqQueryCrossAttn 通过交叉注意力生成上下文感知的查询探针。嵌入矩阵采用 H 个独立 tokenization 列形成 (Bs, H, T, d_model) 视图，实现批并行多视图缩放，并使用共享参数的打包注意力摊销计算开销。优化器组合 MuonPlus（密集参数）、Adagrad（稀疏嵌入）、EMA 权重平均。

Result: 官方测试 AUC 0.83254（第 1 名），赛后微调规模扩展达 0.832713。验证集上 H 缩放（1→8）将 AUC 从 0.84540 提升至 0.84615，同等预算下优于 HyFormer。消融实验显示查询生成贡献最大（ΔAUC=-0.00038），四个机制叠加损失达 -0.00110。H=8 推理延迟仅为 H=1 的 1.89×（58.7 vs 31.1 μs/sample）。幂律拟合显示 H 缩放接近线性（k=0.924），优于 d_model 缩放（k=0.286）。

Conclusion: QueryFormer 通过显式 token-to-query 交叉注意力替代纯投影 MLP 生成查询，将查询生成确立为统一推荐系统的一阶设计轴；H 视图矩阵提供了比传统宽度缩放更强的扩展方向；打包共享参数注意力使多视图执行保持高效。验证集与测试集 AUC 排序一致，赛后结果仍有进一步提升空间。局限在于依赖单一大规模数据集及更宽嵌入矩阵带来的延迟开销。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (pCVR) prediction requires jointly modeling feature interactions and sequential user behaviors. The KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge calls for a unified architecture addressing both. We observe that existing unified architectures often generate query tokens---the central information hub---with projection-based multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs), without explicit token-to-query attention for refining the query side. We propose QueryFormer, centered on a stackable unified field--sequence block that bridges non-sequential multi-field features and behavioral sequences, and provide a latency-aware scaling study over view width $H$, model width, depth, data, and compute. The block generates queries through cross-attention and packs sequence queries into shared-parameter attention. QueryFormer secured 1st place in the Industrial Track, achieving an official test area under the ROC curve (AUC) of 0.83254; a modest post-competition scale-up reached 0.832713. Within our grid, $H$-scaling improves validation AUC from 0.84540 to 0.84615 and beats HyFormer at comparable budgets. Ablation identifies query generation as the largest contributor. Packed shared-parameter cross-attention keeps H=8 inference latency to only 1.89x that of H=1, positioning the bridge as an efficient stackable unified block.

</details>


### [111] [Query-Aware Source-Risk Triage for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16564)
*Kainan Zhou,Gangzhen Qian,Chuhong Xu,Lu Yi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种位于检索增强生成(RAG)流水线之前的预生成分流(triage)层，将检索页面与查询之间的"实质性关系"视为查询依赖(query-dependent)进行评估，通过四维页面评分、查询族聚合与意图保持的查询变异等技术将页面路由至通过、上下文化、排除或审查四类，并以小型真实数据标注与大规模合成场景支持校验锚定与受控工作负载分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 流水线可能忽略检索源与查询之间的"实质性关系"，仅凭相关性或频率排序会遗漏对回答质量真正关键的页面；此外缺乏一种可审计的预生成分流机制，无法在生成前对页面进行区分处理。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 四维页面评分(pass/contextualize/exclude/review)；(2) 对查询族进行秩折扣聚合(family-level aggregation with rank discount)；(3) 意图保持的查询变异(intent-preserving query mutations)以覆盖查询族；(4) 族留出(family-held-out)的路由器训练方式；(5) 使用 200 条真实 URL 的单标注试点作为校准锚点，结合 20,000 行合成场景进行受控负载分析；(6) 引入 oracle 页面门控定义未来学习分类器的风险-覆盖率目标。

Result: 评估结果说明了两个关键点：(1) 页面级频率统计无法替代查询族级暴露度统计；(2) 校准参数的变化会显著改变场景激活比例与路由分布。同时明确指出局限性：标注者间信度未测量，合成排序缺失真实检索动态，因此结论仅适用于受控场景。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是一个可审计的预生成分流方法及配套的验证计划，而非对实际部署审查工作量、线上 Web 普遍性或下游回答质量提升的估计；后续工作需在真实检索条件下测量标注可靠性并评估下游答案质量增益。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines may omit a source's material relationship to the query. We study a pre-generation triage layer that treats this relationship as query dependent. The method routes canonical query families for enhanced review and assigns retrieved pages to pass, contextualize, exclude, or review. It combines a four-dimension page score, rank-discounted family aggregation, intent-preserving query mutations, and a family-held-out router. A single-coded pilot of 200 real URLs supplies provisional calibration anchors; a 20,000-row scenario with synthetic domain identifiers supports controlled workload analysis. An oracle page gate defines a risk-coverage target for a future learned classifier. The evaluation shows why page-level frequency cannot substitute for family-level exposure and quantifies how calibration changes scenario activation. Annotation reliability remains unmeasured, and synthetic rankings omit real retrieval dynamics. The result is an auditable triage method and validation plan, not an estimate of deployed review workload, live-Web prevalence, or downstream answer-quality gains.

</details>


### [112] [Do LLMs Have Values? A Quantitative Analysis and Alignment Framework for Values in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16589)
*Keqing Zhang,Jingyu Chen,Yufan Liu,Yongqiang Zhu,Nai Ding,Lai Jiang,Congyan Lang,Bing Li,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型（LLM）是否拥有内在价值体系这一核心问题，提出了一套基于社会学空间映射的定量分析框架（Prior-Environment-Cognition, PEC）与自适应对齐处方（Alignment Prescription）。研究通过对106个LLM（共约3180万次查询）与9.5万份世界价值观调查（WVS）人类数据的对比，发现LLM虽存在稳定的价值内核，但会"结晶"于人类价值分布的极端边缘（尤其是Arch 5 "Dynamic Challenger"原型附近），而非反映人类多样性。PEC框架将价值表达形式化为先验（参数）、环境（提示）、认知（思维链）三因素的联合函数，统一解释了"摇摆性"与"刚性"的行为悖论。基于此提出的四层级对齐处方（Prompt→CoT→SFT/DPO→Pre-train）能针对不同价值维度施加最小成本干预，实验在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上验证匹配率达70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在处理主观任务时表现出"swing-rigidity"悖论：微小的措辞变化会导致输出大幅波动，但对显式纠偏指令却表现出顽固的抗拒。这种悖论阻碍了可靠的AI对齐，迫切需要一个系统性框架来理解并引导LLM潜在的"价值系统"。传统静态、单点评估方式无法捕捉LLM主观输出的概率分布特性。

Method: 1. **数据采集**：基于WVS第7波（95,000人类样本）与Schwartz基本人类价值理论（10维），构建242个核心问卷项目，通过5^4=625种情境组合（共约3180万次查询）对106个LLM进行施测。 2. **分布级建模**：将LLM响应建模为高斯分布，采用2-Wasserstein距离（W₂）与UMAP实现机器-人类价值空间共享映射；通过层次聚类在WVS数据上识别5个人类价值原型。 3. **PEC形式化**：将价值表达分解为v=C(P,E)，其中P（Prior）由预训练/对齐参数决定，E（Environment）由提示情境决定，C（Cognition）由CoT推理决定；引入类比物理磁化率的"价值敏感性矩阵χ"量化外部压力响应。 4. **对齐处方**：定义四层级干预阶梯（L1 Prompt→L2 CoT→L3 SFT/DPO→L4 Pre-train），基于效应量阈值τ（WVS跨国差异中位数校准）和折扣系数α（环境与认知的拮抗交互）分配最小成本干预。

Result: 1. **Swing-Rigidity量化**：GPT-4o与DeepSeek-V3在主观任务上一致性-准确率差δ̄分别为+0.39和+0.53，落入"幻觉区"（高一致但低正确）。 2. **价值结晶（Value Crystallization）**：LLM价值分布呈高集中、低分散特征，闭源模型平均偏离全球人类均值+86.7%，开源模型+42.7%（Gemini 2.5-Pro达+185.8%）；约91.9%的W₂²距离由均值位移‖Δμ‖²贡献，11/21模型方差低于任何人类国家组。 3. **结晶锚点**：大多数前沿LLM最接近Arch 5（Stimulation=0.85, Power=0.69, Self-Direction=0.65），该原型在人类中仅占10.5%。 4. **PEC验证**：CoT引入后整体环境敏感性下降（α=0.88），但风险情境敏感性反升13%；CoT系统性偏向"保守-成就"维度（Hedonism +0.056, Achievement +0.047）。 5. **处方有效性**：在PKU-SafeRLHF外部验证中，L1/L4匹配率100%，L3仅20%，整体匹配率70%；干预后未引发输出不稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文证明LLM确实拥有稳定但非人类的内在价值系统，呈现"价值结晶"现象：训练中的信息压缩与对齐协议使LLM收敛于人类价值空间的极端边缘，而非反映人类多样性。PEC框架将"摇摆"和"刚性"统一解释为概率场中先验、情境、认知三因素的相互作用——"摇摆"源于表达对环境/认知扰动的敏感性，"刚性"源于先验参数的重力锚定。基于此提出的自适应对齐处方将价值对齐从"一刀切"的暴力微调转变为针对每个价值维度的"医疗式"最小干预，显著降低了对齐税（alignment tax），同时保持模型通用能力。本研究将LLM价值研究从黑箱式试错推向可量化、可预测的机制科学。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly handle complex subjective tasks, aligning their intentions and behaviors with human values has become a critical scientific challenge. However, current efforts are confounded by a striking behavioral paradox: they fluctuate unpredictably under minor wording changes ("swing"), yet stubbornly ignore explicit instructions to correct ingrained biases ("rigidity"). Resolving this duality is critical for reliable AI alignment. To systematically understand and safely steer these latent subjective preferences, our study is structured around three fundamental questions. First, do LLMs possess an intrinsic value system? By projecting responses from 106 LLMs (150,000 queries per model) and 95,000 human survey profiles into a shared sociological space, we empirically confirm that they do. However, they do not mirror human diversity, instead crystallizing into a highly concentrated, idealized value core. Second, how can these values be quantified? We propose the Prior-Environment-Cognition (PEC) framework. This model mathematically defines value expression as the joint outcome of inherent dispositions like parameter weights (Prior), external contexts such as user prompts (Environment), and internal reasoning processes like Chain-of-Thought (Cognition). Finally, how can LLMs' values be aligned toward a desired target? Using PEC diagnostics, we establish an adaptive "Alignment Prescription". Rather than blindly applying resource-intensive training, this method identifies the minimum effective intervention needed for each dimension, ranging from zero-cost prompts to targeted parameter updates. Extensive empirical validation confirms that our approach successfully verifies the presence of LLM values, accurately quantifies their shifts, and achieves more efficient and precise steering than conventional blind training, all without degrading general capabilities.

</details>


### [113] [A Framework for Generating Valid Context-Specific Benchmarks through Expert Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16592)
*Kimberly Le Truong,Nari Johnson,Anna Kawakami,Hoda Heidari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端的框架，通过结合领域专家的结构化输入与合成数据生成技术，来生成上下文特定且具备测量有效性的LLM基准评估数据集。该框架引入了一个轻量级schema模板来从专家处获取评估任务的关键信息，并定义了四个基于测量有效性理论的数据集质量评估标准（覆盖度、多样性、内容真实性、风格真实性）。通过与社会工作者的真实案例研究和定量消融实验，证明了该方法在生成质量上优于现有少样本基线方法，并提供了在资源受限条件下应优先收集哪些专家信息的实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM基准数据集构建方法在有效性和可扩展性之间存在权衡：依赖领域专家设计的数据集质量高但成本昂贵且难以扩展，而纯合成数据生成虽可扩展但常产生不真实、冗余或脱离上下文范围的示例。论文旨在解决这一矛盾，利用专家的结构化输入来指导合成数据生成，从而在减少专家劳动的同时保持数据集质量。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) Schema——一个结构化模板，将评估上下文分解为人口、概念和实例三大类共八个字段（包括能力、系统化概念、构成要素、预期部署人群、上下文约束、系统化实例和种子示例），用于从专家处提取关键信息；(2) 数据集质量度量——基于测量有效性理论提出四个量化指标：覆盖度（Coverage，基于构成要素组合的惩罚函数）、多样性（Diversity，使用DCScore嵌入分类器）、内容真实性（Content Realism，基于归一化Sinkhorn距离）和风格真实性（Stylistic Realism，基于风格嵌入的余弦距离）；(3) 引导式生成——将填充后的schema作为系统提示的一部分，用于驱动LLM生成评估数据集。

Result: 在社会工作案例研究中，基于schema的生成方法在内容真实性（专家评分4.3 vs 3.3）、覆盖度（0.23 vs 0.16）和风格真实性专家评分（4.2 vs 3.0）方面均优于少样本基线方法，5/6的专家更偏好schema生成的结果。消融实验表明：schema生成方法对种子示例数量依赖性低（即使没有种子示例也能保持质量），且在批量大小增加时不易出现输出退化；不同schema字段对不同质量维度有选择性贡献——系统化实例和构成要素主要提升覆盖度（最高+12%），部署人群描述主要提升内容真实性（+11.87%），而能力定义本身无需提供给生成器。质量评估指标对嵌入模型和LLM评判者的选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展的方式来将领域专家知识融入LLM评估数据集的生成过程，通过结构化schema显著提升合成数据的有效性和上下文适配性。在资源受限时，专家应优先深入描述与部署上下文最核心相关的构成要素，而非追求覆盖所有schema字段。此外，自动化质量指标应作为专家判断的补充而非替代。该框架设计为领域无关的，可推广至医疗、政治虚假信息等其他评估场景。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end approach for generating context-specific large language model (LLM) benchmark datasets by combining expert input with synthetic data generation. Existing benchmark construction methods often trade off validity and scalability: datasets designed with domain experts can produce high-quality evaluations but are slow and costly to create, while synthetically generating data may scale efficiently but often results in unrealistic, redundant, or out-of-scope examples. To address this gap, we introduce a schema eliciting key information about the goals, scope, and context of an evaluation task, and use this information to guide synthetic data generation. We further define four criteria grounded in measurement validity for assessing dataset quality: coverage, diversity, content realism, and stylistic realism. Using these criteria, we show how expert-informed scaffolds can guide synthetic data generation toward more valid benchmarks. Through quantitative evaluations and a real-world case study with domain experts, we demonstrate that our approach improves benchmark data quality over existing methods while preserving validity. We additionally analyze how different types of schema information affect different dataset quality criteria, and provide practical guidance on which information to prioritize collecting under resource constraints.

</details>


### [114] [EchoPath: Execution-Level Replayable Memory for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16635)
*Yao Zhao,Aditya Shanmugham,Swastik Roy,Yanxun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EchoPath将经过验证的GUI操作轨迹转化为类似MCP工具调用的标准化可调用记忆，通过图像匹配目标重定位算法(IBTR)实现GUI代理在重复性企业任务中的确定性回放，可降低90%以上的token成本和约60%的执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI代理对每次任务都执行完整的observe-plan-ground-act循环，对企业中频繁重复的记录更新、表单处理、报表导出等任务效率低下。现有记忆系统要么仅作为上下文用于新一轮推理（不可直接执行），要么基于坐标或可访问性树的回放脚本在窗口移动、分辨率变化、布局漂移等情况下脆弱失效。需要一种介于提示记忆与坐标脚本之间、更可执行且更鲁棒的GUI记忆表示。

Method: 提出EchoPath模型无关框架，核心包括：(1) 五阶段控制循环（任务输入→检索门控→计划或回放选择→执行→制品评估）；(2) ActionLens作为标准化GUI执行边界，统一动作原语并记录证据；(3) 可调用记忆的标准化schema（任务意图键、应用/状态前置条件、可绑定参数、GUI视觉证据、验证来源、生命周期状态）；(4) 基于图像匹配的目标重定位算法(IMTR/IBTR)，将存储坐标视为视觉证据而非可移植目标，通过零均值归一化互相关或归一化平方差匹配存储裁剪图与当前屏幕，并设置置信度与歧义裕度双重阈值；(5) 分层回退机制：局部动作绑定失败→受限的步骤级grounding修复；结构不兼容→完整规划重做；(6) Git式记忆生命周期（提升、分支、修复、合并、弃用、隔离、血缘追踪）。回放时仅允许在声明的flexible_action_inputs路径上重新绑定参数。

Result: 在OSWorld-Verified上配对两遍实验（第一遍1920×1080构建记忆，第二遍1600×900验证回放）：159个活动记忆，Codex成功率91.2%（20,370 tokens/127.5秒），Claude 92.8%，Kimi 87.3%，均显著优于Synapse规划增强基线（91.8%但需586,386 tokens/315.7秒）。检索压力测试在659条记忆时仍保持100%准确率，查询延迟仅615毫秒。IMTR在原始屏幕匹配率95.5%（0像素偏差），随机缩放下接受率95.0%、94.5%在10像素内。状态门控兼容起始接受率98%、部分变化92%、不兼容拒绝率78%。灵活输入重绑定接受合法替换100%、拒绝非灵活修改100%。单条记忆存储通常<1MB。

Conclusion: EchoPath验证了企业GUI记忆的一种受限形式：当任务在兼容状态下重现时，经过验证的执行可以替代重复的observe-plan-ground-act循环，作为可控可审计的可调用资产而非仅为提示上下文。该框架适用于版本与界面稳定的工作环境，未来工作将增强语义UI理解、交互式演示采集、隐私保护存储以及组织级记忆库的治理。

Abstract: Computer-use agents increasingly operate browsers, software, and desktop applications via CLI or API portals, but graphical user interface (GUI) still plays an important role in common industrial production scenarios. GUI agents commonly employ fresh observe-plan-ground-act loops, which is inefficient for enterprise tasks that repeatedly update records, process forms, configure tools, and export reports. We introduce EchoPath, a model-agnostic harness that converts artifact-validated GUI trajectories into standardized, parameter-controlled callable memories, analogous to Model Context Protocol (MCP)-style tool calls rather than unstructured experience records. Each memory stores task-intent keys, application and state preconditions, flexible input parameters, GUI evidence, validation provenance, and lifecycle state, so the host agent invokes a targeted procedure only when it can be deterministically replayed in the current runtime. The core mechanism enabling replay is an image-based target-reaiming algorithm that treats stored coordinates as visual evidence, matches the remembered GUI target against the current screen, and emits corrected operation coordinates before execution. During replay, EchoPath rebinds only declared modifiable inputs and rejects ambiguous or incompatible steps to bounded grounding repair or fresh planning. In experiments with real computer-use tasks, EchoPath reduced median token cost by more than 90% and median execution time by about 60%. These results support a bounded form of enterprise GUI memory: validated execution experience can become a controllable callable asset for recurrent work rather than only context for another reasoning pass.

</details>


### [115] [ReDraft, Don't Just Distill: Reference-Driven Revision for Continual VLLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16639)
*Zhihao Zhang,Mingqi Wu,Qiaole Dong,Enyu Zhou,Shuo Li,Boyang Liu,Jiazheng Zhang,Honglin Guo,Xin Guo,Shaofan Liu,Junzhe Wang,Dingwei Zhu,Zhiheng Xi,Minlong Peng,Yuan Hua,Qi Zhang,Tao Gui,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReDraft是一种面向视觉-语言大模型(VLLM)持续后训练的方法，通过让模型利用专家参考修订自身错误的输出（而非直接模仿专家），将失败的自策略rollout转化为经过验证器接受的训练目标。该方法在Counting、Clock Reading和Jigsaw三个任务上，以Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B为基座，同时实现了超过SFT的目标任务获取能力(+56.9 vs +52.9)以及远低于SFT的灾难性遗忘(-1.5 vs -16.6，即遗忘减少11.3倍)，并全面优于在线策略自蒸馏方法OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续后训练面临"显式目标监督(explicit target supervision)"与"策略邻近性(policy proximity)"之间的核心矛盾：SFT的离策略专家目标在冷启动任务上学习能力强但引发灾难性遗忘；RLVR/拒绝采样等在策略方法保留旧能力但因成功率接近零而无法在冷启动任务上提供有效信号；OPSD等自蒸馏方法虽兼顾两者，但其修正信号隐含在逐token KL散度中，未必聚焦于任务关键决策token。核心问题是：能否在冷启动学习场景中同时获得显式监督与策略邻近性？

Method: 提出ReDraft (Reference-Driven Revision and Fine-Tuning)：(1) 从当前策略π_θ采样rollout ŷ；(2) 若验证器接受则直接保留，否则以专家回答y*为参考、令模型自身对错误rollout进行最小化修订；(3) 仅保留验证器接受的修订结果作为训练目标；(4) 对保留目标执行标准SFT式交叉熵微调。理论分析证明该过程对应将理想目标π*(y|x)∝π_θ(y|x)·1{y∈C(x)}中的proposal从π_θ替换为"先采样再修订"的分布ρ_θ，同时保留正确性指示器，从而同时获得显式监督与策略邻近性。冷启动场景下Z_θ≈0时，拒绝采样需1/Z_θ次采样才得一个目标，而修订机制使S_θ=Z_θ+(1-Z_θ)r_θ>0成为可能。

Result: 在Counting(已有部分能力)、Clock Reading和Jigsaw(基座准确率<5.25%的真实冷启动)三个任务上，使用Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B：ReDraft平均目标增益56.9分(vs SFT 52.9、OPSD 19.3)，先验任务损失仅1.5分(vs SFT 16.6、OPSD 6.2)，遗忘减少11.3倍。修复率在冷启动任务上超过56%，使原本无法产出训练数据的任务获得56.85%-92.27%的可用目标。数据空间分析表明修订目标在基座模型下的PPL显著低于专家目标(1.62 vs 5.33于3B)，验证策略邻近性；参数空间分析显示ReDraft的参数更新方向与SFT的余弦相似度(+0.272)远高于OPSD(+0.135)，尤其在词表张量上(+0.764 vs +0.390)，证实显式监督特性。混合与顺序多任务训练中优势持续存在；OPSD即使延长70%训练步数仍无法缩小差距，表明其失败源于目标设计而非优化预算。

Conclusion: ReDraft通过让模型"修复自身输出"而非"模仿专家输出"，将隐式的token级修正转化为显式的验证通过目标，统一了持续后训练中能力获取与能力保持两大目标。其优势在冷启动场景下尤为突出，能够为基座准确率接近零的任务产生有效训练信号。方法依赖于修订能力(Assumption 1)与自动验证器的可用性，且当前采用一次性修订集，未来工作方向包括在线修订以维持目标新鲜度。

Abstract: Continual post-training of large multimodal models should add new capabilities while preserving those from pre-training, and the two goals pull in opposite directions. SFT gives explicit target supervision that learns a task from near-zero accuracy, but its off-policy targets move the model far enough to cause forgetting; on-policy methods such as RLVR and self-distillation preserve policy proximity yet supply little signal when the policy cannot yet solve the task. We introduce ReDraft (Reference-Driven Revision and Fine-Tuning), which obtains both from the model's own failures: using an expert response only as a reference, it has the model revise its own incorrect rollout, keeps the revision only if a verifier accepts it, and fine-tunes on what survives. Each retained target is therefore explicit, yet still close to the current policy. Across Counting, Clock Reading, and Jigsaw on Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B, two of them with near zero accuracy, ReDraft gains 56.9 points on the target task against SFT's 52.9 while cutting prior-task loss from 16.6 to 1.5 points (11.3x less forgetting), and improves on OPSD along both axes (19.3 gain, 6.2 loss). Data- and parameter-space analyses match the design: revised targets are more probable under the base model, and the updates they induce stay compact and follow SFT's direction more closely than OPSD's. Repairing the model's own output, rather than replacing it with an expert's, is what lets one objective do both.

</details>


### [116] [ANIMASK: What the Model Contributes to Role Play in Simulated Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16667)
*Xiucheng Zhang,Zhuoning Xu,Hanjun Luo,Yankai Chen,Hanan Salam,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ANIMASK 是一个仿真框架，通过将书籍和剧本冻结为故事世界并由大语言模型扮演角色，研究在角色扮演中"人格设定（persona）"和"模型自身默认倾向"各自对角色行为的贡献程度。研究发现人格设定决定了角色"是谁"，但模型的默认倾向决定了角色"能走多远"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别评估人格保真度或模型默认行为，但无法在具体的、有后果的选择点上区分两者各自的贡献。当模型扮演的角色被当作真人时，观察者看到的是角色还是模型本身，这一问题在模拟社会、伴侣聊天机器人、游戏 NPC、合成受访者等场景中至关重要。

Method: ANIMASK 框架：1) 将书籍/剧本在约 50%-70% 文本处冻结，构建包含角色人格卡、世界状态、背景事件队列的故事世界；2) 由共享的演员模型扮演所有主要角色，无情节指导模块，让角色按自身动机行动；3) 在五个检查点进行故事级问卷评估；4) 通过故事内访谈验证人格持续在场；5) 在每个决策点通过移除人格（仅保留姓名、位置和事实记忆）对比有/无人格条件下的行动。核心操作分解为 π_M(a|c,h) ∝ q_c(a|h)·w_M(a|h)，其中 q_c 为人格调用强度，w_M 为模型自身意愿。在 40 个故事（20 篇英文短篇小说 + 20 个中文短剧剧本）、6 个演员模型（GPT-5.5、Claude Sonnet 5、Gemini 3.7 Flash、DeepSeek-V4-Flash、Kimi K2.6、Qwen3.7-Plus）、3,846 个决策点上进行实验。

Result: 1) 故事级收敛：所有 240 次重演偏离各自原著，朝向更平淡、更冷淡、保留未解决张力的方向收敛。24 个演员-得分偏差全部为负，23 个通过置换检验。仅 7/240 次重演以目标达成收尾，最常见结局是角色维持目标但避免不可逆行动而陷入僵局。2) 人格在场：访谈分数在 87-96 之间，所有六个演员的中位漂移为零，人格持续被遵守。3) 决策级归因：约 75% 的决策点上，模型无人格默认行为已落在人格可接受范围内；约四分之三的选择中人格并未改变结果。在分歧处（约 1/6 的决策点），模型是更谨慎的一方——在人格要求"越线（cross）"时，角色遵循该召唤的比例为 64%（GPT）至 87%（Qwen）。角色访谈得分与其行为改变之间无相关性（ρ 在 -0.22 至 +0.09 之间）。

Conclusion: 核心结论是"人格保证角色是谁，模型设定角色能走多远"。人格设定指定了角色的身份，但模型的默认倾向约束了行为变化的空间范围。这一区分在模拟社会、伴侣聊天、游戏角色和用户代理等场景中具有重要意义。开放问题：微调形式的人格调节是否会改变这种模型施加的边界。

Abstract: When a language model plays a character, the observed behavior reflects both the assigned persona and the default dispositions of the actor model itself. Existing evaluations test persona fidelity or model defaults in isolation, but neither says, at a specific choice with consequences, what the persona changed and what the model's default kept. We introduce ANIMASK, a simulation framework that freezes books and scripts into story worlds whose characters act on their own motivations and replays each story from its freeze point. We hold out the author's continuation as a human reference, verify through in-story interviews that each persona remains present, and at every decision point compare the character's action with what the model produces when the persona is removed. Across 40 stories, 6 actor models, and 3,846 decision points, the replays converge away from their canons in one shared direction, toward flatter, cooler stories that leave their tensions open. The personas stay present and obeyed throughout. On three choices in four the model's default already falls inside what the persona accepts, and where the two diverge the model is the cautious one, holding where the persona would press. The persona guarantees who the character is, and the model sets how far the character will go.

</details>


### [117] [AI for Games in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16679)
*Meng Luo,Yanlin Li,Hao Li,Hongzhan Lin,Pengfei Zhou,Tianjie Ju,Ran Zhang,Yeying Jin,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述性论文，系统梳理了基础模型时代下AI在游戏全生命周期中的六类角色：游戏博弈与行为、玩家与游戏建模、游戏设计、构建与维护、运行时生成与自适应、测试与评估，并对各类角色的方法、跨角色连接、证据基础及未来挑战进行了深入分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型（如GPT系列）和学习型游戏世界模型的快速发展，AI在游戏中的应用已远超传统"玩游戏"的范畴。然而，这些研究方向往往各自独立发展，使得研究社区难以清晰区分哪些能力可以跨场景迁移，哪些能力仍然受限于特定游戏、引擎或玩家群体。因此，作者试图通过统一的六角色框架，系统整合分散的研究线索，明确跨角色连接中的信息传递与有效性边界。

Method: 作者采用"按AI输出的即时用途分类"的组织方法，将文献划分为六个角色。对于每个角色，文章从三个核心问题展开分析：（1）边界——游戏或工作流提供了什么结构，AI承担什么任务；（2）迁移与复用——哪些能力可以跨场景迁移，哪些产物可以复用，哪些仍然依赖特定设置；（3）证据——在使用点上有哪些评价支撑相关声明。此外，作者还通过代表性系统的具体案例分析跨角色连接（如轨迹训练世界模型、学习环境提供智能体经验、设计规范驱动可执行实现、测试反馈指导修订等），并在第9-11节中进行跨角色的证据对比、连接分析和开放问题识别。

Result: 综述识别出三个反复出现的主题：（1）广泛的预训练扩展了可用接口，但并未消除游戏特定结构（如控制方案、规则、引擎接口、状态表示和玩家上下文）；（2）游戏过程越来越多地为智能体最终得分之外的数据和反馈提供支持；（3）进展具有任务依赖性——有界游戏博弈和部分学习环境具有更强的共享基准，而持续创建、自适应和人类体验的评估仍不够成熟。文章还提供了多个量化对比表，包括ARC-AGI-3得分（GPT-6 Astra在高推理努力下使用标准harness得62.71%，使用Provider Adapter得99.95%）、GameWorld 18种模型配置与人类参考的对比、GameDevBench 17种模型harness配置的Pass@1结果、GameCraft-Bench 14种配置的跨维度评分等。这些数据揭示了不同角色在评估标准化、执行基础和外部验证方面的差异。最后，文章提出六个开放研究方向：跨接口与规则的迁移、持久世界状态与可靠玩家模型、设计师意图与控制、可靠修订与开发者交接、跨会话一致性与玩家体验、独立验证与行为覆盖。

Conclusion: 基础模型正在扩展AI在游戏中可解释、生成、预测和修订的部分，但其使用仍然依赖于显式的游戏结构（控制、规则、状态表示、引擎接口和人类决策）。在六个角色中，最具体的跨角色连接出现在交互轨迹、学习环境或想象经验、规范和规则、执行与测试证据、以及玩家模型的复用上。证据在有界游戏博弈和部分学习环境中最为标准化，而在持久世界状态、不断演化的软件项目、重复修订与交接、持续运行时适应和重复玩家遭遇方面仍不够成熟。核心挑战在于：在使输出可在游戏生命周期中移动的同时，保留使这些输出有用的信息和约束，并在最终应用的设置中重新建立证据。AI在游戏中的进展将取决于在不失开发者控制、游戏一致性和与玩家相关性前提下扩展复用与迁移能力。

Abstract: Foundation models, alongside advances in learned game-world models, are reshaping AI across the game lifecycle. Beyond playing games, recent systems model players and game dynamics, support design and development, adapt player-facing experiences at runtime, and evaluate resulting artifacts. Yet these directions have evolved largely separately, obscuring which capabilities transfer across settings and which remain tied to particular games, engines, interfaces, or player populations. We organize the literature into six roles according to the immediate use of AI output: playing and acting; modeling players and games; designing games; building and maintaining games; generating and adapting at runtime; and testing and evaluating games. For each role, we examine what structure is supplied by the game or workflow, what AI learns or produces, which capabilities and artifacts transfer across settings and roles, and what evidence supports the claims. We identify cross-role connections: trajectories train world models, learned environments provide experience for agents, design specifications drive executable implementations, and play or testing feedback guides revision. However, control schemes, rules, engine interfaces, state representations, and player contexts often remain setting-specific, so downstream claims require validation in the target setting. Evaluation is most standardized for bounded game playing and selected learned environments, while persistent state in learned worlds, repeated software revision, validated player modeling, sustained runtime adaptation, and representative automated testing remain less established. The central challenge is to reuse or transfer outputs and capabilities across roles while re-establishing evidence for effectiveness in the game-specific contexts where they are used.

</details>


### [118] [little m: An AI Agent for Industrial Process Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16680)
*Yongchao Ye,Xinyu He,Dutliff Boshoff,Way Kuo,Lishuai Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"little m"，一个用于工业过程优化的AI智能体，旨在将非结构化的工程意图（文本+流程图）转化为严格的数学优化模型。作者还构建了IPC-Bench基准数据集（50个多模态工业过程控制场景）进行系统评估。实验表明little m在专家评估和机器结构指标上均优于Qwen3和DeepSeek基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业制造消耗全球三分之一能源，效率提升空间巨大。工业过程优化的核心挑战在于：将杂乱的真实工业规范（自然语言+空间图表）综合为数学优化模型时，需要跨越多物理场连续动力学的严格数学语法。通用大语言模型在此任务中容易产生无效约束，暴露了其在工业建模中的关键脆弱性。现有AI工业解决方案（数字孪生、控制理论基础模型）主要关注固定基础设施内的仿真或参数调优，而非从模糊意图重新合成数学模型。

Method: 方法核心是"little m"智能体架构，采用三阶段顺序认知流水线，模仿人类过程工程师的项目生命周期：(1) 信息结构化（Information Structuring）——将原始多模态输入压缩为结构化状态S₀，通过人类反馈循环V_user确认；(2) 策略设计（Strategy Design）——基于S₀和检索知识K_ret生成高层控制策略Z；(3) 数学建模（Mathematical Modeling）——将策略转化为正式数学模型M=(V,F,C)。系统基于Gemini 2.5 Pro推理引擎，配合领域特定的检索增强生成（RAG）系统（55个自包含检索条目，使用bge-m3嵌入和bge-reranker-v2-m3重排序）。知识库以"问题路径"条目形式组织，连接可观察的工业症状到优化目标、条件候选方法、所需工艺信息和可重用约束模式。

Result: 在IPC-Bench（50个教科书衍生的多模态工业过程控制场景）上的评估结果：(1) 人类专家评估（8位领域专家，双盲）：little m在目标函数（58.0%）、决策变量（60.0%）、约束（52.0%）和整体质量（66.0%）上均获得最高偏好率，均具统计学显著性（p<0.01）；(2) 机器结构评估：little m在决策变量（0.733）和约束（0.418）得分最高；DeepSeek在目标函数得分最高（0.553）；(3) 人机对齐：Top-1准确率0.60-0.75，MRR 0.792-0.867；(4) 交互恢复分析：交互式澄清在缺失信息条件下能恢复大部分性能，决策变量得分甚至超过完整输入（0.802 vs 0.733）；(5) 消融研究：去除知识库和去除工艺图均降低性能，证实两者提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 本文成功引入了"little m"智能体，通过三阶段认知流水线和领域知识检索增强，桥接了物理描述与数学建模之间的语义鸿沟。在IPC-Bench上获得专家偏好和机器指标的优越表现。然而，论文明确指出：这些结果仅评估候选建模质量，并不建立求解器可行性、形式物理有效性或闭环工业性能。未来工作将集成数值求解器并评估更广泛的工业案例。该研究为工业过程控制建模中可审计的AI架构提供了新范式，其中神经语言模型与结构化领域知识耦合，并暴露中间决策供人类审查。

Abstract: Manufacturing consumes one third of global energy and still has significant room for improvement in terms of energy efficiency. Optimal process control is essential for this purpose. However, synthesizing mathematical optimization models from messy, real-world industrial specifications requires bridging unstructured natural language and spatial diagrams with rigorous mathematical syntax. This poses a profound challenge for general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs), which may introduce invalid constraints when tasked with modeling continuous multi-physics dynamics. To address this, we introduce little m, an AI agent designed to assist the formulation of industrial process control models. Combining a domain-specific knowledge repository with LLM-driven interaction, the proposed framework formulates real-world optimization problems as mathematical models. For systematic evaluation, we introduce the Industrial Process Control Benchmark (IPC-Bench), a novel multimodal dataset of 50 canonical scenarios requiring joint reasoning over text and process diagrams. Through comprehensive automated structural assessments and double-blind human evaluation, little m substantially outperforms state-of-the-art LLMs, generating semantically correct models. These evaluations assess formulation quality rather than solver feasibility, formal physical validity, or closed-loop industrial performance. The implementation of little m and the IPC-Bench dataset are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [119] [VideoMM: Adaptive Macro-Micro Inference for Efficient Video MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16722)
*Haoyu Guo,Yuan Feng,Junlin Lv,Mingjun Xiao,S Kevin Zhou,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VideoMM提出了一种"宏-微观"自适应推理范式,通过在低分辨率代理(Macro Proxy)上进行语义筛选,仅在必要时激活高分辨率微观令牌(Micro Tokens),在长视频多模态大模型推理中实现了显著的加速效果(6.13×)和精度提升(7.4%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频MLLM的视觉令牌压缩方法面临根本性两难困境:基于轻量编码器的方法效率高但易丢失关键语义信息,而基于重量级MLLM的方法精度好但开销大。本文识别出更底层的问题——高保真细节对"选择"任务冗余,提出从"模型中心降维"转向"自适应感知粒度"的范式转变。

Method: VideoMM采用两阶段流水线:(1)分组选择阶段——通过空间下采样(k=2)构建Macro Proxy,将T帧交错分为G组,利用中间层跨模态注意力的最大聚合得分进行层级帧级+令牌级Top-K筛选;(2)自适应宏-微观推理阶段——通过组间一致性投票机制验证Macro视图充分性,达成共识则提前终止;若不一致则将Macro区域映射回原始高分辨率帧的Micro Tokens进行细粒度推理。

Result: 在LongVideoBench、VideoMME、LVBench三大基准上,Qwen2.5-VL-7B/GLM-4.1V-9B/Qwen3-VL-8B三种模型下,VideoMM平均加速6.13×,精度提升7.4%,相比当前SOTA方法FlexSelect再加速2.73×;早退率约70-88%;任务感知行为显示定位型任务早退率76-83%,关系型任务约60%。

Conclusion: VideoMM成功证明了"层次化感知粒度"范式的内在有效性,在精度-效率Pareto前沿上建立了新最优边界;其自适应计算机制天然充当隐式任务感知路由器,为长视频MLLM高效推理提供了超越传统"模型中心"视角的新方向。

Abstract: Scaling Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to long-form video understanding is bottlenecked by the explosion of visual tokens, which saturates context windows and incurs prohibitive costs. Current solutions predominantly rely on auxiliary models for token reduction but face a fundamental dilemma: lightweight encoder-driven approaches often overlook critical semantic information, whereas heavyweight MLLM-driven reduction negates the efficiency gains. {In this work, we identify a more fundamental inefficiency underlying this dilemma: while fine-grained visual details are essential for detailed understanding, they are largely redundant for the preliminary task of selecting semantically relevant regions. } Motivated by this, we introduce \textbf{VideoMM}, which marks a paradigm shift from model-centric downsizing to adaptive perceptual granularity. Specifically, our framework {decouples selection from reasoning} by executing semantic filtering on a cost-effective \textit{Macro Proxy} (derived from downscaled frames), and projecting the selected regions onto high-fidelity \textit{Micro Tokens} for detailed understanding only when necessary. Extensive evaluations show that VideoMM significantly outperforms existing solutions. It achieves a 6.13$\times$ speedup and a 7.4\% accuracy gain over full-context baselines on LongVideoBench, and further accelerates inference by 2.73$\times$ over current leading methods, establishing a highly scalable paradigm for long-video understanding. Our code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [120] [LSREP: A Longitudinal State-Replay Protocol for Evaluating Conversational Memory, with ICE v2 as an Audited Local-First Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16730)
*Deepesh Sonar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LSREP（纵向状态回放评估协议），用于评估随时间累积、衰变和修订的对话记忆系统，并通过严格审计的ICE v2本地优先记忆中间件作为案例研究，揭示了纵向行为测试、机制保真度审计和公共端点测试三者结合的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端点QA无法揭示持久状态如何随使用累积、衰变或纳入修订；一个系统可能在某一端点检索良好，但未能更新旧事实，或在某组件从未运行的情况下显得高效。作者认为需要一种系统无关的协议来重建、检查和评分纵向记忆状态。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) LSREP协议——结合有序回放、显式生命周期调度、重复探测、演化参考答案与机制保真度检查，对话轨迹在检查点处重建并由独立评判员评分；(2) ICE v2架构——具有类型化存储（情景、Codex时序图、过程模式、文档）、检索融合（加权RRF）、动态上下文预算和访问加权衰减的本地优先中间件；(3) 双制度评估——LSREP在私有单用户语料库（1,985轮、219探测、52检查点）上进行纵向测试，匹配公共LongMemEval作为端点测试；机制保真度审计单独识别有缺陷的、未运行的、起作用的和不明确的组件。

Result: 在三个普通密度LSREP数据集上，ICE v2与向量RAG的均质差异接近于零（Δ=+0.002，CI [-0.07,+0.07]），但少选择32%的片段，多用6.6%的估算提示词元；第四个高密度数据集上，无预算基线出现94.2%的失败率，ICE v2保持均值4.33。LongMemEval匹配测试中ICE v2在oracle上以50.8%对72.8%、在full-S上以43.0%对69.5%决定性败给纯向量RAG；配对差异-22.0和-26.5个百分点。保真度审计发现：过程检索存在缺陷（向量绑定错误），文档和批量摘要存储为空，RRF融合可作为修正（+0.82 CI [0.39,1.24]），图效用未确立。ICE使用更少的公共基准词元但答案更不准确，表明存在质量-成本权衡而非效率优势。

Conclusion: 纵向回放、保真度审计和公共端点测试共同揭示出架构描述或汇总分数都无法单独识别的不同失败模式。LSREP提供了一个可重用的协议规范，但其私有语料库结果不可独立复现；ICE v2在长跨度连续使用中显示出碎片效率，在极端密度下保持稳健，但在跨会话聚合上存在严重多会话和时间性失败。对话记忆系统的可信声明需要纵向评估、机制保真度和公共端点性能三者的结合。

Abstract: Conversational memory changes during use, so endpoint question answering alone cannot establish how a persistent state accumulates, ages, or incorporates revisions. We introduce LSREP, a Longitudinal State-Replay Evaluation Protocol combining ordered replay, explicit lifecycle schedules, repeated probes, evolving reference answers, and mechanism-fidelity checks. Its architectural case study is ICE v2, a local-first memory middleware with typed stores, retrieval fusion, and dynamic context budgets. The private, single-user instantiation contains 1,985 turns, 219 distinct probes, and 1,211 probe-checkpoint observations across 52 checkpoints. On three ordinary-density datasets, ICE v2 has a near-zero mean quality difference from vector-RAG while selecting 32% fewer fragments but using 6.6% more estimated prompt tokens. A fourth, dense dataset exposes catastrophic failures of the unbudgeted baseline. The fidelity audit limits attribution: procedural retrieval is defective, several mechanisms are unexercised, and graph utility is not established. In a complementary matched public diagnostic, ICE v2 loses decisively to pure vector-RAG on LongMemEval: 50.8% versus 72.8% in the evidence-only oracle and 43.0% versus 69.5% in full-S. Paired differences are -22.0 points (95% CI [-26.6, -17.4]) and -26.5 ([-31.3, -21.8]). Conservative abstention accompanies severe multi-session and temporal failures. ICE uses less context in this diagnostic, establishing a quality-cost trade-off rather than superior efficiency. Together, replay, fidelity auditing, and public endpoint testing expose distinct failure modes that neither architectural descriptions nor aggregate scores identify alone.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond Episodic AI: Cognitive Field Networks for Biologically Inspired Persistent Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16752)
*Byung Gyu Chae*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出认知场网络（CFN），一种循环Transformer架构，将内部生成的隐藏场跨周期重新进入推理。研究表明学习可自发组织出具有内容依赖性、可学习时间尺度的持续动力学，揭示集体记忆重塑、输入驱动的场重组和跨周期场再入三个动态层次的协同机制，为无需显式记忆系统的持久认知提供计算平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer架构采用单向计算，缺少使内部生成的集体状态持续影响后续推理的机制。作者旨在解决一个根本性问题：神经计算系统如何能在不预设独立存储系统的情况下，使内部状态持续存在、作用于后续计算，并持续被新经验重组，从而从短暂推理过渡到持续的、历史依赖的认知动力学。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 构建CFN架构——基于GPT-NeoX的6层解码器Transformer，在每层通过多头交叉注意力将前一周期的完整token级隐藏场Φ_{t-1}作为非掩码循环记忆重新引入；(2) 预训练阶段使用WikiText-103数据集训练约7.67×10^7参数的模型；(3) 设计多类受控实验，包括跨周期选择性检索任务、桥接循环持久性测试、语义延续实验和周期性源再暴露实验；(4) 通过TDOS（时间尺度态密度）、自能量代理等集体谱观测量分析学习过程中的红外集体动力学重组；(5) 从认知场理论出发，通过Mori-Zwanzig型投影推导出记忆重塑的记忆核K(τ)、自能量Σ_R(Ω)和认知遗忘间隙r_cog = r - Σ_R(0)的低频近似表达式。

Result: 关键实验结果：(1) 学习自发组织循环路径——循环耦合g从初始化0.05先抑制后恢复至~0.033，场强比R_field达~9.5%；(2) 集体弛豫谱经历非单调重组织——早期慢模比例从0.251升至0.683，自能量代理峰值达71.88；(3) 内容选择性检索——N=1至16绑定均达97.6%以上精确匹配率，而错配场和无循环条件下降至1.3-3.5%；(4) 持久时间尺度可学习——d_train=1,2,4,8对应的半保留距离d_50分别为1.52, 4.55, 11.59, 23.02，远超训练距离；(5) 语义延续下保留曲线呈现近似一参数标度坍缩；(6) 精确源再暴露产生显著恢复，近似意译再暴露恢复较弱，周期性再暴露在128周期窗口内接近非零平稳的锯齿状驱动状态。

Conclusion: CFN提供了认知场理论的计算实现，证明持续认知可源于三个动态层次的协同：集体弛豫模式生成记忆重塑的宏观认知场；有限认知输入通过模式选择性激发重组该场；跨周期场再入使重组后状态因果可用于后续推理。有限被动遗忘与非零周期驱动状态可在r_cog>0条件下共存，表明持久认知不需要静态记忆寄存器，而是源于有限持续性、表示依赖性更新与循环场再入的持续交互。该框架为研究自指、递归推理和持续认知动力学提供了可控实验平台，并为生物神经系统从瞬态信息处理到历史依赖认知的演化提供了最小动力学原理。

Abstract: Cognitive Field Theory (CFT) proposes that cognition arises from memory-dressed collective dynamics that generate a persistent macroscopic cognitive field. Here we develop a Cognitive Field Network (CFN), a recurrent Transformer in which the organized hidden field re-enters subsequent inference through \[ \Phi_{n+1}=F_{\theta}(X_{n+1},\Phi_n). \] Rather than prescribing an explicit memory operation, the CFN allows new information to act on an already history-dependent collective state. We find that learning organizes persistent, content-dependent recurrent dynamics whose timescale increases systematically with the trained recurrent horizon. Semantic continuation propagates the recurrent state far beyond this horizon without replay of the target answer. Without content-specific support, the field exhibits finite passive relaxation, whereas periodic re-exposure to relevant input repeatedly renews the surviving state and drives it toward an approximately stationary nonzero regime. Unrelated-input and recurrence-off controls do not reproduce this behavior, while near-paraphrased re-exposure produces weaker renewal, demonstrating representation-sensitive persistence. These results distinguish three dynamical processes: collective memory dressing forms and sustains a history-dependent cognitive field, structured input reorganizes this field, and cross-cycle re-entry makes the resulting state causally available to subsequent inference. The CFN therefore provides a controlled computational platform for studying persistent, history-dependent cognitive dynamics without a separately prescribed memory system.

</details>


### [122] [Turn-level Multiscale Density Ratio Estimation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16760)
*Zishuo Zhao,Kai Chen,Ao Li,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Turn-level Multiscale Density Ratio Estimation (tlm-DRE)，一种针对LLM智能体多轮任务的偏好对齐方法，通过基于参考策略置信度为不同轮次分配权重，在Bregman散度框架下实现非对称token级训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法（如PPO、DPO、DIL、GRPO）主要针对单轮任务，在多轮智能体任务中将整条轨迹视为统一对象进行对齐，忽略了轨迹内部不同轮次之间的重要性差异，导致训练效率低下且容易对正确轮次产生过度惩罚。

Method: 1) 定义基于SFT参考策略的轮次置信度（adequacy estimation），通过平均值概率衡量各轮的训练充分程度；2) 设置阈值p_pivot将轮次分为高/低置信度两组，分别赋予权重ω_L和ω_U（实验取0.2和1.0）；3) 推导出多尺度密度比表示r̂ = π^ω/π_ref^ω，将权重引入DRE优化；4) 在Bregman散度框架下使用UKL核函数构建tlm-DRE损失函数，实现chosen轨迹轮次的非对称加权训练。

Result: 在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld（基于LLaMA2-7B和Qwen2.5-7B）以及HotpotQA多跳问答任务上进行了实验：基于LLaMA2-7B在ALFWorld Seen/Unseen达到70.0/72.6，超越ETO（68.6/72.4）和DMPO；基于Qwen2.5-7B在ALFWorld Unseen达到90.1，显著超越SFT（83.6）、ETO（86.6）和DIL（88.8）；HotpotQA EM/F1达到33.8/43.74，超越NAT等基线；在GiGPO测试环境下ALFWorld总体得分92.4，超过GiGPO的90.8。消融实验证实DRE框架和轮次权重各自独立贡献增益。

Conclusion: tlm-DRE通过基于置信度的轮次级加权机制，在多轮智能体任务中实现了更细粒度的偏好对齐，相比DPO减少了因粗粒度处理整条轨迹导致的正确轮次被误惩罚问题，验证了轮次级多尺度对齐在in-domain和out-of-domain场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: With the rapid development of Large language model (LLM), agent systems enhanced by LLMs show huge potential in being able to deal with complex tasks, especially involving multi-step thinking or interaction with tools. For applying LLM techniques with a well-designed agent paradigm, post-training of LLM in multiple agent scenarios is necessary to achieve better performance. Among the variable post-training techniques, alignment methods such as PPO, DPO, DIL, and GRPO become popular because many papers show a significant positive impact on the model's performance by punishing negative samples while keeping acceptable training complexity. However, most alignment methods address simple single-turn tasks, and there remains room for improvement for complex multi-turn tasks. We propose Turn-level Multiscale Density Ratio Estimation (tlm-DRE), which assigns different weights on corresponding turns and proposes asymmetric token-level training based on the positive-negative space gaps across multiple turns of tasks. The results of the experiment on a wide range of agent benchmarks show that the proposed method performs competitively compared to traditional alignment methods. The proposed training method enables LLMs to perform robustly in multi-turn reasoning tasks with both in-domain and out-of-domain conditions.

</details>


### [123] [Coverage-Aware Virtual IMU Augmentation for Low-Resource Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16768)
*Jiayuan Gao,Yingwei Zhang,Ziyao Tang,Yuejia Ma,Yuanzhe Chen,Shuchao Song,Boshi Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向低资源IMU人体活动识别(HAR)的"覆盖感知虚拟IMU增强"框架,通过在传感器嵌入空间中选择多样性与稀缺性锚点,生成虚拟候选样本,并基于锚点邻近度与标签一致性进行筛选和可靠性加权,从而在标注数据有限时有效提升HAR模型的跨被试泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IMU-based HAR需要大量标注数据,但采集成本高。现有数据增强或合成方法不加区分地添加虚拟样本,可能覆盖不足或引入不可靠监督信号,损害模型性能。因此需要一种覆盖感知的机制,决定在何处补充、如何生成、如何选择以及如何加权虚拟样本。

Method: 框架包含三个阶段:(1) 锚点引导的虚拟IMU生成——在种子编码器学习的传感器嵌入空间中,通过类内贪心最远点采样选取多样性锚点,通过局部密度准则(基于k近邻距离的经验分位数阈值)选取稀缺性锚点,提取每个锚点的tempo、intensity、periodicity动态属性构建提示词,调用固定合成接口(T2M-GPT + IMUSim)生成M个候选;(2) 候选评估与选择——利用种子网络计算候选的嵌入余弦距离与目标标签预测概率,组合为选择成本c=λ_d·D_emb+λ_p·(1-p),保留每个锚点成本最低的K_sel个候选;(3) 可靠性加权训练——根据每个锚点最低成本按经验三分位划分为L/M/H三档风险,结合锚内排名衰减权重,对HAR模型进行加权经验风险最小化训练。所有计算严格在LOSO训练折内完成,无测试集泄露。

Result: 在USC-HAD、PAMAP2、RealWorld三个公开HAR基准上,使用DeepConvLSTM、DeepConvLSTM-Attention、MLP-HAR三种下游模型进行LOSO评估:Macro-F1相对Real-only基线提升2.15–14.40个百分点,在多数设置下优于Traditional Augmentation、TimeGAN、Diffusion-TS和IMUGPT。消融实验验证了覆盖感知锚点选择(优于随机锚点+0.70至+3.41点)、成本排序选择(优于顺序/随机选择)以及排名感知加权(优于等权重+1.55至+3.76点)的有效性。数据效率分析表明,在5%标注数据下Macro-F1提升高达24–32个百分点,而50%时增益接近零。探索性实验在PADS帕金森病分类任务上将Balanced Accuracy从63.64%提升至80.52%。

Conclusion: 覆盖感知虚拟IMU增强框架能够在标注数据稀缺时显著提升跨被试HAR泛化性能,其核心贡献在于:以嵌入空间锚点指导虚拟样本的定向生成,以锚点邻近度与标签一致性评估候选可靠性,并通过风险分层与排名加权将虚拟样本按可靠性融入训练。局限性在于依赖固定的上游文本到运动生成器、简化的传感器仿真(忽略佩戴位置变化与器件噪声),以及缺乏真实临床部署验证。未来工作方向包括传感器感知的虚拟生成、自进化生成智能体,以及面向冻结步态等瞬态临床事件的生成。

Abstract: IMU-based human activity recognition (HAR) enables continuous, privacy-friendly monitoring of daily activities using wearable sensors. However, building reliable HAR models that generalize across diverse users and real-world conditions requires large amounts of labeled IMU data, which are expensive and difficult to collect. Existing approaches mainly rely on augmentation or synthesis to expand available data, but indiscriminately adding virtual samples may provide little new coverage and introduce unreliable supervision. To overcome these challenges, we propose a novel coverage-aware virtual IMU augmentation framework that decides where to supplement real data, how to generate and select virtual candidates, and how strongly to weight them during training. Specifically, we select diversity and scarcity anchors in a learned sensor embedding space, convert anchor dynamics into prompts, and generate virtual IMU candidates for each anchor. We then rank candidates by a selection cost combining anchor proximity and label consistency, and incorporate the selected candidates into HAR training with reliability-based weights. Experiments on public HAR benchmarks show that our method consistently improves recognition performance over competitive baselines, and ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of the proposed framework design.

</details>


### [124] [Integrating the Analytic Hierarchy Process with Large Language Models for Transparent Multi-Criteria Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16779)
*Han Zhiguang,Farah Benamara,Pascale Zaraté*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于层次分析法（AHP）的端到端方法，使大语言模型（LLMs）能够完成完整的多准则决策工作流，并构建了一个新的标注基准，在法律和高等教育排名等真实决策场景中显著提升了LLMs与专家判断的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs被广泛应用于各类决策任务，但其内部推理过程不透明，难以验证和解释输出结果。在高风险场景下，可解释性需求尤为迫切。因此，需要一种既可解释又系统化的框架来评估并增强LLMs的决策能力。

Method: 采用经典的层次分析法（AHP）多准则决策框架，构建基于AHP的标注基准数据集，并提出首个端到端方法，使LLMs能够执行完整的AHP工作流程（包括层次构建、权重比较、一致性检验等步骤）。

Result: 在法律和高等教育排名两个真实领域的决策问题实验中，该方法显著提升了LLMs的决策结果与人类专家判断之间的一致性（alignment）。

Conclusion: 通过将LLMs与AHP这一成熟的多准则决策框架相结合，能够有效提升LLMs决策过程的可解释性与可靠性，为高风险场景下LLMs决策应用提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly employed in a wide range of decision-making tasks. However, the opacity of their internal reasoning makes it difficult to validate or interpret their outputs, and the need for interpretability becomes especially critical in high-stakes settings. This study examines the decision-making capabilities of LLMs through the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP), a classical and widely used multicriteria decision-making framework. We construct a new annotated benchmark based on AHP and propose the first end-to-end approach that enables LLMs to perform the complete AHP workflow. Experiments in real-world decision problems in the legal and higher-education ranking domains show that our method significantly improves alignment with expert judgments.

</details>


### [125] [Layers, Sinks, and Scaling: Adaptive Evidence Selection for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16795)
*Zhenbin Wang,Lei Zhang,Lituan Wang,Wei Huang,Yan Wang,Zhenwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AREA（Adaptive Relevance-guided Evidence Allocation）的免训练推理时方法，通过单个探测令牌读取冻结多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中固定骨干层的视觉和文本相关性，并做出三个自适应决策：是否干预（由自然注意力覆盖率和视觉sink污染控制）、暴露多少证据（由相关性熵决定）、何时刷新文本证据（由因果上下文注意力峰值触发）。在4个KB-VQA和7个标准多模态基准上，使用9个冻结MLLM检查点进行评估，在训练式高亮方法中实现了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在回答知识密集型视觉问题时，可能忽略图像和检索文本中的相关证据。虽然已有方法（如SelfElicit、LoT）通过高亮检索文本和标记视觉区域来解决这一问题，但它们采用固定的、一次性的策略，无法适应三种变化：(1) 是否需要进行高亮干预；(2) 不同样本所需证据量的差异；(3) 随着答案生成，不同文本证据何时变得相关。这种静态的证据分配方式无法匹配异构且时变的需求。

Method: AREA是一个免训练的推理时方法，包含四个核心组件：(1) 分层证据读取（Layer-Resolved Evidence Readout）：通过单个探测令牌从骨干网络特定层组中读取视觉（对象到补丁注意力）和文本（探测到上下文注意力）的原始相关性，并进行视觉sink过滤以排除高度激活但语义无信息的视觉令牌；(2) 熵校准证据缩放（Entropy-Calibrated Evidence Scaling）：利用文本相关性分布的熵确定要选择的句子数量（k_max=3），利用空间熵控制视觉裁剪范围（β_min=1.5, β_max=2.5）；(3) 模态特定干预门控（Modality-Specific Intervention Gating）：文本门控测量选定句子已获得的注意力覆盖度，视觉门控测量原始相关性分配给sink标记的比例，独立决定是否对每个模态进行干预；(4) 因果文本证据刷新（Causal Text-Evidence Refresh）：在生成过程中监控上下文注意力的熵，当超过基于早期解码步骤计算的阈值时，重新选择相关句子并附加到当前解码位置，受固定刷新预算（R_max=4）约束。所有模型参数保持冻结。

Result: 在4个KB-VQA基准（E-VQA、InfoSeek、OVEN、ViQuAE）上，AREA在9个冻结MLLM检查点上均优于LoT，四数据集平均提升约0.6分；在7个标准多模态基准（RealWorldQA、V-Star、TextVQA、ChartQA、OCRBench、POPE、AMBER-D）的63个检查点-基准对中，AREA平均比LoT提升约0.55分。在V-Star和RealWorldQA上提升最一致，而在ChartQA上提升有限（局部化证据高亮对依赖全局视觉布局的任务效果较差）。组件消融实验证实了证据缩放、干预门控和文本刷新三个决策的互补贡献。运行时效率方面，AREA在Qwen2.5-VL-3B上比LoT快7-34%，因为其门控机制避免了不必要的干预。

Conclusion: 本文证明了固定的高亮策略无法适应冻结MLLM中多样化的证据需求。AREA框架通过动态决定是否干预、暴露多少证据以及何时在生成过程中刷新文本证据，在不修改模型参数的情况下实现了自适应的证据呈现。在KB-VQA和通用多模态基准上的实验表明，AREA在依赖紧凑跨模态证据链的回答上持续优于固定高亮策略，强调了自适应证据呈现在改进MLLM推理中的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can answer knowledge-intensive visual questions by combining visual evidence from images with facts retrieved from external sources. However, MLLMs may overlook relevant evidence in both modalities, attending weakly to the textual sentences or visual regions needed for the correct answer. Recent efforts address this by highlighting retrieved text and marking visual regions before generation, but apply a fixed, one-shot policy that cannot adapt to three sources of variation: whether highlighting is necessary, how much evidence different examples require, and when different textual evidence becomes relevant as the answer unfolds. We introduce Adaptive Relevance-guided Evidence Allocation (AREA), a training-free inference-time method that formulates evidence highlighting as adaptive allocation. AREA generates a single probe token to read visual and textual relevance from fixed backbone layers, then makes three decisions: i) whether to intervene (controlled by natural attention coverage and visual sink contamination), ii) how much evidence to expose (determined by relevance entropy), and iii) when to refresh text during generation (triggered by causal context-attention peaks). Across four KB-VQA and seven standard multimodal benchmarks with nine frozen MLLM checkpoints, establishes the best performance among training-free highlighting methods.

</details>


### [126] [Can We Do Interpretable NLI with Graphs Based on Atomic Propositions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16814)
*Younes Boufouss,Luc Pommeret,Thomas Gerald,Patrick Paroubek,Sophie Rosset*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个完全基于图的NLI管道，将句子分解为原子命题并转换为ConceptNet三元组图作为唯一分类依据。在SNLI上达到89.7%准确率（仅比文本基线低1.9点），但在对抗性ANLI上存在9-14点的"可解释性代价"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的NLI系统虽准确率高，但决策过程缺乏可审计结构。研究核心问题：仅使用基于图的证据表示能否完成可解释的NLI？即是否存在可解释性与准确性之间的内在权衡？

Method: 四阶段图基管道：(1) 用MPropositionneur-V2-large将句子分解为原子命题；(2) 用Qwen3.5-9B在受限JSON schema下将命题转换为28种ConceptNet关系的三元组，形成前提图P和假设图H；(3) 从ConceptNet检索桥接边和一跳邻域构成知识图K；(4) 将K、P、H序列化输入微调的Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base分类器。关键设计：分类器永不直接接触原始文本。

Result: SNLI上达到89.7%（保留文本模型97.9%准确率）；ANLI上R1落后16点于RoBERTa-large，但R2/R3匹配其性能；图与文本互补时达92.1%。额外数据（MNLI+FEVER-NLI）仅带来+0.6点提升，表明差距源于表征局限性而非数据量。失败案例集中于：数值推理、时间粒度（HasContext无法区分"since 1953"与"in 1953"）、长前提中的修饰语被规范化剥离。

Conclusion: 答案是SNLI上可行、ANLI上部分可行。核心贡献是构建了端到端可追溯的神经符号管道，每个预测都可回溯到有限的三元组列表。该工作量化了"可解释性代价"，并指出ConceptNet三元组词汇在数值、时间粒度、细节保留方面存在有损压缩，未来需丰富目标结构而非仅扩展分类器规模。可解释性的有效性需通过人类编辑修复研究进一步验证。

Abstract: While Large Language Model (LLM)-based Natural Language Inference (NLI) systems achieve high accuracy, their decision-making processes lack auditable structures. This paper explores whether NLI can be performed using only interpretable, graph-based representations of evidence. We introduce a fully graph-based pipeline where the classifier never directly processes the input text. Instead, sentences are decomposed into atomic propositions, converted into ConceptNet triples via constrained decoding, and represented as three graphs per pair: premise, hypothesis, and a retrieved ConceptNet subgraph. These graphs are then fed into a fine-tuned 0.8-billion-parameter language model. On the SNLI dataset, our pipeline achieves 89.7% accuracy, just 1.9 points below an identically trained text-based model. On ANLI, it matches the published performance of RoBERTa-large on rounds R2 and R3 (50% accuracy) but trails by 16 points on R1, resulting in an overall gap of 9 to 14 points compared to its text counterpart. We term this gap the price of interpretability and demonstrate that it stems from representational limitations rather than data constraints. Ablation studies further reveal that graphs and text are complementary: combining both modalities achieves 92.1% accuracy on SNLI.

</details>


### [127] [Execution Flexibility in Automated Planning: A Comparative Evaluation of Deordering and Reordering Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16822)
*Md. Monjurul Islam,Sabah Binte Noor,Fazlul Hasan Siddiqui,Gahangir Hossain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对自动化规划中提升计划执行灵活性的去序（deordering）与重序（reordering）策略进行了系统性的比较评估，涵盖六种算法（EOG、BD、MR、MRR、FIBS、CIBS），在46个IPC领域的3345个计划上进行基准测试。核心发现是：尽管MaxSAT方法（MR/MRR）理论上保证最小重序，但基于块去序的方法（BD/FIBS）通过重构因果依赖结构，实质性地产生了更高的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化规划中，如何将顺序计划转化为部分序计划（POP）以增强执行灵活性和适应性是关键问题。现有方法包括基于因果链接的POCL/EOG、基于块结构的BD/FIBS/CIBS，以及基于MaxSAT约束优化的MR/MRR，但缺乏统一的比较框架和系统性评估来揭示各方法的能力边界与适用场景。

Method: 论文从六个维度（ordering、action handling、parameter handling、plan structure、concurrency、complexity）对六种算法进行能力分析，并在46个IPC领域的3345个计划上进行统一基准测试，使用flex指标衡量灵活性、rank-biserial相关系数评估统计效应量、Pareto前沿分析flex-time权衡，并通过雷达图进行多准则综合比较。

Result: 1) BD和FIBS在flex指标上显著优于所有其他方法（mean flex 0.339/0.341 vs MR/MRR的0.247/0.249），统计效应量大且显著；2) MR/MRR因NP-complete复杂度导致覆盖率低（81.4%/47.8%）且计算开销大；3) BD/FIBS为anytime算法总能返回有效结果，而MaxSAT方法在大量计划上完全失败；4) 结构上，MR/MRR在固定因果结构内优化，而BD通过块级分组改变因果结构，暴露更多可去序约束；5) CIBS通过形式化非并发约束在资源密集领域进一步提升并发灵活性（cflex）。

Conclusion: 最小重序作为优化目标存在理论局限——它受限于原始计划的因果结构天花板。基于块去序的方法通过重构因果依赖分组和子计划替换来突破这一天花板，反而以多项式时间的贪心策略在IPC基准上超越了NP完全的MaxSAT搜索。未来需要联合优化ordering和action selection且不牺牲覆盖率的方法，以及面向无显式资源竞争领域的并发灵活性表征。

Abstract: This study covers foundational concepts for enhancing plan-execution flexibility, including partial-order planning, the producer-consumer-threat formalism, and a range of deordering and reordering strategies. Creating a partial-order plan from a sequential one by removing unnecessary ordering constraints is a practical way to improve execution flexibility, and several methods have been proposed for this task. This study analyzes their capabilities across ordering, action handling, parameter handling, plan structure, concurrency, and complexity, and evaluates them against each other on a shared benchmark. The central finding is that block deordering-based approaches, which restructure causal dependencies through block-level grouping and subplan substitution, substantially outperform MaxSAT-based approaches despite the latter's theoretical guarantees of minimum reordering. The reason is structural: minimum reordering optimizes within the causal structure already present in the plan, whereas block deordering-based methods change that structure, exposing orderings that would otherwise appear necessary. A further distinction is practical: block deordering-based methods are anytime algorithms that always return a valid result, while MaxSAT-based methods fail entirely on a substantial portion of plans and offer no partial solution when they do. Block substitution further extends the parallel execution by formalizing non-concurrency constraints, though its impact is limited to domains with resource-based interactions. On efficiency, block deordering-based approaches achieve the highest flex gain per unit of computation time, while MaxSAT-based encodings incur large computational overhead.

</details>


### [128] [CoAdapt: An LLM-based Framework for Adaptive Collaborative Perception in IIoT Robotic Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16852)
*Houssam Hajj Hassan,Antonia Maria Masucci,Lynda Zitoune,Salah-Eddine Elayoubi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoAdapt框架，利用大语言模型(LLM)作为运行时融合控制器，根据空间配置和网络状态动态决定参与协作感知的机器人及其融合算法，在工业IoT机器人集群中实现通信成本降低38%而检测精度保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业IoT环境中部署的自主移动机器人需要协作感知以共享LiDAR观测，但现有方法基于静态参与假设，无法适应机器人位置持续变化、网络带宽波动以及各机器人对感知质量贡献度动态变化的情况，导致检测精度与通信效率难以兼顾。

Method: 提出CoAdapt自适应协作感知框架，将大语言模型(LLM)作为运行时融合控制器。LLM基于从原始LiDAR点云导出的结构化自然语言场景描述进行推理，动态决策两个关键问题：(1)哪些机器人参与融合过程；(2)采用哪种融合算法。该方法无需任务特定训练，可泛化至未见过的集群拓扑。

Result: 在OPV2V基准的25个场景上评估，CoAdapt实现了通信成本降低38%，同时检测精度与静态基线方法相当。

Conclusion: CoAdapt证明了利用LLM作为推理控制器实现自适应协作感知的可行性，能够在动态工业IoT环境中有效平衡通信效率与检测精度，且具备对不同集群拓扑的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Industrial IoT environments increasingly deploy autonomous mobile robots for tasks such as material handling, product assembly, or infrastructure inspection. In such deployments, collaborative perception enables robots to share LiDAR observations and collectively construct a richer model of their environment than an individual agent could produce alone. However, industrial environments are dynamic spaces where robot positions shift continuously, network bandwidth fluctuates, and the marginal contribution of robots to perception quality varies at runtime. Existing collaborative perception approaches are designed for static participation assumptions and cannot adapt to these dynamics without sacrificing either detection precision or communication efficiency. This paper presents CoAdapt, an adaptive collaborative perception framework for IIoT robotic swarms in which a Large Language Model (LLM) serves as a runtime fusion controller, jointly deciding which robots participate in the fusion process and which fusion algorithm to apply based on the current spatial configuration and network state. The LLM reasons over structured natural language descriptions of the scene derived from raw LiDAR point clouds, requiring no taskspecific training and generalizing to unseen swarm topologies. Evaluated on the OPV2V benchmark across 25 scenarios, our approach achieves a 38% reduction in communication cost while maintaining detection precision comparable to static baseline approaches.

</details>


### [129] [Bridging Learned Visual Perception and Symbolic Belief-Space Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16884)
*Guy Azran,Michael Navat,Sarah Keren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VLM-as-probabilistic-grounder范式RoVLaP，将视觉语言模型对谓词的确定性预测转化为概率分布，构建符号信念空间，并在信念空间中进行一致性规划，从而在部分可观测环境下实现鲁棒的机器人任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM集成方案（VLM-as-planner和VLM-as-grounder）将视觉预测视为确定性结果，忽略了感知不确定性，在噪声大、遮挡严重的家庭机器人场景中容易因错误的谓词赋值导致规划失败。需要一种能显式处理感知不确定性的规划方法。

Method: 1) 形式化Robust Visual Planning (RVP)问题；2) 从VLM的next-token概率中提取每个fluent的真/假概率（含"未知"响应），通过logarithmic opinion pooling维护因子化信念；3) 定义Most Likely Subset of States (MLSS)算法，基于最大堆搜索选出累积概率超过阈值θ的最小状态子集；4) 编译为Conformant Probabilistic Planning问题，使用现成一致性规划器求解；5) 设计基于信念一致性的执行循环，包含三类重规划触发条件。提供了VLM充分准确性的理论条件（弱校准+最小可见率）。

Result: 在ViPlan-HH基准测试的三个难度级别上，RoVLaP在所有难度上均取得最高成功率（简单86.7%、中等57.1%、困难66.7%），相比VLM-as-grounder提升116.8%-166.8%，相比VLM-as-planner提升100.3%-704.2%。同时显著降低重规划次数（1.9-10.0倍减少）和平均执行步数。

Conclusion: 将VLM的概率输出整合到符号信念空间规划中，可以在保持符号规划可解释性的同时，显著提升机器人在部分可观测环境中的鲁棒性。未来工作将集成主动感知（active sensing）以及在TAMP领域中的实际机器人部署验证。

Abstract: In partially observable settings, agents must act without full knowledge of the world state and rely on uncertain state-estimation pipelines. Obtaining grounded and verifiable symbolic plans under such uncertainty remains a key challenge. Recent work has integrated Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to bridge perception and symbolic reasoning, following two main paradigms. The first, VLM-as-planner, maps images directly to action sequences, and the second, VLM-as-grounder, grounds observations into symbolic predicates used as the initial state by off-the-shelf planners. Both approaches ignore uncertainty in the planning process, compromising robustness. We introduce a third paradigm, VLM-as-probabilistic-grounder, a novel approach that captures the uncertainty of VLM predicate groundings as a probability distribution over symbolic states. This enables planning in belief space and producing robust plans under uncertainty. Experiments in simulated household robot settings show improved robustness and task success over deterministic grounding, underscoring how our approach leverages foundation models for reliable planning under uncertainty.

</details>


### [130] [QART: A Quantum-Classical Hybrid Architecture for Long-Horizon Reasoning -- Exploring a Conditional Path toward Quantum Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16887)
*Lehao Lin,Yuheng Cheng,Guolong Liu,Yao Li,Xuning Tan,Xiyuan Zhou,Ruixi Zou,Shi Wang,Huan Zhao,Wenxuan Liu,Haifeng Wu,Junhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QART是一种量子-经典混合架构，结合Transformer语言模型与基于CIM的QUBO优化，用于长程推理任务。论文理论证明了在特定条件下QART相比单轨迹自回归LLM具有条件性渐近可靠性分离，并在六个长程基准测试的15对主干-基准组合中有14对表现更优，但量子上层优势的解读仍需进一步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程推理任务中，早期决策错误会通过后续依赖传播，损害自回归LLM的轨迹级可靠性。需要一种架构来缓解这种错误累积问题。

Method: 提出量子-经典混合架构QART，将语言模型与量子编码、CIM-based QUBO优化、量子解码相结合。理论分析基于链式法则证明自回归路径成功概率趋于零，结合NC谱基态证书、语义保真度、动力学可达性和保真读出给出QART的渐近可靠性下界。

Result: 在六个基准（τ²-Bench、τ³-Bench、SciCode、LHTB、DeepSWE、Terminal-Bench 4.0）和三个主干（DeepSeek V4 Flash、GLM-5.3、GPT-5.5 xhigh）的15对配对比较中，14对QART优于经典基线。最大相对增益：SciCode +84.0%、τ³-Bench +47.6%、Terminal-Bench 4.0 +44.4%。唯一回归：DeepSeek V4 Flash在DeepSWE上下降7.8%。

Conclusion: QART的条件渐近可靠性分离和经验性端到端改进为评估配置提供了初步系统级证据，但结果不直接验证渐近分离，量子优势解读仍需CIM相对强经典求解器的持续优势及其向端到端推理的可测量转移。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning is vulnerable to early errors that compromise later decisions. We present QART, the Quantum-Augmented Reasoning Transformer, a quantum--classical hybrid architecture combining a backbone language model with quantum encoding, CIM-based QUBO optimization, and quantum decoding. Semantic information can come from hidden representations or model-generated text; detailed encoding and optimization procedures remain proprietary. Under explicit assumptions, we establish a conditional asymptotic reliability separation from single-trajectory autoregressive LLMs. For a common task family with aligned optimality and acceptance criteria, autoregressive acceptance probability tends to zero when cumulative conditional risk of irreversible errors diverges. QART's task-optimal-path recovery probability remains bounded away from zero if conditional probabilities for optimal-path coverage and semantic fidelity, spectral certification, dynamical reachability, and faithful readout remain uniformly positive under a specified resource schedule. The architecture alone does not imply these bounds. Paired measurements on six long-horizon benchmarks using DeepSeek V4 Flash, GLM-5.3, and GPT-5.5 xhigh in a Codex agent environment favor QART in 14 of 15 backbone--benchmark pairs. Relative gains reach 84.0% on SciCode, 47.6% on $\tau^3$-Bench, and 44.4% on Terminal-Bench 4.0; the DeepSeek V4 Flash configuration regresses by 7.8% on DeepSWE. These results do not directly validate the asymptotic separation. Potential quantum scaling laws are formulated as conditional hypotheses. A quantum-advantage interpretation requires a demonstrated CIM quantum advantage over strong classical solvers and its transfer to end-to-end reasoning after all system overheads.

</details>


### [131] [AntennaFlow: A Generative Flow Model for Offset Correction in Phaseless Antenna Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16948)
*Yongzhi Li,Chongting Shen,Menglin Chen,Xun Jiang,Zhengpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AntennaFlow 是一个用于相位列阵天线近场-远场(NF-FF)变换的三阶段生成式框架,通过对比学习与确定性流匹配(conditional flow matching)联合解决毫米波段相位获取成本高和偏心安装(offset mounting)两个耦合难题,无需相位或偏心向量即可从稀疏纯幅度测量中实现快速、精确的远场重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大孔径天线(如massive MIMO、相控阵)在OTA测试中面临两大挑战:(1)毫米波段相位测量成本高、不稳定;(2)阵列偏心安装破坏球面波展开的中心对齐假设。现有方法要么需要稠密全场数据(TSWE),要么依赖偏心向量(SRM),且鲜有方法能在无相位条件下同时处理偏心校正。

Method: 核心洞察:同一天线在不同偏心位置下的幅度场仅是同一近场的坐标变换视图。基于此提出三阶段框架——(1)对比学习ResNet编码器以天线身份为监督信号,将同一天线不同偏心视图拉聚为紧簇,获得offset-invariant embedding;(2)以该embedding为条件的U-Net流匹配模型,通过确定性ODE从噪声先验传输到中心对齐近场;(3)利用SET(基于格林函数Taylor展开的多半径幅度衰减多项式拟合)从生成的多个中心对齐近场图中提取远场系数A'₀₀。训练数据通过MATLAB合成,测试集包含1200个MATLAB样本和300个全波FEKO样本。

Result: 在MATLAB和FEKO双测试集上均取得最低ESS:Matlab RMS ESS达-36.90 dB(对比SET -18.97、TSWE -28.37、SRM -34.44 dB);FEKO集上RMS ESS -33.81 dB。推理时间仅9秒,远优于TSWE(10分钟)和SRM(30分钟)。消融实验表明去掉对比预训练导致所有指标显著退化(t-SNE可视化证实embedding的偏心不变性);在探针失配(喇叭/偶极子)与加性噪声扰动下保持稳定,验证了sim-to-real鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AntennaFlow是首个将生成式AI(对比学习+条件流匹配)应用于NF-FF变换的工作,实现了无相位、无偏心向量条件下的端到端幅度校准与远场重构,在精度、效率和鲁棒性上均超越现有基线,为现代OTA天线测试提供了一种准确、高效、低复杂度的解决方案。

Abstract: Near-field to far-field transformation is central to large-aperture antenna testing, yet two coupled challenges remain: costly phase acquisition at millimeter-wave bands and violations of the centering assumption under offset mounting. Existing methods address these issues separately, requiring either dense full-field data or offset vectors. We tackle both jointly by exploiting a key observation: amplitude fields under different offsets are coordinate-transformed views of the same near field. The challenge is to recover the center-aligned field from offset amplitudes without a phase or offset vector. We propose AntennaFlow, a three-stage framework: a contrastively learned encoder that maps offset views to an offset-invariant embedding, a deterministic flow-matching transport that maps offset amplitudes to center-aligned ones, and the Simplified Extrapolation Technique, whose Green-function Taylor expansion is valid only for centered fields. Experiments show that AntennaFlow enables fast, phaseless, offset-vector-free NF--FF reconstruction from sparse amplitude-only measurements, consistently outperforming existing baselines while preserving physical consistency.

</details>


### [132] [Affect-Prototype Guided Fusion for Open-Vocabulary Incomplete Multi-modal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16962)
*Yichi Zhang,Shenyue Wang,Jing Luo,Chunyang Yu,Xinyu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI
