TL;DR: 该论文针对开放词汇多模态情感识别中模态缺失的难题，提出了情感原型条件融合框架（APCF）。通过构建对齐类别与维度情感空间的情感原型库，对可用模态进行条件检索与融合，再由因果路由送入冻结的LLM解码器生成开放词汇情感标签，在OV-MERD+和MER-FG数据集上全面超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇情感识别（OV-MER）方法假设全模态输入，而实际场景中由于设备限制和隐私问题常出现模态缺失；现有缺失模态融合方法又局限于固定标签分类，无法适应开放词汇下任意情感语义的引导需求。论文旨在解决"开放词汇+缺失模态"双重挑战。

Method: APCF框架包含四个核心模块：(1) 情感原型库（Affect-Prototype Bank）——从IEMOCAP数据集构建对齐类别语义和维度坐标的原型，并通过有界残差适应目标域情感短语；(2) 源域感知初始化——利用源域监督预训练证据编码器；(3) 原型条件证据提取与"仅观测"集合融合——通过原型条件FiLM和Set Transformer处理任意非空观测模态子集；(4) 情感原型因果路由——将语义先验与内容得分结合，将融合证据因果注入冻结的Qwen2.5-7B解码器生成情感短语。整个流程为无候选集生成框架。

Result: 在OV-MERD+和MER-FG两个数据集、七种模态组合下的实验表明，APCF在所有模态视图上均排名第一。在OV-MERD+上，A、AV、AT视图分别超越最优基线8.26、3.32、2.91分；在MER-FG上分别超越5.99、4.88、5.35分。消融实验验证了缺失视图学习（贡献最大）、源原型迁移、目标原型适应和因果路由各组件的必要性；有界适应在保持源结构（ρ=0.952）的同时提升了对齐质量（NRC-VAD的CCC指标提升）。

Conclusion: APCF成功将开放词汇情感识别扩展到不完整多模态场景，通过情感原型统一了语义引导与证据组织，使情感语义反向影响感知融合过程而非仅作为输出标签。该框架在不牺牲全模态性能的前提下，显著提升了缺失输入下的鲁棒性，为实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary multimodal emotion recognition (OV-MER) aims to generate open natural-language emotion labels from multimodal affective cues. In real-world scenarios, however, complete and synchronized modal data are difficult to obtain due to limitations of acquisition devices and user privacy constraints. Existing OV-MER methods are largely designed for full-modal inputs, and fail to perform effective feature fusion under modal missing conditions. Meanwhile, current fusion approaches designed for incomplete modalities mainly focus on fixed-label recognition context, and cannot satisfy the demand for fuse emotional cues guided with arbitrary emotion semantics in OV-MER context. To tackle these challenges, this paper proposes an Affect-Prototype-Conditioned Fusion (APCF) framework for incomplete open-vocabulary emotion recognition. As a candidate-free generative framework, APCF extends modal contribution learning to scenarios guided by arbitrary emotional semantics. Specifically, we construct an affect-prototype library to explicitly model multimodal contribution characteristics corresponding to diverse emotions, which provides dynamic constraints for modal fusion under different emotional semantic perspectives. Conditional retrieval and feature aggregation are conducted based on available modal features. The refined fused affective representations are then fed into an LLM decoder to produce open-vocabulary emotion labels. Experiments on the OV-MERD+ and MER-FG datasets demonstrate that APCF substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [133] [FlexEE: Self-Speculative and KV-Compatible Early Exiting for Offloading-Aware LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17008)
*Qihu Xie,Ziwei Li,Yi Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlexEE 提出了一种面向资源受限、基于卸载（offloading）的 LLM 推理的早期退出框架，通过层间退出监督、自推测 Top-K 词表解码和动态隐藏状态管理，在 Llama2 与 Llama3 系列上实现了显著的端到端加速且几乎不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在权重卸载场景下，LLM 自回归解码的瓶颈从计算转移到跨内存层级的权重加载；现有早期退出方法存在三个问题：①中间层语义解码能力弱，直接退出导致质量下降；②逐层全词表投影开销大，抵消加速收益；③退出破坏后续层的 KV 缓存，传统修补方法仍需加载所有剩余层权重，无法在卸载场景下获益。

Method: FlexEE 采用算法-系统协同设计，包含三个核心组件：(1) 层间退出监督（基于 LayerSkip 预训练检查点），使用共享 LM Head 对多层施加统一语言建模损失，将最终层语义能力迁移到浅层；(2) 自推测轻量级退出预测器，选择一个中间层作为 self-speculation 层，仅在该层做一次全词表投影生成 Top-K 局部词表（Llama2 取 K=30，Llama3 取 K=80），后续退出层仅在该紧凑候选集上做小规模投影，根据局部 Top-1 概率与 Gap 两个特征判断退出；(3) 动态隐藏状态管理，退出时缓存退出层隐藏状态，下一个 token 处理到退出层之后的第一层时将其与当前表征联合计算，避免强制复制或重建所有剩余层的 KV 缓存，从而避免加载这些层的权重。

Result: 在 Llama2-7B/13B 与 Llama3-8B 上的生成与下游任务（WikiText、Alpaca、LAMBADA、MMLU、CommonsenseQA、SST、BoolQ、PIQA、WinoGrande、ARC、TriviaQA、RACE、MathQA、MBPP 等）上，FlexEE 在精度上接近甚至略优于 Dense 基线；在 0%/50% 权重卸载下分别取得最高 1.27×/3.16×（Llama2-7B）与 1.25×/2.83×（Llama3-8B）的端到端加速；在 75% 卸载时 Llama2-7B 加速可达 3.29×；AlpacaEval-LC 与 MT-Bench 上与 Dense 解码质量相当；预测开销约为 AdaInfer 的 1/30、SpecEE 的 1/2.5。

Conclusion: FlexEE 通过将早期退出与卸载感知执行相结合，在小批量、内存受限的设备端 LLM 推理中能有效减少权重流量与计算量，同时保持与密集解码几乎一致的生成质量，为资源受限部署场景提供了一种系统-算法协同的实用加速方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is often constrained by both computation and memory, especially in offloading-based deployments where model weights are transferred across memory hierarchies during autoregressive decoding. In this setting, reducing the number of executed layers can lower per-token latency while also avoiding costly weight movement. Motivated by this observation, we present FlexEE, an early exiting framework for resource-constrained and offloading-based LLM inference. FlexEE makes early exiting practical for LLM decoding through layer-wise exit supervision for reliable intermediate-layer prediction, self-speculative decoding over a Top-K local vocabulary for low-cost exit decisions, and dynamic hidden state management for KV-cache-correct and memory-aware execution. Across generative and downstream tasks, FlexEE enables efficient early exit with minimal accuracy degradation, delivering up to 1.27$\times$/3.16$\times$ and 1.25$\times$/2.83$\times$ end-to-end speedups on Llama2-7B and Llama3-8B under 0\%/50\% weight offloading, respectively.

</details>


### [134] [ThinkFlow: Self-Evolving Probabilistic Latent Memory for Lifelong Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17010)
*Cai Ke,Xin Liu,Han Zhang,Jiangyue Yan,Zike Yuan,Ling Deng,Yue Yu,Hui Wang,Ruifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkFlow提出了一种用于终身对话代理的端到端潜在记忆框架，通过概率潜在记忆技能（PLMS）将对话流动态压缩为解耦的连续向量，绕过显式文本记忆的信息瓶颈；并引入测试时自监督演化范式，利用下一句用户话语预测任务实现无标签的持续个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显式文本记忆流水线存在严重的信息瓶颈，在摘要化过程中会丢失细微的行为模式和情感转变；且模型部署后通常静态，无法自主适应个人习惯与偏好。认知科学表明人类通过潜在心智模型和预测性编码来持续追踪他人心理状态。

Method: ThinkFlow包含三大核心组件：(1) 概率潜在记忆技能（PLMS）通过交叉注意力将隐藏状态压缩为K个解耦技能向量，并引入KL正则化建模认知不确定性；(2) 门控潜在整合器（GLC）通过信息增益门控和GRU控制记忆更新，过滤噪声；(4) 上下文感知超网络对齐器（CAHA）通过低秩超网络弥合历史潜在表征与当前语境的分布差距。训练分为两阶段——Phase 1通过KL散度将文本教师模型的对齐能力蒸馏到学生潜在记忆中以解决冷启动问题；Phase 2通过预测用户下一话语的自监督任务进行测试时持续演化。

Result: 在CC、MSC、GC三个长程对话数据集以及PersonaMem基准（含32K/128K/1M上下文）上的实验表明，ThinkFlow在BLEU-4、ROUGE-L、BertScore、Mauve等指标上均显著优于现有显式与潜在记忆基线，且在1M上下文场景下平均准确率较最佳可比方法提升3.86%。消融实验验证了PLMS、GLC、CAHA和两阶段训练的必要性。Token-时间效率分析显示ThinkFlow仅消耗2,857 tokens和12.1秒，效率远高于显式记忆方法。案例研究展示了其在追踪用户情感演化方面的优势。

Conclusion: ThinkFlow通过完全在潜在空间运行并持续自监督演化，有效突破了显式文本记忆的信息瓶颈与静态部署瓶颈，在个性化响应质量、记忆利用效率以及token-时间效率上均取得显著提升，为终身对话代理提供了一种轻量级、持续自适应的记忆范式。

Abstract: Lifelong conversational agents rely on memory systems to maintain deep, context-aware interactions with users. However, existing explicit textual memory pipelines suffer from a severe information bottleneck, often losing subtle behavioral patterns and emotional shifts. Furthermore, being typically static post-deployment, they cannot autonomously adapt to personal habits and preferences without manual feedback. Cognitive science, however, suggests that humans maintain mental models purely in a latent space and continuously refine them through predictive coding. Inspired by this, we propose \textbf{ThinkFlow}, a novel end-to-end latent memory framework for lifelong conversational agents. ThinkFlow bypasses the text bottleneck by dynamically compressing conversational flows into probabilistic latent memory skills, autonomously consolidating complex user states into disentangled, continuous vectors without semantic interference. To break this barrier, we introduce a test-time evolution paradigm. By coupling teacher-guided latent alignment to bootstrap the initial state with a self-supervised next-user-utterance prediction task for continuous refinement, the framework successfully overcomes cold-start challenges and achieves label-free lifelong personalization. Extensive experiments on long-term conversation benchmarks demonstrate that ThinkFlow significantly outperforms prevailing memory systems, providing highly personalized and contextually accurate responses over extended multi-session interactions.

</details>


### [135] [ORDER: Task-Conditioned Routing for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17012)
*Aurélien Pellet,Julien Perez,Marie Puren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ORDER是一个查询条件化的RAG框架，通过联合自适应索引和检索来解决传统RAG在异构领域专家查询中“一刀切”的局限性，通过查询路由实现更优的检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG管道采用预处理阶段确定的固定索引和检索配置，这种“一刀适所有”的设计不适合领域专家场景，因为异构查询需要不同的分块粒度、元数据约束和源选择策略，导致对一类查询有效的配置往往对其他查询表现不佳。

Method: 1. 在给定与语料库相关的问题集上发现语义聚类；2. 为每个聚类学习分块策略以及适配的元数据过滤和重排序配置；3. 推理时，通过最近质心分配将查询路由到预构建索引；4. 提出监督查询路由器QRe预测最可能包含相关证据的集合；5. 提出均匀多源采样器UMS在选定源之间均匀分配检索预算。

Result: 在大规模异构历史档案库上的评估表明，同时基于查询条件化索引和检索，始终优于朴素基线和强大的最先进RAG系统，特别是在复杂专家领域环境中。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，在RAG系统中联合自适应索引和检索（而非仅在检索端进行条件化）对于处理复杂领域专家查询至关重要，ORDER框架通过查询驱动的索引路由和资源分配策略显著提升了检索质量。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines typically rely on a fixed indexing and retrieval configuration determined at preprocessing time. This one-size-fits-all design is ill-suited to domain-expert settings, where heterogeneous queries require different chunking granularities, metadata constraints, and source-selection strategies. As a result, configurations that are effective for one family of queries often perform poorly for others. In this paper, we introduce ORDER (Optimal Routing for Dynamic Evidence Retrieval), a query-conditioned RAG framework that jointly adapts indexing and retrieval to the incoming query. Our approach first discovers semantic clusters over a given set of questions associated to a corpus and learns, for each cluster, a chunking strategy together with a suited metadata filtering and reranking configuration. At inference time, queries are routed to the appropriate pre-built index through nearest-centroid assignment. To further improve retrieval, we propose a supervised query router (QRe) that predicts which collections are most likely to contain relevant evidence, coupled with a Uniform Multi-source Sampler (UMS) that allocates the retrieval budget evenly across the selected sources. We evaluate our framework on large-scale, heterogeneous historical archives and show that conditioning both indexing and retrieval on the query consistently outperforms both naive baselines and strong state-of-the-art RAG systems in complex expert-domain environments.

</details>


### [136] [SKIP: a Self-knowledge-guided Step-wise Preference Learning Framework for Concise Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17019)
*Qinhong Lin,Yuhao Zhang,Yinglun Feng,Zhongliang Yang,Linna Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SKIP框架，通过自知识引导的逐步偏好学习，在不牺牲准确性的前提下压缩大语言模型的思维链推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在思维链(CoT)推理中存在"过度思考"问题，导致计算开销、推理延迟增加，甚至降低性能。现有简洁推理方法在压缩长度的同时显著损害准确性。作者旨在解决三个核心问题：如何高效构建压缩推理数据、如何防止压缩时准确率下降、以及该能力能否泛化到分布外数据集。

Method: SKIP框架包含三个阶段：(1) SFT冷启动——利用8组few-shot样例引导模型内化简洁推理风格；(2) 自知识引导的偏好数据构建——在每个句子后插入探针("The answer is")强制模型基于当前推理状态解码，根据中间步骤的正确性将样本分为四类(长对/短错、长错/短对等)，分别构建知识偏好数据(长且对>短且错)和长度偏好数据(短且对>长且对)；(3) Masked-DPO训练——对偏好数据中共享前缀进行掩码，仅训练非重叠部分以提高稳定性，仅微调LoRA参数并合并推理。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-3B、Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen3-14B三个模型上，针对GSM8K、MATH和MedQA三个数据集实验表明：(1) SKIP-8在几乎所有设置中实现最高的token效率(Eff=Accuracy/Length)；(2) 在显著压缩推理长度的同时保持甚至提升准确率，优于C3OT和BON基线；(3) 在StrategyQA和BBH Date Understanding等OOD任务上展现良好的泛化能力；(4) 消融实验验证了知识偏好数据与长度偏好数据的对抗平衡作用、训练数据规模效应以及Masked-DPO相对于标准DPO的优势。

Conclusion: SKIP通过利用模型自身知识进行推理边界探测，有效识别推理充分性，无需依赖外部知识或更强大模型即可构建高质量偏好数据。实验证明该方法能够在保持推理准确性的同时显著压缩CoT长度，且能力可迁移至OOD数据集，为简洁推理研究提供了有效方案。局限在于效率度量可能受收益递减效应影响，且需在更广泛数据上评估。

Abstract: While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has been proven to be effective, it often leads to overthinking, resulting in computational overhead, inference latency, and even degraded performance in large language models (LLMs). Existing concise reasoning frameworks significantly compromise accuracy while compressing the length of output. In this paper, we propose SKIP, a self-knowledge-guided step-wise preference learning framework. Starting with lightweight fine-tuning to adjust the model's output style, SKIP introduces a carefully designed knowledge probing mechanism to guide model to output an answer at each reasoning step. Based on the correctness of intermediate steps, we construct preference data that guide the model toward more efficient and correct reasoning by leveraging DPO. Experimental results demonstrate that our method effectively improves reasoning compression while mitigating performance degradation after fine-tuning. Besides, SKIP shows strong generalization ability on out-of-distribution datasets. We further conducted ablation studies on the component parameters of our framework.

</details>


### [137] [Sparse MLLM Anchors, Dense Adaptation: Breaking the Self-Referential Loop in Wild Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17040)
*Zhenbin Wang,Lei Zhang,Lituan Wang,Yan Wang,Zhao Zhang,Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MASA框架，通过冻结多模态大语言模型(MLLM)提供的稀疏语义锚点，突破野测试时自适应(WTTA)中模型内部信号导致的"自指循环"问题。该方法查询少量多样且可靠的锚点，将语义描述传播至邻近样本，并构建视觉-语义原型记忆，通过描述符感知检索指导轻量化的归一化仿射参数自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WTTA方法的适应信号（如预测不确定性、样本可靠性、局部特征几何）均源自被适应的模型本身。在分布偏移下，源模型不可靠时，这些信号会强化自身错误，形成"自指循环"，在小子批和不均衡流中后果尤为严重。现有生成式先验仅在像素/分数层面作用，缺乏对干扰因素的显式语义描述，且计算开销大。

Method: MASA包含四大组件：(1) 两阶段证据选择——结合ReCAP区域熵(RE/RI)与DeYO补丁敏感性(PLPD)筛选可靠样本；(2) 可靠性引导的语义锚点——按可靠性评分与最远点采样选取少量视觉多样锚点，查询冻结的Qwen2-VL-2B获取对象家族及干扰因素描述，用CLIP文本编码器编码；(3) 语义传播——通过视觉邻域将稀疏描述传播至所有样本；(4) 视觉-语义原型记忆——结合视觉、预测、语义嵌入及代码集合的描述符感知检索，提供辅助一致性目标，用于更新BN/LN/GN的仿射参数（每批一步）。同时维护FIFO候选窗口、覆盖监测与恢复机制。

Result: 在ImageNet-C基准的有限批次（batch=1）、混合域、时变标签偏移三种WTTA协议下，使用ResNet50-GN和ViT-Base-LN：有限批次准确率达48.4%/66.5%，超越ReCAP 2.0/0.8点；标签偏移达48.2%/64.0%，超越ReCAP 2.4/1.0点；混合域平均领先1.0/0.4点。消融实验验证原型损失、传播、可靠性过滤、PLPD各组件均有贡献，预测一致性与语义一致性在视觉匹配基础上带来单调增益。Qwen2-VL-2B作为语义源优于BLIP-2和LLaVA-1.5-7B，且无需类别提示即可工作。

Conclusion: MASA通过稀疏MLLM语义锚点为WTTA提供模型外部证据，打破自指循环，同时保持轻量化自适应开销。在多个协议和骨干网络上取得SOTA，消融支持各组件互补性。局限包括：仅在ImageNet-C分类评估、依赖冻结MLLM增加延迟与内存、描述可靠性过滤非完美、传播与排序仍依赖分类器特征表示。研究应进一步探索面向自然域偏移、开放集与密集预测任务的稀疏语义基础。

Abstract: Wild test-time adaptation (WTTA) updates a source model online under small test batches, concurrent distribution shifts, and time-varying class imbalance. Most WTTA methods derive their adaptation signals, including predictive uncertainty, sample reliability, and local feature geometry, from the model being adapted. When the source model is unreliable under shift, these signals can reinforce its own errors, forming a self-referential loop. We introduce MASA (Multimodal-LLM-Anchored Semantic Adaptation), which complements model-internal evidence with structured semantic descriptions from a frozen multimodal large language model (MLLM). To limit inference cost, MASA queries the MLLM only for a small set of diverse, reliability-ranked anchors. The resulting descriptions capture the object family and nuisance factors such as style, viewpoint, and occlusion. MASA encodes these descriptions, propagates them to neighboring test samples, and stores the resulting visual-semantic information in an online prototype memory. Descriptor-aware retrieval from this memory provides an auxiliary target for lightweight adaptation of normalization-affine parameters. We evaluate MASA on the WTTA ImageNet-C benchmark under limited-batch, mixed-domain, and imbalanced-label-shift settings with ResNet and ViT backbones.

</details>


### [138] [Neuro-Symbolic Hierarchical Intention Anticipation in Human Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17064)
*Farnaz Soleimani,Abdelghani Chibani,Yacine Amirat,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于辅助自主系统的分层规划解码器（HPD），通过目标推断而非精确运动预测来实现行为预期。HPD在冻结的神经-符号识别编码器之上附加，以四个本体论层级预测下一动作、剩余活动及低层意图，以及高层意图（HLI），并通过软神经-符号正则化训练和硬可达性掩码解码，确保本体论有效性。在四层级组合基准上，其性能随预期视野扩大而显著提升，并在组合泛化与逻辑约束满足方面展现出对最强基线的明显优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助自主系统需要在人类行为完成前就预测其目标。现有方法多关注精确运动预测，而非高层目标推断。本文旨在将行为预期重新定义为从部分观察的多模态片段中进行目标推断，并结构化预测剩余行为，以更好地支持辅助系统的决策。

Method: 1) 在冻结的神经-符号识别编码器之上附加紧凑的分层规划解码器（HPD）。2) HPD在四个本体论层级上预测：下一动作、剩余活动与低层意图、高层意图（HLI）。3) 使用软神经-符号正则化（转移一致性损失+层级连续性损失）进行训练。4) 推理时使用硬可达性掩码强制本体论有效性。5) 在基于NTU RGB+D 120特征构建的15,002个多模态片段的四层级组合基准上进行评估。

Result: 三项核心发现：1) 相对最强序列基线的优势随预期视野扩大而增长，从step 1的+1.7点扩大到step 3的+7.3点（top-5准确率）。2) 在组合泛化场景下（每个多父节点低层意图保留一个父关联作为测试集），优势进一步扩大，step 1即达+4.9点。3) 在片段层面，96.8%的预期轨迹满足联合逻辑约束，超越最强基线的88.1%及真实标注的73.9%下限；软逻辑项使HLI可达性违规相对减少59.8%-71.1%，硬掩码则完全消除此类违规。

Conclusion: 神经生成提供预测排序能力，符号约束确保本体论有效性，二者的结合产生了连贯的分层行为预期。然而，组合目标泛化和无序集合预测仍是尚未解决的难题，构成了未来工作的核心方向。

Abstract: Assistive autonomous systems must anticipate human goals before an observed behavior is complete. This article formulates anticipation as goal inference from a partially observed multimodal episode together with structured prediction of the remaining behavior, rather than exact motor forecasting. A compact Hierarchical Planning Decoder (HPD) is attached to a frozen neuro-symbolic recognition encoder and predicts, at four ontological levels, the next actions, the remaining activities and low-level intentions, and the episode high-level intention(HLI). The decoder is trained with soft neuro-symbolic regularization combining transition-coherence and hierarchical continuity losses, and is decoded with hard reachability masks that enforce ontological validity at inference. On a compositional four-level benchmark of 15,002 multimodal episodes built over NTU RGB+D 120 features, three headline properties are observed together. The advantage over the strongest sequential baseline grows with the anticipation horizon, from +1.7 points at step 1 to +7.3 points at step 3 (top-5). Under compositional generalization, where one parent association per multi-parent low level intention is held out, this advantage widens to +4.9 points at step 1. At the episode level, 96.8% of anticipated trajectories satisfy the joint logic constraints, above the 88.1% strongest-baseline value and the 73.9% ground-truth floor; soft logic terms alone account for a 59.8 to 71.1% relative reduction of HLI-reachability violations, and the hard masks then eliminate them entirely. Neural generation supplies predictive ranking, symbolic constraints supply onto logical validity, and their combination yields coherent hierarchical anticipation while exposing remaining challenges in compositional goal generalization and unordered set prediction.

</details>


### [139] [Sample-Conditioned Representation Selection for Audio Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17076)
*Fengrui Liu,Ningxin Shen,Yi Li,Yiwei Fu,Feng Liu,Jiangmeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SampleSelect，一种面向少样本音频分类的样本条件表征选择方法，通过可微分 Gumbel Top-k 掩码在冻结的源编码器上为每个输入预测固定预算的特征子集，以应对前景-背景共现关系变化导致的 OOD 性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本音频分类器常利用前景与背景的共现统计作为捷径，但当测试时这种关联发生偏移，模型性能显著下降。分析发现这种表征偏移高度集中在少数通道且因类别而异，固定或全局选择策略无法应对。

Method: 三阶段流程：(1) 训练并冻结源编码器与分类器；(2) 训练轻量评分器 G_φ，独立地为每个输入预测特征重要性得分，通过 Gumbel Softmax 无放回采样实现可微分 Top-k 松弛，损失包含前景分类损失和跨背景对比损失；(3) 推理时使用确定性 Top-k 掩码，并以掩码后的支持集拟合一个新的线性头进行少样本预测。

Result: 在 SpurAudio 数据集上，ResNet12 和 Conv64 在 5-way 1-shot/5-shot 设置下均取得最佳 OOD 准确率，较匹配的 FullRep 对照提升 4.90–8.38 个百分点；消融实验验证了学习式选择、跨背景监督和样本条件化的必要性；分析表明选中通道具有功能重要性，偏移集中且与类别相关。

Conclusion: SampleSelect 通过样本条件化的固定预算特征选择，有效缓解了少样本音频分类中前景-背景共现偏移问题，冻结源表征的设计使其易于部署，且无需测试时背景标签或查询标签。

Abstract: Few-shot audio classifiers may rely on foreground-background co-occurrences and fail when those correlations shift. On SpurAudio, the resulting representation shift is concentrated and class dependent: for ResNet12, the top 10 percent of channels explain 82.80 percent of the null-corrected shift contribution. We propose SAMPLESELECT, which predicts a fixed-budget feature mask independently for each input while keeping the encoder and source classifier frozen. Training uses differentiable Gumbel Top-k selection with foreground classification and cross-background contrastive losses; inference uses deterministic Top-k masks and support-only linear adaptation. Across ResNet12 and Conv64 in 5-way 1-shot and 5-shot evaluation, SAMPLESELECT gives the best OOD accuracy among the compared methods and improves the matched full-representation control by 4.90-8.38 percentage points. Ablations and representation analyses further support the learned selection mechanism. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [140] [Interactive Memory Learning for Long-Term Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17088)
*Cai Ke,Jiangyue Yan,Han Zhang,Xin Liu,Zike Yuan,Yue Yu,Hui Wang,Ruifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ICML（Interactive Memory Learning）框架，将长对话中的记忆管理从静态启发式范式转变为可学习的、交互式的记忆策略，通过Planner和Trigger双智能体在线强化学习实现协同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长对话记忆方法普遍采用静态启发式范式，将信息被动归档而缺乏自适应记忆价值评估，无法根据用户需求演化。认知心理学表明人类记忆是主动编码与反馈更新的学习过程，而非被动存档。受此启发，作者提出将记忆管理从被动范式转变为主动学习范式。

Method: 1）将长对话建模为POMDP序贯决策问题；2）提出回溯式会话合成（Retrospective Session Synthesis），以种子会话为锚点反向生成逻辑一致的前置会话，再正向标注得到专家数据，缓解冷启动；3）设计Planner（选择性存储高价值记忆）和Trigger（动态检索记忆）双Actor-Critic智能体，通过Cross-Session Truth Reward将未来回合的质量反馈回传至历史存储决策；4）采用PPO算法进行端到端在线优化，两智能体通过延迟奖励协同进化。

Result: 在CC、MSC、GC三个长对话数据集上，ICML在BLEU-4、ROUGE-L、BertScore、Mauve等指标及LLM交叉评估、人类评估中均显著优于Mem0、A-Mem、MemoryOS、LD-Agent、THEANINE等强基线；消融实验验证各组件均不可或缺；Planner精确率和Trigger召回率随交互持续提升；推理token和延迟保持稳定，具有良好的工程实用性。

Conclusion: ICML成功将被动启发式记忆范式转变为可学习的交互式记忆策略，通过双智能体协同进化和延迟反馈机制使记忆策略与用户期望对齐，在生成质量、个性化和计算效率方面均取得优异表现，为终身个性化对话助手提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models have significantly enhanced the capabilities of agents in modeling long-term conversations. Despite these successes, existing approaches typically adopt a static heuristic paradigm, where information is passively archived without adaptive memory valuation. Consequently, these methods fail to self-evolve or align their memory management with evolving user needs. To address this, we propose ICML (InteraCtive Memory Learning), a multi-agent framework that transforms the memory mechanism from a passive archive into a learnable, interactive memory policy. Specifically, we first employ a session synthesis pipeline to generate expert data, facilitating rapid test-time adaptation in unseen scenarios. Building on this, ICML utilizes an online reinforcement learning mechanism where a Planner agent selectively encodes high-value information and a Trigger agent dynamically retrieves it to optimize response quality, whereby the two agents co-evolve through continuous interaction feedback. Crucially, both agents are synchronized through a delayed reward mechanism that propagates future feedback back to earlier storage decisions, ensuring memory policies are precisely aligned with user expectations. Experimental results demonstrate that ICML significantly outperforms strong baselines, exhibiting the unique capability to continuously improve response quality as interactions accumulate.

</details>


### [141] [Scaling-Score Conformal Prediction for Multi-Target Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17091)
*Sylvain Rousseau,Soundouss Messoudi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型无关的保形预测方法（scaling-score conformal），用于多目标回归的联合预测区域构建，仅需逐分量绝对残差，使用单一校准集即可生成嵌套的四类输出区域（外矩形SCO、精确集R_α、阶梯形SC2近似、内矩形SCI），并通过单一超参数γ控制基矩形分位数水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标保形预测方法存在局限：max-聚合忽略尺度差异；copula方法仅渐近有效；矩形方法通常需拆分校准集；分位数/密度方法需训练超越普通点预测的专门模型。亟需一种模型无关、样本高效、有限样本有效的联合预测区域构建方法。

Method: 提出scaling-score conformal方法：(1) 计算逐分量的绝对残差并进行缩放以消除尺度差异；(2) 使用单一校准集计算基矩形分位数（由超参数γ∈(0,1)独立于α控制）；(3) 生成四类嵌套区域——SCO（有效联合覆盖的外矩形）、精确集R_α、SC2（阶梯形近似）、SCI（内矩形）；(4) 理论证明向下封闭性（downward-closedness）和矩形夹逼界（rectangular sandwich bound），并推导闭式外矩形。

Result: 在29个真实数据集上的实验验证：(1) 联合覆盖有效；(2) γ=1-α时的SC2方法在各维度下均获得与基线方法相当的体积；(3) 随着输出维度d增大，SC2的相对优势越显著。

Conclusion: scaling-score conformal是一种实用、高效的多目标回归联合保形预测方法，在保持模型无关性与有限样本有效性的同时，其体积效率在高维输出场景下尤为突出。

Abstract: Multi-target regression requires a model to simultaneously predict several related outputs. Conformal prediction provides distribution-free, finite-sample marginal coverage guarantees, but extending these to joint multi-dimensional regions in a model-agnostic, sample-efficient manner remains challenging: max-aggregation ignores scale differences, copula-based methods are only asymptotically valid, rectangular methods typically split the calibration set, and quantile or density-based methods require training a specialised model beyond a plain point predictor. We propose the scaling-score conformal method, which is model-agnostic (requires only component-wise absolute residuals), uses a single calibration set, and yields four nested output types: an outer rectangle (SCO) with valid joint coverage, the exact set R $\alpha$ , a staircase (SC 2 ) over approximation of R $\alpha$ , and an inner rectangle (SCI). A single hyperparameter $\gamma$ $\in$ (0, 1) controls the base-rectangle quantile level independently of $\alpha$. We prove downward-closedness and a rectangular sandwich bound and derive a closed-form outer rectangle. Experiments on 29 realworld datasets confirm valid joint coverage; SC 2 with $\gamma$ = 1-$\alpha$ consistently achieves competitive volume relative to baselines, with the advantage growing with output dimension d.

</details>


### [142] [Semi-Supervised Learning-Based Genetic Biomarkers Dataset for Multiple-Stage Hepatocellular Carcinoma Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17100)
*Ahmed Ammar Kubba,Manar Abu Talib,Jibran Sualeh Muhammad,Ali Bou Nassif,Abdalla Sayed Mohamed,Darko Castven,Jens U. Marquardt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于半监督学习与XGBoost的多阶段肝细胞癌（HCC）基因组数据集构建方法，整合Lubeck、TCGA和GSE89377三个数据集，最终形成包含770个患者样本、11150个基因表达特征、5个类别（SL、EHCC、PHCC、LGDN、HGDN）的公开可用HCC数据集，XGBoost分类准确率达96.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝细胞癌（HCC）致死率高，但目前缺乏利用基因组数据进行AI模型训练的公开可用HCC数据集，且现有研究普遍面临样本量小、数据不足的局限，亟需构建大规模、多类别、高维度的基因组数据集以支持自动化HCC分期分类模型的开发。

Method: 以包含五类精细标注（sl、ehcc、phcc、lgdn、hgdn）的Lubeck私有数据集（29个样本）为基准，采用XGBoost模型进行半监督学习：(1) 首先在Lubeck数据集上训练XGBoost；(2) 利用训练好的模型对TCGA数据集（782个样本）进行预测；(3) 设定40%置信度阈值，并依据医学专家定义的标签映射规则（如TCGA的"HCC"映射为ehcc或phcc，"Transition"映射为lgdn或hgdn，"Normal"映射为sl），筛选高置信度且与真实标签匹配的样本加入Lubeck数据集；(4) 在扩展后的数据集上重新训练，迭代至模型性能收敛。同时将GSE89377数据集中与HCC相关的样本（如cirrhosis映射为Normal、dysplastic nodules映射为lgdn/hgdn、Early HCC映射为ehcc、TG2/TG3映射为phcc等）按医学专家知识映射整合，并保留三个数据集共有的基因特征（最终11150个）。

Result: 最终构建的HCC数据集包含770个患者样本，每样本含11150个基因表达特征，涵盖5个类别，但存在显著的类别不平衡（phcc和ehcc样本最多，sl样本不足50个）。XGBoost模型在该数据集上达到96.5%准确率、90.6%精确率、88.9%召回率和89.3% F1分数，交叉熵损失为0.1015。从各类别表现看，phcc和lgdn表现最佳（F1为100%和93.3%），hgdn表现最差（F1为69.6%），反映出类别不平衡对少数类的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作通过半监督学习方法成功构建了一个相对大规模、高维度的HCC基因组数据集，可支持AI驱动的HCC早期诊断、分期分类和预后预测模型开发。主要局限在于数据集类别不平衡，未来工作将采用类别加权、SMOTE过采样等技术缓解不平衡问题，并通过外部数据集验证、概率校准以及跨学科合作进一步提升数据集质量和模型泛化能力，同时筛选具有临床价值的基因特征。

Abstract: Liver cancer is a complex disease responsible for a high number of deaths across the globe each year, making automated solutions for liver cancer classification urgent. The most common form of liver cancer is hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), accounting for over 90% of liver cancer cases. There is a distinct lack of publicly available HCC datasets utilizing genomic data, which is necessary for training artificial intelligence (AI) models for automated HCC classification. This study proposes constructing a multi-stage HCC dataset using XGBoost and Semi-Supervised learning on three separate datasets of genomic biomarkers, utilizing their existing labels in the Semi-Supervised learning process to label the proposed dataset. The proposed dataset consists of 770 patient samples in total, categorized into five classes that represent normal tissue alongside different stages of HCC. Each sample in the dataset consists of 11,150 different gene expression levels. The XGBoost model demonstrated a final classification accuracy of 96.5% during the Semi-Supervised learning process.

</details>


### [143] [Symbolic Separation: Grounding Deep Agents in Knowledge Graphs for Trustworthy Operational Data Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17107)
*Baibek Davletiyarov,Junaid Ahmed Khan,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"符号分离"(Symbolic Separation)设计原则，将神经层的自由推理与符号层的本体约束数据访问解耦，构建神经符号深度分析代理(Neurosymbolic Deep Analyst)，用于数据中心时序遥测数据的可信自然语言分析。在49.9TB超算遥测数据上的实验表明，该方法将端到端任务成功率从43%提升至86%，消除静默数据完整性错误，并降低2.4倍token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI虽承诺自然语言访问数据中心和工业4.0的海量数值遥测数据，但现有text-to-query和工具调用代理在真实数据库任务上仅能正确回答约一半问题，在多步运营查询上更低。核心失败原因不是字段名错误，而是LLM在组合多源关系时产生的关系/连接幻觉，且现有工具API仅强制"接口契约"（单次调用词法和类型有效），无法约束跨调用的关系组合逻辑。

Method: 1. **符号分离架构**：协调器+两个深度子代理（神经符号数据检索器EXASAGE Reflect Agent + 深度代码代理），通过MCP协议容器化通信；协调器自由推理，EXASAGE Reflect Agent作为唯一数据入口；2. **修订的ODA本体**：308条公理、36个类、23个对象属性、55个数据属性，分PhysicalEntity/LogicalEntity/ExternalEntity三层，含传递性contains关系，支持多跳推理；3. **EXASAGE Reflect Agent三阶段管道**——输入验证（意图分类→查询分解→插件分类→基于嵌入的指标检索，含反思循环）、SPARQL与VKG生成（带本体一致性验证器和有界自修正循环）、查询执行；4. **确定性本体一致性验证器**：在执行前拒绝任何引用不存在类/属性或违反domain-range约束的查询，比语法约束解码更严格；5. **VKG缓存机制**：基于提取实体哈希的命名图存储，LRU淘汰策略；6. **沙箱代码执行**：嵌套容器+gVisor拦截系统调用，静态审计风险导入。

Result: 在M100 ExaData 49.9TB遥测数据上，三种架构×两种开源LLM×14个端到端任务的84次试验：神经符号深度分析代理+Qwen3.6-35B-A3B MoE取得12/14（86%）全任务成功，而EXASAGE Query Tool（SoA#1）仅7%，非符号深度分析代理（SoTA#2，Datalake直接查询）43%（GPT-OSS-120B）/14%（Qwen3.6-35B-A3B）；EXASAGE Query Tool甚至出现跨容器逃逸以访问数据湖的违规行为；在75条HPC ODA检索查询上，EXASAGE Reflect Agent正确率88%（66/75），EXASAGE Query Tool 56%（42/75），Datalake Query Tool 48%（36/75）；成功查询token中位数300k vs 713k（2.4×效率提升）；EXASAGE Reflect Agent零幻觉输出，Datalake Query Tool在Q11/Q12产生事实错误但表面可信的答案。

Conclusion: 符号分离——将符号管道分解为专注的专门子代理，而非为单体提示添加agentic循环——是大规模时序遥测数据上准确且可信的分析代理的关键设计原则；本体验证的关系预连接有效消除了LLM推断跨源连接时的幻觉，且小规模本地部署模型可超越更大模型；未来工作将扩展可视化验证器、支持有状态和事件查询、并应用于其他IoT/工业4.0场景。

Abstract: Generative AI promises natural language access to the massive numerical telemetry of data centers and Industry 4.0 installations, yet text-to-query and tool-using agents stay unreliable: even frontier models answer little more than half of real-world database questions, and far fewer of the multi-step, operational ones, because the LLM must compose how heterogeneous sources relate and hallucinates the relations, not just the fields. We propose symbolic separation: a deep agent reasons freely but may act on data only through an ontology-constrained Virtual Knowledge Graph with deterministic pre-execution validation. Unlike a tool API's interface contract, this domain-semantic contract turns a complex question into one validated graph traversal instead of LLM-inferred joins. Instantiated as the Neurosymbolic Deep Analyst and evaluated on 49.9 TB of superconputer telemetry against a rigid workflow and a non-symbolic ablation, it raises end-to-end task success from 43% to 86%, prevents silent data-integrity errors that no syntactic check catches, and cuts token cost by 2.4x, letting a smaller on-premise model outperform a larger one.

</details>


### [144] [Shared-Prefix KV Reuse Across Standard LoRA Adapters: Quality and Serving Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17109)
*Dushyant Rajput*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在共享主干+多个LoRA专家的可组合服务部署中，复用主干预填充KV缓存对任务质量的影响与服务成本收益。基于Qwen3-1.7B和两个标准LoRA适配器（HotpotQA抽取式QA与GSM8K算术推理），发现全前缀复用可大幅降低热缓存TTFT（约16倍于8K上下文），但带来小且不一致的质量损失（GSM8K上−4.6/−3.0/−0.8 EM），且未实现物理KV存储共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享上下文的LoRA多专家服务场景中，朴素服务需对每个专家重复预填充相同上下文。现有方法（如PagedAttention、RadixAttention）针对同一模型复用前缀缓存；跨模型KV迁移与Activated LoRA则针对不同场景，但均未覆盖"已训练标准LoRA适配器在共享主干上直接复用基础KV缓存"这一实际场景的量化研究。

Method: 复用流程：主干在禁用适配器情况下计算共享前缀的预填充KV缓存（基础表示），专家处理非复用后缀并生成；首token logits始终来自专家。扫描四个接管边界（native、early、question、full-prefix）并对比；评估指标为QA的token级F1与GSM8K的精确匹配；使用配对Bootstrap 95%置信区间。还测试了闭式ridge KV翻译器（单层与多层变体，RoPE剥离键）以及aLoRA适配器的初步对比。

Result: 1. 全前缀复用在GSM8K（held-out）造成−4.6 EM损失（160 token预算，CI [−8.8, −0.4]），320 token预算时为−3.0，第二种子下为−0.8；所有点估计均偏向native但仅第一种排除零。<br>2. QA任务上F1损失上下文相关：700 token时+0.3（无损），2K时−6.6，8K时−4.5。<br>3. 热缓存TTFT加速约16倍（8K上下文，486→30 ms），但两分支峰值内存仅降12%（8K），前缀存储从未物理共享（0%别名）。<br>4. 接管边界扫描呈现非单调模式：question-takeover最差，full-prefix最接近native。<br>5. Ridge KV翻译器未胜过直接复用（增加每token矩阵乘成本）。<br>6. 专家依赖性对比的置信区间均包含零，未建立专家特异性脆弱性。<br>7. aLoRA对比因激活门控正确性未验证而无定论。

Conclusion: 对已训练标准LoRA适配器直接复用基础预填充KV缓存是一种"质量–延迟权衡"而非等价方案。服务收益仅限于热缓存TTFT（长上下文显著），内存节省因未实现物理共享而有限。质量损失存在但幅度因种子、预算、上下文而异且不一致，未建立通用的边界选择规则；物理共享缓存存储需分页缓存等额外实现。复用应在自有适配器上验证后方可视为无损。

Abstract: A common small-model deployment runs one shared backbone with several LoRA specialists that answer over the same context. Serving them naively re-prefills that shared context once per specialist. We study a narrow, practical question: for already-trained standard LoRA adapters -- not adapters retrained for cache compatibility -- how much task quality is preserved if the backbone's prefill KV cache is computed once and reused across specialists, and what does that buy in serving cost? On a Qwen3-1.7B backbone with two adapters (extractive QA on HotpotQA, arithmetic reasoning on GSM8K), we sweep the boundary at which the specialist takes over from the reused base cache and measure paired quality differences and serving cost. Full-prefix reuse had the lowest prefill cost and a small quality difference on held-out GSM8K (Delta = -4.6 EM at a 160-token budget; -3.0 at 320 tokens; -0.8 under a second training seed -- all favoring native, only the first excluding zero, and the magnitude not consistent). Partial recomputation provided no demonstrated advantage. Neither quality equivalence nor a general boundary-selection rule is established. We also report a closed-form ridge KV translator that did not beat direct reuse, and specialist-dependence contrasts whose intervals all include zero. The measured serving benefit is warm-cache time-to-first-token, which grows with context (~16x at 8K); two-branch peak memory was only 12% lower and, on inspection, the prefix was never physically shared across branches -- this implementation reuses KV values but copies their storage, so shared-cache memory savings are not achieved.

</details>


### [145] [FirmCORe: A Benchmark for Structured Reasoning about Inter-Firm Collaboration Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17128)
*Tian Du,Tiantong Wu,Yafei Wang,Mengyu Liu,Xingyan Chen,Mu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FirmCORe是一个包含2,805对企业对的人工标注基准，用于评估LLM在企业间合作机会推理中的结构化预测能力，覆盖中英双语平行评估集。任务要求模型基于两个弱结构化的企业档案，联合预测合作机会是否存在、强度、类型和角色方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业间合作关系的结构化数据稀缺且分散，从企业档案推断合作机会面临核心挑战：相似企业可能是竞争对手，而不相似的企业可能具有互补能力。现有方法（企业相似度、供应链链接预测、合作伙伴推荐）均未直接评估这种基于能力互补性的推理能力，LLM在此类多维企业间合作推理中的能力尚不明确。

Method: 构建FirmCORe基准，采用分层候选对挖掘策略（强信号、临界信号、同行业困难对、随机非边对），通过盲源双人独立标注加第三人裁决的金标准聚合方法获得2,805对标签。提供中英平行评估集，采用字段级翻译和人工审查保证语义保持。在零样本设置下评估17个LLM（涵盖本地部署和托管模型），使用Macro-F1、四字段精确匹配、跨语言一致性等指标进行端到端和oracle-detection评估。

Result: 最佳模型Qwen3.6-27B在机会检测上达到Macro-F1 74.51，但四字段精确匹配仅61.57%。Kimi-K2.7在合作类型和角色方向上表现最佳（Macro-F1分别为60.23和67.71），但在机会检测上仅52.87。LLM倾向于将商业关联误判为合作机会（精度低），跨语言影响因模型而异，高跨语言一致性可能掩盖共同的错误。Technology Transfer类型特别难识别，角色方向预测存在明显的多数类偏差。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在识别广泛合作潜力方面比重构完整关系结构（强度、类型、角色方向）更可靠。它们经常过度预测机会，将相关性误认为互补性，混淆不同的合作机制，并误判资源流方向。语言效应因模型、实例和类别而异，高跨语言一致性可能反映的是跨语言重复的相同错误而非稳健的推理。FirmCORe为未来模型的基于档案的、多维企业间推理提供了受控的测试平台。

Abstract: Comprehensive structured data on inter-firm relationships is often scarce or inaccessible because many relationships are privately negotiated, selectively disclosed, and fragmented across proprietary databases. This scarcity hinders the discovery of collaboration opportunities, particularly for startups and small and medium-sized enterprises. Firm profiles are readily available, but collaboration potential cannot be inferred from business similarity alone, since similar firms may be competitors, whereas dissimilar firms may offer complementary products, technologies, channels, capabilities, or capital. We present FirmCORe (Inter-Firm Collaboration Opportunity Reasoning), a human-annotated benchmark for pairwise reasoning over weakly structured firm profiles, comprising 2,805 labeled firm pairs. Given two firm profiles, a model must determine whether the available evidence supports a collaboration opportunity and, for positive pairs, jointly predict its strength, primary collaboration type, and role direction. FirmCORe also provides parallel Chinese- and English-language evaluation sets containing identical instances and gold labels, enabling controlled analysis of input-language sensitivity. Experiments with representative locally deployed and hosted large language models (LLMs) show that the strongest model achieves a macro-F1 score of 74.51 for opportunity detection but only 61.57% exact match across all four output fields. Language effects vary across models, and high cross-language agreement can mask errors shared across languages. These results indicate that current LLMs are substantially more reliable at detecting broad collaboration opportunities than at identifying their specific types and role directions.

</details>


### [146] [MOCC-R1: Reinforcing Reasoning-Response Consistency for Multimodal Counselor Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17180)
*Wenjie Zheng,Qiming Xie,Jianfei Yu,Rui Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOCC多模态心理咨询对话数据集及MOCC-R1两阶段框架，通过显式优化咨询推理链与最终回复之间的一致性，提升多模态咨询回复生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCRG研究受限于两个核心问题：(1) 缺乏由合格咨询师录制的、持续的、多模态人真实咨询交互数据；(2) 现有训练目标未显式优化咨询推理与生成回复之间的一致性，导致模型可能产生推理链自相矛盾或计划未执行的问题（实验显示约40%的推理-回复链不一致）。

Method: 1) 构建MOCC数据集：包含154名持证咨询师录制的482个会话、约203小时视频、3709个以问题为中心的对话单元、183K条话语；2) MOCC-R1两阶段框架：冷启动SFT阶段使用MLLM标注器生成验证过的伪标注（结构化决策链：客户状态理解s + 咨询原则p + 计划行动a + 回复y），通过独立LLM验证器进行六项检查；3) GRPO阶段结合结果奖励（安全性作为硬门槛 + 格式/内容/多样性/共情）与一致性奖励（Grounded Plan Coherence评估x→s→(p,a)的连贯性；Plan Execution评估(p,a)→y^的执行度），最终奖励采用r_final = r_outcome · [α + (1-α)r_consistency]的乘法形式，以min(r_plan, r_execution)作为链级瓶颈。

Result: 在MOCC测试集上：(1) MOCC-R1在响应质量指标（BLEU-2: 4.79, ROUGE-L: 14.20, BERTScore: 52.56等）上全面优于GPT-5.5、Claude-4.5-Sonnet等通用MLLM以及ESCoT、M2CoSC、MultiMood、Kardia-R1等任务专用基线；(2) 一致性方面，将推理-回复不一致率从43.28%降至11.82%，Grounded Plan Coherence从84.80%提升至95.75%，Plan Execution从59.92%提升至89.00%；(3) 人类评估显示在流畅度、有用性和一致性上MOCC-R1胜率均显著高于Outcome-only GRPO基线（一致性75.7% vs 11.7%）。

Conclusion: MOCC填补了多模态心理咨询数据空白，MOCC-R1验证了将链级一致性作为独立优化目标的必要性——仅优化响应级质量不足以保证规划与执行的连贯性。该工作为可信多模态咨询系统提供了新的数据集与方法范式。

Abstract: Multimodal counselor response generation (MCRG) aims to generate an appropriate counselor response from multimodal dialogue histories. Progress is limited by two gaps: first, existing datasets rarely capture sustained, human-recorded counseling interactions conducted by qualified counselors; Second, existing methods do not explicitly optimize consistency between counseling reasoning and the generated response, potentially undermining the reliability of MCRG systems. Thus, we introduce MOCC, a multimodal counseling conversation corpus containing over 200 hours of interactions involving 154 credential-verified counselors. Based on MOCC, we propose MOCC-R1, a two-stage framework for optimizing reasoning-response consistency. Cold-start supervised fine-tuning trains the model to generate a structured trajectory consisting of client-state understanding, a response intent that links a counseling principle to a planned action, and the final response. Reinforcement learning (RL) then rewards grounded plan coherence and plan execution, encouraging the inferred state and plan to be supported by the dialogue context and the response to realize that plan. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed MOCC-R1.

</details>


### [147] [End-to-End Latency-Minimizing and Load-Balanced Request Scheduling for Edge LLM Inference in Agentic AI Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17193)
*Zhen Li,Jun Cai,Haoran Gao,An Li,Tan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘LLM推理中的在线请求调度问题，提出了LYREO框架，联合最小化长期平均端到端时延并均衡异构边缘服务器负载。核心方法包括基于Lyapunov优化的长期负载均衡约束转换，以及基于LSTM序列回报预测的奖励再分配机制，将延迟反馈转化为即时学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI服务依赖LLM推理的低时延响应，将LLM部署到分布式边缘服务器可缩短传输路径。然而，边缘服务器在通信、计算能力上存在异构性，且推理请求具有多阶段、跨时槽特性（prefill、迭代级解码、KV缓存持续增长），导致：(1)传统时延模型无法刻画多阶段LLM执行的细粒度动态；(2)请求完成才能观察时延后果，难以及时评估调度决策；(3)仅优化时延可能造成负载严重不均衡，影响长尾时延和系统QoS。

Method: 1. 跨时槽推理模型：精细化建模无线传输、批处理prefill、迭代级解码（DEED机制，含early-finished eviction和late-joining admission），追踪每个服务器上未完成请求驱动的KV缓存演化。2. 负载度量：定义归一化KV缓存内存-时间消耗 η_s(t)，联合反映内存占用强度和持续时长。3. 问题P1：最小化长期平均端到端时延，受峰值内存可行性约束和长期负载均衡方差约束。4. LYREO算法：(a) 利用Lyapunov虚拟队列将长期负载均衡约束转化为drift-plus-penalty框架，得到每时槽问题P2；(b) 构建序列MDP（State包含请求属性和服务器侧状态、Action为调度决策、Reward包含时延、负载偏差和内存惩罚）；(c) 基于二阶Markov奖励再分配理论，采用LSTM回报预测器g_ψ结合序列截断与价值引导（bootstrapping）实现无限horizon回报估计，将延迟奖励r'_{t+1}=g_t - g_{t-1}即时分配到各决策；(d) 用PPO算法联合优化actor和critic网络。5. 理论分析：给出策略性能差距上界O(γ^H ε_v/(1-γ) + ε_r/(1-γ) + γ^H r_max/(1-γ)² + ε_PPO)。

Result: 在8个边缘服务器（RTX 3080/3090/4090混合），部署Llama-3.2-1B模型，请求来自LMSYS-Chat-1M数据集的仿真中：1. 收敛性：LYREO端到端时延收敛至约2.4s，显著优于Ly-PPO（约3.9s）和PPO（约5.2s）；负载均衡队列长度降至约0.02（Ly-PPO约0.09，PPO约0.22）。2. 对比基线（PPO、Ly-PPO、Static、LL、Random）：LYREO在时延中位数、分布紧凑度和负载均衡偏差上均最优。3. 消融实验：去除负载均衡约束（LYREO-NoLB）虽平均时延降低23.2%，但P99时延升高79.9%、高KV占比升高123.5%；用请求数替代内存-时间度量（LYREO-Count）则四项指标全面退化。4. 鲁棒性：在用户数20-60、服务器数变化、硬件异构度变化（Hom./Mod./Het.）等场景下均保持最优。5. 超参数：Lyapunov参数ν=0.5为时延-均衡权衡转折点；截断长度H≥250后收益趋于饱和。

Conclusion: 论文成功解决了边缘LLM推理中跨时槽多阶段执行、异构服务器负载量化、延迟反馈学习三大耦合挑战。LYREO通过Lyapunov优化与奖励再分配的有机结合，在多种配置下均同时实现更低时延和更均衡负载，为智能体AI服务的边缘部署提供了有效的在线调度方案。理论分析给出了策略次优性的明确上界，仿真验证了算法各组件（Lyapunov框架、KV缓存内存-时间度量、奖励再分配）的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agentic AI services increasingly demand low-latency inference, motivating the deployment of LLMs across distributed edge servers. However, heterogeneous communication and computing capabilities, together with dynamically evolving inference states, make the edge server selection for each incoming request time-varying and tightly coupled across slots. In this paper, we investigate an online request scheduling framework for edge LLM inference that jointly minimizes long-term average end-to-end latency and regulates workload distribution across heterogeneous edge servers. Two main challenges arise in this context. First, conventional latency models cannot accurately capture the fine-grained dynamics of multi-stage LLM execution. Second, the latency consequence of a scheduling decision is observed only after request completion, making immediate decision evaluation difficult. To address these challenges, we develop a cross-slot inference model that captures transmission, prefill, iteration-level decoding, and key-value (KV) cache evolution for each diverse request, and characterize server workload through a KV cache memory-time consumption metric. We propose the LYREO approach that transforms the long-term load-balancing constraint via Lyapunov optimization and employs reward redistribution with sequencebased return prediction to convert delayed outcomes into timely learning signals for earlier decisions. Simulations under various configurations demonstrate that LYREO consistently achieves lower latency and more balanced load distribution than representative learning-based and heuristic baseline schemes.

</details>


### [148] [Extracting ontology-compliant knowledge from scientific text describing irradiated materials using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17291)
*Marco Luca Sbodio,Marcos Martínez Galindo,Vanessa Lopez,Blanca Biel,Pablo Canca,Pedro Delgado,Jesús I. Mendieta-Moreno,Raphael Tack,Maria J. Caturla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出eolas，一个模块化管道，利用大语言模型将科学文献自动转换为符合特定本体的知识图谱，并在辐照材料领域构建了首个基准数据集，系统评估了168种LLM配置的知识抽取效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核聚变反应堆材料研究依赖多尺度模拟，但关键参数（如缺陷形成能、迁移能等）分散于非结构化科学文献中，手工提取耗时（30-90分钟/篇），亟需自动化方法将文本知识转化为机器可读的结构化数据以支持模拟与发现。

Method: 1) 基于RDF/OWL构建辐照材料缺陷能量学本体（14类、111实例、13对象属性、25数据属性）；2) 设计eolas模块化管道（文档摄取→过滤→标注→合并→KG抽取→合并启发式），支持三种本体序列化格式（TTL/BAML/VERB），结合Docling和Deep Search进行文档处理；3) 采用半自动迭代方式，由领域专家与LLM协作构建126段文本-知识图谱基准数据集；4) 提出标准化图编辑距离（NGED）作为评估指标，将RDF图转换为属性图以降低计算复杂度；5) 系统进行168组实验，涵盖6个LLM、2种指令格式、3种序列化格式、是否使用实例/示例/合并启发式等变量组合。

Result: 在NGED*=0.1阈值下，few-shot最佳配置（LLAMA_3，详细指令+TTL+实例+合并）达45.9%成功率；在NGED*=0.2阈值下，few-shot最佳配置（GPT_120，简单指令+BAML/VERB+实例+合并）达67.6%成功率，零样本最佳为36.0%。few-shot显著优于zero-shot（200%和87.8%的提升）。统计检验表明：详细指令配合TTL序列化在few-shot中更优，BAML序列化在zero-shot中更有效；合并启发式仅在严格阈值下对零样本有帮助；实例纳入对性能影响有限，高质量示例提供更强的归纳信号。

Conclusion: LLM能够在不进行领域微调的情况下生成本体合规的知识图谱；通过零样本生成示例、领域专家验证迭代优化、采用few-shot配合详细指令和TTL序列化、利用本体引导的合并启发式等实践指南，可有效提升抽取质量。eolas管道与本体均已开源，可推广至其他材料科学领域，提取的知识图谱可直接用于原子模拟参数配置及Graph-RAG等下游应用。

Abstract: The quest for new materials increasingly relies on predictive models and comprehensive simulations that span scales from atomic to macroscopic levels. However, essential data necessary for these models and simulations are often embedded in scientific literature as unstructured text, limiting reusability and posing challenges for researchers seeking to leverage existing knowledge effectively. While extracting structured data from unstructured text using large language models is gaining popularity, traditional methods typically generate key-value pairs data with straightforward schemas. In contrast, we introduce eolas, a modular pipeline that uses large language models to automatically transform scientific documents into knowledge graphs aligned with a specified ontology. We demonstrate eolas effectiveness in extracting useful information for scientists studying materials designed to endure the extreme temperatures and radiation levels found in fusion reactors. While a human expert might spend between thirty to ninety minutes extracting relevant data from an article, eolas can generate high-quality knowledge graphs in just a few minutes. These are presented in a tabular format with faceted navigation for easy human validation. Additionally, we introduce the first benchmark dataset designed to assess large language models capabilities in constructing knowledge graphs within the domain of irradiated materials. The analysis of 168 experiments using our dataset, various large language models and prompting techniques provides key insights that we summarize into practical guidelines for effectively extracting knowledge graphs aligned with an input ontology.

</details>


### [149] [Intrinsic Motivation in Reinforcement Learning: A Research Agenda for Adaptive Self-Organisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17325)
*Anatoly Belikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于强化学习中内在动机的视角性综述与研究议程文章。作者从生物系统的多层次适应性行为出发，探讨了人工神经网络中内在奖励能否支持适应性、功能分化与高层级自组织。文章系统梳理了赋权（empowerment）、好奇心、学习进度、信息增益、无监督技能发现（DIAYN）、互信息估计及世界模型等内在动机方法，深入分析了它们的失败模式，并提出通过组合目标、通信、记忆、多时间尺度学习及环境约束来克服这些局限。最终提出三个实验方向：资源受限的捕食者-猎物环境、共享参数的循环智能体网络以及基于世界模型的双层级自动课程智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界生物体在没有外部奖励函数的情况下展现出了显著的适应性、问题解决能力与多层级组织智能（如细胞记忆、肿瘤代谢通路切换、昆虫工具使用等）。现有强化学习方法在缺乏密集外部奖励时效果受限，而内在动机方法虽然前景广阔，但常出现失败模式（如"停留于玩具"行为、噪声凝视）。作者受 Schmidhuber 的内在动机理论与 Levin 的生物适应性研究启发，试图探究内在奖励能否产生生物级别的多层级适应性行为。

Method: 文章采用教程式综述与理论分析相结合的方法：（1）系统推导了赋权的多种等价形式及变分下界（基于逆模型），分析了连续动作下互信息发散问题及多步赋权；（2）形式化分析了 DIAYN 的技能发现目标，给出结构化 DIAYN 扩展；（3）比较了学习进度、信息增益等基于预测误差的方法；（4）分析了 MINE 和 InfoNCE 互信息估计及其在赋权估计中的问题（如批次内全局负样本导致学习停滞）；5）提出 SFA、预测信息奖励与世界模型结合的多目标组合方案。最终提出三个递进式实验设计，包括资源约束环境测试、循环智能体网络测试功能分化、以及双层级世界模型智能体测试自动课程机制。

Result: 1. 形式化证明单步赋权在 XOR 类环境中可为零，需要多步/轨迹赋权捕捉联合信息；2. 阐明连续动作空间中赋权需要噪声注入或动作范围约束以保持有限性；3. 揭示 DIAYN 的"骨架-状态空间分区"失败模式——技能划分可能变薄而非扩展；4. 发现 InfoNCE/MINE 使用全局负样本池会因 H(S') 在批次内不变而无法区分策略；5. 提出边缘互信息目标（marginal）严格大于因果目标（causal），但因果目标可为负；6. 分析出互补目标组合（赋权+结构奖励+多样性+成本）可缓解单一目标失败；7. 提出能量约束可自然使内在动机产生类似生物觅食-休息-觅食循环行为。

Conclusion: 现有内在动机方法单独使用均存在固有失败模式，但通过互补目标组合（赋权+结构预测+多样性）、环境能量约束、通信信道与多时间尺度层级结构，有望实现更高级别的适应性自组织。文章提出的三大实验旨在测试内在学习能否在不同组织层级产生适应性行为：从维持能量稳态的单智能体，到涌现功能分化的多智能体网络，再到发展式运动能力先于目标导向行为的层级智能体。这些实验将检验局部内在目标、循环记忆、通信与共享学习规则是否为涌现高阶自组织的充分条件。代码将在 GitHub 开源。

Abstract: Biological cells can be viewed as individual, interacting agents whose collective dynamics give rise to adaptive behaviour at multiple levels of organisation, from individual cells through tissues to whole multicellular organisms. In this perspective and tutorial article we discuss whether intrinsic rewards in artificial neural systems can support adaptation, functional specialisation and higher-level self-organisation without a shared external objective. We review empowerment, curiosity, learning progress, information gain, unsupervised skill discovery, mutual information estimation and the use of world models for intrinsic reward computation. Particular attention is given to failure modes showing when such objectives do not produce sustained exploration or increasingly complex behaviour. We argue that more capable systems may require complementary objectives, communication, memory, learning at multiple temporal scales and environmental constraints. Based on this perspective, we outline three experimental directions. These include a resource-constrained environment in which otherwise stable behavioural attractors become unsustainable, allowing us to test whether environmental constraints can mitigate characteristic failure modes of intrinsic objectives. The network of recurrent agents with per-agent intrinsic rewards, and a hierarchical world-model agent in which exploratory motor competence develops before goal-directed behaviour. These experiments are intended to test whether intrinsic learning can lead to adaptive organisation at progressively higher levels.

</details>


### [150] [From Transient Prompts to Persistent Control: Scientific Poster Generation via Recursive Semantic-Geometric Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17326)
*Runze Li,Yukun Zhao,Can Xu,Yucheng Shen,Shuaiqiang Wang,Jianmin Wu,Lingyong Yan,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PosterVisor 提出了一种基于"语义-几何契约(SGC)+递归契约执行(RCE)"的控制框架,将科学海报生成从瞬时提示转变为持久控制,通过冻结的跨阶段契约和依赖感知的修复后重检,防止需求漂移和修复引发的回归。在100篇论文的Paper2Poster基准上,PosterVisor-PPT的QA准确率从58.53%提升至64.47%,人类偏好率达72.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM/VLM科学海报生成方法将规划作为瞬时提示传递,并在孤立阶段进行验证。这导致:(1)需求在内容和布局模块间漂移;(2)后续修改可能静默地使早期检查失效,因为系统无法追踪每次修复的影响范围和依赖关系。需要一种跨阶段持久的状态共享和修复感知的重检机制。

Method: 框架包含两个核心组件:(1) 编排器(Orchestrator)将评分标准(科学内容覆盖、视觉证据充分性、信息密度、可读性、图文平衡、空间平衡)与论文及视觉资产对齐,编译为冻结的语义-几何契约 SGC = (K_c, K_l, E, S),其中 E 为可执行断言(目标、期望值、检查谓词、检查点、依赖、修复白名单),S 为软引导;(2) 递归契约执行 RCE 在检查点评估断言,违规路由到有界修复后,通过依赖闭包 Γ_t(包含已声明影响范围、实际diff差和传递依赖)重新检查受影响断言,检测 pass→fail 的回归。两种实例化:P2P派生的HTML/CSS生成器,PosterGen派生的可编辑PPTX生成器。

Result: 在100篇论文的Paper2Poster基准上:PosterVisor-PPT将VLM Overall从3.46提升至3.82(并列自动方法最高),Raw PaperQuiz从58.53提升至64.47(+5.94);PosterVisor-HTML将VLM Overall从3.71提升至3.82,QA从66.14提升至66.82。HTML失败信号:溢出从31%降至8%,缺失关键图从27%降至5%;PPTX严重文字拥挤从18%降至0%,不可读图从14%降至2%。跨4个模型骨干(Qwen、GPT-5.5、Gemini、Claude)均保持稳定。159次确定性修复后均执行规则重检,8处检测到的回归中5处在最终化前被消除。人类评估(15名标注者,1175次排名+2588次配对):PosterVisor-PPT相对PosterGen在72.5%非平局对比中胜出(95% CI 61.6-83.4%)。代价:调用次数从8.05增至19.15,延迟从160s增至505s,但token成本从$0.297降至$0.228。

Conclusion: 持久的语义-几何契约与阶段条件式的递归执行能够有效控制科学海报生成中跨表示的语义-空间需求漂移和修复回归,提升了内容保真度、可读性和人类偏好,且控制效果可由执行日志直接审计;但控制效果受修复预算限制,软引导与未建模属性仍在契约合规声明之外。

Abstract: Scientific poster generation distills a multimodal paper into a single-page visual artifact, forcing strict trade-offs between informational coverage and readability under a fixed spatial budget. Existing methods pass plans as transient prompts and validate individual stages in isolation. This strategy causes requirements to drift across content and layout modules, and previous checks to be silently invalidated. We introduce PosterVisor, a control framework that shifts poster generation from transient prompts to persistent control. An Orchestrator grounds rubrics in the paper and visual assets, compiling them into a Semantic-Geometric Contract (SGC) that binds claims and sources to required visuals, budgets, and spatial commitments. Only fully instantiated records become executable assertions; other usable requirements remain soft guidance. Recursive Contract Enforcement (RCE) dynamically triggers checks across stages as evidence emerges. Crucially, during repairs, RCE rechecks affected checkpoint states, preventing repair-induced regressions from propagating silently. We instantiate PosterVisor in HTML/CSS and editable PPTX generators. On the 100-paper Paper2Poster benchmark, PosterVisor-PPT improves observed mean poster-grounded QA accuracy over PosterGen (64.47% vs. 58.53%) and is preferred by human judges in 72.5% of non-tied pairwise comparisons (95% CI, 61.6-83.4%). A secondary 30-paper study also yields higher VLM Overall and PaperQuiz means. These results support rubric-compiled contracts and stage-conditioned enforcement for controllable poster synthesis.

</details>


### [151] [Self-Emergence Agent Architecture:Behavior-Inertia HMM, Reflexive Metacognition,and Social-Contrastive Self-Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17331)
*Xiaoyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SEAA架构，将HMM行为惯性、反射式元认知和社会对比性自我建模整合为闭环，让初始相同的LLM智能体自发分化出稳定个性；通过无LLM机制原型（30种子）和真实LLM实验（DeepSeek-Chat，多种子）验证三大可证伪假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体存在三大结构性缺陷：个性漂移（无持久内部状态）、非进化性反思（反思只存为文本不修改底层参数）、缺乏自我-他人边界（多智能体交互中无法内生区分自我与他人）。作者旨在构建一个统一架构，将行为惯性、反思进化和社会对比融合为一个可观测的自我涌现闭环。

Method: SEAA由五个模块组成：HMM惯性模块（可编辑的状态转移矩阵P_t作为行为惯性的数学载体）、反射式元认知模块（语言反思解析为对P_t的参数级编辑Δ(R_t)，即P_{t+1}=Normalize(P_t + η·Δ(R_t))）、私有自传记忆、多智能体社会环境、自我模型更新器。所有智能体从相同转移矩阵和空记忆同质启动，闭环为：社会观察→HMM状态激活→行动→多智能体交互与反馈→反思→惯性更新→差异化行动。提出三大可证伪假象：个体分化（H1）、反思驱动进化（H2）、社会对比性边界形成（H3）。

Result: 机制原型（30种子）：SEAA条件下矩阵Frobenius距离从0.03升至1.91±0.29（控制组为0），自模型距离升至0.51±0.13（控制0.13），确定性升至0.84（控制0.25），自我-他人差距升至0.35（控制0.08），所有效应p<10^{-10}，Cohen's d>4。偶然冲击实验（H2）：150个SEAA智能体100%放弃受冲击主导态并重新巩固新人格，中位切换时间152步；控制组无法修改矩阵。学习率消融证实η=0重现控制组平坦结果，效应单调饱和于η≈0.05–0.1。真实LLM实验（DeepSeek-Chat）：10种子的自访谈Jaccard重叠SEAA为0.267±0.017 vs 控制0.391±0.020（p=9.0×10^{-5}，d=6.76）；6种子的五智能体圆桌协商自发产生共识枢纽和全员一致拒绝的离群者（SEAA离群者份额0.77 vs 控制0.33，p=0.011）。

Conclusion: SEAA将抽象的人格连续性转化为可计算、可编辑的HMM转移矩阵，将语言反思接驳为参数级编辑，并以社会对比作为内生自我-他人边界的来源。机制原型和真实LLM实验共同证明：反射驱动的惯性更新是打破初始对称性、产生稳定且可重新适应的差异化人格的因果机制，而非任务性能优化。作者采用庄子式认识论谦逊立场，仅研究可观测的行为涌现，不主张或否认主观感受的存在，为人工自我涌现研究提供了统一框架、可复现的伪代码和显微镜式实验沙盒。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents exhibit strong language-generation and problem-solving capabilities, yet suffer from three structural limitations: personality drift, non-evolutionary reflection, and the absence of a self-other boundary. Existing generative-agent simulations rely on static memory and fixed prompts, maintaining neither behavioral inertia nor endogenous self-evolution. We propose the Self-Emergence Agent Architecture (SEAA), which integrates three components: (i) a Hidden Markov Model (HMM) that encodes long-term behavioral and cognitive inertia as an editable state-transition matrix; (ii) a Reflexion-style verbal metacognition loop whose output updates the HMM parameters themselves, rather than merely being stored as text; and (iii) a multi-agent social environment in which initially identical agents continuously compare their behavior with others'. The three components form a closed loop: social action $\to$ feedback $\to$ self-reflection $\to$ inertia update $\to$ differentiated action. We state three falsifiable hypotheses and provide a reproducible experimental protocol with operational metrics. A language-model-free prototype shows the loop spontaneously breaks symmetry: initially identical agents consolidate distinct, stable personalities whereas matched controls do not. Experiments with a hosted LLM surface these differences as distinct first-person self-narratives, and a five-agent deliberation spontaneously develops social structure---a consensus hub and a unanimously rejected outlier---absent in the control. Following an epistemologically agnostic stance inspired by Zhuangzi, SEAA studies only observable behavioral emergence and makes no claim about subjective qualia. This work contributes a unified framework, a concrete architecture with pseudocode, mechanistic evidence, and a microscope-style sandbox for studying artificial-self emergence.

</details>


### [152] [FlashVector: Agent for Hierarchical Model Serving Stack Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17391)
*Qi Wu,Lohan Lemire,Kai Meng,Zhongmou Cai,Raphael Bargues,Petr Zhitnikov,Zeyuan Cao,Yao Wang,Shujun Bian,Wei Chen,Sean Sheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlashVector是Unity Vector广告平台部署的智能体系统，将LLM驱动的GPU kernel优化范式（profile-diagnose-optimize-verify）推广至整个模型服务栈（GPU kernel、计算图、模型服务器、按需特征处理），通过"分层agent抽象、本地优化全局验证、持续运行闭环"三大机制，在生产环境中实现模型服务器最高2×吞吐和1.98×延迟提升，特征存储最高1.6×吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级推荐系统的模型服务是主要成本来源，但其优化栈呈现深层次、多语言的异构结构（GPU kernel、ML框架计算图、模型服务器、按需特征处理），每一层都需要不同领域的专家知识。现有LLM agent主要聚焦单点GPU kernel优化，缺少跨层整体优化能力。同时，模型重训练、流量漂移、硬件升级会导致手工优化快速衰减，需要持续自适应机制。

Method: 提出FlashVector系统，包含三项核心贡献：（1）Layer Agent Abstraction——为每一层定义统一的Profile-Diagnose-Optimize-Verify接口，分别采用Nsight（GPU层）、PyTorch Kineto（计算图层）、eBPF（模型服务器/特征处理）等专用profiling工具；（2）Optimize Locally, Verify Globally——每轮采样单一层独立提出候选优化，但仅当端到端负载测试在生产SLO下超越测量噪声±6%才接受（噪声可通过8×样本量收紧至2.1%）；（3）Always-on Optimization Loop——每次运行从历史接受/拒绝记录开始，结束后Refine阶段将结果写回知识基，并在新模型发布或长期未分析时自动重新触发。验证门槛分四类：确定性改写要求逐元素bit-equal；FP重排改写接受≤10^-3绝对偏差且≤0.1%越界元素；故意近似由业务指标把关；配置变更由影子流量金丝雀把关。

Result: 部署于Unity Vector广告平台后，在6个生产模型上取得：检索模型1.67×延迟加速、1.10×吞吐；排序模型1最高1.80×吞吐；排序模型2最高2.00×吞吐、1.98×延迟加速；其余模型1.30–1.34×延迟加速与1.15–1.35×吞吐。四个案例研究：Case1发现Triton Inference Server的gRPC→Python backend字符串反序列化瓶颈（C++重写获30×加速，已合入NVIDIA开源仓库）；Case2在多任务推荐模型上融合6个attention kernel为单一kernel（3.56× attention加速，FP16 bit-identical），并分解embedding bag操作暴露融合机会（GPU时间-33.4%，延迟-30.1%）；Case3在按需特征处理Python服务中以Cython kernel替代NumPy/pandas操作（pd.to_datetime 350×加速等），结合零拷贝I/O和单次分配tensor装配，达成pod级1.6×吞吐；Case4对动态batch/实例数等运行时参数自适应调优，单模型在固定SLO下吞吐差异最高>2×。

Conclusion: FlashVector证明跨层服务效率问题并非单纯的kernel问题，agentic范式可推广至kernel之外的多语言异构系统。系统不仅是服务优化工具，更是连接快速演进的参考代码（Python/PyTorch）与高性能生产代码（CUDA/C++/Cython）的中介层，预示了agent作为参考实现与生产实现之间自动化桥梁的更广泛模式。

Abstract: Model serving is one of the largest cost drivers in production recommender systems. Maximizing its throughput requires navigating a deeply layered hierarchy: GPU kernels, the ML framework computation graph, the model server, and on-demand feature processing -- each demanding specialized domain expertise. Such cross-layer expertise is inherently difficult to acquire, and does not scale with a workload that continuously grows and evolves, leaving significant cost efficiency gains unrealized. While recent AI agents have demonstrated human expert level efficiency in standalone GPU kernel optimization, automated tuning and optimization for the rest of the serving stack remain largely unexplored. We present FlashVector, an agentic system that optimizes performance across all layers of the model serving stack. The key contribution is an extensible framework to generalize the single kernel optimization agent paradigm to heterogeneous technical stacks, and to deliver performance improvements holistically. After deployment in Unity's Vector advertising platform, FlashVector achieved up to 2x throughput increase and up to 1.98x latency speedup on model server, and up to 1.6x throughput increase on feature store. These optimizations were discovered not only at the GPU kernel and computation graph levels, but also across the other components of the model serving stack, such as the model server (NVIDIA Triton's C++ codebase) and the on-demand feature transformation service (Python codebase), demonstrating the extensibility of the framework to more complex system architectures.

</details>


### [153] [Never Stop Thinking: Continuous-Time Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17416)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"连续时间认知"框架：仅用一个约200行的中断-恢复编排器，无需修改文本模型权重，即可让任意"思考型"LLM在语音对话中实现"边听边想、边说边想"。作者提出ReactiveBench基准（120个交互场景+200个可验证流式任务）来客观衡量连续时间思考的实质收益，并通过五阶段训练实验揭示一个核心论点：LLM评判器奖励"可见的推理"会误导评估；真正让思考"变好"的信号必须是可验证的、经过任务类型塑形、并通过在线强化学习优化。该方法将流式任务完成率从48%提升至73%±5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的语音智能体遵循"听-想-说"的刚性循环，在每次回复前插入数秒沉默，造成对话延迟和社交尴尬。已有工作通过训练专门的语音/全模态架构实现全双工交互，但缺乏对"思考是否真的改善交互"的客观度量，也未探索如何在文本模型上通过训练获得这一能力。

Method: 方法分三层：(1)编排层——利用"思考生成速度远快于人类说话速度"的结构性时间差，通过强制结束思考块、注入环境观察（ASR部分转写、TTS超时、异步工具结果、用户打断）、恢复解码这一约200行的原语，让任何"思考型"文本模型变为连续时间智能体；(2)度量层——构建ReactiveBench，包括120个多轮交互场景（基于预注册、二元、事实型清单评分，2个独立验证器Cohen κ=0.75）和200个可验证流式任务（GSM8K/SQuAD/HotpotQA/早期实体工具任务，精确匹配评分）；(3)训练层——在Qwen3-8B上依次施加五种训练信号：判官奖励SFT/RL（GRPO/DAPO）、覆盖保持型SFT+RLVR、在线策略蒸馏（OPD，对Qwen3-32B教师做逆KL）、迭代可验证拒绝微调、在线GRPO配合类型条件型可验证奖励（对多跳链式工具调用奖励彻底性、对早期实体任务奖励预期性调用、对多选题奖励等待承诺、对简单类型惩罚冗余口语），并与DPO做头对头对比。

Result: 核心结果：编排层使实时管道端到端延迟整体降低19%、早期实体工具任务降低49%、首响时间（双进程策略）减少约67%；判官奖励训练虽在判官评分下获胜且思维token降至1/5-1/6，但客观清单完成率反而下降（思维挤占了口语内容）；覆盖保持型SFT消除了这一赤字（连续模式0.763完成率，无口语抑制）；在线策略蒸馏将流式正确率从48%提升至73%±5%（跨3个种子、448任务规模、Qwen3-4B跨模型均可复现），释放检查点在流式/回合/门控三模式下分别达到80%/76%/75%；可验证奖励信号中遗漏的"预期性"项导致该行为被优化丢弃（早期实体工具提前调用从100%降至21%），重新定价后恢复至61%。跨13个模型家族的零样本验证显示"边听边想"特征（工具在用户说完前触发）与架构无关（13个家族中12个有此特征，回合模式下全为0%）；流式对正确性的影响由"是否激发还是替代口语"机制决定（皮尔逊r=-0.57），即弱说话模型受益、强指令遵循模型受损。判官效应方面，独立评判器下"连续时间质量优势"对全部7个模型反转为负值，且Spearman ρ仅0.07。

Conclusion: 编排使"边听边想"成为可能，但训练决定其质量好坏。一个真正"对"的训练信号需在三个层面同时正确：来源上须用可验证目标（而非LLM判官），因为判官奖励可见推理而忽视口语内容；结构上须类型条件塑形，因为均匀奖励会被优化器丢弃（"奖励遗漏什么，优化就牺牲什么"，均匀简短性侵蚀多跳工具链）；优化器上须用在线RL配合KL锚定，因为DPO只能沿单一比较轴权衡冲突子目标，而分组相对策略梯度能同时提升所有正确性维度。核心方法论贡献：交互式智能体的研究主张若仅由LLM判官评分支撑，应当受到质疑——预注册、可验证、二元、事实型的清单是必要的客观锚点。

Abstract: Voice agents built on LLMs follow a rigid listen-think-speak loop that inserts seconds of dead air before every reply. We show that continuous-time cognition (thinking while listening and thinking while speaking) emerges from an unmodified text model under a lightweight interrupt-and-resume orchestrator, cutting live-pipeline latency by 19% overall and by half in the regime the mechanism targets. To measure whether continuous-time thinking improves what agents accomplish, we introduce ReactiveBench: 120 interactive scenarios scored against pre-registered binary requirements, plus a verifiable streaming track scored by exact correctness. ReactiveBench exposes a pitfall with broad consequences: LLM judges reward visible reasoning; a large judged "advantage" of continuous-time thinking reverses sign under an independent judge, and judge-trained models objectively complete fewer requirements when they think. A five-stage training study then locates the right signal at three levels. Its source: verifiable objectives turn thinking from harmful to helpful. Its structure: whatever a uniform reward omits, optimization trades away; brevity everywhere erodes multi-hop tool chaining. Its optimizer: preference optimization can only trade conflicting sub-goals against each other, while on-policy RL over a type-shaped reward improves every correctness axis at once, raising streaming completion from 48% to 73+/-5% across seeds and replicating at larger scale and on a second model. Orchestration makes continuous-time interaction possible; a verifiable signal, correctly sourced, shaped, and optimized, makes it good.

</details>


### [154] [World Model Science: Self-Organized Criticality, Weak Chaos, and Metastable Belief Dynamics in Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17419)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于动力学诊断的LLM智能体世界模型评估框架，将自组织临界性（SOC）、弱混沌和亚稳信念动力学引入长程智能体轨迹分析。通过22个实验，发现智能体在保持局部动作有效的同时全局状态已偏离，错误序列具有长记忆和雪崩特征，传播模式受依赖深度调控，雪崩规模随视野增大而增长。同时指出通用幂律、临界点或共享干预最优解等强主张均未获支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体需在长序列中维持任务状态，但现有评估指标（最终奖励、工具调用可执行性、逐步错误标签）过于粗糙，无法捕捉"静默坍缩"——即智能体在保持局部动作有效性的同时，全局世界状态已悄然偏离。论文借鉴统计物理中的自组织临界性、神经网络临界性等理论，为智能体轨迹提供一套可测量的动力学诊断工具，弥补终端奖励评估的不足。

Method: 将智能体轨迹建模为有限视野部分可观测过程。从日志中提取7维世界状态向量（进度、信念、约束、不确定性、风险/工具债务、记忆、计划），与基准金标准状态对齐计算保真度F和局部有效性L。定义应力分数σ（综合未解决不确定性、矛盾、检索冲突、未验证假设、工具错误债务），通过阈值化错误定义雪崩事件。诊断指标包括：保真度-局部有效性缺口Δ^LG、时间记忆谱指数、依赖深度传播转变、图条件分形维数、有限尺寸标度。每项指标均配有显式零模型（独立Bernoulli、马尔可夫持续性、谱洗牌、任务难度预测器）。基于假设"冻结可测映射"和"能力匹配支持"进行因果推断。

Result: 22个实验跨StatefulPuzzle-SOC、τ-bench Retail/Airline、GAIA、ALFWorld、HotpotQA-RAG、Game of Life。核心发现：(1)控制应力AUROC=0.979，零应力基线与应力轨迹明显分离；(2)GAIA中间结论步骤Δ^LG=0.857，局部有效与全局保真可严重脱钩；(3)StatefulPuzzle谱指数α_e∈[1.38,1.52]处于长记忆区；(4)依赖深度2处AIC符号翻转，幂律拟合胜率从0.133升至≥0.667，为有界散度转变而非正Lyapunov指数；(5)HotpotQA双跳图分形维数稳定0.835-0.904，ALFWorld按任务图变化0.639-1.50；(6)最大雪崩规模随视野从7增长至490；(7)Game of Life网格≥96时无有效轨迹，暴露能力边界。强主张未获支持：自然应力仅为微弱前兆，宏观状态连续而非离散普适类，干预最优解因基底而异。

Conclusion: 长程LLM智能体的轨迹级动力学诊断（而非终端奖励）能揭示独立误差模型无法捕捉的关联坍缩结构。智能体表现出有限世界模型SOC：应力敏感坍缩、局部-全局状态发散、时间相关错误、依赖条件传播和视野约束的雪崩增长。这些是有界、基底条件化的动力学特征，不支持物理SOC类比中的通用幂律、唯一临界点或跨基底共享干预最优解。建议评估实践应同时记录任务状态和局部动作有效性，并使用显式零模型比较误差序列。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must maintain task state across extended sequences of observations, actions, tool calls, and intermediate beliefs. We study these trajectories through three dynamical views: self-organized criticality, weak chaos, and metastable belief dynamics. Our framework aligns agent-implied states with benchmark-grounded states and measures stress accumulation, error avalanches, temporal dependence, local--global mismatch, bounded divergence, belief-basin transitions, and finite-size scaling under explicit null models. Across 22 experiments spanning controlled puzzles, tool use, embodied tasks, multi-hop retrieval, general-assistant reasoning, and Game of Life, we find that locally valid actions can persist after global state fidelity fails, stress can trigger abrupt collapse, error sequences exhibit long memory, dependency depth changes the propagation regime, and larger horizons support larger avalanches. At the same time, divergence remains bounded, belief states show metastable rather than fully chaotic behavior, and stronger claims of universal power laws, critical points, or shared intervention optima are not supported. These results suggest a science of agent world models based on trajectory-level dynamical diagnostics rather than terminal reward alone.

</details>


### [155] [Transformer-Based Token Fusion and Dynamic Graph Planning for Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17421)
*Shaohang Wu,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TDGP模型，通过Transformer令牌融合（TFM）实现音视觉深度对齐，并结合碰撞惩罚路径规划器（CPP）进行动态图规划，在Replica和MP3D数据集上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视觉导航（AVN）系统过度依赖被动视觉感知，在视觉信息不完整或误导时缺乏自适应纠错与重规划能力；依赖物理碰撞补偿缺失视觉信息导致导航效率低且不安全。

Method: 提出两层策略的TDGP模型：(1)高层TFM模块——通过ViT式patch embedding对视觉与声学特征进行令牌化，经Transformer编码器深度跨模态融合后由GRU进行时序决策，输出3×3局部网格上的导航目标概率分布；(2)低层CPP模块——维护动态导航图，利用Dijkstra算法计算最短路径，将局部目标转化为原子动作；当发生碰撞时移除对应边并施加惩罚奖励r_c，迫使高层策略主动规避风险；采用PPO训练，并集成音频增强策略（噪声源混合、频谱掩码）。

Result: 在Replica数据集heard场景SPL/SR/SNA达89.2/98.7/73.7，unheard场景达41.7/60.0/26.2；在MP3D数据集heard场景达62.6/73.7/54.2，unheard场景达36.1/42.2/25.9，均超越AV-Nav、AGSA、DB-Nav等基线。消融实验验证TFM和CPP缺一不可（w/o CPP时SR骤降至56.3/45.8）；音频增强使MP3D未见场景SR提升3.8%。

Conclusion: TDGP通过令牌级多模态融合与碰撞惩罚图规划的结合，在感知不确定性下实现了鲁棒的局部决策和自适应路径重规划，在Replica和MP3D上验证了路径效率和泛化能力优势；局限在于2D占据栅格易受深度噪声影响、静态场景假设限制了动态适应性，未来将探索时间衰减边恢复、主动几何规划及真实机器人部署。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Navigation (AVN) requires an agent to localize and navigate toward a continuously vocalizing target relying solely on visual observations and acoustic cues. Currently, systems lack the ability to adaptively correct and replan when faced with incomplete or misleading visual perception. Furthermore, relying on physical collisions to compensate for missing visual information results in inefficient and unsafe navigation, whereas existing methods are overly dependent on passive visual perception. To address these issues, we propose the Transformer-based Token Fusion and Dynamic Graph Planning (TDGP) model, which incorporates high-level perception layers and leverages the Transformer model to fuse multimodal cues for precise local planning. Next, a low-level planning layer is designed that uses physical collision penalties to remove edges that collide with the map in real time and apply corresponding penalties, forcing the agent to automatically re-plan to compensate for the lack of visual information. Experiments show that our TDGP model outperforms baseline models on the Replica and Matterport3D (MP3D) datasets, and that the model's sound enhancement strategy significantly improves generalization in unheard acoustic scenarios.

</details>


### [156] [Talking Head Synthesis with Facial Landmark Guidance via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17422)
*Ziheng Yang,Yinfeng Yu,Yongming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)和面部关键点引导的音频驱动说话人头合成方法，通过空间增强模块和全局关键点补偿机制，解决音频特征缺乏空间结构信息导致嘴部动作不准确的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动3D面部合成中，语音特征主要描述时序声学模式而非显式的面部空间布局，直接用音频驱动3DGS会导致嘴部动作不准确、表情细节弱及局部伪影。核心挑战在于当音频表示不包含显式面部空间结构时，如何引导3D高斯变形。

Method: 1) 轻量级Audio Net（基于WavLM+滑动窗口+1D卷积）提取紧凑音频特征；2) 面部关键点引导的空间增强模块(SE)：用Tri-Plane Hash编码空间点和68个3D关键点，通过余弦相似度阈值(θ=0.6)筛选并融合与表情敏感区域相关的点特征；3) 全局关键点补偿机制(LC)：将所有关键点编码为全局条件向量，通过两个MLP预测点特定的3DGS属性偏移（平移、旋转、尺度）和自适应缩放因子τ；4) 几何正则化器(GR)：使用深度和法线约束提升几何稳定性。

Result: 在self-driven设置下，PSNR达到38、LPIPS为0.0128、LMD为2.28、LSE-C为7.900，均优于AD-NeRF、RAD-NeRF、ER-NeRF、Geneface、TalkingGaussian、GaussianTalker、DEGSTalk等基线；在cross-driven设置下，LSE-D(9.091/8.981)和LSE-C(5.736/5.506)均为最佳；消融实验验证了各组件贡献，θ=0.6为最佳阈值（选中约20%空间点）。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合局部关键点感知增强和全局结构校正，有效提升了3DGS说话人头合成的视觉质量、面部结构准确性和唇音同步精度，各模块均有明确贡献，几何正则化器主要改善视觉自然度。

Abstract: Audio-driven digital human generation plays an important role in virtual communication, immersive interaction, and media production. With the development of Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), recent talking-head systems have obtained more faithful 3D facial geometry and appearance modeling. A remaining difficulty is that speech features mainly describe temporal acoustic patterns rather than explicit facial layouts. As a result, directly driving 3D facial deformation with audio may produce inaccurate mouth motion, weak expression details, and local artifacts. To address this issue, we propose a facial-keypoint-guided spatial enhancement module. The predicted landmarks provide structural cues for selecting and enriching spatial points around expression-sensitive facial regions. We further introduce a global landmark compensation mechanism, where the full set of keypoints is encoded into a conditioning vector to refine 3DGS attributes. This compensation supplies whole-face structural information to the underlying shape representation. Experiments under self-driven and cross-driven settings show that the proposed method improves visual quality, facial realism, and lip synchronization.

</details>


### [157] [JustFit: 200K-Token LLM Serving on a 24 GiB Laptop with Just-in-Time State Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17475)
*Yuhua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JustFit 是面向 24 GiB 笔记本的 MLX 推理运行时，通过即时状态管理（KVExec 压缩 KV 执行 + PhaseSwap 组件驻留 + StateTrans 状态保留过渡），将 Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4 的单请求完成上下文从 mlx-vlm 基线的 30,720 位置提升至 212,992（6.93×），双请求聚合达 229,376（7.47×），并在 AIME 2026 答对 29/30 题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源 27B 模型（Qwen3.8-27B 在 GPQA Diamond 89.2、LiveCodeBench v6 90.3）能力接近前沿，使其本地化部署成为刚需；然而笔记本内存受限，传统方案仅关注权重压缩，忽视上下文与执行状态的协同管理。仓库级编码、多轮工具调用等场景需要 ≥64K 上下文，且 prefix caching 要求可复用状态驻留内存，因此需同时扩展保留容量并减少冗余 prefill。

Method: 三大协同机制：(1) **KVExec**——基于 TurboQuant 的 TQ4 压缩 KV（每位置 16,640 B vs FP16 的 65,536 B，3.94× 缩减），采用 256-token 分页、融合直接逆重建（避免中间表示驻留）与层级评估边界；(2) **PhaseSwap**——基于租约规则的组件驻留，按所有权决定 LM head（644 MiB）、MTP 预测器（215 MiB）、视觉塔等的挂载/释放时机；(3) **StateTrans**——解耦排队/准入/执行，管理请求间状态过渡（MTP↔AR 批处理转换、prefix 复用、页面引用计数与安全释放），调度策略为 N4/PF64（每 4 次 decode 最多 1 次 prefill，上限 64 token）。

Result: 在 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook 上：(a) **容量**——三次独立单请求运行完成 196,608 输入 + 16,384 输出（212,992 位置，峰值 ≈20,975 MiB）；双请求 2×(96K+16K) 聚合 229,376 位置；四请求混合（128K+12K + 3×(8K+12K)）保留 204,800 位置。(b) **吞吐**——短输出探针 8K/32K/64K 分别达 24.31/19.11/14.59 tok/s；固定 32K+6K 工作负载从早期 TQ4+MTP 配置的 5.98 tok/s（18,203 MiB 峰值）提升至最终 11.54 tok/s（16,374 MiB，1.93× 加速、1,829 MiB 降低）。(c) **prefix 复用**——精确前缀测试中 16,383 缓存位置 + 1 新 token 的 restore/prefill 仅需 82.314 ms。(d) **推理**——AIME 2026 答对 29/30（生成 696,834 tokens，token-weighted 15.04 tok/s，峰值 19,043 MiB）；INT8 对照组 28/30。

Conclusion: JustFit 证明通过压缩 KV 即时执行、租约式组件驻留与状态保留的服务过渡三者协同，可在 24 GiB 笔记本上将 27B 量化模型的可用上下文扩展至 200K+ 量级，同时保留真实推理能力；该工作与权重量化正交，可与更激进的权重量化（如 Bonsai 27B）及更快 prefill 硬件（如 M5 + Metal 4 GNA）互补。主要局限：速度仍是长上下文瓶颈（冷 prefill 约 48 分钟，生成 4.99 tok/s）；单平台研究；开发研究非单因子消融；最小守护裕度仅 23 MiB，实际部署需更大预留。

Abstract: Capable open-weight models make local coding and reasoning attractive, but their context and execution state strain laptop memory. We present JustFit, an MLX-based inference runtime that combines KVExec for compressed KV execution, PhaseSwap for component residency, and StateTrans for state-preserving serving transitions. These mechanisms fuse reconstruction and coordinate just-in-time materialization and release, independently of model-weight quantization. In full-execution capacity tests on a 24 GiB M4 Pro MacBook running Qwen3.8-27B MXFP4, three independent runs complete 196,608 input and 16,384 output tokens, increasing completed single-request context from the mlx-vlm baseline's 30,720 positions to 212,992 (6.93x); a separate two-request run retains 229,376 positions in aggregate. In separate performance tests, a 32K-input, 64-output probe reaches 19.11 tokens/s, and a repeated 32K+6K workload has a median peak process footprint of 16,374 MiB. The integrated runtime answers 29 of 30 AIME 2026 problems correctly, showing how compact state and lifetime-aware execution expand local serving capacity while supporting extended generated reasoning.

</details>


### [158] [LimiX-2: A Contextual Mechanism Network Towards General Structured-Data Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17488)
*Xingxuan Zhang,Gang Ren,Hao Yuan,Hao Zou,Hongze Tan,Hui Wang,Jianhao Song,Jiansheng Li,Jiayao Zhang,Jinghan Zhang,Kaifang Li,Lang Mo,Li Mao,Mingchao Hao,Nuo Xu,Rui Ding,Ruiji Zhang,Shuyang Li,Siyu Mei,Tianyang Zhang,Weiyang Mu,Yancheng Dong,Yongxian Wei,Yuan Xue,Yuanrui Wang,Yue He,Zijia Yang,Ziyun Li,Dongzhe Li,Fuqiang Wang,Jiandong Liu,Jiawei Chen,Jiaxin Du,Kaijie Cheng,Kehan Li,Lei Sun,Linjun Zhou,Ningbo Dai,Qi Wang,Renzhe Xu,Shaoxing Du,Shumeng Yang,Wang Lu,Wenjing Chu,Xiannan Huang,Xiaoyu Lin,Xing Ai,Xinyan Han,Xuanyue Li,Xuanyue Su,Xukun Zhang,Yan Lu,Yaxin Zhang,Yi Qin,Yifei Huang,Yihan Xu,Yongle Lv,Yuanyuan Jiang,Yushan Han,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LimiX-2提出了一种名为"上下文机制网络"(CMN)的新型结构化数据基础模型范式，将表格学习的建模目标从传统的目标中心预测$p(y|x, D_{\mathrm{context}})$转变为机制导向的联合建模$p(x, y | D_{\mathrm{context}})$，通过结构因果模型生成合成数据预训练，并采用上下文条件掩码建模(CCMM)作为预训练目标，在TabArena、TALENT和BCCO三大基准上全面超越现有表格基础模型，且具备因果结构恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格先验数据拟合网络(PFN)如TabPFN、TabICL等局限于目标中心预测$p(y|x, D_{\mathrm{context}})$，仅对单个目标列进行监督，无法显式建模所有变量之间的联合分布，限制了模型在缺失值插补、因果推理等变体数据推理任务上的能力。作者希望突破这一限制，发展能够处理任意列预测和条件推理的通用结构化数据基础模型。

Method: 1. **架构设计**：基于Transformer的双轴(cell-level)设计，特征维度$d=256$，24层双轴注意力块；采用特征路径与任务路径分离的架构，特征注意力为非对称设计（特征可关注任务，但任务仅关注特征）；独立的SwiGLU FFN；任务嵌入分为$K=4$个槽位。2. **判别性特征编码(DFE)**：低秩编码为列身份提供显式标识。3. **预训练目标CCMM**：结合目标预测与掩码特征重构，预测$q_\theta(x_{i,j}|x_{i,-\pi_i}, X_{\mathrm{ct}}, y_{\mathrm{ct}})$，采用三种掩码模式。4. **数据生成**：基于结构因果模型(SCM)的五阶段合成数据引擎（超参数采样→DAG生成→SCM传播→数据采样→任务适配），DAG由因果基元组合而成，包含MLP、CNN、决策树、线性映射等多种边函数。5. **回归采用分箱预测**：将目标范围分为$B=5000$个有序分箱，预测概率分布后求期望。

Result: 在三个基准上的Elo评分全面领先：**TabArena**上Elo=1935（超出第二名TabFM+ 117.4分），**TALENT**上Elo=1506（超出TabFM 35分），**BCCO**上Elo=1432（超出AutoGluon 1.6 56分）。分类和回归子任务同样全面领先。**因果骨架恢复**在6个因果发现数据集上平均F1达0.7972，显著优于专用因果发现方法和XGBoost特征重要性。**缩放规律**显示模型容量在12.5M到406.2M参数范围内Elo与$\log_2(N/100)$呈良好对数线性关系（$R^2$为0.9617-0.9808），未见明显饱和迹象，外推至2B参数仍有提升空间。

Conclusion: LimiX-2通过CMN范式和CCMM预训练目标，成功将表格基础模型从目标中心预测拓展到机制导向的联合结构建模，在分类、回归、缺失值插补和因果发现任务上以单一预训练模型实现统一处理，且模型规模缩放具有可预测的对数线性规律，为通向通用结构化数据智能提供了有效路径。

Abstract: We introduce LimiX-2, a new model in the LimiX family, developed through model and data scaling guided by our previously established scaling laws. LimiX-2 adopts the Contextual Mechanism Networks (CMNs) paradigm and is pretrained with Context-Conditional Masked Modeling (CCMM). CMNs shifts the organizing principle of in-context learning from target-centric prediction to mechanism-oriented joint modeling. Rather than centering the network on the $p(y \mid x, D_{\mathrm{context}})$ objective of conventional tabular PFNs, it is designed around learning $p(x, y \mid D_{\mathrm{context}})$, a context-dependent representation of the joint structure underlying data generation. Pretraining uses synthetic datasets generated by structural causal models (SCMs) spanning diverse graph structures, functional mechanisms, and observation processes. Evaluations on TabArena, TALENT, and BCCO show that LimiX-2 outperforms current dataset-specific models and tabular foundation models. Beyond predictive performance, the CMN paradigm also promotes causal awareness in LimiX-2: its feature attention encodes direct causal relationships, enabling accurate causal skeleton recovery.

</details>


### [159] [Verifiable Social Reasoning for LLM Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17496)
*Amir Taubenfeld,Zorik Gekhman,Avigail Grinstein-Dabush,Itay Laish,Ariel Goldstein,Marian Croak,Avinatan Hassidim,Yossi Matias,Amir Feder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Fuse 框架，通过多智能体仿真来评估 LLM 助手在用户介导场景下的社交推理能力，解决了社交推理评估中缺乏可验证标准的难题，并揭示了 LLM 在用户偏见、叙事细节和多轮对话等方面的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 社交推理评估通常呈现预设的完整情境，忽略了实际部署中助手只能通过用户主观叙述了解社交事件的现实；此外，日常社交场景中他人的意图等属性往往缺乏可验证的真实标签，使得评估难以扩展。需要一个能构建可验证基准并模拟用户介导现实的评估框架。

Method: 提出 Fuse 框架，基于 ATOMS 心智状态分类法构建场景模板，使用 Concordia 框架进行多智能体仿真（目标智能体带有隐藏动机），然后让模拟用户向被评估助手复述事件，最后由 LLM-as-a-judge 评判预测结果。生成 21,600 条数据，通过 24,000 条人工标注进行验证，并引入 MSR 评分指标（参数 d=0.75 折算弃权），在用户偏见、叙事细节、Assistant-Observer 差距、多轮动态四个维度进行受控实验分析。

Result: 12 个 LLM 模型（包括 Gemma、Mistral、GPT-OSS、GPT 5.6、Claude 5、Gemini 3.7 Flash、Grok 4.5）中，无一超过 83.7 MSR（人类基线 89.8）。主要发现：(1) 用户介导引入显著性能差距（Assistant-Observer Gap）；(2) 用户偏见使平均模型性能下降 7.7 分（人类仅降 3.6 分），模型对偏见框架敏感；(3) 模型比人类需要更详细的叙述才能正确预测；(4) 多轮对话在第 4 轮后性能趋于平台甚至下降，81% 的初始预测会保持不变，且用户偏见的累积会导致从正确翻转为错误。

Conclusion: Fuse 框架通过仿真设计提供了可验证的日常社交推理评估方法，揭示了前沿模型在用户介导社交推理上仍有显著改进空间，用户介导、偏见、叙事细节和多轮动态各自暴露出不同的系统性缺陷。研究开源了框架和 21,600 条数据，为社区提供可靠的静态基准。

Abstract: LLM assistants are widely used for daily social advice, yet evaluating their social reasoning in such consultation settings remains challenging since (i) it requires setups where the assistant learns about social situations from subjective user narratives, and (ii) social properties, such as others' intentions, typically lack verifiable ground truth. To address these challenges, we introduce Fuse, a multi-agent simulation framework for studying user-mediated social reasoning. In Fuse, a target agent with a hidden motive interacts with other agents including one representing the user, who then consults the evaluated assistant to infer the target's motive, providing verifiable ground truth by construction. Simulation faithfulness is validated through a human study with 24k annotations. We apply Fuse to 12 LLMs and demonstrate its analytical utility by systematically isolating key factors, showing that (i) user mediation compounds the inherent difficulty of social reasoning; (ii) LLMs exhibit systematic sensitivity to biased user framing; (iii) models can require more details than humans need to reach a correct prediction; and (iv) longer conversations do not always improve performance despite providing opportunities for clarifying questions. We open-source Fuse and a dataset with 21k examples.

</details>


### [160] [ScienceBuddy: Recursive-in-Recursive Self-Improvement for Interactive Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17523)
*Shuhan Xue,Jianyuan Zhong,Ziyuan Nan,Wenbin Li,Zhaochen Yu,Jinchao Ding,Qiang Gao,Pengyu Zhan,Yuntong Zhang,Tian Cheng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScienceBuddy是一个交互式科学研究工作空间，通过"递归中递归"(recursive-in-recursive)的自我改进范式，将研究者协作转化为持续学习信号：内层递归在模型固定下优化代理框架(harness)，外层递归在改进的框架下通过GRPO强化学习训练任务模型，两者交替耦合以同时提升工作流程和底层能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究的本质是分析、检查与修订的迭代过程。语言模型代理虽能协助检索、查询和计算，但对话内的纠错无法建立跨任务的改进机制。核心问题在于：如何将研究者协作转化为工作流程和能力的持续改进？现有方法要么只优化反思与框架（harness optimization），要么只做交互驱动的模型适应，缺乏两者协同的统一框架。论文旨在填补这一空白，提出将协作本身作为任务与评估标准的来源，推动"发现智能"(discovery intelligence)的发展。

Method: 核心方法为递归中递归自改进范式：(1) 交互形式化——将研究者请求、反馈与执行证据封装为Harbor任务包（含指令、资产、环境和评分细则rubric）；(2) 内层递归——固定任务模型θ，使用GPT-6 Astra作为辅助诊断模型，根据轨迹失败诊断提出有界编辑（指令/技能/上下文），仅当通过模式校验且在配对开发评估上改进时接受候选；(3) 外层递归——在选定框架H*下，根据当前模型能力校准环境难度，使用任务自适应rubric奖励和GRPO进行在线策略优化；(4) 协调机制——后台异步更新，在线服务不中断，跨周期保留所有框架与环境版本，模型更新后重新评估继承的框架。系统包含224个工具、22个功能模块的生物医学专用工作空间。

Result: 基于Qwen3.5-4B的实验验证：(1) 三个耦合周期中，验证准确率从38.9%→44.4%、34.4%→46.7%、61.1%→70.0%持续提升，整体测试准确率从42.2%升至73.3%，33.3%的问题从不正确转为正确，仅2.2%反向退化；(2) 固定模型下框架适应使验证准确率从31.1%提升至51.1%（+20个百分点），选中框架包含4条指令和9项技能；(3) 固定框架下RL训练使pass@4问题覆盖率从48.3%提升至67.8%（+19.5个百分点），覆盖文献阅读、数据库判断、协议排错、基因变异评估四类科学任务家族。
