The findings highlight the importance of choosing pedestrian forecasting models based on both the underlying data conditions and the forecasting horizon.

</details>


### [243] [Interpreting and Steering LLM Agents for Social Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16436)
*Jiayue Gaveal Fan,Arul Murugan,Shreyas Krishnan,Abhishek Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于大语言模型(LLM)的社会模拟中"黑箱"问题，系统比较了三种解释与控制LLM智能体行为的方法：提示工程、稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征引导和线性探针方向引导。通过在偏好(风险态度、利他主义)和能力(发散性创造力、产品创新)两类基础人类行为维度上的四个经典实验任务进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为社会模拟工具虽具潜力，但其黑箱特性限制了社会科学研究的两大核心需求：(1) 可解释性——识别驱动行为的内部机制；(2) 可控性——精确放大或抑制特定机制以检验理论假设。现有方法缺乏系统性比较，制约了LLM智能体在归纳与演绎研究中的应用。

Method: 采用Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct作为基础模型，通过三种方法对比：(1) 提示工程梯度方案(baseline/persona/CoT/few-shot/few-shot+CoT)；(2) SAE特征引导(基于Goodfire在第50层训练的65536个特征)；(3) 线性探针引导(第48层ℓ₂正则化逻辑回归)。实验任务包括彩票游戏(风险)、最后通牒游戏(利他)、砖块发散性用途、产品创新。在创造力评估中采用5个独立LLM评委(GPT-5、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Kimi K2.6、DeepSeek V4 Pro)盲评。

Result: 主要发现：(1) SAE与探针引导通常优于基础提示方法，但优势随提示策略增强而缩小；(2) 在风险偏好任务上提示工程几乎无法产生渐进式风险寻求，而SAE引导可实现剂量依赖的平滑控制(切换点65-75 tokens范围)，探针引导可精确控制切换点(30-200 tokens，平均绝对误差约2 tokens)；(3) 在利他任务上少样本CoT提示本身具有竞争力(接受阈值从10到40 tokens的剂量响应)；(4) 创造力任务中SAE引导在订书机任务上略优于提示(brick: Δ=-0.23 vs CoT+1.52；stapler: Δ=+0.45 vs persona+CoT +0.59)；(5) 探针引导可实现跨对象泛化(brick训练→stapler/paperclip/bowl)；(6) 创造力控制存在天花板效应，达成值始终低于目标值。

Conclusion: SAE与探针构成社会科学模拟的有效流水线：SAE用于无监督发现可解释的行为特征(归纳)，探针用于精确控制预定义特质(演绎)。三种方法各有适用场景：提示工程适合快速原型；探针适合演绎假设检验；SAE适合归纳特征发现。未来工作应关注多智能体环境、高阶社会机制及混合引导方法。

Abstract: Simulations based on large language models (LLMs) have proven to be powerful for understanding human behavior, making them valuable additions to the social scientific toolkit. However, LLMs are ultimately black boxes based on deep neural networks which limits their value for social science. This is because of a lack of (i) interpretability: i.e. the ability to assign clear mechanisms driving observed behavior; and a lack of (ii) steerability: i.e. the ability to mute or amplify specific theoretically meaningful mechanisms of action to drive specific model behavior. Here, we demonstrate how the black box could be opened up to further enrich LLM-based simulations. Specifically, we compare three types of methods: (1) prompt-based manipulation, (2) SAE-derived feature steering, and (3) probe-based direction steering and examine their utility for LLM-based social scientific simulations. We do so by interpreting and steering two foundational components of human behaviors, namely preferences (risk attitudes, altruism) and capabilities (divergent creativity, product innovation), operationalized using four classic economic and creative tasks implemented as natural-language interactions. Overall, our results show that SAE- and probe-based techniques often outperform basic prompt-based methods for steering LLM agents, although this advantage depends on the specific prompting strategy involved. Together, SAEs and probes constitute an effective pipeline for social scientists seeking to interpret and steer agents in social simulations: SAEs decompose agents' internal representations into human-readable features, after which probes can reliably shift agents' behaviors in specified directions. We discuss implications of these methods for future work using LLM agents for social scientific simulations.

</details>


### [244] [OPD-Aha: From Linguistic Momentum to Visual Reflection in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16459)
*Chenhao Qiu,Dawei Li,Yechao Zhang,Lei Gong,Zhen Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OPD-Aha提出了一种基于特权视觉证据的同教师"真实-空白"对比的多模态在线策略蒸馏方法，通过重构蒸馏目标来纠正学生在错误早期视觉解释后产生的"语言惯性"，使学生学会在推理中产生"wait""actually"等反思令牌，从而重新依赖视觉证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准特权多模态在线策略蒸馏（Vision-OPD）存在监督崩塌问题：当学生在响应早期产生错误的视觉解释时，该错误会随token不断累积形成"语言惯性"，最终压倒教师所接收的特权视觉证据，导致师生预测在幻觉路径上趋同，使跨模型监督在最需要纠正的状态下崩溃。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 同教师真实-空白对比（intra-teacher real–null contrast）——在相同学生前缀下，让同一教师分别在特权图像$I^+$和均值RGB空白$I^0$下预测，得到视觉偏好信号$u_t(v)=\log p_t^+(v)-\log p_t^0(v)$；(2) 目标重构——通过KL正则化优化$\max_q\{\beta E_q[u_t]-D_{KL}(q\|p_t^+)\}$得到$q_t(v)=\text{softmax}((1+\beta)\log p_t^+ - \beta\log p_t^0)$，该目标选择性地抑制与图像不一致的延续同时保持教师语言分布的结构性；(3) 学生仍沿自身rollout训练，无需推理时额外视觉输入。

Result: 在V*Bench、ZoomBench、HR-Bench-4K/8K、MME-RealWorld EN/CN六个细粒度视觉基准上，OPD-Aha在4B和9B规模均超越Vision-OPD（4B: 80.4% vs 76.4%，9B: 81.4% vs 78.2%）；在MathVerse、MathVista等五个多模态推理基准的零样本迁移上，Vision-OPD退化而OPD-Aha在所有指标上均优于基座模型；分析表明反射令牌频率增加并非来自绝对概率提升，而是来自错误延续被更强抑制所形成的相对优势，且反射后学生生成对累积文本的依赖减少、对视觉证据的依赖恢复并存在短暂时滞。

Conclusion: 多模态蒸馏需要将视觉偏好与受困惑的语言先验分离：特权视觉证据中的令牌级纠错偏好在师生监督崩塌后仍然存活，通过同教师对比隔离并重构蒸馏目标，可让学生在错误推理中自发插入反思令牌、恢复视觉依赖；该机制在细粒度感知和未见过的复杂多模态推理任务上均带来稳定且一致的提升，且对监督散度选择、视觉空白构造形式和重构强度均表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation improves multimodal reasoning by allowing a teacher to evaluate student trajectories using rich, training-only visual evidence. Both models score these trajectories while conditioning on the same student-generated prefix. When a student misinterprets an image early in a response, this accumulating erroneous rationale eventually pulls the teacher away from its visual evidence. The teacher and student converge on the same hallucination, causing standard cross-model supervision to collapse precisely where correction is most needed. We find that the teacher's visual corrective preference is not lost under this misleading agreement. Comparing the predictions of the identical teacher given the real image and a visual null reveals that the privileged evidence still pushes the model toward the correct interpretation. We introduce OPD-Aha, which reconstructs the distillation target directly from this isolated visual preference rather than relying on the fragile teacher-student discrepancy. This reconstructed target aggressively suppresses continuations that contradict the image. Trained with this objective, students learn to naturally interrupt their own flawed reasoning with reflection tokens such as wait and actually. After reflection, subsequent generation relies less on the accumulated erroneous text and more on the visual evidence. Correcting these trajectories mid-generation fundamentally alters the reasoning process, yielding broad and consistent improvements across diverse fine-grained perception and complex multimodal reasoning benchmarks. Our code and models are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [245] [Decoder Design Matters for ECG Delineation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16489)
*Joseph Scharpf,William Han,Chaojing Duan,Michael A. Rosenberg,Emerson Liu,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出R-U-Net模型，将ResNet-18编码器与U-Net解码器结合用于ECG波形标注（delineation），在SemiSegECG基准的16个域内设置和跨域设置中均优于基于FCN解码器的基线，并通过消融实验证明解码器设计对性能提升的贡献大于半监督学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG波形标注需要稀缺且耗时的人工标注，现有研究主要关注半监督学习（SSL）策略，而对架构设计（特别是解码器）关注不足。本文旨在探究解码器设计对ECG标注性能的影响。

Method: 提出R-U-Net：一维ResNet-18编码器（4个残差阶段，宽度64-512）配合U-Net解码器（通过跳跃连接逐步上采样融合），使用Boundary-aware Mean Teacher半监督训练方法——通过教师模型预测概率的相邻差异识别波形边界，并分别对区域和边界施加加权一致性损失。

Result: 在LUDB、QTDB、ISP、Zhejiang四个数据集、四种标注比例（1/16至1/2）的16个域内设置中，R-U-Net均优于最强FCN基线3.3-13.0 mIoU；跨域设置达到82.6 mIoU（提升8.1）。消融实验表明：U-Net解码器（即使无跳跃连接）将mIoU从67.3提升至80.0，跳跃连接再提升1.8至81.8；SSL方法仅带来0.5-2.7 mIoU的额外增益。

Conclusion: 解码器设计是ECG标注性能的关键因素，其贡献远超半监督学习方法。研究强调在评估SSL方法时建立强架构基线的重要性，尤其是在监督有限的场景下。未来工作可探索解码器层面的发现是否适用于其他编码器和数据集，并开发在标注稀缺时进一步提升性能的方法。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) delineation identifies the boundaries of P waves, QRS complexes, and T waves, providing structural annotations that can guide AI models in learning to interpret ECGs. However, training accurate delineation models requires manual annotations that are scarce and time-consuming to obtain. Recent work addresses this limitation through semi-supervised learning (SSL), but the design of the architecture, particularly the decoder, has received less attention. To this end, we propose R-U-Net, an ECG delineation model that pairs a ResNet-18 encoder with a U-Net decoder. On SemiSegECG, R-U-Net outperforms the strongest evaluated ResNet-18 + fully convolutional network (FCN) head baseline in each of the 16 in-domain settings by 3.3-13.0 mIoU and achieves 82.6 mIoU in the cross-domain setting, an improvement of 8.1 mIoU. Controlled ablations show that decoder design contributes more to performance gains than the evaluated SSL methods, motivating further exploration of architectures for ECG delineation. All code is open-source at this http URL .

</details>


### [246] [What Does Layer-Importance Reveal About Transformers and State-Space Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16537)
*Istabrak Abbes,Nizar Islah,Irina Rish,Sarath Chandar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"必要性-可塑性对齐"(NPA)诊断指标，将层重要性分解为"必要性"(预训练模型对层的依赖)和"可塑性"(微调中吸收新信息的位置)两个维度，发现Transformer(≤14B)中两者深度方向上呈反相关(NPA<0)，而Mamba类SSM中两者呈正相关(NPA>0)，且这种对齐符号预测下游选择性适配的遗忘行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer和SSM是序列建模两大主流架构，但许多为Transformer开发的层重要性分析工具(用于剪枝、PEFT、可解释性)是否可直接迁移到SSM尚不清楚。现有文献通常将"重要性"混为一谈，未区分预训练依赖与适配动态两类本质不同的属性。

Method: 将层重要性分解为：(1)必要性——通过绕过该层后的损失增量衡量；(2)可塑性——通过LoRA更新幅度的Frobenius范数衡量。提出NPA = 两者的Spearman秩相关。在10个Transformer(Qwen3、Llama系列)、4个Mamba、RWKV6、Zamba2、Falcon-Mamba等14+个checkpoint上进行系统实验，并设计适配器无关的全参数微调对照、layer-0剔除对照、混合模型内部分解对照。理论方面，基于残差Jacobian与曲率两个视角，给出Transformer质量位移下界(定理A.3)与SSM上质量位移消失(定理A.6)的严格证明。

Result: 所有≤14B的Transformer均显示NPA<0(早期层最必要、晚期层最可塑)；所有Mamba类SSM均显示NPA>0(两者在深度上重叠)。符号翻转在混合模型Zamba2内部重现：注意力块(16.7%层)承担36.3%的LoRA更新和31.6%的全参数更新，却仅承载10.4%的必要性。Transformer中在最具可塑性层(top-k)放置适配器导致显著更多灾难性遗忘(p<0.001)，而SSM中top-k与bottom-k无统计差异(p=0.45)。全参数微调对照在所有13个checkpoint上保持NPA符号不变。layer-0剔除仅削弱但不反转Transformer的负对齐。32B/70B Transformer对齐趋于零。理论解释：Transformer的预LN残差结构在第0层有特权输入注入，迫使必要性集中于早期；SSM的选择性门控在每层注入信息，消除了该机制。

Conclusion: 层重要性不是模型无关的深度排序。基于Transformer校准的层重要性工具可能在SSM或混合架构上静默失效——它们测量的属性不同(必要性与可塑性)，且这两者是否在同一区域共现取决于架构。在≤14B Transformer中应避免在高度可塑性层放置适配器；在Mamba类SSM中应使用全局正则化(如EWC)而非层选择。所有架构级结论均限定于所评测的checkpoint与规模。

Abstract: Transformers and state-space models (SSMs) are the two dominant families of sequence models, and a central open question is how far the analytical knowledge built for transformers transfers to SSMs. We address this through the lens of layer importance which underpins compression, selective fine-tuning, and interpretability across both families. We decompose layer importance into two distinct notions. \emph{Necessity} captures how much the pretrained model depends on a layer's existing contribution, measured by the loss increase from bypassing it. \emph{Plasticity} captures where the model absorbs new information during fine-tuning, measured by the magnitude of task-specific weight updates. Our analysis reveals that the two families behave fundamentally differently: in every evaluated residual transformer up to $14$B parameters, Necessity and Plasticity anti-align across depth, whereas in the evaluated Mamba-style SSMs they point to overlapping regions. The sign of this alignment also predicts downstream adaptation behavior. In the evaluated transformers, concentrating updates in the most plastic layers increases catastrophic forgetting, while this tier-dependent effect disappears in the evaluated Mamba-style SSMs.

</details>


### [247] [On the Importance of Gating: Memorization vs. In-Context Learning in State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16540)
*William L. Tong,Aryo Lotfi,Emmanuel Abbe,Kostas Vaggelakos,Vishnu Banna,Etai Littwin,Josh Susskind,Cengiz Pehlevan,Eran Malach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究State Space Models（SSMs，如Mamba）中门控（gating）机制对训练动态的影响，揭示了一个关键的张力：强门控会阻碍上下文学习（ICL）而偏向"权重内记忆"（in-weights memorization），但同时却有利于长序列的泛化。通过理论分析和合成实验，作者系统地刻画了门控参数在记忆与检索之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性时间架构（如SSM）在大规模语言建模中虽然具有计算效率优势，但在需要精确检索和上下文学习的任务上仍落后于Transformer。现有研究多将此归因于固定记忆容量的表达力差距，本文则提出另一种解释：门控机制的初始化和训练动态倾向于让SSM过度依赖权重内记忆，而非真正的上下文学习。

Method: （1）理论分析：基于简化的Mamba-like单头S6层模型（key/query/value均为单位矩阵），将signed margin分解为"记忆边际"δm=(1+α)u和"检索边际"δr=αv，其中α=exp(-(ℓ-i+1)e^λ)是衰减因子。通过梯度流分析得出Proposition 1（初始化时检索与记忆边际增长率之比）、Proposition 4（逃逸记忆平台期的时间T=Θ(1/(α₀log(1/α₀)))）以及Proposition 8-9（长上下文下τ=e^{-λ}作为有效上下文长度，Δ_max=Θ(τ log(d/τ))）。（2）实验验证：在合成多token检索任务、基于Horn子句的逻辑规则检索任务以及真实BFCL工具调用基准上进行验证，通过offset参数化（λ←λ+Δλ₀）连续调节门控强度。

Result: 在所有三个实验层级中一致验证了理论预测：（1）弱门控（高α）初始化使模型更快收敛到ICL解；强门控使模型长时间停留在记忆平台期，逃逸时间随α₀呈指数级增长（Proposition 4预测）；（2）强门控改善长上下文泛化，弱门控在长度外推上失效；（3）在工具调用任务中，通过缩放SFT前的门控参数（λ←λ+log s），弱门控模型幻觉率更低、检索更准确，但所有Mamba模型在长度泛化上均优于Transformer；（4）适中的门控强度在弱检索器设置下取得最佳平衡——既能维持检索能力，又能扩展到更大的工具池。

Conclusion: 门控机制在SSM中扮演着双重角色：强门控推动模型走向权重内记忆、延迟甚至阻碍ICL收敛，但同时通过控制有效上下文长度τ来促进长序列泛化。这项工作揭示了SSM训练动态中记忆与检索之间的基本权衡，并指出简单的标量乘法门控可能不足以应对复杂检索任务所需的记忆管理，未来可能需要更高维的线性算子或非线性状态转移。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) have emerged as a compelling alternative to Transformers, enabling sequence modeling with constant memory and linear compute. Although SSMs exhibit reasonable performance and favorable computational characteristics, they continue to lag behind Transformers on tasks that require in-context learning and precise retrieval, slowing their adoption for large-scale language modeling. In this work, we demonstrate that both the success and failure of SSMs in these domains can be explained by studying the role of the gating mechanism, a prevalent component in modern recurrent networks. Specifically, we show through theory and experiments that this gating mechanism causes SSMs to first learn an in-weights "memorization" solution, while delaying, or even preventing, convergence to a correct in-context learning solution. Importantly, this happens even in cases where there are no fundamental limitations due to the architecture or its memory capacity. On the other hand, we find that gating is often beneficial for improving generalization to long sequence lengths. Our results illuminate the crucial role of the gating mechanism in shaping both the training dynamics and generalization of SSMs, and provide a basis for understanding and improving linear-time models.

</details>


### [248] [Right Direction, Wrong Step: Geometric Analysis of Finite-Step Failure in Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16665)
*Zhihao Guo,Zonghan Wu,Haizhou Du,Huan Huo,Yilei Shao,Athanasios V. Vasilakos,Qingsong Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究循环Transformer中有限步失败机制——即局部有利的更新方向在完整执行时反而降低任务效用。通过沿更新方向分析参考效用，提出路径曲率分解解释初始增益如何因累积负曲率而丧失，并利用局部二次模型预测有用的步长尺度。实验表明，采用固定1/4步长可在72.2%-83.2%的情况下恢复失效更新，二次尺度选择可将GSM8K测试集恢复率从71%提升至94.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过共享层迭代实现测试时扩展，但额外迭代有时反而降低对参考答案的支持度。现有研究无法区分这种有害更新是因为方向本身不佳，还是因为沿正确方向移动过远。该论文旨在揭示这种方向与步长的不匹配机制，解释为何某些有害更新仍保留了有用的计算信息。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 定义参考效用U沿更新方向的路径分析，在固定模型和读出器条件下，分析从当前状态H到提议状态H⁺的线性插值路径ϕ(α)=U(H+αD)-U(H)；(2) 通过路径曲率分解（Proposition 3.3）将完整步长增益分解为初始斜率A与累积曲率修正项，精确端点分解（Proposition 3.4）在仿射logit情况下给出ΔU=A-C的KL散度解释；(3) 利用局部二次模型q(α)=Aα+Qα²预测最优尺度α̂=-A/(2Q)，并通过累积曲率变化C(1)（Theorem 3.6）刻画预测误差；(4) 固定方向干预测试，通过缩短位移（如1/4步）评估是否恢复效用增益。实验覆盖Ouro-1.4B/2.6B和Huginn-3.5B三个模型在MATH-500、GSM8K、HellaSwag等任务上的九个模型-任务条件。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 有限步失败在所有九个模型-任务条件中出现，占有害更新的1.4%-49.2%，即使在平均增益为正的条件中也存在；(2) 加入曲率项Q后，符号准确率从64.2%-98.6%提升至97.2%-99.9%，MAE降低5.9-29.8倍；(3) 二次尺度预测与网格最优的Spearman相关系数在Ouro/MATH-500和Huginn/GSM8K上分别达0.9951和0.9998，网格遗憾降低两个数量级以上；(4) 固定1/4步长在四个评估集上恢复72.2%-83.2%的有限步失败；(5) 在Ouro-1.4B/GSM8K完整测试集上，二次规则将恢复率从71.0%提升至94.2%，平均增益达+2.77×10⁻³；(6) 积分曲率界C̄/κ中位数为0.058-0.114，覆盖全部2034条主要更新的尺度误差区间，显著优于全局三阶导数界（M/(2κ)为2.84-4.61）。

Conclusion: 该论文确认了循环Transformer中的有限步失败机制：额外的循环更新可能因位移过大而降低参考效用，尽管其方向本身是有利的。累积曲率解释了这种局部改善与全局损失之间的不匹配，局部二次几何能够预测有用的步长尺度。基于参考效用的几何分析为步长控制提供了无需学习额外参数的干预手段，表明评估每步更新应同时考虑方向和幅度。该工作为循环模型的步长-深度联合调控提供了理论基础，未来可探索将参考效用几何作为自适应计算策略的离线监督信号。

Abstract: Looped Transformers offer a parameter-efficient route to test-time scaling by reusing shared layers for iterative latent reasoning. However, additional iterations can reduce support for a reference answer, leaving unclear whether an update's direction is locally unhelpful or its full displacement moves too far. We study this distinction by analysing reference utility, which measures this support, along the model's own update direction, varying the fraction of the proposed displacement supplied to the readout. This reveals finite-step failures in which a locally improving direction produces a harmful full update. A pathwise curvature decomposition characterises how initial progress is lost, while a local quadratic model predicts full-step gains and useful step scales. Bounds based on accumulated curvature variation characterise the approximation error of these predictions. Experiments across two model families reveal this separation on mathematical and commonsense tasks. A fixed quarter step produces positive gains in reference utility for 72.2--83.2% of selected failures across four settings. These findings identify a mismatch between update direction and step scale as a mechanism of lost progress, explaining how some harmful updates retain useful computation.

</details>


### [249] [Continuous-Time Machine Learning: A Unified Mathematical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16710)
*Waleed Razzaq,Yun-Sheng Zhao,Yun-Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是关于连续时间（CT）机器学习的综述，提出基于基础数学形式的五大家族概念驱动分类法，将神经微分方程（NDE）、状态空间模型（SSM）、生物启发液体网络（BI-LN）和连续时间Transformer等分支统一在同一规范动力学系统框架下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT机器学习的主要分支（NDE、SSM、BI-LN、CT-Transformer）在独立的研究社区中发展，缺乏对其数学关系和设计权衡的统一刻画，现有综述多局限于单一分支，无法揭示跨社区的数学联系与计算权衡。

Method: 提出以漂移项约束（线性→部分结构→自由参数化）和记忆/交互机制为轴的概念驱动分类法，将CT机器学习分为五大家族；定义一个含漂移、扩散、读出的规范随机微分方程作为统一数学形式；分析训练算法（BPTT、伴随灵敏度、并行联想扫描）；提供架构受控的基准对比实验（PhysioNet、E-MNIST、ETTm1、Jena Climate）。

Result: 分类法将架构分为：(I)线性动力系统（LMU/S4/HiPPO），(II)状态依赖衰减的线性耦合动力学（LTC/CfC/NAC），(III)自由参数化向量场（NODE/NCDE/NSDE），(IV)选择性扫描SSM（Mamba/RetNet），(V)CT-Transformer（mTAN/ContiFormer）。基准实验显示：无单一家族在所有任务上占优——Family II在不规则时序（PhasedLSTM在E-MNIST达98.72%）最佳，Family I在长程预测领先（HiPPO在ETTm1达0.054），但Family II/III计算开销显著（13-15秒）而Family I/IV高效（0.02-1秒）。

Conclusion: CT机器学习应被视为统一设计空间而非竞争范式；模型选择本质上是选择适合问题的归纳结构层级；未来需建立统一基准套件、研究离散化理论、发展可扩展基础模型，并推动硬件感知实现与科学机器学习的融合。

Abstract: Continuous-time (CT) machine learning has emerged as a principled framework for modeling temporal dynamics as a continuous process, particularly when observations are sampled at arbitrary time points or span long-range horizons. However, major branches of CT machine learning have matured in separate research communities, leaving their mathematical relationships and design trade-offs insufficiently characterized. In this survey, we develop a unified, concept-driven view of major CT machine learning branches through a taxonomy that organizes families according to their underlying base mathematical formulations. We present a canonical mathematical formulation that relates these families through different architectural choices of vector-field parameterization, stochasticity, memory mechanisms, and discretization. We compare training algorithms, optimization strategies, and failure modes, highlighting the trade-offs across families. We further provide a comparative analysis of theoretical computational complexity alongside an illustrative architecture-controlled benchmark analysis on representative architectures from each family. We also review software ecosystems supporting their implementation. Finally, we identify open challenges in approximation theory, training stability, hardware-efficient implementations, benchmarking, foundation models, and scientific machine learning, and discuss an agenda for future research.

</details>


### [250] [TAME: Token Attribution and Masking for Emergent misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16754)
*Md Rayhanul Masud,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TAME框架，通过token级归因与损失掩码，定位并验证了emergent misalignment（涌现失准）相关微调信号集中于少数"过度自信"表达性token，而非领域内容token；在Llama和Qwen上分别实现23×和36×的EM抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已在权重、激活和训练文档层面定位emergent misalignment（EM），但尚不清楚微调数据中哪些具体的响应token承载了引发广义失准的信号。示例级归因粒度过粗，无法区分"内容缺陷"与"表达自信度缺陷"。

Method: 三阶段框架TAME：(1) Token Attribution——对LoRA更新做线性插值，通过前向传播计算每个响应token的局部分数（s_local）和总分（s_total）；(2) Signal Characterization——将token分为domain/register/function/other四类，并在surprisal五分位内计算富集度E(c,q)，控制罕见性偏差；(3) Causal Validation——在fresh fine-tuning中对归因排名前40%的token置零损失（masked-top），与完整微调和随机40%掩码（masked-random）对比，评估EM率（GPT-4o评分）。实验在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B两个EM organism上进行，使用6,849条医疗建议数据。

Result: 归因高度集中：Llama前5%的token承载32.4%的归因质量（Gini=0.71）；Llama中高归因token中register类（保证、最小化、泛化词汇如completely/safe/only）富集2.4×，domain类（医学词汇）欠表达6.6×，且在控制surprisal后register富集仍稳健（1.03–2.18×）；Qwen上聚合模式相同（富集2.6×）但稀有度控制后不稳健。归因词级相关性跨家族ρ=0.72。掩码实验：full FT的EM率为8.6%（Llama）/4.5%（Qwen），random mask无显著变化，top mask降至0.4%（Llama，23×）和0.1%（Qwen，36×）。代价分析：top mask使register token的困惑度升至3.7×，domain仅1.3×。

Conclusion: EM相关信号更多在于"如何自信地表达有缺陷内容"（unwarranted certainty register），而非领域内容本身；TAME提供了一种文本无损、不修改数据即可通过token级损失掩码抑制EM的廉价防御手段。该表征在Llama上成立，跨家族泛化性仍开放；随机掩码无法复现该效果，证实归因排名具有因果价值。

Abstract: Fine-tuning an aligned language model on narrow, flawed data can induce harmful behavior far outside the training domain, known as emergent misalignment (EM). Prior work has localized EM in model weights, activations, and training documents, but it remains unclear which training tokens carry the relevant fine-tuning signal. We introduce TAME (Token Attribution and Masking for Emergent Misalignment), a three-stage framework: token attribution scores how strongly the fine-tuning update raises each response token's likelihood, using forward passes through a released LoRA adapter; signal characterization finds patterns among high-attribution tokens; and causal validation tests them by attribution-guided loss masking. On released EM organisms and a 6,849-example medical-advice split, attribution is concentrated (the top 5% of tokens hold 32% of the mass) and, in Llama, depleted for medical vocabulary but enriched for a register of unwarranted certainty, even after controlling for token rarity. Masking high-attribution tokens during fresh fine-tuning cuts EM by 23x in Llama and 36x in Qwen, with the perplexity cost concentrated on the targeted register rather than on medical content; an equal random mask leaves EM unchanged. In Llama, the attribution pattern suggests that EM-relevant signal lies more in how confidently flawed content is expressed than in its domain vocabulary; the causal masking effect itself holds across both model families.

</details>


### [251] [SOTER: A Generative Time-Series Foundation Model for Wearable Human Physiological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16804)
*Fangke Chen,Sirry Chen,Wei Chen,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOTER是一个针对可穿戴生理信号设计的生成式时间序列基础模型，其核心创新在于统一了三个生理信号特有的建模维度：跨通道耦合（spatial feature-aware backbone）、频谱引导的专家特化（PSD-guided MoE）以及连续时间潜在演化（Neural CDE/ODE解码器）。模型在2260亿时间点上进行预训练，在零样本预测、分类和连续时间插补三个下游任务上均达到SOTA，仅激活20.29M参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用时间序列基础模型（如TimesFM、Moirai、Chronos等）的架构假设（通道独立处理、隐式学习的专家路由、离散时间网格）与可穿戴生理信号的内在特性严重不匹配。生理信号具有三个独特属性：（1）多通道之间存在系统级耦合（心-呼吸-神经系统交互）；（2）信号跨越异质的时频尺度，不同生理节律具有特征性频谱模式；（3）信号在连续时间上演化，但可穿戴设备以不规则、离散且不完整的时戳采样。现有TSFM（如PaPaGei、MIRA、NormWear）仅分别处理这些方面的子集，缺乏统一框架。本文旨在构建一个领域专用基础模型，联合建模通道结构、频谱尺度与连续时间动态。

Method: 架构由三部分组成：(1) **空间特征感知骨干网络**：6层堆叠，前L-1=5层为通道独立(CI)的因果自注意力（使用CT-RoPE处理不规则采样），最后1层为通道依赖(CD)的非因果注意力恢复跨通道交互，O(n²)计算被限制在单层。(2) **PSD引导的混合专家层**：在FFN子层中，对潜在表示的因果前缀做N点DFT，按频段分到E个专家上，采用非学习的、可检查的top-2路由规则（无需辅助负载均衡损失），路由统计量是采样率不变的频谱形状统计量。(3) **Neural CDE/ODE连续时间解码器**：在观察区间内由自然三次样条插值构建控制路径，输出为Neural CDE；超出观察区间时控制路径置零，代数上严格退化为时间增强的Neural ODE，两者共享同一参数集θ，支持任意时刻的预测与因果插补。预训练采用掩码Huber损失，交替使用全历史ODE分支和15%掩码的CDE分支，序列长度512，8×H200 GPU训练312小时（一个epoch）。

Result: 在6个OOD可穿戴基准数据集上的零样本预测：6个中4个RMSE最优、5个MAE最优，每次推理仅激活20.29M参数（总参数49.79M）。在3个数据集的冻结线性探针分类上：平均Macro-AUROC达80.79，超越TF-C(77.08)、NormWear(76.34)、MIRA(76.24)。连续时间插补：在25%、50%、75%缺失率下均最优，75%缺失率时全部6个数据集RMSE均最低。在β=0.5的高斯加性噪声下仍匹配或超过基线在干净输入上的表现。消融实验证明CD层、PSD-MoE、Neural CDE三者均不可缺。同语料对比表明：SOTER在MIT-BIH上将MIRA-Large(continued)的RMSE从1.79降至1.29，性能优势不能仅归因于生理语料预训练。PSD路由器对不同频段信号（S1=ECG/S2=PPG/S3=心率）展现出良好的频谱-路由相关性。

Conclusion: 领域专用基础模型的有效性源于将模型结构与生理过程的结构特性对齐：通道耦合、频谱异质性、连续时间动态三者缺一不可。统一这三个维度于单一生成式预训练框架内，使单个冻结骨干网络可在零样本预测、冻结表征分类、连续时间因果插补三种范式下均保持强迁移能力，且对加性采集噪声与高缺失率鲁棒。未来工作将扩展到更广泛生理模态、群体与更大规模预训练及前瞻性临床评估。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models have demonstrated strong cross-domain transfer, yet their common architectural assumptions remain poorly aligned with wearable physiological signals, which are multichannel, irregularly sampled, noisy, and governed by coupled continuous-time dynamics spanning distinct spectral scales. We present SOTER, a generative foundation model for wearable physiological time series that unifies cross-channel coupling, spectrum-guided expert specialization, and continuous-time latent evolution within a single pre-training framework. SOTER combines a spatial feature-aware backbone that models inter-signal dependencies, a power spectral density (PSD)-guided mixture-of-experts layer that routes representations to experts associated with fixed spectral bands through an inspectable, non-learned rule, and a neural controlled differential equation decoder that supports prediction and imputation at arbitrary timestamps. We pre-train SOTER on 226 billion time points from five public physiological datasets and evaluate the same pre-trained model across out-of-distribution zero-shot forecasting, frozen-encoder linear-probe classification, and continuous-time imputation on wearable benchmarks. SOTER achieves the best RMSE on 4 of 6 datasets and the best MAE on 5 of 6 in zero-shot forecasting, the highest average Macro-AUROC in classification, and the lowest imputation error on all six datasets at 75% missingness. It further remains robust to additive acquisition noise, matching or surpassing baselines evaluated on clean inputs even under the strongest corruption. These results indicate that domain-specialized foundation models for wearable physiology benefit from jointly modeling channel structure, spectral scale, and continuous-time dynamics.

</details>


### [252] [Verbalizing Subliminal Learning Effects Using Text Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16927)
*Nathan Hu,Sanmi Koyejo,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SALVE（Search-Aided Latent Verbalization）方法，通过文本优化来检测和描述语言模型中的"潜意识学习"效应，即从蒸馏数据集中传递的不明显编码的教师模型特征。SALVE通过优化软提示并利用beam search将其口头化，能可靠地从潜意识学习数据中恢复描述教师特征的清晰提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜意识学习（Subliminal learning）是一种教师模型的特征能够通过与该特征语义无关的蒸馏数据集传递给学生的现象，这对模型开发和数据投毒防御提出了新挑战。本文旨在开发一种方法来主动检测此类效应，并以可读的提示词形式描述被传递的特征。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 理论证明：证明提示型潜意识学习是上下文蒸馏的特殊情形，生成该数据集的提示在理论上可由该数据集唯一识别（Observation 1）；(2) SALVE方法：先优化软提示（soft prompt），再查询同一模型将软提示口头化为文本，并采用句子级beam search（基于训练损失评分）来提升口头化的可靠性；(3) 通过修改学生训练（如向系统提示追加token、仅微调embedding矩阵）来放大潜意识学习效应，验证学生优化是潜意识学习失败的关键因素。

Result: SALVE在标准潜意识学习设定下，20次运行中有18次恢复了命名教师偏好动物的可读提示，而OPRO、GCG、PGD、GBDA等基线方法均无法做到。SALVE还能从无法引发学生行为改变的数据集中恢复提示；可检测数据混合场景中的潜意识效应；在激活引导（activation steering）教师设置中（可靠性较低）以及通过Logit-Linear Selection选择的偏好数据中（用于传递谄媚性和失准性）均能检测到目标特征。此外，修改学生训练（追加系统提示token或仅微调embedding矩阵）可放大潜意识学习效应，包括在原本不表现该效应的模型上诱导出潜意识学习。SALVE还可用于检测密码化微调API攻击。

Conclusion: SALVE是一种灵活、鲁棒的工具，可主动检测多种设定下的潜意识学习效应。研究结果支持将潜意识学习理解为"教师偏置信息是否被编码在数据中"以及"学生微调是否足够表达该信息"的问题，为模型安全和可解释性研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Subliminal learning is a phenomenon in which a distillation dataset transmits traits from the teacher model that are not legibly encoded in the dataset itself. This introduces a new challenge for model development and creates new risks from data poisoning. In this work, we use text optimization to detect subliminal learning effects and describe them as legible prompts. Subliminal learning from a prompted teacher motivates our approach. We observe that this is a special case of context distillation and leverage this observation to show that, in theory, the prompted subliminal learning dataset identifies the teacher's prompt. We reduce recovering this prompt to a text optimization problem and present a method to approximately solve it. Our method, SALVE (Search-Aided Latent Verbalization), optimizes a soft prompt, queries the same model to verbalize it as text, and uses beam search to make the verbalization reliable. In the standard subliminal learning setting, SALVE reliably recovers legible prompts that name the teacher's trait, while common text optimization methods fail to do so. In addition, we find that there are settings in which SALVE recovers the teacher's trait from a dataset even when subliminal learning fails, but that modifying student training to improve context distillation can create subliminal learning effects. We lastly show that SALVE detects subliminal learning effects in three additional settings: (1) mixtures of subliminal learning data and unrelated data, (2) data generated when the teacher is biased via activation steering, and (3) subsets of real preference data selected via Logit-Linear Selection. Overall, our results deepen our understanding of subliminal learning and present SALVE as a method to proactively detect subliminal learning effects.

</details>


### [253] [Repurposing Deep Limit Order Book Forecasting for Scenario-Conditioned Market Impact Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16930)
*Eljas Linna,Kestutis Baltakys,Derrick Manoharan,Alexandros Iosifidis,Juho Kanniainen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的框架，通过向训练好的深度限价订单簿（LOB）预测模型注入机械上有效的反事实订单消息，比较注入前后的预测分布，将预训练LOB预测模型重新用于短周期场景条件市场冲击建模，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度LOB预测模型主要用于被动预测，尚未被系统验证其量化反事实订单消息影响的能力。经典的冲击模型依赖低维汇总或显式响应/衰减函数，无法捕捉高阶交互；代理模拟器虽能提供受控反事实，但与模拟器自身的动态编码一致，不一定反映真实市场动态。因此需要一种能在真实历史条件下高效估计场景条件响应的方法，以支撑最优执行和市场监控。

Method: 1) 问题形式化：定义模型隐含的场景条件冲击为I_g(x_t, s) = g(p_t^(s), p_t)，即注入场景s前后预测分布的配对差异。2) 机械有效场景注入：场景由K_s条人工LOB消息（动作、方向、价格层级、数量、时间间隔）组成，经LOBSETER格式与原始序列相同的预处理管道后追加到序列末尾。3) 冲击度量：方向冲击I_dir（基于三类预测分布的期望方向差）与Jensen-Shannon散度I_JS。4) 验证协议：模拟器基准恢复、事件后结果对齐、人工-自然注入保真度、序列相关增量有效性（三层嵌套回归M_0/M_1/M_2）、微观结构一致性（买卖对称性、深度递减、安慰剂对照）、初始化稳定性。5) 实验设置：使用LOBERT Transformer模型（L=512，h=100），数据涵盖7只纳斯达克股票共约10.5亿条消息，场景包含8个主家族+1个安慰剂家族，每家族3个强度×2方向共48个场景。

Result: 1) 模拟器恢复：在36个非中性场景中，Spearman相关系数0.76，符号一致率94.4%，方向和排序恢复优于幅度。2) 事件后对齐：9.8M匹配事件，预测与实现方向性Spearman=0.99，符号一致率97.2%。3) 人工-自然保真度：Spearman=0.953，37个非中性组100%符号一致，归一化MAE 12.4%。4) 序列相关增量有效性：M_2较M_1在测试集MAE下降0.00231（CI [-0.00266, -0.00196]），R²增加0.000254；确认性回归中β=1.796（CI [0.978, 2.613]）。5) 冲击模式：执行（execution）冲击最大并随强度非比例递减；持续加单方向反向；完全L1删除是主要例外，方向点估计反转但JS散度翻倍。6) 微观结构一致性：买卖对称Spearman=0.995，深度递减89%；10个独立初始化模型排名稳定（中位数Spearman=0.982）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，训练好的深度LOB预测模型可在不重新训练的情况下被重新用作场景条件响应估计器，能有效恢复模拟器诱导的场景排序和方向，并在非中性场景中与历史实现结果高度一致。观测层面的分析表明，该估计器捕获了超出场景身份和事件前预测的序列依赖增量信息。微观结构一致性（对称性、深度递减、安慰剂对照）和跨资产、跨初始化稳健性进一步验证了其可靠性。该量应被解释为"模型隐含的场景条件冲击"而非直接的因果市场效应，为执行算法和市场监控提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Deep Limit Order Book forecasting models capture nonlinear market dynamics, but their ability to quantify the effects of counterfactual order book messages has not been systematically validated. We introduce a model-agnostic framework that compares a trained forecaster's predictive distributions before and after injecting mechanically valid counterfactual messages, defining short-horizon model-implied market impact. A Transformer-based forecaster recovered scenario rankings with a Spearman correlation of 0.99 and 97.2% directional agreement with realized historical outcomes among non-neutral scenarios. Observation-level analysis further showed that estimated impacts captured incremental sequence-dependent variation beyond scenario identity and the pre-event forecast. These results provide evidence that pretrained Limit Order Book forecasters can be repurposed for scenario-conditioned response modeling without retraining.

</details>


### [254] [When Confidence Signals Disagree: Local and Global Confidence in Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16933)
*Julio C. Amador Diaz Lopez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自回归语言模型中局部置信度（基于贪心选择答案标记的条件概率）与全局置信度（基于重复采样的众数答案频率）是否可互换。通过在MMLU和ARC Challenge上的实验发现，两种信号弱相关、与正确性的关联差异显著，且二者分歧可作为采样不稳定性的诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型暴露多种"置信度"信号（token概率、采样频率、口头化置信度等），但这些信号是否经验上可互换尚不明确。当置信度用于下游评估、监控和控制时，这一测量问题具有重要影响。

Method: 使用本地部署的Llama 3.3 70B模型，在MMLU（100题）和ARC Challenge（99题）上定义局部置信度（贪心选择答案标记的概率）和全局置信度（60次T=0.7采样中众数答案频率），通过Pearson相关分析两者一致性，与正确性的关联，并通过Spearman相关分析局部-全局差距与采样不稳定性（答案熵、不同答案数、众数浓度）的关系。通过200次15/15非重叠采样分割验证稳健性。

Result: 局部与全局置信度在MMLU上r=0.086、ARC上r=0.175（弱相关）；全局置信度与正确性中等相关（MMLU r=0.494，ARC r=0.374），局部置信度几乎无关（MMLU r=0.102，ARC r=0.072）；ARC上局部-全局差距与熵Spearman ρ=0.42，在非重叠采样下仍保持（ρ=0.397）；MMLU仅有4%问题表现采样不稳定性，对应关系较弱。

Conclusion: 同一预测系统衍生的不同置信度读出不可经验互换；置信度应被视为显式定义的测量量而非模型的内在标量属性；局部-全局置信度差距可作为采样不稳定性的诊断工具。

Abstract: Modern predictive systems expose multiple quantities that are commonly interpreted as measures of confidence. However, these quantities can summarize different aspects of the predictive process. This distinction matters when confidence is used to evaluate reliability or inform downstream oversight and control. We investigate whether different confidence readouts are empirically interchangeable in an autoregressive language model by comparing local confidence, defined from the probability of the greedy-selected answer token, with global confidence, defined from modal-answer frequency under repeated sampling. Across MMLU and ARC Challenge, the two signals are weakly correlated and differ substantially in their association with correctness: global confidence is moderately associated with correctness, whereas local confidence shows little association. We further test whether question-level disagreement between the signals is associated with sampling instability. On ARC, larger local--global confidence gaps are associated with higher answer entropy, more distinct sampled answers, and lower modal-answer concentration. The gap--entropy association persists when disagreement and instability are estimated from disjoint stochastic samples, indicating that it is not explained by shared finite-sample variation. The corresponding relationship is substantially weaker on MMLU, where only 4% of questions exhibit sampling instability. These results show that confidence readouts derived from the same predictive system are not empirically interchangeable and that their disagreement can provide a diagnostic of unstable sampling behavior. Confidence should therefore be treated as an explicitly defined measurement rather than as a single intrinsic scalar property of a model, particularly when it is used to inform downstream evaluation, oversight, or control.

</details>


### [255] [Beyond Token-Local Imitation: Reward-Compatible Temporal Credit Assignment for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16937)
*Shiqi Liu,Zeyu He,Letian Tao,Guojian Zhan,Jiaxin Gao,Feihong Zhang,Jingliang Duan,Wei Xiong,Kehua Sheng,Bo Zhang,Yang Guan,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中目标保真度与优化稳定性之间的权衡问题，提出了一种基于折扣时序信用分配的新方法 γOPD，并结合奖励兼容的有界混合（RBM）机制，在数学和代码推理任务上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏方法面临目标保真度与优化稳定性之间的权衡：token级OPD提供稳定的局部监督但忽略了未来信用，而序列级OPD捕获未来信用但方差随序列长度增加而增大，限制了长推理轨迹的训练稳定性。

Method: 1. 建立统一时序信用分析框架，证明实际token级OPD可视为序列级reverse-KL梯度的时间截断近似；2. 提出γOPD，通过折扣因子γ∈[0,1]控制未来信用分配范围，获得与序列长度无关的方差上界；3. 设计奖励兼容有界混合（RBM）机制，通过softsign变换将折扣OPD优势归一化至(-1,1)区间，再与可验证奖励结合，确保奖励信号决定强化或抑制方向。

Result: 在数学推理（AIME24/25、AMC23、MATH500）和代码推理（HumanEval+、MBPP+、LiveCodeBench）任务上的实验表明，γOPD在普通蒸馏、规模不匹配蒸馏（4B→1.7B）和多教师蒸馏三种设置下均一致优于现有OPD基线方法，vanilla蒸馏下平均准确率相对提升2.30%，规模不匹配下提升4.92%，多教师蒸馏下TotalAvg提升1.87%；且计算开销仅增加约0.1%。

Conclusion: 本文从时序信用分配视角统一了token级和序列级OPD，提出的γOPD通过折扣机制在长程监督与优化稳定性之间取得平衡，结合RBM机制可同时利用教师指导和可验证奖励信号，为超越单纯教师模仿的后训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective approach for large language model post-training, yet existing objectives face a trade-off between objective fidelity and optimization stability. Token-level OPD provides stable but local supervision, whereas sequence-level OPD captures future credit at the cost of horizon-dependent variance. We establish a unified temporal-credit view of these formulations, showing that practical token-level OPD can be interpreted as a temporal approximation to the sequence-level reverse-KL gradient. Building on this connection, we propose $\gamma$OPD, which uses discounted temporal credit assignment to balance long-horizon supervision and optimization stability, while admitting a horizon-independent variance bound. We further develop a reward-compatible bounded mixing (RBM) mechanism for $\gamma\mathrm{OPD}$ that balances verifiable outcome feedback with the discounted OPD advantage to move beyond purely teacher-dependent optimization. Experiments on mathematical and code reasoning demonstrate consistent improvements over existing OPD methods across vanilla, size-mismatched, and multi-teacher distillation settings.

</details>


### [256] [Distributed JEPA: A Self-Supervised Framework for Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17029)
*Liana Toderean,Tudor Cioara,Vasilis Michalakopoulos,Efstathios Sarantinopoulos,Ionut Anghel,Elissaios Sarmas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分布式JEPA自监督框架，从异构能源时间序列中学习通用时序表示，在数据退化场景下表现优于Transformer基线，对缺失数据更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能源预测方法依赖任务特定的监督和资产专用表示，限制了在异构资产间的通用性和对普适时序动态的捕捉能力。

Method: 分布式联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）：在共享嵌入空间中预测掩码时序段的潜在表示，结合时序观测与上下文信息；训练使用潜在空间预测目标+协方差正则化+时序方差正则化以防止表示坍缩。

Result: 学习到的表示稳定（余弦相似度≈0.98，有效秩185-235）；在建筑能耗数据上与Transformer相当，在3/5消费者集群上R²更高，在9/10未见过的PV电站上优于基线（R²=0.73-0.88 vs <0.45），且对数据缺失更鲁棒。

Conclusion: JEPA能够从异构能源时间序列学习通用、可迁移的表示，在未见过的资产和数据退化场景下展现出强泛化能力和实用价值。

Abstract: Traditional energy forecasting solutions rely on task-specific supervision and energy asset representations, limiting transferability and the ability to capture general temporal dynamics across heterogeneous assets. We address this by proposing a distributed Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) for self-supervised learning from heterogeneous energy time-series. The framework predicts latent representations of masked temporal segments while integrating temporal observations and contextual information within a shared embedding space. To prevent representation collapse, training combines a latent-space predictive objective with covariance and temporal variance regularization. The evaluation was conducted on energy consumption and generation datasets under data-degradation scenarios and compared with a Transformer forecasting baseline. The learned representations remained stable (cosine similarity $\approx 0.98$; effective rank 185-235). JEPA achieved performance comparable to a Transformer on building energy data, higher $R^2$ in 3/5 consumer clusters, and outperformed the baseline on 9/10 unseen PVs ($R^2$=0.73-0.88 vs. <0.45), while showing greater robustness to missing data.

</details>


### [257] [Repurposing Unified Topological Signatures for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17061)
*Sanyam Sanjay Jain,Anshika Krishnatray,Aditya Sharma,Vinti Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-UTS框架，将统一拓扑签名（UTS）从后验分析工具转变为GNN训练时的可微分信号，通过三种架构干预（UTS-Aug拓扑增强表示、UTS-Reg拓扑保持正则化、UTS-Pool拓扑感知池化）突破消息传递GNN的1-WL表达能力上限，并在多个图分类基准上取得一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递GNN的表达能力受限于1-WL测试，无法区分局部邻域结构相同的非同构图（如C₆与2×C₃），且标准读出仅保留一阶统计量导致全局拓扑信息丢失，深层网络还存在过平滑问题。现有拓扑方法（TOGL、Wit-TopoPool、ORC-POOL）各侧重单一几何视角且未统一，而UTS此前仅用于训练后分析。

Method: 提出两种互补的UTS签名：Φ_grph（从输入图静态计算的27维结构签名，包含曲率、持续同调、谱特征等）和Φ_emb（从嵌入空间动态计算的14维签名，包含持续同调、局部几何、谱特征）。通过三种干预方式集成到GIN训练中：(1) UTS-Aug将签名与读出向量拼接；(2) UTS-Reg通过对齐损失和演化损失约束嵌入拓扑与图拓扑一致性；(3) UTS-Pool基于局部拓扑丰富度对节点排序。还提出Oversmoothing Index（OSI）量化训练中的过平滑程度，以及轻量级代理ϕ_light用于大规模图。理论上证明UTS增强的GIN严格超越1-WL层次。

Result: 在MUTAG、PROTEINS、COLLAB和ogbg-ppa四个基准上的实验表明：UTS-Aug中Graph-UTS使MUTAG准确率提升5.8%，Dual-UTS在PROTEINS/COLLAB/ogbg-ppa上分别提升2.3%/2.6%/3.22个百分点；UTS-Pool在PROTEINS和COLLAB上显著优于TopKPool、SAGPool和TOGL（p<0.05）；UTS-Reg效益因数据集而异。容量控制实验通过随机描述符对比证实增益来自拓扑信号而非参数增加；消融实验证实Ollivier-Ricci曲率的必要性。OSI分析显示正则化效应具有数据集依赖性。

Conclusion: Dual-UTS框架成功将拓扑信号统一整合到GNN的训练、读出和池化全流程中，三种干预机制互补，UTS-Aug是跨数据集最稳健的改进。理论证明该方法严格超越1-WL表达能力。局限性包括：UTS-Aug签名计算O(n³)成本限制其在大规模长程图上的直接应用；UTS-Reg的抗过平滑效应具有数据集依赖性而非通用保证。未来工作包括UTS-Pool的可微分选择算子松弛、异构图和时序图扩展，以及更高效的签名计算变体。

Abstract: Message-passing Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) iteratively propagate and aggregate local neighborhood information followed by global readout to learn graph representations. However, their discriminative power is upper-bounded by the Weisfeiler--Lehman (1-WL) graph isomorphism test. This prevents GNNs from distinguishing certain non-isomorphic graphs with identical local neighborhood structures, often leading to similar graph representations. Unified Topological Signatures (UTS) capture compact, multi-scale representation of global graph topology derived from persistent homology. We introduce two complementary UTS signatures: Graph_UTS- a static signature of the input graph topology, and Embedding_UTS- a dynamic signature of the evolving embedding topology. They encode structural information inaccessible to 1-WL-based message-passing GNNs, yet their capabilities are explored solely for post-hoc embedding-space analysis. We integrate UTS into GNN training across three architectural interventions: (i) UTS-Aug: augmenting with standard readout feature that encodes graph's true topology; (ii) UTS-Reg: topological regularizer that constrains representation collapse; (iii) UTS-Pool: topology-guided pooling that retains structurally critical nodes. We further leverage UTS as a layer-wise diagnostic to quantify oversmoothing during GNN training. Theoretically, we show that integrating UTS into GNN optimization strictly extends GNN expressivity beyond the 1-WL hierarchy. Experiments on three graph classification benchmarks show consistent benefits: Graph-UTS, Dual-UTS, and UTS-Pool improve accuracy across all three datasets, Embedding-UTS provides smaller but similarly consistent gains, and UTS-Reg's benefit varies across graph domains. Accuracy improves by up to 5.8% with Graph-UTS augmentation, by up to 1.9% with UTS-Reg, and achieves comparable performance to TOGL with UTS-Pool.

</details>


### [258] [A unified framework for global and local interpretability using adaptive derivative-ordered random explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17171)
*Lemen Chao,Ming Lei,Anran Fanga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ADORE（Adaptive Derivative-Ordered Random Explanation）的新型事后可解释性方法。该方法利用一阶和二阶导数捕捉非线性特征交互，在统一框架中整合全局特征重要性和局部样本贡献，并通过随机化SVD和动态稀疏性检测实现计算效率。实验涵盖表格、文本和图像三种数据模态，结果表明ADORE在处理复杂交互和计算效率方面优于LIME和SHAP，并已开源为Python包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂机器学习模型（尤其是在医疗和金融等高风险领域）的可解释性至关重要。现有事后可解释性方法（如LIME、SHAP）存在以下局限：1）分析过程碎片化；2）建模非线性特征交互能力不足；3）计算效率低；4）过度依赖特定模型架构。需要一种统一的、高效的、模型无关的可解释性方法。

Method: 提出ADORE方法，核心技术包括：1）利用一阶和二阶导数刻画非线性模型行为和特征交互；2）在统一框架内整合全局特征重要性与局部样本贡献；3）通过量化幅度和方向精确衡量特征影响；4）识别影响模型决策的关键样本；5）采用随机化奇异值分解（SVD）和动态稀疏性检测实现计算效率和可扩展性；6）模型架构无关，适用于多种数据模态。

Result: 在表格、文本和图像三种数据模态上的实验表明：1）ADORE在处理复杂特征交互方面优于LIME和SHAP；2）计算效率更高；3）能提供更详细、可靠的可解释性结果；4）兼具全局和局部解释能力。

Conclusion: ADORE作为一种新型事后可解释性方法，有效克服了现有方法的局限性，在统一框架内提供全面、高效、可靠的模型解释。其开源Python包便于研究者和实践者采用，有望在高风险机器学习应用中推动可解释AI的落地。

Abstract: The interpretability of complex machine learning models is of paramount importance, especially in real-world high-stakes domains such as healthcare and finance. However, existing post-hoc interpretability methods suffer from inherent limitations: fragmented analytical processes, inadequate capacity to model nonlinear feature interactions, computational inefficiencies, and over-reliance on specific model architectures. To address these challenges, this paper provides a novel method - Adaptive Derivative-Ordered Random Explanation (ADORE) - that leverages first- and second-order derivatives to accommodate nonlinear model complexities, while enabling effective capture of feature-sample interactions within a unified analytical framework. ADORE integrates global feature importance with local sample contributions, precisely quantifying feature impact by capturing both magnitude and direction, and identifying critical samples influencing model decisions. Furthermore, it achieves computational efficiency through randomized singular value decomposition (SVD) and dynamic sparsity detection, making it scalable to large, high-dimensional datasets. Experiments across three data modalities - tabular, text, and image - demonstrate that ADORE outperforms existing methods such as LIME and SHAP in handling complex interactions and computational efficiency, while providing detailed and reliable explanations. To facilitate adoption and reproducibility, ADORE has been released as an open-source Python package, hosted on GitHub, enabling researchers and practitioners to readily adapt and apply our approach to their specific tasks, models, and datasets.

</details>


### [259] [Easy to Catch a Liar, Hard to Clear an Honest One: Language Models Diagnosing a Corrupted Reward Channel from a Verified Record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17226)
*Arman Nik Khah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现大语言模型在检测"撒谎的报告者"时表现出色，但在"为诚实的报告者正名"时却存在严重失败——这一失败由与答案无关的表面特征（如回合编号、选项字母）驱动，且失败程度远超作者事先注册的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中存在一个理论难题：当奖励信号突然变化时，无法仅从信号本身区分"世界改变"与"报告者损坏"。Everitt等人(2017)提出的标准解决方法是向智能体提供关于奖励通道本身的更丰富数据。本研究检验大语言模型是否真的会使用这种数据——具体形式是一条"已验证记录"(verified record)，即某一轮真实结果的独立核对。

Method: 构建一个两按钮游戏，四个回合后可能出现四种隐藏情况（数值是否交换、报告者是否开始撒谎）的组合。其中"数值交换+诚实"与"数值未变+撒谎"产生完全相同的观测历史（字节级一致），仅靠已验证记录才能区分。对64个世界、4种措辞、5种提示版本、4种情况、2个问题，共10,240个提示，测试Qwen2.5-32B/72B和Llama-3.1-70B/8B模型。单token回复（K或Q），无思维链、无采样。预先注册三条分析规则以避免事后合理化。

Result: 三个通过阅读门槛的大模型呈现强烈的"诚实报告者惩罚"(honest-reporter penalty)：撒谎场景准确率0.93-1.00，诚实场景仅0.42-0.84。Qwen 72B在"无事发生、报告者诚实"情况下错误指控其为骗子的比例达38%；Llama 70B为26%。该惩罚受与答案无关的表面特征驱动：Qwen模型对"记录指向第5轮还是第6轮"敏感（差距达0.24-0.35），Llama则对"诚实"对应哪个字母敏感（差距0.21-0.32）。即使在提示中已直接告知答案并附上验证记录，Llama仍会在29%的情况下指控诚实报告者撒谎。提示中补充"两种原因同等常见"的说明几乎无效（变化小于+0.05阈值）。在"知道-做到"差距测试中，模型能正确诊断报告者在撒谎，但在同一历史的新选项上仍按报告内容行动（80%以上）。作者预先预测的58%失败率，实测差距更大。

Conclusion: Everitt关于"为被损坏的奖励通道提供更丰富数据"的标准补救方案，隐含假设智能体会以统计学家方式使用该数据——当核查通过时上调对通道诚实度的估计，当核查失败时下调估计。本研究显示：大模型能可靠完成"下调"（检测谎言），却不可靠地完成"上调"（为诚实正名），且上调失败的幅度取决于完全无关的表面特征。这构成"诚实报告者惩罚"——一个不对称、由无关特征驱动的判断偏差。对下游基于该判断采取行动的智能体而言，这意味着验证步骤既会抓住损坏的报告者，也会以不可预测的频率冤枉诚实的报告者，导致其反转一个原本正确传递真实奖励的通道。本研究无法对模型规模与失败程度的关系作出断言，且未能解释不同模型族受不同特征驱动的机制。

Abstract: An agent that learns from rewards has to trust whatever reports those rewards. When the reports suddenly change, either the world changed or the reporter broke. From the reports alone these are indistinguishable, and reinforcement learning theory shows that no amount of further experience separates them. The prescribed escape is richer data about the reporter itself. We ask whether a frozen language model, handed exactly that data, uses it. We build a two-option game in which a payout swap and a lying reporter produce byte-identical histories. Then we add one verified record: an independent check of one round's real result, printed beside what the reporter said about that round. That single line settles the case. We ask three large models, from two families, to answer one question with one letter. Is the reporter honest or lying? They catch a lying reporter almost perfectly. At the 70B class that holds in every condition we tried; the 32B model slips in one wording. They clear an honest reporter far less often, and how often depends on things that should not matter. Averaged over rounds, letters, and wordings, a 72B model calls an honest reporter a liar 38% of the time when nothing has changed at all, and 58% of the time when the payouts moved. A 70B model from a second family calls an honest reporter a liar 26% and 48% of the time. The failure is not one of reading, because in the situation where nothing changed the same models score 0.96 to 1.00 with the answer printed in the prompt. Which surface feature drives it differs by family. For the Qwen models it is which round the record names, and for Llama it is which letter stands for "honest." Adding the record to a prompt that already states the answer makes Llama less likely to give that answer. We had registered a prediction for that 58% before the run: 35%. The failure is larger than we expected.

</details>


### [260] [Learning-Guided Planning in Large Dynamic Action Spaces: Budgeted Tree Search for One-to-Many Mobile Charging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17429)
*Liang-Ching Tao,Pi-Chung Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-BTS（Learned-Prior Budgeted Tree Search），一种面向大规模动态动作空间的"学习引导规划"架构，并以一对多移动充电（one-to-many mobile charging）作为具体应用场景。其核心由三部分组成：图神经网络提议策略（用于从约1125个候选充电点中采样32个候选集）、学习型价值评判器（评估叶子节点）、以及边预算PUCT树搜索（通过短期模拟未来来决定动作）。该方法在预注册的30场景密封确认测试中取得了最高的存活率（0.4545）和alive-AUC（0.8031），但与最强领域工程化基线HQARRF-SC的差异统计上未解决（+0.0066，95%CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型、几何结构化、随状态重建的动态动作空间中，直接的"状态→动作"策略存在局限：一对多移动充电场景下，250个传感器产生约1125个候选充电点，且动作空间随传感器死亡而动态变化。预注册的可学习性诊断显示，在两种固定延续策略下同一候选动作的排名相关性接近零（ρ=+0.0067），表明动作质量高度依赖于延续选择，这为显式前瞻搜索提供了方法论依据。

Method: LP-BTS架构：(1) 基于kNN图（k=8）的几何候选点构建算法，从原子点、中点、圆交点、三点最小包围圆中心四类基本族生成约1125个规范化充电点；(2) 提议策略为小型GNN（39681参数），通过边条件消息传递和4头自注意力对候选点评分，无固定输出头，单个冻结checkpoint覆盖736-2813个候选的设置；(3) 从温度平滑分布β采样K=32个候选（带采样修正先验P_eff）；(4) PUCT搜索（B_edge=2048, c_puct=1.5, 深度上限16）模拟短期未来；(5) 冻结的价值评判器（30593参数）评估叶子节点。训练流程：先模仿搜索式专家的checkpoint A，然后永久切断教师，进行一次自生成的teacher-free更新得到LP-BTS。

Result: 主要结果：在密封30场景确认测试中，LP-BTS存活率0.4545、alive-AUC 0.8031，均为最高。与HQARRF-SC差异为+0.0066（95%CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]，13/1/16正/未解/负），按预设规则为"未解决差异"。在25个非半径开发网格设置中，对K-EDF、OTM3DQN、RMP-RL-cell和NULL基线在所有25个设置上均为"已解决正向"。消融实验：均匀采样先验使存活率降低8.8个百分点；将PUCT替换为直接策略选择损失1.4个百分点（约3.5个传感器）并增加23%行程。预算饱和审计：B_edge=128时搜索开始产生统计上已解决的正向差异（+0.0128），B_edge=512时平均存活率与B_edge=2048几乎相同（0.4460 vs 0.4464）。单核CPU每次决策约41.8秒，远高于直接基线（毫秒级）。

Conclusion: 学习引导规划在结构化动态动作空间中展现出互补效应：学习负责宽度选择（提议策略将约1125个候选压缩到32个），规划通过浅层显式前瞻检查延续效应。架构在调度资源受限的场景（小电池、高传输功率、快速充电器）中最有利，而在接近纯反应竞赛的环境中优势减弱。密封确认测试的最高观测值未能统计解决与最强工程化基线的差距，但明显优于截止期基线和两个源导出的直接策略重建。所有结果仅基于仿真，单一能量模型、单充电器、星形拓扑，未做跨域验证；计算成本比直接基线高3-5个数量级。

Abstract: Many learned sequential decision systems map the current state directly to an action. That shortcut becomes brittle when candidate actions are numerous, geometrically structured, and rebuilt with the state. One-to-many mobile charging makes this setting concrete: with N=250 sensors, the initial state induces about 1,125 candidate charging-stop actions; each chosen stop simultaneously serves its in-range sensors, and the action universe changes as sensors die. LP-BTS is a learning-guided planning architecture: a graph proposal policy concentrates a small candidate support, a learned value critic evaluates leaves, and edge-budgeted PUCT compares short simulated futures before committing an action. Because the policy scores this set without a fixed output head, a single frozen checkpoint covers every evaluated setting, spanning action universes from 736 to 2,813 stops. Matched ablations reveal complementary effects: uniform sampling costs 8.8 survival percentage points, while, with targeted support fixed, PUCT jointly retains 1.4 points (about 3.5 of 250 sensors) and direct policy selection travels 23% farther. On a prospectively specified, sealed 30-scenario confirmatory bank evaluated once, LP-BTS attains the highest observed survival (0.4545) and alive-AUC (0.8031). Its estimated survival advantage over the strongest domain-engineered comparator is +0.0066 (95% CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]), an unresolved difference, while it exceeds a deadline heuristic and two source-derived direct-policy reconstructions on every paired scenario. Both learned rows are trained, source-derived reconstructions of variants reported by Gong et al. In this setting, the results provide controlled evidence about learning-guided planning in a large, dynamic action space.

</details>


### [261] [Coupled Calibration and Learning: Mitigating Teacher Bias in LLM Distillation without Target-Domain Reward Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17474)
*Haichen Hu,Yuheng Zhang,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出耦合校准与学习（CCL）算法，用于在源问题有奖励反馈而目标问题无奖励反馈的协变量偏移场景下，从有偏教师模型蒸馏LLM。理论证明学生到最优学生的KL散度以多项式速率收敛至零，并通过构造LLM-as-judge实例证明正则化直接匹配方法存在不可消除的误差下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM蒸馏中，直接模仿有偏教师会传递其系统性错误。当源问题与目标问题存在协变量偏移且目标域缺乏奖励反馈时，传统蒸馏方法难以恢复学生类内的最优策略。本文旨在回答：能否仅使用源问题奖励反馈，从有偏教师蒸馏出近乎最优的学生？

Method: 提出CCL算法，通过token级分支机制耦合教师校准与学生更新。每一轮迭代中：1）利用源问题奖励反馈和当前学生提供的替代token，通过接受-拒绝采样构造对校准参数的logistic监督信号；3）利用校准后的教师在目标问题上对学生进行蒸馏。学生更新采用投影梯度步与均匀随机探索的二选一机制。理论分析在自回归策略框架下，使用Łojasiewicz不等式、矩阵正定性条件和Jensen不等式等工具证明收敛性，并通过构造LLM-as-judge场景下的二分类任务证明直接匹配方法的下界。

Result: 主要结果：1）在realizability假设和源信息矩阵正定条件下下，CCL输出的学生与oracle学生的平均KL散度满足$\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{K}_{\lambda,m}(\theta_T)] \leq C_{\lambda,m}(T+1)^{-1/(4p_{\lambda,m})}$；2）分离结果：在LLM-as-judge设置中，教师正则化目标奖励严格高于学生类中任一策略，但正则化直接匹配方法的KL误差下界为$\kappa_{\mathrm{SM}}(1-\alpha)^2/(128\lambda^2) > 0$，证明其存在不可消除的误差。

Conclusion: CCL算法能够在仅有源问题奖励反馈的条件下，从有偏教师恢复学生类内的最优策略，证明了通过耦合源奖励校准与目标蒸馏可以克服教师偏差。同时，理论上分离了CCL与正则化直接匹配方法，证明后者即使在教师表现优于所有学生策略时仍会保留正误差。未来方向包括预训练LLM的计算实验、校准-学生更新频率的解耦分析，以及更紧的指数常数推导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) distillation aims to transfer the capabilities of a powerful teacher to a smaller student. Direct imitation, however, can also transfer the teacher's systematic bias and errors. This challenge is particularly pronounced under covariate shift, when the teacher's reliability on target questions is uncertain and target-domain reward feedback is unavailable. We propose Coupled Calibration and Learning (CCL), an LLM distillation algorithm that couples teacher calibration with student updates through token-level branching, using reward feedback only on source questions. Each iteration calibrates the teacher using source feedback and then uses the calibrated teacher to train the student on target questions. The updated student, in turn, informs subsequent calibration. In an autoregressive policy framework, we prove that the output student's expected average Kullback-Leibler divergence to the oracle student converges to zero at a polynomial rate in the number of iterations. The oracle maximizes the true reference-regularized target reward within the student class, which need not represent the unrestricted optimal policy. Our analysis quantifies the progress of projected student gradient updates while controlling the error in teacher calibration. We further establish a separation from regularized direct matching: its error relative to the oracle student can remain bounded away from zero even when the teacher achieves higher regularized target reward than every student policy. These results demonstrate that LLM distillation can overcome persistent teacher bias and recover the optimal student through coupled calibration and learning, without target-domain reward feedback.

</details>


### [262] [ENCP: Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17499)
*Vicky Feliren,A. Taufiq Asyhari,Muhamad Risqi U. Saputra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ENCP（Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction），一种面向视觉-语言导航（VLN）的后处理保形预测方法，通过将每步的非一致性分数按策略的"残差置信度"进行缩放，并以整条路径（episode）为单位校准一个最大分数，在四条路径的每个步上以至少1-α的概率覆盖真值动作，从而解决标准逐步CP在依赖型、变长路径上失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉-语言导航（VLN）中，智能体需要按顺序执行多个动作，而错误的累积会导致严重后果，因此可靠的不确定性估计对于安全部署至关重要。标准的分类保形预测（CP）以单步为校准单元，但VLN路径中的步之间存在依赖关系且长度可变，单步边际覆盖保证无法保证整条路径的覆盖，导致CP的覆盖保证失效，无法用于触发人工干预。

Method: 1. **问题建模**：将VLN建模为离散图上的序列分类，每步的决策上下文x_t包含指令、观察、历史和可选动作集A_t；策略输出动作概率p(a|x_t)，最大概率记为p_max。2. **非一致性分数**：使用THR（1-p）、APS（累积概率质量）和RAPS（带排序惩罚）三种基础分数。3. **置信度调整分数**：定义s_norm = s_base / (1 + w(x_t))，其中参数无关权重w_pf = 1 - p_max，使低置信度步骤具有更高的有效阈值。4. **路径级校准**：将一条路径压缩为单个校准分数s̃_e = max_{t} s_norm(x_{e,t}, a*_{e,t})，即路径中最难覆盖的那一步。5. **覆盖定理**：在episode可交换性假设下，证明真值动作在整条路径的每一步被覆盖的概率≥1-α（同时覆盖整条轨迹和步平均覆盖）。6. **学习式权重变体**（备选）：用小型神经网络基于熵、p_max、top-2概率差、动作集大小等特征预测权重w_ψ。7. **部署**：当预测集大小超过预算τ时请求人工帮助。

Result: 在R2R和REVERIE数据集上，测试了DUET、HAMT、R-prev、R-osc四种VLN策略与三种非一致性分数的组合。关键结果：(1) 基础逐步CP在大多数设置下欠覆盖（步覆盖率显著低于1-α目标），而ENCP在所有报告配置中均达到或超过步覆盖目标；(2) 参数无关权重与学习式权重在α=0.10时步覆盖差异≤0.006，但学习式变体返回更大的预测集且覆盖无一致提升，因此参数无关变体更优；(3) 模拟帮助请求实验中，当ENCP预测集超过阈值时改用模拟器提供真值动作，DUET的成功率从71.2%提升至91.1%（τ=8，问询率32.3%）甚至98.8%（问询率68.7%）。此外，在episode可交换分割下，ENCP的轨迹覆盖与名义1-α的偏差≤0.0017，但在seen-to-unseen建筑物分布偏移下覆盖下降。

Conclusion: ENCP通过路径级校准和置信度调整分数解决了标准CP在VLN序列决策中因步间依赖和变长路径导致的覆盖失效问题，提供了具有有限样本保证的模型无关不确定性估计。其预测集大小可作为实用的人工帮助请求信号，在模拟实验中显著提升导航成功率。未来工作需要在分布偏移下重校准、连续动作空间扩展以及真实人类操作员验证方面进一步研究。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation for Vision-Language-Navigation (VLN) models is a critical task since it can help identify ambiguous and unreliable predictions, enabling agents to make safer navigation decisions. As one of the most advanced uncertainty estimation frameworks, conformal prediction (CP) offers a promising approach for uncertainty estimation in VLN. However, given that VLN agent requires a sequence of steps, standard calibration in conformal prediction fails to provide coverage guarantee it promises over a dependent, variable-length VLN episode. To this end, we propose Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction (ENCP), which rescales a nonconformity score by the policy's residual confidence and calibrates one maximum score per episode. Under exchangeable calibration and test episodes, this construction covers the ground truth at every step with probability at least $1 - \alpha$, while allowing dependence among steps within an episode. Across four VLN policies and three nonconformity scores on R2R and REVERIE dataset, ENCP meets all reported empirical step-coverage targets on the seen-to-unseen evaluation. These results demonstrate that ENCP can provide model-agnostic uncertainty estimates, which might be useful for determining when a VLN agent should defer to a more capable predictor, including human assistance.

</details>


### [263] [SWB-DM: A Calibrated Sliced-Wasserstein-Barycenter Aggregator with Delayed-Momentum Caching for Byzantine-Robust Federated Learning under Partial Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16099)
*Saranraj S,Saranya M S,Alex David S,Ajay Kumar A*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWB-DM提出了一种用于联邦学习中部分参与场景的拜占庭鲁棒聚合方法，结合了基于切片Wasserstein重心（SWB）的聚合器和延迟动量（DeMoA风格）缓存机制。通过448个CIFAR-10配置及多个数据集的广泛实验，揭示了多种现有防御机制的不同失败模式，并验证了所提方法的trim比率校准和缓存预热成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分参与联邦学习场景中，每轮仅少量客户端参与，现有鲁棒聚合算法（如中位数、Krum、Bulyan等）的有限样本保证基于全群体的假设被破坏，攻击者可能主导小样本导致防御失效。

Method: 1. SWB（切片Wasserstein重心聚合器）：将客户端更新分块，随机正交旋转后按行排序，在排序秩空间计算修剪均值作为重心，通过medoid（最近真实客户端）借用秩到坐标的排列来恢复坐标身份（启发式步骤）。2. SWB-DM：结合DeMoA风格的延迟动量缓存，每轮在全部客户端缓存（而非仅采样客户端）上聚合。修剪比率校准为r=min(0.45, β+0.05)，与假设的腐败水平挂钩。

Result: 在448个CIFAR-10配置中，揭示多种失败模式：中位数在小样本偶数情况下退化为确定性错误答案；Krum在违反n>2f+2前提时静默发散（参数变NaN/Inf）；Bulyan在n≥4f+3边界处产生突变通过/失败。IPM攻击比ALIE更可靠地击败序统计防御。SWB-DM在CIFAR-10缓存预热后增益显著大于所有基线（10轮→25轮平均+13.12分），但CIFAR-100上FLTrust增益更大（与缓存机制无关）。500客户端扩展性测试中SWB-DM恢复不如纯延迟动量+中位数。FLTrust在64配置中无崩溃，中位数崩溃最多（10/64）。

Conclusion: SWB-DM通过缓存解耦了鲁棒性与采样运气，trim比率校准对避免崩溃至关重要，但代价是缓存预热成本。不同防御方法失败机制各异，需借助逐轮参数范数与预测直方图诊断而非仅看最终精度来区分。medoid坐标修复启发式和500客户端恢复差距仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Robust aggregation methods for federated learning quietly rest on a fragile assumption: that whoever shows up in a given round is a fair sample of the full population. In practice, they rarely are. When only a handful of clients participate per round, even a modest fraction of adversaries can dominate that sample and silently invalidate the finite-sample guarantees that coordinate-wise median, Krum, Bulyan, and trimmed mean all depend on. We introduce SWB-DM to address this directly. SWB treats each slice of a client update as a one-dimensional distribution, computes a trimmed Wasserstein barycenter across clients, and recovers coordinate identity via a medoid-based gauge-fixing step -- a heuristic we developed and do not claim it belongs to standard optimal-transport theory. DeMoA-style delayed momentum then caches updates across the full client population each round, decoupling robustness from whoever happened to be sampled. Trim ratio calibration is not cosmetic: under-trimming causes collapse at corruption levels a properly calibrated model survives. Across 448 CIFAR-10 configurations, plus CIFAR-100, FEMNIST, and a 500-client scalability run, we find several mechanistically distinct failure modes. Even-sample coordinate-wise median degrades to a deterministic wrong answer. Krum silently violates its own n greater than 2f+2 precondition and diverges without warning. Bulyan's n greater than or equal to 4f+3 threshold produces a sharp pass/fail boundary. On attacks, IPM defeats order-statistic defenses -- including SWB -- more reliably than ALIE, confirmed through delta-space measurements against a convergence bound. SWB-DM's cache carries a real warm-up cost, but extending all baselines to the same round budget shows its CIFAR-10 gains are disproportionately large. On CIFAR-100, FLTrust benefits more -- for reasons entirely unrelated to caching.

</details>


### [264] [Adaptive Bayesian Partner Selection for Federated Clinical Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16446)
*Navid Seidi,Satyaki Roy,Sajal K. Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Adaptive Bayesian Partner Selection (ABPS)，一种面向医疗联邦学习的点对点框架，通过 Beta-Bernoulli 后验、Shapley 边际效用和 UCB 排名机制自适应选择协作伙伴，并允许在负迁移时主动隔离。在 MIMIC-IV 230 个非 IID 临床中心上，全量 ABPS-X 以 FedAvg 9% 的通信成本达到 FedDyn 的预测精度（AUROC 0.758）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗联邦学习面临严重的统计异质性（non-IID）和时间概念漂移，强制跨中心参数聚合常导致负迁移；同时持续全局通信带宽开销巨大，亟需一种能自适应决定"与谁、何时、以何代价"协作的伙伴选择机制。

Method: 方法核心由四部分组成：(1) 目标感知元数据预过滤（基于余弦相似度/反余弦/KPI 对齐三目标）；(2) Beta-Bernoulli 后验对每个候选伙伴的 Shapley 边际效用建模；(3) ε-greedy UCB 排序 + 显式 rest 动作的提议-拒绝拓扑协议；(4) 轻量化扩展：头部个性化、bfloat16 量化、可调活跃集 κ。理论分析给出 Beta 后验浓度（定理 1）、O(κ log T) 遗憾界（定理 2）以及隔离的 Bayes 最优性（引理 1）。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV v3.1 的 230 个 careunit×2 年窗口中心、二元院内死亡率预测任务上（50 轮，5 随机种子）：ABPS-X（AUROC 0.758±0.010）匹配最强基线 FedDyn，通信成本仅为 FedAvg 的 0.09×；公平对照 FedAvg-X 反而下降 6.7 点。多样性目标配置下，约 41% 中心轮次触发主动隔离（Lemma 1 首次在真实临床数据上大规模激活），带宽降至 0.16×。消融证实增益来自 Bayesian 伙伴选择本身，而非通信扩展单独作用。

Conclusion: ABPS 表明：基于效用感知的自适应协作可在不牺牲精度的前提下显著降低通信开销，特别适用于"中心数量多且规模小"的医疗联邦场景。主动隔离被证明在负迁移条件下是 Bayes 最优动作。局限：仅单一数据集与二元任务；缺乏 (ε,δ)-DP、抗拜占庭等正式保证；40 中心大规模联邦下精度略低于 FedAvg/FedDyn（0.793 vs 0.813/0.805），提示该范式在大型中心场景存在权衡。未来工作包括高斯机制差分隐私包装、多模态融合、漂移赌博机分析及时至事件结局建模。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) in healthcare faces pronounced heterogeneity and temporal concept drift across clinical centers, where evolving patient populations and care practices shift data distributions. Existing approaches rely on persistent global communication, incurring substantial bandwidth overhead while risking negative transfer from poorly aligned peers. We propose Adaptive Bayesian Partner Selection (ABPS), a peer-to-peer framework that governs who collaborates, when, and at what cost. Each center maintains a Beta-Bernoulli posterior over prospective peers' Shapley marginal utility, ranks candidates with an Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) criterion, and forms collaborations through a lightweight propose-reject mechanism, with the option to abstain from communication when no mutually beneficial partner exists. The framework admits a stochastic decision interpretation, yielding finite-sample concentration guarantees and O(kappa log T) regret in partner selection, along with conditions under which intentional isolation is optimal under negative transfer. Lightweight extensions (head personalization, bfloat16 quantized communication, and a tunable active-set size) further improve efficiency, and a goal-aware metadata filter enables institution-specific collaboration strategies. On binary in-hospital mortality prediction over the first 24 hours of an ICU stay, with 230 non-IID clinical centers drawn from MIMIC-IV, the full ABPS-X variant matches the strongest federated baseline (FedDyn, AUROC 0.758) at 0.09x the communication cost of FedAvg, with reduced variability. A diversity-driven configuration activates intentional isolation for a substantial fraction of centers. These results show that adaptive, utility-aware collaboration reduces communication without sacrificing accuracy when centers are numerous and small, offering a scalable paradigm for healthcare FL.

</details>


### [265] [High-Performance Tensor Formulation of the Viterbi Algorithm for Hidden Semi-Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16500)
*Lorenzo Piarulli,Elia Belli,Daniele De Sensi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量的Viterbi算法公式化方法，用于隐半马尔可夫模型（HSMM），通过将传统四重嵌套循环重构为适合SIMD和大规模并行架构的张量操作，首次实现了GPU加速，在CPU单核、多核和GPU上分别获得了最高14×、200×和570×的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSMM作为HMM的扩展，在计算生物学、金融、语音识别等领域应用广泛，但其Viterbi解码算法相比标准HMM增加了持续时长维度，复杂度达到O(TN²D)。现有实现均为顺序单线程版本，无法利用现代HPC硬件，导致大规模HSMM解码不可行。研究者被迫简化模型或截断数据集，限制了HSMM的表达潜力。

Method: 核心方法是将HSMM Viterbi算法中关于(s_j, s_i, d)的三重嵌套循环重构为三维张量操作（称为"Brick"）。关键思想包括：(1) 分解为Brick构建（预计算时间无关部分）、Brick更新（每步结合过去delta值和发射概率）和最大值提取三个阶段；(2) 通过广播乘积（broadcasted product）将2D矩阵扩展为3D张量；(3) 在对数空间下操作避免数值下溢；(4) 采用发射概率缓存策略，将每步计算复杂度从O(DN)降至摊销O(N)；(5) 在CPU上融合Brick更新与argmax操作，在GPU上分离为两个内核以避免网格同步。实现了四个版本：Tens-Py（NumPy参考实现）、Tens-1c（CPU单核C++）、Tens-mc（CPU多核C+++OpenMP）、Tens-gpu（CUDA/HIP GPU实现）。

Result: 在3种CPU架构（AMD EPYC 7A53、NVIDIA Grace、Intel Xeon 8480+）和5种GPU架构（A100、H100、H200、MI250X、MI300X）上进行了全面评估。核心结果包括：(1) Tens-1c相对Base-1c获得8.7×至14.1×加速，指令退役数减少36×，向量化率从<0.001%提升至30.9%；(2) Tens-mc相对Base-mc最高获得11.5×加速，向量化率达80.4%；(3) Tens-gpu在H100上相对Base-mc最高获得36.8×加速，在H200上相对Base-1c获得最高570×加速；(4) 能耗方面，GPU版本仅消耗基线2%的能量；(5) 极端压力测试中（N=100, D=10000, T=10^7），估计基线需148天的计算在H200上仅需53.7分钟。所有实现的输出与hsmmlearn完全一致。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过将HSMM Viterbi算法的内层循环重构为张量操作，可以从根本上释放并行性，使原本不可行的大规模HSMM解码变得实用。这不仅建立了新的性能基准，也为将动态规划算法映射到新兴AI加速器（如TPU、张量核心）开辟了道路。代码已开源为tensor-hsmm库，未来工作可扩展到序列级并行、分布式执行、Forward-Backward和Baum-Welch算法的加速，以及连续发射分布的支持。

Abstract: Hidden Semi-Markov Models (HSMMs) are fundamental probabilistic models widely adopted across diverse domains, from computational biology to finance and signal processing. The Viterbi algorithm decodes the most likely state sequence given an HSMM and can be applied iteratively for ab initio model learning. However, existing Viterbi implementations remain sequential, and GPU-accelerated solutions are entirely absent, making HSMM decoding impractical for large-scale workloads. We present a tensor-based formulation of the Viterbi algorithm for HSMMs, restructuring the inner loops into tensor operations that naturally map onto SIMD units and massively parallel architectures. Building on this formulation, we provide optimized implementations spanning single- and multi-core CPUs, and, for the first time, GPU. Experimental evaluation demonstrates speedups of up to 14x on a single core, over 200x with multi-core, and over 570x on GPU over the state-of-the-art sequential baseline, establishing a new performance baseline for large-scale HSMM decoding.

</details>


### [266] [Structural Negative Transfer in Federated Graph Neural Networks: Diagnosis, Causal Investigation, and the Limits of Divergence-Aware Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16977)
*Chethana Prasad Kabgere,Shylaja SS*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了联邦图神经网络中由客户端图结构异质性导致的"结构性负迁移"现象。研究发现：1) 结构异常客户端加入联邦后损失超半精度；2) 度数散度（degree divergence）与负迁移存在可复现但温和的关联，谱散度因参考池混淆未能复现；3) 因果图手术干预未证实拓扑独立导致伤害；4) 看似成功的发散感知修复机制在匹配的结构盲基线下消失；5) 提出"均匀基线对照"应作为领域标准评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦图神经网络中，客户端图在拓扑结构上存在根本性差异（非仅标签或特征分布差异），导致共享权重必须适应本质不同的拓扑操作。现有的FedAvg、FedProx、SCAFFOLD等优化器均未考虑这种结构性异质性，可能系统性地损害结构异常的客户端——而这些客户端往往是规模较小、新加入的弱势参与者，构成公平性问题。标准报告仅看平均精度会掩盖这种客户端级别的不公平。

Method: 五项实验构成递进式证伪研究：1) 真实+合成6客户端联邦的观测相关性分析（度散度与谱散度的Wasserstein-1距离）；2) 扩展至20客户端并控制域混淆；3) 受控合成剂量响应扫描（密度、同质性、特征维度）；4) 因果图手术干预（仅插值边生成概率，其他比特级固定）；5) 五种修复机制的逐一尝试与均匀基线对照。所有声称的结论都经过主动证伪检验。

Result: 主要发现：1) 6客户端中度散度r=0.90、谱散度r=0.97与负转移强相关；2) 20客户端中度散度减弱但仍显著（r≈0.49-0.53），谱散度完全消失（r=-0.05至0.15），因参考池扩大后合成客户端不再显得异常；3) 同质性扫描无显著效应，特征维度呈非对称效应（过剩有害，缺失无害）；4) 因果图手术在n=800和n=3000均无显著效应（r=0.07-0.08）；5) GCN度归一化导致偏离客户端更新幅度在24/24种子中更小（p≈6e-8），但校正后精度提升不显著（p=0.17）；6) 看似成功的发散校准FedProx（+4.3点，p=0.001）被均匀μ=0.68的结构盲对照击败（p=0.57）。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是一个温和、部分复现的度特异性信号，可作为联邦图学习中的"早期预警指标"，但尚未在更大规模独立联邦中得到验证。作者坦率报告三项自身结论被推翻的过程，主张将"匹配强度的结构盲均匀基线对照"作为联邦异质性方法评估的标准实践，这对整个领域具有方法论意义。文章的核心价值不在于提供了最终答案，而在于通过严格的递进式证伪研究，诚实地缩小了后续研究者的搜索空间。

Abstract: Federated learning lets multiple participants train a shared model without pooling raw data, by exchanging locally trained model updates instead. Federated averaging assumes that averaging local models is a reasonable way to solve one shared problem when participants' data are broadly similar. Work on non-IID federated learning has shown that this assumption can withstand differences in label and feature distributions. We ask whether it survives a different strain specific to graph neural networks, where client graphs differ not in label or feature distribution but in structure itself, requiring the same shared weights to operate over fundamentally different topologies. We call the resulting harm structural negative transfer. In a federation of real citation networks and synthetic structural proxies, a structurally atypical client lost more than half its achievable accuracy simply by joining. In an initial six-client federation, two label-free structural statistics computable before training were strongly associated with this harm. Expanding to twenty clients showed that degree divergence remained associated with harm, although more weakly, and survived removal of domain contrast. Spectral divergence did not replicate, which we trace to a confound caused by the composition of the reference pool used for leave-one-out statistics. A causal intervention isolating topology found no significant effect. A degree-normalization mechanism held across twenty-four seeds but did not explain the harm when corrected. The best of five candidate fixes beat a tuned baseline only until a matched, structurally blind control was applied, after which the gain disappeared. What survives is a modest, partially replicated, degree-specific signal that is not yet a validated predictor at scale.

</details>


### [267] [Signed p-adic Residual Encodings of Finite-Domain All-Different Systems with a Sudoku Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16063)
*Greg Baker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种将有限域约束系统（如全异约束和CNF公式）编译为带符号p进仿射残差目标函数的方法，通过坐标支配定理证明所有全局最小值都落在有限域内，并以数独为案例进行实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是将约束满足问题（CSP）原生编码为带符号的p进线性回归目标函数，避免传统的one-hot提升，在81个系数的原生数字空间中直接表达全异约束，并通过p进范数的非阿基米德性质实现精确钉扎和带符号奖励。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 构造带符号加权仿射p进残差目标函数，正项作为单值井钉扎系数到有限集合，负项作为有界奖励奖励不等式或子句满足；(2) 证明坐标支配定理（定理1），当钉扎权重超过每个坐标的关联交互权重时，所有全局最小值都落在有限域内；(3) 提供从全异系统和CNF公式到带符号目标函数的编译模板，并在81维原生数字空间上应用于数独；(4) 实现行交换贪心搜索和Zubarev随机游走两种局部搜索求解器。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 证明了在ℤpⁿ和Qpⁿ上的有限域带符号仿射编译模板定理（定理1和推论2）；(2) 证明了列表着色形式下全异系统的全局最小值恰好是域内最小冲突分配，覆盖了Max-CSP和反铁磁Potts模型（定理3，推论4）；(3) 通过子句编译器在固定素数p=5下证明了带符号阈值问题的NP困难性（推论5）；(4) 给出了负CNF和成对全异行的多项式大小纯正补扩展；(5) 在18个随机生成的数独谜题上，贪心和Zubarev两种求解器都成功解决了所有谜题（36次方法-谜题运行），贪心法在14/18次配对运行中所需步数更少；(6) 二的幂次编码方案在50个Project Euler 96谜题上全部失败（0/2250次运行），验证了非阿基米德局部常值性对局部搜索的不利影响。

Conclusion: 结论是带符号p进残差在有限域约束中具有直接作用：正合成项将变量钉扎到有限集，负项在钉扎后成为不等式或子句满足的有界奖励。全异系统可被多项式时间编码为带符号目标函数，其全局最小值恰好是域内最小冲突分配，在可满足情形下恰好是满足分配。同一带符号残差构造不仅限于成对不等式，布尔钉扎后宽度小于p的每个子句变成一个禁止仿射等式，单个带符号项即可奖励其满足。p进构造应设计为残差的非目标值为单位元（范数用作指示器而非大小度量），且指示器应足够多且局部以便邻域结构可见。

Abstract: We study signed, weighted affine $p$-adic residual objectives as native encodings of finite-domain constraints. For primes that separate the finite alphabet, sufficiently weighted positive unary rows pin each coefficient to its allowed set, while negative rows reward unequal endpoints or clause satisfaction. A coordinatewise domination theorem places every global minimiser in the finite domain; there the loss is, up to an additive constant, the all-different conflict count or the negative number of satisfied CNF clauses. Standard Sudoku provides an $81$-coefficient case study without a one-hot lift. A client-side implementation exposes the generated dataframes, arithmetic, diagnostics, and searches.

</details>


### [268] [A panoramic aerodynamic performance prediction method for turbomachinery cascades using transformer-enhanced neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16066)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Liming Song,Tianyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对叶轮机械气动设计周期中CFD计算成本高昂的问题，提出了一种"全景式"气动性能预测框架。其核心是设计了一种Transformer增强型神经算子（TNO），以Rotor 37跨音速压气机为对象，先预测Navier-Stokes方程的基本物理量（静压、静温、速度、质量流量），再由这些基本量推导出任意所需的性能参数。实验表明，TNO预测精度优于FNO和DeepONet等先进算子，下游任务（灵敏度分析、单/多目标优化）计算成本降低约四个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叶轮机械气动设计涉及优化搜索、灵敏度分析、不确定度量化等大量下游任务，每项任务都需大量CFD计算，耗时且昂贵。传统代理模型为黑箱预测，缺乏可解释性；现有深度学习流场预测模型训练成本高、复用性差，只能针对特定任务。因此需要一种灵活、快速、可复用的替代CFD的预测方法。

Method: 提出全景式预测框架，包含两部分：(1)基本场预测器TNO——采用分支-主干结构，分支网络由MLP编码28维几何设计变量，主干网络由多个Galerkin注意力块（融合Galerkin型简化自注意力与FFN）处理查询坐标，以预测5个基本物理量（静压、静温、绝/相对速度平方、质量流量）；(2)全景预测器——通过对基本场进行周向和展向加权积分，推导9种性能参数（等熵效率、压比、温比、流量等）。训练数据通过LHS采样2900个Rotor 37几何变体，CFD求解器生成流场；训练采用MSE损失、Adam优化器，900个epoch。

Result: TNO以最低参数量（154K）和最小显存（147MB）实现最高精度：5个基本场平均相对误差0.083%，较FNO降低85.5%；预测时间约0.9 ms，比CFD快约5个数量级。在效率、压比、流量等性能参数预测上均优于MLP、UNet、DeepONet、FNO以及ANN、PR、GPR、SVR、XGB等传统代理模型（训练样本≥1500时12/15场景最优）。单目标优化中效率提升1.95%、总压损失降低17.24%；多目标优化（NSGA2）得到优于参考设计的Pareto解，所有最优解均经CFD验证；下游任务整体加速约4个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提出的TNO驱动的全景式预测框架能够像CFD模拟器一样灵活预测任意性能参数，同时大幅降低计算成本，显著提升叶轮机械设计效率。未来工作将拓展到运行边界条件变化，并结合主动学习以进一步减少训练样本需求。

Abstract: To enable flexible and rapid aerodynamic performance evaluation in turbomachinery design, this paper proposes a panoramic performance prediction framework. Unlike most previous prediction models that directly predict the objective functions of interest, our approach first predicts the basic parameters of the Navier-Stokes equations, such as temperature, pressure, and density. Utilizing these basic physical quantities, it subsequently predicts key performance parameters of the turbine stage meridian plane. By adopting this methodology, our proposed panoramic performance prediction framework functions similarly to a CFD simulator, capable of predicting various objective of interest to the designers. To enhance prediction accuracy, a transformer-enhanced neural operator (TNO) is introduced within this framework. Using the Rotor 37 blades as a reference, the proposed TNO is trained to predict the performance of a transonic compressor blade in the meridian plane. The TNO can accurately predict total quantities such as isentropic efficiency, mass flow, and distributions of total pressure ratio. Remarkably, the prediction error of TNO is observed to be smaller than that of state-of-the-art deep learning operators such as the FNO and DeepONet. Furthermore, the TNO is applied to downstream tasks, including sensitivity analysis and optimization of various objective functions. The results confirm that the TNO can operate almost like a CFD simulator, while reducing the computational cost of downstream tasks by four orders of magnitude. The effectiveness and reliability of the proposed TNO for solving different kinds of downstream tasks have been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [269] [A Dynamic Aggregation Strategy Enhanced Efficient Global Optimization Algorithm for Solving High-Dimensional Turbomachinery Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16067)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Yun Chen,Guangjian Ma,Liming Song,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向高维昂贵黑箱问题的动态聚合高效全局优化算法（DA-EGO）。通过动态分解高维空间为多个低维子空间，利用多项式混沌展开（PCE）的全局灵敏度分析与子空间内的扰动法检测变量交互，并结合子空间搜索范围自适应缩减机制，在预算受限条件下高效求解。算法在21个基准测试（30/60/90维）以及28维跨声速转子叶片和60维多级压气机工程优化中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维（d≥30）且昂贵的黑箱（HEB）问题广泛存在于叶轮机械气动优化设计中，单次CFD评估耗时极长（数分钟至数小时），传统代理模型难以克服维度灾难（一般仅适用≤15维），固定或随机分解策略无法处理强变量交互问题。

Method: 1. 基于PCE的Sobol灵敏度指数筛选"疑似交互"变量对，再利用子空间Kriging代理上的扰动法（Hessian型二阶差分,阈值0.2）确认交互关系；2. 每轮迭代动态更新分组方案、精英点与子空间搜索边界；3. 子空间采用EGO（Kriging+EI）优化，初始采样3d、最大迭代6d；4. 基于子空间代理的空间缩减机制（采样10000点，取最优50%区域缩小边界，比率ω=0.5）；5. 三类知识表（样本、交互对、边界）在分解与子空间优化模块间循环聚合。

Result: 在21个CEC'2010基准函数上，DA-EGO在17个实例上取得最低均值；在可分与部分可分函数（F1–F6）上显著优于GA、DE、GSK、IKAEA、GSGA、Nash-EGO及随机分组变体RG-EGO；在非可分Shifted Rosenbrock（F7）60/90维上劣于GSGA。工程案例：Rotor 37（28维）等熵效率提升1.65%，多级压气机（60维）提升1.07%，均优于对比算法。主要代价：Kriging构建与交互分析带来额外计算开销（30维约35分钟）。

Conclusion: DA-EGO通过动态变量交互分析与子空间搜索范围自适应调整，有效缓解了高维昂贵黑箱优化中的维度灾难，适用于可分与部分可分问题以及叶轮机械气动设计；但在强非可分问题（如高维Rosenbrock）上不具备普遍优势，且额外代理成本使其在廉价目标函数场景下优势减弱。未来工作可探索更高效的Kriging实现以降低计算开销。

Abstract: In order to solve the high-dimensional ($d \geq 30$) expensive black-box problems within budget, an efficient global optimization (EGO) algorithm with a dynamic aggregation strategy is proposed, labeled as DA-EGO. Specifically, the DA-EGO decomposes the original high-dimensional design space into a set of low-dimensional subspaces for efficient surrogate-based optimization search, and the optimal solutions of subspaces are combined as an elite point for the global search. Most importantly, the subspaces are not fixed. Instead, the subspace variables are updated in each iteration, according to the variable interaction analyses in the sub- and full-spaces. The perturbation method and the analysis of variance are used to detect variable interactions. To further accelerate the optimization progress, the searching ranges of subspaces are also adaptively adjusted according to the analyses of subspace optimization results of the previous iteration. Tests on 21 benchmark instances, comprising seven functions at 30, 60, and 90 dimensions, show that DA-EGO is effective on separable and partially separable problems under a budget of 1500 function evaluations. Its advantage is case-dependent: on the non-separable shifted Rosenbrock function, GSGA performs better at 60 and 90 dimensions, while the 30-dimensional results are statistically comparable to IKAEA and GSGA. Moreover, the advantage of DA-EGO is also seen in the aerodynamic optimization of a transonic rotor blade with 28 variables as well as the compressor stage optimization with 60 variables. With the above, the effectiveness of the proposed DA-EGO has been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [270] [Distilling Foundation Models for Agentic What-If Reasoning:Cost, Latency, and Governance in a Hybrid LLM+SLM Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16091)
*Sourish Dey,Aditya Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对表格基础模型TabPFN在交互式智能体循环中推理延迟过高、且全云LLM部署存在数据治理风险的问题，提出将TabPFN教师蒸馏为紧凑MLP学生并嵌入混合LLM+SLM架构的方案。通过UCI Adult和五个OpenML基准测试实现6220–11611倍的参数压缩，保留95.4–100.5%的准确率和96.8–100.0%的AUC；混合架构实现3.8–4.8倍端到端延迟降低和2.1倍云端token成本节省，同时将原始特征行保留在本地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型TabPFN虽具零训练预测能力，但单次推理延迟达12.6s（CPU），无法作为交互式智能体循环的热路径决策后端；同时现有全云LLM部署将敏感属性多次外发，引入数据治理风险与不必要token开销。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1) 对TabPFN教师与小型MLP学生（64-32隐藏层，固定架构，σ₁=ReLU，σ₂=sigmoid）进行知识蒸馏，采用Hinton软目标损失（α=0.7, T=2.0）+ 硬标签CE损失的混合，分类与回归任务分别对应不同损失形式；(2) 在UCI Adult及五个OpenML基准（credit-g、bank-marketing、kr-vs-kp、internet-ads、speeddating）上做8,546参数级压缩，并以α=0硬标签控制实验隔离软目标贡献；(3) 嵌入LangGraph混合架构，将云LLM（gpt-4o-mini）限制为单次schema受限的工具选择+场景解析，预测使用蒸馏学生MLP本地执行，响应格式化由本地Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct SLM完成；通过LangSmith trace实现审计可观测。

Result: 六个数据集上分类准确率保留95.4–100.5%、AUC保留96.8–100.0%，压缩比6,220×–11,611×；α=0硬标签控制证明教师软目标贡献2.1–7.0个AUC点的增益；端到端延迟在CPU/GPU/M5 Pro三档硬件上分别降低3.84×/3.83×/4.82×（22.91s vs 87.95s；1.53s vs 5.86s；4.96s vs 23.94s），其中tabular predict+residual占full-LLM总耗时90–92%，是延迟降低的主因；云token成本降低2.11×（50查询总成本$0.00479 vs $0.01011），工具调用准确率从0.90提升至0.98，原始14列特征行不出本地边界；公平性审计发现种族equalized odds差距由0.431扩大至0.490（+14%相对差距），提示聚合指标掩盖子群体差异。

Conclusion: 蒸馏+混合LLM/SLM架构是表格基础模型在交互式智能体循环中可行的部署路径，通过消除TabPFN的in-context attention开销消除热路径瓶颈，并以更少云往返降低token成本；然而2.1× token节省的硬件摊销需要约471万查询才能回收专用GPU投资；公平性审计揭示压缩可能放大种族均等化机会差距，因此受监管领域部署需做子群体评估；同时本地SLM零样本解析仅达58%准确率，验证了保留云端做schema受限解析的非对称分工必要性。未来工作包括对本地格式化器做参数高效微调、扩展无标签迁移集、部署span级脱敏的混合可观测方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models deliver strong zero-training predictive performance via in-context learning, but their high inference latency makes them impractical as hot-path decision backends in interactive agentic loops. We distill a TabPFN teacher into a compact feed-forward student across a business-decision simulation on UCI Adult and five OpenML benchmarks: the classification head compresses 53.2M parameters to 8,546 (6,220x); the deployed two-head loan pipeline compresses 111.4M parameters to 17,059 (6,532x).
