The student retains 95.4-100.5% accuracy and 96.8-100.0% AUC, with the lowest accuracy retention on credit-g at 95.4%; an alpha = 0 hard-label control shows that the teacher's soft targets provide a 2.1-7.0 AUC point gain.

</details>


### [271] [Evaluating Open-Weight E-Commerce Agents with Environment-Grounded Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16093)
*Nimit Shah,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对电商购物代理的确定性、可复现的评估环境，通过在对话开始前预先提交目标购物车、商品揭示时间表和角色参数，并在对话过程中保留工具调用轨迹和环境状态，使得评估器能够将终端购物车成功率分解为可验证的细粒度能力诊断（检索、购物车管理、目录接地、工具使用、用户体验）。在 8 个 20B–35B 开源权重代理、每模型 160 次试验、44 项指标的评估中，该方法揭示了行动不足、过度购买、无根据属性描述和检索失败等被单一成功率掩盖的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一的最终购物车成功率掩盖了代理完成任务的路径差异：无法区分是检索失败、过度购买、虚构属性还是行动不足。因此需要一个能够保留中间行为证据、将最终结果分解为可操作诊断的电商评估环境。

Method: 构建一个基于 10000 商品控制目录（按六家零售商类别分布聚合）的确定性电商环境：(1) 场景生成器在对话前预提交隐藏目标购物车、商品数量、按价格加权几何分布的篮筐构造、四级难度（直接购买/轻度探索/多商品/复杂），以及揭示时间表（批量/线性/非线性三种揭示风格）；(2) 用户模拟器扮演顾客，控制器按揭示时间表引导对话，并双向交互（读取模拟器输出以终止试验、注入实时指令）；(3) 评估器结合状态重放（按转录顺序重放工具调用重建购物车）、目录行、揭示时间表、模拟器预期工具调用集，进行规则检查（Rule）、模型判断（Model）和混合检查（Hybrid）；(4) 44 项指标覆盖结果、检索、购物车、工具使用、体验五类维度，包括按声明类型拆分的幻觉检测（动作/数值/属性）和基于工具类别的惩罚梯度的工具调用正确性。

Result: 在 8 个开源权重代理（GPT-OSS-20B、Gemma4-26B/31B、Qwen3-30B、Qwen3.5-35B/27B、Qwen3.6-35B/27B）上的评估显示：精确购物车成功率从 0.30 到 0.57 不等，但不同难度层级下排序不稳定。诊断出四类失败模式：(1) Qwen3-30B 行动不足（每对话仅 15.4 次调用，63% 试验耗尽回合预算）；(2) Qwen3.5-35B 过度购买（购物车精度 0.89 但达到最高召回 0.85，每对话 44.1 次调用）；(3) GPT-OSS 与 Qwen3-30B 属性幻觉率约 0.24，是其他模型四倍但数值幻觉率正常；(4) Qwen3-30B 幻动作率最高（0.158）。验证消融证明只有保留全部模拟器记录才能对请求相对的工具调用和当前请求项的检索成功做出可信判断。跨评委一致性检验显示工具调用正确性一致性最高（r=0.924），澄清一致性最低（r=0.490）。

Conclusion: 环境接地的验证能将终端购物车成功率转化为可操作的诊断，区分被成功率掩盖的失败模式（检索、购物车管理、目录接地、工具使用），为部署和修复决策提供更精细的依据。更广泛的启示是：可靠的代理验证需要环境保留评估中间行为所需的证据（状态和信息可得性），使评估从"是否成功"升级为"如何行为"。该工作作为可复现的部署前测试，补充了真实零售流量上的验证。

Abstract: A shopping conversation has many routes to the same cart, and a task-success rate reduces all of them to one score. We build a deterministic and reproducible e-commerce environment that precommits each trial's customer and trajectory parameters, including the persona, difficulty, target cart, and an item reveal schedule. A simulated consumer attempts to buy a target cart from the environment with assistance from the evaluated model. The environment guides the simulator's actions and records every assistant action alongside the environment state at that point. After the trial, these records allow the evaluator to assess individual parts of the conversation against the retained evidence. For example, the evaluator penalizes a search for failing to surface a target product only when the customer has already mentioned that product. We further use this evidence to apply different penalties to tool calls depending on how the assistant's actions compare with an expected tool-call set. Our environment also interacts with the simulator bidirectionally, reading its output to stop the trial when the simulator determines that the customer has become too frustrated and injecting directives in real time that specify when to explore, defer buying an item, or recall a previous exchange. This interaction creates an open-ended and verifiable simulation. Across eight open-weight agents from 20B to 35B parameters, with 160 trials per agent and 44 metrics, the resulting capability profiles distinguish under-action, over-purchase, unsupported product attributes, and poor search, all of which terminal success obscures.

</details>


### [272] [LLM Inference in a Flash!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16161)
*Sebastian Zhao,Minseo Kim,Coleman Hooper,Luca Manolache,Michael W. Mahoney,Yakun Sophia Shao,Kurt Keutzer,Amir Gholami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向Flash存内计算（CIM）的LLM推理算法，通过端到端纯整数量化和基于稀疏字典编码的KV cache压缩，在保持精度的同时大幅降低动态KV流量与系统延迟/能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理受限于"内存墙"，Flash CIM虽具备大容量与近数据计算优势，但缺乏高精度浮点支持且写耐久性有限，难以直接承载权重和动态KV cache。

Method: 1) 端到端INT8权值/激活量化+组粒度（g=128）激活平滑，结合二阶多项式近似的整数Softmax/RMSNorm/SiLU及INT16再量化；2) 基于过完备字典（M=32768原子/层/流）的稀疏字典编码，用投影式贪婪追踪替代OMP的最小二乘refit，并配合查询感知的层级稀疏（K_low=3, K_high=16，Top-10%细化）+局部窗口（W=128, U=32）管理KV cache。

Result: Llama-3.1-8B WikiText-2困惑度仅较FP16基线上升0.0349（7.5454→7.5803）；LongBench平均保留FP16全KV基线99%性能，动态KV cache流量降至1/15；系统建模显示S2配置下相对NPU+DRAM基线，短上下文（1K）延迟/能耗节省3.1×/2.7×，长上下文（256K）达4.4×/6.8×。

Conclusion: 通过算法-硬件协同设计，可在Flash CIM上高效部署LLM推理，证明纯整数量化与稀疏字典KV压缩是缓解LLM内存瓶颈、释放CIM潜力的有效路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown impressive capabilities across a range of natural language processing tasks, and LLM inference has emerged as a critical workload for enabling downstream applications. The demands of serving LLM inference are becoming increasingly challenging as requests shift toward longer sequences and heavier inference, driven by retrieval-augmented generation, inference-time compute scaling, and long-context applications. Additionally, these challenges are compounded by hardware trends, as memory capacity and communication bandwidth are not scaling as fast as increases in workload complexity. Compute-in-Flash is a promising solution to address memory bandwidth limitations by moving computation close to memory, and to exploit the large capacity of SSD technologies. However, it is challenging to deploy LLMs on these systems as they lack support for high-precision floating point operations and have limited write endurance. In our work, we aim to address these challenges by designing inference algorithms to enable LLM inference on Flash compute-in-memory devices. We present an end-to-end integer-only quantization approach to eliminate expensive floating-point computations. To address the limited write endurance, we design a dictionary-based KV cache compression strategy based on sparse dictionary coding that represents each KV vector as a linear combination of static dictionary vectors. These algorithmic improvements enable us to exploit the benefits of Compute-in-Flash for both model weights and KV cache, and to minimize expensive data transfer operations. Across Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen-2.5-7B, our combined method exhibits limited accuracy degradation while reducing dynamic KV cache traffic by 15$\times$.

</details>


### [273] [Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16170)
*Jordan Eckert,Henry Schenck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPINE（Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions），一种新型原型生成方法。该方法将每个类别建模为嵌入的1-复形（由顶点和连接线段组成的骨架网络），而非传统的点集。初始边集来自类别条件下的Mapper图，由数据决定哪些局部簇被连接，后续阶段在分类目标下拟合顶点。在17个基准数据集上的10折交叉验证中，SPINE在匹配预算下达到了最高的平均准确率（0.9110）和最佳平均排名（2.44），在七个竞争方法中的五个上具有统计显著性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的原型缩减方法都返回有限的点集，使用最近原型规则将特征空间划分为Voronoi单元。当用点表示连续区域时，所需点数随区域大小增长而非随问题难度增长。作者希望引入一种能同时携带边和顶点的模型，因为一条边代表连接两个端点的线段，可以用两个存储点覆盖连续区域的位置。此外，作者指出拓扑数据分析（TDA）工具与监督原型缩减领域长期分离，而邻近图编辑规则和增长神经气体的诱导连通性已表明类的连通性对缩减具有有用信息。

Method: SPINE将每个类建模为嵌入的1-复形 S_c = (V_c, E_c)，由Mapper构造确定初始边集，后续阶段在分类目标下拟合顶点。方法分为五个阶段：
- **Phase 0（初始Mapper结构）**：对每个类独立构建Mapper图，透镜为类内第一主成分（PC1），区间数遵循 ℓ_c = ⌈(8n_c)^{1/5}⌉ 的启发式，增益固定为 g=0.25，区间内点使用HDBSCAN聚类（参数 κ 和 λ 全局固定）。
- **Phase 1（退火表示）**：使用类似SOM的竞争学习更新顶点位置，但格点是神经图本身而非固定网格，邻域权重按图上的跳数距离计算，参数按几何级数衰减。
- **Phase 2a（边筛选）**：以验证风险为准则，贪心地删除那些不能严格降低验证风险的边。严格性至关重要，因为宽松的规则可能因数值偶然剥离边。
- **Phase 2b（判别拟合）**：使用GLVQ家族的相对距离裕度，通过分段复合求导更新顶点位置，使用平方距离的预条件以保证步长不随拟合改善而发散。
- **Phase 3（归因驱动增长）**：通过在持有最多归因错误的部位二分边或延伸节点来增加顶点，每个类超过预算直到 ⌈1.2 M_c⌉ 个顶点。
- **Phase 4（剪枝至预算）**：通过验证风险贪心地删除顶点，仅允许反向执行Phase 3的两种操作（同胚保持删除），强制删除只在特殊配置下发生。
决策规则为观测被分配给实现 |S_c| 距离最小的类，即线段本身参与决策而非仅用于拟合。

Result: 在17个基准数据集（涵盖178到20,000个观测、2到60个特征、2到26个类）上进行分层10折交叉验证，与7种原型缩减方法（Random、SPOT、RSP3、KMeans、LVQ3、GLVQ、GNG）对比：
- SPINE获得最高平均准确率0.9110，优于LVQ3（0.9015）和RSP3（0.8915），最佳平均排名2.44。
- Friedman检验拒绝八种方法等价的假设（χ²=47.54, p=4.4×10⁻⁸）。
- 在Holm校正的Wilcoxon符号秩检验下，SPINE显著优于Random、GLVQ、SPOT、RSP3和KMeans五种方法，对LVQ3和GNG未达显著性。
- 在12个数据集上超过使用完整训练集的Full参考，在4个上落后。
- 优势集中在二分类数据集（平均排名1.70），多类数据集上较弱（平均排名3.50）。
- 关于图线段的贡献：在匹配预算下差异小且不显著，但预算扫描显示，使用完整图的增益从25%预算时的0.0009单调增长到0.5%预算时的0.0056。
- 总体而言，SPINE在25%预算下领先，在0.5%预算下排名第五；建议在中等预算下使用。
- 构建成本：SPINE与GLVQ的幂律标度指数相近（1.99 vs 1.95），在14/17个数据集上比GLVQ快，与GNG的成本比随训练集大小上升。

Conclusion: SPINE是首个将Mapper构造用于监督原型缩减的方法，通过用嵌入式1-复形替代点集原型，使线段本身参与决策规则。该方法在匹配预算下取得了最佳平均准确率和排名，统计上显著优于5/7个竞争方法。线段的作用在低预算时最显著（符合覆盖论证），但整体方法在中等预算下表现最佳。局限性包括：所有超参数（透镜、聚类器、接受准则、步长规则、边界限制）均固定为先验启发式而非通过交叉验证选择，因此敏感性未测试；多类问题表现较弱因各类独立构建，固定预算随类数增多而被细分；Phase 0的覆盖在预算已知前固定，低预算时导致必须进行非同胚删除。未来工作方向包括：建立参数的理论依据、研究类别间相互信息的扩展、采用连续位点评分消除表示误差回退、以及使Phase 0预算感知。

Abstract: Prototype reduction replaces a training set with a smaller representation, and the established methods return a finite set of points. We propose Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions (SPINE). The model for each class is an embedded 1-complex rather than a point set. Its initial edge set is a class-conditional Mapper graph, so the data decide which localized clusters are joined. Later phases fit the vertices under a classification objective, and an observation is assigned to the class whose complex is nearest. The segments therefore enter the decision rule and not only the fitting. We evaluate SPINE on seventeen benchmark datasets under stratified 10-fold cross validation, against seven other prototype reduction methods at a matched budget. SPINE attains the highest mean accuracy and the best average rank. It is significantly better than five of the seven competitors under Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. A budget sweep shows that the decision rule using the entire graph segments contribute most when prototypes are scarce, while the method as a whole competes best at moderate budgets. Construction cost places SPINE with the discriminative methods, and it is faster than generalized learning vector quantization on fourteen of the seventeen datasets.

</details>


### [274] [Z-Loss Backward Geometry in Dense Output Heads and Sparse Routers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16179)
*Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从反向传播（backward-pass）视角重新审视 Z-loss，将其视为通过架构和实现路径传输的 logit 梯度"源"（backward source），而非仅作为标量惩罚。提出了 backward-transport 框架，分离标量源幅度与 softmax 形状，并分析了公移位（common shift）、权重绑定路径、输出到隐藏层增益（gain）、融合损失一致性、top-k 路由器缩减尺度等传输因子。实验表明前向 Z-loss 值相同的配置可能产生不同的参数更新，架构感知变体能在保持验证困惑度的同时降低后向几何尾部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将 Z-loss 仅理解为对 softmax log-normalizer 的标量惩罚。然而，在现代 Transformer 训练中（大词汇表 softmax、top-k 路由、融合损失、混合精度优化器交互），标量前向值相同并不保证优化器接收到的参数更新相同。亟需一个框架来区分标量尾部控制与实际传输的更新尾部控制。

Method: 提出 backward-transport 框架：(1) 定义 Z-loss logit 梯度 δ_z^Z = 2λ(log Z - c)p 作为"反向源"；(2) 分离源构造（标量幅度 × softmax 形状）与架构传输（logit Jacobian J_θ^⊤）；(3) 推导传输因子：公移位敏感度、权重绑定梯度分解、输出到隐藏层增益（各向异性加权）、融合源一致性、top-k 路由器有效尺度；(4) 提出诊断度量（p99|μ|、P_Z^{99.9}、A_p^{99}、Δ_src、Adam 状态端点等）和架构感知干预（centering、factorized、gain-aware、scale-matched）。实验涵盖 GPT-2 和 Pythia 系列在 WikiText-103 和 FineWeb-Edu 上的预训练诊断、匹配持续预训练、融合内核审计、应力端点和端到端 MoE 训练。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 相同前向 Z-loss 值可对应截然不同的 logit 空间梯度和参数更新；(2) raw-logit Z-loss 可降低标量尾部但不改变输出到隐藏层增益 A_p^{99}；(3) 路由 top-k 缩减改变有效路由器系数（active-route mean 尺度 1/k，active-route sum 尺度 k）；(4) 权重绑定使输出侧正则器变成共享词表的多路径耦合更新（GPT-2 上 Z-loss 梯度对绑定表的范数约为 CE 梯度的 3.5%，与 CE 余弦仅 0.022，几乎正交）；(5) 前向标量一致（相对误差 ~10^-4）时后向源误差可达 0.065-0.183，导致 Adam 方向误差 12-26%；(6) 应力设置（λ=10^-3）中原始 logit 变体使 p99 梯度范数增 5-8 倍，Adam 二阶矩增 20-42 倍，fp16 损失尺度安全余量从 6718 降至 731；(7) 中心化变体（Centered Z-loss）能同时降低 P_Z^{99.9} 和 A_p^{99}，且不引入更新离群值；(8) GPT-2 Medium 上 PPL 改善 -0.268，Pythia-160M 上原始 Z-loss 反而使 PPL 恶化 9.2 点。

Conclusion: Z-loss 应被理解为通过架构和实现传输的 logit 梯度源，而非仅是标量对数归一化惩罚。降低原始 logit 尾部并不等于降低优化器面向的更新尾部。中心化、分解、增益感知加权和路由器尺度匹配针对不同的传输坐标，并非可互换地降低同一标量的方法。建议采用基于诊断的决策层级（先识别激活的源/传输坐标，再针对性干预），并将验证质量与传输诊断联合报告。实践上，低系数区域可保持 PPL 但暴露验证质量与更新尾部/混合精度余量之间的权衡。

Abstract: Z-loss has been widely applied to the logits of language-model output heads and sparse mixture-of-experts routers. Z-loss constrains the softmax log-normalizers of these output heads and routers, thereby limiting large-logit excursions, reducing finite-precision roundoff exposure, and avoiding training-loss divergence. These use cases arise in modern Transformer settings where large-vocabulary softmax heads, top-$k$ routing, fused losses, and mixed-precision optimizers interact. Z-loss has typically been understood only as a scalar penalty on the log-normalizer. This paper instead analyzes Z-loss from a backward-pass perspective, focusing on the gradients produced by the Z-loss penalty. The logit-space gradient, which we call the backward source, is injected at the logit boundary of the Z-loss branch of backpropagation; consequently, the backward source's effect depends on the architecture and implementation through which the gradient is transported. We develop a backward-transport view for Z-loss that separates the source's scalar amplitude and softmax shape from the transport factors. These factors include common-shift coordinates, tied-embedding pathways, output-to-hidden gain, fused-loss source consistency, optimizer-facing updates, and top-$k$ router reduction scale. These diagnostics show that nearly identical forward Z-loss values can coexist with distinct logit-space Z-loss gradients and, after architectural and optimizer transport, distinct parameter updates. The transport diagnostics also explain why raw-logit Z-loss can reduce scalar tails without changing output-to-hidden gain and why active-route reductions alter the effective router coefficient. Across evaluations of models in the GPT-2 and Pythia families on WikiText-103 and FineWeb-Edu, architecture-aware variants reduce backward-geometry tails while maintaining comparable validation perplexity in low-coefficient regimes.

</details>


### [275] [Anatomy of Associative Recall in Fixed-State Recurrences: A Matched-State Decomposition, an Interference Wall, and a Curriculum That Breaks It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16183)
*Julian Boesch,Andrew Wee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对固定状态循环模型（线性注意力与状态空间模型）在关联记忆任务上的表现进行了系统性、可控的分解研究，揭示了短卷积是主导因素、秩1转移的真实贡献、条件性的干扰墙，以及通过课程学习打破该墙的机制，并证明这些"武装"改进不损害状态跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定状态循环模型被广泛报告在关联记忆任务上落后于注意力机制，但整体架构比较无法识别具体哪个组件（短卷积、转移结构、衰减）导致了这一差距。此外，长上下文检索中的失败模式常被归因于容量或衰减，但未得到受控分解的验证。需要一个在固定状态预算下逐旋钮切换的实验框架来解构这些声称。

Method: 采用匹配状态预算（1024个状态元素）的因子设计，沿三个单旋钮轴逐一切换：短因果卷积（width-4深度可分离）、转移结构（秩1 delta规则 vs 对角线）、以及衰减（标量门控）。所有实验在统一的纯PyTorch参考实现中运行，配合状态匹配的纯PyTorch Mamba-2对照和原子无关的Triton反向传播，在受限状态（d=32）和充足状态（d=128）下分别测试。统计协议采用10-20颗种子、置换检验、重绑定控制（评估模型是否真正绑定而非值显著性），以及二项分布的锁定率与Fisher精确检验。任务包括掩码多查询关联记忆（MQAR）、干扰堆检索（4对+干扰物）、碰撞键变体（干扰物重用表键），以及S5状态跟踪护栏。

Result: 1. 短卷积是跨族可迁移的主导杠杆：在delta族中带来+0.47，在对角线族中带来+0.44，匹配状态下均为最大单杠杆；2. 秩1转移在卷积存在时贡献仅+0.034（p=0.0023），与状态匹配的Mamba-2打成平手（p=0.88），因此"秩1类优于对角线类"的声称不成立；3. 衰减的表观成本在20颗种子下消失（p=0.86），表现为锁定率二项分布而非均值效应；4. 干扰墙是训练覆盖差距而非容量限制：同一架构在朴素训练下达到概率水平（0.021），通过距离课程学习后达到1.000；5. 训练在此类干扰任务上是"锁定彩票"：课程学习将锁定率从1/10提升到7/10（p=0.02）；6. 长度边界L=256时均匀课程完全失败（0/9），但形状化渐变课程可锁定4/5（p≈0.005）；L=512时基于时间的渐变失败（0/6），但成功门控的渐变锁定6/6（p=0.0011）；7. 双向去噪器的查询优先读取属性在10颗种子下未显示出对因果训练的可测量优势（p=0.63）；8. 武装后的单元在S5状态跟踪的所有深度上显著优于未武装版本（p≤0.0044）。

Conclusion: 论文将"循环模型在记忆任务上表现差"的笼统判断分解为五个可测量的成分：缺失的卷积（可免费修复）、转移效应（单元内真实存在）、衰减税（标量门控特定）、稀疏监督下的干扰失败（可通过课程学习在长度边界内修复）、以及方向性属性（双向去噪器原生具备查询优先读取）。核心猜想是：在固定状态循环的容量范围内，表面的检索墙实际上是训练覆盖差距。双向循环去噪器在扩散语言模型中具有原则性的记忆优势——其目标本身提供了第二次读取——但需要至少两层和短卷积。武装这些改进在状态跟踪上无成本甚至有益。整体而言，这些结果将注意力-循环模型的混合决策从架构选择转变为在武装和课程学习之后的更小、更精细的设计选择。

Abstract: Fixed-state recurrences--linear attention and state-space models--are reported to lag behind attention on associative recall, but whole-architecture comparisons cannot say which ingredient is responsible. We decompose masked multi-query recall at a fixed state budget along three single-knob axes: a short causal convolution, the transition structure (rank-1 delta rule vs. diagonal), and decay. The convolution dominates (~+0.5 recall in both families under matched training): comparisons that pit convolution-free cells against a convolution-equipped Mamba measure the missing convolution, not the recurrence. The rank-1 transition beats its diagonal ablation by +0.19/+0.32 at 16/32 pairs, but the margin shrinks to +0.03 once both cells carry the convolution, and a state-matched Mamba-2 ties the unarmed rank-1 cell: no class claim survives. Cells that solve 32-pair recall degrade gracefully with load yet fall to chance retrieving 4 pairs from a distractor haystack--flat across lengths and transitions. Interference under sparse supervision, not capacity: a distance curriculum takes the unchanged architecture from 0.021 to 1.000. Training is a lock-in lottery--a seed either locks in or does not--and the curriculum is the lever. Lock-in rises from 1/10 to 7/10 (p=0.02); dense supervision adds nothing; at L=256 a shaped ramp reopens a boundary the uniform curriculum cannot (4/5 vs. 0/9); and at L=512, where the ramp collapses (0/6), gating it on measured accuracy locks in 6/6 (p=0.001). Bidirectional denoiser cells, reading the query before the haystack, show no measurable advantage over causal training (ten seeds), and collision-key retrieval needs two layers. Arming for recall is free on an S_5 state-tracking guardrail--the armed cell is significantly better at every depth (p<=0.0044). These replace "recurrent models are bad at recall" with a measured decomposition and two cheap interventions.

</details>


### [276] [Decoy Direction Optimization: A Post-Hoc Defense Against LLM Abliteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16204)
*Aashiq Muhamed,Mona T. Diab,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DDO（Decoy Direction Optimization），一种针对LLM拒绝特征消融（RFA）攻击的事后权重编辑防御方法，通过在MLP神经元中注入与拒绝方向正交的高幅度非线性诱饵信号，污染攻击者的对比估计器，使其消融无害特征而非真实的安全机制。DDO无需对基础模型微调，在六大家族模型上将标准RFA的ASR降至10%以下，成本比训练型防御低30–450倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重语言模型的安全护栏可被Refusal Feature Ablation (RFA) 轻易绕过。现有的训练型防御方法（如Circuit Breakers、ReFAT、RepBend）需要针对每个checkpoint进行昂贵的微调，无法跟上开放权重模型的快速发布节奏。需要一种快速、低成本、可组合的后期硬化工具。

Method: DDO通过重编程少量低影响力SwiGLU/GeGLU神经元实现门控读写映射：(1) 在神经元中写入与拒绝方向对齐的触发器（触发条件）；(2) 在神经元中写入与拒绝方向正交的诱饵输出方向。SwiGLU的门控机制silu(βa)·a使得诱饵响应在有害提示上呈非线性放大，从而在攻击者的对比估计中引入诱饵主导的偏差。理论方面证明了子空间重叠界（定理1、2），形式化了攻击者-拒绝方向重叠与对比矩阵之间的关系；推论3给出固定能量预算下的最优谱分配原则。优化采用四部分损失：拒绝保持、良性保留、估计器混淆、首token锚定。

Result: 在六个模型家族（Llama-3、Yi-1.5、Qwen3、Gemma-2、Mistral、GLM-4）上将标准RFA的ASR降至10%以下。在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上：标准RFA ASR从85.1%降至1.8%（加去偏后1.0%）；Heretic权重级攻击ASR从88.7%降至18%；自适应多阶段攻击最坏情况ASR为65%（与训练型防御RepBend的58%相当）。在六种其他模型上均实现<10%标准RFA ASR。优化成本约2分钟/单A100 GPU，比训练型基线低30–450倍。保留MT-Bench ≥5.82的连贯生成能力。

Conclusion: DDO证明了机制性替代方案的可行性——重新利用MLP神经元破坏攻击者的对比估计器——可以在标准RFA上匹配训练型基线效果，成本大幅降低。核心洞见在于对比攻击的有效性取决于其估计的对比度：通过注入大幅度的、有害选择性、拒绝方向正交的诱饵信号，DDO迫使攻击者消融诱饵而非真实拒绝机制。局限性包括：持续自适应重估计仍是挑战；某些模型出现过拒绝；权重级攻击（Heretic）上仍弱于RepBend。

Abstract: Safety guardrails in open-weight language models can be readily bypassed using Refusal Feature Ablation (RFA), a technique that identifies and projects out a linear refusal direction from the residual stream, often achieving a high attack success rate (ASR) while preserving model capability. Defending against these attacks typically requires computationally expensive safety finetuning for every new checkpoint. We introduce Decoy Direction Optimization (DDO), a fast, post-hoc weight-editing defense that requires no base-model finetuning. Our approach is based on a simple mechanistic insight: ablation attacks rely on contrastive estimators to find the refusal direction. Rather than trying to hide the true refusal circuitry, DDO actively injects a high-magnitude, nonlinear decoy signal into the network's MLP neurons. When an attacker attempts to locate the refusal direction, the decoy corrupts their estimator, tricking them into ablating a harmless orthogonal feature while the actual safety mechanism remains intact. We prove a spectral bound formalizing this effect and evaluate DDO across six model families, achieving <10% ASR under standard RFA. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, DDO remains comparable to trained defenses under adaptive multi-phase attacks (65% vs. 58% worst-case ASR) and reduces Heretic weight-level attack ASR from 88.7% to 18%, all at 30 to 450 times lower optimization cost per configuration than the trained baselines.

</details>


### [277] [How I learned to stop worrying and love StopGrads: Stationarity, Convergence, and a case study on Flow Map Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16222)
*Max W. Shen,Mark Goldstein,Zichu Wang,Aahlad Puli,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为使用 stopgrad 的训练目标建立了基于变分法的理论分析框架，提出"stopgrad 回归原理"统一刻画了流映射、RL、扩散采样器等目标的驻点，并证明 MeanFlow/Improved MeanFlow/slim ESD/slim LSD 的真实流映射是唯一驻点且半梯度流指数收敛，最后提出 slim stopgrad 减半内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stopgrad 在实践中广泛使用，但缺乏统一的理论理解：它产生的 semi-gradient 不是原目标的梯度，可能非保守且不收敛，导致 stopgrad 训练缺乏理论基础。先前工作中（如 MeanFlow、ESD、LSD）即使使用 stopgrad 也没有分析驻点与收敛性。

Method: (1) 通过变分法形式化 stopgrad 操作符，给出 functional semi-gradient 的定义；(2) 提出 Stopgrad Regression Principle（Theorem 1），对一类 stopgrad 目标给出驻点 = T[f, E[y|x]] 不动点的充要刻画，并给出加性标签噪声下唯一性条件（Corollary 1）；(3) 对 MeanFlow、IMF、ESD、LSD 应用定理，证明真实流映射是唯一驻点；(4) 证明这些 semi-gradient 不是任何标量函数的梯度（Corollary 6）；(5) 提出 slim stopgrad 放置（slim ESD/slim LSD），消除二阶导数；(6) 在 functional semi-gradient flow 下，证明 Fτ 是 F0 与真实流映射 F* 的闭式复合，给出指数收敛速度。

Result: 理论结果：(a) MeanFlow/IMF/slim ESD 的真实流映射是唯一驻点，且非加性标签噪声情况下仍可证明唯一性（归约到 PDE/Lemma 2）；(b) MeanFlow 不带 stopgrad 时真实流映射不是驻点（Proposition 4，高斯情形显式验证）；(d) functional semi-gradient flow 给出 Fτ(t,u,x) = F0(s_{t→u}^τ, u, F*(t, s_{t→u}^τ, x))（Euler）或 = F*(s_{u→t}^τ, u, F0(t, s_{u→t}^τ, x))（Lagrangian），其中 s 为"交接时间"指数收敛于 t 或 u；(e) 数值验证 220M 点上 Pearson 相关系数 1.000000；(f) slim ESD/LSD 训练减少内存约 2×，CIFAR-10 slim ESD 在 FID-1/10/50/100 上均优于原始 ESD sg，slim LSD 与 LSD orig sg 相当但内存更优。

Conclusion: 本文为 stopgrad 优化提供了坚实的理论根基：(1) Stopgrad 回归原理统一刻画了广泛 stopgrad 目标的驻点，使设计/分析新 stopgrad 目标成为"套模板"；(2) 验证了 MeanFlow 等流映射训练中 stopgrad 是必要的（移除则真实流映射不再是驻点）；(3) 尽管 semi-gradient 场非保守，半梯度流仍能（指数地）收敛到唯一驻点；(4) slim stopgrad 变体同时具备"已知驻点"与"无二阶导数"两个优势且内存减半，可直接应用于实践中。未来工作：物理信息神经网络 (PINN) 等 PDE 损失也具有类似结构，可应用该原理设计 stopgrad 放置。

Abstract: Stopgrads are widely used in training machine learning models, but stopgrads can alter the gradient, stationary points and convergence guarantees of the original objective, which can make stopgrad training theoretically ungrounded. We introduce a stopgrad regression principle, which identifies a general template for stopgrad objectives with a closed-form characterization of stationary points and their uniqueness, unifying stopgrad objectives for flow maps, reinforcement learning, and diffusion samplers. We provide theoretical grounding for optimizing stopgrad flow map objectives by showing their unique stationary point is the true flow map, and showing positive convergence results for Eulerian and Lagrangian objectives, including MeanFlow and improved MeanFlow. Remarkably, we show that under functional semi-gradient flow, the learned flow map has a closed-form expression composing the initial flow map and the true flow map. We additionally use our stopgrad regression principle to propose modified stopgrad placements for flow map objectives which reduce training memory by 2x.

</details>


### [278] [Test-Time Unlearning via Sparse Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16229)
*Pingzhi Li,Jinhao Duan,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Nakul Agarwal,Kwonjoon Lee,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Sijia Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ARIA方法，将LLM遗忘重构为基于稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征的检测器门控推理时干预，冻结模型权重，在遗忘相关生成状态时选择性抑制目标知识，实现了更好的遗忘-保留权衡并对攻击具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于权重修改的LLM遗忘方法存在严重的遗忘-效用权衡问题：更强的遗忘会降低模型通用能力，且遗忘的知识可能在微调或提示攻击下恢复。需要一种不修改权重即可实现选择性遗忘的方法。

Method: ARIA (Autoencoder-gated Inference-time Unlearning)：1) 在通用语料上预训练逐层稀疏自编码器(SAE)并冻结；2) 用SAE潜变量在遗忘/保留轨迹上训练稀疏线性逻辑回归检测器头部，特征包括激活频率、最大激活和早期窗口最大激活；3) 推理时，检测器作为门控：当分数超过阈值时输出"我不知道"，否则使用冻结的原始模型分布；4) 通过融合CUDA核实现在线统计更新，几乎无额外开销。理论上证明遗忘泄漏和保留退化分别受检测器假阴性和假阳性率约束。

Result: 在TOFU、R-TOFU和WMDP三个基准上，使用DeepSeek-R1-Distilled-Qwen-1.5B和Gemma-3-1B-it两个模型，ARIA实现了WMDP-cyber遗忘集准确率降至0.00%，MMLU仅下降0.50-1.36%；R-TOFU上AFE和CFE均超过97%。在三种后遗忘对抗攻击（重复采样、提示攻击、持续SFT）下遗忘变化小于1%。特征级案例研究发现部分保留退化源于遗忘数据中的回答风格偏差（如"正确答案是"等开场模板），而非目标知识本身。

Conclusion: ARIA将遗忘重新定义为冻结模型上的检测器门控访问控制而非全局权重编辑，通过结构性地解耦遗忘与保留，实现了更强的遗忘效果、对攻击的鲁棒性和更好的保留效用，且无需重训练或重新部署。特征级可解释性还揭示了标准遗忘基准中可能存在风格偏差问题，为未来遗忘任务构建提出了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove specific knowledge from a trained large language model (LLM) without retraining from scratch. Existing methods modify model weights via gradient ascent and its advances. While effective on certain benchmarks, these weight-based approaches exhibit a sharp forget-utility trade-off, where stronger forgetting of target knowledge can degrade model utility, and unlearned knowledge may reappear under post-unlearning fine-tuning or prompt attacks. We propose ARIA (autoencoder-gated inference-time unlearning), a test-time unlearning method that leaves model weights intact and gates access to unwanted knowledge only when generation enters a forget-related state. ARIA uses sparse autoencoder (SAE) latents to train a lightweight linear detector, then applies an interpretable intervention on triggered states with negligible test-time overhead. Empirical evaluations on TOFU, R-TOFU, and WMDP show that ARIA improves the forget-retain trade-off over weight-based baselines across both a thinking model (DeepSeek-R1-Distilled-Qwen-1.5B) and an instruction model (Gemma-3-1B-it), e.g., reducing WMDP-cyber forget-set accuracy significantly while keeping MMLU within 1% of the pre-unlearning model. We further introduce three post-unlearning adversarial attacks targeting weight-space and decoding-space recovery, and find that ARIA remains robust under all three, with forgetting changing by less than 1% under attack. A feature-level case study leveraging the interpretability of ARIA suggests that some retain degradation may reflect response styles underlying the unlearning data rather than leakage of the targeted knowledge itself, highlighting a potential source of bias in unlearning task construction.

</details>


### [279] [The record is part of the task: matched-record evaluation of text classifiers across maintenance, safety and recall reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16267)
*Hisham Ihshaish,Peter Mayhew,Tasnim M. A. Zayet,Ana Del Amo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"匹配记录评估"(matched-record evaluation)设计，主张文本分类器的评估必须将记录(同一案例在不同工作流阶段产生的不同文本)的选择视为评估的一部分。基于GE Aerospace维修事件、NASA ASRS安全报告和NHTSA车辆召回三个系统，证明记录差异对性能的影响远大于模型架构或表示差异。GE数据显示客户报告与技术员报告之间的macro-F1差距达0.456，而最大模型差异仅0.092。ASRS与NHTSA显示出不同的记录依赖模式，部分模型比较结论随记录变化而反转。结论：评估应在目标决策点的可用信息上运行，并报告记录与标签的产生方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本分类器评估通常在工作流开始前选择一个记录，而同一案例在维修、安全、召回等流程中会由不同作者在不同阶段产生多个记录(例如客户报告、技术员报告、维修记录)。这些记录包含不同时间点存在的信息，对应不同的决策点。已有研究(尤其是临床NLP)表明文档背景会调节模型行为，但缺乏在同一案例的多记录上固定标签与数据划分的系统性比较。论文旨在验证记录选择本身是否比模型架构选择对分类性能的影响更大。

Method: 设计匹配记录评估框架：(1) 对同一案例的多个记录(v₁…v_V)固定案例、标签与评估划分，分别训练评估；(2) 定义两个估计量：记录对比Δ_R(同一流程下不同记录的性能差)与模型对比Δ_M(同一记录下不同模型的性能差)，以及交互项D衡量模型比较是否随记录变化；(3) 引入"决策时刻可用性"概念X_i^(v) = g_v(H_i(τ_v))，定义可接受记录集𝒜_i(τ)；(4) 在三个系统上实验：GE Aerospace航电LRU维修(5792事件，4类装配替换标签，独立于叙述)、NASA ASRS航空安全报告(二元主要问题，分析员编写的摘要与标签共生产)、NHTSA车辆召回(16类部件分类，监管者编码)；(5) 模型族涵盖TF-IDF基线、BiLSTM、Avi2Vec领域嵌入、RoBERTa预训练Transformer；(6) 配对自举法推断+Holm校正的近似随机化检验。

Result: GE数据：三个字段的macro-F1为客户报告0.327、技术员报告0.783、维修行动0.910；客户→技术员Δ_R = +0.456 [0.419, 0.492]，技术员→维修行动Δ_R = +0.127，最大模型差异仅0.092。Avi2Vec超过GloVe-200仅0.049。ASRS：分析员摘录在所有学习序列模型上优于报告者叙述+0.015 [0.011, 0.019]，但在词法基线下不存在；TF-IDF与BiLSTM的排序在叙述与摘录之间反转。NHTSA：缺陷摘要始终最强，超过后果字段+0.115，超过补救字段+0.148；类别词表掩码与时间外评估保持排序不变。模型对比的交互项D在ASRS上+0.017，表明模型比较本身具有记录依赖性。审计显示GE技术员文本误分类中126/168(约75%)为叙述-交易不一致而非模型错误。

Conclusion: 记录选择是评估的一部分，其对分类性能的影响可远超模型架构或表示选择。评估应在目标决策点可访问的记录上运行，并显式记录记录与标签的产生过程(谁写、何时写、为何写)。这一原则对维修分诊等决策点明确的应用具有直接工程意义：改进信息捕获比更换模型更可能提升性能。该设计适用于任何案例被多次文档化且标签产生过程可追溯的场景。未来工作应包括前瞻性信息捕获干预以分离因果因素，以及跨更多LRU族与维护系统的复制验证。

Abstract: Many operational cases are documented more than once, at different workflow stages and for different purposes, yet model evaluations normally select one of these records before model comparison begins. We treat that selection as part of the evaluation and compare matched records of the same cases under fixed labels and splits in three systems: GE Aerospace repair events, NASA ASRS safety reports and NHTSA vehicle recalls. Across the three GE fields, for events whose label comes from parts transactions independently of the narratives, held-out macro-F1 ranged from 0.33 to 0.91. A difference of 0.46 separated the customer report, written before shop work, from the technician report, written after diagnosis but before the transaction that generates the label. That difference is substantially larger than the representation and architecture differences tested on the same events. The public systems showed different patterns: the NHTSA defect summary remained strongest under every model family tested, whereas the ASRS analyst synopsis outperformed the reporter narrative under learned sequence models but not under lexical baselines. Secondary analyses showed that some model comparisons were also record-dependent. Evaluations should be run on the information available at the intended decision point and should report how both the record and the label were produced.

</details>


### [280] [Scaling Laws for Physics-Aware ACOPF Surrogate Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16282)
*Yijiang Li,Emon Dey,Stefano Fenu,Massimiliano Lupo Pasini,Teja Kuruganti,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了基于学习的AC潮流(ACOPF)代理模型在不同训练目标下的标度律(scaling laws),比较了纯MSE回归与物理感知增强拉格朗日(AL)目标在模型容量、数据集规模和网络物理规模三个维度上的表现差异。核心发现是:训练目标不仅决定代理模型的最终性能,更决定了其性能随规模提升的演化速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACOPF代理模型的价值取决于物理可行性而非仅预测精度。物理感知目标(如AL)能改善约束满足但增加每步计算开销,然而这一权衡在规模扩展时的行为特征尚未被刻画。对于在领导级HPC系统上训练基础模型的电力系统社区而言,缺乏经验性标度律指导资源分配决策。

Method: 采用Heterogeneous Graph Transformer (HGT)架构,在OPFData数据集上进行两组实验:(1)在case2000上对模型规模(100M-671M参数)和数据集规模(48K-240K样本)进行6×5网格扫描,分别用MSE和AL训练;(2)在跨越三个数量级的8个拓扑(case30到case10000)上以固定100M模型进行跨拓扑研究。在Frontier和Aurora上进行同平台对比,量化训练时间、内存占用和可行性收益。在对数空间中用幂律拟合单轴和联合标度关系。

Result: MSE联合标度指数为(α_N=0.949, β_D=0.596),主要受模型容量限制;AL复合分数标度指数为(α_N=0.284, β_D=0.292),容量与数据平衡。跨拓扑实验中,约束违反随网络规模增长的指数为:MSE下k=1.196,AL下k=0.513,违反增长速率AL约为MSE的一半。匹配预测损失时,AL将归一化约束违反降低近30倍,代价是训练时间增加约一个数量级,而内存差异可忽略(约30 MiB)。分解显示AL对有功平衡改善最大(Δk=0.87),对线路限额改善最小。

Conclusion: 训练目标本身即是一项标度决策,影响代理模型质量随规模演化的速率。MSE和准确性提升不会等比例改善约束可行性。网络物理规模是区别于模型/数据的独立标度轴,物理感知训练对大网络和广覆盖范围的基础模型价值最大。AL的计算开销主要落在训练时间而非内存上,前者可被离线训练摊销。该研究为电网基础模型在固定预算下的设计决策提供了经验依据。

Abstract: Learning-based surrogates for AC optimal power flow (ACOPF) promise large speedups over classical solvers, but their operational value depends on physical feasibility as much as predictive accuracy. Physics-aware objectives such as the augmented Lagrangian (AL) improve constraint satisfaction at additional per-step cost, yet how this trade-off behaves with scale is uncharacterized. We sweep model and dataset sizes under both MSE and AL training, and characterize how constraint violation changes with network size across grids. Both objectives improve as power laws, but at different rates: MSE is governed primarily by model capacity, while AL is balanced across both. Violation grows roughly twice as fast with network size under MSE as under AL. On matched hardware, AL reduces violation by nearly $30\times$ for an order of magnitude more training time, with negligible added memory. The training objective determines not only where a surrogate lands but how its quality evolves with scale.

</details>


### [281] [Differentially Private Semantic Plans for Aggregate Insight Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16283)
*Behrooz Razeghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DP-SPIN 是一个可信策展人框架，在固定语义原子宇宙上发布差分隐私语义计划（包含已接纳原子和噪声质量），通过后处理生成自然语言摘要，从而对文本集合进行可比较的集合范围聚合洞察生成，并支持记录级和用户级差分隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端差分隐私聚合洞察方法（如 URANIA）侧重于数据相关聚类的描述，而非针对独立于语料库预定义的语义概念进行可比较的集合范围测量。CLIO 等启发式方法缺乏正式的差分隐私保证，而 CLIOPATRA 表明对抗性插入的对话可绕过此类保护。本文的动机是建立一个正式的差分隐私框架，用于在预定义语义概念上进行可比较的聚合测量，同时确保生成的摘要与已发布的隐私对象一致。

Method: 该方法分三个阶段：(1) 在可信策展人内部，将每条记录映射到固定原子宇宙上的 K-稀疏、L1-范数有界非负贡献向量，聚合为语义草图；(2) 通过全草图机制（高斯/拉普拉斯加 top-L 后处理）或稀疏机制（序贯指数机制接纳原子后释放噪声质量）发布差分隐私语义计划；(3) 解码器仅接收释放的语义计划和固定指令，公证验证器检查原子提及、数值声明、比较和排名声明的一致性。用户级隐私通过对每用户聚合贡献进行 L1 裁剪实现。

Result: 在 CFPB 投诉叙述、亚马逊 All Beauty 评论和 Yelp 餐厅评论上进行评估，使用不相交的辅助集与保护目标集分割。记录级和用户级实验表明：(1) 全草图机制在高斯和拉普拉斯噪声下均保留了大部分非私有 top-L 语义质量；(2) 稀疏私有接纳在较小隐私预算下质量损失更大但随 ε 增大改善；(3) 探测原子实验表明接纳率随支持度增加而提高；(4) 与非私有参考、DP 关键词/类别直方图基线和 URANIA 式基线相比，DP-SPIN 在 OpenAI 评分中保持竞争力且忠于释放计划。形式化隐私保证在记录级和用户级的加/删与替换邻接关系下均成立。

Conclusion: DP-SPIN 提供了对文本集合进行差分隐私聚合洞察生成的正式框架，通过固定语义原子宇宙上的结构化语义计划实现了隐私保护，同时通过计划一致的自然语言生成确保了与释放计划的一致性。实验证明了其在三个真实世界数据集上的计划效用和摘要一致性。

Abstract: \texttt{URANIA} provides end-to-end differential privacy (DP) for summaries of data-dependent clusters. However, its cluster--keyword release does not directly provide collection-wide aggregates for semantic concepts defined independently of the protected corpus. Records may express several concepts, records expressing the same concept may be assigned to different clusters, and cluster identities need not correspond across analyses. Consequently, cluster-level statistics do not directly provide comparable measurements of predefined concepts across collections or repeated analyses. We introduce \texttt{DP-SPIN}, a trusted-curator framework for aggregate measurement and summarization over semantic concepts fixed independently of the protected target records. Each record is mapped to a bounded sparse nonnegative vector over these concepts, whose sum forms a semantic sketch. A differentially private mechanism releases a semantic plan containing admitted concepts and noisy masses; normalized semantic-support values and support bins are obtained by post-processing. For user-level privacy, each user's aggregate contribution is clipped to a fixed bound. The language model receives only the plan and fixed decoding instructions, while a public verifier checks concept mentions, reported values, comparisons, and rank claims against the released plan. The final summary is differentially private by post-processing. We establish record- and user-level DP guarantees under add/drop and replacement adjacency. We evaluate \texttt{DP-SPIN} under record-level privacy on CFPB complaint narratives, Amazon All Beauty reviews, and Yelp restaurant reviews, and under user-level privacy on Amazon and Yelp. We compare \texttt{DP-SPIN} with non-private plan and summary references, DP keyword and category histogram baselines, and a \texttt{URANIA}-style baseline with a fixed public keyword vocabulary.

</details>


### [282] [Drift Field Net: Learning Ocean Lagrangian advection fields from in-situ and satellite observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16288)
*Théo Archambault,Pierre Garcia,Mattia Romero,Anastase Charantonis,Dominique Béréziat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Drift Field Net (DFN)，一种基于深度学习的海洋表面流场预测模型，通过卫星观测数据（SSH、SST、海面风）预测7天欧拉速度场，并采用新颖的两阶段训练策略（OSSE预训练+拉格朗日微调）优化粒子轨迹预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 北太平洋副热带环流（NPSG）是海洋漂浮垃圾的主要聚集区，准确预测拉格朗日粒子漂移对海洋清理策略至关重要。现有物理模型和直接预测粒子轨迹的深度学习方法存在计算成本高或难以处理欧拉-拉格朗日表示不匹配的问题。

Method: 1) 基于SimVP的ORCAst架构，编码SSH、SST和风场输入，输出7天速度场预测；2) 三阶段训练：GLORYS OSSE预训练→真实drifter数据的欧拉微调→基于可微分拉格朗日平流积分器的拉格朗日损失微调；3) 在OSSE和真实数据上分别评估，与业务化Mercator v3.6系统对比。

Result: 在in-situ drifter轨迹上，DFN将7天预测的平均定位误差降低约20 km（相对于Mercator v3.6），拉格朗日微调额外减少10 km误差。模型还能有效再现北太平洋垃圾带（GPGP）的汇聚区域，与ADIS遥感塑料密度观测吻合。频谱分析表明拉格朗日微调改善了方向预测和有效分辨率。

Conclusion: DFN通过预测欧拉流场而非直接预测粒子轨迹，实现了一次推理即可平流大量粒子的高效计算优势。物理约束的拉格朗日损失与深度学习结合，显著提升了业务化海洋预报能力，对海洋污染监测和清理具有实际意义。未来可扩展至全流域范围并引入风浪预报以提升风驱流预测。

Abstract: The North Pacific Subtropical Gyre (NPSG) is a major accumulation zone for floating plastic debris, resulting from basin-scale convergent ocean circulation. Effective cleanup strategies in this region rely on accurate forecasts of Lagrangian particle drift. Here, we introduce Drift Field Net (DFN), a deep neural network that predicts ocean surface flow fields from operational satellite observations. DFN is trained using a novel two-stage strategy that combines pretraining on simulated data with Lagrangian fine-tuning based on an advection-consistent loss function. This physics-informed optimization directly improves the accuracy of particle trajectory predictions. We evaluate DFN against an operational physics-based forecasting system and demonstrate the potential of deep learning for ocean surface flow prediction. On in situ drifter trajectories, DFN reduces the mean positioning error by 20 km after a 7-day forecast compared with the operational model. Furthermore, Lagrangian fine-tuning with the proposed advection loss further reduces the positioning error by 10 km, highlighting the benefits of incorporating Lagrangian constraints into the training process.

</details>


### [283] [Agentic Search Spaces for Tabular Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16309)
*Renat Sergazinov,Artem Chistyakov,Sergey Pankevich,Artem Babenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用LLM智能体为表格机器学习模型自动生成扩展HPO搜索空间的方法，通过将模型分解为模块化流水线，由智能体为每个模块生成候选实现，再用经典HPO算法联合优化，从而在45个数据集上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM智能体在规划、代码生成等方面取得快速进展，但其在表格机器学习中的实用价值仍未充分探索。现有表格模型通常附带固定的HPO搜索空间，而实践者需要手动测试新技术的组合，缺乏系统化的探索方法。论文旨在验证LLM智能体是否能自动扩展搜索空间，从而提升表格模型性能。

Method: 方法核心是将每个表格模型分解为模块化流水线（涵盖预处理、嵌入、架构、训练和推理），由LLM智能体为每个模块生成候选代码实现（如不同的优化器、嵌入方法等），这些模块作为分类变量加入HPO搜索空间，再由Optuna的TPE采样器进行联合优化。智能体仅针对模型运行一次（不接触目标数据），生成的成本可跨数据集分摊。论文还对比了端到端的autoresearch循环，发现其因探索效率低、上下文有限等问题表现不佳。

Result: 在45个数据集上，扩展搜索空间在几乎所有模型族上均有提升：DNN单模型平均相对增益0.5%，GBDT为0.8%，TabICLv2为0.5%；中小型回归数据集上增益可达2.0%。智能体RealMLP集成是所有模型族中最强的，仅次于多模型AutoGluon系统。在TabArena基准上，智能体空间在5个模型族中改善了4个的Elo分数，两个最强智能体集成超越了AutoGluon。所有增益在相同调优预算下实现，无需额外成本。

Conclusion: LLM智能体作为自动搜索空间设计器，在表格ML中具有实用价值。它无需替换专业模型或进行昂贵的特定数据集实验，即可将广泛的ML实现知识转化为可重用的、特定于模型的HPO空间。智能体提供的多样性通过集成策略显著放大，是性能提升的关键来源。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of LLM-based agents for planning, code generation, and debugging, their practical value for tabular machine learning remains underexplored. In this paper, we investigate a concrete use case: whether state-of-the-art agentic AI systems can design extended HPO search spaces for established tabular models that outperform the standard search spaces provided by the model authors. Specifically, we represent each tabular model as a modular pipeline covering preprocessing, embeddings, architecture, training, and inference. We then task the agent to propose candidate code implementations for each module and use a classical HPO algorithm to jointly optimize over these candidates and the model's default hyperparameters. Compared with the base HPO spaces, the expanded search spaces improve the performance of nearly every model family across a suite of 45 datasets, with average relative gains of 0.6%, rising to 2.0% on small-to-medium regression datasets. Notably, these gains come at no extra tuning cost: the enlarged spaces outperform the base under the same tuning and ensembling budgets. The gains transfer to the recent TabArena benchmark, where the agentic spaces improve the official Elo scores of four of the five model families and the two strongest agentic ensembles surpass the best AutoGluon ensemble of conventional models. Overall, our study suggests that LLM agents can provide practical value for tabular ML by expanding the design space.

</details>


### [284] [Robust Fault Detection in Mechanical Multimodal Time Series via Self-Supervised Cross-Modal Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16314)
*Magnus Munk Jensen,Dorte Hammershøi,Rafał Wiśniewski,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于跨模态重建的多模态无监督故障检测框架，结合自适应测试时阈值机制，在分布外条件下保持稳健的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业系统普遍配备异构传感器，但现有方法通常独立处理各模态或仅进行简单特征级融合，无法利用正常系统行为的跨模态关系；同时，实际部署中工况变化引发的分布偏移会显著降低检测性能。

Method: 多编码器-多解码器自编码器架构，每种模态由专用编码器处理，通过多头自注意力融合模块生成共享潜在表示，并采用三种重建损失（自重建、共享潜在重建、留一法跨模态重建）；推理阶段引入条件感知的自适应测试时阈值，采用门控指数加权更新策略追踪工况漂移。

Result: 在AMPERE、KAIST和IMAD-DS三个工业数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分布内条件下保持强检测性能，在严重的分布外工况下相比单模态和早期融合基线展现出显著更优的鲁棒性，尤其在最严苛的运行区间增益最大。

Conclusion: 跨模态重建策略通过迫使模型学习共享的潜在系统动力学而非模态特异性捷径，增强了对工况变化的鲁棒性；解耦表示学习与决策校准的条件感知阈值机制为真实工业部署提供了可靠的故障检测方案。

Abstract: Fault detection is essential in industrial systems, enabling early identification of abnormal behaviour and improving safety, reliability, and operational efficiency. Modern systems increasingly rely on heterogeneous sensing modalities that capture complementary aspects of the underlying physical process. However, existing data-driven anomaly detection methods often process each modality independently or use simple feature-level fusion, limiting their ability to exploit cross-modal relationships that characterize normal system behaviour. Their performance also commonly assumes similar training and deployment distributions, whereas real-world operation is affected by changing operating conditions, environmental influences, and system degradation that induce distribution shifts and reduce detection performance, especially in unseen regimes. In this work, we propose a multimodal anomaly detection framework based on cross-modal reconstruction of heterogeneous time-series sensor data. Rather than modeling each modality independently, the framework learns system dynamics by reconstructing each modality from the others, thereby exploiting complementary information across modalities. This integrates information across sensing channels without requiring explicit temporal alignment or identical sampling rates, while improving robustness to sensor noise, missing measurements, and modality-specific disturbances. To address distribution shifts during real-world deployment, anomalies are identified using cross-modal reconstruction error and an adaptive test-time thresholding mechanism that adjusts to changing operating conditions. Experiments on three industrial case studies show strong fault detection performance and substantially improved robustness under out-of-distribution conditions, with the largest gains observed in the most challenging operating regimes.

</details>


### [285] [Generative models for simulation based filtering: Formulations and Empirical Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16317)
*Mohammad Al-Jarrah,Wei Deng,Bamdad Hosseini,Amirhossein Taghvaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非线性滤波问题的统一生成模型框架，将分析步视为从预测分布到后验分布的传输映射，并基于此推导出三种新滤波器（随机插值滤波器SIF、流匹配滤波器FMF、薛定谔桥滤波器SBF）。通过两阶段调参程序，在二次观测模型和Lorenz-63系统上与OTF、KRF、SIR粒子滤波和EnKF进行精度、计算时间、集合大小和状态维度的系统对比。研究表明所有生成滤波器都能解析EnKF和SIR无法处理的多模态后验，但没有单一生成框架占优，最佳方法取决于在线预算和集合大小，且各滤波器产生的粒子轨迹规律性存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性滤波中后验分布往往非高斯且多模态，传统方法如EnKF（高斯假设）和SIR粒子滤波（高维退化）难以有效处理。现有耦合/传输型滤波器（OTF、KRF）虽能处理多模态，但缺乏在统一框架下对不同生成建模方法的受控比较，无法指导实践选择。

Method: 核心方法：统一采用三角传输映射G(y,z)=(y,T(y,z))，固定y分量传输z分量以实现条件化。三种新滤波器为：(1) FMF将传输实现为ODE流，学习向量场通过闭式回归；(2) SIF推广为含噪随机插值，可通过ODE或SDE推断，学习速度场和得分函数；(3) SBF通过前向-后向SDE最小化能量求解漂移，g→0时退化为OT映射。两阶段调参：第一阶段冷启动训练初始网络，第二阶段在线精调优化器超参数。误差度量采用轴对齐切片Wasserstein-2距离（aa-SW2）。

Result: 在二次观测模型（n=10, N=10⁴）和Lorenz-63上对比显示：(1) 所有生成滤波器均能恢复双模态后验，而EnKF和SIR出现模式崩溃或高斯近似失效，SBF除外；(2) 维度扫描中SIR误差快速退化，EnKF误差始终较高，生成滤波器误差随维度仅轻微增加；(3) 预算扫描中OTF从在线精调中受益最大（约2倍误差降低），SBF和KRF几乎无改善；(4) SIF-SDE在高预算下精度最高但计算成本高出EnKF/SIR约4个数量级；(5) OTF和KRF在小集合时表现更优；(6) 粒子轨迹方面，OTF和KRF保持模式时间持久性，而FMF/SIF/SBF的粒子轨迹频繁穿越。

Conclusion: FMF与SIF变体具有相近的精度-成本权衡，SIF-SDE随集合增大优势扩大；SBF因训练需SDE仿真而成本最高；OTF受对抗训练收敛限制，KRF受推理时逐坐标二分反演限制。跨族比较：OTF和SIF-SDE追踪相似的成本-精度曲线，OTF更适合小在线预算/小集合，SIF-SDE更适合大集合大预算。粒子轨迹规律性差异对下游控制与推断任务的影响有待未来研究。

Abstract: This letter presents a unified formulation and a controlled numerical comparison of generative-model approaches to the nonlinear filtering problem. Under this formulation the analysis step is realized by a transport of the forecast distribution to the posterior, the approaches differing only in how that transport is selected and learned. We derive three new filters, based on stochastic interpolants, their deterministic flow-matching limit, and Schrödinger bridges realized through forward--backward SDEs. We develop a two-stage tuning procedure that separates the training of the generative model from its online refinement. The resulting methods are compared against the optimal transport filter (OTF), the Knothe--Rosenblatt filter (KRF), the sequential importance resampling (SIR) particle filter and the ensemble Kalman filter (EnKF), in terms of accuracy, computational time, and sensitivity to ensemble size and state dimension. The results indicate that every generative filter resolves multimodal posteriors that the EnKF and SIR do not, that no single generative framework dominates, the preferred method being set by the available online budget and ensemble size, and that the filters differ in the regularity of the particle trajectories they produce.

</details>


### [286] [Channel-Informed Neural Network for Physical Layer Key Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16341)
*Jose Angel Sanchez Viloria,George Sklivanitis,Dimitris Pados,Elizabeth Serena Bentley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种信道感知的多任务循环神经网络框架,用于从原始IQ测量中生成物理层密钥。该框架通过深度度量学习与信道监督相结合,将学习到的密钥表示锚定于底层多径信道结构,并利用Sionna-RT射线追踪进行数据增强。在POWDER测试平台上的室内外SDR实测评估中,密钥多样性显著提升(NIST率0.94/0.99/0.99),但揭示了密钥多样性与协调可靠性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘网络中分布式资源受限设备需要轻量级、去中心化的密钥建立机制,而传统基于基础设施的密钥分发在间歇性连接场景下面临挑战。物理层密钥生成(PKG)利用无线信道互易性实现无需直接交换密钥的安全通信,但现有方法要么基于预计算信道描述符,要么仅优化PKG管线的单个阶段,缺乏直接从原始IQ观测中提取并受多径信道结构约束的神经特征提取器。

Method: 1. 信号预处理:对GLFSR BPSK探测序列进行RMS归一化,通过Hanning窗分帧和短时傅里叶变换生成32×16×2的实值时频谱图。2. 多任务RNN架构:采用双向GRU(384隐藏单元/方向)提取时序特征,经密集归一化块和全局平均池化后,通过L2归一化得到共享嵌入,后接两个任务头——均值中心符号量化的二元特征头(W=128位密钥)和线性信道估计头(2P=32实数输出对应P=16条路径的复数信道抽头)。3. 训练目标:联合优化四元组度量学习损失(迫使Alice-Bob/Bob-Alice嵌入距离小于Alice-Eve/Bob-Eve距离,边界参数α=β=0.5, γ=0.1)与归一化均方误差形式的信道感知损失(λ_ch=0.3),使用RMSprop优化器,学习率0.0001。4. 数据增强:Sionna-RT射线追踪生成10×10空间网格的500个Alice-Bob-Eve三元组,在5-30 dB SNR范围内生成7500个合成四元组,结合POWDER室内(USRP B210/X310)和室外(USTAR/EBC/Guest House)OTA实测数据。5. 协调与隐私放大:Reed-Solomon码RS(24,16)与RS(32,16)进行协调,SHA-3哈希实现隐私放大。

Result: 1. 位不一致率(BDR):所有场景中Alice-Bob互易对的BDR低于Eve相关对,验证了信道感知学习的区分能力。2. 密钥多样性:射线追踪数据增强将NIST(唯一密钥率)从基础RNN的0.21/0.29/0.33提升至CI-RNN+Sionna的0.94/0.99/0.99(室内/室外1/室外2)。3. 协调率:射线追踪增强以牺牲协调成功率为代价换取多样性(室内场景RS(24,16)下AB-BA协调率从0.67降至0.08;RS(32,16)下从0.93降至0.36),但Eve协调率也相应降低。4. 随机性:成功协调的密钥通过NIST统计测试套件的9项测试(近似熵、累积和、块内频率等),优于[24]中未能通过随机游走变体测试的基线。5. 推理效率:NVIDIA H200 GPU上每样本推理时间2.56 ms,计算量0.04 GFLOPs,模型含1,290,656参数(5.02 MiB),适合边缘部署。

Conclusion: 本文提出的信道感知学习框架证明了将物理层密钥生成特征锚定于可测量的多径传播结构的可行性。Sionna-RT射线追踪增强显著提升密钥多样性,但揭示了核心设计权衡:增加对信道变化的敏感性虽提高密钥熵与唯一性,却降低互易一致性与协调可靠性。未来工作将研究协调感知的训练目标、自适应量化与纠错策略、替代信道感知损失,并在移动性、干扰、更大网络拓扑及主动对抗攻击下进行更广泛验证。

Abstract: Physical-layer key generation (PKG) enables wireless devices to establish shared keys from reciprocal channel observations without directly exchanging the key. This capability is attractive for edge networks, where distributed and resource-constrained devices may require lightweight key establishment with limited access to centralized infrastructure. We introduce a channel-informed neural network for PKG that derives binary key features directly from received IQ measurements while explicitly grounding the learned representation in the underlying multipath channel. The proposed multi-task recurrent neural network jointly learns reciprocity-preserving binary features and an auxiliary channel estimate using a training objective that combines deep metric learning with channel-informed supervision. Structured channel sounding enables channel estimation from over-the-air measurements, while Sionna-RT ray tracing is used to augment training with additional propagation conditions. We evaluate the framework using indoor and outdoor software-defined-radio measurements collected on the POWDER radio testbed. Across all evaluated scenarios, the proposed model produces lower bit disagreement for reciprocal Alice-Bob observations than for Eve-related observations. Ray-traced data augmentation substantially improves key diversity, increasing the unique-key rate to 0.94, 0.99, and 0.99 across the indoor and two outdoor scenarios, respectively. Successfully reconciled channel-informed keys pass the selected NIST randomness tests prior to SHA-3 privacy amplification. The results demonstrate the potential of channel-informed representation learning for decentralized wireless key establishment while highlighting an important tradeoff between key diversity and reconciliation reliability.

</details>


### [287] [Multi-Label Proportion Learning for Sea-Ice Type Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16347)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Andrew P. Barrett,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督多标签比例学习框架用于海冰类型预测，将冰图多边形级标注直接用于训练，避免了传统监督方法中将多边形退化为单一主导类标签所带来的不适定问题。框架由两个模块组成：第一模块采用多示例学习（MIL）进行水冰二分；第二模块采用多标签比例学习（MLPL）结合证据狄利克雷聚合来预测冰型组成比例，并通过融合AMSR2亮温与ERA5再分析数据的多模态辅助正则化进一步提升性能。在AI4Arctic数据集上显著优于有监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于深度学习的海冰类型预测需要像素级或patch级标注，但冰图仅提供多边形级WMO egg-code标注，包含各冰型的部分浓度。现有的有监督方法通常将多边形退化为单一主导冰型进行训练，这丢弃了部分浓度信息、引入标签噪声，构成不适定的学习问题，限制了模型精度。论文旨在直接从多边形级比例标注学习，避免不必要的标签近似。

Method: 1. 形式化：将每个冰图多边形视为一个bag，由内部patch组成；监督信号为bag级比例向量。2. 模块一（水冰分类）：基于ResNet-50特征提取器+注意力top-k MIL池化，使用focal损失训练二分类器。3. 模块间patch选择：利用PCA+k-means聚类+注意力权重选择高区分度的patch子集。4. 模块二（冰型比例预测）：采用MLPL，将patch级logit通过softplus映射为非负证据，经bag级求和与归一化得到狄利克雷浓度参数，以闭式KL散度作为损失，允许不同patch贡献不同证据量。5. 多模态扩展：Late Fusion架构将SAR（ResNet-50）与AMSR2/ERA5/地理-时间辅助通道（MLP）分别编码后融合；引入AMSR2冰浓度一致性损失和ERA5环境兼容性损失作为辅助正则项。6. 进一步改进：bag大小校准、不确定性加权训练。

Result: 在AI4Arctic数据集上：SAR-only模型较最佳有监督基线（ResNet-50等）MAE降低14.5%（达0.247），平均冰型F1翻倍至54.8。多模态模型（Late Fusion + Aux-Cons）MAE进一步降至0.194（较SAR-only再降21.5%），平均F1达77.4%（较SAR-only提升41.2%，较监督多模态基线提升52.7%）。证据狄利克雷头优于softmax-mean、ABMIL、TransMIL、DeepLLP、EasyLLP、LLP-BP等基线。scene-disjoint切分下仍保持最佳（MAE 0.260, F1 51.3）。Late Fusion优于Early Fusion；SIC一致性正则主要改善薄冰类，ERA5环境兼容性正则主要改善老冰/冰川类，二者互补。25% patch预算带来3.6×训练加速、4.2-5.7×推理加速，同时多模态模型在该预算下达到0.194 MAE。

Conclusion: 论文成功将海冰类型预测重新定义为弱监督多标签比例学习问题，提出的双模块框架（binary MIL + MLPL）能直接从多边形级egg-code标注学习，避免了主导类近似带来的信息损失。证据狄利克雷聚合机制通过让patch贡献可变证据而非固定softmax质量，有效减少了边界patch和模糊纹理对bag级估计的稀释。融合AMSR2被动微波与ERA5大气再分析的多模态辅助正则化进一步显著提升精度。该框架在AI4Arctic上大幅超越现有有监督和弱监督基线，且在scene-disjoint评估下仍保持优势。未来工作将聚焦于证据校准以提供有意义的预测不确定性、主动学习以及图空间建模与多任务共享编码器扩展。

Abstract: Sea-ice type prediction is important for climate monitoring, maritime navigation, and decision-making in polar regions. The main source of label data for this task is the ice chart, produced manually by ice analysts who interpret satellite imagery to delineate ice zones into polygons. Although ice charts are valuable, their production is labor-intensive and expensive, motivating recent efforts to automate the process using deep learning. However, deep learning models require patch-level (or pixel-level) label data for training, while ice charts provide only polygon-level annotations. As a workaround, supervised approaches often create approximate patch-level labels from polygon-level ice chart labels by assigning each sample the dominant ice type of its parent polygon. This approach enables supervised training but creates an ill-posed learning problem with intrinsically approximate solution. In this paper, we redefine sea-ice type prediction as a weakly supervised multi-label proportion learning problem to be able to directly use the polygon-level ice chart labels and avoid unnecessary label approximation for improved prediction accuracy. To address this problem, we propose a two-module framework where first Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) is used for water--ice classification, and then a multi-label proportion learning (MLPL) is introduced for ice-type composition prediction. We further extend this framework with a multimodal model that integrates SAR imagery with AMSR2 brightness temperatures and ERA5 reanalysis data through modality-guided auxiliary regularization. Evaluated on the AI4Arctic dataset, the SAR-only model reduces MAE by 14.5\% and more than doubles mean ice-class F1 over the best supervised baseline. The multimodal model further reduces MAE by 21.5\% and raises mean F1 by 41.2\% over the SAR-only model, and by 52.7\% over the supervised multimodal baseline.

</details>


### [288] [Federated stochastic bilevel optimization with fully first-order gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16350)
*Yihan Zhang,Rohit Dhaipule,Chiu C Tan,Haibin Ling,Hongchang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦随机双层优化算法FedSVRBGD-FO，仅使用一阶梯度信息，无需计算Hessian和Jacobian矩阵，显著降低了运行时间。算法采用常数单时间尺度学习率，并设计了新的势函数用于收敛性分析。理论证明算法达到O(1/(Nε⁵))的收敛速率，具有关于设备数量N的线性加速。实验在超参数优化和超表示学习任务上验证了算法的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦随机双层优化算法大多需要计算二阶Hessian和Jacobian矩阵，导致运行时间较长，尤其是在高维问题中。此外，现有的一阶方法使用迭代依赖的学习率，存在安全隐患（可被攻击者推断训练阶段），且采用双时间尺度学习率难以调参。本文旨在解决这些挑战。

Method: 1. 将双层优化问题转化为带约束的单层问题，再利用惩罚方法转化为无约束的min-max优化问题；2. 提出FedSVRBGD-FO算法，每个设备使用一阶随机方差缩减梯度更新本地变量；3. 引入常数单时间尺度学习率协调不同变量的更新；4. 设计新的势函数用于收敛性分析，其中势函数各项系数独立于惩罚参数λ。

Result: 1. 理论收敛速率T=O(κ¹⁰/(Nε⁵))，关于设备数N具有线性加速；2. 通信复杂度为O(κ⁹/ε⁴)；3. 当N=1时，单机设置下的收敛速率与现有最优方法匹配；4. 实验在a9a、w8a、covtype数据集上，与FedNEST、FedMBO、LocalBSGVRM、FedBiOAcc等二阶方法相比，收敛速度更快，运行时间更少。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于完全一阶梯度的联邦随机双层优化算法，无需计算二阶信息即可达到与现有二阶方法相当的收敛保证，同时在实践中具有更高的计算效率。常数单时间尺度学习率的设计提升了安全性与实用性，实验验证了算法在超参数优化和超表示学习任务上的优越性能。

Abstract: Federated stochastic bilevel optimization has been actively studied in recent years due to its widespread applications in machine learning. However, most existing federated stochastic bilevel optimization algorithms require the computation of second-order Hessian and Jacobian matrices, which leads to longer running times in practice. To address these challenges, we propose a novel federated stochastic variance-reduced bilevel gradient descent algorithm that relies solely on first-order oracles. Specifically, our approach does not require the computation of second-order Hessian and Jacobian matrices, significantly reducing running time. Furthermore, we introduce a novel learning rate mechanism, i.e., a constant single-timescale learning rate, to coordinate the update of different variables. We also present a new strategy to establish the convergence rate of our algorithm. Finally, the extensive experimental results confirm the efficacy of our proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [289] [Autonomous Droplet Navigation via Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16369)
*Rajneesh Anand,Mayuresh V. Kothare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）方法，在重力驱动的倾斜平台上实现了液滴在复杂受限微流控几何路径中的自主导航，无需仿真或解析模型即可从有限的物理交互数据中学习有效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液滴在芯片实验室（lab-on-a-chip）平台中具有广泛应用，但液滴因接触角滞后、可变形性和毛细钉扎效应而表现出非线性、依赖历史的动力学特性。经典控制器和预编程轨迹难以应对多转弯受限几何环境中的自主液滴传输问题。

Method: 在重力驱动的"迷宫"（Labyrinth）平台上，利用薄层硅油膜减少接触线钉扎，通过双轴倾斜提供重力驱动力，顶部摄像头实时跟踪液滴位置。采用离线训练的模型基强化学习（MBRL）策略，从有限的物理交互数据中学习；系统仅依靠视觉观测信息，在油膜厚度、瞬时接触角和液滴形变等状态部分可观测的条件下运行。

Result: 所学策略在直线、直角和弯曲弧线路径（包括外角几何）上实现了可靠的液滴导航。在简单几何上训练的策略可零样本迁移到直角和阶梯路径，并在仅使用五分之一训练数据的情况下在弯曲弧线上达到完全成功。

Conclusion: 该研究表明基于模型的强化学习能够有效应对液滴操控中的非线性和部分可观测性挑战，为液滴基智能化学实验室微流控系统的自主运行提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Precise manipulation of liquid droplets underpins lab-on-a-chip platforms for diagnostics, chemical synthesis, and biological assays. Yet autonomous droplet transport through confined geometries of varying complexity remains an open challenge. Droplets exhibit contact-angle hysteresis, deformability, and capillary pinning, which make their response to actuation nonlinear and history dependent, that classical controllers and pre-programmed trajectories cannot cope in multi-turn environments. Here we demonstrate autonomous navigation of a liquid droplet through geometries of increasing complexity on a gravity driven (Labyrinth) platform using model-based reinforcement learning. A thin silicone oil film reduces contact-line pinning while two-axis tilt supplies the gravitational driving force, and an overhead camera tracks the droplet in real time. An offline-trained policy discovers effective tilt strategies from limited physical interaction data, without simulation or analytical droplet models. The system operates under partial observability, as oil-film thickness, instantaneous contact angle, and droplet deformation state remain hidden from the controller. Despite these challenges, the learned policy achieves reliable navigation across straight, right-angle, and curved-arc paths, including outside-corner geometries. We further demonstrate that a policy trained on a simpler geometry transfers to complex ones, succeeding zero-shot on right-angle and staircase paths and reaching full success on a curved arc with a fifth of the training data. The findings suggest promising avenues for enabling droplet based microfluidic systems to serve as intelligent chemical laboratories.

</details>


### [290] [Certified Uncertainty Propagation in One-Shot Federated Bayesian Models via Posterior Event Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16373)
*Mahyar Mohammadi,Mohammad Hossein Badiei,Abolfazl Yaghmaei,Hamed Kebriaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对单次通信联邦贝叶斯神经网络（One-Shot Federated BNN）提出了一种部署一致的概率安全认证框架。该框架通过将各客户端的局部后验事件沿部署聚合规则（FedAvg）进行"后验事件传输"（Posterior Event Transport），在完整聚合像被验证为安全后才纳入对应的概率质量。对于非负加权 FedAvg，笛卡尔积超矩形在聚合后的像恰好是一个加权超矩形，不引入集合过近似。实验在 MNIST 和 Fashion-MNIST 上使用标签狄利克雷异质分区进行，结果表明传输证书范围为 22.51%–46.89%，而直接全局证书范围为 72.05%–91.39%，揭示了预测精度与可认证安全并不必然一致，且 FedAvg 与 Product-of-Gaussians 融合在各架构下表现各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单次通信联邦贝叶斯学习中，部署模型由各客户端特定后验分布采样的参数经聚合得到，局部认证证书无法直接保证聚合后模型的安全性。现有的中心化 BNN 概率认证方法假设单一后验分布，未考虑多客户端聚合所引入的部署分布变化，因此无法直接应用于联邦场景。需要一种部署一致的认证框架，将局部后验事件通过聚合映射传递并验证聚合像。

Method: 1. 每个客户端构建成对不相交的轴对齐超矩形区域（在局部权重空间中），并利用对角高斯后验以闭式（误差函数）计算各区域的后验概率质量。2. 服务器形成各客户端区域的笛卡尔积元组，通过 FedAvg 聚合映射（Θ_g = Σ αᵢΘᵢ，αᵢ ≥ 0）计算精确的加权超矩形聚合像（无需集合过近似）。3. 使用区间界传播（IBP）作为验证器，仅当完整聚合像 G_k 被验证为安全时，才将对应联合事件概率 ∏p_{i,kᵢ} 累加到证书中。4. 在客户端后验独立假设下，联合事件概率可因式分解为局部质量的乘积，因此求和得到部署安全概率的有效下界（定理 1）。5. 还对比了 FedAvg 推前分布直接认证以及 Product-of-Gaussians（PoG）融合下的直接认证。

Result: 在 MNIST 和 Fashion-MNIST（标签狄利克雷异质分区 α∈{0.5,...,10}，客户端数 n∈{2,3,5}，架构 1×64、1×128、2×64）上的实验表明：(1) 传输的 FedAvg 证书在所有配置中均非空（22.51%–46.89%），验证了理论框架的实用性；(2) 直接全局证书（PoG: 72.05%–91.39%；FedAvg: 72.58%–91.20%）显著高于传输证书，因其直接在全局后验空间中搜索安全区域；(3) 预测精度最高的 1×128 架构并未产生最高的可认证后验质量，1×64 网络反而在所有直接认证对比中表现最佳；(4) 客户端数增加会降低传输证书（笛卡尔积元组空间增大、几何兼容性降低），而狄利克雷浓度 α 增大会提高传输-直接证书比；(5) PoG 与 FedAvg 两种融合规则在不同架构下互有优势，无统一优劣排序；(6) 局部证书和传输证书均低于直接证书，三者衡量不同的概率量。

Conclusion: 该框架为单次通信联邦贝叶斯神经网络提供了部署一致的安全认证下界，核心创新在于：(1) 将局部后验事件通过聚合映射传输后再验证聚合像，解决了局部证书无法直接推断全局安全的问题；(2) 对非负加权 FedAvg 给出笛卡尔积超矩形像的精确加权超矩形表征，无需集合过近似；(3) 明确区分了传输证书与 FedAvg/PoG 下的直接证书所衡量的不同概率量。实验证明该证书在所有评估配置中均为非空有效下界，且揭示了预测精度与可认证安全不必同步变化、不同全局后验构造在不同架构下表现各异的事实。主要局限在于完整笛卡尔积元组评估的组合爆炸问题，未来工作将探索分布式原始-对偶优化、多智能体强化学习和随机广义纳什均衡等方法以可扩展地识别和剪除不可验证的参数组合。

Abstract: Probabilistic certification of Bayesian neural networks lower-bounds the posterior probability that a model satisfies a verifier-defined safety property. In one-shot federated Bayesian learning, however, the deployed model is obtained by aggregating parameters drawn from client-specific posterior distributions, so local certificates do not directly guarantee safety of the aggregated model. This paper develops a deployment-consistent certification framework by propagating local posterior events through the deployment aggregation rule, with an exact geometric characterization for Federated Averaging (FedAvg). Each client constructs disjoint hyper-rectangular regions in parameter space and computes their probability masses. The server forms Cartesian products of these regions, maps them through the deployment rule, and retains a product event only when its aggregation image is verified to satisfy the safety property. Under independent client posteriors, each product-event probability factorizes into local masses, and summing verified disjoint events yields a lower bound on safety probability of the deployed model. For FedAvg with nonnegative aggregation coefficients, the image of a Cartesian product of axis-aligned hyper-rectangles is exactly a weighted hyper-rectangle, introducing no set over-approximation. We distinguish the proposed transported-event certificate from direct certification under posterior distributions induced by FedAvg and Product-of-Gaussians aggregation. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST under label-Dirichlet heterogeneity show that the transported FedAvg certificate ranges from 22.51% to 46.89%, while direct global certificates range from 72.05% to 91.39%. Results show that predictive accuracy and certifiable safety do not necessarily follow the same trend, and that global posterior constructions can exhibit distinct certification behavior across architectures.

</details>


### [291] [Bounded Adjustment with Reliability-Guided Embedding for Imbalanced Learning with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16380)
*Mushir Akhtar,Akarsh J.,M. Tanveer,Mohd. Arshad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BARGE（受限调整与可靠性引导嵌入）是一种单阶段单网络训练目标，通过先验调整的密度幂得分（bounded prior-adjusted density-power score）与可靠性引导的角度几何（reliability-guided angular geometry）相结合，解决类不平衡学习与标签噪声耦合失败模式的问题，无需噪声率或转移矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别平衡学习需要频率校正以防止多数类主导决策规则，但这会放大被错误标记的少数类样本的影响。交叉熵损失在网络对错误标签高度自信时梯度不会消失。现有方法很少在单一网络训练协议内同时控制预测层与表示层的影响，且通常需要估计噪声率或转移矩阵。

Method: BARGE 损失由三部分组成：(1) 分类得分 L_cls：先验调整后的密度幂严格适当得分（β_C = min{1/2, 1/log C}），在调整概率空间内严格适当，有限范围，在自信矛盾下梯度下降；(2) 紧凑性损失 L_comp：使用调整后目标概率作为停止梯度可靠性权重，对类内特征进行角度收缩，按类别等权重归一化以保证类间均衡；(3) 分离损失 L_sep：对分类器方向之间正余弦相似度的单边惩罚，防止类方向正对齐。整个目标 L = L_cls + η(L_comp + L_sep)。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny ImageNet 上的长尾和阶梯不平衡设置中评估。干净标签下（12个设置），BARGE 综合排名第二，4个设置最优。标签损坏下（20%和40%随机错误标签替换，6个设置），BARGE 在所有数据集-损坏组合中均达到最低平均平衡误差，将六设置平均值从最强基线 LA 的 72.32% 降至 70.00%，并在所有损坏设置中获得最高 macro-F1 和 macro-AUPRC。消融实验验证类等角度紧凑性对有界得分有补充贡献。计算开销与现有方法持平，推理不变。

Conclusion: 有界预测影响与可靠性引导几何是处理类不平衡与标签不确定性耦合问题的互补机制。BARGE 适合类别频率严重偏斜、标注质量不确定、且无可靠噪声转移模型或干净标签校正程序可用时的场景。其局限在于：当某稀有类的所有可用标签都不可靠时，类内归一化无法恢复该类。

Abstract: Class-balanced learning and label noise create a coupled failure mode: frequency correction prevents majority classes from dominating the decision rule, but can amplify incorrectly labeled minority examples. We introduce BARGE (Bounded Adjustment with Reliability-Guided Embeddings), a single-stage objective combining a bounded, prior-adjusted density-power score with reliability-guided angular geometry. Its classification score is strictly proper in the adjusted probability space and recovers balanced Bayes ordering under clean supervision and the true class prior. Under label contamination, its finite range bounds classification-risk perturbation at a fixed predictor, while its logit gradient redescends when the model confidently contradicts the supplied label. The adjusted target probability also weights class-equal feature compactness, and a one-sided separation term discourages aligned class directions. BARGE requires neither a noise rate nor a transition matrix, uses one network, and leaves inference unchanged. We evaluate it on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny ImageNet under long-tail and step imbalance, clean labels, and 20% and 40% random incorrect-label replacement. Across 12 clean settings, BARGE ranks second overall and attains the lowest error in four. Under corruption, it achieves the lowest mean balanced error in all six dataset-corruption settings, reducing the six-setting average from 72.32% for the strongest competitor to 70.00%. It also obtains the highest macro-F1 and macro-AUPRC in every corrupted-label setting. Ablations show that class-equal angular compactness improves on the bounded score alone. These results support bounded predictive influence and reliability-guided geometry as complementary mechanisms for imbalanced learning with uncertain labels.

</details>


### [292] [Attention Mean Fields Predict Average Representation Dynamics and Reveal Context-Specific Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16382)
*Micah Adler,John W. Byers,Mark Crovella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Transformer注意力机制的平均场(mean-field)分析方法，将单个注意力头的平均行为建模为一个可逐层迭代的核(kernel)，从而能够预测语言模型表示几何的动态演化。该方法既能在语料库层面预测平均表示演化，也能通过上下文条件版本识别上下文特异性计算（以均值场偏差量化）。研究发现：早期训练阶段模型行为完全等同于其均值场；偏差分解为注意力路由协方差与值的语境化两项；偏差可用于发现已知ICL电路并提取可迁移的函数向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的表示几何在运行过程中不断演变，单纯依赖模型无关的统计量（如共现）无法刻画这种动态过程。研究者需要一个由模型自身行为定义的原则性基线，以区分头部的平均行为与上下文特异性计算。

Method: 1. 定义注意力平均场核P_{st}：从语料库中统计类型s的查询对类型t的键所放置的平均注意力权重。2. 在两个平均场假设下（注意力-值独立性、忽略曲率），推导平均贡献的闭式估计 z^_s = Σ_t P_{st}·v(c_t)。3. 构建开放循环迭代算法(Algorithm 1)：从输入嵌入出发，逐层应用均值场核与冻结的MLP权重，无需任何测量残差。4. 引入(s,t)-条件与上下文条件均值场，后者通过重整化W_{st}在上下文中的类型得到。5. 将头部的实际输出与上下文条件均值场预测的差定义为均值场偏差D_MF，并可加性分解为协方差项（注意力路由异常）与语境化项（被传输值的语境化）。

Result: 1. 均值场预测在GPT-2、Gemma-2、Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-3-14B上准确追踪各层类型质心（GPT-2中层居中余弦相似度>0.9）。2. 早期训练中，Pythia 160m/1.4B/12B三规模下用均值场替换全部注意力头几乎不改变loss；偏差ΔL与D_MF在归纳头形成窗口（约步骤1000-2000，对应~2×10^9 token）同步急升。3. 在Pythia-160m训练过程中，144个头的均值场偏差与行为归纳得分的跨头Spearman相关性在归纳转变处达峰值0.71。4. 在GPT-2上按语料库偏差排名前20的头部包含已知归纳头（L5H5、L6H9、L7H10）、IOI姓名移动头（L9H9、L9H6等）、位置头（L1H8、L2H7等）以及作者新识别的内容移动头（L9H8、L5H2、L8H3、L6H8）。5. 在Gemma-2-2B上，均值场残差提取的函数向量在6项任务上与因果中介(AIE)选择具有可比的迁移准确率。6. GPT-2层1五个头部（L1H2/3/8/10、L2H7）通过BOS门控机制将绝对位置编码为新的低维子空间曲线，下游因果消融使loss上升+0.0117 nats（为对照的38倍）。

Conclusion: 均值场分析提供了：(1) 对类型质心跨层演化的紧凑准确预测；(2) 训练过程中模型何时从均值场过渡到上下文特异性计算的精确诊断；(3) 一种任务无关的上下文计算度量——均值场偏差，它可加性分解为注意力路由与值语境化两个机制；(4) 从单次前向传播提取可迁移函数向量的新方法。模型行为可视为全局（语料库平均）结构与局部（上下文）创新的叠加，均值场刻画前者，残差量化后者。

Abstract: A language model's representation geometry is not predetermined; it evolves as the model runs. A faithful account of that geometry must capture that dynamic process, and so cannot be based solely on model-independent statistics such as co-occurrence. Here we introduce a mean-field analysis of attention. The average attention from one token to another defines a kernel that carries representations layer to layer and can be iterated through the network to model how the geometry is transformed. We condition this average two ways. Conditioned on a whole corpus, the kernel predicts the average-case evolution of representation geometry. Conditioned instead on a single context, it predicts the expected geometry for that context. A head's departure from that prediction, its \emph{mean-field deviation}, isolates the context-specific computation that the mean field misses. Under the corpus-conditional reading, the kernel yields an open-loop model: from the input embeddings and the frozen weights alone, we can iterate the kernel and the model's own MLPs over token representations, never consulting a measured deviation at any layer. The resulting prediction is highly accurate. In early training the model and its corpus mean field are indistinguishable. Replace every attention head with its mean field, and the substitution leaves the loss on real text unchanged. Around the onset of induction, the two diverge, and the gap widens as representations become contextualized. Under the context-conditional reading, deviation from the mean field is a task-agnostic measure of context-specific computation. The residual decomposes additively into unusual attention routing and contextualization of the transported values. Across controlled induction and few-shot settings, greater deviation tracks greater reliance on in-context information.

</details>


### [293] [Online Gradient Computation for Warping Gaussian Process Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16472)
*Emilio Ruiz-Moreno,Konstantinos Slavakis,Baltasar Beferull-Lozano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对扭曲高斯过程（Warped GP）提出了一种新颖的在线学习方法，通过推导瞬时负对数似然梯度的精确递归计算公式，实现潜在GP矩与扭曲参数的联合在线更新，并结合ALD稀疏化技术控制计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高斯过程假设观测服从联合高斯分布，难以处理现实世界中常见的非高斯观测（如重尾、偏斜、严格正值等）。扭曲GP通过参数化变换"扭曲"将非高斯观测映射到潜在高斯空间，但现有流式变体要么周期性优化扭曲参数，要么牺牲解析可处理性以换取更高模型容量，缺少能够联合在线更新潜在GP矩与扭曲参数的方法，尤其在内存受限或实时任务中难以应用。

Method: 1. 理论推导：证明扭曲GP的瞬时负对数似然（NLL）相对于扭曲参数的梯度可进行精确递归计算（定理3.1），仅需在标准递归GP基础上额外维护一个状态变量 B_n = Ż_n Ω_n。2. 算法设计：将递归GP更新、ALD稀疏化技术与递归梯度计算整合为统一的在线算法（Algorithm 1），使用Adam等一阶优化器配合投影到可行域 Θ 进行扭曲参数更新。3. 稀疏化机制：采用近似线性依赖（ALD）技术维护动态字典，控制计算复杂度为 O(D_n² + rD_n)。

Result: 在1D回归基准任务上（正弦信号+高斯噪声+立方根变换，101000个观测）：1. 扭曲GP预测与真实生成分布高度吻合；2. 学习到的扭曲变换在观测密集区域接近真实立方根变换，数值反演稳定；3. ALD字典最终保留68个原子；4. 经验NLL达到 -0.28 nats/观测，相比配置相同的标准GP有0.76 nat的改进。

Conclusion: 本文提出了扭曲GP瞬时NLL的递归梯度计算方法，仅需在标准递归实现基础上增加一个状态变量。基于此设计了联合更新潜在GP矩与扭曲参数的在线算法，在基准实验中验证了有效性。未来工作包括核参数和观测噪声方差的在线学习，以及在更广泛基准上的评估。

Abstract: Warped Gaussian processes (GPs) handle non-Gaussian observations by mapping them into a latent standard GP via a parametric transformation called warping. Existing streaming variants, however, either optimize the warping parameters periodically or sacrifice analytical tractability for a higher model capacity. To bridge this gap, we show that the gradient of the instantaneous negative log-likelihood of a warped GP admits an exact recursive computation. Based on this result, we propose a novel online method for warped GPs that jointly updates the latent GP moments and optimizes the warping parameters.

</details>


### [294] [FlowATC: Aircraft Trajectory Prediction via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16528)
*Mathurin Petit,Emir Torun,Louis Brusset,Jordan Kam,Alexandre M. Bayen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowATC提出了一种基于流匹配(Conditional Flow Matching)的架构，将飞机轨迹预测视为序列修复任务，使用DiT(扩散Transformer)骨干网络在旧金山湾区115万条ADS-B轨迹上训练，无需航图或程序标签监督。模型在多模态预测中优于DDPM、CVAE和确定性基线，并能从数据中自动恢复已知的空域结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空空管需要精确的短期轨迹预测以维持安全间隔。然而，给定相同的观测历史，飞机的未来轨迹本质上是多模态的(例如，左转或直行取决于跑道分配和流量排序)。传统的确定性预测器只能给出条件均值，在多模态情况下产生物理上不合理的"平均"轨迹。生成式建模的最新进展(CFM/DDPM)能够对完整分布进行采样，非常适合此任务，但现有的航空轨迹预测生成模型尚未充分利用这些技术。

Method: 1. 数据：115万个ADS-B轨迹窗口(43个观测点+43个未来点，≈128秒预测范围)，覆盖以SFO为中心的60海里地理围栏，保留ADS-B的原生不规则采样间隔；特征为6维(x, y, z, vx, vy, vz)局部切平面笛卡尔坐标。
2. 架构：基于DiT的块因果Transformer，观测token与加噪的未来token通过拼接进入序列；AdaLN用于流时间t∈[0,1]条件注入；采用序列修复框架，未来token使用双向注意力。
3. 训练目标：CFM(条件流匹配)回归速度场；对比DDPM、CVAE(Tractron++风格)、确定性LSTM、常速度基线。
4. 评估指标：minADE@K/minFDE@K、KDE-NLL(密度校准)、CRPS(分支点机动保真度)。

Result: 1. 在匹配参数量下(1.5M)，CFM在minADE@20上比DDPM低11-26%，比CVAE低41%；Flow Tiny在水平方向达390.5/686.1m(minADE/FDE@20)。
2. CFM约在7M参数时饱和数据信息；Flow Small与Flow Large性能相当但延迟低2.3倍(67ms vs 157ms)。
3. 单样本生成模型在K=1时不优于确定性基线；K≥5时生成模型优势显现。
4. 流匹配和扩散在NLL上保持平稳(≈20nats)，而CVAE在长时间跨度上发散至≈60nats。
5. 在28个航路分支点(2,329条测试航段)上，Flow Large对历史盲采样器的CRPS降低47%，直行比例观测32.9% vs 预测32.2%，相关系数0.99。
6. 无需航图监督即可恢复SFO的NIITE FOUR离场程序等已知空域结构。
7. 时间下采样(步长2)仅损失4.9% minADE@20，推理时间减半；步长8时损失44.4%但加速5倍。
8. 单GPU可服务约150架飞机(Flow Tiny)或20架(Flow Large)的预测更新。

Conclusion: FlowATC证明了基于CFM的序列修复框架在飞机轨迹预测中的有效性，核心结论包括：(1) 最佳K精度和密度校准是相关但不同的特性，生成模型主要在多模态覆盖上占优；(2) CFM在匹配参数量下始终优于DDPM；(3) 模型在时间下采样和超出训练跨度的查询下表现稳健；(4) 仅从绝对笛卡尔坐标中即可学习到空域结构，在分支点重现实际飞行的机动分布。论文指出未来可通过附加ATC语音、天气场、空域图等上下文token进一步提升转向决策点的预测精度。

Abstract: Building accurate decision-support tools for next-generation air traffic control requires robust trajectory prediction models. We present a flow-matching architecture trained exclusively on historical aircraft trajectories, with no route labels or chart supervision. Trained on 1.15 million Automatic Dependent Surveillance-Broadcast trajectory windows collected over the San Francisco Bay Area, the model generates aircraft trajectory distributions that closely match historical traffic, reproducing known airspace structure around San Francisco Airport such as the shape of SFO's published NIITE FOUR departure procedure. Our model is trained directly on the native, irregular ADS-B sampling interval. Trajectory prediction is cast as sequence inpainting using a block-causal Transformer that denoises future state tokens conditioned on the observed history using Conditional Flow Matching or Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models. We compare our architecture against constant-velocity, deterministic-Long Short Term Memory, and Conditional Variational Autoencoders baselines. At matched parameter count, CFM outperforms DDPM by 11-26% in minADE@20, and both generative objectives surpass the CVAE baseline by 31-41%. We further show that the error degrades gracefully with prediction horizon, and the architecture remains effective when retrained on temporally decimated feeds. Lastly, we sample $K$ independent completions, yielding spatial probabilistic occupancy estimates that can serve as input to downstream conflict-risk estimation.

</details>


### [295] [AsyncCouple-Flow: Asynchronous Cross-Modal Coupling and Flow Matching for Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16573)
*Zhixiang Wu,Yining Liu,Bo Zhao,Szu-Yu Chen,Huiran Duan,Chu Lin,Chuanguang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AsyncCouple-Flow 是一个用于多模态时空预测的统一框架，联合解决异步采样率、部署时模态缺失和长程误差累积三个难题，通过 MATS 令牌稀疏化、ACCG 异步耦合图和 Flow-Matching 预测头实现非自回归单遍预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态时空预测面临：模态时空采样率不一致需损失性插值；部署时模态频繁缺失而训练时假设完整；自回归解码器在长程预测中误差累积放大。作者主张联合而非孤立解决这三个耦合问题。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) MATS 模块对各模态进行尺度感知编码并用共享重要性评分器 Top-k 选取令牌；(2) ACCG 用可学习图建模 (模态, 时间, 位置) 三元组节点，边权重由时间偏移、语义相似度、物理先验三因子构成，缺失模态直接作为掩码节点；(3) Flow-Matching 头将多步预测建模为条件 ODE，单次积分生成整个轨迹，配合随机模态 dropout 训练。

Result: 在 WeatherBench-MM（ERA5+GOES+ISD）和 PEMS-BAY-MM 上，AsyncCouple-Flow 在 MAE/RMSE/CRPS/SSIM 四项指标上均优于 10 个 SOTA 基线；在缺失 2 个模态时仍优于基线完整输入的精度；24h 温度场功率谱在 ±10% 保真带内，优于自回归和扩散基线的频谱过平滑；推理延迟 150ms，比 CrossViViT 和 PreDiff-50 快 1.4×–3.1×。

Conclusion: AsyncCouple-Flow 通过 MATS+ACCG+Flow-Matching 联合设计，统一解决了 MM-STF 中的异步性、缺失鲁棒性和长程漂移三大问题，实验验证了在精度、鲁棒性、频谱保真度和效率方面的优势。

Abstract: Multi-modal spatio-temporal forecasting (MM-STF) supports weather nowcasting, traffic prediction, and earth-system modeling by combining heterogeneous sources such as physical fields, satellite imagery, and in-situ sensors. Three obstacles persist: (i) modalities have different spatio-temporal sampling rates, forcing lossy interpolation onto a unified grid; (ii) modalities are frequently missing at deployment due to sensor outages or revisit gaps, while most methods train with full availability; and (iii) autoregressive decoders accumulate errors over long horizons, amplified by multi-modal conditioning. We propose AsyncCouple-Flow to address these issues jointly. A Modality-Aware Token Sparsification (MATS) module performs scale-aware tokenization and uses a shared importance scorer to select top-k tokens per timestep, producing equal-length sequences. An Asynchronous Cross-Modal Coupling Graph (ACCG) replaces fixed cross-attention with a learnable graph whose edges encode time offsets, semantic similarity, and modality-specific physical priors, enabling fusion under arbitrary asynchrony and missingness. A Flow-Matching Forecasting Head models multi-step prediction as a conditional ODE, trained with stochastic modality dropout and integrated jointly to avoid autoregressive drift. Experiments on ERA5+GOES+ISD weather forecasting and PEMS-BAY traffic prediction with multi-source side information show that AsyncCouple-Flow outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and remains robust with up to two missing modalities. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [296] [Recovering Physical Parameters from Fragmented Observations via Exact Distributed Spline Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16579)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于张量积B样条的精确分布式合并方法，用于从分散观测中恢复物理参数。该方法利用固定基函数岭回归统计量的可加性结构，各数据持有方仅需计算并传输局部Gram矩阵和矩向量，合并结果在数学上与集中式拟合完全一致，无需共享原始数据或迭代同步。通过完整的"场重建→导数提取→线性回归"管道，扩散系数恢复误差0.11%，波速恢复误差0.12%，分布式合并相对集中式拟合引入零退化，并在41年NOAA海面温度实测数据上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学观测（海洋、大气、医疗影像等）天然分散于不同空间、时间、机构中，且往往受隐私或带宽限制无法集中。已有方法如联邦学习需要多轮迭代同步，模型合并方法（如Model Soups、Git Re-Basin、RegMean）处理参数平均但非精确聚合；物理参数恢复方法（如PINN、SINDy、PDE-FIND）通常假设集中访问。核心问题是：在不共享原始数据的前提下，能否从分散观测中精确重建连续可微场并恢复物理参数。

Method: 1) 数学基础：对固定基函数B样条展开，岭回归目标函数的法方程仅依赖Gram矩阵 G=Φ⊤Φ 和矩向量 h=Φ⊤u 两个充分统计量，且二者对数据方具有可加性。2) 分布式合并：各数据方 s 计算 (G_s, h_s)，合并后解 c*=(∑G_s+λI)⁻¹∑h_s 与集中式拟合完全一致。3) 张量积场表示：空间方向用三次B样条，时间方向用B样条或傅里叶谐波，展平为线性系数向量。4) 物理参数恢复：在规则网格上用中心差分计算导数，对线性PDE（如扩散方程 u_t=D∇²u、波方程 u_tt=c_w²∇²u）做最小二乘回归恢复参数。

Result: 1) 扩散方程（D=0.05）：从10000个随机时空观测恢复 D_rec=0.05006，相对误差0.11%。2) 波方程（c_w=1）：恢复 c_w,rec=0.9988，相对误差0.12%；但稀疏含噪条件下（1000观测、σ=0.02）二阶导数放大噪声，误差升至8.6%。3) 分布式合并验证：两个数据方各5000观测、空间二分，Gram合并与集中式拟合在RMSE和恢复参数上完全相同，预测差 δ_max<10⁻¹⁵（浮点精度）。4) NOAA实测：41年月度海面温度（~441万网格-月值），西/东太平洋两数据方合并，RMSE=0.639°C，与集中式一致；单方在其区域外RMSE>>16°C。5) 导数网格独立验证：对解析场值直接用相同差分格式，扩散误差0.45%、波速误差0.13%，量化了离散化贡献。

Conclusion: 对于固定特征、平方误差、岭正则化框架，张量积B样条场的Gram矩阵和矩向量构成可组合充分统计量，使分布式合并在数学上等价于集中式拟合（精确算术下零退化），且仅需传输 O(K²+K) 大小的统计量。该框架将分布式学习、可解释场表示和物理信息推断三条线索统一为一条管道，从合成PDE到真实气候数据均得到验证。但该精确性仅限于固定基函数线性参数设置，不可推广到多层联合训练网络（因前层变化会使后续层Gram矩阵失效）；有限差分导数估计仍是物理参数恢复的精度瓶颈；共享统计量的隐私泄露问题未做分析。

Abstract: Scientific measurements are frequently distributed across locations, time periods, and institutions. Combining such fragments into a continuous, differentiable field enables recovering governing physical parameters from its derivatives. This paper makes two contributions toward that goal. First, the established additive structure of fixed-basis ridge-regression statistics is applied to tensor-product spline fields: each data holder computes a local Gram matrix and moment vector, and the merged solution is mathematically identical to centralized fitting, with no raw data shared and no iterative synchronization. This property is specific to the fixed-feature squared-error setting; the present derivation does not establish an analogous guarantee for general jointly trained multilayer networks. Second, a complete pipeline connects distributed observations to physical parameter inference through field reconstruction, derivative extraction, and linear regression. The diffusion coefficient is recovered to 0.11% error and wave speed to 0.12% error; in both cases, distributed merging introduces zero degradation relative to centralized fitting. Application to 41 years of NOAA sea-surface temperature data confirms the result on real spatiotemporal observations.

</details>


### [297] [A Weighted Kernel Method for Approximation that Adapts to Learned Multivariable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16606)
*John E. Darges,Laura Weidensager*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"总灵敏度核"（Total Sensitivity Kernels, TSK），一种基于加权ANOVA核的自适应核方法，通过学习每个输入的TSK因子来自动适配目标函数的多变量结构，在高维函数逼近中显著优于标准乘积核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维黑箱函数在数据有限时难以精确逼近，因为许多函数存在低有效维度结构（部分输入不重要、仅部分输入组有强交互），但事先不知道这种多变量结构。现有方法如HDMR、sparse learning等需预设或筛选重要变量，缺乏统一的自适应框架。核方法的泛化误差界严格依赖于RKHS范数，因此若能选择使目标函数RKHS范数最小的核，就能从根本上改善逼近精度。

Method: TSK定义为乘积形式核 κ_Σ(x,y) = ∏(1-Σ_k+Σ_k κ_k(x_k,y_k))，其中d个TSK因子Σ_k ∈ (0,1)参数化了2^d个ANOVA分量的权重γ_u = ∏_{k∈u}Σ_k · ∏_{l∉u}(1-Σ_l)。关键步骤：1) 理论层：证明目标函数RKHS范数平方 g(Σ)=‖f‖²_Σ 在(0,1)^d上是强凸的（定理3.4），保证唯一最优解存在；2) 一阶最优性条件给出耦合方程（公式3.4/3.5），将Σ*_k表示为各ANOVA分量能量的比率，涉及"in"/"out"能量；3) 实践层：用最小范数插值的RKHS范数 g_M(Σ)=f^T K⁻¹f 作为经验替代，通过L-BFGS最小化求解；sigmoid重参数化保证约束；4) 理论保证：证明g_M一致收敛于g（定理4.1），结合强凸性得参数估计误差界O(√(ε_M/m))。

Result: 在Sobol' g-function（高/中/低复杂度）上，TSK全面优于标准核；在高维基准测试中：100D函数（TSK与ARD误差相当，远优于标准核和ANOVA核约两个数量级）、1D扩散问题（TSK与ARD最优）、2D热扩散（ARD最优，TSK次优）、修改Griewank函数（TSK比ARD优一个数量级）。TSK因子能正确恢复输入重要性排序（与Sobol指数排序一致），且在含高阶交互的函数上比ARD更具优势——两种自适应机制互补。

Conclusion: TSK提供了一种系统性的自适应框架，通过学习d个参数将2^d个ANOVA权重参数化，并以最小化RKHS范数为准则。强凸性确保理论最优解的存在唯一性和参数识别的稳定性。对于具有可利用多变量结构的目标函数，该方法在逼近精度上有显著提升。TSK因子提供了一种与Sobol指数相关但不同的"模型内在、核依赖"的灵敏度解释。理论适用于无噪声、独立输入的确定性逼近；未来工作包括扩展到POD权重、含噪数据、依赖输入，以及与其他核学习方法（ARD、多核学习）的集成。

Abstract: Approximating the input-output behavior of a multivariable black-box function from limited data is challenging when blind to the importance of its inputs and their interactions. We introduce total sensitivity kernels (TSKs), a method based on families of weighted ANOVA kernels that learn and adapt to this multivariable structure. TSKs parameterize the weights on each multivariable component of the target function by factors for each input. We propose learning these factors directly from function evaluations by selecting the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) in which the target function has minimum norm. Under suitable conditions, we show that this norm-minimization problem admits a unique solution, and we establish consistency of a finite-data formulation based on minimum-norm interpolation. The learned TSK factors characterize the participation of individual inputs across interactions and main effects, providing a kernel-dependent notion of input sensitivity related to total Sobol indices. Numerical experiments demonstrate that adapting the kernel to learned multivariable structure can substantially improve approximation accuracy over a standard product kernel.

</details>
