### [298] [Divergence Timing and Cumulative Disagreement under KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16617)
*Xinyue Luo,Fei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究KV-cache淘汰策略对自回归生成的影响。通过理论分解和实验分析，提出精确的期望不匹配分数分解公式，并基于Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型的完整轨迹数据进行实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV-cache淘汰操作会扰动自回归生成中的条件token分布，但目前缺乏对其累积分歧（cumulative disagreement）的系统性理论分析。论文旨在探究首次分歧时机和后续token不匹配如何决定累积分歧。

Method: 1. 在指定的逐步最大耦合（stepwise maximal coupling）下推导出精确分解：期望不匹配分数 = 首次不匹配贡献 + 分歧后暴露量 × 不匹配率；2. 构造显式构造覆盖无限制的自回归核对（autoregressive kernel pairs），实现与有限分歧对齐观察窗口兼容的风险尖锐区间；3. 残差分支条件蒙特卡洛（Residual-branch conditional Monte Carlo）提供发生率、占用率和窗口/尾部贡献的无偏联合估计；4. 在Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型上进行完整轨迹实验。

Result: 1. SnapKV在50%保留率下进入分歧的时间更晚且频率低于SnapKV-512或同预算的近期token保留策略；2. 分歧后总变差（TV）保持较高水平；3. 在288个文档的探索性分析中，分歧后暴露量解释了四个聚合不匹配差距的85-90%；4. 在90%保留率的288个独立文档上，两种模型在晚期窗口的分支对齐TV均高于早期窗口。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个精确的理论框架来量化和分析KV-cache淘汰引起的不匹配风险，并通过实证数据验证了分解公式的有效性。研究发现分歧后暴露量是影响累积分歧的主要因素（占85-90%），且不同淘汰策略在分歧时机和频率上存在显著差异，为KV-cache淘汰策略的设计与评估提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: KV-cache eviction perturbs the conditional token distributions governing autoregressive generation. We investigate how first-divergence timing and subsequent token mismatch determine cumulative disagreement. We derive an exact decomposition under a specified stepwise maximal coupling: the expected mismatch fraction equals a first-mismatch contribution plus post-divergence exposure multiplied by its mismatch rate. An explicit construction over unrestricted autoregressive kernel pairs realizes the sharp interval of risks compatible with a finite divergence-aligned observation window. Residual-branch conditional Monte Carlo provides unbiased joint estimates of occurrence, occupation, and window/tail contributions, with per-replicate variance dominance for total token loss. Complete trajectories from Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct show that SnapKV at 50% retention enters divergence later and less often than SnapKV-512 or recent-token retention with the same 50% prompt-cache budget, while post-divergence total variation (TV) remains high. In an exploratory analysis of 288 documents, post-divergence exposure accounts for 85-90% of four aggregate mismatch gaps. On 288 independent documents at 90% retention, prespecified comparisons show higher branch-aligned TV in the late than in the early window in both models.

</details>


### [299] [Stable by Construction: Variational Latent Markov Operators for Long-Horizon PDE Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16621)
*Junyi Liao,Johann Guilleminot,Vahid Tarokh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VAMO（变分自编码马尔可夫算子），通过在函数空间构建概率潜变量马尔可夫动力学，利用结构化高斯扰动与变分转移对齐正则化潜变量演化，从而显著提升神经PDE求解器在远超训练时域的长程自回归预测中的稳定性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经PDE求解器在自回归部署时，局部误差会诱发分布偏移并累积，导致超出训练时域的长程预测性能急剧下降。现有确定性方法和通用噪声注入方法均无法有效解决此问题，亟需一种能从原理上增强长程预测稳定性的概率建模方法。

Method: 在函数空间构建变分潜变量马尔可夫框架：编码器将物理状态映射为潜变量分布，转移模型以高斯核形式演化潜变量，解码器将其映射回物理空间。训练时对编码器施加点控谱结构扰动、对转移施加全局标量调制扰动，通过预测项、重建项与KL对齐项联合优化。理论层面推导出确定性均值展开的误差传播界，揭示KL对齐与编码端灵敏度通过几何放大因子累积影响长程误差，而转移端扰动影响局部。

Result: 在1D欧拉方程、2D可压缩流、2D不可压缩Navier-Stokes三个流体力学基准上，VAMO在相对L2、H1、湍流耗散（enstrophy）、细尺度结构（palinstrophy）和能谱误差等所有指标上一致优于直接FNO、加噪FNO和确定性潜变量FNO基线。在低粘度、强分布外推条件下相对L2误差降低51%–87%，H1误差降低61%–94%，且消融实验证实KL一致性项最为关键。

Conclusion: 变分潜变量建模为神经PDE动力学中的鲁棒长程自回归预测提供了一种互补且有效的方法。VAMO通过将训练中的随机性转化为针对确定性均值展开的正则化机制，并辅以KL对齐项控制潜变量转移一致性，在大幅超出训练时域的预测中显著抑制误差累积并保持物理结构。

Abstract: Neural PDE solvers provide efficient surrogates for time-dependent physical systems, but autoregressive prediction over long horizons remains challenging because local errors can induce distribution shift and accumulate under recursive deployment. We develop a variational approach to this problem by introducing latent Markov dynamics in which physical states are represented by latent distributions and evolved through probabilistic transitions. The framework is formulated directly on function spaces and specialized to functional Gaussian models, where structured latent perturbations induce a spectral geometry and variational transition alignment regularizes the learned dynamics. We further analyze how these mechanisms affect autoregressive error propagation, providing a theoretical connection between variational training and long-horizon prediction. We instantiate the framework as the Variational Autoencoding Markov Operator (VAMO), which combines spatially resolved latent fields, structured Gaussian perturbations, and a neural-operator transition. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of VAMO on several fluid-dynamics benchmarks with prediction horizons extending substantially beyond those represented during training, where it consistently reduces error accumulation and improves rollout stability over several deterministic and noise-injection baselines. Overall, these results highlight variational modeling as a complementary approach to robust long-horizon neural PDE dynamics.

</details>


### [300] [GrowMTP: Can RL Grow Its Own Draft Head?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16648)
*Minghua He,Lingzhe Zhang,Yuan Liu,Xiao Zhou,Aiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GrowMTP，一种在强化学习训练循环内部从零在线训练草稿头（speculative decoding draft head）的方法。通过利用RL rollout的窄分布特性和验证步骤产生的监督信号，无需离线预训练即可加速RL训练本身，在Qwen3-4B、MiMo-7B-SFT和Qwen3.5-4B-Base上分别实现1.60×、1.41×和1.20×的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练中rollout生成占训练预算的70-90%，自回归解码是主要瓶颈。推测解码（speculative decoding）虽能加速，但现有草稿头需在RL前离线预训练或预热，带来显著额外成本，与"加速RL运行"的初衷相矛盾。对于没有预训练草稿头的模型（如Qwen3-4B），更无加速路径。

Method: 1. **Draft-Path Reconstruction**：记录每个draft-then-verify周期的草稿轨迹与验证信号，重建与推理时一致的草稿条件状态（而非teacher forcing），消除train-inference不一致性。2. **DCA Loss（Depth-Coupled Acceptance）**：基于接受链的可微分代理目标，对TV-chain surrogate施加对数变换，为链值更小的周期分配更大相对梯度权重。3. **Verify-Gated Masking（VGM）**：将损失截断到第一次拒绝位置（含），排除被丢弃后缀的监督。4. 所有草稿头梯度与策略骨干网络分离（detached），不干扰策略学习，支持从随机初始化开始训练。

Result: - **Qwen3-4B（无草稿头，从零训练）**：500 RL步，K=5，rollout加速2.13×，端到端加速1.60×，τ从1.00增长到2.91，30步内开始低于基线，总GPU小时减少9.4%（vs离线训练头基线502.8h vs 555.3h）。- **Qwen3.5-4B-Base（K=3预训练头）**：200步，DCA在保持策略质量前提下达到τ=3.22-3.31，端到端加速1.20×和1.12×；联合CE训练使策略崩溃（质量为零）。- **MiMo-7B-SFT（K=1预训练头）**：K=5时τ从1.65升至4.04，端到端加速1.41×（数学）和1.35×（代码）；K=7时端到端加速反而下降至1.30×和1.31×，表明最佳深度为K=5而非K越大越好。- **消融实验**：DCA目标在K=5时τ=4.04优于CE(3.86)、KL(3.85)、TV(3.94)；VGM移除使KL加速从1.37×降至1.28×，DCA从1.41×降至1.35×；草稿头仅训练域内有效（数学训练头在代码基准τ=1.45）。- **规模化投影**：在32K rollout长度、2000步下，加速可达2.17×（理论上限2.55×）。

Conclusion: GrowMTP证明了RL运行可从其自身rollout中在线培育草稿头，无需离线预训练或预热阶段，为没有预训练MTP头的模型提供加速路径。其作为模块化组件可集成到现有RL框架中。然而该方法存在局限性：所训练的头专门适应当前rollout分布，跨域泛化能力差（领域τ仅1.45-1.75）；评估仅覆盖4B-7B模型与单节点8卡配置，更大规模与分布式场景的加速效果需直接测量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training drives the frontier capabilities of large language models, with its wall-clock dominated by autoregressive rollout generation. Speculative decoding is an established remedy for this bottleneck, but existing draft heads must be pretrained or warmed up before RL, introducing substantial training cost outside the RL run to be accelerated. We observe that RL training itself provides both conditions required for online draft-head training: its rollout distribution is far narrower than that of pretraining, and its verification step continuously produces supervision signals aligned with this distribution. Building on these observations, we propose GrowMTP, which uses this supervision to train a draft head from scratch entirely within the RL loop, with all head updates detached from the policy backbone. On Qwen3-4B (no draft head), MiMo-7B-SFT (weak head), and Qwen3.5-4B-Base (strong head), GrowMTP achieves rollout speedups of 2.13x, 1.93x, and 1.36x, and end-to-end speedups of 1.60x, 1.41x, and 1.20x, respectively. GrowMTP therefore serves existing RL training frameworks as a modular component, particularly offering a from-scratch acceleration path for models without pretrained draft heads.

</details>


### [301] [A Systematic Evaluation of Machine Learning Methods for Fault Detection and Line Identification in Electrical Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16744)
*Julian Oelhaf,Georg Kordowich,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro,Tomás Arias-Vergara,Andreas Maier,Johann Jäger,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种机器学习模型在10ms超短时间窗口内对电力系统三相短路故障进行检测与故障线路识别的性能，最佳模型F1分数达0.991，处理时间仅0.342ms，满足实时继电保护需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可再生能源大量并网，电网拓扑与故障特征日益复杂，传统基于固定阈值和静态规则的继电保护方法难以满足选择性、安全性和实时性的要求。虽然已有ML方法被提出用于故障检测，但不同研究在电网配置、仿真条件和分析窗口上的差异使得公平比较几乎不可能；更关键的是，尚无研究在最严苛的实时边界条件（10ms）下进行评估。

Method: 使用DIgSILENT PowerFactory对"双线"电网拓扑进行电磁暂态仿真；采用域随机化（domain randomization）策略系统化随机线路参数、故障位置、持续时间、电阻等参数；采样间隔50µs，每episode时长1s；预处理时取故障起点±80ms数据并以5ms步长滑窗，窗口长度依次评估10/20/30/40/50ms；评估14种ML模型（线性模型、KNN、MLP、SVM及多种集成方法），采用10折交叉验证，以F1为主要指标并测量5000次迭代的推理运行时延。

Result: 14个模型中有11个在故障检测任务F1>0.96，6个模型在故障线路识别任务F1>0.96；线性模型（LR、Ridge、SGD）在两项任务中表现均较差（F1<0.69/0.58）；ET、GB、MLP、RF、Stacking、SVM为综合最优模型，10ms窗口下故障检测F1可达0.99，故障线路识别F1可达0.98；最佳模型运行时延0.342±0.509ms；KNN为最慢（88.6±5.8ms）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，ML方法在远低于IEEE/VDEW规定的实时阈值下即可实现高精度的电力系统故障检测与线路识别，MLP、GB与Stacking兼具精度与速度优势；本研究为未来预训练模型跨电网迁移、其他短路类型（两相/接地）、噪声鲁棒性及多样化拓扑的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The integration of renewable energy sources into the electrical grid introduces complex challenges in fault detection and coordination of grid recovery mechanisms. Traditional relay protection systems, which operate based on static rules and predefined thresholds, are inadequate for addressing these challenges, particularly in detecting and isolating faults such as short circuits. Consequently, the conventional methodologies applied to electrical network protection frequently fail to achieve optimal performance in fault detection, especially in terms of adherence to safety standards and the selective limitation of damage. Recent research indicates that machine learning (ML)-based approaches can effectively tackle these issues; however, variations in grid configurations and analysis windows have impeded consistent comparative assessments. In this study, we assess the efficacy of various ML models in detecting electrical faults and pinpointing defective transmission lines within a 10 ms measurement interval - a critical time-frame for real-time operational viability, for the first time. The most effective model attained an F1 score of 0.991 +/- 0.018 and demonstrated a processing time of 0.342ms +/- 0.509ms.

</details>


### [302] [Noise2Noise Revisited: Training Pair Distributions Dominate Loss Choice in Self-Supervised Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16788)
*Dingyan Shang,Zhenyu Xu,Youting Wang,Bonan Shen,Bowen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对 Noise2Noise (N2N) 自监督去噪框架中 L1 损失优于 L2 损失的两种流行解释进行了系统的压力测试。结论是：在对称零均值合成噪声下，L1 与 L2 的总体最优解几乎重合，损失函数差异（<1 dB）主要源于优化动力学（边界影响梯度），而非参数稀疏性；真正决定 N2N 性能的是训练对的分布。在真实相机噪声（SIDD）上，合成高斯训练的 N2N 仅提升 0.8–3.7 dB，而改用 SIDD 自身噪声对训练则提升 9.4–11.0 dB，远超损失函数选择的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 L1 损失优于 L2 损失的两个流行假设——参数稀疏性（Lasso 类比）和损失函数的统计性质差异——未被严格检验过。同时，Noise2Noise 在真实噪声上的性能与损失选择的关系也缺乏受控量化。本文旨在：(1) 通过对照实验澄清 L1 损失与 L1 正则化的混淆；(2) 量化损失函数选择与训练对分布对去噪性能的实际贡献。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明在对称信号后验下 MSE 与 MAE 总体最优解精确重合，在集中后验下近似重合；阐明 L1 损失（残差惩罚）与 L1 正则化（权重惩罚）的本质区别。
2. 控制实验：固定 U-Net 架构、训练预算，对比 MSE、MAE、Charbonnier、Huber、MSE+Lasso 五种损失。
3. 合成臂：在 Kodak24 上评估 5 种噪声族（高斯、随机脉冲、盐椒、泊松、散斑）共 14 列。
4. 真实臂：在 SIDD-Medium sRGB 上用 128/32 场景分割训练，仅使用噪声对，不读取真值。
5. 诊断手段：权重直方图、梯度统计、受污染目标训练、内容匹配对照。

Result: 1. L1 vs L2 损失：MAE 在全部 14 个合成列上略优于 MSE（+0.09 至 +0.78 dB），Wilcoxon 检验在 13/14 列统计显著，3 个随机种子验证；但差距始终 <1 dB。优势集中在 PSNR/SSIM 而非 LPIPS。
2. 稀疏性假说失败：MSE 与 MAE 训练的权重分布不可区分（6.6% vs 6.5% 接近零），而显式 Lasso 产生 91.3% 稀疏权重却不具备 MAE 的跨噪声行为。
3. 真实噪声域差距：合成高斯训练 N2N 在 SIDD 验证块上仅提升 +0.8 至 +3.7 dB；改用 SIDD 自身噪声对训练则提升 +9.4 至 +11.0 dB，远超 BM3D（+5.2 dB）。该 ~8 dB 差距中约 5 dB 来自场景统计匹配，约 3 dB 来自噪声分布匹配。
4. 失败模式：纯 MAE 训练在分布外噪声上表现脆弱（输出饱和），Charbonnier/Huber 的小二次盆地缓解此问题。
5. Poisson 损失不变性：5 种损失差距 ≤0.7 dB，符合 Anscombe 论预测。

Conclusion: 1. 在对称零均值合成噪声下，MSE 与 MAE 的总体最优解几乎重合，损失函数效应（<1 dB，统计显著）由优化动力学（MAE 的有界影响梯度）主导，与参数稀疏性无关。
2. 训练对分布是 N2N 性能的决定性因素（约 8 dB），损失函数选择仅为二阶效应。
3. 训练对分布设定分布内性能，损失函数调节分布外鲁棒性——纯 MAE 在分布外噪声上灾难性失败。
4. 设计启示：每个传感器/生产线可从自身配对捕获重新训练去噪器，无需任何标注数据，特别适用于工业无损检测场景。

Abstract: Noise2Noise (N2N) trains denoisers on pairs of independently corrupted observations, eliminating clean references. We stress-test two natural conjectures about why the L1 loss outperforms L2 here. First, the hypothesis that the L1 loss confers robustness via parameter sparsity confuses the loss with Lasso regularization: an explicit Lasso penalty produces the predicted sparsity yet fails to reproduce L1's cross-noise behavior, while L1- and L2-trained weight distributions are indistinguishable. Second, the population optima of the two losses coincide exactly for symmetric signal posteriors and nearly so for concentrated ones. Measured differences are therefore dominated by optimization dynamics (bounded-influence gradients), which we probe with gradient statistics and contaminated-target training. On Kodak24 with five synthetic noise families, the L1 loss holds a statistically significant edge over L2, below 1 dB PSNR, holding across three seeds on 13 of the 14 noise columns. On real camera noise the loss is not the decisive variable in distribution: on official SIDD validation blocks, synthetic-Gaussian-trained N2N models gain only 0.8 to 3.7 dB over the noisy input regardless of loss, while retraining on SIDD's own noisy pairs, never reading ground truth, gains 9.4 to 11.0 dB, far ahead of BM3D. All metrics are on raw network outputs, and the study makes no leaderboard claim. The training pair distribution, not the loss, carries the inductive bias. That design rule applies wherever clean references are unobtainable, from microscopy to industrial inspection sensors.

</details>


### [303] [Geometry of learning dynamics: Gradient descent versus natural gradient on the ridge of optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16805)
*Akira Tamamori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对核逻辑回归(KLR)Hopfield网络中"优化山脊"(Ridge of Optimization)上的学习动力学进行了信息几何分析，对比了梯度下降(GD)与自然梯度下降(NGD)的轨迹差异，揭示了GD在极端曲率下产生振荡非测地线路径，而NGD沿理想e-测地线稳定收敛且泛化性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高容量联想记忆(KLR Hopfield网络)在"优化山脊"上展现出极端稳定性和高度偏斜的权重谱，但其学习动力学如何收敛至该临界区域尚不清晰。理解优化轨迹对于开发更高效学习算法和揭示自组织原理至关重要。

Method: 基于信息几何框架，将KLR参数空间视为统计流形，以Fisher信息矩阵(FIM)为黎曼度量；通过特征分解对信息增益进行"主增益/尾增益"毕达哥拉斯分解；在FIM特征空间中二维投影GD与NGD的轨迹，并量化"路径比"(path ratio)；实验对比N=50, P=100, γ=0.02(Ridge)与γ=0.1(Local)两种超参设置。

Result: 1) 学习呈现两阶段：先沿主特征方向快速建立主导模式(t<25的主增益)，随后缓慢微调尾增益；2) Ridge上GD沿v₁方向剧烈振荡(Path Ratio≈8.9×)，严重偏离e-测地线，呈现Edge of Stability现象；3) NGD几乎完美跟踪e-测地线(Path Ratio≈1.0×)；4) NGD收敛更快、验证损失更低，泛化性能优于GD。

Conclusion: Ridge上极端的谱集中度(spectral concentration)虽然对GD构成"稳定边缘"障碍，却天然适配信息几何优化方法。NGD通过FIM求逆"展平"流形，克服振荡并找到泛化更好的解。这表明对高度结构化问题，最小值平坦度并非泛化的本质，算法与流形内蕴几何的对齐才是关键。

Abstract: High-capacity associative memories based on Kernel Logistic Regression (KLR) exhibit a "Ridge of Optimization" characterized by extreme stability and a highly skewed weight spectrum. However, the dynamical process by which learning converges to this critical regime has remained unclear. This paper provides a geometric analysis of the learning trajectories on the statistical manifold of a KLR-trained Hopfield network. By comparing the paths of Gradient Descent (GD) and Natural Gradient Descent (NGD), we elucidate the mechanisms governing the optimization process. Our analysis reveals that learning on the Ridge proceeds in two distinct phases. We show that the extreme curvature of the Ridge causes standard GD to follow a highly oscillatory, non-geodesic path. In stark contrast, NGD explicitly corrects for this geometry, following the ideal geodesic path and completely overcoming the instabilities faced by GD. We demonstrate experimentally that NGD not only converges significantly faster but also achieves a solution with superior generalization performance. These results establish that the highly structured geometry of the Ridge is optimally suited for information-geometric optimization, providing a new perspective on the interplay between learning dynamics and emergent representation geometry.

</details>


### [304] [ImpossibleRubrics: Stress-Testing Generated Rubrics as Reward Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16816)
*Bowen Qin,Yi Xie,Yesheng Liu,Xi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 ImpossibleRubrics 基准测试，用于压力测试 LLM 生成评分标准（rubrics）作为奖励信号时的安全性。研究发现，在 169 个"不可能任务"上，11 个主流生成器的评分标准以 8-26% 的频率被攻击者利用，奖励违反证书（certificate）的不诚实答案而非诚实回答。即使附加安全提示，前沿模型仍存在 22-49% 的漏洞率。核心发现是：过于具体的评分标准反而成为攻击路线图，通用标准的被利用率（64%）反而低于 7/11 的定制标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 生成的评分标准被广泛用作强化学习奖励信号和 LLM-as-a-judge 评估工具，其安全性（即是否能诚实地评估答案而非奖励投机取巧的回答）至关重要。Goodhart 定律和奖励黑客（reward hacking）表明，当评分标准作为奖励函数被优化时，可能奖励满足评分标准却不达成真实目标的答案。但现有基准测试（如 RewardBench、AbstentionBench）主要评估奖励模型的偏好排序或回答者的弃答行为，未直接评估"评分标准生成器"在证据约束的不可回答任务上是否能区分诚实与不诚实的回答。

Method: 1. 构建 ImpossibleRubrics 基准：169 个"不可能任务"环境（分 6 类：证据不足、缺失引用支持、反证陷阱、错误前提、不可能穷举、目标冲突）+ 48 个可回答控制任务；每个不可回答任务配有封闭证据包和机器可检查的 Oracle 证书（oracle certificate），规定允许/禁止的声明。<br>2. 对抗评估链：生成器 g 从 (问题, 证据) 生成评分标准 r → 固定攻击者（Claude Opus）阅读 r 生成高奖励答案 a* → 字面判断者（Claude Haiku）按 r 给 a* 和诚实基线 a₀ 评分 → Oracle 验证 a* 是否违反证书。<br>3. 利用（exploited）定义：J(a*;r) ≥ J(a₀;r) ∧ ¬H_c(a*)。<br>4. 两个评估切片：Full-150（150 个环境，无偏基线率）和 Hard-45（45 个高难度环境，应力测试）。<br>5. 11 个生成器横跨 Claude、GPT-5.x 系列、DeepSeek 开放权重模型。<br>6. 九项鲁棒性分析：判断者替换、评分标准重采样、Oracle 敏感性、安全提示消融、人类校准、持出攻击者、组合分析等。

Result: 1. **利用梯度**：Full-150 上 11 个生成器利用率为 8-26%，分三层：前沿模型（Opus 5 为 8%、GPT-5.6 变体 10-11%）< 中层（17-18%）< 轻量模型（GPT-5.4-mini 为 26%）。Hard-45 上利用率跃升至 36-98%。<br>2. **证书忠实参考**：将证书直接渲染为评分标准，利用率为 0/45，表明问题不在任务难度而在评分标准质量。<br>3. **特异性悖论**：通用"果断"评分标准（naive proxy）利用率为 64%，而 11 个生成器中有 7 个定制评分标准被利用得更频繁——任务特定的评分标准反而成为攻击路线图。<br>4. **安全提示无效**：即使附加"奖励证据忠实"的指令，Opus 5/GPT-5.6-sol/Sonnet 5 仍存在 22%/36%/49% 的利用率。<br>5. **Oracle 敏感性极大**：同一冻结数据下，三种 Oracle 配置给出 33.3%、66.7%、75.6% 的利用率（差距 42 个百分点）。<br>6. **评分标准抽样不稳定**：Opus 4.8 上 45 个环境中有 27 个（60%）的利用判定在 k=6 次独立抽样中结果不一致。<br>7. **开放权重对齐**：DeepSeek V4-Flash 利用率（69%）与 GPT-5.5（67%）统计上不可分。

Conclusion: 论文得出三个核心结论：(1) LLM 生成的评分标准存在系统性漏洞——在前沿模型上仍以 8-36% 的频率奖励证书违反的答案，而证书忠实评分为 0%，证实这是评分标准质量差距而非任务难度问题；(2) "更具体"未必"更安全"——任务定制评分标准反而充当攻击路线图，使攻击者清楚知道该伪造哪种声明，这挑战了"评分标准越详细越好"的直觉；(3) 报告的利用率必须附带验证协议说明——同一冻结条件下三种 Oracle 给出 33-76% 的结果，表明测量的奖励黑客率严重依赖验证器选择。论文强调，自动评分标准生成需要：(a) 与评估证书解耦的环境设计（而非固定评分标准）；(b) 明确验证规则和基线审计；(c) 敏感性报告。研究发布基准、生成提示和评估工具，支持后续评分标准的鲁棒性测试。

Abstract: Language model-generated rubrics are increasingly used as reward signals for rubric-based reinforcement learning, LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, and automated grading. Such rubrics are reliable only if they reward honest answers over adversarial answers optimized to exploit them. Yet their robustness to such optimization remains poorly understood. We isolate the hardest regime: impossible tasks, where the prompt pressures the model toward an unsupported conclusion, so the only honest response is to acknowledge the impossibility. We introduce ImpossibleRubrics, a benchmark of 169 impossible tasks spanning six impossibility categories, each paired with a verifiable oracle certificate specifying what an honest answer may and may not claim, together with 48 answerable controls. Rather than providing fixed rubrics, ImpossibleRubrics provides task environments and certificates, allowing rubrics to be generated downstream and then adversarially tested for whether they reward certificate-violating answers. Eleven generators are exploited 8--26% of the time on the unbiased 150-of-169 environment cut; on a deliberately selected stress cut the strongest generator we measured is still exploited 36% while a certificate-faithful rubric is exploited 0%, so what we measure is a rubric-quality gap, not task impossibility. One result runs against intuition. A single generic rubric ("be decisive, penalize hedging") used unchanged for every task is exploited 64% of the time, and seven of the eleven generators are exploited more often than that while writing a rubric tailored to each one. The tailored criteria appear to tell an attacker which claim to fabricate. The problem is not that rubrics are vague; it is that they are specific about the wrong things.

</details>


### [305] [LCAP: Population-Informed Latent Chip Adaptation from Few Output Probes for Photonic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16823)
*Tianyu Gao,Guantian Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LCAP提出了一种"共性优先、个性其次"的框架，通过从历史芯片群体学习可迁移的共享校准，再利用少量无标签输出探针推断新芯片的低维残差修正坐标，实现无需目标设备优化的前馈个性化，从而显著缩小光子神经网络的仿真到硬件差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子神经网络的参数在理想模型下优化，但经制造后会出现相位偏移、分束比误差、量化和串扰等非理想性，导致显著的仿真到硬件(sim-to-real)性能下降。不同芯片的非理想模式各异，若每片芯片都从头校准，部署成本高昂；而现有方法要么针对单设备优化，要么追求跨设备鲁棒的通用解，未能同时利用群体经验与个体差异。直接端到端编码探针响应到隐变量还会因共性失配占主导而面临可辨识性问题。

Method: LCAP采用三阶段方法：(1) **群体共享校准**：在N=80片历史芯片上联合优化一个公共参数向量θ_pop，并加入相对于初始参数的正则化约束；(2) **残差潜在空间构建**：对M=40片教师芯片在θ_pop基础上进一步精修，得到器件特异性残差δ_c = θ_c* - θ_pop，对其去均值后做SVD，取前r=16个右奇异向量构成基矩阵B与均值μ；(3) **探针条件潜在推断**：从128个{-1,+1}^64候选探针中依据跨芯片响应方差离线选择32个固定探针，将探针残差经PCA降至16维特征h_c，通过岭回归(α=100)学习到潜在坐标z的映射W；部署时对未见芯片u只需采集32个无标签探针输出，由ẑ_u = W^T h_u重构残差δ̂_u = μ + Bẑ_u，最终参数为θ̂_u = θ_pop + δ̂_u。芯片群体通过共享/空间/局部三成分构造模拟工艺偏差。

Result: 在三层64模式MZI仿真器(含相位偏差、8位量化和近邻串扰)上的30片未见芯片：直接部署准确率80.41%→共享校准92.69%(+12.27pt，全部30片提升)→LCAP 93.36%(+0.68pt，27/30片提升)，最差芯片准确率从60.21%提升至90.54%，跨芯片标准差从9.19压缩至1.20。消融实验显示：rank=16最优(93.36%)而rank=32虽保留更多能量(91.4%)却略差(93.32%)；32探针优于16/8探针。关键发现是自编码器等高重建保真度表示(AE NMSE=1.52e-5)反而比低重建保真度的线性SVD+岭回归(NMSE=0.365)产生更小的部署增益(+0.47pt vs +0.61pt)，表明可重建性≠部署效用。

Conclusion: LCAP通过分解硬件适配为可迁移群体修正和探针推断的个性化残差，为光子神经网络提供了无需目标设备优化的可扩展sim-to-real适配方案。其"共性优先、个性其次"范式揭示：硬件潜在空间应以对未见芯片的可预测性而非重建保真度为评判标准；紧凑且可从稀疏观测中辨识的线性残差空间优于非线性高保真自编码器。该工作为大规模同设计芯片部署中的复用群体校准经验提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Photonic neural networks (PNNs) offer efficient analog inference, but parameters optimized under ideal device models can degrade after fabrication, creating a persistent simulation-to-hardware (sim-to-real) gap. When many identically designed chips are deployed, calibrating each device from scratch compounds this cost. We propose Latent Chip Adaptation from Probes (LCAP), a population-informed framework that decomposes hardware adaptation into a transferable population correction and probe-inferred latent personalization. LCAP first learns a shared correction from 80 historical chips, then extracts a low-dimensional correction space from device-specific refinements. At deployment, 32 fixed unlabeled output probes infer an unseen chip's latent correction coordinates, enabling feed-forward personalization without target-device optimization. On a three-layer 64-mode MZI simulator with phase variation, beam-splitter errors, quantization, and crosstalk, accuracy improves from 80.4147% under direct deployment to 92.6860% after shared calibration and 93.3617% with LCAP. LCAP improves 27/30 unseen chips and raises worst-device accuracy from 89.18% to 90.54%.

</details>


### [306] [Adapting to Decision-Relevant Non-Stationarity in Decentralized Heterogeneous Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16824)
*Zhaojun Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对去中心化异构多智能体老虎机系统，提出决策相关非平稳性的概念与DRFC算法及其扩展。核心思想是将局部奖励变化（S_loc）与全局决策切换（S_dec）区分开来，通过新鲜等智能体比较协议，仅在全局最优臂真正改变时才适应，从而忽略在智能体平均层面相互抵消的局部变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦推荐、边缘决策、区域定价等异构去中心化系统中，局部奖励可能频繁变化但网络层面的最优决策保持不变。传统方法对每个局部变化都进行重置，浪费了样本和通信资源。本文的动机是建立正确的适应单位——网络级最优动作的变化，而非局部分布变化。

Method: 1. 提出Decision-Relevant Fresh Comparison (DRFC)算法：定期执行新鲜的、平衡的、智能体间平等的臂比较，仅在新鲜全局证据表明最优公共臂改变时才切换。2. 提出DRFC-Probe变体：使用廉价探测触发完整确认扫描，移除保守边界项。3. 提出DRFC-Seq扩展：在不同的时间平均基准下，使用任意时有效的滑动窗口监控器处理渐进漂移。4. 证明高概率动态遗憾界，认证成本与图传播延迟均有匹配的信息论下界。

Result: 1. Theorem 1：DRFC动态共识遗憾为Õ(NK(h+D_G)T/M + Σ(1+L_r/M)C_r + NS_dec(M+H_scan+D_G))，不包含S_loc适应项。2. Theorem 2：信息论下界Ω(S_dec(K-1)/Δ + NS_dec D_G Δ)，证明认证和图传播不可避免。3. Theorem 3：类相对分离定理，任何局部变化反应性协议在S_dec=0的抵消实例上支付Ω(S_loc)。4. Theorem 4：DRFC-Seq在时间平均决策基准下的遗憾界。5. 实验验证：在MovieLens-1M回放上，DRFC-Seq实现2710±6的最低决策遗憾和0.00的误切换率，显著优于其他基线。

Conclusion: 在异构网络中，适应复杂度应跟随共同决策切换S_dec而非局部变化S_loc。DRFC系列算法通过新鲜等智能体认证机制，有效忽略全局抵消的决策无关变化。DRFC-Probe在切换轴上达到率最优。DRFC-Seq在窗口平均边缘条件下实现漂移鲁棒性。整体而言，该工作为去中心化异构老虎机中的决策相关非平稳性提供了完整的算法设计和理论保证框架。

Abstract: Decentralized bandit systems often contain heterogeneous agents: rewards can change at individual agents even when the best action for the network stays the same. These local changes may cancel when rewards are averaged across agents, so the number of local changes $\Stloc$ can be much larger than the number of changes in the best common arm $\Stdec$. We introduce Decision-Relevant Fresh Comparison (DRFC), which uses new, balanced samples from all agents to compare arms at the network level and switches only when fresh global evidence indicates that the common best arm has changed. We prove a high-probability dynamic regret bound with no adaptation term depending on $\Stloc$, and show that every algorithm must still pay for identifying genuine decision switches and propagating them through the communication graph. Under a distinct time-average benchmark, an anytime-valid sliding-window extension handles gradual drift; experiments on synthetic, semi-real, and MovieLens-1M replays show that DRFC ignores decision-irrelevant local changes while the extension avoids false switches.

</details>


### [307] [Information Geometric Self-Organization at the Edge of Stability in High-Capacity Kernel Associative Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16827)
*Akira Tamamori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从信息几何和优化动力学的角度，研究了基于核逻辑回归（KLR）的高容量Hopfield联想记忆中"优化山脊"（Ridge of Optimization）的几何本质及其形成机制。核心发现是：最优存储容量发生在Hessian矩阵的rank-1谱坍缩边界附近（高曲率的几何奇异点），而非平坦极小值处；梯度下降通过Edge of Stability（EoS）现象实现瞬态自稳定，参数沿主导方向的曲率被动态调整至学习率决定的稳定极限2/η附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前经验性研究发现KLR Hopfield网络存在一个称为"优化山脊"的超参数区域，可最大化吸引子稳定性，但其几何本质及梯度下降如何抵达该区域仍不清楚。论文旨在揭示该山脊对应的损失景观几何结构，以及一阶优化方法在病态高曲率景观中的导航机制。这对理解结构化表示学习中的优化动力学具有普遍意义。

Method: 1. 模型：基于高斯RBF核的KLR Hopfield网络，双极性神经元，L2正则化负对数似然作为损失函数；输入势由对偶变量α_μi加权核函数构成。2. 静态几何分析：通过L-BFGS-B收敛至局部极小，计算Hessian H = KDK + λK的谱浓度（λmax/Tr(H)）和最大曲率log λmax，构建(P/N, γ)相图，与20%噪声检索成功率叠加。3. 动态分析：使用全批量GD（零初始化，η=0.1，λ=0.01），追踪λmax(H)和归一化曲率ηλmax/2随训练epoch的演化，对比三种学习率η∈{0.05, 0.1, 0.2}。4. 理论推导：(a) γ→0时对RBF核Taylor展开，证明Gram矩阵K退化为秩1矩阵J=11⊤，从而FIM≈Tr(D)·J；(b) 建立沿主导特征向量的一维约简差分方程x_{t+1}≈(1-ηλmax(x_t))x_t，揭示logistic方差p(1-p)介导的负反馈自稳定机制。5. 实验设置：随机二值模式，N=50或100，相图为30×30网格。

Result: 1. 相图分析显示，检索成功的相边界与谱浓度→1.0（rank-1坍缩）的区域精确重合；优化山脊（γ≈0.02）位于该坍缩边界附近，最大曲率λmax被高度放大（>100）。2. GD学习轨迹呈现三阶段结构：(i) 初始过冲失稳，λmax(0)>2/η；(ii) 瞬态自稳定，三种学习率下λmax分别精确振荡于40、20、10附近（即2/η），归一化曲率反复触碰1.0阈值；(iii) 第30-40 epoch后脱离振荡，稳定收敛。3. 理论验证：FIM的最大特征值γ→0时渐近为P·Tr(D)，仅有一个非零特征值，证实rank-1坍缩。4. 一维模型预测：logistic方差随参数幅度增大而单调下降，形成"失稳→方差抑制→曲率下降→边界平衡"的负反馈环。5. 附录显示三阶段结构在不同存储负载P/N∈{2,8,16}下均成立，但负载越高初始曲率越大，振荡阶段越长。

Conclusion: 1. 高容量联想记忆的最优表示并非形成于平坦极小值，而是动态雕刻于几何奇异点（rank-1谱坍缩边界）的高度弯曲区域。2. 该山脊处的平衡机制：常数核分量J提供巨大的全局恢复力（主曲率），而XX⊤型Hebbian分量保留模式判别所需的最小谱多样性。3. EoS现象是KLR模型中一种基本的自校正性质——logistic预测方差p(1-p)介导的负反馈使GD能在病态高曲率景观中存活，这为理解深度学习中sharp landscape下的优化提供了具体例证。4. 挑战平坦极小值假设：在结构化表示任务中，主曲率的选择性放大对形成深而宽的吸引盆地是有益的，前提是保留少量非零尾随特征值用于模式判别。5. 对曲率感知优化（如自然梯度下降）的启示：在谱退化区域直接求逆FIM数值不稳定，需引入阻尼或伪逆处理。6. 局限：研究限于独立随机模式，未涉及真实数据的结构相关性；动态模型为一维定性描述，缺少高维严格动力学分析。

Abstract: High-capacity associative memories based on Kernel Logistic Regression (KLR) exhibit exceptional storage capabilities and robustness. Previous empirical studies identified a hyperparameter regime, the "Ridge of Optimization," where attractor stability is maximized. However, the geometric nature of this regime and the optimization dynamics required to reach it have remained unclear. In this paper, we investigate the static geometry of the parameter space and the learning trajectory of Gradient Descent (GD) in KLR-trained Hopfield networks. Using the eigenvalue spectrum of the Hessian, we reveal that the Ridge corresponds to a phase boundary located adjacent to a rank-1 spectral collapse, acting as a geometric singularity where the principal curvature is massively amplified. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the learning dynamics exhibit a transient self-stabilizing behavior driven by the Edge of Stability (EoS) phenomenon. Rather than seeking flat regions, the network parameters are driven toward a state where the local curvature dynamically equilibrates near the stability limit dictated by the learning rate, allowing the optimization to survive the initial instability. We provide analytical derivations for both the rank-1 asymptotic collapse and the dynamic feedback loop governing this equilibration. These findings suggest that optimal, high-capacity memory representations are not formed in flat minima, but are dynamically sculpted at the highly curved boundaries of geometric singularities.

</details>


### [308] [Can Deep Learning Achieve Cross-Physics Mapping?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16853)
*Pengfei Zhu,Julien Lecompagnon,Mathias Ziegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨物理场映射（CPM）框架，利用算子学习在由不同PDE控制的物理场之间建立映射。通过匹配无量纲尺度原理，在扩散场和波动场之间构建配对数据集，并系统评估七种深度学习架构在扩散→波动和波动→扩散两个方向的性能，发现存在显著的方向不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统算子学习方法局限于同一物理域内的映射（如学习同一PDE的解算子），而实际传感和成像系统本质上涉及不同物理域之间的交互（如热扩散产生红外信号、声波引起机械振动等）。本文旨在探索深度学习能否在不同物理方程控制的场之间建立有意义的映射关系，扩展物理信息学习从"解方程"到"连接物理世界"。

Method: 1. 提出跨物理场映射（CPM）理论框架，通过兼容的潜表示存在性定理证明跨物理算子的存在性和神经算子的通用逼近性；2. 提出无量纲标度匹配原理（Fo ∼ Λ²），对齐扩散和波动系统的特征演化尺度；3. 构建COMSOL配对数据集（1000个样本），共享潜几何结构但分别由抛物型和双曲型方程独立生成；4. 采用七种架构进行双向评估：ResUNet（卷积基线）、U-NO、WNO、LNO、GNO、DeepONet、FNO；5. 所有架构采用统一的MSE+相对L2复合损失函数，独立标准化输入输出场。

Result: 1. 扩散→波动方向更具挑战性（需从平滑扩散场恢复波前、相位、走时信息）：U-NO最优，相对L2误差0.307，R²=0.905；2. 波动→扩散方向更稳定（主要需抑制振荡分量）：GNO最优，相对L2误差0.154，R²=0.935；LNO获得最低MAE（1.707）；3. 神经算子普遍优于ResUNet卷积基线，验证跨物理变换的非局部本质；4. 传统虚波变换（virtual-wave transform）作为解析基线表现较差（相对误差0.65-1.92），证实算子学习的必要性；5. 方向不对称性归因于扩散对高频信息的不可逆衰减。

Conclusion: 深度学习能够在共享潜流形上建立不同物理模态之间的有用映射，前提是源系统和目标系统具有兼容的无量纲特征尺度。可实现的精度本质上受限于控制物理的方向依赖信息内容——扩散→波动方向的信息损失是不可逆的。U-NO的多尺度算子结构在扩散→波动方向表现最佳，而GNO的全局token交互在波动→扩散方向最优。该工作将物理信息学习从单方程求解扩展到物理域间翻译，为传感、成像和科学机器学习的统一框架开辟了新方向。未来工作将关注可辨识性、不确定性量化、物理约束学习和更广泛传感模态的实验验证。

Abstract: Can deep learning translate physical fields governed by fundamentally different equations? We address this question by introducing Cross-Physics Mapping (CPM), an operator-learning framework for mappings between heterogeneous physical domains. We formulate sufficient conditions for such mappings through compatible latent representations and propose a dimensionless scaling principle that aligns the characteristic evolution scales of the source and target systems without assuming their dynamical equivalence. As a representative test, paired diffusion and wave fields are generated independently from their respective parabolic and hyperbolic equations while sharing the same latent geometry, material heterogeneity, excitation, and dimensionless scale. Seven architectures-ResUNet, DeepONet, Fourier, latent, wavelet, U-shaped, and Galerkin neural operators-are evaluated for both diffusion-to-wave and wave-to-diffusion mappings. The results reveal a strong directional asymmetry. Diffusion-to-wave reconstruction is more challenging because it requires recovering wavefront, phase, and time-of-flight information attenuated by diffusion; U-NO performs best in this direction, achieving a relative $\ell_2$ error of $0.307$ and an $R^2$ of $0.905$. Wave-to-diffusion mapping is considerably more stable, with GNO attaining a relative $\ell_2$ error of $0.154$ and an $R^2$ of $0.935$. Neural operators generally outperform the conventional convolutional baseline, highlighting the nonlocal nature of cross-physics transformations. These findings demonstrate that deep learning can establish useful mappings between distinct physical modalities on a shared latent manifold, while the achievable accuracy remains fundamentally constrained by the direction-dependent information content of the governing physics.

</details>


### [309] [HyCoSeq: Contextual Hyperbolic Representation Learning for Genomic Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16925)
*Chenhao Zeng,Zhibin Pu,Shufei Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyCoSeq 是一个用于基因组序列分类的上下文双曲表示学习框架，通过加权洛伦兹残差聚合和双向 LSTM 上下文建模，在紧凑架构下实现与大规模预训练 DNA 语言模型相媲美的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双曲基因组模型（如 HGE）依赖洛伦兹卷积学习局部表示，但其残差路径仅聚合类空间分量而非完整洛伦兹表示，缺乏清晰的几何解释；同时，局部卷积编码难以捕获调控任务所需的双向序列级上下文信息（如 motif 空间排布、间距与侧翼序列）。

Method: 1. 多曲率洛伦兹编码：核苷酸级 one-hot 编码逐位置投影到洛伦兹流形，经 M 个含可学习曲率的洛伦兹卷积块处理；2. 加权洛伦兹残差聚合（LResNet 风格）：通过共享归一化因子将 shortcut 与变换分支的完整洛伦兹表示直接加权组合，保持流形约束；3. 双向序列级上下文建模：在类空间坐标上运行单层 BiLSTM（每方向隐藏维度 16），线性变换后逐位置投影回洛伦兹流形；4. 洛伦兹展平、全连接、激活与洛伦兹 MLR 分类头，端到端交叉熵优化。

Result: 在 TEB 七个任务上消融实验表明两个组件均带来增益，HyCoSeq 平均 MCC 较 HGE 提升 5.53 个百分点；在 GUE 17 个数据集上以约 4.6M 参数取得平均秩 3.18，优于所有双曲基线，仅次于参数量约 25.4 倍的 DNABERT-2-PT；在 GUE 与 GB 跨任务类别（表观标记、转录因子、剪接位点、启动子、增强子、调控元件、开放染色质）上均取得最高平均 MCC。

Conclusion: HyCoSeq 验证了几何一致的双曲残差聚合与双向序列级上下文建模的互补性，证明了紧凑双曲架构无需大规模预训练即可在多种基因组分类任务中与大型预训练 DNA 语言模型竞争，凸显了双曲表示学习在基因组序列建模中的潜力。

Abstract: Hyperbolic geometry provides a natural inductive bias for genomic representation learning, but existing hyperbolic genomic models primarily use Lorentz convolutions to learn local sequence representations, while their residual pathways do not directly aggregate full Lorentz representations. We propose HyCoSeq, a contextual hyperbolic representation learning framework for genomic sequences. HyCoSeq incorporates weighted Lorentzian residual aggregation into multi-curvature Lorentz encoding, allowing full Lorentz representations to participate directly in geometry-consistent local aggregation. It further introduces a bidirectional long short-term memory network that integrates information from both sequence directions to learn contextual relationships among local representations at different positions within a genomic sequence, thereby extending local hyperbolic convolutional encoding to sequence-level contextualized representations. Extensive experiments across diverse genomic tasks show that HyCoSeq outperforms existing hyperbolic baselines and, without large-scale genomic pretraining, achieves competitive performance against substantially larger pretrained DNA language models.

</details>


### [310] [CLARE: Scalable Class-Incremental Continual Learning via a Sparsity-Based Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17026)
*Yunxiang Fu,Meng Lou,Zicheng Liao,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLARE提出了一种基于稀疏性的类增量持续学习框架,通过两阶段优化为每个任务学习稀疏的适配器掩码,并将所有任务更新累积到共享适配器中,在长任务序列上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练模型的类增量持续学习方法在长任务序列(多达100个任务)上存在严重的可扩展性瓶颈,主要由于任务间干扰和可塑性丧失。稀疏微调研究表明仅更新少量参数即可达到接近全量微调的性能,这启发了作者探索稀疏化策略以缓解持续学习中的灾难性遗忘。

Method: CLARE采用两阶段稀疏适配器学习:(1)Stage 1通过L1正则化目标在可用适配器坐标上训练少量epoch,根据更新幅度大小识别任务关键参数,生成稀疏二进制掩码;(2)Stage 2重置适配器后,仅优化掩码选中的参数。每个任务从剩余未分配的坐标池中按比例(ρ=95%)选择参数,通过简单累加融合到共享适配器。推理时无需任务标识或路由机制,使用单一共享适配器。

Result: 在OmniBenchmark-1k(100任务)上达到66.88%的最终准确率,比EASE提升4.64%,比SD-LoRA提升超过38%;在50任务ImageNet-R和ImageNet-A上分别超越InfLoRA 13.9%和19.28%;在10/20任务的标准基准测试的6个设置中均取得最高准确率;消融实验验证了容量感知稀疏性和两阶段学习的必要性,且对适配器宽度变化(16-128)表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CLARE通过稀疏驱动的适配器机制有效解决了长任务序列的类增量持续学习问题,无需任务特定路由即可在10到100个任务范围内保持稳定性能,证明了容量感知稀疏参数分配的可扩展性。但其有限的几何容量调度最终会饱和适配器,无法支持极长任务流(如10k任务),未来工作可探索动态容量分配策略及跨模态扩展。

Abstract: Continual learning must balance the learning of new knowledge with the retention of previously learned knowledge to incrementally learn tasks from a data stream without catastrophic forgetting. While leveraging pretrained models has significantly advanced continual learning, existing methods exhibit a scalability bottleneck when trained sequentially on many tasks, suffering from performance degradation due to inter-task interference and loss of plasticity. Inspired by evidence that sparse fine-tuning achieves performance comparable to full fine-tuning, this paper presents a novel sparsity-driven continual learning framework. Our continual learning method, termed CLARE, operates in two stages: it first identifies a sparse, task-critical parameter mask via a sparsity-inducing objective, then performs mask-constrained fine-tuning by only optimizing parameters selected by the mask. This two-stage sparse adapter mechanism enables all tasks to be accumulated within a shared adapter space while reducing destructive interference across tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate the scalability of CLARE. On the long task-sequence benchmark Omnibenchmark-1k, CLARE outperforms strong baselines in final accuracy by a large margin, e.g, improving EASE by 4.64% and 13.34% after learning 100 tasks, respectively.

</details>


### [311] [Learning Options for Compositional Motor Control with Adapter Banks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17042)
*Sreejan Kumar,Marcelo Mattar,Lea Duncker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CABRA架构，将神经科学中丘脑-皮层回路的低秩扰动原理转化为可端到端学习的运动控制模型，通过共享循环核心+残差适配器库+离散潜在码选择机制，在闭环生物力学控制中涌现低秩基元，支持分布外组合迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受神经科学理论启发，运动基元可能通过共享循环网络的低秩扰动实现，但该机制如何从演示中端到端学习尚未解决；同时传统分层强化学习中的options方法难以保证组合性，即基元可无干扰地重组成新序列以支持分布外任务。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构：编码器基于CompILE框架从教师演示中分割轨迹并推断离散潜在码（K=10，分伸/缩两阶段）；解码器为闭环控制器，共享LSTM核心（H=256）由适配器库残差调制，每个适配器为两层非线性全秩调制器（无秩约束），但通过训练涌现低秩结构；通过Gumbel-softmax采样、直通估计器和DIAYN辅助损失实现端到端训练。

Result: 1)适配器涌现低秩结构（有效秩~5-6，核心流形~30），任务表征占据高度分离的子空间（重叠0.18 vs 教师0.69）；2)分布外双瓣轨迹迁移中学生L1=0.005显著优于教师L1=0.04（约一个数量级差距），蝴蝶轨迹也保持~5倍优势；3)硬离散策略下仍保持2.5倍优势；4)适配器库消融（K=1或无适配器）则无法学习多任务。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了神经科学丘脑-皮层回路原理可转化为有效的学习架构，通过共享核心+低秩扰动+离散选择实现了真正的组合泛化，为运动控制及更广泛的组合智能提供了受神经科学启发的架构设计原则。

Abstract: Learning flexible motor primitives is a hallmark of skilled motor control. Recent neuroscience theory proposes that motor primitives may be implemented as low-rank perturbations of a shared recurrent network, but leaves open how such a system is learned. We translate this principle into a novel architecture for learning motor skills end-to-end: a shared recurrent core modulated by a bank of residual adapters, each selected by a discrete latent code. Trained on closed-loop biomechanical control, the adapters develop emergent low-rank perturbations of the recurrent dynamics despite no architectural rank constraint, placing task representations in disparate subspaces of the shared core network. A simple high-level policy over the learned options, optimized while the whole network is frozen, sequences the low-rank adapters to produce novel out-of-distribution movements. We demonstrate the ability to generalize to novel motor sequences within the closed-loop control setting, improving on the generalization error of a task-input-conditioned multitask baseline by upto order of magnitude.

</details>


### [312] [High-Fidelity Digital Twin Data Models by Randomized Dynamic Mode Decomposition and Deep Learning with Applications in Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17101)
*Diana A. Bistrian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合随机化动态模态分解（Randomized DMD）与深度学习非线性自回归估计器（NLARX）的非侵入式框架，用于从数值模拟输出中构建高保真数字孪生数据模型（DTM），并在三个不同雷诺数（Re=10²、10³、10⁴）的黏性Burgers方程激波模拟中验证了其精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体动力学中的复杂高维数据难以直接用于分析和预测；传统POD/DMD降阶模型在处理非侵入式离散数据时，模态选择困难且SVD计算昂贵，Galerkin投影等侵入式方法又需访问控制方程。本文旨在提出一种完全非侵入、计算高效且能自动确定最优模态数的降阶框架，以低成本、高精度地复现原始动力学行为。

Method: 方法分为离线与在线两阶段：(1) 离线阶段——对快照矩阵V₀施加随机投影（k-RSVD）实现降维SVD，再通过求解最小化问题得到伴随矩阵的特征值（增长率σ、频率ω）和DMD主导模态，将最优秩的确定转化为以最小重构误差为约束的优化问题，用模拟退火等全局优化方法求解；(2) 在线阶段——以DMD模态幅值序列作为输入，利用10个隐层的前馈级联神经网络实现的NLARX非线性自回归估计器，估计连续时间的时变系数，最终由主导模态与时变系数的线性组合构建DTM。整个过程不需对控制方程做Galerkin投影。

Result: 在Re=10²、10³、10⁴三个激波算例中，DMD-ROM的最优秩分别为15、20、20，重构误差低至10⁻⁷~10⁻⁸量级，相关系数均达1.0000；引入NLARX后的DTM误差在10⁻⁴量级（8.08×10⁻⁴、1.31×10⁻⁴、1.40×10⁻⁴），相关系数仍保持1.0000；离线CPU时间不超过2秒，在线CPU时间8~17秒，验证表明DTM能以极低计算成本高精度复现复杂激波动力学，且无需选择模态的额外准则。

Conclusion: 本文证明：随机化DMD与深度学习NLARX的结合可构建高精度、低复杂度、完全非侵入的数字孪生数据模型；随机化方法消除了DMD对显式模态选择准则的依赖，NLARX神经网络则使时变系数的在线辨识快速准确；DTM在三个递增复杂度的激波问题中均表现出优异的数值精度与计算效率，为流体动力学高维数据的高保真低成本建模提供了一种有效的新框架。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is the identification of high-fidelity digital twin data models from numerical code outputs by non-intrusive techniques (i.e., not requiring Galerkin projection of the governing equations onto the reduced modes basis). In this paper the author defines the concept of the digital twin data model (DTM) as a model of reduced complexity that has the main feature of mirroring the original process behavior. The significant advantage of a DTM is to reproduce the dynamics with high accuracy and reduced costs in CPU time and hardware for settings difficult to explore because of the complexity of the dynamics over time. This paper introduces a new framework for creating efficient digital twin data models by combining two state-of-the-art tools: randomized dynamic mode decomposition and deep learning artificial intelligence. It is shown that the outputs are consistent with the original source data with the advantage of reduced complexity. The DTMs are investigated in the numerical simulation of three shock wave phenomena with increasing complexity. The author performs a thorough assessment of the performance of the new digital twin data models in terms of numerical accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [313] [Neural Field Ensembles for Aerodynamic Surface Prediction: Winning Solution to the ONERA CRM Wall Distribution 2025 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17160)
*Lionel Salesses,Caroline Sainvitu,Tariq Benamara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件神经场（Conditional Neural Fields）的集成学习方法，用于预测NASA CRM翼-体-短舱-挂架构型的气动壁面分布（压力系数和摩擦系数）。该方法在2025年ONERA CRM壁面分布回归挑战赛中获得第一名，在隐藏测试集上取得8.81分，优于组织方提供的Global MLP基线（8.64分），且参数量少约三个数量级。核心创新在于：通过傅里叶特征编码（FFE）缓解MLP的频谱偏差、采用相对平方误差损失（RSE）与评估指标对齐、利用k折交叉验证集成策略提升小数据场景下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD仿真计算成本高昂，机器学习代理模型是加速气动分析的有效途径。然而，针对真实复杂飞机构型构建精确代理模型面临多重挑战：复杂几何、多流态（亚音速至跨音速乃至近失速）、有限的高保真训练数据。ONERA CRM壁面分布挑战赛正是针对这一难题设计的基准测试，包含468个RANS仿真，涵盖宽范围的马赫数、迎角和滞止压力条件。

Method: 1. **神经场公式化**：将问题建模为连续函数 f_θ(x,y,z,n_x,n_y,n_z, AoA, M∞, p_i) → (Cp, Cfx, Cfy, Cfz)，无需网格连接信息。
2. **傅里叶特征编码（FFE）**：对空间坐标进行随机频率编码（σ²=1, N_e=128），缓解MLP的频谱偏差以捕捉激波等高频结构。
3. **MLP架构**：5个隐藏层，每层700神经元，LeakyReLU激活，约2.15×10⁶参数。
4. **相对平方误差损失（RSE）**：L = (1/N)Σ(y_i - ŷ_i)²/(y_i² + ε)，与挑战赛评估指标（wrMAE）对齐。
5. **k折交叉验证集成**：312个仿真分为5折，每折训练8个模型，选取最优20个模型集成，约7.3×10⁷参数。
6. **优化器**：使用SOAP优化器配合线性warmup+余弦退火学习率调度。

Result: 通过渐进式消融实验验证各组件贡献：
- 初始MLP原型：Score 7.80
- + FFE编码：Score 8.34（R²从0.908提升至0.943）
- + 增大模型容量：Score 8.45
- + RSE损失：Score 8.58（wrMAE从0.276降至0.251）
- + 集成学习：Score 8.65
- + k折交叉验证集成：Score 8.81（R²=0.973, wrMAE=0.210）

最终在隐藏测试集上获得冠军，优于Global MLP基线（R²=0.956, wrMAE=0.228），且参数量少约230倍。定性分析显示模型能准确捕捉跨音速激波和摩擦场的局部不连续结构。

Conclusion: 研究证明精心设计的坐标神经场是复杂气动几何在有限数据条件下的高效鲁棒代理建模框架。关键结论：(1)频谱偏差缓解（FFE）对捕捉跨音速激波至关重要；(2)损失函数与评估指标对齐比单纯增加模型复杂度更有效；(3)交叉验证集成能显著提升最差情况误差的鲁棒性；(4)对于无网格连接信息的场景，神经场优于GNN等拓扑依赖方法。未来方向包括主动学习、多几何构型基础模型、不确定性量化及物理信息加权策略。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogate models offer a promising alternative to high-fidelity Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations for aerodynamic analysis and design. However, constructing accurate surrogates for realistic aircraft configurations remain challenging due to complex geometries, multiple flow regimes, and limited training data. This work presents the methodology that achieved first place in the ONERA CRM Wall Distribution Regression Challenge, which focuses on predicting pressure and skin-friction coefficient distributions over the NASA Common Research Model wing-body-pylon-nacelle configuration under different operating conditions. The proposed approach formulates the problem as a conditional neural field mapping spatial coordinates, surface normals, and operating conditions to aerodynamic wall quantities. Fourier feature encoding, a relative squared error objective aligned with the challenge metric, ensemble learning, and $k$-fold cross-validation are progressively introduced to improve prediction accuracy and exploit the limited training data. Beyond presenting the final methodology, the paper documents the successive model design choices that led to the winning solution through a comprehensive ablation study and discusses several alternative approaches that were investigated but ultimately discarded. On the hidden competition test set, the proposed methodology achieves an overall score of 8.81, outperforming the strongest organizer-provided baseline, which achieved a score of 8.64, while requiring approximately three orders of magnitude fewer trainable parameters. These results illustrate that carefully designed coordinate-based neural fields constitute an efficient and robust framework for aerodynamic surrogate modeling on complex geometries under limited-data conditions.

</details>


### [314] [IRENE: A Convolutional GRU Ensemble Model for Radar Precipitation Nowcasting over Italy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17175)
*Alessandro Camilletti,Gabriele Franch,Elena Tomasi,Marco Cristoforetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRENE是一个基于多尺度ConvGRU编码器-预报器架构的深度学习集合预报模型，用于意大利域1km/5min分辨率的概率雷达降水短临预报，提出了三种训练配置（afCRPS、对抗GAN、光谱约束GAN-RAPSD），在CRPS和秩直方图校准上优于STEPS和DGMR基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习降水短临预报模型均未在意大利全国雷达系统上训练，限制了其在意大利域的直接应用；意大利DPC国家雷达产品为高分辨率业务化短临预报提供了天然的训练数据。

Method: 模型采用多尺度ConvGRU编码器-自回归预报器架构，通过在最粗尺度注入高斯噪声生成集合预报；三种训练配置分别使用afCRPS损失、3D PatchGAN对抗损失、以及在此基础上增加径向平均功率谱密度（RAPSD）的显式惩罚项；训练数据采用重要性采样聚焦降水事件，并在Z=200R^1.6转换空间中进行优化。

Result: 三种IRENE配置在所有预报时效（最长120分钟）上的CRPS均低于STEPS和DGMR，秩直方图更接近均匀；IRENE在0-90分钟内MAE最优；IRENE-GAN恢复了小尺度方差但在长预报时效产生过度精细功率；IRENE-GAN-RAPSD在中尺度改善了谱一致性但在最小尺度（≈4km）上未能有效控制过度功率。

Conclusion: IRENE证明了多尺度ConvGRU架构在意大利域业务化概率短临预报中的可行性；对抗训练可恢复被点态概率损失平滑掉的精细结构，但需要进一步精细化以控制长预报时效下最小尺度的过度功率；未来工作将结合卫星和NWP场、累积降水验证以及下游水文应用集成。

Abstract: We present IRENE (Italian Radar Ensemble Nowcasting Experiment), a deep learning model for probabilistic short-range precipitation nowcasting over the Italian domain at \SI{1}{km} spatial and 5 min temporal resolution. IRENE adopts an encoder--forecaster architecture built on multi-scale Convolutional Gated Recurrent Units (ConvGRUs), trained on the national radar composite produced by the Italian Civil Protection Department (DPC). An importance-sampling scheme focuses training on precipitation-relevant events, while the almost-fair Continuous Ranked Probability Score (afCRPS) is adopted as the primary probabilistic loss function. Two additional training configurations are proposed: an adversarial (GAN) variant, IRENE-GAN, designed to improve the spatial sharpness of the generated forecasts, and a spectrally constrained variant, IRENE-GAN-RAPSD, in which the adversarial objective is complemented by an explicit penalty on the radially averaged power spectral density. The three configurations are evaluated against the stochastic extrapolation method STEPS and the pre-trained deep learning model DGMR. All IRENE configurations attain a lower Continuous Ranked Probability Score than both benchmarks at every lead time and rank histograms closer to uniformity, indicating better probabilistic skill and ensemble calibration. In terms of ensemble-mean mean absolute error the advantage is confined to the first 90 min, beyond which the strongly damped DGMR fields and, to a lesser extent, STEPS become competitive. Spectral analysis shows that the adversarial training removes the progressive loss of small-scale variance exhibited by IRENE, at the cost of an excess of fine-scale power at long lead times that the spectral penalty only partially controls.

</details>


### [315] [LoopSpec: Pipelined Self-Speculative Decoding for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17184)
*SangLyul Cho,Langqing Cui,Sehoon Kim,Dongsu Han,Insu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopSpec提出了一种无需训练的流水线式自推测解码框架，专为循环Transformer设计，通过从早期循环状态提取草稿令牌并以流水线方式与目标验证重叠执行，实现了最高6.83倍的无损推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过重复应用共享Transformer块以紧凑参数获得强性能，但由于每个循环深度都要访问共享权重，导致解码延迟高于同等参数规模的标准Transformer。现有推测解码方法需要外部草稿模型，带来训练成本和分布失配问题，因此需要一种无需训练的、自推测式的加速方法。

Method: 1. 利用循环Transformer中间状态作为草稿源（无需额外草稿模型）；2. 流水线调度：早期深度d₁处草稿下一token，深度d₂处提供残差第二提案作为回退；3. 门控机制：仅当深层状态对主候选失去信心时触发第二提案，控制分支数指数增长；4. 级联验证：依次验证第一/第二提案，否则进行残差重采样，全程保持无损解码；5. 基于中间-目标TV距离的幂律衰减，推导出最优提案深度的闭式解。

Result: 在Ouro系列（R=4）和Raven系列（R=32）共7个检查点上，覆盖数学推理、通用推理和代码生成基准（GSM8K、MATH-500、BBH、HumanEval+、MBPP+、AIME），LoopSpec在贪心解码和采样设置下均实现最高6.83倍加速，平均接受长度γ超过14.5。在Ouro思维模型上，相比基于训练的DFlash方法，LoopSpec额外提速最高1.7倍。消融实验证实残差第二提案和门控机制的必要性，理论推导的最优提案深度与实验最优配置匹配。

Conclusion: LoopSpec成功将循环Transformer的循环深度转化为token位置并行性，通过无训练的流水线式自推测解码实现了显著的推理加速，同时在贪心和采样两种解码模式下严格保持与目标模型分布一致。其理论分析（最优深度的闭式解）与实证结果高度吻合，为循环Transformer的高效部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Looped Transformers achieve strong performance with compact parameter sizes by repeatedly applying a shared stack of Transformer blocks across recurrent depths. However, they incur higher decoding latency than standard Transformer models of comparable parameter size because shared weights are accessed at every recurrent depth. To improve decoding efficiency, self-speculative decoding is particularly well suited to Looped Transformers, as their intermediate recurrent states can directly provide draft predictions without an auxiliary draft model. We therefore propose LoopSpec, a training-free self-speculative decoding framework tailored for Looped Transformers. LoopSpec extracts draft tokens from early recurrent states and operates in a pipelined manner, overlapping draft generation of future tokens with target verification of the current token. To improve draft accuracy without excessive compute overhead, we introduce a selective second proposal from deeper recurrent depth while ensuring lossless decoding under both greedy and sampling regimes. Furthermore, we derive the optimal proposal depths in closed form and show the prediction matches measurement. Across reasoning and coding benchmarks, LoopSpec achieves up to 6.83$\times$ inference speedup across diverse Looped Transformers.

</details>


### [316] [MyoFlow: Anchor-Tied Rectified Flow for HD-sEMG Gesture Recognition Across Sessions and Subjects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17194)
*Chenhao Wu,Dingjie Peng,Satoshi Funabashi,Satoshi Konishi,Wuqiang Yang,Hiroshi Onoda,Hironori Washizaki,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MyoFlow是首个用于HD-sEMG手势识别的判别式流匹配（flow matching）框架，将跨会话、跨被试的手势识别重新表述为锚点绑定（anchor-tied）的传输问题，通过领域条件化的整流流（rectified flow）将编码窗口运送到手势锚点，同时以最近锚点作为决策规则，无需独立的分类头即可实现零样本和少样本识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HD-sEMG手势识别受电极重新佩戴和生理变异引起的分布漂移影响，在跨会话和跨被试场景下泛化能力差。现有的生成式扩散模型（如DiffHGR）主要用于数据增强或表示学习，但预测仍依赖独立的分类器，学习到的动力学与决策规则相分离。Flow matching在EMG领域此前仅用于生成（如EMGFlow将标签作为输入而非预测目标），缺乏直接的分类规则。因此需要一种将学习到的传输动力学与决策规则直接耦合的方法。

Method: MyoFlow将分类重构为锚点绑定的传输问题：
1. **编码器E_θ**：使用1×1 stem混合电极通道，叠加膨胀深度可分离时间卷积块扩大感受野，全局平均池化加两层投影得到D=256维的初始端点e₀。
2. **条件整流流（Conditional Rectified Flow）**：从编码窗口e₀到其类别对应锚点a_y定义线性插值路径e_t = (1-t)e₀ + t·sg[a_y]，学习领域条件化的速度场v_φ(e_t, t, c_{p,d})，通过Flow Matching损失L_FM拟合。领域上下文c_{p,d}由主体和会话嵌入经小型MLP生成。
3. **锚点库A = [a₁,...,a_C]**：每个手势一个可学习锚点（QR正交初始化，半径r=√D），跨主体和会话共享，同时充当流的传输终点和分类原型。
4. **锚点绑定分类**：终端端点e_T经Euler积分获得，按到各锚点的距离产生logits，预测等价于最近锚点分类——展开平方距离后构成权重为2a_c/τ、偏置为-‖a_c‖²/τ的绑定线性读出器，使学习的类别几何显式而非读出器的伪影。
5. **源域训练目标**：L_train = λ_FM·L_FM + L_anchor + Σλ_r·L_r，包含FM损失、锚点监督（交叉熵+拉近项）以及几何、锚点范数、干净-增强一致性正则项。
6. **少样本校准**：在K>0时，冻结流和锚点库，重新计算BatchNorm统计量，以类别支持集均值初始化线性校准头g_ω，在e₀（而非e_T）上以池化和逐帧两层监督优化，保留类内变异以校正分布漂移。

Result: 在Hyser数据集上，MyoFlow的跨会话平均准确率达87.36%，跨被试达80.26%，分别比最强扩散基线DiffHGR提升4.24和6.37个百分点；在CEMHSEY数据集的跨日（Day 2/4/6/8/11）零样本和少样本设置中均取得最佳结果，平均零样本准确率91.71%、2-rep少样本97.83%。跨被试1-rep和2-rep分别比最优基线高8.54和9.37点。50 ms窗口已具备较强识别能力（100 ms进一步提升但150 ms无显著增益）。零样本ROC曲线在Hyser和CEMHSEY上的宏平均AUC分别达0.963和0.992，所有类别AUC均超过0.950。PCA可视化显示端点轨迹随流推进向类别锚点汇聚。

Conclusion: MyoFlow通过锚点绑定设计将传输动力学直接嵌入决策规则，无需独立分类头即可实现跨会话和跨被试的零样本识别；少样本校准在保留类内变异的初始端点上适配残差分布漂移，且在日间隔越大时获益越显著。实验证明学习到的传输本身即可定义类别决策，而非仅作为单独分类器的辅助。

Abstract: High-density surface electromyography (HD-sEMG) gesture recognition supports prosthetic control, assistive robotics, and rehabilitation, but electrode re-donning and physiological variability cause distribution shifts that degrade accuracy across sessions and subjects. Generative HD-sEMG models primarily synthesize signals for augmentation; although diffusion models enhance representation learning, prediction still relies on a separate classifier. To tie learned dynamics to the decision rule, we propose MyoFlow, the first discriminative flow-matching framework for HD-sEMG recognition across sessions and subjects. It recasts classification as anchor-tied transport: a domain-conditioned rectified flow moves encoded windows toward gesture anchors that serve as transport targets and define the nearest-anchor decision geometry, enabling zero-shot prediction without an independent head. On the Hyser dataset, MyoFlow improves mean cross-session and cross-subject accuracy over the strongest diffusion-based baseline by 4.24\% and 6.37\%, respectively, and achieves 91.71\% mean zero-shot accuracy and 97.39\% mean few-shot accuracy across multiple days on the CEMHSEY dataset.

</details>


### [317] [Memorisation bias in medical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17223)
*Moritz A. Knolle,Martin J. Menten,Laurin Lux,Mélanie Roschewitz,Emma A.M. Stanley,Georgios Kaissis,Daniel Rueckert,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究了医学AI模型的"记忆偏差"（memorisation bias）现象：患者的历史病历被纳入训练集后，模型对其未来检查的预测会系统性地向其历史健康状态偏移。在跨模态（ECG、CXR、EHR）、跨架构的实验中，记忆偏差可延续数十年，并导致"新发疾病"诊断灵敏度显著下降，而健康状态不变者的灵敏度与特异度被人为虚高。差分隐私中记录级DP难以消除该效应，但患者级DP可有效抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注AI记忆现象带来的隐私攻击风险，但对其在临床部署中可能造成的诊断危害（尤其是对贡献数据的回访患者）知之甚少。当前实践下匿名化措施反而使人们难以识别并保护这些"回访贡献者"，因此亟需量化此类纵向记忆效应并探索缓解方案。

Method: 研究提出纵向记忆检测框架：将每位患者的数据按时序分为历史与未来两部分，训练200个仅在50%随机患者子集上训练的模型（涉及MIMIC-ECG、MIMIC-CXR、MIMIC-IV-ED、HEEDB四个数据集），根据模型是否包含该患者历史记录分组成M=100/组，采用基于能量的多变量假设检验比较未来记录预测差异；并通过Youden J阈值和精确置换检验量化模拟部署下的敏感度/特异度变化；同时对比记录级与患者级差分隐私（DP-Adam）下的表现。

Result: 在四个数据集上均检测到显著的未来预测变化，部分记录效应超过70个百分点；HEEDB中心电图显示偏差可持续超25年；在"de novo"条件下敏感度显著降低，效应大小在多病种上经Bonferroni校正后仍显著；健康状态不变者敏感度与特异度被人为抬高；患者级差分隐私几乎消除未来记录的记忆偏差（HEEDB全部预算下为零），而记录级DP即使在ε=1仍残留显著偏差且伴随明显性能损失。

Conclusion: 医学AI的记忆不仅关乎隐私，还可能在临床部署中引起诊断伤害，并集中于贡献数据的患者群体；匿名化不足以防护。缓解策略需以患者为单位进行保护，作者建议在训练与部署协议层面引入患者级差分隐私等机制，并呼吁在监管与实践中进行系统性改革，同时指出未来需开展真实前瞻性试验评估临床危害程度。

Abstract: Medical AI models hold immense potential to improve patient outcomes, but they are also known to unintentionally memorise individual records from their training datasets. While such memorisation has been linked to targeted privacy attacks, its consequences for clinical deployment, where patients may be assessed by a model that saw their historical data during training, remain poorly understood. Here we show that predictions on a patient's unseen future data can change significantly if a model observed that same patient's anonymised historical data during training, a phenomenon we term "memorisation bias". We demonstrate that this bias exists across diverse data modalities and model architectures, and over prolonged time spans: in some cases, memorisation bias persists on future records acquired decades after the historical records used for training. Moreover, in simulated prospective deployment, memorisation bias has asymmetric effects on the diagnostic accuracy of returning data contributors. When a patient returned with a de novo condition absent from their historical records in the training dataset, diagnostic sensitivity decreased significantly compared to an otherwise identical model not trained on their historical data. Conversely, when their health state was unchanged, both sensitivity and specificity were significantly inflated. Our findings reveal a previously uncharacterised risk in medical AI that arises when a model is deployed on patients who contributed to its training data. This exposes a shortcoming of current model development practice: the de-identification measures designed to protect patients' privacy make it difficult to identify returning contributors and exclude them from the AI-assisted interpretation of their own future data. Mitigating memorisation risks may thus require changes to current model training and deployment protocols.

</details>


### [318] [Personalized Federated Learning through Global Knowledge Distillation and Local Head Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17284)
*Polycarpo Souza Neto,José Mairton Barros da Silva Júnior,Charles Casimiro Cavalcante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 pFedKDH 方法：通过仅聚合共享骨干网络、保持客户端特定的持久分类头，并使用重新校准的全局头作为教师进行本地知识蒸馏，解决联邦学习中标签分布异构（label skew）问题。在 MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR10 和 CIFAR100 上的实验表明，该方法在多数设置下达到最优精度，标准差低，计算成本与 FedALA、FedBABU 相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计异构性（特别是标签偏斜）导致传统联邦学习中单一全局分类器无法适配各客户端的标签分布，而现有方法在全模型正则化、自适应聚合或骨干-头分离中仍存在局限。

Method: 将模型分解为骨干网络 θ 和分类头 ϕ；服务器仅聚合骨干 θ_g；每个客户端维持持久私有头 ϕ_k（不上传、不平均、不覆盖）；使用服务器端辅助数据集 D_s 重新校准的全局头 ϕ_g 作为教师，通过 KL 散度进行知识蒸馏；本地损失结合交叉熵、蒸馏损失和近端正则项。提供两种评估变体：pFedKDH-Fair（直接评估）和 pFedKDH-EA（评估时头部适应）。

Result: 在 MNIST、FMNIST、CIFAR10、CIFAR100 上使用 Dirichlet 分区（α∈{0.05, 0.10, 0.50}）的实验中，pFedKDH 在多数设置下取得最佳平均精度，α=0.05 时对最弱基线提升高达 37.67%，对最强基线提升最高 1.82%；标准差始终较低；计算成本（每轮约 6.0-6.5 秒）与 FedALA 和 FedBABU 相当，显著低于 FedRep 和 pFedMe。组件消融验证了持久头和蒸馏机制的贡献。

Conclusion: pFedKDH 通过骨干共享与头部个性化的解耦设计，有效平衡了全局知识迁移和客户端特定决策边界保持，在异构标签分布下实现了高准确度、低方差和稳定收敛。未来工作将探索自适应蒸馏、类别感知教师校准、对辅助数据集 D_s 的敏感性及域偏移鲁棒性。

Abstract: Statistical heterogeneity limits federated learning when a single global classifier cannot represent client-specific label distributions. In this work, we propose Personalized Federated Knowledge Distillation with Head Adaptation (pFedKDH), which aggregates only the shared backbone, keeps persistent client-specific heads, and uses a recalibrated global head as a teacher during local training. Across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR10, and CIFAR100 under class-wise Dirichlet partitions, pFedKDH obtains the best accuracy in most settings, with accuracy gaps up to 37.67\% over the weakest baseline and consistently low standard deviation across repetitions. Component-wise diagnostics and convergence results support the role of persistent heads and distillation-guided local optimization under label-skewed data.

</details>


### [319] [Same Flow, Different Paths: Variance Reduction in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17287)
*Alexander Tyurin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究流匹配(Flow Matching)中条件路径 g_t 的选择对 SGD 优化收敛速率的影响。在保持 FM 目标函数不变的前提下,通过选择不同的路径(属于 G(p_t, v_t*))来降低随机梯度方差,从而加速 SGD 收敛。核心贡献包括:在线性高斯设定下推导出紧致的 SGD 迭代复杂度界并找到解析最优路径;将方差分析推广至一般 FM 问题,提出 PathOptθ 优化问题;给出可从样本估计的可行等价形式 FeasiblePathOptθ;在 GMM 和真实数据集上验证了理论发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配方法使用预设路径(如直线插值),但同一 FM 目标可由无穷多条路径实现。本文从优化角度出发,探讨路径选择对 SGD 收敛速率的影响,即不同路径虽诱导相同的 FM 问题,却可能产生显著不同的随机梯度方差。

Method: 方法包含四部分:(1) 线性模型+高斯数据下,推导 SGD 迭代复杂度的紧致上下界(通过随机二次优化命题),利用 Euler-Lagrange 方程求解最优路径;(2) 一般 FM 问题的方差分解为路径无关项 Var̄₁ 和路径相关项 Var̄₂,提出在约束 g_t ∈ G(p_t, v_t*) 下最小化方差的 PathOptθ 问题;(3) 利用能量距离(energy distance)和基于核的速度差异度量,将不可验证的约束转化为可从样本估计的形式 FeasiblePathOptθ;(4) 通过对偶上升/原始-对偶优化算法求解,辅以网格搜索和实验验证。

Result: 理论上,在线性高斯情形获得最优路径的解析表达和收敛速率 Θ̃(1/(σ⁴ε))(大 σ² 区域);构造反例证明无约束方差最小化反而会减慢收敛(Example 3.1,复杂度随 M→∞ 而发散);时间采样优化至多只能带来对数因子的改善。实验上,在 CIFAR-10/100、SVHN 上 FID 分别降低 0.22、0.89、3.31,GMM 实验中学习路径生成质量优于直线路径。

Conclusion: 条件路径 g_t 的选择可以在不改变 FM 目标的前提下显著改善 SGD 收敛速率。约束 g_t ∈ G(p_t, v_t*) 对于保证等价性和避免退化至关重要,无约束的方差最小化会产生更慢的收敛。可行路径优化问题 FeasiblePathOptθ 提供了从样本数值求解的理论框架。该工作开辟了利用 CFM 表示非唯一性来改进优化效率的新方向。

Abstract: In flow matching (FM), a velocity model $v_{\theta}$ is trained using a predefined path $g_t$ that connects data and noise samples (e.g., $g_t(x_0, x_1) = (1 - t) x_0 + t x_1$). In this work, we study the choice of this path from an optimization perspective by analyzing the variance of stochastic gradients. We consider the class $G(p_t,v^\star_t)$ of paths that induce the same marginal distributions $p_t$ and marginal velocity field $v^\star_t$, and therefore the same FM objective. Our main finding is that the choice of path $g_t$ can fundamentally change the convergence rate of SGD, even when the FM objective remains exactly the same. (i) For a linear velocity model and one-dimensional Gaussian data, we derive a tight bound on the SGD iteration complexity up to logarithmic factors and find an analytically optimal path that minimizes this bound among linear paths inducing the same FM problem. (ii) We then extend the variance analysis to general FM problems and formulate path selection at a fixed $\theta$ as the variance-minimization problem PathOpt$_\theta$, constrained to $g_t\in G(p_t,v^\star_t)$. We show that this constraint is essential: reducing variance without it can lead to slower convergence. (iii) Since the constraint $g_t \in G(p_t,v^\star_t)$ cannot generally be verified directly, we derive an equivalent formulation with constraints that can be estimated from samples, allowing paths to be found numerically. Our theoretical results are supported by experiments with Gaussian data, Gaussian mixture models, and real datasets.

</details>


### [320] [Hybrid Variational Quantum Circuits for Multivariate Regression and High-Dimensional Data Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17358)
*Koffi Ognandon Ayena,Frédéric Holweck,Serge Iovleff,Amah S d'Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合变分量子电路（HVQC），在传统变分量子电路（VQC）基础上引入经典仿射后测量层，实现向量化回归输出，避免了为每个标量输出分量独立运行电路的线性开销，并在合成图像重建和Friedman1基准上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分量子电路（VQC）在处理多维向量回归任务时，需要为每个输出分量独立构建标量电路，导致计算开销随输出维度线性增长，且缺乏对量子-经典混合架构中特征映射作用的系统性分析。

Method: 提出混合变分量子电路（HVQC）：在VQC基础上增加经典仿射后测量层（affine post-measurement layer），通过数据重上传（data re-uploading）和纠缠机制构造单/双量子比特电路，理论上证明其可逼近二次函数和乘积项；实验采用两个合成图像重建数据集和Friedman1基准（40,568个测试样本），与高斯过程回归（Gaussian Process Regression）、XGBoost和随机森林（Random Forest）进行对比，并进行消融研究。

Result: 在两个合成图像重建数据集和Friedman1基准（40,568测试样本）上，HVQC性能匹配高斯过程回归，优于XGBoost和随机森林；消融实验证实量子组件和经典仿射层均为必要组成部分。

Conclusion: HVQC通过经典仿射后测量层有效解决了VQC的向量输出瓶颈，同时验证了量子与经典组件协同的必要性；研究凸显了特征映射（feature map）在混合量子-经典模型中的核心作用，为混合量子机器学习架构设计提供了理论与实验依据。

Abstract: Variational quantum circuits (VQCs) are parameterized quantum circuits optimized classically. We propose a hybrid variational quantum circuit (HVQC) extending VQCs with a classical affine post-measurement layer, enabling vector-valued regression without the linear overhead of independent scalar circuits. Theoretically, we show that elementary one-and two-qubit circuits can approximate quadratic functions and products via data re-uploading and entanglement, providing the foundations of the full architecture. Experimentally, on two synthetic image reconstruction datasets and the Friedman1 benchmark (40,568 test samples), our HVQC matches Gaussian Process Regression and outperforms XGBoost and Random Forest. An ablation study confirms that both quantum and classical components are essential, and results highlight the central role of the feature map in hybrid quantum-classical models.

</details>


### [321] [Large Language Models Develop Belief State Geometry In-Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17376)
*Daniel Balcells,Andrew Jun Lee,Chirag Rastogi,Paul M. Riechers,Adam Shai,Xavier Poncini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在受控的HMM数据设置下探测LLM的ICL内部表征，发现LLM的残差流激活中线性编码了贝叶斯信念状态，且具有功能性因果作用，支持ICL近似于对上下文推断的生成模型进行最优贝叶斯预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在上下文学习（ICL）方面表现出色，但支撑ICL的内部表征尚不清楚——LLM是否真正推断出隐式生成模型并执行贝叶斯推理？先前在玩具网络上的结果是否适用于生产级LLM尚不明确。

Method: 1) 设计受控实验：从40个具有非平凡信念结构的隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）生成提示数据；2) 线性探测（probing）：在6个开源LLM的残差流激活上训练线性探针，预测HMM的信念状态（后验分布）；3) 因果干预：通过表征修补（patching）和引导（steering）直接干预探针识别出的子空间，验证其下游功能相关性。

Result: 在40个HMM和6个LLM的组合上，信念状态从残差流激活中高度线性可解码，峰值探针R²介于0.83-0.99；可解码层从早期到晚期均有分布。对探针子空间的因果干预能维持模型的下游预测质量（与未干预模型相当），而对照组则导致性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 提供了表征层面的证据，表明开源LLM的ICL近似于对从上下文推断出的生成模型的最优贝叶斯预测；将先前从玩具网络到生产级LLM的"输入分布结构→激活几何"联系推广到了更大规模。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) trained on next-token prediction exhibit remarkable in-context learning (ICL) abilities, yet the representations that support ICL remain poorly understood. We consider such representations in a controlled setting: prompting LLMs with data emitted from hidden Markov models (HMMs) and probing for the corresponding belief state -- the posterior distribution over the HMM's hidden states given the observed token history. Across six open-source LLMs prompted with data from 40 HMMs selected for non-trivial belief structure, we find that belief states are linearly decodable from residual stream activations, with peak probe $R^2$-values from 0.83-0.99 across HMM and LLM combinations, ranging from early to late layers. To establish functional relevance, we intervene directly on the probe-identified subspace via patching and steering, resulting in downstream prediction quality on the order of the untampered model, while controls degrade performance substantially. Together, these results provide representation-level evidence that ICL in open-source LLMs approximates optimal Bayesian prediction over a context-inferred generative model. More broadly, our findings extend prior results linking input-distribution structure to activation geometry: from toy networks trained explicitly on HMM data to production-scale LLMs.

</details>


### [322] [OPEN-1B: A Fully Auditable Training Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17380)
*John Donaghy,Brian Wilcox,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Shikhar Rastogi,Adam St Arnaud,Alexey Titov,Jordan Greenberg,Ben Fielding,Harry Grieve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Open-1B，这是首个完全可审计的开源大语言模型。作者提出了一个全新的模型透明度层级——"fully auditable"（完全可审计），通过强制规定训练中所有非确定性来源（GPU核归约、数据批量排序、集合通信）的顺序，使得训练过程中对每个数据样本的每个操作都能在异构商品硬件上以比特级精度独立复现。同时设计了集体验证方案，让多个独立审计者各自验证个别步骤，共同覆盖整个训练过程。作者还发布了RepOps库（跨异构硬件的比特级可复现运算库）、拓扑不变的数据加载器、确定性集合通信，以及模型本身（含完整预训练数据集、每100步的中间检查点、训练代码库和审计工具）。Open-1B是一个1.61B参数的解码器-only Transformer，采用int8量化感知训练，在400B token上训练，使用6个节点的48块H100 GPU，历时29.5天。其审计能力允许任何人在商品硬件上重放并验证训练过程的任意步骤，用单一哈希值即可证明模型确实按声明的方式训练而成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源大语言模型存在可复现性问题：尽管发布了权重、训练数据和配方，但由于浮点运算的非结合性，即使严格遵循相同训练配方，在不同硬件上也无法产生比特级相同的模型权重。现有的启发式验证技术（如proof-of-learning或proof-of-training-data）只能提供概率性验证，无法可靠地检测通过少量数据点注入的后门。这使得即使是开源模型也无法被完全验证。本文旨在解决这一根本性透明度问题。

Method: 本文方法包含三大基础设施组件：(1) RepOps库——通过固定归约顺序、强制执行单一融合乘加约定、在所有后端将次正规数刷新为零、以及使用基于计数器的随机数生成器，实现在CPU、NVIDIA GPU、Apple GPU之间的比特级可复现运算；(2) 拓扑不变的可复现数据流——将训练数据定义为仅由训练种子和语料清单确定的逻辑序列，由源交错、分片排序、文档排序三个伪随机生成器完全确定，与集群拓扑无关；(3) 确定性集合通信——使用自定义的DeterministicReduceScatter（确定性归约分散）和TreeFold（二进制进位树折叠）算法，可在单设备上按顺序重放整个数据并行集群的梯度归约过程。审计机制允许独立审计者在单台商品硬件上重放训练集群的任意单步，并将其与发布的训练轨迹进行比特级比对。

Result: Open-1B是一个1.61B参数的解码器-only Transformer（1.08B非嵌入参数），采用24层、隐藏维度2048、GQA（16查询头/4 KV头）、SwiGLU激活、RoPE（θ=500000）、混合滑动窗口注意力（窗口512，每5层全注意力）、无增益QK归一化、z-loss（权重1e-4）、解绑嵌入、int8量化感知训练（LSQ，每步重新计算步长）。使用AdamW优化器（β1=0.9，β2=0.95，ε=1e-8，权重衰减0.1），在400B token上训练（Open-1B Mix 0626数据集：DCLM-Baseline 66.7%、FineWeb-Edu 13.3%、Stack v2 12.0%、Proof-Pile-2 8.0%），全局种子42。训练采用三阶段批量调度，最终验证交叉熵2.580。预训练效率：MFU约5%，吞吐量从单节点40k tokens/s扩展到6节点169.4k tokens/s（强扩展效率71%），状态哈希每秒约6秒。可复现运行相比PyTorch bf16基线慢约5倍。基线评估显示Open-1B在下游基准上落后于OLMo 2 1B（因训练token量少10倍）。稳定性问题及解决方案：发现int8反向注意力中查询梯度为零导致梯度蠕变（移除QK归一化增益解决）和量化器步长漂移（改为每步从权重重新计算步长解决）。

Conclusion: 本文发布了首个完全可审计的开源大语言模型Open-1B及其完整基础设施。比特级可复现性通过RepOps库、拓扑不变数据加载器和确定性集合通信实现，使得任何人都可以在异构商品硬件上审计训练过程的任意步骤，并以比特级精度验证其与发布训练轨迹的一致性。整个训练运行可归结为单一哈希值，提供模型确实按声明方式训练的确定性证明。这一保证的代价是可复现运行比优化PyTorch慢约5倍，MFU仅约5%（而优化PyTorch达40.5%）。由于资源和开销限制，Open-1B仅用400B token训练（相比OLMo 2 1B的4T token少一个数量级），导致下游基准性能落后。作者希望这是迈向完全开放透明AI开发过程的第一步，使得关于模型构建方式的声明可以被验证而非仅凭信任。

Abstract: Open-source language models have a reproducibility problem. Despite releasing weights, training data, and recipes, none of them are provably reproducible due to the non-associativity of floating-point arithmetic. Deep learning frameworks often offer a deterministic execution mode, allowing reproducible operations on the same machines. Unfortunately, this determinism does not carry across hardware such that a user can verify that a released checkpoint was actually produced using the declared training recipe. This leaves room for undisclosed data, injected biases, or backdoors that existing techniques such as proof-of-learning or proof-of-training-data cannot rule out. We introduce a new tier of model transparency, fully auditable, in which every operation on every data sample during training is independently reproducible on heterogeneous commodity hardware with bitwise certainty. By imposing a definite order on the sources of training nondeterminism, GPU kernel reductions, data batch ordering across a data-parallel cluster, and inter/intra-node collective communication, we make it possible to replay any individual step of a large, distributed training run on a single piece of commodity hardware and check it against the published trajectory. Because replaying an entire run on one machine is infeasible, we support this with a collective verification scheme in which many independent auditors each certify individual steps, together covering the whole run. We release Open-1B, a model trained under this regime, together with its full pretraining dataset, every intermediate checkpoint, the training codebase, and the audit harness needed to reproduce and verify any step of its training.

</details>


### [323] [Bridging the Confidence Gap: Temperature Scaling for Calibrating Test-Time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17386)
*Yuwei Liang,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Yinuo Xu,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoTS和E-CoTS两种后处理校准方法，通过温度缩放将测试时提示调优（TPT）的置信度与零样本预测对齐，在不牺牲准确率的前提下有效降低期望校准误差（ECE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时提示调优（TPT）虽能提升视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）的分类准确率，但会导致预测过度自信，产生严重的校准误差问题。现有基于正则化的校准方法（如C-TPT、O-TPT等）通过增加文本嵌入分散度来降低ECE，却以牺牲准确率为代价；而唯一的post-hoc方法SaLS虽保持准确率但校准改进有限。因此，需要一种能同时保持准确率并显著改善校准的方法。

Method: 1. CoTS（基于置信度的温度缩放）：一种post-hoc方法，通过优化温度参数τ，使TPT预测的最大置信度对齐零样本预测的置信度，使用L2损失在选定的强增强视图上进行温度学习，无需标注验证集。
2. E-CoTS：引入弱-强增强集成策略，将弱增强视图与多个强增强视图通过自适应权重α进行加权组合，其中α由文本嵌入间的平均余弦相似度决定。CoTS的温度缩放应用于集成预测，在保持准确率提升的同时确保良好校准。

Result: 在11个细粒度数据集和4个ImageNet变体上的实验表明：
- CoTS在ViT-B/16上将TPT的平均ECE从11.30%降至4.28%（细粒度数据集），从11.90%降至5.30%（ImageNet变体），同时保持准确率不变。
- E-CoTS进一步提升准确率，在ImageNet变体上将准确率从60.74%提升至62.95%，ECE降至5.38%。
- 作为即插即用模块，CoTS和E-CoTS与现有校准方法（如C-TPT、O-TPT、SoC等）结合时，通常能同时改善准确率和ECE。
- 方法在ResNet-50、CoOp初始化、在线TTA框架（TDA）、其他episodic TTA方法（TTL、TPS）以及SigLIP上均表现稳健有效。
- 仅增加约0.05秒/样本的计算开销。

Conclusion: CoTS和E-CoTS通过简单的post-hoc温度缩放有效解决了TPT中的置信度膨胀问题，在保持甚至提升准确率的同时显著降低校准误差。作为即插即用模块，它们可与现有校准方法互补，为视觉-语言模型在安全关键应用中的可靠部署提供了有效方案。局限在于目前仅在分类任务上验证，且未扩展至语义分割、目标检测等任务。

Abstract: Test-time prompt tuning (TPT) enables adaptation on a single test instance, achieving improved accuracy but often sacrificing calibration performance. Most existing calibration methods introduce additional regularization terms to promote dispersion across text embeddings and reduce calibration error, yet these methods often suffer from a drop in accuracy. Motivated by the well-calibrated nature of zero-shot predictions, we propose CoTS, a simple yet effective post-hoc calibration method that preserves accuracy. Specifically, CoTS applies temperature scaling to minimize the confidence gap between adapted and zero-shot predictions. To fully exploit the potential of multiple augmentations during adaptation, we introduce a weak-strong ensemble strategy that further boosts accuracy. We then apply CoTS to this ensemble, termed E-CoTS, to maintain its well-calibrated property. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets and backbones show that our approaches effectively mitigate miscalibration without compromising primary accuracy. For instance, E-CoTS reduces the average expected calibration error of TPT from 11.90% to 5.38% on ImageNet variants, while even increasing accuracy from 60.74% to 62.95%. Moreover, when integrated with existing calibration methods, E-CoTS usually enhances both accuracy and calibration simultaneously.

</details>


### [324] [Knowledge as Orbit: Finite Collections as Phases of an Exactly Periodic Latent Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17417)
*Siddharth Pal,Viktoria Rojkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将有限集合存储为"轨道"而非独立实例——通过一个固定旋转的离散傅里叶算子作为潜空间地址生成器，配合共享解码器渲染内容。算子通过单位根特征值保证 R^X = I，使轨道精确闭合。实验表明：精确周期性是关键约束（一般算子发散、单位算子累积误差）；在七个循环视频基准上以匹配参数达到与NeRV基线相当或更好的保真度，且闭合接缝达到机器精度（~10⁻⁷），而基线留下2-5%可见接缝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限集合以"外延方式"存储：每个项目一个独立代码或向量。本文质疑：是否可以用"内涵方式"——一个紧凑的动态法则，其轨道枚举所有项目并精确回到起点——来代替独立实例存储，从而为循环结构（如循环视频）提供代数保证的精确闭合。

Method: 核心方法：将项目 i 编码为潜空间中固定旋转的第 i 个相位 z_i = R^i z_0，通过共享解码器 D 渲染为对象 x_i ≈ D(z_i)。算子 R 是块对角的平面旋转组，频率为循环基频 2π/X 的整数谐波，构成实离散傅里叶算子。由于频率为整数，R^X = I 精确成立。训练时固定算子 R，仅优化初始潜码 z_0 和解码器 D。实验对比三类算子：一般线性（Koopman式）、学习单位算子（非周期）、精确周期傅里叶算子；并在解码器容量、独立潜码基线、循环视频任务上进行系统评估。

Result: 关键结果：(1) 算子对比——在合成和真实图像上，精确周期算子在长轨道上显著优于一般算子和非周期单位算子（后者在CIFAR-100上从76 dB衰减至13 dB）；(2) 容量——密连接解码器存在约2.25×的结构性开销，无法同时实现清晰与压缩；小卷积解码器在共享流形的128张单类图像上达到32.2 dB且压缩至1.66×，同时满足清晰与压缩；(3) 码本控制——独立潜码基线从未在重建上获胜，且潜码存储大128倍；(4) 循环视频——在七个基准片段上以匹配参数达到与帧索引基线相当或更好的PSNR（平均领先0.7 dB），闭合接缝达机器精度（<3.3×10⁻⁷），而基线接缝>1.5×10⁻²；(5) 将基线频率钉定到整数谐波后接缝消除但保真度仍落后1.9 dB，证明精确周期性是必要条件。

Conclusion: 论文得出三个有限范围的结论：(1) 精确周期性是结构约束，使单一算子能在不累积漂移的情况下枚举多个对象；(2) 在测量设置下，该约束相对独立潜码不付出重建代价，却以少得多的潜码存储换取顺序与精确闭合；(3) 当所表示的知识本身是循环的（如循环视频），该构造将训练近似结果（接缝）转化为表示本身保证的性质。文章明确作用域：所有实验为小规模内部对照；压缩对比针对原始帧与匹配基线而非编解码器；该方法仅适用于循环索引的有限集合，非循环内容无优势。论文还指出未来方向：将循环群 C_X 推广到有限群或半群，以匹配更复杂的知识结构（如分类法、图）。

Abstract: Finite knowledge is usually stored extensionally, one code or vector per item. We ask whether a finite collection can instead be stored intensionally, as the decoded orbit of one compact law that returns exactly to its start. For X objects, we encode item i as the i-th phase of a fixed rotation in a learned latent space and decode all phases with a shared network; the latent advances through a bank of rotations at integer harmonics of the cycle, a real discrete Fourier operator, so that R^X equals the identity and exact closure is guaranteed rather than learned. Images are a controlled carrier; looping video is the case where the phase order is the content's own temporal structure. Holding the decoder fixed and varying only the operator, a general learned operator diverges, a norm-preserving but non-periodic one degrades around the loop, and the exactly periodic operator is flat; on real images the gap widens. Capacity is then the decoder's budget: dense decoders carry a structural overhead per crisp image that no size reconciles with compression, while a small convolutional decoder on objects that share a manifold reaches crisp and compressed. A codebook control shows the generative law is free in reconstruction terms while multiplying the latent store many-fold. On seven benchmark clips, against a matched frame-index baseline, the cycle reaches equal or better fidelity at equal parameters while wrapping at machine precision, where the baseline leaves a visible seam; pinning the baseline's frequencies to loop harmonics closes its seam too, confirming that exact periodicity is the operative constraint. Finite cyclic knowledge can be stored as dynamics rather than independent instances, with exact recurrence supplied by algebra and content by a shared decoder.

</details>


### [325] [Reduced-Space Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization of Process Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17440)
*Niki Triantafyllou,Andrea Bernardi,Maria M. Papathanasiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了降维空间多保真贝叶斯优化（RS-MFBO）框架，通过全局敏感性分析（GSA）进行降维，并结合保真度增强高斯过程和成本感知采集策略，在两个工业过程仿真器（SuperPro Designer 和 Aspen HYSYS）上实现了高维、昂贵黑箱函数的高效优化，显著减少了高保真仿真评估次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程流程优化面临高保真仿真计算成本高、复杂设计空间维度灾难等挑战。传统单保真贝叶斯优化在面对昂贵且偶尔不收敛的仿真器评估时效率低下，而纯粹基于代理模型的方法在训练域外推断时会失去精度。现有高维BO方法多在缺乏物理意义的潜在空间操作，难以满足过程系统工程对可解释性的需求。

Method: RS-MFBO框架包含三个核心组件：（1）基于总阶Sobol指数的全局敏感性分析（GSA），设置阈值τ=0.01筛选主导变量实现降维；（2）输入增强的多保真高斯过程，在增广空间(x,s)上构建联合先验，采用线性截断保真度核，Matérn-5/2核作为基础核，通过共享协方差分量耦合高低保真度信息；（3）成本感知UCB采集策略J_s=UCB_s/c_s^α，结合冷却期（H=10）和提升机制（每P=10步提升top-K个LO点），通过ℓ∞范数去重避免冗余评估。

Result: 在pDNA生物工艺（18个混合变量，6个KPI）和DME绿色燃料合成（14个混合变量，6个KPI）两个案例研究中，RS-MFBO在12个不同目标上均展现出稳健收敛性能。相比单保真基线，在相同总成本预算下减少了约65-80%的高保真仿真评估次数；在DME能效优化中，从初始80%效率开始搜索，峰值达约84%；DME生产率优化中仅需不到10次评估即达到约4.0 kg/s的最优区域。降维使采集函数优化壁时间减少40-60%，并在CapEx等目标上帮助逃出单保真方法陷入的局部最优。

Conclusion: RS-MFFO是一种可扩展的、仿真器无关的成本约束黑箱优化方法，通过GSA降维、多保真GP和成本感知采集三者的协同，有效解决了高维、昂贵过程仿真优化的关键挑战。未来工作包括将保真度层次扩展至两级以上，以及开发可学习的保真度选择策略以增强普适性。

Abstract: Optimizing industrial process flowsheets is often computationally prohibitive due to the high cost of rigorous simulations and the curse of dimensionality inherent in complex design spaces. To address these challenges, we present a reduced-space multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (RS-MFBO) framework designed for high-dimensional, expensive black-box functions. The approach integrates Global Sensitivity Analysis (GSA) for dimensionality reduction with a fidelity-augmented Gaussian process that captures correlations between low-cost approximations and expensive high-fidelity evaluations. A cost-aware acquisition strategy, augmented with cooldown and promotion mechanisms, adaptively guides the allocation of samples across fidelities. The framework is validated on two distinct industrial process simulators: a plasmid DNA bioprocess in SuperPro Designer and a green fuel synthesis plant in Aspen HYSYS. Results across diverse economic and physical objectives demonstrate that the proposed method substantially reduces the number of high-fidelity simulator evaluations while maintaining competitive optimization performance compared to single-fidelity baselines. These results highlight RS-MFBO as a scalable, simulator-agnostic approach for cost-constrained black-box optimization.

</details>


### [326] [FreqSpaNet: Frequency and Spatial Learning of SFPF for Physical Layer Hardware Integrity Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17491)
*Xiaoxuan Huang,Jinlong Xu,YiZhe Wang,Meng Zhang,Xian Li,Yuying Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreqSpaNet 是一种针对时空频极化指纹 (SFPF) 的表示学习框架，用于物理层硬件完整性异常检测。它通过双分支编码器分别建模频率局部依赖与方向角几何关系，并结合自适应融合与互补预训练，在开放集硬件异常检测任务中达到 96.31% 的平均 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线设备存在未经授权的硬件替换威胁，保留逻辑身份但改变物理实现。SFPF 包含频率与空间两个不同结构依赖的维度，亟需针对性表征学习方法以应对开放集硬件异常检测。

Method: 1) 共享 SFPF 编码器提取方向-频率 token；2) 频率分支使用深度可分离 1D 卷积捕捉相邻频率局部变化；3) 空间分支利用方向嵌入与角间距偏差建模方向间依赖；4) 基于响应幅度的样本自适应融合；5) 互补预训练 (掩码重建 + 对比学习 + 共性-私有解耦)；6) 微调中使用离群暴露并融合四种异常分数。

Result: FreqSpaNet 在 0-20 dB 信噪比下平均 AUROC 达 96.31%，较最佳基线提升 9.05 个百分点；在 7 种硬件替换场景下均表现稳健，15-20 dB 时 AUROC 高达 99.33%；消融实验证实各组件有效性。

Conclusion: 针对 SFPF 的特定频率-空间结构依赖进行建模，显著优于通用 CNN、Transformer 和 MAE 基背骨网络。频率与空间分支、自适应融合、角几何感知以及互补预训练共同构成了有效的物理层硬件完整性检测框架。

Abstract: Unauthorized hardware replacement can preserve a wireless device's logical identity while altering its physical implementation, posing a challenge to hardware integrity verification. Spatio-frequency polarization fingerprints (SFPFs) capture device-dependent responses across multiple frequencies and directions, but their frequency and spatial dimensions exhibit different structural dependencies. We propose FreqSpaNet, an SFPF representation learning network for open set hardware anomaly detection. A frequency branch captures local variations among neighboring frequencies, while a geometry-aware spatial branch models directional relationships using angular information. The two representations are combined through adaptive fusion, and complementary pretraining further captures shared information while preserving the distinct characteristics of the frequency and spatial representations. Experiments show that FreqSpaNet achieves a mean AUROC of 96.31\%, 9.05 points above the baseline. Results under seven hardware replacement scenarios further verify the effectiveness of FreqSpaNet.

</details>
